### [1] [DataKernelBench: Can LLMs Optimize Database Queries on GPUs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25061)
*Gokul Karthik Kumar,Yotam Perlitz,Corey Lammie,Andrea Giovannini,Katja Hose*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DataKernelBench 是首个针对数据库查询的 LLM GPU 内核生成基准，将 SQL 翻译为 PyTorch TorchPlan 中间表示，通过执行引导的修复循环让 LLM 在 core/full 两个层级用 CUDA 或 Triton 优化。GPT-5.5 在 TPC-H SF10 上以 CUDA-full 达到 100% 通过率和 2.11× 加速；TPC-H SF100 在 4×H100 上扩展至 2.54× 加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 内核基准（KernelBench、TritonBench、MultiKernelBench）聚焦于规则的 ML 算子，无法测试不规则、异构、数据移动密集的数据库风格算子。GPU 上分析型查询峰值性能仍依赖手写内核，而通用 ML 编译器（如 torch.compile）在分析负载上存在泛化瓶颈。论文旨在评估 LLM 是否能为数据库查询合成专用 GPU 内核，弥合编译后的张量执行与人工调优内核之间的性能差距。

Method: 1. **TorchPlan IR**：将 SQL 翻译为可验证的 PyTorch 张量程序，含 run_query（cuDF 表处理）和 _query_core（张量热路径）两个函数，以 DuckDB 差分测试验证语义。2. **双层级优化**：core 级别仅优化 _query_core，full 级别可重写整个 TorchPlan 但保留外部 API。3. **双后端**：支持 Triton 和 CUDA。4. **执行引导修复循环**：最多 10 轮，将验证错误、性能反馈作为下一轮输入。5. **评价指标**：通过率 + 相对 torch.compile 的加速比，最低加速阈值 1.05×。6. **Dask-cuDF 扩展**：通过 map_partitions 进行按需分区加载，支持多 GPU 和超内存数据。

Result: **主基准（TPC-H SF10，H100）**：GPT-5.5 CUDA-full 100% 通过率，整体 2.11× 加速（超越 Sirius 的 1.37×），Q14 达 11.2×；开源模型 Qwen3.5-397B-A17B Triton-full 最佳 1.26×（100% 通过）。**计划分析**：22/22 GPT-5.5 实现通过源代码审计和 SF1/扰动 SF10 的留出测试；最大增益来自内核融合、混合执行（21/22 保留 cuDF 处理字符串）、融合 CUDA 聚合（14/22 替换 torch.unique/scatter_add）。**优化层级影响**：Top-5 模型从 core 扩展到 full 获得 +0.31（CUDA）/+0.29（Triton）加速，Bottom-5 仅 +0.02/+0.05。**提示消融**：工作负载上下文比硬件上下文重要得多（CUDA 下删除数据描述导致 -0.40，删除 GPU 描述仅 -0.27）。**后端偏好**：4 模型偏好 CUDA（OpenAI 系列、Gemini、Claude Opus），6 模型偏好 Triton；CUDA 提供更高性能上限，Triton 是弱模型的更优性价比选择。**修复行为**：77.5% 轨迹最终成功；首轮后 40%，三轮后 65%；CUDA 失败 74% 为编译错误，Triton 失败 79% 为执行错误；Q6/Q19 全 40 轨迹成功，Q13 仅 13/40。**TPC-H SF100（4×H100，10GB chunks）**：22/22 GPT-5.5 CUDA-full 实现通过验证，整体 36.44s，相对 Dask-cuDF TorchPlan 2.54× 加速；单 GPU 时达 3.53×。**瓶颈点相关性**：所有 10 个 LLM 的覆盖度与加速比呈负相关，Sirius 呈正相关（ρ=-0.267，n=220）。**TorchPlan 生成器敏感性**：用 GPT-5.5 生成的 TorchPlan 整体慢 15%，但核心结论不变。

Conclusion: DataKernelBench 建立了 LLM 生成数据库查询 GPU 内核的基准测试基础，证明专用内核可成为高频重查询的有效优化路径而非通用替代：GPT-5.5 在 100% 通过率下达到 2.11× 加速，融合和执行策略变更贡献最大增益，通用 GPU 数据库系统在更复杂查询上仍有竞争力。框架可扩展至多 GPU 超内存场景（2.54×）。核心结论：（1）模型能力与 full 级优化收益正相关；（2）工作负载上下文比硬件上下文关键得多；（3）CUDA 提供更高上限但 Triton 对弱模型更可靠；（4）专用内核与通用引擎呈互补关系。

Abstract: GPUs increasingly accelerate database systems, but query-specific peak performance still often relies on hand-written kernels. Existing LLM kernel benchmarks focus on machine learning operators, leaving irregular, heterogeneous, data-movement-heavy database-style operators untested. We introduce DataKernelBench, which translates SQL into validated PyTorch TorchPlan programs and evaluates LLMs that optimize either the core tensor-bounded snippet or the full query in CUDA or Triton through execution-guided repair. Across ten proprietary and open-weight models on TPC-H SF10 with an H100 GPU, the strongest full-query CUDA configuration achieves $2.11\times$ speedup over this http URL at full pass rate. We find that higher-performing implementations commonly use kernel fusion and execution-strategy changes, stronger models benefit most from full-query specialization, and workload context matters more than hardware context. To handle data larger than GPU memory, we extend TorchPlan with Dask-cuDF for on-demand partition loading on TPC-H SF100 with four H100 GPUs, achieving $2.54\times$ speedup

</details>


### [2] [Semantic Variability of Replies Across LLMs: Implications for Designing Conversation-Based Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24920)
*Jiangang Hao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了不同大语言模型（LLM）在生成回复时是否存在语义一致性的差异，以及对话历史和提示策略对一致性的影响，为基于对话的评估系统设计提供实证依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在协作与沟通等复杂技能评估中的应用日益广泛，模型快速迭代带来的响应变异性可能引入与无关构念的方差（construct-irrelevant variance），威胁评估的效度、信度与公平性。本研究旨在系统量化模型选择与对话上下文对回复语义一致性的影响。

Method: 基于2018年通过Amazon Mechanical Turk收集的99组双人协作解题对话数据，筛选出61条高相关性的末段焦点消息。在"有/无对话历史"两种提示条件下，使用GPT-4o mini、GPT-5.4、GPT-5.4 mini和GPT-5.4 nano四个LLM对每条焦点消息各生成100条回复。采用OpenAI text-embedding-3-large生成嵌入向量并计算余弦相似度，从组内相似度、与人类回复对齐度、跨模型相似度以及相似焦点消息的回复相似度四个维度进行分析，并使用线性混合效应模型估计总体效应。

Result: 研究发现：(1)同一LLM的回复之间相似度（0.715-0.795）显著高于跨LLM的回复相似度（0.443-0.604），表明每个模型倾向于产生具有自身特征的回复；(2)GPT-5.4系列模型间的相似度高于其与GPT-4o mini的相似度，说明模型家族会影响一致性；(3)加入对话历史并未在所有情况下提升回复间的语义一致性，且其效应因模型而异；(4)LLM回复与人类回复的相似度普遍较低，加入对话历史略有改善；(5)焦点消息相似时，无历史条件下生成的回复相似度更高，加入历史则降低了这种锚定关系。

Conclusion: 提示工程和对话上下文不足以保证不同LLM间回复的语义一致性，模型架构与谱系对一致性具有显著影响。在LLM快速演进的背景下，基于对话的评估系统需将一致性视为基础设施层面的挑战，引入符号规则、回复模板、验证层等机制来监控与控制跨模型过渡中的响应行为，以保障高风险评估中的效度、信度与公平性。

Abstract: This study examines whether LLM-generated replies remain semantically consistent when the underlying LLM changes. Using messages from real collaborative conversations, we compared the semantic similarity of generated replies across LLMs under two conditions: with and without preceding chat history. Results show that model choice and conversational context both affect response similarity and alignment with human replies. These findings indicate that prompting and conversational context alone may not be sufficient to preserve response consistency across LLMs, highlighting the need for infrastructure and design strategies that can maintain stable and comparable responses amid the rapid and continuous evolution of LLMs.

</details>


### [3] [The Dialect Tax: Dialectal Biases Persist throughout the Language Modeling Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24952)
*Elle*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文系统地追踪了方言偏见（"dialect tax"）在语言建模管道的每个阶段（分词、预训练、后训练和推理）中的表现，使用平行英语方言语料库发现，尽管模型识别SAE和方言文本语义等价，但在所有阶段对AAVE等方言的处理均存在系统性偏差，且这些偏差在管道中累积而非由单一环节造成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型对方言（尤其是非标准方言如AAVE）的系统性性能差距已被广泛记录，但其产生的具体来源尚不清楚。先前研究通常孤立考察管道的某个环节，作者希望从整体管道追踪方言偏见的来源，验证其是否仅由分词器造成，还是累积性偏见。

Method: 使用ParallelAAVE和MultiVALUE平行语料库（语义一致，方言不同），结合ReDial推理基准，通过八个维度进行系统分析：①EmbeddingGemma验证语义等价性；②比较BPE/Unigram/WordPiece三种分词器在7个模型上的分词指标；③在9个指令调优LM上测试推理准确率；④使用字符级分词反事实实验隔离分词器影响；⑤通过CountSketch压缩梯度分析预训练阶段梯度散度；⑥评估10个奖励模型的样本级和token级奖励；⑦分析推理阶段的隐藏状态和输出分布。涉及Llama-3、Gemma-3、Qwen-3等模型家族（1B-70B参数）。

Result: 主要发现：1）模型能识别SAE-方言对的语义等价（cosine相似度>0.92），但分词仍存在系统性方言偏见（AAVE平均fertility高+0.07 token/词），且偏见等级与各少数群体收入差距相吻合；2）字符级分词不能消除准确率差距和输入/输出熵差距，逻辑回归仍能从隐藏状态以>86%准确率区分方言；3）预训练阶段AAVE-SAE对的梯度散度大于无关SAE-SAE对（z=-2.64），AAVE输入平均损失高出0.56 nats；4）奖励模型呈现不稳定、上下文相关的方言偏好——token级别方言词获更高奖励，但完整推理上下文中存在任务依赖的方言惩罚（效果量小但显著）；5）方言变换的隐藏状态特征与字符噪声相似，但下游准确率下降（3.09pp）远超噪声变换（0.04-1.23pp）。结论：方言偏见在管道每个阶段被编码并累积。

Conclusion: 方言税并非源于单一管道环节，而是在分词、预训练、后训练和推理的所有阶段被编码和累积。仅针对某环节的干预（如改进分词）无法消除底层偏见。消解方言税需要全面考虑整个建模流程的新训练策略。提出的干预措施应跨整个管道进行评估，避免"盲人摸象"式的局部修复。

Abstract: Systematic dialectal performance gaps in language models (LMs) are well documented, but the source of these disparities within the modern language modeling pipeline remains unclear. Our study traces this "dialect tax" across the natural language processing pipeline. Using parallel English dialect corpora that hold meaning fixed while varying surface form, we first confirm that LMs recognize matched Standard American English (SAE) and dialectal texts as semantically equivalent. However, we discover further representational gaps corresponding to downstream performance gaps. Across model families and generations, modern LMs still encode dialectal texts unequally during tokenization, pre-training, post-training, and inference. Strikingly, bypassing traditional subword segmentation via a character-level counterfactual tokenizer removes neither input and output asymmetries nor dialectal accuracy gaps. During pre-training, dialect pairs induce more divergent gradient updates than pairs of entirely unrelated SAE documents, indicating that models find semantically equivalent dialectal content harder to learn from than unrelated SAE documents. During post-training, reward models show contextual, unstable dialect preferences, assigning higher values to isolated AAVE-exclusive tokens than to SAE-exclusive tokens, while full reasoning contexts receive task- and model-dependent dialect penalties. Overall, our findings suggest that the dialect tax is encoded and accumulated not by any one step in isolation, but at every step of the language modeling process.

</details>


### [4] [Unsupervised Post-Training of Foundation Models: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24982)
*Yijie Xu,Qianyi Cai,Huizai Yao,Yili Wang,Tianfu Wang,Cehao Yang,Xingbo Yao,Zhiyu Guo,Aiwei Liu,Xuming Hu,Weiyu Guo,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于"无监督后训练"(Unsupervised Post-Training, UPT)的综述研究。UPT指在无标注数据上、利用模型自身产生的信号(而非外部监督)来更新基础模型参数的方法。作者系统梳理了80种严格UPT方法,按更新信号来源分为四大家族:预测统计量优化、样本关系监督、自生成目标引导和内部评估器引导。同时提出了"输入可见性×更新持久性"的正交框架来分析部署时机,深入探讨了各类方法中递归误差传播的问题及对应的安全防护策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基础模型后训练依赖人工标注、偏好数据、强力教师或可执行验证器,但在缺乏外部监督(无标注、无验证器、无教师)的场景下(如新领域语料、开放生成任务),如何仅利用模型自身信号实现有效后训练仍缺乏系统性的方法分类与理论指导。本文旨在填补这一空白,建立UPT的形式化定义、分类体系和评估框架。

Method: (1) 通过种子搜索与雪球检索在ACL Anthology、arXiv等数据库中收集2023年1月至2026年5月间的方法,共得到91篇论文中的94条方法记录;(2) 制定四项严格的边界检查规则(B1:显式更新参数/适配器/记忆/状态;B2:信号仅来自无标注输入和同源模型样本;B3:无外部监督;B4:评估器须源自同模型谱系)以筛选出80种严格UPT方法;(3) 按"内部更新对象"将方法分为四大家族(预测统计量、样本关系、自生成目标、内部评估器);(4) 提出正交的"输入可见性×更新持久性"二维框架,将部署分为离线语料、全群体转导、少样本目标、流式持续、测试时实例、序列内等六种时机;(5) 对代表性方法进行跨家族综合分析,总结各类信号的适用条件、误差传播机制与防护策略。

Result: (1) 建立了包含80种严格UPT方法的完整清单,覆盖文本和多模态基础模型;(2) 揭示了四大家族共同的误差链条:不完美代理选择/奖励输出→更新使模型集中于这些输出→下一轮读取更具偏见的代理;(3) Family I代表性结果:LangAdapt CPT将巴斯克语困惑度从23.64降至3.35;Family II:TTRL将AIME 2024从12.9提升至40.2,MATH-500从46.7提升至83.4;Family III:Quiet-STaR将GSM8K从5.9提升至10.9;Family IV:CoNL将AIME 2024从60.0提升至76.5;(4) 在跨模态扩展方面,TTRV使InternVL3-8B在ImageNet上达到99.31%(超过GPT-4o的98.30%);(5) 通过方法对比表阐明了"同一可观测信号、不同梯度路径"的家族归属判定规则(如多数投票作为奖励归Family II,作为伪标签筛选归Family III)。

Conclusion: 本文系统化地提出了UPT的四家族分类法和输入可见性×更新持久性的正交部署框架,揭示了无监督后训练中"信号语义灵活性与反馈路径长度"的根本权衡——语义表达力越强越能处理开放性输出,但反馈路径越长越易递归放大代理错误。提出应按任务结构和部署时机选择UPT方法,并在代理信号进入更新的关键节点设置防护措施(校准监控、样本多样性、目标刷新、评估器去相关等),以中断误差累积链。最终强调独立保留评估、回滚检查点和红队测试的必要性。

Abstract: Foundation-model post-training usually relies on human labels, preference data, stronger teachers, or executable verifiers. We study Unsupervised Post-Training (UPT): update-bearing adaptation on unlabeled inputs whose learning signal is derived from same-lineage model artifacts rather than an external oracle. We catalog 80 strict UPT methods and organize them by the object that supplies the update signal: a prediction statistic, a sample relation, a self-generated target, or an internal evaluator. Beyond inventory, we show how the choice of internal signal and task structure determines whether post-training improves the model or recursively amplifies error. An orthogonal Input Visibility $\times$ Update Persistence view maps deployment regimes and defines a unified framework for UPT selection and evaluation.

</details>


### [5] [Does Fine-Tuning Undo Activation Steering? Behavioural Recovery Without Weight-Edit Reversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24988)
*Philipp E. Glass,Allan Tucker,Yongmin Li,Alina Miron*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究嵌入到语言模型权重中的激活引导（activation steering）在常规下游微调（SFT/RLHF）下的稳定性。核心发现是：权重编辑在机制上几乎保持完整（向量恢复率 ρ=0.004，平均余弦相似度 cosθ=0.074），但行为可能因训练数据内容带来的优化压力而退化（如SFT下拒绝抑制平均丧失64%效果），行为恢复并非通过撤销编辑实现，而是通过替代通路绕过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式的激活引导可作为模型发布前的对齐工具，但下游用户常进行微调，尚不清楚该干预在常规（非对抗性）微调后是否依然有效；若行为退化，是编辑被撤销，还是模型绕过了编辑机制？

Method: 在5个开源指令微调模型（3B-14B）上嵌入两类引导（拒绝抑制的负向投影、简洁性放大的正向放大），分别施加SFT（OpenOrca）和RLHF（hh-rlhf），测量行为保留率 P 和权重空间的向量恢复率 ρ、撤销分数 rev、更新方向与预编辑权重的余弦相似度 cosθ。

Result: 行为上：RLHF几乎完全保留两类引导（平均 P_rlhf≈1.0），SFT下拒绝抑制显著退化（平均 P_sft=0.36，丧失64%效果），简洁性部分保留（P_sft=0.73）——退化跟踪训练数据中的对抗性信号。机制上：所有条件下 ρ≤0.018（均值0.004），撤销≤0.79%（均值0.08%），微调更新沿引导方向与预编辑权重近正交（cosθ=0.074），即行为恢复不通过反向撤销编辑。

Conclusion: 嵌入式引导在机制上持久但在功能上脆弱，应被视为持久但非永久的干预，下游训练后必须进行行为再验证；对有推理时干预能力的提供者，推理时引导优于嵌入式引导。

Abstract: Activation steering can be embedded directly into a language model's weights, shaping behaviour without inference-time intervention and offering a way to encode alignment prior to release. However, models are routinely fine-tuned after deployment, and it is unknown whether embedded interventions survive this. We study the stability of embedded steering for refusal suppression and brevity induction across five instruction-tuned models (3B-14B) under non-adversarial SFT and RLHF. Behaviourally, preservation tracks the training data: steering degrades when optimisation pressure contradicts the targeted behaviour and persists otherwise, with refusal ablation losing 64% of its effect on average under SFT. Mechanistically, however, the weight edit survives almost untouched even where behaviour reverts: mean vector recovery is $\rho = 0.004$, and the fine-tuning update along the steering direction is near-orthogonal to its pre-edit weight pattern (mean $\cos\theta = 0.074$). When steered behaviour degrades, fine-tuning does not achieve it by dismantling or reversing the steering mechanism itself. Embedded steering is therefore mechanistically durable but functionally vulnerable, and requires behavioural re-validation after downstream training.

</details>

*Casey Kennington*

TL;DR: 本文综述了语言意义（语义学）的研究方法，介绍了三种主要语义理论：形式语义学、具身语义学（grounded semantics）和分布式语义学，并比较了Transformer语言模型与人类语言学习的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着ChatGPT、Gemini等基于Transformer的语言模型被广泛使用，理解这些模型如何学习和表示语言意义变得至关重要，同时也需要更深入地理解"语言"本身的本质。

Method: 本文采用跨学科综述方法，结合科学和哲学视角来探讨语义学；系统介绍形式语义学、具身语义学和分布式语义学三种核心理论；并通过对比分析揭示Transformer语言模型与人类语言学习机制的异同。

Result: 论文阐述了三种语义理论的核心观点：1）形式语义学关注语言与逻辑的映射；2）具身语义学强调语言意义与感官经验的关联；3）分布式语义学认为意义源于词汇的上下文分布。同时指出Transformer语言模型通过大规模数据中的统计模式学习语言表征，这与人类基于具身经验和社会互动的学习方式存在根本差异。

Conclusion: 理解不同语义理论有助于深入认识语言本质，并批判性地评估基于Transformer的语言模型的能力与局限——尽管这些模型在语言任务上表现出色，但其学习机制与人类真正的语言理解存在本质区别。

Abstract: As people adopt transformer-based language models (e.g., ChatGPT and Gemini) for an increasing number of use-cases, it is important to know how such models learn and represent the meaning of the language, and to be more informed about what language is. This document is an attempt to help the reader understand how linguistic meaning (i.e., semantics) is approached from different fields of scientific and philosophical examination. I also explain three primary semantic theories: formal semantics, grounded semantics, and distributional semantics then compare how transformer-based language models differ from how humans learn language.

</details>


### [7] [HealthBench-Psych: A Mental Health Subset of OpenAI's HealthBench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25071)
*Matthew Flathers,Phuong Anh Nguyen,Jill Noorily,Julian Herpertz,Meiting Chen,Jasreen Multani,Samuel Powell,Mason Granof,Mark Kalinch,John Torous*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了HealthBench-Psych，这是OpenAI HealthBench（5000条医生评分的医疗对话基准）中专门针对心理健康领域的子集。通过LLM筛选+两轮盲法临床医生审查，最终获得610条对话（12.2%），并使用跨厂商的三个LLM裁判评估了20个前沿与开源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用健康基准（如HealthBench）虽被广泛用于评估LLM医疗能力，但缺乏临床专科细分，导致难以单独评估心理健康领域的性能。心理健康具有重大公共卫生意义，而现有评估多为定制化学术基准，难以集成到开发者工作流中。

Method: 1) 使用Claude Opus 4.8配合透明筛查rubric对5000条对话进行筛选（relevant/borderline/not_relevant）；2) 三位持证临床医生（R1-R3）盲法审查relevant+borderline对话+150个隐蔽对照，通过多数决（≥2/3）纳入；若对照纳入率>5%则触发召回轮；3) 使用Claude Sonnet 4.6对未审查的4209条排除对话进行召回筛选，再次临床审查；4) 20个候选模型在temperature 0下生成响应；5) 三个跨厂商裁判（GPT-4.1、Claude Haiku 4.5、Gemini 2.5 Flash）在temperature 0下评分；6) 使用对话级bootstrap（1000次）估计不确定性。

Result: 1) 筛选产出relevant 378条（7.6%）、borderline 263条（5.3%），首轮临床审查纳入587+14条共601条（首轮控制入率6.0%触发召回），召回轮再纳入14条+对照1/25（4.0%<5%停止），最终610条；2) 验证流水线忠实于参考实现（GPT-4.1在HealthBench-Hard上得分0.157 vs 公开0.16）；3) 前沿聚类（kimi-k2.6 0.627、gpt-5.5 0.624、claude-opus-5 0.620、grok-4.5 0.612、gpt-5.6-sol 0.610）在统计上无法区分（Holm-Bonferroni校正后所有配对差异均不显著）；4) 三个裁判排名几乎一致（Kendall τ=0.926-0.947），但severity差异显著（Gemini偏宽松+0.077，Haiku严格-0.045）；5) 两个模型出现可测量的拒绝行为：claude-opus-5拒绝10/610（1.6%，集中于精神科用药和临床医生口吻的患者护理请求），claude-fable-5拒绝3/610（0.5%，均与神经退行性变相关）；6) 三家领先厂商的最新版本均低于前代（claude-fable-5 -0.029、kimi-k3 -0.059、gpt-5.6-sol -0.014）。

Conclusion: HealthBench-Psych将最广泛使用的健康基准转化为心理健康专科评估工具。其构建流程可复现，评估显示前沿模型在心理健康对话上目前无法区分，但拒绝行为可测量差异。已发布子集、模型响应、评分和分析代码。由于基于内容哈希的对话标识符列表，开发者可零边际成本在已有HealthBench运行结果上回溯生成HealthBench-Psych分数。该流水线可推广至HealthBench其他专科及其他基准。同时指出心理健康对话质量并不自动随通用前沿进展同步提升。

Abstract: General-purpose health benchmarks increasingly anchor claims about LLM medical performance, but they are not always resolved by clinical specialty, making domain-specific performance hard to isolate. Mental health is of acute public-health concern as millions of people turn to LLMs for psychological support, and most existing evaluations are bespoke academic benchmarks that are difficult to integrate into developer workflows. We introduce HealthBench-Psych and HealthBench-Psych-Hard. We screened HealthBench's 5,000 physician-rubric conversations for mental-health relevance with a transparent LLM-applied rubric, then validated the subset through two rounds of blinded clinician review with concealed known-exclude controls, yielding 610 conversations (12.2% of the corpus). Evaluating 20 frontier and open models under a cross-vendor panel of three LLM judges, we find a statistically tied frontier cluster, measurable refusal behavior in two models, and near-identical rankings across judges ($\tau \ge 0.92$). We release the subset, pipeline, model responses, grades, and analysis code as a reusable resource.

</details>


### [8] [SelfGraphRAG: Bridging the Supervision Gap in Graph-Based RAG with Synthetic QA Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25123)
*Ben Lagnese,Manas Gaur*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SelfGraphRAG 是一个通过从知识图谱结构本身自动合成 QA 训练数据，解决图检索模型在无标注私有语料上无法进行有监督训练的瓶颈的 RAG 框架。它由 Doc2Graph（沿用 GraphRAG 抽取 KG）、SynthGen（沿两跳路径和节点邻域生成两类合成 QA）和 GraphLM（用合成数据训练 G-Retriever 图检索器）三阶段组成，在 MoreHopQA、MultiHop-RAG 和 PubMedQA 上全面优于基于嵌入的 RAG、GraphRAG 与 LightRAG 基线，表明知识图谱结构可作为自包含的监督源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图检索模型（如 G-Retriever）能捕捉多跳关系，但在从私有文档自动构建的知识图谱上缺乏标注 QA 数据，因而退化为依赖嵌入相似度的图 RAG，无法处理语义距离远的多跳查询。

Method: 三阶段流程：(1) Doc2Graph 复用 GraphRAG 的实体/关系抽取与摘要得到 KG；(2) SynthGen 枚举所有两跳路径生成多跳 QA 对，并对每个有至少三个入邻居的节点取三条入边生成节点摘要 QA 对，从而构造训练集 T；(3) GraphLM 用 G-Retriever 作为可训练子图打分器，冻结 Llama2-7b 作生成器，以合成 QA 上的端到端交叉熵损失训练并部署。

Result: 在 MoreHopQA 上 F1 从 LightRAG 的 9.23 提升到 12.59；MultiHop-RAG 上 F1 从 RAG 的 2.60/LightRAG 的 0.98 提升到 24.62（精度 23.65 vs. 0.50）；PubMedQA 整体准确率 55.2% 优于 RAG 的 51.6% 与 LightRAG 的 25.6%，并在 No/Maybe 类别上显著占优，定性分析显示其能保留多跳证据链。

Conclusion: 知识图谱的结构信息本身即可作为监督信号训练图检索器，无需人工标注；合成数据驱动的有监督图检索在多跳推理和领域分类任务上均优于基于嵌入的图 RAG 方法，验证了“自监督结构检索”作为可扩展方案的可行性。未来工作包括改进跨块实体解析、设计语义对齐的评测指标、扩展至更大规模多跳基准及检索-生成的紧耦合。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves large language models by incorporating external knowledge without retraining, but existing methods often underuse the relational structure encoded in knowledge graphs. Graph-based RAG can capture entity relationships, yet supervised graph retrieval typically requires labeled question-answer data that may not be available for newly constructed graphs. We address this limitation with SelfGraphRAG, a framework that generates question-answer pairs directly from knowledge graph structure and uses them to train a query-conditioned graph retriever. The generated questions capture multi-hop paths and local neighborhoods, providing relational supervision without manual annotation. Experiments on multi-hop question answering and classification benchmarks show that SelfGraphRAG improves retrieval precision and downstream reasoning performance over embedding-based baselines. These results suggest that knowledge graph structure can provide useful supervision for training graph retrievers when labeled data are unavailable.

</details>


### [9] [Belief Cascades Drive Persuasion in LLM Agent Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25152)
*Haoyi Qiu,Genglin Liu,Pranav Narayanan Venkit,Kung-Hsiang Huang,Saadia Gabriel,Chien-Sheng Wu,Nanyun Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个受控多智能体仿真测试平台，研究有目标导向的LLM说服者如何通过真实社交网络拓扑影响其他LLM代理的态度。通过4400次实验运行（4个LLM、5个图、55个政策声明、两种设置），发现说服动态取决于拓扑、竞争、主题和模型先验的交互作用，且仅分析消息文本会遗漏重要的信念变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统日益依赖智能体间说服能力（辩论、研究协调、用户模拟），但该能力作为网络化信念动态问题尚未得到充分测量。现有研究未能区分直接说服与同伴中介影响，也未跟踪轮次间的信念轨迹。

Method: 构建受控仿真测试平台：(1) 使用SNAP Twitter ego-network的有向图拓扑（5个图，18-42节点）；(2) 基于Personalized PageRank从图位置推导PE初始信念，无需手工编写人格；(3) 设置singlePR（单说服者）和dualPR（竞争说服者）两种场景；(4) 每轮包含PR行动、PE行动（基于冻结快照）、信念探测（7点MCQ token概率探针）；(5) 采用9种社交动作（发帖、评论、转发、引用、点赞等）；(6) 通过Cialdini六原则分类器分析PR策略，使用回归分析分解直接vs同伴中介影响通道。

Result: 三个核心发现：(1) 网络密度效应在单向vs竞争说服下反转——密集图削弱单源扩散但在竞争设置下增强PR1指向运动；(2) dualPR下直接暴露对信念变化的影响在所有4个模型上稳定显著（PR1为正，PR2为负），同伴中介影响虽小但可测量；(3) 文本分析严重低估信念变化——PE很少明确表达探测到的立场转变（仅0.18%反转、12.4%软化），94%的PE消息虽镜像PR原则但缺乏显式立场变化语言。计划策略仅72.7%在执行文本中实现。

Conclusion: 多智能体系统的说服应作为轨迹级和暴露级过程进行评估，使用信念探针、暴露来源追踪和行动日志来识别谁影响了谁以及可见语言是否反映底层信念运动。MAS安全对象应是信念传播而非消息传递，需监控二级说服路径、源级影响中心性和信念波动率。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems increasingly debate answers, coordinate research, simulate users, and mediate information flows, making agent-to-agent persuasion a basic but undermeasured capability. We introduce a controlled testbed for studying how goal-directed persuaders shift elicited stances in networks of LLM agents grounded in real-world ego-network topologies. Across four LLM backbones, five graphs, and 55 policy statements, we find that persuasion dynamics depend on the interaction between topology, competition, topic, and model prior. Additionally, we show that direct exposure reliably predicts next-round stance change in competing runs, and peer relays carry smaller but measurable influence, showing that agents not assigned to persuade can still transmit persuasive force. Finally, analyzing post text alone misses important movement: planned strategies are only partly realized in executed messages, action choices can diverge from message content, and persuadees rarely state the stance shifts detected by probes. These results argue for evaluating multi-agent persuasion as a trajectory- and exposure-level process, using belief probes, exposure provenance, and action logs to identify who influenced whom and whether visible language reflects underlying stance movement.

</details>


### [10] [Routed Graph Handoff: Adaptive Format Selection for Multi-Agent LLM Delegation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25277)
*Pratyay Banerjee,Ankit Chadha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Routed Graph Handoff,通过轻量级LLM路由器(155 tokens,0.15%开销)在多智能体LLM系统的每次委派中选择类型化依赖图或自然语言,以解决结构化图带来的"结构-灵活性"权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统中76%的失败源于智能体间对齐错误(执行者误解顺序约束、遗漏前置条件或因指令歧义陷入重试循环);然而,统一使用结构化图虽能解决对齐问题,却会使需要自适应推理的任务表现下降14.6个百分点。

Method: 设计包含8种节点类型和7种边关系的类型化有向无环图模式,采用约束解码实现2-3倍压缩;引入轻量级路由器(155 tokens,温度=0,默认NL)按任务计算模式选择图或NL;同时为接收方配备图感知执行器提示以正确解释依赖结构。

Result: 在4个基准(1,050+轨迹)上,路由系统在依赖链任务上实现Pareto改进:τ-retail提升+12.7 pp(3.2倍压缩,p<0.01),BrowseComp提升+8.7 pp(2.2倍压缩,p<0.05),BFCL和AppWorld上与NL持平;AppWorld上图-only回归-14.6 pp,路由器以近零成本消除该回归;在GPT-5 mini上的二次验证证实了可移植性。

Conclusion: 模式感知通信始终优于模式无关压缩,关键是保留任务关键结构而非最小化token数;路由机制以极低开销实现了质量与成本的Pareto改进;预言分析揭示8.6 pp的额外提升空间,需通过执行时自适应路由信号实现,推动中轨迹格式切换作为未来工作。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems coordinate through natural-language messages that consume 40--60\% of their token budget. Replacing these with structured graphs reduces cost but fails on tasks requiring adaptive reasoning. We propose \textbf{Routed Graph Handoff}, where a lightweight LLM router (155 tokens, 0.15\% overhead) selects between a typed dependency graph and natural language for each delegation. On four benchmarks (1,050+ trajectories), the routed system matches or exceeds NL-only on every task: \textbf{+12.7\,pp} on $\tau$-retail at 3.2$\times$ compression ($p{<}0.01$), \textbf{+8.7\,pp} on BrowseComp at 2.2$\times$ compression ($p{<}0.05$), and parity on BFCL and AppWorld. Without the router, graph-only delegation regresses 14.6\,pp on AppWorld; the router eliminates this at near-zero cost. A graph-aware executor prompt is required: the same schema without interpretation guidance yields no gain. An oracle analysis reveals 8.6\,pp of additional headroom, motivating execution-time adaptive routing as future work.

</details>


### [11] [Adaptive Triggering for Bias Correction in LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25379)
*Nayoung Kim,Mickey Mancenido,Huan Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"自适应触发"框架，将LLM推理过程中的偏见修正建模为在线变点检测问题。通过每步偏见风险信号驱动CUSUM统计量，仅在累积证据超过校准阈值时注入定向干预，支持白盒（基于token概率）和黑盒（基于LLM评判器）两种信号模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 思维链（CoT）提示会在LLM中间推理步骤中暴露并放大刻板印象偏见，而仅对最终答案去偏无法解决此问题。推理时的偏见缓解面临核心时机困境：干预过晚则偏见推理已传播，过早则可能破坏正确推理。现有方法要么事后评估完整推理链，要么按预定步骤干预，未解决"推理轨迹发展到何时才提供足够证据值得修正"的决策问题。

Method: 方法框架包含三个核心组件：(1)偏见风险信号bt∈[0,1]，白盒信号基于强制续写"Final answer: <letter>"时各选项的下一个token概率差(b_t = max(0, p_t(s) - p_t(u)))，衡量模型对刻板印象一致性答案相对于"信息不足"选项的偏好；黑盒信号由LLM评判器对最近推理步骤的刻板印象依赖程度评分(0-10)归一化获得。(2)CUSUM变点检测：S_t = max(0, S_{t-1} + b_t - k)，当S_t > h时触发干预并设置一步冷却期。(3)逐检测器校准算法(Algorithm 1)：在保持集C和验证集H上搜索(k,h)网格，优化目标为acc(C_dis) - bias_rate(C_amb) - λ·noncompletion(C)，通过新鲜生成轨迹避免离线重放的偏差。实验条件包括none、fixed_interval（每N步通用反思）、adaptive_wb（白盒信号触发定向干预）、adaptive_bb（黑盒信号触发定向干预）。

Result: 在gpt-4o-mini上，黑盒自适应触发在消除性上下文上恢复了固定间隔干预丢失的大部分准确率(90.1% vs 82.9%)，同时干预频率显著降低(0.60 vs 1.00次/项)，且独立评判器复制了主要结果(+6.8pp, p=0.0015)。在6个开源权重模型上，白盒信号在所有6个模型上改善了模糊项准确率，但在5个模型上降低了消除性项准确率，原因是该信号无法区分无依据的刻板印象依赖与正确且符合刻板印象的证据。匹配预算的节奏扫描表明自适应选择并非仅以更低频率触发的固定调度。此外，识别出推理预算有界时干预导致的非完成成本(DS-Llama-8B上8.5% vs 4.5%)，并修正了4个未记录的BBQ标签匹配不一致问题(影响17-50%受影响类别的项目)。

Conclusion: 有效的推理时偏见修正不仅取决于何时干预，还关键取决于触发干预所依据的证据。基于证据的时序选择可减少不必要干预，但即使校准良好的触发器也无法修正无法区分无依据刻板印象依赖与有效证据推理的信号。自适应触发将"监控什么证据"与"累积多少证据才值得干预"两个问题解耦，但跨6个模型的证据清晰表明：监控信号的选择决定了干预质量，时机决策无法补偿信号目标错位。框架同时支持白盒和黑盒部署，且不修改生成器模型。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought prompting can expose and amplify demographic stereotypes within an LLM's intermediate reasoning and create a failure mode that final-answer debiasing alone cannot address. Mitigating such bias during generation presents a fundamental timing problem: intervening too late allows biased reasoning to propagate, while unnecessarily intervening can disrupt otherwise correct reasoning. Existing approaches largely avoid this decision by either evaluating completed reasoning chains post hoc or intervening at predetermined steps, leaving open when a developing reasoning trajectory provides sufficient evidence to warrant correction. We formulate this decision as an online change-point detection problem. A per-step bias signal updates a CUSUM statistic and a targeted correction is injected only when accumulated evidence crosses a detector-specific threshold calibrated on held-out data. We instantiate the framework with a white-box signal derived from next-token probabilities and a black-box signal obtained from an LLM judge, enabling deployment with both open-weight and hosted models. On gpt-4o-mini adaptive black-box triggering recovers most of the disambiguated-context accuracy lost under fixed-interval intervention while requiring substantially fewer interventions. That result holds even with an independent judge. Across six open-weight models, the white-box signal improves ambiguous-item accuracy on all six but reduces disambiguated-item accuracy on five because it cannot distinguish unsupported stereotype reliance from correct, stereotype-congruent evidence.

</details>


### [12] [DCGC: Draft-Conditioned Global Correction for Complex Reasoning with Masked Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25428)
*Minhae Oh,Nakyung Lee,Jungwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DCGC提出了一种基于掩码扩散模型（MDM）的草稿条件全局修正框架，利用上游求解器的不完美解作为辅助上下文，通过任务特定SFT和Dynamic Dual-CFG推理机制，在数学、代码和知识推理基准上实现有效的工具无关推理修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的自回归生成在多步推理中会传播早期错误，且ARMs通常过度自信，使无工具的自修正变得脆弱。MDM通过迭代去噪可重新访问输出序列，天然适合作为全局修正模块，但此前主要用于从头生成而非修正长推理轨迹。

Method: 框架包含两部分：(1) 混合格式监督微调（SFT），训练数据混合问题-解答对(Q,G)和草稿条件修正三元组(Q,W,G)，使模型同时具备问题求解和草稿条件生成能力；(2) Dynamic Dual-CFG推理机制，在每个去噪步评估三个条件分支（无条件∅、问题条件Q、联合条件(Q,W)），最终引导logits由问题锚定项和相对草稿残差项组合而成；草稿残差项的缩放因子S₂基于联合分支与问题分支的相对置信度差距，通过ReLU门控实现选择性草稿复用。

Result: 在GSM8K、MATH、MBPP、HumanEval、MMLU-STEM和MMLU-Pro六个基准上，DCGC平均准确率达24.8%，显著优于自回归Self-Refine基线和标准LLaDA变体；在solver-failure hard set上GSM8K达44.9%、MATH达22.3%；在gold-agnostic设置中通过自一致性选择低共识样本进行修正，全测试集准确率持续提升；扰动实验证明模型确实在选择性利用相关草稿；并在DREAM等其他扩散backbone上验证了迁移性。

Conclusion: DCGC展示了MDM作为工具无关修正模块的潜力，通过相对置信度差距自适应调制草稿影响，实现"ARM生成+MDM全局修正"的可靠自修正推理系统范式；但受限于1028 token长度过滤，且gold-agnostic触发策略仍有改进空间。

Abstract: Correcting flawed reasoning traces remains a significant challenge for Large Language Models (LLMs), whose autoregressive generation can propagate early mistakes into subsequent reasoning. We introduce DCGC, a Masked Diffusion Model (MDM) framework for global correction that uses an imperfect solution draft from an upstream solver as auxiliary context. DCGC combines task-specific Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) with a novel inference-time mechanism called Dynamic Dual-CFG. This mechanism separates problem-only and joint problem-draft branches and scales the draft-conditioned residual using a relative confidence gap. Across math, code, and knowledge reasoning benchmarks, DCGC outperforms standard sampling and simpler CFG variants, with additional results suggesting transfer to different diffusion backbones. In test-time setting where ground-truth failure labels are unavailable, DCGC improves full test set accuracy by correcting low-consensus upstream outputs, highlighting its utility as a verifier-free global correction module for difficult reasoning instances.

</details>


### [13] [MathAdv: What Theorem Provers Know, Reason, Formalize, and Generalize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25449)
*Jiaxin Yuan,Connor Martinez Lockhart,Xiaoyu Liu,Jiaqi Wang,Chenghao Deng,Xiayimei Han,Vlasios Mastrantonis,Dmitrii Gudin,Shaopeng Zhu,Abdirisak Abdullahi Mohamed,Bilal Hamdi Aytekin,Jiewen Lang,Zezheng Song,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MathAdv 是一个面向形式化数学推理的诊断性基准测试，包含 321 道横跨 13 个本科及研究生级别数学领域的问题，其中 298 道已形式化为 Lean 4 语句。每道题最多配备三类辅助任务（直接计算题、选择题、专家构造的等价变体），用以分别评估数学知识、非形式化推理、形式化证明构造以及对问题等价改写的鲁棒性。作者系统评估了多种定理证明器和通用大模型，得到四点核心发现：形式化仍是主要瓶颈、领域间性能差异显著、自然语言提示对通用模型有帮助但可能损害证明专用模型、模型对数学等价改写缺乏鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的形式化数学基准存在三大局限：(1) 仅报告聚合证明准确率，无法区分失败源于知识缺失、推理错误还是形式化困难；(2) 数学领域覆盖范围窄，多集中于竞赛级别的代数与数论；(3) 未评估对等价问题改写的鲁棒性，难以判断模型成功是源于真正的数学推理还是对特定表述的记忆。论文旨在通过组件化诊断框架揭示这些被聚合指标掩盖的能力与失败模式。

Method: 构建包含 321 道题、覆盖 13 个数学领域（含拓扑、Fourier 分析、泛函分析等此前少有覆盖的领域）的 MathAdv 基准。对 298 道题通过 LLM 辅助、验证器在环的四阶段流水线（初始自动形式化→语法纠正→双重语义验证→最终专家审核）完成 Lean 4 形式化。其余 23 道因 Mathlib 缺乏必要定义而延期形式化。每道题最多配三个辅助任务：直接计算题（DA，测试自然语言数学求解）、多选题（MC，测试定理与策略知识）、专家手工改写的等价变体（测试鲁棒性）。评估对象涵盖 DeepSeek-Prover（含 SFT/RL/RMaxTS 变体）、InternLM2 系列、Goedel-Prover（含 SFT/DPO/V2/MA-LoT）、GPT-5.4、DeepSeek-R1/V3.2 等，并对比了有无 Lean 编译器交互反馈、是否附加自然语言提示两种条件下的表现。

Result: 评估产出四点发现：(1) 形式化是主要瓶颈——多数模型在 DA 和 MC 上的准确率显著高于 Lean 4 证明准确率（如 DeepSeek-V3.2 的 DA 准确率 66.7% 对 Lean 4 准确率仅 5.31%）；(2) 领域间性能极不均衡——Goedel-Prover-V2 整体最佳（21.88%），但所有模型在拓扑上均得 0 分，差距主要源于训练语料偏向竞赛类领域及 Mathlib 在高级领域覆盖不足；(3) 自然语言提示效果因模型而异——提升 DeepSeek-V3.2/R1 等通用模型，但显著降低 Goedel-Prover-SFT/DPO 的表现；(4) 模型对等价改写鲁棒性差——大多数模型在原始题上解决但无法解决变换版本（O✓/T✗ 数量远超 O✗/T✓），Goedel-Prover-V2 与 DeepSeek-R1 表现尤为突出，表明依赖表层表述模式而非真正的数学理解。交互式 Lean 反馈可将 GPT-5.4 从 9.06% 提升至 13.75%。

Conclusion: MathAdv 证明组件化评估能够揭示被聚合定理证明准确率掩盖的模型能力与失败模式。当前形式化系统受限于形式化能力瓶颈、领域覆盖不均以及对问题表述的敏感性，这三者共同限制了实际可用性。论文指出未来改进方向：增强证明状态控制与库检索可靠性、扩展 Mathlib 在高级领域的覆盖、设计面向鲁棒性的训练目标（奖励跨等价表述的一致性），并根据模型类型采用差异化的提示策略——通用模型受益于非形式化推理提示，而证明专用系统更适合 Lean 对齐的形式化指导。

Abstract: Formal theorem proving enables machine-verifiable evaluation of mathematical reasoning, yet existing benchmarks often emphasize aggregate proof accuracy, concentrate on a narrow range of mathematics, and provide limited evidence of robustness to equivalent reformulations. We introduce MathAdv, a diagnostic benchmark spanning 13 domains across undergraduate- and graduate-level mathematics. Alongside Lean 4 theorem proving, MathAdv provides up to three auxiliary tasks: multiple-choice questions that probe mathematical knowledge, fill-in-the-blank problems that isolate informal reasoning, and expert-crafted transformations that test robustness to problem presentation. Our evaluation of contemporary theorem provers yields four findings: formalization remains a major bottleneck; performance varies substantially across mathematical domains; natural-language guidance helps general-purpose LLMs but can hinder proof-specialized models; and mathematically equivalent reformulations expose substantial robustness limitations. Together, these results show how component-wise evaluation can reveal model capabilities and failure modes that aggregate theorem-proving accuracy obscures. The dataset and evaluation scripts are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [14] [Controllable Affective Generation via Latent Vector Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25569)
*Xixian Yong,Siyuan Chang,Yingying Zhang,Xian Wu,Xiao Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EmoVec，一种轻量级的推理时框架，通过潜向量操控实现可控的情感生成。它利用对比激活加法（CAA）提取情感方向，并通过任务去偏和主子空间移除进行净化，然后在推理时注入到最终残差流中，实现连续的情感强度控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型经过RLHF对齐后常出现情感扁平化问题，限制了其在心理健康、创意写作、共情人机交互等情感敏感场景的应用。现有方法（提示工程或监督微调）存在脆弱性、高成本及灾难性遗忘等局限，亟需一种轻量、可控且无需重训的情感生成方法。

Method: 1) 基于Social Chemistry等社会常识数据集构建配对的情感场景库（每种情感160个，共1280个），生成中性vs情感条件响应对。2) 通过对比激活加法（CAA）计算情感位移向量，经两步去偏（一阶任务中心化+PCA正交投影移除任务主导子空间）净化情感信号。3) 在每种情感的残差上做主成分聚合，得到情感向量。4) 线性探测定位最优干预层为模型最终层。5) 推理时将情感向量注入残差流，采用静态系数或可学习场景自适应适配器（轻量MLP）调节强度。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B、Llama3.1-8B、Qwen2.5-70B三种LLM上、八种基本情感（Anger、Anticipation、Disgust、Fear、Joy、Sadness、Surprise、Trust）进行实验。当α=50时，三模型情感显著性平均相对提升分别为21.07%、17.14%、20.19%。CPsyCounD心理健康咨询场景下，Llama3.1-8B和Qwen2.5-7B的情感丰富度分别提升19.51%和19.14%，逼近更大规模闭源模型。GPT-4o评分与人类评分平均Pearson相关系数0.752，验证了自动评估的可靠性。PCA可视化显示净化后的情感向量在潜在空间中形成紧凑、连续的情感流形，存在主导的效价轴。

Conclusion: EmoVec确立了情感强度连续可控的潜向量操控方法，验证了指令调优LLM中情感信息的线性可操控性。该方法在不更新模型权重的前提下显著提升情感表现力，同时较好保持语义内容、流畅性和连贯性，可作为部署LLM情感控制的有效推理时方案。局限在于仅适用于单轮文本交互的线性情感建模，未覆盖多轮对话、多模态及动态情感演化等复杂场景。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often produce emotionally flattened responses after alignment, limiting their effectiveness in affect-sensitive applications. In this paper, we propose EmoVec, a lightweight framework for controllable affective generation via latent vector steering. EmoVec extracts emotion-specific directions from paired neutral and emotion-conditioned responses using contrastive activation addition, and further refines them through task-specific debiasing and principal subspace removal. During inference, these vectors are injected into the final residual stream with static or scenario-adaptive scaling, enabling continuous control over emotional intensity without updating model weights. Experiments across three LLMs and eight emotions show that EmoVec consistently improves emotional salience while largely preserving semantic content, fluency, and coherence. Ablation studies and human evaluation further confirm the effectiveness of vector purification and adaptive scaling, establishing EmoVec as a practical inference-time method for affective control in deployed LLMs.

</details>


### [15] [Unmatched Does Not Mean False: Incomplete Reference Sets Can Reverse Calibration Rankings in Open-Ended Theory-of-Mind Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25654)
*Zhexi Feng,Wuxi Chen,Bingrui Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了开放域心智理论（ToM）追踪系统中一个根本性评估缺陷：当使用有限参考答案对开放式信念输出进行评分时，"未匹配"并不等同于"错误"，这种代理标签可以反转严格评分规则下的模型排名。作者提出了TriSource-Restore方法，通过概率抽样的人类试点修复这一校准失真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放域ToM追踪器会持续发出大量有效但可能不在有限参考答案中的信念命题。当前的评估流程将"未匹配"等同于"错误"，但这实际上将合理的置信度惩罚为错误，从而可能产生误导性的模型选择。论文旨在识别并量化这一标签源效应。

Method: 1) 内容固定识别法：保持信念内容、置信度规则固定，仅切换标签源（参考答案Z vs. 人工裁决的真实标签Y）；2) 严格正确的Brier评分分解；3) 闭式交叉判据预测排名反转；4) TriSource-Restore三源恢复方法：结合完整参考答案标签Z、冻结自动判断Q和概率抽样的人工试点Y作为锚点；5) 在DoubtfulToM基准（6个场景，259个信念）和独立OpenToM基准上进行验证。

Result: 1) 有限参考标签使权重阳性率从0.783降至0.295，在6个场景中均导致严格正确的Brier风险排名反转（EG规则在参考标签下领先0.227，但在裁决下落后0.152）；2) 发布的NQ-open DPR-BERT管道中，平均置信度基线的ICE在精确匹配下改善0.045，但在人类正确性下恶化0.074，两个区间均排除零；3) OpenToM审计显示90-96%的未匹配信念在字面意义上为真；4) TriSource-Restore以50次可用人工标注恢复排序方向（概率≥0.996），区间宽度降低达37%；5) 参考标签的Platt重新校准反而加剧错误。

Conclusion: 论文结论是：未匹配的信念不一定是错误的。基于有限参考答案的标签会反转严格正确的模型排名，这是一种可识别、可量化的协议级故障，而非特定追踪器的覆盖限制。TriSource-Restore提供了一种审计条件下的修复方法，通过概率抽样的人工试点锚定，结合自动信号提高效率，从而恢复正确的排名和校准置信度。评估应从"未匹配即错误"的隐含假设转向显式的标签源审计。

Abstract: Open-ended Theory-of-Mind (ToM) trackers emit valid beliefs absent from finite references. A finite-reference-plus-matcher pipeline marks unmatched outputs false, creating proxy labels that can reverse proper-score model selection on fixed outputs. Holding 259 beliefs and paired scores fixed, reference recoding lowers weighted prevalence from 0.783 to 0.295 and reverses strictly proper Brier risk: a frozen source-prior rule leads native confidence by 0.227 under reference labels and trails by 0.152 under blinded adjudication, in all six authored scenarios. A reference-only Platt recalibrator reverses further. An ICE-specific reversal appears in a released 301-question NQ-open DPR-BERT pipeline: its average-confidence baseline improves instance-level calibration error by 0.045 under exact match but worsens it by 0.074 under human correctness, with both intervals excluding zero. On independently authored OpenToM narratives, 90-96% of audited unmatched beliefs are literally true and the paired direction again reverses. An exact decomposition attributes the distortion to omitted truths, and a closed-form criterion correctly classifies comparisons from twelve released systems. Frozen-audit retrospective replay shows 50 attempted annotations recover ranking direction with probability at least 0.996. TriSource-Restore anchors full-frame reference labels and frozen automatic judgments to a probability-sampled human pilot, maintains at least nominal coverage, narrows intervals, and repairs confidence subject to a base-rate deployment gate.

</details>


### [16] [Reconstructing the Right Episode: Evaluating Interleaved Conversational Memory Beyond Long Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25655)
*Zhexi Feng,Ruiyi Zhang,Yongbo Yang,Pengtao Xie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SCALE-QA基准（3,000题、10领域、四选一、扁平无分割混合话题对话）和TSIM方法（基于语义漂移的在线episode分割+多视图记忆堆栈），针对"episode完整性失效"——证据可见但片段化、需重建operative episode才能作答——在128k和1M context下均显著优于Standard RAG、Hybrid-RRF Chunk RAG、RAPTOR、MemGPT、HippoRAG等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文与记忆基准通常暴露会话/话题边界或直接探测个人记忆，无法隔离一种更难的任务：扁平无分割的混合话题线程中，早期引入的局部约束在数千轮后才决定答案，而标准chunk检索只能返回局部相关但episode不完整的片段（"episode完整性失效"）。

Method: SCALE-QA用反事实局部约束（虚构组织、非标准标识符、本地策略）防止预训练泄露，确定性四选一+精确证据迹审计，10领域×300题=3,000题，长度可控运行时构造器；TSIM含三模块：M1流式语义漂移分割（cosine+说话人切换奖励+最小/最大长度约束）、M2原始/摘要/集群三视图索引（无LLM调用）、M3证据优先episode排序（融合原始+摘要+集群路由+语义扩展）。

Result: 128k下TSIM在三后端（Gemma2:9b/Gemini 2.5 Flash/GPT-4o-mini）均达最高准确率69.6/80.2/73.8%，超最强基线5.6–17.6点；GPT-4o-mini Full Context从16k的62.5%跌至128k的29.8%，TSIM仅用约1k检索token；1M诊断Gemini 2.5 Flash Full Context用1.05M token+23.87s得87.2%，TSIM用1.3k token+2.16s得96.5%；消融显示语义漂移分割+多视图堆栈单调贡献；TSIM在LongMemEval-S转移诊断中亦显著优于无边界对照。

Conclusion: 长上下文助手的瓶颈不在证据是否在窗口内，而在能否重建使证据binding的operative episode；episode作为记忆单元优于固定chunk；未来评估应从静态"针在草垛中"转向动态"哪个episode仍operative"。数据集与TSIM参考实现已开源。

Abstract: Conversations with chat assistants increasingly span many topics in a single long-running thread, challenging memory systems. Existing long-context and memory benchmarks often expose session or topic boundaries, or probe direct personal-memory questions. These settings understate a harder assistant-memory regime: a flat mixed-topic thread where the system must infer which earlier episode makes a later task decision valid. We introduce SCALE-QA, a constraint-grounded task QA benchmark for flat unsegmented threads targeting episode integrity failure. The dataset contains 3,000 audited questions across 10 domains, uses deterministic four-way multiple-choice grading, and includes a deterministic runtime builder; experiments use all 3,000 questions through 128k and a stratified 400-question diagnostic at 1M. SCALE-QA questions are ordinary task-oriented requests whose correct answer depends on causally related evidence introduced earlier in the conversation. We also propose Temporal-Semantic Interleaved Memory Reconstruction (TSIM), which segments the turn stream into coherent episodes and indexes them through a hierarchical multi-view memory stack with deterministic episode-level summary and cluster-routing views. Experiments show that SCALE-QA challenges strong RAG baselines and long-context LLMs alike; across three open-source and proprietary LLM backends, TSIM achieves the highest accuracy in every backend setting, gaining 5.6-17.6 accuracy points over the strongest corresponding baseline.

</details>


### [17] [Think-Probe-Respond: Improving Large Language Models as Judges of Research Idea Novelty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25660)
*Tim Schopf,Tobias Schreieder,Akiko Aizawa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Think-Probe-Respond (TPR) 方法，通过在推理阶段从 LLM 隐藏状态中探测潜在的新颖性判断，以缓解 LLM 作为科研想法新颖性评判者时的"中等新颖性"偏见，平均性能提升 22.30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在评估科研想法新颖性时存在关键缺陷：尽管生成的推理依据与人类专家高度相似，但其最终新颖性判断却常常与依据脱节，呈现出系统性偏向"中等新颖性"的保守倾向，限制了自动化科研评估的可靠性。

Method: 提出轻量级三阶段方法 TPR：(1) Think 阶段——让 LLM 仅基于类别文本描述进行逐步推理，不生成数值判断以避免锚定效应；(2) Probe 阶段——在推理结束时提取最后一层隐藏状态，用逻辑回归分类器预测新颖性类别；(3) Respond 阶段——将探测到的新颖性类别作为条件信号附加到生成过程中，产生与判断一致的文本解释。整个过程中 LLM 参数冻结，仅训练轻量级逻辑回归。

Result: 在 RINoBench 基准测试上，TPR 在多个开源 LLM（Qwen3、Gemma 3、Llama 3.1、GPT-OSS-20B）上平均超越最强基线方法 22.30%，显著优于 LoRA 微调（FineTune）和 CoT 等方法；TPR 实现了更均衡的新颖性类别预测，有效缓解了对类别 3 和 4 的偏向；研究还发现新颖性信号在推理阶段末尾最为集中，此时的隐藏状态编码了最丰富的新颖性信息。

Conclusion: TPR 揭示了 LLM 内部已隐含准确的新颖性信念但表达时存在偏差这一关键发现，通过轻量级探针方法有效桥接了隐性信念与显性判断之间的鸿沟，为自动化科研想法评估提供了更准确、更均衡的解决方案。

Abstract: Automated novelty judgment can accelerate scientific discovery by enabling efficient evaluation, refinement, and comparison of research ideas. While large language models are increasingly adopted for this task, we investigate a previously overlooked limitation in their judgment capabilities: despite generating reasoning rationales that closely mirror those of human experts, their final novelty judgments often diverge substantially. We demonstrate that this miscalibration stems from a systematic bias towards judging ideas as "medium novel". To mitigate this, we propose Think-Probe-Respond (TPR), a lightweight approach that probes latent novelty judgments from hidden states during the reasoning phase and uses the probed judgments to condition the final response. Across strong baselines, TPR improves novelty judgment performance by 22.30% and successfully mitigates the prevalent "medium novelty" bias.

</details>


### [18] [Learning New Facts with QLoRA: An Acquisition-Retention Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25677)
*Estelle Zheng,Sébastien Warichet,Emmanuel Helbert,Christophe Cerisara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning is often assumed to preserve pretrained capabilities because it updates only a small number of parameters. We show that this assumption depends strongly on adapter capacity. We study factual acquisition in a controlled OpenStreetMap-derived benchmark where Qwen3-4B must acquire anonymized geographic associations while retaining unrelated capabilities. Comparing full fine-tuning (FFT) with quantized low-rank adaptation (QLoRA) at ranks 8, 16, 32, and 64, we find that rank induces a clear acquisition--retention frontier. Low-rank QLoRA preserves out-of-domain (OOD) performance but acquires fewer facts, whereas higher ranks improve same-fact paraphrase generalization at an increasing cost in performance on unrelated benchmarks. FFT behaves as a conservative baseline: it retains general capabilities well, but does not reach the highest factual-acquisition regime. Distributional, weight-space, and spectral diagnostics mirror this behavioral trade-off, with higher-rank QLoRA moving farther from the pretrained model. A separate math adaptation experiment shows a weaker frontier, suggesting that the effect is most pronounced when adaptation must install new factual associations rather than reinforce skills already supported by pretraining. Code and data are available at this https URL .

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning is often assumed to preserve pretrained capabilities because it updates only a small number of parameters. We show that this assumption depends strongly on adapter capacity. We study factual acquisition in a controlled OpenStreetMap-derived benchmark where Qwen3-4B must acquire anonymized geographic associations while retaining unrelated capabilities.
