### [70] [Praxist: From Experimental Artifacts to Solution Lineages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25955)
*Jin Li,Ahmed Murtadha,Zhiyu Wang,Qiwen Chen,William Chen,Yifei Wu,Guan Wang,Andy L. Siy,Jiayi Yang,Mengsha Huang,Wenhao Li,Yixuan Liu,Shuailin Pan,Mingli Yuan,Sen Song,Yuhao Sun*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Praxist提出了一种以"证据继承"为中心的自主R&D代际系统，将可复现实验产物转化为类型化证据图（发现、前沿通道、议程），而非将每次尝试视为独立事件。在MLE-bench 75项任务上以约1/12的成本（$3,054 vs $38,370）取得60枚奖牌（80%，49金），优于Claude Code基线（55枚，73.3%，34金）；并在火箭着陆、量化交易、SLAM、托卡马克磁控制四个开放性工程案例中均超越任务原生基线，同时保留可审计的谱系记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主R&D代理系统虽能在基准上展示能力，但每次尝试近乎独立，搜索树仅记录"发生了什么"而不记录"哪个设计元素产生了改进、其证据是否经住验证、如何与其他元素重组"，导致长程研究反复重学相同教训，成本远高于持续工程实践所能承受，且成果难以追溯。

Method: Praxist采用"产物→证据"代际循环：每代从继承状态（前沿F、议程A、Gems G、谱系L）出发；通过Deep Innovation Gate（DIG）为每个peer分配设计契约，并通过Quantified Diversity（QD）将契约分散到不同机制族/干预面/意图的设计单元中；C个并行peer构建可复现产物并由外部评估器评分；结果被解释为类型化发现（正面/负面/诊断性/不确定/程序性）；PI角色组（Builder/Skeptic/Portfolio/可选External-validity）独立出备忘录，Chair合成下一代理议程（继续/停止/验证/探索）；每ρ代压缩Gems；谱系图谱记录所有研究对象及其派生/支持/挑战/更新关系。最终输出为最佳确认通道产物及其完整谱系。

Result: MLE-bench全75任务：Praxist获60枚奖牌（80.0%，49金/10银/1铜），Claude Code+Opus 4.8基线55枚（73.3%，34金/16银/5铜），按复杂度分Low 90.9% vs 81.8%、Medium 81.6% vs 76.3%、High 60.0% vs 53.3%，成本$3,054 vs $38,370（约1/12）。四个案例研究：(1)火箭着陆——确定性混合控制器在12288/12288轨迹上100%成功，Weco自主优化器同任务仅17.12%，起始工件4.03%；(2)量化交易——LSTM-PPO策略28季度walk-forward CAGR 53.07%，为等权基线22.80%的2.3倍，26/28季度为正；(3)SLAM——CovSched模块在14条NTU-VIRAL序列上将视觉通路处理时间平均削减72.4%（43.2%–81.6%），轨迹精度不减；(4)托卡马克磁控制——HybridJacobianPDV1在共同视野下总生存率1264/1500优于PCS风格基线1222，WNRMSE p95为2.86 vs 2.99，全视野精度指标未胜出但生存-精度Pareto前沿显著推进。

Conclusion: Praxist证明了长程自主R&D可同时实现更强产物与低一个数量级的成本，关键在于将证据继承而非仅分数排名作为系统级要求。其代际结构、类型化通道、Gems压缩与可审计谱系共同提供了生产研究所需的操作特征——而非基准演示所能建立的那种。谱系使结果附有可检查的形成路径，支持后续人类研究者或代理系统的复用与扩展；未来方向包括跨活动继承、面向更慢/更嘈杂评估器（如实验室科学）的扩展，以及通过谱系实现更深的人机协作。

Abstract: Autonomous R\&D agents now write, run, and improve executable artifacts under automated evaluation---but largely as laboratory instruments: shown on curated benchmarks, with gains that are hard to trace to a cause and costs well above what sustained engineering practice absorbs. The limitation is structural. Most systems treat each attempt as nearly self-contained, so logs, memories, and search trees record what happened without establishing which design element produced an improvement, whether its evidence survived validation, or how it recombines with others. Long campaigns therefore keep re-learning the same lessons. We introduce Praxist, a lineage-centered generational system that converts reproducible artifacts and evaluator outcomes into a typed evidence graph of findings, lane-structured frontiers, and agendas. Separating local artifact construction from cohort-level evidence synthesis lets later attempts inherit validated mechanisms, unresolved claims, and useful constraints, and leaves results attached to an inspectable lineage. On the standardized 75-task MLE-bench suite, the finalized official-grader results give Praxist 60 medals (80.0\%), 49 of them gold, against 55 medals (73.3\%) and 34 gold for a Claude Code baseline on Claude Opus 4.8---at a recorded model spend of US\$3,054 versus US\$38,370, roughly a twelfth of the cost. Four case studies---quantitative trading, LiDAR-inertial-visual SLAM, tokamak magnetic control, and rocket landing---carry the same process into open-ended engineering problems, improving on each task-native baseline in headline accuracy, survival, or resource cost, with the discovery path on record. Stronger artifacts at an order of magnitude less spend, each backed by an auditable lineage, are, to our knowledge, first brought together here: the operating profile production research requires, not the one a benchmark demonstration establishes.

</details>


### [71] [BVR Sim: An Open and High-Throughput Environment for Heterogeneous Air-Combat Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25419)
*Haocheng Sun,Mulai Tan*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: BVR Sim 是一个开源的 Gymnasium 风格超视距空战强化学习环境，支持异构飞机平台（F-15、F-16、F/A-18、F-22等），提供统一战术动作接口、双 Python/C++ 后端、实体导向观测，并通过 MAPPO/HAPPO 集成验证与跨机型策略迁移实验展示了其有效性和高吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超视距（BVR）空战是部分可观测、长时序决策、能量管理与有限武器的多智能体强化学习难题。现有开源模拟器（BVR Gym、LAG、B-ACE、WUKONG）在保真度、吞吐量与学习友好性之间存在权衡空间，且大多不支持单场景内真正的异构机型。BVR Sim 旨在填补这一设计空白，提供一个完整的、面向异构 BVR 战术学习的端到端 MARL 环境。

Method: 1) 系统架构：采用 JSON/JSONC 场景配置每个作战单元的机型、武器、传感器、控制器与对手；2) 多速率设计：战术决策间隔 Δt=0.4 s，JSBSim 内部子步进 ≤0.02 s；3) 统一战术动作接口 MultiDiscrete(15,15,9,2)，输出期望航向、高度、速度变化与开火决策；4) 模型特定内环控制器：将战术命令映射为副翼、升降舵、方向舵与油门命令；5) 双后端：Python 参考实现与 pybind11 加速的 C++ 内核；6) 实体导向观测：固定宽度 (N_e, 40) 的实体表，含自身、敌机与导弹槽位；7) 组合奖励与终止管理；8) 多智能体框架适配器（HARL、MARLBenchmark、UHRL）；9) 遥测与可视化（ACMI/Tacview、Web、OpenGL、DX11、UnrealCV 桥接）。

Result: 1) 吞吐量：C++ 后端在 1v1 场景达到 260.65 steps/s（RTF=104.26×），10v10 场景仍保持 55.43 steps/s，2.72×–6.55× 快于 Python 后端；2) MARL 集成：HAPPO 与 MAPPO 在 2v2 任务上经过约 2250 万环境步后均呈现持续奖励增长；3) 跨机型迁移：仅在 F-16 上训练的 PPO 策略无需重训练即可迁移至 F-15、F/A-18、F-22、F-4N，平均胜率 45.50%（带机型适配）vs 13.21%（不带适配）；在 F/A-18 上 22.00% vs 1.88%，F-22 上 80.11% vs 0.00%；4) 多机型训练（PPO-All）将平均胜率提升至 55.29%；5) 导弹制导案例：相比比例导航，多阶段复合制导在 16.21 NM 高离轴角发射下命中时间从 36 s 缩短至 28 s，末端马赫数从 1.35 提升至 1.71。

Conclusion: BVR Sim 验证了一个以异构平台建模、共享战术接口与机型特定控制器、以及高吞吐量工作流为核心的开放环境设计。其双后端在 10v10 场景仍优于实时运行，HAPPO/MAPPO 验证了与标准 CTDE 学习器的兼容性，跨机型迁移实验表明冻结的高层策略在配合对应机型内环控制器时可在多种 JSBSim 飞机上保留有效战术行为。代码以 GPLv3 协议开源（github.com/lizi-Margin/bvr_sim，标签 arxiv-v1）。这些结果验证的是环境与工作流，而非优越的学习算法或作战模型；未来工作包括多种子冻结评估、自博弈、显式协调分析、异构 5v5 学习及离线 RL/模仿学习扩展。

Abstract: Beyond-visual-range (BVR) air combat is a challenging reinforcement-learning domain characterized by partial observability, long-horizon decision making, energy management, and limited weapons. We present BVR Sim, an open-source Gymnasium-style environment designed for heterogeneous air-combat reinforcement learning. BVR Sim supports multiple JSBSim aircraft models, including the F-15, F-16, F/A-18, and F-22, with configurable weapons, sensors, controllers, and opponents. A unified tactical action interface specifies desired heading, altitude, speed, and weapon release above aircraft-specific inner-loop controllers, enabling policies to operate across heterogeneous platforms. The environment provides interchangeable Python and accelerated C++ backends, entity-oriented observations, compositional rewards, scripted opponents, replay and visualization, and adapters for multi-agent learning frameworks. At a 0.4-s decision interval, the C++ backend achieves 104 simulated seconds per wall-clock second in 1-vs-1 and remains practical through 10-vs-10 scenarios. A policy trained only on the F-16 transfers without retraining to four unseen aircraft, reaching a 45.5% mean win rate with aircraft-specific controller adaptation. MAPPO and HAPPO experiments further verify end-to-end compatibility with standard multi-agent reinforcement-learning pipelines.

</details>


### [72] [Trust-Aware Sequential Decision Making and Rollout Planning for Resilient Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25690)
*Roee M. Francos,Daniel Garces,Orhan Eren Akgün,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Stephanie Gil*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向多机器人系统的信任感知序贯决策与Rollout规划框架，用于在智能体可能被攻击者入侵并进行定位欺骗（GPS spoofing）的场景下实现弹性在线路由。研究构建了一个"监测感知-距离受限"的对抗模型，通过分层二部匹配策略在限制欺骗幅度的同时最大化对路由分配的影响力；提出了融合概率定位信任（使用真实GPS欺骗数据校准）与任务执行行为证据的信任监控器；并揭示了未被检测到的攻击者会破坏规划器与执行之间的一致性，从而导致Rollout规划失去相对于基础策略的预期成本改善。通过基于真实旧金山出租车需求和GPS欺骗数据集的大规模仿真实验，验证了信任感知对抗者移除能够恢复稳定的路由性能和Rollout相对于IA-RA基础策略的实证优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人系统的序贯决策通常假设规划信息可靠且智能体按照预期执行，但被入侵的智能体可能同时违反这两个假设，导致规划模型与物理执行之间的"规划-执行失配"（planner-execution mismatch）。在在线路由与任务分配场景中，这种失配会造成不合理的分配、重分配震荡、服务延迟和请求取消的累积，最终破坏系统稳定性。现有研究大多将信任估计与规划解耦处理，且未考虑"监测感知型"攻击者（刻意限制欺骗幅度以降低可检测性）的策略性行为。论文的核心动机在于将信任作为修改规划状态（而非仅作为检测信号）的机制，以解决规划器-执行一致性问题。

Method: 1. 问题形式化：区分"真实状态"和"感知状态"，定义平均成本稳定性判据。
2. 信任感知序贯规划框架：将每个智能体分类为合作(C)/可疑(S)/对抗(A)，从规划状态中移除被判为对抗的智能体，形成"可信活跃车队"和"可信规划状态"。
3. 监测感知距离受限欺骗模型：引入参数d_max限制欺骗范围，提出分层二部匹配策略（按合作者可达性分层，按最大化匹配数、最小化层索引、最小化总欺骗位移的字典序优化），实现影响力-可检测性权衡。
4. 双源信任证据：(a) 定位信任：基于真实GPS欺骗数据，用QDA+Beta分布校准，随位置偏差Δ连续插值，经滑动窗口聚合分类；(b) 行为信任：用Beta分布累积成功接送(w_pick)、请求过期(w_exp)和有害重分配震荡(w_churn)的证据，按后验置信度分类。
5. 信任融合：任一分支判为A则A；两分支均判C才C；其余为S。
6. 信任感知Rollout规划：以IA-RA为基策略，使用one-at-a-time蒙特卡洛有限时域前瞻，在可信规划状态上执行，剔除检测到的对抗者。
7. 实验验证：基于旧金山出租车真实需求数据和真实GPS欺骗数据集，评估检测率、路由成本（未完成请求+累计取消请求）、未完成请求数等指标，测试不同欺骗范围、攻击者比例、自适应攻击、监测配置和前瞻时域的影响。

Result: 1. 单个对抗者即可破坏在完全合作条件下本应稳定的IA-RA车队，限制欺骗半径并不能消除路由层面的破坏效果。
2. 定位和行为信任具有互补性：定位信任对较大欺骗偏差响应快但对隐蔽攻击敏感性低，行为信任独立于欺骗幅度但需要更长累积时间。融合监控器在所有评估配置中均获得了最低路由成本和最少残留活跃对抗者，且保持了低合作者误报率。
3. 无监控的Rollout在存在未检测对抗者时，因前瞻模拟与物理执行发散，可能丧失相对于IA-RA基础策略的成本改善优势。
4. 信任感知移除后，Rollout能够恢复稳定的路由性能和相对于IA-RA的实证优势，但恢复程度取决于前瞻时域长度和合作车队容量。
5. 当车队接近最小稳定容量且前瞻时域较短(H=10)时，检测前累积的取消请求仍会持续影响成本；增大合作容量（49辆车）或延长前瞻时域（H=20或30）可使信任感知Rollout在成本和未完成请求数上优于受监控的IA-RA基策略。

Conclusion: 本文得出核心结论：当规划性能依赖于执行系统的内部模型时，可靠性估计最有价值的方式是用于自适应调整规划模型和决策所基于的状态，而非仅作为辅助检测信号。具体地：(1) 规划-执行一致性是Rollout类模型规划可靠性的关键前提，未被检测的对抗者通过破坏这一前提可使Rollout失效；(2) 信任感知状态构建（剔除不可靠智能体）是恢复这种一致性的有效机制；(3) 信息级（如定位完整性）和执行级（如任务结果）证据互补，二者结合优于任一单独使用；(4) 框架的局限包括：对外部独立定位参考的依赖、行为归因机制的非唯一性、永久移除机制的保守性、有限前瞻的近似性，以及缺少对检测时延、闭环稳定性和Rollout改进的解析保证；(5) 框架的普适性在于其将可靠性评估与序贯规划耦合的接口设计，可推广至MPC、ADP等其他基于模型的决策方法。

Abstract: Sequential decision-making in multi-robot systems typically assumes that planning information is reliable and that agents execute the actions anticipated by the planner. Compromised agents can violate both assumptions, creating a mismatch between the planning model and physical execution. We study this problem in online multi-robot routing under localization spoofing. We introduce a distance-constrained spoofing model for monitor-aware adversaries, together with a tiered bipartite matching strategy that maximizes assignment influence while limiting spoofing magnitude. To mitigate such attacks, we develop a trust-aware monitor that combines probabilistic localization trust, calibrated using real GPS spoofing data, with behavioral evidence from task execution to classify agents and remove detected adversaries from subsequent planning. We further show that undetected adversaries can cause rollout to lose its expected cost-improvement behavior by violating planner-execution consistency. Trust-aware removal restores this consistency after detection, enabling stable routing and recovery of rollout's empirical advantage over the base policy. Experiments using real GPS spoofing datasets and San Francisco taxicab demand demonstrate effective detection and resilient routing across varying spoofing capabilities, adversarial fleet sizes, adaptive attacks, monitoring configurations, and rollout horizons.

</details>


### [73] [HypoForge: A Self-Improving Multi-Agent Framework for Automated Hypothesis Generation and Testing via Scientific Skill Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25770)
*Ziqing Qian,Jiaying Lei,Yifang Wang,Nan Cao*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: HypoForge 是一个面向科学发现的自改进多智能体框架，通过阶段特定的技能学习机制，分别针对假设生成（无显式监督）和假设测试（有经验反馈）两个阶段学习可复用的程序性技能，无需微调基础模型即可在 HypoBench 基准上持续提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 科学家系统多依赖静态提示或固定工作流，无法跨任务积累经验、持续改进，且通常只聚焦科学发现过程的单一阶段。此外，假设生成与假设测试两个阶段的监督信号本质不同（前者缺乏显式反馈，后者有经验性结果），但现有方法普遍采用统一的优化策略，忽略了阶段差异。因此需要一种能将发现经验蒸馏为可复用技能、并针对两阶段特点设计差异化学习机制的自改进框架。

Method: 框架将科学发现解耦为两个阶段：(1) 对抗式假设生成——借鉴 GAN 思想，由假设生成器产出候选假设集，多维判别器（评估经验基础、因果合理性、可证伪性、理论一致性、结构清晰度、操作性、科学新颖性等七个维度）给出分布级评分与对比反馈，经 LLM 蒸馏器提炼为可复用的假设生成技能 S_h；(2) 经验引导的假设测试——由实验设计智能体生成协议、执行智能体产出可运行代码，结合真值结果计算测试通过率 T_h，再由蒸馏器从完整测试轨迹 (h, e, c, r) 中提炼实验设计技能 S_e 和执行技能 S_x。技能更新仅在目标函数提升时被保留，连续 k=3 轮无改进则终止训练。

Result: 在 HypoBench 13 个任务上（DeepSeek-V4-Flash 作为骨干），HypoForge 假设生成 Q(H) 达 0.785、Hit@K 达 0.648，相比 HypoGeniC 和 HypotheSAEs 在 Hit@K 上分别提升 17.6% 和 44.0%；假设测试 T_h 达 0.659、E_h 达 0.966，优于 POPPER、ReAct 及所有技能级变体（No-Skill、AI-Generated-Skill、Human-Designed-Skill）。技能学习曲线显示假设生成 Q(H) 从 0.612 升至 0.808、Hit@K 从 0.208 升至 0.679；测试 T_h 从 0.395 升至 0.669、E_h 从 0.516 升至 1.000。消融实验证实去除判别器反馈或执行结果监督均会导致性能下降。

Conclusion: HypoForge 验证了基于经验引导的阶段特定技能学习能够有效提升科学发现能力，且无需微调基础模型；学到的技能在多样化科学任务间具有良好迁移性。该工作指明了自主科学发现的一个有前景方向：构建能持续积累经验、渐进增强科学推理能力的自进化 AI 智能体系统。未来需改进反馈可靠性（如多智能体共识、人类反馈）、扩展到理论推理与湿实验领域，并解决技能粒度与组织的自动化问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have enabled AI scientist systems to automate scientific discovery, yet existing approaches most rely on static prompting or fixed workflows and fail to accumulate experience for continual improvement. We propose HypoForge, an experience-guided multi-agent framework that learns reusable scientific skills for automated hypothesis generation and hypothesis testing. HypoForge is built on the observation that these two stages involve different supervision signals. For hypothesis generation, where explicit feedback is unavailable, HypoForge adopts an adversarial generator--discriminator mechanism to improve reasoning through comparative critique. For hypothesis testing, where empirical feedback is available, HypoForge learns testing skills from execution outcomes and ground-truth results. By matching skill learning strategies with stage-specific supervision, HypoForge enables continual improvement without fine-tuning foundation models. Experiments on hypothesis generation and testing benchmarks show that HypoForge consistently outperforms existing AI scientist frameworks and skill-level variants. Further analysis demonstrates the effectiveness of the proposed stage-specific skill learning paradigms.

</details>


### [74] [AI Agentic Selective Laser Sintering Process Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25928)
*Peter Pak,Victor Alvarado,Amir Barati Farimani*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型(LLM)的智能体系统，用于选择性激光烧结(SLS)工艺参数的迭代优化。通过在低成本开源Inova Mk1设备上测试三种材料（PA12 GF、PA11 Onyx、PA12 Blend），验证了该系统能利用历史构建数据和ASTM标准测试反馈，以少量迭代次数实现与厂商TDS相当的拉伸和弯曲力学性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SLS工艺参数（如激光能量密度、扫描速度、腔体温度、热斑重叠率等）对最终零件力学性能有显著影响，传统的手动调参过程耗时且依赖经验。不同设备硬件（如Inova Mk1的10W蓝光二极管激光器与Formlabs Fuse系列的30W光纤激光器）的差异进一步增加了参数标定难度。智能体系统通过结合LLM推理、工具调用和持续学习能力，有望实现工艺参数优化的自动化与高效化。

Method: 系统采用Claude Fable作为核心LLM，通过Model Context Protocol (MCP)集成多类工具：材料信息查询、参数测试（5×5贴片阵列）、打印配置文件管理、运行时覆写、历史构建数据检索等。系统后端使用PostgreSQL关系数据库实现动态记忆，存储历史对话和测试结果，支持持续学习。人类通过GUI确认推荐参数和LLM推理过程后再启动构建。打印设备为开源SLS4All Inova Mk1（10W 450nm蓝光二极管激光器），配备热成像仪和光学摄像头用于过程监控。力学性能测试依据ASTM D638（拉伸）和ASTM D790（弯曲）标准，使用Instron 4469万能试验机完成。

Result: 材料1 (PA12 GF)经过15批次手动调参后，最佳批次J实现拉伸模量2482 MPa；材料2 (PA11 Onyx)经4批次智能体优化（O→P→Q→R），拉伸模量从934 MPa逐步提升至1571 MPa（目标1680 MPa）；材料3 (PA12 Blend, 25% PA12 GF+75% PA12 White)经3批次智能体优化（S→T→U），最终拉伸模量1678 MPa，弯曲强度60.6 MPa，超过PA12 White和PA12 GF的TDS及参考值。结果表明智能体系统能有效利用历史数据驱动参数优化，但存在打印床温度分布不均导致的力学性能梯度问题（靠近recoater侧性能更优）。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了基于LLM的智能体系统在SLS工艺参数优化中的可行性，通过持续学习机制和少量迭代即可达到材料TDS规定的力学性能水平。智能体系统的模块化设计（通过MCP协议）使其易于适配不同的LLM和制造设备。然而，设备腔体表面温度分布不均导致的力学性能空间梯度问题仍是亟待解决的关键挑战。

Abstract: Agentic systems enable the intelligent automation of complex workflows, specific to additive manufacturing this is applicable for complex tasks such as process parameter optimization for mechanical properties. This work investigates the AI enabled agentic process optimization within Selective Laser Sintering (SLS) to iteratively improve the tensile and flexural properties of 3 different materials on the Inova Mk1. These materials include PA12 GF, PA11 Onyx, and PA12 Blend (volume mixture of 25% PA12 GF and 75% PA12 White) and with using knowledge from previous builds and minimal guidance from the user, the agentic system was able to optimize process parameters over a small number of iterations to achieve comparable TDS specified mechanical properties. This work showcases the ability for an agentic system to continually learn from updated data, enabling the intelligent automation of complex tasks such as process parameter optimization for selective laser sintering.

</details>

### [75] [Probabilistic Performance Analysis of Parallel Signature Search Strategies in Multi-Level Tree Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25087)
*Jingwei Li,Thomas G. Robertazzi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的概率框架，在任意文件被读取之前，先验地预测多级树网络中五种签名搜索并行策略的期望完成时间，覆盖签名计数已知与未知两种信息条件及三种文件容量类别，并对每条公式标注精确性等级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有签名搜索时间分析要么用签名节点的统计量来刻画所有节点（在小概率p下偏乐观），要么在多签名场景中需要运行时才能获得的信息，无法在搜索启动前给出可比较的先验完成时间预测。实际系统设计需要在调度前评估并行度、规划资源。

Method: 节点扫描时间用关于签名存在与否的混合分布建模；并行层时间用顺序统计量表示；并行子树用中心极限定理与极值理论；已知签名计数时用多元超几何分布刻画子树占用律，并通过生成函数处理容量约束。每条公式标注精确/精确-在-某-参数域/代入法/渐近/下界五类标签，并量化与蒙特卡洛仿真的误差。

Result: 给出了五种策略S1-S5在未知计数条件下的闭合或半闭合公式（T1、T3、T5精确，T2代入法，T4代入+CLT/EVT），及K签名扩展；已知计数条件下用超几何分布与两种放置约定（BE/MB）覆盖S1-S5；蒙特卡洛验证精确公式误差<0.1%，代入法误差在pn≳2时<2%、pn=0.6时达74%，S4渐近公式在n50、M≳10时<1%；多核原型在Apple M1上复现了粗粒度策略分离，但同步开销使S3与S4无法区分；设计示例10^4文件、4层、n=10、p=0.5下，分析耗时<1毫秒，S1-S5完成时间比为1170:156:35.2:4:1。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了在读取任何文件之前的先验完成时间预测，每个表达式都带有精确性标签和量化的精度范围，可直接支持后续的资源成本优化；自然的下一步是扩展到有界处理器池，以及闭合开放的能力约束最小计数分布情形。

Abstract: Hierarchical distributed search, locating a data pattern, or signature, across a tree-structured collection of files, underlies distributed index traversal, deep packet inspection and sequence alignment. A practitioner must decide how much parallelism to employ: scan each layer sequentially, fan out within subtrees, or launch the whole tree at once. Existing analyses answer this only partially: they characterize every node by the statistics of a signature-holding file and, for multi-signature files, need quantities revealed only at run time. We develop a probabilistic framework predicting the completion time of five search strategies, spanning sequential to full-tree parallelism, before any file is read. Node scan times are modeled as a mixture over signature presence, layer times as order statistics, and parallel subtree scans by extreme-value arguments; when signature counts are known, occupancy under capacity constraints is treated by generating functions. Each performance formula carries an exactness label: exact (or exact-in-regime), plug-in, asymptotic or bound, with each approximation quantified against Monte Carlo simulation and its regime identified. A multicore prototype reproduces the coarse separation between full-tree, layer- and subtree-level parallelism, but shows that synchronization overhead can erase the predicted separation between close strategies. The framework delivers a priori completion-time predictions with explicit accuracy regimes and negligible computational cost, the design example evaluated in under a millisecond; these timing models can support subsequent resource-cost optimization.

</details>


### [76] [Hierarchical Shared Memory-Aware Optimization for TRSM on GPU Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25469)
*Xinzhe Chen,Haowei Li,Lijuan Hu,Wenjing Ma,Fangfang Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出HSMA-TRSM，一个针对左下三角TRSM的层次化共享内存感知优化框架，跨平台（NVIDIA A100、H800、海光DCU Z100）优化小规模和大规模两种场景，针对双复数共享内存受限问题设计七级流水线，对角块解耦将共享内存从O(IB²)降至O(IB)并配合自适应分块，相比cuBLAS/rocBLAS峰值加速比达2.05×/2.06×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TRSM作为BLAS Level-3基础算子，广泛用于LU/Cholesky分解、稀疏直接求解器、矩阵求逆等场景，其GPU实现面临三大挑战：小规模(m,n≤64)下前向替换的行依赖与共享内存/LDS容量受限（尤其是双复数类型，64×64矩阵即占满64KB）；大规模下对角块求逆占用O(IB²)共享内存限制了可用分块大小，导致GEMM更新粒度受限；以及跨平台（A100、H800、DCU Z100）性能可移植性差、vendor库固定分块策略无法适应矩阵规模与硬件差异。

Method: 小规模采用循环展开与指令重排序实现计算-访存重叠流水线；双复数特殊场景下设计双线程组七级流水线，将64KB共享内存划分为4×16KB子区域分别存放A的2×2子块与B的4×1子块。规模分类阈值m=n=64由经验性能交叉点确定。大规模提出对角块解耦优化，将对角块求逆共享内存复杂度从O(IB²)降至O(IB)（列级加载+双缓冲机制），从而支持更大内层块大小，并基于离线profiling+在线查表构建自适应分块选择表（按矩阵规模×数据类型→最优块大小映射），运行时零开销查找最优块大小。实验平台包括NVIDIA A100-SXM4-40GB（108 SM，48KB/block，1.6TB/s）、H800 PCIe（132 SM，48KB/block，2.0TB/s）和海光DCU Z100（64 CU，64KB/block LDS，1TB/s），对比cuBLAS v13.2.1和rocBLAS v5.1以及MAGMA，测试四种数据类型（float/double/float complex/double complex）在m=n=1024/4096/8192/16384等规模下的GFLOPS。

Result: 小规模：在m=64处float达到4.64 GFLOPS(DCU)和5.75 GFLOPS(A100)，分别优于rocBLAS 18.4%和cuBLAS 29.2%；双复数m=64时HSMA-TRSM达到3.53/15.88/12.47 GFLOPS（DCU/A100/H800），相比vendor库分别提升96.1%、14.5%、105.4%；峰值加速比2.05×（H800双复数）vs cuBLAS。大规模：DCU上float/double峰值6010/3654 GFLOPS，m=16384加速比1.63×/2.06×；A100上float/double/float complex/double complex峰值17802/16564/18205/17111 GFLOPS；H800上float/float complex峰值36088/38654 GFLOPS；对角块求逆耗时占比从20.7%(m=2048)降至3.7%(m=8192)，GEMM更新占比从54.8%升至88.0%，验证了大问题规模下GEMM主导特性；数值精度方面单精度误差~1e-7、双精度误差1e-16至1e-15，与cuBLAS一致。

Conclusion: HSMA-TRSM通过层次化共享内存感知优化，在三种GPU平台上均能超越vendor库，峰值加速比达2.05×(cuBLAS)和2.06×(rocBLAS)；收益最显著的领域是共享内存受限的双复数小规模场景以及实数类型大规模场景（自适应分块改善GEMM主导的更新阶段），而对成熟vendor kernel优化充分的某些场景提升空间有限；对角块解耦+O(IB)共享内存复杂度是支持更大分块的关键，跨平台性能差异源于硬件吞吐量和每block片上存储预算的差异；未来工作方向包括极小规模的kernel融合、批处理TRSM以及稀疏和混合精度扩展。

Abstract: Triangular Solve with Multiple Right-hand Sides (TRSM) is a fundamental BLAS Level-3 operation that underpins LU/Cholesky decomposition, sparse direct solvers, and matrix inversion. In the left-side lower-triangular case studied in this paper, efficient GPU implementation remains challenging because forward substitution introduces strict row-wise dependencies, and shared memory is too scarce to hold both operand matrices for wide data types such as double complex. This paper presents HSMA-TRSM, a hierarchical shared memory-aware optimization framework for left-side lower-triangular TRSM on NVIDIA A100, NVIDIA H800, and Hygon DCU Z100 accelerators. For the small-scale regime (m,n<=64), we design a pipelined compute-memory overlap mechanism through loop unrolling and instruction reordering, and propose a dual thread-group seven-stage pipeline strategy to address shared memory constraints for double complex types. For large-scale problems, we introduce a diagonal block decoupling optimization with an O(IB)shared-memory footprint for diagonal block inversion, enabling adaptive block size selection based on matrix scale and hardware characteristics. A compile-time configuration selection framework based on offline profiling and online lookup selects the optimal block size per platform with zero runtime overhead. Evaluated on NVIDIA A100, H800, and Hygon DCU Z100, HSMA-TRSM achieves peak speedups of 2.05xover cuBLAS and 2.06xover rocBLAS. The gains are strongest in shared-memory-constrained double-complex small cases and in large real-type cases where adaptive blocking improves GEMM-dominated updates, while mature vendor kernels leave less optimization headroom in some regimes.

</details>


### [77] [psRL: Efficient Training for Agentic AI via Training-Time Prefix Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25683)
*Mianjie Yu,Zizhao Mo,Huanyu Qu,Zhirong Qian,Huanle Xu,Cen Li,Zifeng Zhao,Zhi Zhou,Jinhua Zhou,Jun Xie,Chengzhong Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 psRL，一种针对智能体强化学习（Agentic RL）训练阶段的新型系统，通过利用训练样本间普遍存在的前缀冗余（前缀匹配率常超 90%），将训练系统的性能瓶颈从 rollout 阶段转移到 update 阶段。该系统采用基于语义分组的两种前缀共享机制（inter-batch 与 self-sequence）、token 级微批处理、自适应块分配与动态块缓存策略，在工业级生产工作负载上实现了最高 5.2 倍的训练吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代智能体 AI 训练广泛采用树结构（Tree-structured）和逐步（Step-wise）RL 采样策略以提升样本效率与收敛性能。然而，这些策略虽然使单次 rollout 的边际成本降低，却显著增加了训练样本数量，导致 update 阶段成为端到端训练的新瓶颈（占总时长 40%-58%）。同时，这些结构化采样方式天然产生大量前缀冗余（生产 trace 显示前缀匹配率可达 94.51%），但现有训练系统（如 veRL+Megatron-LM）仍将样本视为独立序列处理，造成巨大的重复计算与存储开销。

Method: psRL 基于训练阶段"全局可见性"（Global Visibility）与"数据不可变性"（Data Immutability）两大特性，围绕工作负载调度与内存管理进行系统性优化。

**1. 灵活的两种前缀共享机制：**
- **Inter-batch Sharing**：将共享前缀的序列分配到不同 micro-batch，先计算的 micro-batch 缓存前缀的 KV 状态与优势值，后续 micro-batch 直接复用。
- **Self-sequence Sharing**：将单一长序列按 chunk 切分到多个 micro-batch，使后缀 token 复用前缀的 KV 状态，借鉴推理系统中的 chunked prefill。

**2. 层次化负载调度：**
- **语义分组（Semantic Grouping）**：按前缀相似度构建 group，保持 group 完整性以最大化前缀复用机会。
- **负载均衡算法**：基于 profile 推导的负载估算模型 T(s) = α₁·l^suf·l + α₂·l^suf + α₃，对 group 按工作负载降序排序，采用贪心算法分配到最闲的 worker。
- **Token 级微批处理（Token-wise Micro-Batching）**：在 token 级别对 micro-batch 进行切分与打包，严格限制峰值内存并显著减少流水线气泡。

**3. 自适应 KV 缓存管理：**
- **Adaptive Block Allocation**：根据前缀树精确识别可复用前缀跨度，按实际长度分配变长连续内存块，消除固定分块带来的内部碎片化。
- **Dynamic Block Caching**：基于前缀树计算每个 KV 块的引用计数（reference count），按 micro-batch 粒度递减；采用严格的 Just-in-Time 策略，保留引用计数 >0 的块，主动驱逐最远被复用的块。

**4. 实现细节：** 基于 veRL 框架，主要修改 Megatron-LM，约 7000 行代码。还将 prefix sharing 扩展到 reference model forward 与 old policy log-probability 计算阶段。

Result: **Update 阶段吞吐：** 在 Search、WebShop、ALFWorld、DTN Agent 四种工作负载上，psRL 相比 veRL 实现 1.2×–5.2× 加速；DTN Agent (Step) 模式下达到 239.1k tokens/s，相比最强基线 SGLang-PS（62.7k tokens/s）提升约 3.8×。

**端到端 RL 性能：** 整条 RL 流水线最高实现 2.1× 加速。DTN Agent 中 Log Old Prob 与 Reference 计算时间从 57.1s/68.3s 分别降至 7.1s/8.3s（约 8× 加速）；update 延迟从 462.1s 降至 88.7s。

**内存效率：** 相比 vLLM-PS 和 SGLang-PS 普遍节省 2×–10× 内存；DTN Agent 上峰值内存从 56.2 GB 降至 33 GB（41% 降幅），且保持高缓存命中率。Dynamic Block Caching 使内存减少超过 54%（从 73.0k MB 降至 33.0k MB）。

**消融实验：** 工作负载调度使 worker 间执行时间更均衡（Tree-Step 模式下从 209.45–305.12s 收紧至 245.13–261.23s）；Adaptive Block Allocation 在固定分块大小之间取得最佳权衡（不同工作负载最优块大小不同，ABA 无需手动调参）。

**可扩展性：** 集群规模从 64 GPU 扩展到 12,288 GPU 时接近线性扩展；DTN Agent 在 12,288 GPU 上达到 1.14M tokens/s，比 veRL 高 4.7×、比 SGLang-PS 高 3.4×。在 8× 序列长度下，所有基线因 OOM 崩溃，而 psRL 仍可运行（Step 模式下 42.1k tokens/s）。

Conclusion: 本文系统性地揭示了智能体 RL 训练中 update 阶段成为新瓶颈的现象，并提出首个利用训练时前缀冗余的系统 psRL。psRL 验证了在 update、reference model forward 和 log-probability 计算三个阶段通过 prefix sharing 实现显著加速与内存节省的可行性。未来工作可将该思路推广到其他具有前缀冗余特征的 RL 阶段与训练场景，并探索与异步训练流水线（如 StreamRL、AReaL）的深度融合。代码即将开源。

Abstract: In modern agentic AI training, the system bottleneck is shifting from rollout to update. Emerging sampling strategies such as tree-structured and step-wise RL greatly increase training sample volume while incurring relatively low marginal rollout cost, causing the update phase to dominate the end-to-end execution time. Crucially, this shift exposes a new optimization opportunity, as production traces reveal substantial prefix redundancy across training samples. In this paper, we propose psRL (prefix sharing for RL), a new training system for agentic AI designed to exploit prefix redundancy among training samples. Leveraging the global visibility and data immutability inherent to the update phase, psRL achieves efficient workload scheduling and memory management for distributed training. Specifically, psRL introduces two novel prefix-sharing mechanisms that enable flexible, fine-grained workload distribution across GPU workers, simultaneously optimizing prefix reuse and achieving load balancing. Moreover, psRL implements a new underlying KV cache manager that facilitates adaptable block-size allocation and dynamic KV caching, maximizing memory utilization while maintaining a high prefix hit rate. Evaluations using production traces demonstrate that psRL outperforms existing systems by up to 5.2x in throughput. The source code will be publicly available soon.

</details>


### [78] [An Oversubscription and Service Pricing Exploitation-Based Profit Maximization Framework for Industry Cloud Resource Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25712)
*Deepika Saxena,Ashutosh Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出OP-PMF框架，通过过订阅技术和异构服务定价模型（DSM/BEM），结合自适应集成学习预测与模糊C均值聚类，实现行业云数据中心的利润最大化与运营成本最小化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行业云数据中心电力成本高昂（占总运营成本30%-50%），而服务器利用率仅10%-50%，资源过度预留导致严重浪费。需要一种能同时利用过订阅和差异化定价来提升利润的云资源管理框架。

Method: 框架包含三个核心单元：(1) 资源预测单元（RPU）：采用自适应集成学习（LR/SVM/NN/RF等基学习器），基于加权评分机制周期性地为每台VM预测未来CPU和内存使用量；(2) VM伸缩单元（VSU）：利用模糊C均值聚类将预测资源使用相似的VM分组，以确定所需VM的数量和类型；(3) 负载均衡单元（LBU）：将用户请求分类为延迟敏感型（DSM，分配到高速服务器以赚取额外费用）和尽力而为型（BEM，分配到低速服务器以降低电力成本）。

Result: 使用Google Cluster和PlanetLab两个真实基准数据集进行实验。相比无OP-PMF的方案：电费降低55.56%，功耗降低60.7%，活跃服务器数量减少51%，资源利用率提升60%，利润提升51.18%；预测准确率约99.6%，RMSE优于NN、RF、LR、SVM等单一模型。

Conclusion: OP-PMF通过协同利用过订阅和异构定价模型，有效解决了行业云资源浪费问题，在电费、功耗、活跃服务器数、资源利用率和利润等关键指标上均显著优于现有方法。未来工作将扩展VM故障转移机制以增强云服务的可靠性与弹性。

Abstract: This article proposed a novel industry cloud resource management framework that exploits resource oversubscription and heterogeneous service pricing models to maximize profitability and operational efficiency for industry cloud providers. The framework proposes an adaptive ensemble machine learning driven prediction model for proactive estimation of resource utilization of Virtual Machines (VM)s based on previous resource utilization of respective users' VMs to minimize resource wastage due to oversubscription by them. Accordingly, the VMs having similar predicted resource usage are grouped using Fuzzy C means clustering. This helps to determine the required number of VMs with specific configuration to be deployed before executing user requests. Concurrently, the framework incorporates two distinct categories of cloud service pricing models, namely the Delay Sensitive Model and the Best-Effort Model. Accordingly, the user requests are classified and executed by selecting the most suitable VMs, with the goal of maximizing revenue and reducing electricity costs in cloud data centers (CDCs). Experimental simulation and comparison against state-of-the-art methods, using two benchmark VM traces, validates the performance of proposed framework. It significantly reduces electricity bills by 55.56 percentage, power consumption and active servers by up to 60.7 percentage and 51 percentage, respectively, while improving resource utilization and profits by up to 60 percentage and 51.18 percentage, respectively

</details>


### [79] [A Spatially-Aware Publish-Subscribe Middleware for IoT Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25728)
*Philipp Ungrund,Kurt Rothermel,Sukanya Bhowmik*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 spaPS，一种基于主题发布/订阅的空间感知中间件，通过几何世界模型支持基于空间关系（如包含、相交、距离等）的订阅过滤，并完全兼容 MQTT5 标准，无需修改现有客户端实现，可部署在资源受限的 IoT 设备上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于位置发布/订阅系统（如地理围栏）仅支持单一空间关系（包含），且要求发布者与物理实体位置绑定，限制了发布者的部署灵活性；缺乏面向 CPS/IoT 的标准化空间感知中间件机制，限制了位置相关应用的普及。

Method: 提出基于几何世界模型的"邻域"(neighborhood) 概念，支持单阶段和多阶段邻域表达；采用 OGC Simple Feature Access 空间关系（Equals、Intersects、Touches、Contains、DWithin 等）；基于 ActiveMQ Artemis 增强代理 + PostGIS 空间数据库；利用 MQTT5 的 user properties 编码邻域描述符与物理实体标识符（peid），无需修改协议；支持两种空间过滤映射模式：数据库内（in-database，B-tree 索引）与内存（main-memory，哈希映射）。

Result: 吞吐量实验中，内存模式可处理约 250,000 消息/秒，数据库模式约 150,000 消息/秒；过滤时间始终处于微秒级；订阅添加时间内存模式为线性（微秒级），数据库模式为对数（毫秒级）；基于柏林 OpenStreetMap 真实数据测试中，约 200ms 预热后过滤时间稳定，验证了实际部署的可行性。

Conclusion: spaPS 通过将空间关系建模、几何世界模型与发布/订阅协议解耦，提供了一种高效、可扩展且兼容现有 MQTT5 生态的空间感知通信方案，适合在资源受限的 IoT 设备上部署，可显著推动依赖空间关系与松耦合的 CPS/IoT 应用发展。

Abstract: Spatial and proximity awareness are critical enablers for efficient communication in Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS) and the Internet of Things (IoT). However, the lack of suitable middleware support and standardized mechanisms for spatial awareness significantly limits the pervasiveness of location-dependent applications. In this paper, we present a novel approach that extends topic-based publish-subscribe, a dominant messaging model in this domain, with integrated spatial filtering and routing capabilities. Our approach leverages a highly flexible geometric method for describing spatial filters, enabling expressive and efficient spatial subscriptions. A geometric world model operates together with an enhanced topic-based broker to realize spatially constrained message dissemination. Importantly, this functionality is achieved using the MQTT5 protocol without violating the standard or requiring any modifications to existing client implementations. Experimental results based on both synthetic and real-world data demonstrate strong system efficiency, minimal computational overhead at the broker, and the practical feasibility of deploying the approach on existing IoT devices.

</details>


### [80] [REE-TM: Reliable and Energy-Efficient Traffic Management Model for Diverse Cloud Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25747)
*Ashutosh Kumar Singh,Deepika Saxena,Volker Lindenstruth*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文提出 REE-TM 框架，面向资源需求差异大、动态性强的云工作负载，将任务分为 Stragglers、Resource Hogs 和 Normal Jobs，结合 TG-QNN 量子启发式工作负载预测、QBHO 优化和 TSECE 熵分析，实现可靠性感知、节能的虚拟机/物理节点调度。Google Compute Cluster 轨迹上的仿真显示，模型达到约 85.6%–97.8% 的可靠性，最高可降至 7.05% 的负载执行失败率和 10.092 kWh 的能耗；相较无预测、无可靠性机制方案，论文报告最高提升可靠性 30.25%、节能 23%。不过，公式、参数表与部分实验叙述存在符号不一致、实验细节不足及“量子”概念偏启发式等问题，外部复现和真实量子硬件有效性仍需验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云端工作负载在计算、内存、带宽、I/O 需求和执行复杂度上高度异质且动态波动，静态或同质化资源分配容易造成过配置、低利用率、拥塞和可靠性下降。作者旨在同时解决提前预测不足、任务类别混淆、虚拟节点到物理节点映射欠佳，以及资源竞争导致的故障和能耗增加。

Method: 方法由三部分组成：①基于 CPU、内存、带宽及 I/O 阈值，将请求分为 Stragglers、Resource Hogs、Normal Jobs，并为各类任务设置不同的虚拟节点与物理节点分配策略；②将资源使用序列编码为量子旋转后的输入，使用 Toffoli Gate 门控量子神经网络（TG-QNN）预测下一时间窗负载，并以量子化黑洞优化算法（QBHO）优化权重；③通过流量状态计算与熵分析模块（TSECE）比较预测与实际负载，识别拥塞及预测误差，并据此调整工作负载、预留资源和启停物理节点。可靠性通过可用性、容错、失效检测和基于资源利用率的故障率建模；能耗则按 DVFS 功耗曲线估算。实验使用三种服务器、四类虚拟节点和 Google Compute Cluster 数据。

Result: 主要实验结果如下：1）TG-QNN 预测：5 分钟预测窗口 MSE=0.0005、MAE=0.0099；60 分钟窗口 MSE=0.0024、MAE=0.0336，优于所列 GNN、ATTN、EQNN、ENN 基线，但 10 分钟窗口 ATTN 的 MSE 略低。2）负载预测误差最低可至 0.0002，流量熵中位数约为 0.0003–0.00075。3）在 100–1000 个请求下，REE-TM 可靠性约 85.6%–97.8%，资源利用率约 42.6%–49.9%，能耗随请求数增加至 10.092 kWh；过载率最低 0.03%，最高 8.20%，分配成功率最高 99.9%。4）相较 W-REE-TM* 和 W-REE-TM**，报告的可靠性最高提升 26.04% 和 30.25%，能耗降低约 20%–23%，活跃物理节点最多减少约 15.79%。5）消融实验表明，TG-QNN 提供基础预测，QBHO 将可靠性从 66.67% 提升至 73.78% 并将 MSE 从 0.0075 降至 0.0003；加入 TSECE 后，可靠性进一步提升至 86.88%，成功率达 98.6%。

Conclusion: REE-TM 通过异质任务分类、量子启发式负载预测、熵驱动资源调整和可靠性感知节点选择，在云数据中心中实现了可靠性、资源利用率和能耗之间的较好折中。论文认为其性能接近具有完美信息的 OPTIMAL 基线，并优于移除预测或可靠性机制的版本。未来工作包括提高模型可扩展性与适应性、研究更先进的量子优化方法，并纳入更多样化的流量模式。

Abstract: Diversity of workload demands lays a critical impact on efficient resource allocation and management of cloud services. The existing literature has either weakly considered or overlooked the heterogeneous feature of job requests received from wide range of internet services users. To address this context, the proposed approach named Reliable and Energy Efficient Traffic Management (REE-TM) has exploited the diversity of internet traffic in terms of variation in resource demands and expected complexity. Specifically, REE-TM incorporates categorization of heterogeneous job requests and executes them by selecting the most admissible virtual node (a software-defined instance such as a virtual machine or container) and physical node (an actual hardware server or compute host) within the cloud infrastructure. To deal with resource-contention-based resource failures and performance degradation, a novel workload estimator 'Toffoli Gate-based Quantum Neural Network' (TG-QNN) is proposed, wherein learning process or interconnection weights optimization is achieved using Quantum version of BlackHole (QBHO) algorithm. The proactively estimated workload is used to compute entropy of the upcoming internet traffic with various traffic states analysis for detection of probable resource-congestion. REE-TM is extensively evaluated through simulations using a benchmark dataset and compared with optimal and without REE-TM versions. The performance evaluation and comparison of REE-TM with measured significant metrics reveal its effectiveness in assuring higher reliability by up to 30.25% and energy-efficiency by up to 23% as compared without REE-TM.

</details>


### [81] [Quantum Blackhole Learning-Optimized Hadamard Neural Network Model for Dynamic Resource Reservation in Industry Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25754)
*Deepika Saxena,Hari Mohan Gaur,Ashutosh Kumar Singh,Anand Mohan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出QB-HNN模型，将量子力学（量子比特与Hadamard门叠加态）与神经网络结合，并使用新型量子黑洞双相优化（QB-BiO）算法训练量子比特权重，用于工业云中异构工作负载预测与动态资源预留。相较LSTM和EQNN，预测误差分别降低36.36%和22.83%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习方法在处理高维、动态、异构云工作负载时泛化能力不足，难以捕捉非线性依赖和量子级变异性；现有EQNN和MCT-AQNN依赖进化算法优化量子比特权重，易陷入早熟收敛和局部最优。需引入更具表达力和智能性的量子优化范式以提升预测精度与适应性。

Method: 1. 提出Hadamard量子神经网络（HQNN）：实值输入通过旋转门和相移门编码为量子比特向量，经前馈传播后在隐藏层和输出层应用受控Hadamard门引入叠加态激活，并通过量子激活函数（含sigmoid约束）输出。2. 设计两种变体：QB-HNN-SAP（单属性预测）和QB-HNN-MAP（多属性同步预测）。3. 提出QB-BiO优化算法：包含量子比特聚类优化（K-Means分簇）、启发式交叉（生成新后代）和位置更新（受簇内黑洞与全局黑洞引力驱动），并基于事件视界半径实现坍缩重生机制以维持种群多样性。4. 利用马尔可夫链和随机搜索理论证明算法以概率1收敛至全局或近全局最优。

Result: 在六种基准数据集（Google Cluster、Web服务器NASA/Saskatchewan、HPC AuverGrid/SHARCNet）上评估，预测窗口5/30/60分钟。MSE和MAE均优于对比方法；归一化MSE较EQNN降低22.83%、较LSTM降低36.36%、较NN-BP降低75.12%；RMSE在GCD-CPU和GCD-Memory上各预测窗口均最低（如30分钟窗口GCD-CPU RMSE=2.42×10⁻³ vs MCT-AQNN 11.6×10⁻³）；收敛于20个epoch内（优于SaDE/BaDE的45个epoch）。敏感性分析表明K=5、Z=25为最优配置，测试MSE最低0.00412。计算复杂度为O(Z×K×N²×M)，与SaDE/BaDE相当。

Conclusion: QB-HNN通过融合量子叠加态和黑洞引力启发的双相优化，在异构云工作负载预测中实现了更高的精度、更快的收敛和更好的稳定性，为下一代量子-云系统中的自适应资源管理奠定了基础。未来工作将集成迁移学习、多步任务学习及混合量子-经典仿真环境，以提升模型在未见/演化工作负载上的鲁棒性和与DevOps管道的互操作性。

Abstract: Accurate workload prediction and proactive resource reservation are crucial for industry clouds. However, the conventional machine learning (CML) models with limited learning capabilities often fail to predict diverse, high-dimensional workloads with sudden changes in resource demand, leading to excessive power consumption and resource management issues. In this context, this article proposes a novel Hadamard neural network with quantum blackhole (QB-HNN) optimization. This model combines the computational efficiency of quantum mechanics with the persuasive learning capability of neural networks (NNs). The workload information is transformed into qubits and propagated via a deep network of qubit neurons comprising a Hadamard-gated activation function to fetch superposition within the QB-HNN model for intuitive pattern learning. Furthermore, a novel quantum blackhole biphase optimization (QB-BiO) algorithm is introduced to train and optimize qubit neural weights. The performance of the proposed model is comprehensively evaluated and compared with five state-of-the-art approaches using six benchmark datasets of three heterogeneous varieties of cloud workloads. The prediction accuracy achieved for an extensive range of workloads confirms its influential performance by minimizing the prediction error up to 36.36% and 22.83% over existing LSTM- and EQNN-based prediction approaches, respectively.

</details>


### [82] [Scalable Multi-GPU Simulation of 3D Multicellular Growth with RNN-Based Workload Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25890)
*Matvey Moisseyev,Huijing Du,Dandan Zheng,Chi Zhang,Hongfeng Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可扩展的多GPU框架，用于基于亚细胞元模型（SEM）的三维多细胞生长模拟。该框架结合了GPU加速、空间分箱、域分解和工作负载感知分区，并通过基于RNN的负载均衡控制器学习对反应式边界调整规则的残差校正，从而在保持负载均衡的同时显著减少不必要的数据迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于亚细胞元模型的多细胞生长模拟能够捕捉复杂的组织发育过程，但元素级交互带来巨大计算开销。细胞运动、生长和分裂不断改变空间工作负载分布，导致初始平衡的分区随时间变得低效。传统反应式负载均衡只在失衡发生后才进行调整，未能利用工作负载的时间演化信息，亟需一种具有历史感知能力的负载均衡策略。

Method: 1. GPU并行化与空间分箱：使用3×3×3邻域分箱将元素交互复杂度从O(n²)降至近似O(n)。2. 多GPU域分解：沿单一空间轴划分连续切片，使用Ghost区实现边界交互。3. 工作负载感知初始分区：基于每个细胞的工作负载分数 w_i = n_i × n_N(i)（元素数×邻域元素数）构建一维工作负载直方图，按累积工作负载均分给各GPU。4. RNN负载均衡控制器：单层GRU（隐藏状态大小24，约2.8K参数），每个内部分区边界维护独立隐藏状态，输入包含时序不平衡、局部不平衡变化、全局不平衡、分区宽度、增长周期信息及反应式建议，输出对反应式边界建议的残差校正，通过tanh函数映射保证调整幅度有界。5. 训练策略：在可微的负载均衡循环代理中离线训练，使用合成工作负载轨迹（随机化秩数、域大小、增长事件和测量抖动），无需实测执行轨迹；采用Adam优化器、余弦退火、50步回溯训练，损失函数包含相对不平衡和L1迁移惩罚。

Result: 1. 单GPU加速：空间分箱GPU实现相比串行CPU基线加速约三个数量级；在大规模（6.3×10⁶元素）配置下，分箱相比GPU全对实现额外获得9.2×加速。2. 多GPU扩展性：435K细胞规模在8 GPU上达到约45%的计算并行效率，16 GPU时降至31%。3. RNN负载均衡：在8 GPU、435K细胞端到端模拟中，将平均全局不平衡从静态分区的11.3%降至3.5%（反应式基线为3.8%），端到端运行时间相对静态分区降低9.0%，切片迁移量相比反应式基线减少7.7×。4. 控制器层评估：RNN在所有秩数下迁移量最少，且时间平均不平衡低于静态基线；在P=16时，持久性和Holt预测方法因频繁边界移动产生5000+切片迁移且效果不佳。5. 科学用例：胚胎表皮发育模拟成功展示了从单层到多层组织的分层过程。

Conclusion: 本文提出的多GPU框架结合GPU加速、空间分箱、域分解和工作负载感知分区，有效解决了SEM多细胞生长模拟的高计算成本和动态负载不均衡问题。RNN引导的负载均衡控制器通过学习残差校正，在保持甚至略优于反应式基线负载均衡的同时，大幅减少了不必要的分区迁移，证明了历史感知控制策略在改善负载均衡与迁移开销权衡方面的有效性。该工作为在现代多GPU平台上高效模拟动态演化的多细胞系统提供了可行路径。未来工作将扩展到百万细胞级别的更大规模生产模拟。

Abstract: Detailed multicellular growth simulations based on subcellular element models (SEMs) can capture complex tissue development, but their element-level interactions impose substantial computational cost. This work presents a scalable multi-GPU framework for 3D multicellular growth simulation that combines GPU acceleration, spatial binning, domain decomposition, and workload-aware partitioning. Cell movement, growth, and division continuously reshape the spatial workload distribution, causing initially balanced partitions to become inefficient over time. To address this, we introduce an RNN-based load-balancing controller that observes recent per-rank execution times and partition states and learns residual corrections to a reactive boundary-adjustment rule. The controller is trained offline in a differentiable surrogate of the load-balancing loop with randomized workload dynamics, requiring no measured execution traces for training. We evaluate the framework in terms of single-GPU acceleration, multi-GPU computation scaling, controller-level load-balancing behavior, and end-to-end simulation performance, with comparisons against static partitioning, reactive load balancing, and conventional time-series prediction baselines. A representative embryonic epidermal development use case further demonstrates the type of spatially and temporally evolving workload targeted by the framework. In our evaluation, GPU acceleration with spatial binning accelerates the interaction computation by roughly three orders of magnitude over a serial CPU baseline. RNN-guided load balancing reduces the mean global imbalance from 11.3% under static partitioning to 3.5%, lowers end-to-end runtime by 9.0% relative to static partitioning, and reduces slice migration by 7.7x compared with the reactive baseline, showing that history-aware control can improve workload balance while avoiding unnecessary repartitioning.

</details>


### [83] [Slasher: Power Flexibility for Cloud Datacenters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26021)
*Liuzixuan Lin,Fiodar Kazhamiaka,Alok Gautam Kumbhare,Chaojie Zhang,Jaylen Wang,Hassan Khan,Rodrigo L. Assis,Mariana Rodrigues,Kyle Woolcock,Nithish Mahalingam,Brijesh Warrier,Rodrigo Fonseca,Ricardo Bianchini*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍Slasher系统，一个由微软Azure开发的通用云数据中心电力灵活性管理系统，通过协调硬件和软件杠杆，在满足电力目标的同时最小化对工作负载的负面影响。论文涵盖电力调节场景分析、电力削减杠杆特征化、系统架构设计、影响模型构建及控制策略评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI应用对电力需求激增及电网容量紧张，云数据中心需要具备动态调节电力消耗的能力。然而，云平台对应用内部细节了解有限，且现有电力管理方法存在显著局限性：电池成本高昂、发电机使用受法规限制、服务器节流可能违反SLO协议。因此需要一种统一的系统，在不同时间尺度和影响范围内协调多种电力调节手段。

Method: 论文采用系统设计与仿真评估相结合的方法：(1) 基于微软20个生产数据中心9天的数据特征化电力削减杠杆；(2) 采用两层次集中式架构（区域编排器+本地控制器）实现多数据中心协调；(3) 使用基于CVaR/期望损失(ES)的通用工作负载影响模型评估服务容量削减的SLO违反风险；(4) 开发基于Python离散事件仿真框架Salabim的高保真模拟器Stratosim；(5) 提出三种服务器关闭策略（AscUtil、AscScore-OnePass、AscScore-ReEval）的贪心算法实现。

Result: 论文主要结果表明：(1) 计算服务器的基础功耗在数据中心总功耗中占主导地位，因此关闭服务器是最大的电力削减机会；(2) CPU频率调节通常以比例较小的功率削减换取不成比例的较大服务容量损失（40%功率削减时中位应用仅保留48%峰值服务容量）；(3) AscScore-ReEval策略显著优于简单策略，在15%电力削减目标下几乎保持零成本，优先关闭托管Spot VM或具有充足冗余容量的服务上的服务器；(4) 算法计算时间为4.5-8秒，可应对5000台服务器和10000个服务的规模；(5) 实际部署验证了电池放电与IT工作负载关闭联合响应快速频率响应(FFR)场景的可行性。

Conclusion: Slasher系统通过协调硬件和软件电力杠杆实现了通用的云数据中心电力管理，在满足电力目标的同时最小化对工作负载的影响。论文提出的风险感知影响模型和基于得分的服务器选择算法能够有效利用服务间的冗余容量，保护高优先级服务。未来的研究方向包括云存储系统的电力削减、丰富的操作空间中的策略优化，以及AI工作负载特有的电力调节机会。

Abstract: Datacenters consume many megawatts of power, and regularly encounter scenarios that require modulating their power draw. These scenarios include datacenter infrastructure failures, power grid failures, grid services, and more, spanning a diverse range of requirements in terms of the power magnitude, the scope of the reduction, the notice time, and other dimensions. To address these scenarios, we have built Slasher, a general system for modulating the power of \azure datacenters to handle scenarios ranging from individual racks to regional multi-datacenter grid events. Slasher coordinates datacenter resources with the goal of meeting power targets while minimizing negative impact on hosted workloads. In this paper, we review the main power modulation scenarios, characterize the power reduction levers using data from production cloud datacenters, describe Slasher's system architecture, and formulate the cloud datacenter power modulation control problem. We also develop a high-fidelity datacenter simulator and propose a workload impact model, using them to design and evaluate power control algorithms.

</details>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [84] [HCC+: Hyperbolic Guarding for Certified Attention Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24971)
*Liangchen Ge*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: HCC+是一种基于庞加莱球几何的理论框架，用于为双曲空间中的注意力检索提供确定性的、与查询无关的认证保证。它利用双曲空间的三个独特性质实现了对注意力权重偏差的严格界限，并附带6.1倍的存储压缩效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文Transformer中高效注意力检索是关键瓶颈。现有方法（如TurboQuant）通过优化重建MSE来减少存储，但无法保证注意力权重在有限精度表示下的保留，尤其在精确检索（ε→0）模式下，小扰动会被非线性注意力核放大。缺乏确定性的、与查询无关的检索认证机制。

Method: 利用庞加莱球（双曲空间）的三个核心几何性质：(1) 边界处体积呈指数增长 → 支持查询无关的静态边界截断；(2) 1-中心覆盖半径呈对数增长 O(log n) → 支持维度无关的关键键识别；(3) 维度无关的填充常数。方法包含三个原则：边界截断（Boundary Truncation）、关键键守护（Critical-Key Guarding, CKG）、标准键聚集集中（Aggregate Concentration）。通过Hyperbolic Weiszfeld算法计算1-中心，分配关键键(14,14)-bit、标准键(4,4)-bit的混合精度。

Result: 两个确定性保证：(1) 精确检索时，逐层注意力偏差<10%理想值；(2) 软注意力时，总变差距离以O(1/√n)衰减（有限样本方差的最优速率）。存储方面，相对于FP16实现6.1×理论压缩比（边界截断1.18×，CKG+HPQ 7.2×，SAVC 2.13×，ECPP 1.25×，累计22.6×，对应6.1×的单因子表示）。提供了首个非欧几何中的确定性、与查询无关的检索认证。

Conclusion: HCC+建立了双曲注意力检索的确定性认证基线，证明了负曲率带来的对数覆盖半径是与欧几里得启发式方法的严格分离（欧几里得为O(n^{1/d})，与维度相关）。其贡献在于认证的存在性而非紧致性。局限包括：n>10^5时δ_min趋于零会导致过多键被标记为关键键；边界投影会改变键间距离但未被纳入误差界；尚未在Transformer基准（如LongBench、PG19）上做实证验证；未评估与FlashAttention/vLLM的兼容性。未来工作包括LLM实证验证、近似双曲嵌入扩展、1-中心计算优化等。

Abstract: We study the Lipschitz stability of attention retrieval in hyperbolic spaces. Existing methods lack deterministic guarantees on attention-weight preservation under finite-precision representations. We introduce HCC+, a theoretical framework exploiting three properties of the Poincaré ball: exponential volume growth enabling query-independent boundary truncation; logarithmic covering radius of hyperbolic 1-centers enabling dimension-independent critical-key identification; and a packing bound with constants independent of the embedding dimension. We prove two deterministic guarantees: for exact retrieval, the per-layer attention deviation is bounded by 10\% of its ideal value; for soft attention, the total variation distance decays as $O(1/\sqrt{n})$, the rate of finite-sample variance. As a consequence of the guarding mechanism, the framework achieves a storage reduction factor of $6.1\times$ relative to FP16. We provide the first deterministic, query-independent retrieval certificate in non-Euclidean geometry.

</details>


### [85] [Fuzzy Pattern Matching in Ordered Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25032)
*Armen Kostanyan,Arevik Harmandayan*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究有序结构中的模糊模式匹配问题，将经典KMP算法中的前缀函数概念推广为"轨迹"(trajectory)，分别针对线性结构和层次结构（有序根树）提出了基于轨迹代数的统一模糊模式匹配算法框架，并将其表示为转换系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的精确模式匹配（如KMP算法）和近似匹配在很多实际场景中过于严格，无法处理字符层面的不确定性。模糊模式匹配允许模式符号关联模糊属性，文本字符只需以一定隶属度满足相应属性即可，更适合实体解析、噪声文本分类、生物信息学等存在变异和不确定性的应用。现有模糊匹配工作多针对线性结构，缺乏对层次结构（偏序集/树）的统一处理框架。

Method: 核心方法是引入"轨迹"(trajectory)概念，将KMP的前缀函数推广为关于已处理序列后缀与模式前缀μ-匹配关系的列表结构。定义轨迹代数Ξ，包含size、isPromoted、promote（O(m)）、rollback（O(1)）等操作。

1. 线性结构（FL-Matching算法）：以τ=[ε]初始化，按顺序处理字符串每个字符，通过rollback回溯和promote推进更新轨迹，当size(τ)=m时报告匹配。算法被建模为有标签转换系统T₁=(S,s₀,F,Σ,Δ)，状态集为轨迹。

2. 层次结构（FH-Matching算法）：针对有序根树，沿前序遍历树，使用visitNode子过程为每个访问节点计算轨迹，借助栈存储未实现事件⟨节点,轨迹⟩。算法被建模为下推转换系统T₂=(S,s₀,F,Σ,Γ,Z₀,Δ)，状态为"事件"（节点与轨迹的配对），区分已实现事件和未实现事件。

正确性证明：轨迹迭代引理说明τ_i恰好由X[1..i]的所有border组成，由此得出head(τ_i)=LB(X[1..i])是不变式，保证算法检测到全部且仅μ-出现。复杂度用势能法分析：FL-Matching时间O(mn)、空间O(m²)；FH-Matching时间O(mN)、空间O(m²H)。

Result: 提出两个算法：FL-Matching在字符串中查找所有模糊模式出现，时间O(mn)、空间O(m²)；FH-Matching在有序根树中查找所有饱和链上的模糊模式出现，时间O(mN)、空间O(m²H)。均被证明正确且完备（找出全部且仅μ-出现），并通过实例演示了执行过程。

Conclusion: 轨迹作为前缀函数在模糊情境下的自然推广，为线性与层次结构中的模糊模式匹配提供了统一的框架。两种算法均保持与同类算法相同的时间复杂度，但FL-Matching相对此前二维前缀表方法具有更低的空间复杂度。该轨迹方法可进一步推广到其他有序数据结构。

Abstract: The problem of pattern matching, that is, finding all occurrences of a given pattern in a string, is one of the fundamental problems in computer science that has applications in many areas. In this paper, we consider fuzzy patterns, defined as sequences of fuzzy properties over the basic alphabet. We first consider fuzzy pattern matching for sequences of elements of the basic alphabet and then extend the problem to partially ordered sets of nodes labeled by elements of the basic alphabet. For sequences, we seek segments that match the pattern, whereas for partially ordered structures, we seek saturated chains of nodes that match the pattern. The key concept underlying the solutions to these problems is the notion of a trajectory, which generalizes the concept of the prefix function used in the Knuth--Morris--Pratt (KMP) algorithm. A trajectory is processed together with the corresponding data structure, allowing the proposed algorithms to be represented as transition systems whose states are trajectories for sequences and trajectories associated with nodes for partially ordered structures. The trajectory-based approach provides a unified framework for fuzzy pattern matching in various data structures.

</details>


### [86] [Improved Low-Overhead Communication-Efficient String Reconciliation and Edit Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25179)
*Michael T. Goodrich,Gonzalo Navarro,Claire A. To*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对分布式字符串调和（string reconciliation）与编辑距离问题，提出了一种基于可逆布鲁姆查找表（IBLT）和局部一致性分块（LCP）的简单低开销通信高效算法。给定两方Alice和Bob分别持有的字符串X和Y，编辑距离为k，该算法使用O(k log³ n)比特通信量和O(n log k)时间开销（高概率成功），同时改善了此前工作计算开销过高的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据库同步、文件系统协调、DNA序列比对等场景中，双方需要交换/比对长字符串，希望通信开销仅与差异程度（编辑距离k）成正比，而非与字符串总长度n成正比。此前的方法在通信复杂度与计算开销之间难以兼顾：Orlitsky的通信最优方法计算开销为n^O(k)；Chakraborty等人和Belazzougui-Zhang的方法虽然计算开销为O(n polylog n)，但要么通信复杂度较高、要么k受限、要么常数因子很大且实现复杂。先前的IBLT方法计算开销也偏高。因此需要一种既通信高效又计算低开销的简单算法。

Method: 1. 利用局部一致性分块（LCP）：通过随机置换π在字符串局部最小值处切分，将X和Y递归地分层处理，每层产生长度O(log n)的chunks，总层数r ≤ log₂ n，形成类似上下文无关文法的树结构（grammar），总大小为O(n)。<br>2. 关键引理：单个编辑操作在每一层序列X_i中最多引起2个连续变化，因此至多影响2r ≤ 2 log₂ n个语法规则（Lemma 6/7/8）。<br>3. 利用IBLT进行集合调和：Alice和Bob使用相同的hash函数π_i和h_i，构造各自的chunk命名集A_i和B_i。由于编辑距离k，两集合差异元素至多2k个。Alice为每层构建IBLT发送给Bob，Bob通过减法解码恢复A_i\B_i，再请求具体chunk内容。<br>4. 去除对k先验知识的依赖：通过对k'=1,2,4,8,...的指数搜索（Corollary 2），通信代价仍为O(k)，时间开销变为O(n log k)。<br>5. 额外优点：所构造的grammar大小为O(γ log(n/γ))，其中γ为最小吸引子大小，对高重复度字符串特别有利。

Result: 理论结果（Theorem 2与Corollary 2）：给定编辑距离上界k（先验已知或未知），算法成功概率≥1-1/η（任意常数c≥1，n<η≤n^c）。<br>- 通信复杂度：O(k log² n (log n + log η))比特，取η = n^c时即为O(k log³ n)比特。<br>- 计算时间开销：先验已知k时为O(n)，未知k时为O(n log k)。<br>- 当k = O(√n)时，可额外用O(n + k²)时间精确计算编辑距离。<br>性能改进对比：相对Orlitsky的n^O(k)开销大幅降低；相对Chakraborty等的O(k² log n)通信和O(n polylog n)开销，通信更优且计算更简单；相对Belazzougui-Zhang的O(k(log² k + log n))通信和O(n polylog n)开销，计算开销更优（线性 vs 多对数级）且对k无限制。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种基于IBLT和局部一致性分块的简单算法，同时实现了低通信复杂度O(k log³ n)和低计算开销O(n)（或去k先验假设下的O(n log k)），突破了先前方法在通信效率与计算效率之间的权衡限制。此外该方法的grammar表示对高度重复字符串（如基因组数据库）天然友好，可使开销随最小吸引子大小γ而非n扩展。后续可考虑使用更复杂的grammar（如O(δ log(n/δ))，δ为归一化子串复杂度）进一步压缩。该工作改善了作者此前同样基于IBLT的成果，在保持简洁性的同时给出了更紧的复杂度保证。

Abstract: Suppose two parties, Alice and Bob, hold long character strings, $X$ and $Y$, respectively, and they are interested in determining how similar $X$ and $Y$ are. {Moreover, they want to exchange the strings with cost proportional to their degree of dissimilarity.} Such problems arise, for example, in database and file system synchronization operations, as well as in DNA sequence comparisons. Since the strings are long, we are interested in methods that are communication-efficient and have low overhead in terms of the computations that Alice and Bob must perform, when the strings are similar enough. In this paper, we provide a simple low-overhead communication-efficient algorithms for such string reconciliation and edit distance problems, determining the edit distance $k$ between $X$ and $Y$ using only $O(k\log^3 n)$ bits of communication and $O(n\log k)$ time overhead, with high probability.

</details>


### [87] [Exact algorithms for optimal discretization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25197)
*László Kozma,Junqi Tan*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对两个几何点集分离问题（最优离散化和点分离）设计了改进的精确指数时间算法，首次突破了朴素的2^n时间下界。最优离散化问题获得O(1.9602^n)算法，点分离问题获得O(1.8906^n)算法，核心思想是利用结构引理证明最优解中水平线和垂直线的较小一方存在上界，从而缩减枚举空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优离散化（将红蓝点集用最少水平/垂直线分离）和点分离（用最少轴平行线分离所有点使其每个格子至多一个点）作为监督学习预处理的经典问题，此前最好的指数时间算法具有O*(2^n)的平凡上界，而参数化算法（按解大小k参数化）虽已被证明FPT，但其依赖项2^O(k²log k)在中等规模k（如k∈Ω(log n)）下劣于2^n方法。如何打破2^n的平凡性障碍是该领域核心问题。

Method: 方法基于朴素搜索算法（猜测一组水平线，用贪心算法唯一确定最优垂直线集合，因竖线集合选取可归结为区间刺集问题用贪心解）；关键创新是证明最优解的结构性质——最小方向线数存在上界。具体地：（1）一般位置下，最优离散化问题min{|H|,|V|} ≤ 0.4n（平衡引理），通过将每条线与唯一分离的颜色对对齐进行计数论证；（2）一般位置下，点分离问题min{|H|,|V|} ≤ (n-1)/3，证明对最优且下界紧；（3）允许点共坐标时，引入退化度d = min{dx, dy}，将上界推广为0.4n+0.6d（最优离散化）和n/3+2d/3（点分离）；（4）由此设置搜索参数α为上述上界，枚举所有该方向子集，对应运行时间为n^O(1)·∑C(n-d,i) ≈ 2^((1-δ)H((0.4+0.6δ)/(1-δ))·n)，通过二进制熵函数估计得到声明的时间界。

Result: 主要结果：（1）最优离散化问题（红蓝分离）的O(1.9602^n)算法（定理1），对一般位置和无一般位置假设均成立；（2）点分离问题O(1.8899^n)算法（一般位置下）和O(1.8906^n)（退化情况下）（定理2）；（3）证明一般位置下点分离的(n-1)/3下界是紧的（构造实例），最优离散化下证明0.3n的下界（构造实例），上界0.4n与下界0.3n仍有差距。

Conclusion: 本文工作首次将两个分离问题的指数时间算法从平凡2^n障碍下提升，基于结构平衡引理缩减子集枚举空间；提出了对最优离散化0.3n与0.4n之间的优雅开放问题，是否可能通过更强的结构性质达到紧界n/3（与单色情形匹配）；另一开放问题是2^o(n)算法的可能性未被排除；公开了ChatGPT 5.5和Claude Opus 4.7在探索和验证中的应用。

Abstract: The optimal discretization problem asks, given two disjoint sets of points $R$ and $B$ in the plane, for a minimal family of horizontal and vertical lines that separate the two sets, so that no cell delimited by the lines contains points from both sets. The problem arises as a pre-processing in supervised machine learning, and has received significant attention in parameterized algorithmics. Answering the question raised by Bonnet, Giannopoulos, and Lampis [IPEC 2017] and Froese [PhD thesis, 2018], it was shown by Kratsch, Masařík, Muzi, Pilipczuk, and Sorge [SODA 2021] that optimal discretization admits a fixed-parameter algorithm with running time $2^{\mathcal{O}(k^2 \log k)} \cdot n^{\mathcal{O}(1)}$, where $k$ is the solution size and $n = |R| + |B|$. In this paper we give an algorithm for optimal discretization that runs in time $\mathcal{O}(1.9602^n)$. We also study the related point separation problem that asks to separate all input points by axis-parallel lines. For this problem we obtain an algorithm with runtime $\mathcal{O}(1.8906^n)$. Our guarantees follow from structural observations about bichromatic and monochromatic point sets, and hold even if points are allowed to share coordinates. To our knowledge, these are the first improvements over the trivial $2^n$ bound for both problems.

</details>


### [88] [Time-Optimal APSP and Matrix Multiplication in Classes of Linear Neighborhood Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25212)
*Édouard Bonnet,Julien Duron,Marcin Pilipczuk,Marek Sokołowski,Szymon Toruńczyk*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文为具有线性邻域复杂度（LNC）的图类设计了时间最优的APSP与矩阵乘法算法。具体地，在二次预处理后，可在O(n)时间内完成邻接矩阵与任意向量的乘法。同时给出了随机线性时间的三角检测算法以及K₄、K₅的近似线性检测算法，并将结果推广至有界VC密度的图类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有APSP和矩阵乘法算法在LNC图类上仍含有额外的多对数因子，且APSP在有界twin-width类中的最优性尚为开放问题。本文旨在消除这些多对数因子，达到时间最优O(n²)，并解决[Bonnet等, ICALP'26]中提出的若干问题。

Method: 核心方法包括：(1) 引入"有界万能对称差"（bounded versatile symmetric difference）概念，并利用Haussler打包引理证明LNC蕴含此性质；(2) 基于最长公共扩展（LCE）数据结构构建判定近孪生对的查询机制；(3) 利用LCE数据结构在O(n²)时间内构造恒定宽度的sd-退化序列；(4) 将sd-退化序列转化为区间二部图分划和稀疏符号树模型；(5) 设计新颖的区间求交数据结构（在word RAM模型下线性时间）以支持SSSP和矩阵-向量乘法；(6) 基于sd-退化序列和Welzl序检测小团。

Result: 主要结果包括：定理1-2给出LNC图类中O(n²)确定性及随机线性时间的sd-退化序列构造；定理5给出O(n+b)的SSSP算法；定理6给出O(n²)的APSP算法（时间最优）；定理7给出矩阵-向量乘法的O(n²)预处理+O(n)查询；定理8给出随机O(n+m)的三角检测；定理9-10分别给出K₄、K₅的近似线性检测算法；定理3-4将结果推广到VC密度ρ的情形；定理31证明k-Clique在LNC类中有ETH下界Ω̃(n^{k/log k})。

Conclusion: 本文系统地解决了LNC图类中APSP和矩阵乘法的时间最优性，消除多对数因子，推广至VC密度类，并为小团检测提供了新的高效算法，同时揭示了团检测问题的ETH条件下的下界，表明对固定k的小团可达到近线性时间，但对一般k难以实现。

Abstract: The notion of linear neighborhood complexity is a very general structural assumption on a graph class, covering most classes of sparse graphs such as planar graphs, graphs excluding a fixed (topological) minor, or bounded expansion graphs, as well as many structured classes of dense graphs, such as graphs of bounded clique-width, twin-width, merge-width, or flip-width. In this work, we present $O(n^2)$-time optimal algorithms for $n$-vertex graphs coming from a class of linear neighborhood complexity for the following problems: $\bullet$ All-Pairs Shortest Paths, $\bullet$ the multiplication of the adjacency matrix $M$ of the input graph with any $n \times n$ matrix. More specifically, after a quadratic preprocessing, we can multiply $M$ with any $n$-vector in $O(n)$ time. This solves several questions raised in [Bonnet, Kim, Geniet, Moon; ICALP '26], and improves and generalizes results in several other recent papers [Bonnet, Giocanti, Ossona de Mendez, Thomassé; STACS '23], [Bannach, Marwitz, Tantau; STACS '24], [Anand, van den Brand, McCarty; NeurIPS '26], [Kozma, Opler '26], and [Cardinal, McCarty, Yuditsky '26]. We also extend our results to classes of bounded VC density. In classes of linear neighborhood complexity, we also give a triangle-detection algorithm in randomized linear time $O(n+m)$ in $n$-vertex $m$-edge graphs, a $K_4$-detection algorithm in randomized $O(n \log^5 n + m \log n)$ or deterministic $O(n^2)$ time, and a $K_5$-detection algorithm in randomized $O(n \log^9 n + m \log^5 n)$ time.

</details>


### [89] [A General Framework for Metropolis-Adjusted Dikin Walks: Dimension-Square Mixing on Polytopes and Log-Det Walks on Spectrahedra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25273)
*Zhao Song,Lichen Zhang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了Metropolis-Adjusted Dikin Walks的统一分析框架,通过保留提议行列式与反向二次型的完整耦合,实现精确对消,从而分别对多面体和半正定规划锥（spectrahedra）上的log-det游走证明了维数平方量级的混合时间,并给出了可实现的精确算术复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Dikin walk分析（包括[Laddha-Lee-Vempala 2020]和[Gu et al. 2024]）在控制Metropolis过滤中的反向局部二次型时存在已知的证明缺陷（Kook指出的d⁹ᐟ⁴上界是此前最完整的）。本文旨在修复这些缺陷,为多面体恢复d²上界,为spectrahedra建立显式依赖矩阵杠杆算子的新上界,同时解决从精确度量到可实现度量的转移问题。

Method: 核心方法是在Metropolis比率中保留完整的对数比:将提议的log det项和反向二次型的"未中心化均值"项同时保留,通过精确代数恒等式（Lemma 4.4与5.3）展示二者恰好对消,从而将剩余项控制为关于Gauss变量的中心化波动。多面体部分依赖Lee-Sidford度量与Lewis权重的行依赖Hessian收缩（Lemma 4.15,新提出的关键技术）；spectrahedra部分使用非交换迹多项式的"配对四迹估计"(Lemma 5.8)与杠杆控制的谱管。两者共享"接受→混合"的约简,并通过提议比较论证与TensorSRHT双种子Metropolis框架,从精确度量转移到可实现度量。

Result: 多面体情形:在n个不等式、凸L-Lipschitz势函数下,正则化Lee-Sidford游走从w-热启动达到总变距离δ的混合步数为Õ((d²+dL²R²)log(w/δ))。Spectrahedra情形:n×n矩阵块的log-det游走混合步数为Õ((ψ*⁺·nd+dL²R²)log(w/δ)),其中ψ*⁺是白化矩阵杠杆算子的谱范数上界,选取κ=Õ(ψ*⁺)。同时证明箭头spectrahedron的接受率障碍表明杠杆缩放是必要的。算术复杂度:多面体Õ((d²+dL²R²)(nd^ω⁻¹+d^ω)log(ew/δ))次运算;spectrahedra在给定ψ̂≥ψ*⁺下Õ((ψ̂nd+dL²R²)(n^ω+n²d³ω⁻⁵)log(ew/δ))次运算。

Conclusion: 通过保留完整Metropolis对数比中的det-quadratic精确对消,可以避免证明平均自协和性所需的强Lewis权重输入,从而同时恢复多面体维数平方混合并为spectrahedra建立新的显示依赖杠杆算子的混合界。框架自然统一了两类凸集的接受-混合约简,并通过双种子随机化策略实现可精确可逆的可执行链。论文同时明确了已知缺陷结果的具体位置以及本文证明的范围。

Abstract: We analyze exact-metric, Metropolis-adjusted Dikin walks by keeping the proposal determinant and reverse quadratic form together. Their leading uncentered terms cancel in the complete logarithmic acceptance ratio, leaving centered fluctuations that can be controlled with second-order tools. For a polytope given by $n$ inequalities and a convex $L$-Lipschitz potential, this yields warm-start mixing in $\widetilde O((d^{2}+dL^{2}R^{2})\log(w/\delta))$ steps for the regularized Lee--Sidford walk. For a spectrahedron with $n\times n$ blocks, the log-det walk mixes in $\widetilde O((\psi^\star nd+dL^{2}R^{2})\log(w/\delta))$ steps, where $\psi^\star$ measures matrix leverage. The two analyses share an acceptance-to-mixing reduction. A proposal-comparison argument transfers the polytope bound to an appropriately padded $O(1/d)$-accurate metric computed from high-precision Lewis weights. For spectrahedra, given $\widehat\psi\ge\psi^\star$, a direct-or-two-seed TensorSRHT construction gives an exact-arithmetic implementation with $\psi^\star$ replaced by $\widehat\psi$ in the mixing bound.

</details>


### [90] [Paging with Per-Replacement Maximum Delay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25290)
*Tianhang Lu,Runtian Ren,Shengcai Liu*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究"按替换最大延迟"分页模型,即缺失页在被加载前可保持挂起状态,代价为1(移动)+该页最早挂起请求的等待时长(而非重复请求数)。主要结论是:在线竞争的 Θ(k) 与 Θ(H_k) 经典层级在等权重下得以保持,但离线 Belady 最远下次使用规则可能失败;权重分散时则出现新的 Ω(√ρ) 竞争下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典分页中每次缺失立即触发替换,而现代非阻塞存储系统可将同一页的并发缺失合并,何时提交挂起的填充成为新的调度决策。论文将这一设计抽象为按替换最大延迟模型,系统需同时决定何时服务及淘汰哪个缓存页,旨在回答"分页的算法结构在允许等待后还剩多少"。

Method: 三种核心技术:(1) 持有恒等式 Delay=∫|B(t)|dt 将页面挂起义务而非请求质量计入代价,使事件层动态规划无需存储请求年龄;(2) 单侧缓存差异势 Φ(S,C)=|S\C| 实现因果懒惰投影,任意策略可因果地转化为非主动策略而不增加移动或延迟;(3) 输入无关时间窗口将原始请求聚合成在缓存与随机选择之前就固定的虚拟分页序列,影子分页在 S 缓存中运行,通过挂起约束投影到物理缓存 C,从而解耦时间选择与淘汰选择。等权重与加权情形下分别证明上下界。

Result: 等权重结果:确定性 (5k+3)-竞争阈值 LRU;随机化 5H_k-竞争(对遗忘对手),均匹配 Ω(k) 与 Ω(H_k) 下界。离线:单洞 O(nk) 精确 DP、固定洞数 XP 精确算法、一般洞数非主动多项式时间 5-近似。但物理延迟最优解可不遵循最远下次使用(三页反例)。加权结果:仍保持 (3ρ+2)-近似、O(ρk) 确定性、O(ρ log k) 随机化上界;但单缓存槽下严格 Ω(√ρ) 随机下界出现,说明权重分散会进入严格竞争比。一般度量 k-服务器上通过最小完美匹配势得到 α(1+4Γ_X) 竞争转移,但比例依赖 Γ_X 无法消除。

Conclusion: 最大延迟以非对称方式保留分页结构:在线竞争层级 Θ(k) 与 Θ(H_k) 在等权重下比经典离线最优性更稳健,但物理 Belady 规则可失败,重复请求从紧迫度计数中消失而缓存状态保持不变;权重分散引入新的计时不确定性,即使 k=1 也产生 Ω(√ρ) 下界。四个开放问题:任意洞数的精确复杂度、改善常数 5 与 (3ρ+2)、闭合权重差距、影子缓存在随机算法中的必要性。一般度量方向需要多尺度计时与几何感知的服务器分配。

Abstract: Classical paging couples every miss to an immediate replacement. We ask what remains of its algorithmic structure when a miss may wait. In our per-replacement maximum-delay model, loading a pending page costs one unit of movement plus the age of its oldest outstanding request and clears the whole page-specific episode. Equivalently, the instantaneous holding rate is the number of pending pages, rather than the number of pending requests. The classical competitive hierarchy survives this change. For cache size $k$, we give a deterministic $(5k+3)$-competitive threshold-LRU algorithm and a randomized $5H_k$-competitive algorithm against an oblivious adversary; classical lower-bound instances give matching $\Omega(k)$ and $\Omega(H_k)$ orders. The randomized algorithm uses cache-independent temporal windows to create an ordinary-paging sequence fixed before any random choices; a shadow paging algorithm is then projected onto nonproactive physical replacements. The offline picture is less classical. We give an exact $O(nk)$ dynamic program with one hole, an exact configuration dynamic program for a fixed number of holes, and a deterministic nonproactive polynomial-time $5$-approximation without fixing that number. Yet farthest-next-use victim selection can be suboptimal in the physical delayed problem already with three pages.

</details>


### [91] [A Spectral Local-to-Global Principle for Spin Systems on Graphs with Girth At Least Five](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25491)
*Xiaoyu Chen,Kuikui Liu*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文证明了对围长至少为5、最大度Δ=Ω(1)的图，当q≥(1+δ)Δ时，q-着色的Glauber动力学快速混合；该结果可推广至满足局部谱收缩条件的一般多自旋系统和反铁磁Potts模型，核心工具是一种新的围长5图谱局部到整体原理及星图上Glauber动力学的傅里叶分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对接近最大度阈值（即q略大于Δ）的图上着色采样问题，缩小对围长的要求，并扩展至一般多自旋系统，绕开已有trickle-down与Weitz自避行走树方法的限制。

Method: 利用Bochner型恒等式将全局谱隙归约为星图局部谱隙；通过定义α-边际界与ε-收缩条件得到局部谱收缩，并在星图上引入正交Hoeffding展开与Schur补方法完成Poincaré不等式证明；最后将抽象结果实例化到反铁磁Potts模型。

Result: 在围长≥5、Δ足够大、q≥(1+δ)Δ下，Glauber动力学谱隙为Ω(1/n)，混合时间为O(n²logq+nlog(1/ε))；对反铁磁Potts模型，条件推广为q≥(1+δ)(1-β)Δ。该结果相比此前Jain-Mizgerd-Vigoda的围长≥11工作，显著降低围长要求。

Conclusion: 新提出的局部到整体谱原理和星图傅里叶分析构成核心方法论贡献，能自然绕开自避行走树限制，应用于一大类多自旋系统；论文明确指出这些主要思路在与GPT-5.6 Sol Ultra的多次交互中发展形成，作者对全部内容负责。

Abstract: It is proved that, for every $\delta \in (0,1)$, the Glauber dynamics for the uniform distribution on proper $q$-colorings is rapidly mixing when $q \geq (1+\delta)\Delta$ and the underlying graph has girth at least $5$ and maximum degree $\Delta = \Omega_\delta(1)$. This result also extends to general multi-spin systems satisfying a $\textit{local spectral contraction}$ condition, including the anti-ferromagnetic Potts model with $q\geq (1+\delta)(1-\beta)\Delta$. These results are achieved by a new spectral local-to-global principle on graphs with girth at least five for general multi-spin systems, and a novel Fourier analysis for Glauber dynamics on a star. The main ideas behind all the proofs were developed through several rounds of interaction with GPT-5.6 Sol Ultra.

</details>


### [92] [Not All Degree Constraints Are Created Equal when Computing Spanning Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25530)
*Narek Bojikian,Alexander Firbas,Robert Ganian,Hung P. Hoang,Krisztina Szilagyi*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了带局部度约束的最小生成树问题（Set of Degrees MST、Bounded Degree MST、Specified Degree MST）在更强的结构参数（特别是treedepth）下的参数化复杂性。之前的工作（ICALP 2026）表明这三个问题在标准结构参数下具有相同的参数化复杂性，但本文证明在有界treedepth图上，三者的复杂性图景出现了根本性的分化：Bounded Degree MST和Specified Degree MST关于treedepth是FPT的，而Set of Degrees MST即使加上反馈顶点数的复合参数仍然是W[1]-困难的。这一分歧是treedepth特有的——在顶点覆盖数参数下三者都是FPT的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作（Bojikian等人，ICALP 2026）已证明三种度约束最小生成树问题在树宽、路径宽、团宽等标准结构参数下具有完全相同的参数化复杂性。然而，在更严格的参数化（如treedepth）下，这三种问题是否仍然保持相同的复杂性？本文旨在探究这一问题，揭示度约束类型（单一度、度上界、可选度集合）在更严格参数化下的本质差异。

Method: 本文采用了两种主要方法：（1）对Bounded Degree MST关于treedepth设计了基于整数线性规划（ILP）的FPT算法，将问题编码为具有有界系数和对偶图有界treedepth的ILP，借助Koutecký-Levin-Onn的算法求解；（2）对Set of Degrees MST通过从多维子集和问题（SMPSS）进行归约，构造精巧的"门控"小工具（selection gadget），证明其W[1]-困难性；（3）对Specified Degree MST利用Cygan-Kratsch-Nederlof的2-标签小工具进行SMPSS归约，证明关于到常数路径宽度图的删除距离的W[1]-困难性；（4）对Set of Degrees MST关于顶点覆盖数通过归约到广义B-匹配问题（利用Gutin等人的算法）证明FPT可解。

Result: 主要结果包括：（1）Theorem 1：Bounded Degree MST关于treedepth是FPT的；（2）Theorem 2：Set of Degrees MST关于treedepth加反馈顶点数是W[1]-困难的，即使在无权图中且每个顶点的可选度数集合大小不超过2；（3）Theorem 3：在无权图中，Set of Degrees MST关于顶点覆盖数是FPT的；（4）Theorem 4：Specified Degree MST关于到路径宽度≤4图的顶点删除距离是W[1]-困难的。这些结果表明，度约束类型在treedepth参数下确实有本质区别，而这一分歧是treedepth特有的。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了三种度约束最小生成树问题在更强的结构参数化下的复杂性分化，明确指出treedepth是导致三种问题行为差异的关键参数。Bounded Degree MST和Specified Degree MST在treedepth下保持FPT，但Set of Degrees MST即使在几乎为树的图上仍然是W[1]-困难的。而在更严格的顶点覆盖数参数下，三者又趋于一致。开放问题包括：Set of Degrees MST在带权图中关于顶点覆盖数是否FPT（可能依赖于加权广义B-匹配问题的解决）。

Abstract: We study the computation of minimum spanning trees subject to local degree constraints. Recent work (ICALP 2026) established that three natural formalizations of this problem share the exact same parameterized complexity under standard structural graph parameters, including treewidth, pathwidth and clique-width. This applies to the cases where every vertex has a single target degree (Specified Degree MST), or a degree upper bound (Bounded Degree MST), or is equipped with a set of admissible degrees (Set of Degrees MST). In this paper, we investigate these problems under more restrictive parameterizations and reveal that their complexity landscapes fundamentally diverge on bounded-treedepth graphs. Specifically, we prove that the former two problems are fixed-parameter tractable when parameterized by the treedepth of the input graph. In sharp contrast, we show that Set of Degrees MST remains W[1]-hard parameterized by treedepth, even when combined with the feedback vertex number (i.e., deletion distance to treewidth $1$). Finally, we show that this divergence seems to be specific to treedepth: we exclude an analogous W[1]-hardness result for Set of Degrees MST w.r.t. the vertex cover number and also rule out fixed-parameter algorithms for the former two problems w.r.t. deletion distance to constant pathwidth.

</details>


### [93] [Partially-Dynamic All-Pairs Maxflow and Effective Resistance via Stable Sparsifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26037)
*Gramoz Goranci,Rasmus Kyng,Maximilian Probst Gutenberg,Yibin Zhao,Gernot Zöcklein*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对部分动态图（仅插入或仅删除边），提出了一种维护任意顶点对最大流值和有效电阻近似的数据结构。对于密集图，更新时间为 $\widetilde{O}_\varepsilon(n^2)$，查询时间为 $\widetilde{O}_\varepsilon(1)$，达到了近最优。核心思想是利用"稳定原则"（stability principle）：通过累积的 leverage score，将更新序列划分为 $\widetilde{O}(n/\varepsilon)$ 个 epoch，每个 epoch 内的图谱近似于 epoch 起始图，从而每个 epoch 仅需重新计算一次谱稀疏化子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态图上精确最大流维护存在强计算下界（基于 OMv 猜想），而现有部分动态场景下的 $(1\pm\varepsilon)$-近似算法在稠密图中仍有改进空间。此前最好的基线方法依赖隐式动态稀疏化子，达到 $\widetilde{O}_\varepsilon(n)$ 的摊销更新时间。对任意对（all-pairs）查询的近似最大流和有效电阻问题在部分动态图上尚未有突破。

Method: 方法的核心是"稳定原则"：利用部分动态图的单调性，监控更新边的累积 leverage score。当累积 leverage score 超过 $\varepsilon/20$ 阈值时启动新 epoch。利用 leverage score 总和为 $O(n\log n)$ 的性质，可证明 epoch 数为 $\widetilde{O}(n/\varepsilon)$。每个 epoch 内所有图版本互为 $(1\pm O(\varepsilon))$-谱近似，因此只需在 epoch 边界重新采样一次谱稀疏化子。具体算法（MaintainSparsifier）维护隐式采样器（ImplicitSampler），并在每次更新时估计 leverage score，仅在累积值超阈值时调用 ReturnSparsifier。最后结合静态全对最大流预言机（来自 [21,20]）和静态全对有效电阻预言机（基于 Spielman-Srivastava 的 JL 引理方法）获得最终结果。

Result: 理论结果：维护 $(1\pm\varepsilon)$-近似的全对最大流和全对有效电阻预言机，查询时间 $\widetilde{O}(1/\varepsilon^2)$（最坏情况），总更新时间 $\widetilde{O}(n^2/\varepsilon^7)$。对于 $m = \Theta(n^2)$ 的稠密图，更新时间为 $\widetilde{O}_\varepsilon(n^2)$，接近最优（信息论下界为 $\Omega(n^2)$）。算法以高概率在自适应对抗下成立。相比之前的 $\widetilde{O}_\varepsilon(mn)$ 基线，在 $m = \omega(n)$ 时有显著改进。这是首次在部分动态图上打破 $\widetilde{O}_\varepsilon(n)$ 的更新时间下限，且支持自适应对抗。

Conclusion: 本文成功将部分动态图上全对最大流和有效电阻的更新时间从 $\widetilde{O}_\varepsilon(mn)$ 改进到 $\widetilde{O}_\varepsilon(n^2)$。核心贡献在于发现了部分动态图的稳定性原则：单调更新序列可被划分为 $\widetilde{O}(n/\varepsilon)$ 个 epoch，每个 epoch 内图的谱变化有界。该方法简洁优雅，且在稠密图上近最优。遗留问题是改善对 $\varepsilon^{-1}$ 的依赖（目前为 $\varepsilon^{-7}$，高于固定对算法的依赖）。

Abstract: We give a randomized data structure for undirected weighted graphs that are partially dynamic, i.e., that undergo either only edge insertions or only edge deletions. The data structure maintains $(1\pm\epsilon)$-approximations to the maxflow value and effective resistance between any queried pair of vertices, with total update time $\widetilde{O}_{\epsilon}(n^2)$ and worst-case query time $\widetilde{O}_{\epsilon}(1)$. Thus, for dense graphs where $m = \Omega(n^2)$, our guarantees are near-optimal. Our algorithms succeed with high probability against an adaptive adversary. Our result follows from a simple stability principle for partially dynamic graphs. We show how to partition an online sequence of $m$ updates into $\widetilde{O}(n/\epsilon)$ epochs such that every graph within an epoch is a $(1\pm O(\epsilon))$-spectral approximation of the graph at the beginning of the epoch. The epochs are determined by the cumulative leverage score of the updated edges: small leverage-score mass implies small spectral change, while the total leverage-score mass over a monotone update sequence is $\widetilde{O}(n)$. Consequently, a spectral sparsifier needs to be recomputed only once per epoch. Applying known static all-pairs maxflow and effective-resistance oracles to these sparsifiers then yields the result.

</details>


### [94] [Nearly Optimal Strong Coresets for $\ell_p$ Subspace Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26047)
*Honghao Lin,Vahab Mirrokni,David P. Woodruff*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了ℓ_p子空间逼近问题的强核集（strong row coreset），将Woodruff-Yasuda (FOCS 2025) 的核集大小从 ε^{-4/p}（p<2时）和 ε^{-p}（p>2时）分别改进为 ε^{-2}，在1≤p<2时达到已知采样下界（对数因子内），并保持接近输入稀疏度的运行时间。技术核心在p<2时结合双标准低秩分解、Lewis权重采样和与输出维度无关的empirical-process界；在p>2时通过保留截断项的精细分析改进行数递推。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子空间逼近是降维和鲁棒低秩逼近的基础原语，除p=2外计算困难，故数据缩减至关重要。已有Woodruff-Yasuda框架解决了维度独立性和近似最优秩依赖k，但精度依赖ε仍有差距：1≤p<2时上界ε^{-4/p}与已知采样下界Ω̃(kε^{-2})相差多项式因子；p>2时上界ε^{-p}是否必要未知。本文旨在确定维度无关强行核集关于ε的正确依赖。

Method: 对于1≤p<2：(1)利用Woodruff-Yasuda双标准低秩子空间F_0得到低秩近似B+E，满足rank(B)=Õ(k)且‖E‖_{p,2}^p≤Γ·OPT；(2)构造结合Lewis权重w_i和残差分数ρ_i的平衡分布q_i≥w_i/(k+r)+ρ_i；(3)将归一化查询损失Z_F(i)分解为四个标量坐标：低秩幂T_F、平行残差幂V_F、带符号交互项H_F、正交残差幂W_F^⊥，利用几何恒等式和Lipschitz映射恢复；(4)分别证明四类的empirical complexity上界为Õ(√((k+r)/m))，通过Dudley界和向量收缩组合，得到m=Õ(kε^{-2})样本即可达到同时一致近似。对于p>2：保留Woodruff-Yasuda单轮采样概率q_i=min{1, N^{p/2-1}τ_i^{p/2}/α}，利用关键点态不等式min{1, cτ_i^{p/2}}≤c^{2/p}τ_i保持截断项，通过trace界∑τ_i≤2k得到行数期望E[nnz(S)]≤2kα^{-2/p}N^{1-2/p}，行数递推N_t≲kα^{-2/p}N_{t-1}^{1-2/p}的不动点为k^{p/2}α^{-1}，代入α=Θ̃(ε^2)得行数Õ(k^{p/2}/ε^2)。最后用Woodruff-Yasuda预稀疏化去除log n因子。

Result: 主要定理：定理1.1（1≤p<2）：运行时间Õ_p(nnz(A)+d^ω+kε^{-2})，核集大小C_p·k·ε^{-2}·(log(C_p k/ε))^{C_p}，当k+1≥C log(1/ε)时匹配Li-Wang-Woodruff的Ω̃_p(kε^{-2})下界。定理1.2（p>2）：运行时间Õ_p(nnz(A)+d^ω)，核集大小(C_p k^{p/2}/ε^2)·(log(C_p k/(εδ)))^{p+5}，将ε依赖从ε^{-p}改进为ε^{-2}，对数指数从O(p^2)降至O(p)。合并两定理得维度无关界Õ_p(k^{max{1,p/2}}·ε^{-2})。

Conclusion: 本文确定了ℓ_p子空间逼近维度无关强核集关于精度参数ε的最优依赖为ε^{-2}，关闭了1≤p<2时与已知下界的差距，并将p>2时的精度依赖从ε^{-p}改进为ε^{-2}。关键技术创新在于：低秩分解配合平衡敏感度采样、四坐标分解下的dimension-independent uniform convergence分析、以及保留截断项的精细行数递推。开放问题：p>2时现有下界Ω̃(k^{p/2}/ε+k/ε^2)与本文上界Õ(k^{p/2}/ε^2)作为k和ε的联合函数仍不匹配，k和精度参数之间是否存在不可避免的相互作用尚待研究。

Abstract: We study strong coresets for $\ell_p$ subspace approximation. Given $\A\in\R^{n\times d}$, the goal is to sample and rescale a small number of its rows to form $\Smat\A$ such that \[ \norm{\Smat\A(\I-\Pmat_F)}_{p,2}^p =(1\pm\eps)\norm{\A(\I-\Pmat_F)}_{p,2}^p \] simultaneously for every subspace $F\subseteq\R^d$ of dimension at most $k$, where $\Pmat_F$ is the orthogonal projector onto $F$. Woodruff and Yasuda (FOCS 2025)~\cite{WY25} obtained coreset sizes $\wtO_p(k\eps^{-4/p})$ for $1\leq p<2$ and $\wtO_p(k^{p/2}\eps^{-p})$ for $p>2$. We improve these bounds to $\wtO_p(k\eps^{-2})$ and $\wtO_p(k^{p/2}\eps^{-2})$, respectively. For $1\leq p<2$, our algorithm runs in $\wtO_p(\nnz(\A)+d^\omega+k\eps^{-2})$ time. The resulting coreset size matches the known sampling lower bound~\cite{LWW21} up to logarithmic factors when $k+1\geq C\log(1/\eps)$ for an absolute constant $C$. For $p>2$, our algorithm runs in $\wtO_p(\nnz(\A)+d^\omega)$ time, matching the running time of the Woodruff--Yasuda framework. We use different techniques in the two regimes. For $1\leq p<2$, we combine a bicriteria low-rank split with Lewis-weight sampling and empirical-process bounds independent of the output dimension. For $p>2$, we give a sharper analysis of the Woodruff--Yasuda construction. By retaining the truncation in its sampling probabilities throughout the row-count recurrence, we show that it achieves the improved $\eps^{-2}$ dependence.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [95] [Evaluating and Preventing Security Smells in AI-Generated Ansible Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24962)
*Pandu Ranga Reddy Konala,Vimal Kumar,David Bainbridge,Junaid Haseeb*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文针对16个AI模型生成的Ansible IaC代码进行安全性评估，发现默认情况下所有模型均产生包含安全异味（security smells）的代码，且表现不如人类开发者。通过扩展CO-STAR提示框架将Ansible最佳实践与CIS基准嵌入提示中，4个模型成功生成合规代码，最佳模型达到95%-100%的CIS合规率，显著优于人类代码的23%-43%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码助手（如Cursor、Bolt）被广泛用于生成基础设施即代码（IaC），但现有研究表明45%-62%的AI生成代码包含漏洞，且68%的云安全事件源于IaC配置错误。基础设施代码中的安全异味（如硬编码凭据）会直接传播到部署系统，造成不安全的运行环境。然而现有研究要么聚焦于应用程序代码安全而非基础设施代码，要么采用检测式方法（静态分析工具在代码入库后识别问题），缺乏针对AI生成IaC安全性的实证研究，且无法在合成阶段预防安全异味。

Method: 方法包含两个阶段：（1）基线评估阶段——使用零样本提示（zero-shot prompt）对16个AI模型（来自Anthropic、OpenAI、Google、Meta、DeepSeek等）进行测试，生成135个Apache Tomcat 10的Ansible角色，并与Ansible Galaxy中的119个人类编写角色进行对比，通过IaC质量框架（9维评分）进行代码质量分析；（2）合规引导生成阶段——扩展CO-STAR提示框架，新增QUALITY和COMPLIANCE两个类别，将Ansible最佳实践和CIS Level 1基准作为A/B/C优先级约束嵌入提示，采用零样本单轮交互方式，在278个角色（Tomcat 135个 + MongoDB 143个）上评估，通过CIS-CAT Pro进行部署后合规验证。

Result: 基线评估显示16个AI模型全部生成包含安全异味（如CWE-798硬编码凭据、CWE-755错误处理缺失、CWE-276权限配置错误）的代码，无一在安全和质量维度上同时超越人类实现。Claude Opus 4（5.07质量分）虽超越质量均值但安全分仅0.6136，Perplexity Sonar（0.669安全分）虽超越安全均值但质量分仅3.88。使用扩展CO-STAR框架后，仅4个模型（Claude Opus 4、Claude Sonnet 3.7、Pixeltral Large、Gemini 2.5 Pro）成功生成合规代码：Claude Opus 4实现95.2%-100%的CIS合规率（Tomcat 95.2%，MongoDB 100%），整体代码质量提升19%-49%，且所有成功角色消除了基线阶段发现的所有安全异味。12个模型失败并非因编码能力不足（部分高分模型如Claude Sonnet 4 SWE-bench 80.2%同样失败），而是因无法遵循多约束指令。人类编写角色合规率仅23.8%（Tomcat）和42.85%（MongoDB）。

Conclusion: 论文得出三项主要结论：（1）AI生成IaC的默认安全态势不足，所有16个模型均产生包含安全异味的代码；（2）12/16模型失败源于多约束指令遵循能力限制，而非编码能力；（3）对于具备指令遵循能力的模型，结构化提示可使最佳模型实现95%-100%的CIS合规率，远超人类代码的23%-43%。研究表明合规代码生成依赖于训练方法而非架构特征（所有成功模型均为闭源），且该方法可通过系统提示直接部署，无需模型重训练。未来工作将扩展至CIS IG2/IG3、STIG/PCI-DSS等替代框架，以及Terraform/Puppet等技术，并探索LoRA微调等方法以解决指令遵循局限性。

Abstract: AI coding assistants generate Infrastructure as Code, yet no work has examined whether this code meets security requirements. This matters because security smells in infrastructure code propagate to deployed systems, producing infrastructure that is insecure and untrustworthy. We evaluate 16 AI models generating Ansible roles for Apache Tomcat v10 and MongoDB v7, analysing 278 Ansible roles against CIS benchmarks. Without security guidance, all 16 AI models produced code containing security smells, resulting in vulnerable infrastructure that fails compliance verification and underperforms code written by human developers. We introduce an approach integrating Ansible best practices and CIS benchmarks into prompts through an extended CO-STAR framework, enabling security smell prevention during synthesis rather than detection after deployment. When this approach is applied, 4 out of 16 models generate compliant code, with the leading model achieving 95%-100% CIS compliance, a fourfold improvement over humans at 23%-43%, with overall code quality improving by 19%-49%. The remaining 12 models fail not because they cannot generate code but because they cannot follow instructions with multiple constraints. For capable models, the approach requires no retraining and can be adopted through system prompts.

</details>


### [96] [ARISMA: Guidelines for AI- and LLM-Assisted Systematic Reviews, Scoping Reviews, and Mapping Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25050)
*Mahyar Tourchi Moghaddam,Mina Alipour*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ARISMA是一份针对AI/LLM辅助系统综述、范围综述和映射研究的治理与报告指南。它将AI定位为受检查、有基准验证、可日志记录、可逆的辅助工具，而非自主审稿者，建立了一个涵盖生命周期分类法、流程指导、治理与出处模型、工具支持框架、AI集成报告清单和验证矩阵的端到端框架。其核心原则是所有重大科学决策必须保持人类可解释、可审计和可问责。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI/ML/LLM快速渗透到证据综合的各个环节（查询制定、筛选、提取、分类、评估支持和报告），而现有PRISMA系列标准无法回答AI使用何时具有方法论正当性、哪些任务适合辅助自动化、哪些输出需要基准验证、哪些决策需要强制人类签署以及AI参与应如何报告这五个核心问题。同时AI辅助综述的实证证据不均衡、任务依赖性强，不足以证明无约束自动化的合理性。这构成了将AI视为方法论伦理问题的紧迫需求。

Method: 通过文献知情的设计过程，结合既有证据综合指南和近期AI辅助综述方法的实证/方法论文献形成初步草案；通过与21名证据综合领域研究者和信息专家进行45-60分钟结构化视频咨询（基于问题中心专家访谈法），围绕生命周期覆盖、AI使用边界、治理检查点、检查清单清晰度和图表准确性五个领域获取反馈；进行迭代修正后形成最终框架。

Result: 产出了包含六大组成部分的框架：(1) 覆盖六大阶段（框架构建、方案化、检索、筛选与富集、证据结构化、综合与报告）的端到端AI角色分类法；(2) 区分辅助性、裁决性和生成性AI使用的层级；(3) 三级信任筛选操作规则（低信任排名、中等信任推荐排除、高后果作为第二审稿人）；(4) 协议优先的治理循环；(5) 包含验证矩阵的AI感知证据流和出处链；(6) AI集成报告清单（Table 1）和验证矩阵（Table 2）。还涵盖法律、隐私、基础设施和可持续性考量，并推荐了具体的工具类别。

Conclusion: ARISMA将AI整合重新定义为治理问题而非生产力问题。核心方法论问题不是AI能否加速综述工作流，而是其对证据选择、解释和综合的影响是否保持透明、可审计和科学可控。框架将负责任的方法论创新与可重现性、可解释性和可问责性相结合，为审查团队、同行评审、编辑和政策制定者提供了评估AI辅助证据综合是否仍处于科学监督下的共同基础。当前工作涉及将ARISMA开发为开源工具。

Abstract: Systematic reviews, scoping reviews, mapping studies, and related evidence syntheses are increasingly difficult to conduct with fully manual workflows as search volumes, update cycles, and synthesis requirements continue to expand. At the same time, artificial intelligence, machine learning, and large language models are rapidly entering review practice across query formulation, screening, extraction, categorization, appraisal support, and reporting. Yet the empirical evidence remains uneven, task-dependent, and insufficient to justify unconstrained automation. Existing standards such as PRISMA 2020, PRISMA-S, PRISMA-ScR, PRISMA-P, PRESS, and SWiM remain essential, but none provides an end-to-end operational standard for when AI use is methodologically appropriate, how it should be validated, which review decisions must remain human-led, and how AI involvement should be reported so that readers can audit it. This paper proposes ARISMA, an AI Reporting and Integration standard for Systematic Methods and Analysis. ARISMA treats AI as an inspected, benchmarked, logged, and reversible assistant rather than an autonomous reviewer. It is built around one governing principle: every consequential scientific decision must remain human-interpretable, human-auditable, and human-accountable. The paper contributes a lifecycle taxonomy, process guidance, stepwise recommendations across the review pipeline, a governance and provenance model, a tool-support framework, an AI-integrated reporting checklist, and a validation matrix. It also addresses legal, privacy, infrastructure, and sustainability considerations. The framework was iteratively refined through structured expert consultation. The result is a practical and auditable guideline for responsible AI-assisted evidence synthesis.

</details>


### [97] [SPECMINE: A Large-Scale Corpus of Spec-Driven Development Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25202)
*Shyam Agarwal,Bogdan Vasilescu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文介绍并发布 SpecMine v1.0：一个面向 AI 编码智能体时代的大规模规格驱动开发（SDD）工件语料库。数据集从公开 GitHub 仓库采集，包含 470,795 个规范文件（覆盖 17 种具名工具和 73,030 个仓库）、独立采集的 98,574 个 Kiro 工件、5,992 个修改规范的 PR 及其变更集，以及 2,421,323 条带类型和来源的追踪引用。配套数据提供完整提交历史、39 项文档结构特征、工具归属、OpenSpec 任务到代码的解析结果，以及 57 张关系表/815 个字段的 MySQL、CSV、Parquet 和 JSONL 发布格式。核心价值是把开发意图层（规范文档）与其 PR、任务、代码文件和生命周期历史连接起来，支持研究 SDD 工具扩散、规范质量、规范—代码演化、人机协作和废弃模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
