Motivation: SDD 正在把结构化自然语言规范（通常由 AI 起草、开发者整理）变成 AI 编码智能体实现代码的主要输入；相关工具自 2025 年快速增长，但此前缺少能跨工具识别规范、标注生成工具、连接规范与代码、PR、问题及提交历史的大规模公开数据集。作者因此希望补足 AI 辅助开发研究中偏重模型输出（对话、PR）的“意图层”，支持软件工程实证研究。

Method: 数据通过 GitHub code search 与 REST API 采集。广谱普查以 spec.md/specs.md 文件名为入口，按文件大小自适应分区规避 GitHub 每次 1,000 条查询上限，过滤包缓存、依赖树和 2024 年以前首次提交的文件，并依据各工具生成的目录/路径指纹标注 17 种工具；Kiro 因采用 .kiro/specs/{requirements,design,tasks}.md 布局而单独普查。作者以文件内容、提交历史、OpenSpec tasks.md、提交消息和仓库 Git tree 构建类型化追踪索引，并在固定提交快照上解析 OpenSpec 任务到代码路径及其是否完成、目标文件是否存在。对 11 种工作流清晰的工具，作者在至少 10 stars 的仓库中基于规范提交关联 PR 采集规范触及 PR 及其完整变更集；结构分析从 Markdown 提取 39 项特征。所有流程包含去重、幂等性、确定性、过滤审计和抽样/截断边界说明。

Result: SpecMine v1.0（2026 年 7 月快照）包含 470,795 个规范文件，分布 73,030 个仓库、44,521 个所有者和 17 种具名工具；另有 Kiro 的 98,574 个工件（12,910 个仓库）。规范关联 780,335 条文件级提交，468,307 个文件含 39 项结构特征；OpenSpec 还包含 266,230 个变更工件和 435,401 条任务到代码的已解析引用。PR 层为 11 种工具采集了 5,992 个规范触及 PR，覆盖 581 个仓库，其中 81.2% 同时修改规范目录外的代码；总计 348,141 条逐文件变更记录。追踪索引有 2,421,323 条带类型和来源的引用：代码 1,278,828、兄弟文档 863,445、PR 151,563、引用 62,267、分支 42,970、问题 22,139，另有 111 条锚点。广谱普查发现 99.7% 规范首次提交于 2025 年及以后、92% 在 2026 年；工具规模以 OpenSpec（274,955 文件/8,926 仓库）、Spec Kit（54,640/10,619）和 caffeine.ai（14,262/13,749）领先，但 caffeine.ai 含大量自动生成应用，应谨慎解释。数据集未压缩约 14.7 GB，发布 57 张表、815 个字段，并提供 MySQL dump、CSV/Parquet、JSONL、Hugging Face 镜像、GitHub schema/加载脚本和 500 仓库样本。

Conclusion: SpecMine 将规范确立为可与代码并列研究的一等软件工件，首次提供跨 18 种 SDD 工具（17 种具名工具加 Kiro）的公开大规模数据层，并能从规范追踪到代码、PR、问题、分支、任务状态和演化历史。作者强调数据仍有边界：基于文件名的普查可能遗漏可达集合中的部分匹配；PR 层是至少 10 stars 仓库的子样本，且只能观察规范与代码同 PR 的共变，不能证明实现关系；没有触碰规范的实现 PR 也不会进入该层。作者建议研究者在论文中明确过滤器、成熟度标准与外推限制，并可利用完整变更集、提交历史和类型化链接替换或强化“同 PR 实现”启发式。总体而言，SpecMine 为研究 SDD 的采用扩散、规范结构与质量、代码实现、漂移和人机协作提供了可复现、可扩展且带审计信息的基础设施。

Abstract: Spec-Driven Development (SDD) is a fast-emerging practice in which a structured natural-language specification, written by a developer, or (more often) drafted by an AI tool and then curated by the developer, drives an AI coding agent's implementation. A wave of tooling (GitHub Spec Kit [3], OpenSpec [4], AWS Kiro [5], and dozens of others) has appeared since 2025, yet the artifacts these tools produce have never been studied at scale. We present SPECMINE, a corpus that captures SDD in public GitHub repositories through two censuses: a broad census of this http URL files covering most tools (470,795 files across 73,030 repositories, attributed to 17 named tools), and a Kiro census of its distinct requirements/design/tasks layout (98,574 files across 12,910 repositories). Each spec is enriched with full repository metadata, complete commit history, and parsed document structure. How a spec becomes code is itself an open question, so for 11 tools we sweep every pull request that touches a spec in their repositories with at least ten stars, capturing 5,992 such PRs across 581 repositories with their changesets. That makes the simplest workflow, spec and implementation changing together in one PR, directly observable, and a census-wide index of 2,421,323 typed references (1.28M to code files, 863k to sibling documents, 152k to PRs, 62k refs, 43k branches, 22k issues) gives a second, independent link from spec to code. SPECMINE lets the community study, for the first time, how software is specified in the age of AI agents.

</details>


### [98] [A Few Pages of Markdown: Committed AI Configuration and Lower Quality Cost after Coding-Agent Adoption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25241)
*Yegor Denisov-Blanch,Shyam Agarwal,Pavel Azaletskiy,Hao He,Rylan Schaeffer,Brando Miranda,Bogdan Vasilescu,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 RAMP（Repository AI Maturity Profile），一个基于版本控制工件的四级累积式 AI 实践成熟度模型，并将其应用于分层重估现有 coding agent 采纳面板，发现：在 agent-first 仓库中，未提交 AI 配置（L1）相比已提交配置（L2+）在认知复杂度与静态分析告警上的质量劣化幅度约为 2 倍与 1.7 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 coding agent 文献只报告采纳者的平均效应，掩盖了团队间巨大差异；既有成熟度框架依赖组织自评，无法与代码库层面的质量结果挂钩。因此需要一种基于版本化、客观工件的仓库级 AI 实践成熟度度量，并检验其是否调节 agent 采纳后的质量与速度效应。

Method: 构建四级累积成熟度模型 RAMP（L1 无配置 → L2 上下文规则 → L3 命名 agent/命令 → L4 多 agent 编排），开发三阶段分类流水线（文件名模式 + 路径/内容语义嵌入 + 优先级融合），通过 Guttman scalogram、置换检验、独立人工标注和 LlM 标注对照进行验证。Study 1 用 441 个企业仓库刻画采纳动态；Study 2 把 RAMP 分层应用到 Agarwal et al. (2026) 的 staggered DiD agent 采纳面板（509 个处理仓库），在 agent-first 子样本内比较 L1 与 L2+ 的产出弹性。

Result: RAMP 在 441 仓库中表现为累积量表（Guttman CR=0.997，CS=0.983），仓库级标签在 35 个保留样本上以 97.1% 复现人工标注。Study 1 显示采纳具有累积、前向、一次性特征：73.8% 工件仅提交一次不再修改。Study 2 中 agent 在各成熟度层级都加速开发（提交 +28%-+38%），但在 agent-first 层级，L1 vs L2+ 的认知复杂度增幅为 +52.7% vs +26.7%（≈2×），静态分析告警为 +24.1% vs +14.0%（≈1.7×）。

Conclusion: 编码 agent 的速度收益对实践成熟度稳健，但其质量成本集中在未将 AI 配置纳入版本控制的仓库上；从零配置迈向"几页 rules 与 coding standards"的 L2 是最关键的差距。由于成熟度为观察性变量，工程规训或模型能力差异可能部分解释差异，结果应作假设生成式解读。RAMP 作为可复用测量工具，未来研究可据此对仓库进行 AI 实践分层、采样与过滤。

Abstract: Coding agents increase development velocity but also technical debt. Prior work reports only average effects across adopters, hiding wide differences between teams. We introduce RAMP (Repository AI Maturity Profile), a four-level cumulative maturity model grounded in version-controlled artifacts that teams commit to configure AI tools. RAMP runs from behavioral rules and coding standards through named agent definitions to multi-agent orchestration, with observed practice concentrated in the first three levels. Across 441 repositories the levels behave as a cumulative scale, and independent human annotation reproduces RAMP's repository-level labels on 97% of a held-out sample. Adoption is cumulative, forward-only, and set-and-forget: 73.8% of artifacts are committed once and never modified. Re-estimating an existing agent-adoption panel within each stratum, agents accelerate development regardless of maturity (28-38% more commits), but quality diverges: among agent-first repositories, where the contrast is identified, those without committed AI configuration show roughly twice the increase in cognitive complexity (+53% versus +27%) and 1.7x the increase in static-analysis warnings. Because maturity is observational, correlated engineering discipline or model capability may explain part of the gap; we present these findings as hypothesis-generating and release RAMP as a reusable instrument.

</details>


### [99] [RotDroid: Cross-Orientation State Equivalence Testing for Detecting GUI Rotation Bugs in Android Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25425)
*Mengdi Qin,Bo Jiang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RotDroid是一个针对Android应用的GUI旋转缺陷自动检测框架，通过构建跨方向的语义等效状态对并利用视觉语言模型进行差异检测，在大规模真实应用中发现了94个未知缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Android屏幕旋转会触发Activity销毁-重建流程，容易引入非崩溃型功能缺陷（NCFs），如布局不一致和状态丢失。现有方法存在两大局限：一是缺乏有效的跨方向状态等价性测试预言；二是简单切换方向难以暴露缺陷，因为缺陷常在与用户操作交错时显现。

Method: 方法包含三部分：(1) 经验研究：从6,031个GitHub issue中筛选898个旋转相关缺陷，构建四类缺陷分类法（旋转执行失败34%、布局失败35%、状态恢复失败23%、其他8%）；(2) RotVL模型：基于Qwen3-VL用LoRA微调，先通过自动收集1,818对非缺陷样本+数据增强生成11,233对缺陷样本构建RotBench数据集（含Component Absence、Overlap、Clipping、Orientation Mismatch、State Loss五类），采用粗到细的两阶段指令设计（先检测后分类定位）；(3) RotDroid框架：通过UI Transition Graph（UTG）建模应用行为，利用深度优先搜索提取状态保持动作序列（SPS），在序列中注入旋转动作生成变体序列以构造语义等效的横竖屏状态对，最后用RotVL检测并定位缺陷。

Result: RotVL-8B在RotBench测试集上Bug Detection F1达85.29%，Bug Classification F1达62.68%，Bug Localization (Orientation) F1达79.97%，Center Point Distance 198px，Area Ratio 3.17，均显著优于Qwen3-VL系列（最大235B）和GPT-5.2（p<0.001）。在自然缺陷集上F1为72.32%，同样优于GPT-5.2。在300个开源Android应用中发现72个提交issue（41已确认，29已修复，1被拒绝，30待审），在103个闭源商业应用中发现22个issue（6已确认），包括Dropbox、Google Phone、Samsung Health等过亿安装量应用。

Conclusion: RotDroid通过结合UTG建模、SPS变异和VLM等效性检查，有效检测开源（78个缺陷）和闭源（22个缺陷）应用中的GUI旋转问题，其中47个被开发者确认或修复，证明其在Android旋转测试中的实用价值。

Abstract: Screen rotation is a fundamental interaction in Android applications, but it often introduces non-crashing functional failures (NCFs), such as layout inconsistencies and state loss, which are difficult to detect automatically. A key challenge is the lack of effective test oracles for checking cross-orientation state equivalence between portrait and landscape views. We propose RotDroid, a testing framework for detecting GUI rotation bugs via cross-orientation state equivalence. RotDroid generates and mutates State-Preserving action Sequences (SPS) to construct semantically equivalent GUI states across orientations. To support reliable oracle checking, we build RotBench, a dataset of paired portrait-landscape GUI states, and develop RotVL, a vision-language model fine-tuned for equivalence checking. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets show that RotVL outperforms state-of-the-art models, and RotDroid detects more rotation-induced failures than existing techniques under equal budgets. In large-scale studies on open- and closed-source apps, RotDroid reports 94 previously unknown bugs, with 47 confirmed or fixed by developers, demonstrating its practical effectiveness.

</details>


### [100] [A Hybrid Usability Approach for Rating Evaluation of M-Commerce Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25550)
*Ahmad Ibtisam,Bilal Khan,Arshad Ali*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究针对五款知名M-commerce（移动商务）应用，综合现有可用性模型并提出了一种混合可用性模型，通过前向逐步多元线性回归预测应用评分，并使用PRED(x)和K折交叉验证进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动应用的成功取决于其有用性，而评分是衡量这一点的关键指标。该研究旨在识别对M-commerce应用评分有显著贡献的可用性因素，从而帮助开发者和设计者优化应用以获得更好的用户评价。

Method: 1. 文献综述现有可用性模型并综合其因素；2. 提出包含8个因素（可学性、一致性、人为因素、可沟通性、有效性、可操作性、效率、满意度）的混合可用性模型，每因素含若干标准；3. 对5款应用（daraz, shophive, home shopping, Symbios, yayvo）各收集40位用户的数据；4. 使用前向逐步多元线性回归构建评分预测模型；5. 采用PRED(x)和K折交叉验证方法评估和验证模型。

Result: 基于前向逐步多元线性回归构建了评分预测模型，并通过PRED(x)和K折交叉验证技术对模型进行了评估和验证（具体数值结果在所提供的摘要中未呈现）。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个综合的混合可用性模型框架，并通过实证数据验证了其在M-commerce应用评分预测方面的可行性，有助于理解和提升影响移动商务应用评分的关键可用性因素。

Abstract: The success of any mobile application relies on its usefulness and rating is considered as an important measure in this regard. This research work focuses on identifying usability factors, which contribute significantly towards the rating of M-commerce apps. This work intends to explore existing usability models consisting of different factors along with a set of criteria and evaluate in terms of rating estimation by considering 5 well-known mobile applications, namely (i) daraz, (ii) shophive, (iii) home shopping, (iv) Symbios. (v) yayvo. Then, this work provides a hybrid usability model for rating prediction of M-commerce applications. The initial hybrid usability model comprises of (i) learnability, (ii) consistency, (iii) human factors,(iv)communicativeness,(v)effectiveness, (vi) Operability, (vii) efficiency, (viii) satisfaction. Each factor consists of some criteria. Keeping in view the factors of hybrid usability model, the data was collected from 40 users for each application. Furthermore, Forward Stepwise Multiple Linear Regression based rating prediction model is suggested by analyzing each criterion of all factors of hybrid usability model. Finally, the model is assessed and validated by using PRED(x) and K-fold techniques.

</details>


### [101] [From General Agents to RCA Experts: A Self-Evolving Harness for Root Cause Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25661)
*Haiyu Huang,Jiewei Lyu,Zhihan Jiang,Jinyang Liu,Xiao He,Tieying Zhang,Wu Xiang,Michael R. Lyu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: OpsHarness是首个面向根因分析（RCA）的自演化外部agent harness，通过分层操作知识库、idea-card工具库和双门验证的自演化循环，将通用agent（如Codex、Claude Code）包装为高效的RCA专家，在多个基准和工业部署中显著超越现有专用RCA agent和裸通用agent。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代SRE自动化RCA通常有两种方式：直接使用通用agent或从头构建专用RCA agent。研究发现快速迭代的通用agent已普遍超越专用RCA agent，但仍不足以满足生产需求，差距源于agent通用能力之外的外部适配层——harness。当前LLM agent范式中，harness工程成为决定任务表现的关键，但Ops领域仍缺少外部harness，且现有方法无法从诊断经验中持续自演化。

Method: OpsHarness采用数据平面与控制平面分离的架构。数据平面h=(K,T)包含分层操作知识K=(K0,K1,K2,K3)（通用背景、目标系统profile、从轨迹挖掘的工作流骨架与操作/规则）和idea-card工具库T。控制平面包含setup、diagnose、evolve、verify四个生命周期工作流。自演化通过轨迹挖掘（标记正/负轨迹）、证据挖掘与提案合成（从正确轨迹提取共性模式、从错误轨迹进行对比修正生成原子提案）、双门验证（内门要求源案例严格提升、外门要求保留测试集无回归）实现，仅通过双门验证的提案才会被原子提交，使harness在持续使用中不断积累系统专属诊断经验。

Result: 在OpenRCA（335案例）、RCAEval（270案例）和工业部署数据集上，OpsHarness在四种骨干模型（GPT-5.5、Claude Sonnet 4.6、GLM-5.2、DeepSeek-V4）上均达到最佳性能，平均Final A@1达59.0%，相比裸通用agent提升63.4%，相比专用RCA agent提升4.02倍。自演化使harness在12个诊断窗口中A@1接近翻倍（最终达0.83 vs 不演化0.43），而去掉验证门后性能反而退化（0.33），证明了双门验证防过拟合的有效性。在工业数据集上达到0.74 A@1（vs 裸agent 0.24），开销与裸agent相当（106k tokens/325秒），远低于专用agent（1.7-3.5倍）。

Conclusion: OpsHarness证明了在LLM agent时代，RCA的关键不在于重建agent，而在于构建围绕通用agent的外部harness。通过自演化能力，harness能将诊断经验转化为可复用的专业知识，在不熟悉的系统中逐步积累系统专属经验并持续改进。该工作填补了Ops领域外部harness的空白，为AIOps提供了与快速迭代的通用agent兼容、可持续演化的新范式。

Abstract: Automated root cause analysis (RCA) with large language models (LLMs) has drawn growing attention. Today, SREs typically automate RCA with LLMs in one of two ways: directly using a general-purpose agent (e.g., Codex or Claude Code) for diagnosis, or building a specialized RCA agent from scratch. As mainstream general agents grow more capable and iterate quickly, our quantitative study finds that the former now often surpasses the latter. Its accuracy, however, still falls short of production needs, and this gap stems mainly from the external adaptation layer outside the agent's general capabilities, namely the harness. We therefore argue that LLM-based RCA should focus on this external harness, reusing the strong general capabilities of a modern agent rather than rebuilding an agent from scratch. A key capability of such a harness is to self-evolve, accumulating system-specific experience from past diagnoses so that it gets better the more it is used. We introduce OpsHarness, a self-evolving RCA harness that turns diagnosis experience into reusable expertise. Its data plane combines layered operational knowledge with an idea-card tool library, while its control plane coordinates setup, diagnosis, evolution, and verification. During evolution, OpsHarness contrasts successful and failed trajectories, converts their evidence into atomic proposals, and admits updates only through a dual-gate verification process designed to prevent overfitting and regression. Across two public benchmarks and an industrial deployment, OpsHarness achieves 59.0\% top-1 accuracy, improving over a bare general agent by 63.4\% and over baseline RCA agents by 4.02$\times$.

</details>


### [102] [Secret MCP: Evidence-Bounded and Context-Isolated Design Specification Generation from Web Screenshots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24944)
*Yeongjin Jo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Secret MCP，一个开源本地系统，将多参考截图到代码任务重构为证据约束与上下文隔离的设计规范生成问题，通过逐参考顺序处理、可审计DESIGN_INDEX中间产物和sampler抽象，确保一个参考对应一次请求、一份文档的编排不变量，同时应对MCP sampling在2026年被弃用的传输层限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 截图到代码系统以渲染实现为目标，但截图缺失文档结构、交互逻辑、响应式规则和来源溯源，难以区分观测与猜测；多参考提示还存在证据或推断从一个参考污染另一个参考的风险。需要一种保留来源溯源、支持逐参考审计的中间规范产物与编排隔离机制。

Method: 系统分为检索、证据准备、模型调用、存储、检查五个模块。检索阶段支持用户查询、过滤器与持续性排除；证据准备对桌面长截图自动旋转、透明通道压平、缩放至≤1200像素宽、JPEG质量78编码，并按≤1600像素高度带80像素重叠切片，记录prepared-space与source-space坐标映射和8色调色板量化；移动端证据单独保留。契约schema为secret-mcp/design-index/v2，包含19个必需章节（目标范围、证据与坐标、路由清单、应用外壳、导航、页面规格、章节深挖、组件抽象、令牌与颜色、排版、资产与图标、响应式矩阵、交互与动效、可访问性、数据与内容、前端架构、任务图、页面验收、不确定性），并要求测量、观察、推断、未知四种显式标签。生成阶段通过sampler回调实现顺序逐参考处理，MCP适配器使用sampling/createMessage并设置includeContext: none，备用适配器通过新建进程和工作目录提供更强执行边界，每个工件保存后才进入下一个参考。

Result: 在commit c130c9c上评估。RQ1：npm ci、audit、build、lint全部通过。RQ2：实际检索排除1个参考后选择2个参考，准备9张证据图，发送2次sampling请求，生成2份文档，请求中跨参考标识符出现次数为0，排除参考从未被采样，运行耗时约2.85秒。RQ3：对3份外部生成的design index做静态词法审计，全部19个必需章节齐全，每份文档恰好1个唯一gdweb-*标识符，共8个页面规格、3579行、48154词、1943个px数值、319个六位颜色字面量，证据标签分布显示Inferred声明显著多于Measured和Observed。

Conclusion: Secret MCP验证了编排不变量（单参考单请求单文档、排除生效、证据隔离、原子保存、产物可审计）与契约结构合规，但未声明语义正确性、视觉保真度或像素级重建精度；sampler抽象使核心不变量与被弃用的MCP sampling传输解耦，便于迁移到直连模型API，同时使用须遵守第三方截图的版权与商标限制。

Abstract: Screenshot-to-code systems optimize for rendered implementations, but screenshots omit document structure, interaction logic, responsive rules, and provenance needed to distinguish observation from guesswork. Multi-reference prompts also risk contaminating one reference with evidence or inferences from another. We present Secret MCP, an open-source local system that produces one auditable design specification per public web reference. It separates retrieval, evidence preparation, model invocation, storage, and inspection. Long captures are resized and tiled with overlap; evidence records preserve prepared- and source-space coordinates and a measured color palette. A 19-section contract covers page inventories, navigation geometry, responsive matrices, components, accessibility, acceptance criteria, and explicit labels for measured, observed, inferred, and unknown claims. References are processed sequentially through a sampler interface. The evaluated MCP adapter sends one sampling/createMessage request per reference with includeContext set to none; a fresh-process adapter provides a stronger boundary. We evaluate commit c130c9c at two levels. A live retrieval and fixture-model integration run selected two references after excluding a third, prepared nine evidence images, issued two sampling requests, and produced two documents with zero cross-reference identifier occurrences. A static audit of three externally generated design indexes found all 19 required sections in every document, one unique reference identifier per document, eight page specifications, 1,943 pixel-valued measurements, and 319 color literals. These tests establish orchestration invariants and syntactic contract compliance, not semantic or visual reconstruction accuracy. The sampler abstraction preserves these boundaries across direct model APIs and transports despite MCP sampling's 2026 deprecation.

</details>


### [103] [The Evolution of Binary Decompilation in the Modern Era: A Taxonomy, Literature Review, and Future Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24955)
*Omar Abusabha,Sungjae Hwang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 这是一篇针对二进制反编译领域的系统性文献综述（SLR），对过去几十年间发表的66项反编译研究进行了全面梳理，构建了涵盖架构范式、任务导向技术、评估方法及基础设施的综合分类体系，并指出了14项关键开放挑战与未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二进制反编译已成为软件工程和安全分析的基础技术，并随着现代机器学习方法（尤其是LLM）的兴起而快速发展。然而，该领域的研究分散于不同任务、系统和评估方法中，缺乏统一的系统性梳理，使得研究者难以全面理解技术演进脉络、设计选择的影响以及改进方向。本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 遵循Kitchenham等人的系统文献综述（SLR）指南，聚焦于C语言二进制反编译研究。检索源包括Google Scholar、IEEE Xplore和ACM Digital Library，从210个候选关键词缩减为64个反编译专用关键词，制定了纳入与排除标准，对120余项研究进行手动筛选，最终纳入72项主要研究（其中66项作为核心分析对象）。围绕5个研究问题展开分析：架构范式（RQ1）、任务导向技术（RQ2）、全流水线评估（RQ3）、能力评估指标（RQ4）、工具框架与基准（RQ5）。

Result: 1. **架构范式分类（RQ1）**：将全流水线反编译器演进划分为四代——传统反编译（G1，如dcc、Boomerang、Hex-Rays、Ghidra等）、动态反编译（G2，如Revenge、Decompetition）、字节等价翻译（G3，如BED）以及神经反编译（G4，如BTC、Neutron、LLM4Decompile）。<br><br>2. **任务导向技术分类（RQ2）**：归纳出八项核心任务，包括类型恢复（T1）、控制流结构化（T2）、表达式简化（T3）、编译器习语恢复（T4）、函数名恢复（T5）、变量名恢复（T6）、代码精炼（T7）和代码摘要（T8），每项任务均按静态/动态、启发式/学习式等维度进行细分。<br><br>3. **评估能力与指标（RQ3-RQ4）**：总结了三类评估目标（正确性E1、可用性E2、可读性E3）和八种评估能力（紧凑性、结构化、可读性、正确性、完整性、可用性、相似性、可重编译性），并系统整理了各研究所用的具体度量方法。<br><br>4. **基础设施与基准（RQ5）**：分析了分析工具链（反汇编器、二进制提升器、解析器、求解器等）、ML模型选择（判别式vs生成式、Transformer/LLM主导）、基准套件（开源工具、大规模仓库、恶意软件集、随机程序生成器、编程竞赛数据集）和编译器配置（GCC/Clang主导、ELF格式主导、x86架构主导）。

Conclusion: 论文识别出覆盖范围、技术和社会-技术三个维度共14项关键开放挑战，包括：标准化基准缺失、可靠ground truth不足、ISA感知技术欠缺、传统反编译保真度有限、反编译后验证缺位、专用度量指标匮乏、神经反编译的输入上下文限制、LLM泛化性验证不足、解编译器局限性认知不足、开源研究产物稀缺以及法律伦理不确定性等。未来研究应聚焦于构建更具代表性的基准、开发专用评估指标、推进LLM驱动的自适应反编译工作流，并强化可复现性与伦理考量。机器学习尤其是大语言模型的深度集成被视为实现更智能、更语义化反编译的关键方向。

Abstract: Decompilation has become a foundational technique in software engineering and security analysis, and it is now advancing through the integration of modern machine learning (ML) approaches. This article presents a systematic review of decompilation studies published over the past decades and develops a comprehensive taxonomy of methodologies employed in contemporary research. We further examine trends in evaluation metrics, tools, and benchmarks used to assess state-of-the-art approaches. Our review reveals key challenges, such as the lack of reliable ground truth and the absence of standardized benchmarks, which hinder rigorous comparison. Finally, we outline future research directions.

</details>


### [104] [Model-Based Agentic Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25174)
*James C. Davis,Kelechi Kalu,Huiyun Peng,Parth V. Patil*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Coding agents increase implementation capacity without automatically making project intent, system structure, or acceptance evidence explicit. As implementation becomes abundant relative to engineering judgment, the scarce work shifts toward choosing useful abstractions, producing evidence, and determining which obligations govern acceptance. Existing workflows address parts of this gap through larger prompts, repository retrieval, or perchange review, but still require agents and engineers to reconstruct consequential properties. As an alternative, we present Model-Based Agentic Software Engineering (MAGE). MAGE is a framework and a theory for building trustworthy autonomy from commodity intelligence. MAGE addresses a representation problem and an authority problem: it externalizes the smallest purposeful representation needed to answer an engineering question, then gives settled obligations proportionate authority through constraints, sensors, validators, and gates. It keeps uncertain intent open and turns recurring reconstruction and judgment into durable engineering structure that later work can inherit. We developed MAGE from a longitudinal case and refined it through six independently reported industrial accounts. Across these sources, MAGE explains how externalized knowledge, bounded action, independent evaluation, and retained human authority can compose into a governed engineering environment, and proposes tests of when that environment turns commodity intelligence into durable engineering progress.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI enhancement unavailable (llm_error). Original arXiv metadata is preserved. Error: AI returned empty structured response

Method: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Result: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (llm_error). Original arXiv metadata is preserved. Error: AI returned empty structured response

Abstract: Coding agents increase implementation capacity without automatically making project intent, system structure, or acceptance evidence explicit. As implementation becomes abundant relative to engineering judgment, the scarce work shifts toward choosing useful abstractions, producing evidence, and determining which obligations govern acceptance. Existing workflows address parts of this gap through larger prompts, repository retrieval, or perchange review, but still require agents and engineers to reconstruct consequential properties. As an alternative, we present Model-Based Agentic Software Engineering (MAGE). MAGE is a framework and a theory for building trustworthy autonomy from commodity intelligence. MAGE addresses a representation problem and an authority problem: it externalizes the smallest purposeful representation needed to answer an engineering question, then gives settled obligations proportionate authority through constraints, sensors, validators, and gates. It keeps uncertain intent open and turns recurring reconstruction and judgment into durable engineering structure that later work can inherit. We developed MAGE from a longitudinal case and refined it through six independently reported industrial accounts. Across these sources, MAGE explains how externalized knowledge, bounded action, independent evaluation, and retained human authority can compose into a governed engineering environment, and proposes tests of when that environment turns commodity intelligence into durable engineering progress.

</details>


### [105] [Metis: Typed Runtime Mediation for Tool-Using Software Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25322)
*Jun Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Metis 是一个面向工具调用软件智能体的多提供商运行时系统，通过将模型输出流转换为类型化事件，在外部副作用发生前做出权限决策、调度排序与生命周期管理。其核心贡献在于以"运行时中介"视角统一处理权限路由、并发调度类、终端结果闭合与子智能体边界等机制，并在冻结源码工件上以配对实测、消融与故障矩阵进行机制级验证，但不声称模型能力、语义安全或相对优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具调用智能体正从"建议代码"演变为直接修改仓库、进程、网络与图形界面的执行主体，但现有研究多聚焦于提议动作的策略或任务环境的 harness，缺少对"调用被提议后由谁放行、与其它调用如何排序、如何记录终端结果、中断或上下文压缩后如何保持提供商有效历史"这一下游系统层问题的回答。论文指出"合法的 provider 请求未必被授权，授权的调用未必产生有序闭合的历史"，因此需要一个把权限、调度、结果与生命周期显式化的运行时中介层。

Method: 核心方法是将模型提议的调用转换为类型化运行时事件并衍生出执行轨迹（trace），关键机制包括：(1) 五模式权限平面（five-mode permission plane）与批处理预检（batch ASK），交互审批、协议权限回复与无头策略在统一 gate 终止；(2) 能力解析器（registry/aliases/enabled 检查 + schema + 懒加载 ToolSearch）；(3) 四类调度器 Safe / Queue / Exclusive / Background，配合结果闭合与 orphan repair；(4) 子执行边界：子智能体获得克隆 gate 与过滤后的 registry，配合 worktree 与 transcript；(5) 历史与生命周期：context tiers / memory / budget / session / checkpoint / spill；(6) 配套进程外的 frontmost-app gate 用于计算机输入。评估方法采用冻结源码工件进行机制级验证，包括 30 对配对真实 I/O 消融、10 例故障矩阵、子边界消融、五条调用路径的权限 oracle 以及 5 个模型条件 × 3 次试验的最小 Read-marker 协议。

Result: 在 30 对配对真实 I/O 测试中，四类中介将中位耗时从强制串行下的 25.958 ms 降至 14.146 ms；配对均值差为 -12.295 ms（95% bootstrap 区间 [-12.968, -11.694]），所有 30 对均显示中介更快。10 例故障矩阵暴露了重复标识符（duplicate-identifier）与回滚（rollback）的边界限制。子边界消融中，gate + registry 全开条件成功阻断声明的未授权效果并隐藏全部 5 个 escape 工具；移除两项保护后两项观察均逆转。仅决策权限 oracle 在 5 条调用路径上全部命中 10 个声明案例。5 个模型条件在固定 Read-marker 协议上均以 3/3 试验通过。论文强调这些结果支持分派、权限路由、子权限与 provider-有效轨迹闭合等"有界声明"，不证明模型能力、语义安全、回滚正确性或相对优势。

Conclusion: Metis 将工具调用智能体的运行时中介形式化为带有显式权限、调度、结果与生命周期边的类型化事件图，并在冻结工件上验证了四类调度器的并发收益、五模式权限平面的跨路径一致性、子边界克隆 gate 与过滤 registry 的隔离效果、以及终端结果闭合与 orphan repair 的标识符覆盖保证。同时承认存在重复标识符与回滚边界等已暴露限制，以及在模型能力、语义安全、回滚正确性和跨运行时对比上的有效性外推受限。未来工作需要更广泛的研究以支撑更广泛的结论，论文本身保持机制中心、范围受限的定位。

Abstract: Software agents connect probabilistic model output to operations that change repositories, processes, networks, and graphical applications. We present Metis, a multi-provider runtime that converts provider streams into typed events before admitted calls reach external effects. Its execution path makes permission decisions, interference classes, terminal results, and lifecycle transitions explicit and inspectable. We evaluate these mechanisms on frozen source artifacts. Across 30 matched real-I/O pairs, four-class mediation reduced median elapsed time from 25.958 ms under forced serialization to 14.146 ms. The mean paired difference was -12.295 ms (95% bootstrap interval [-12.968, -11.694]), with mediation faster in all pairs. A ten-case fault matrix exposed duplicate-identifier and rollback limits. In a child-boundary ablation, the full gate-plus-registry condition blocked the declared unauthorized effect and hid all five escape tools. Removing both protections reversed both observations. A decision-only permission oracle matched all ten declared cases across five invocation routes. Five model conditions also completed a fixed Read-marker protocol in 3/3 trials each. These results support bounded claims about dispatch, permission routing, child authority, and provider-valid trace closure. They do not establish model competence, semantic safety, rollback, or superiority over another runtime.

</details>


### [106] [Point-in-Time Audit Before Alpha: Public-Archive Availability and a Negative Matched-Budget Study on BTC Perpetual Futures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25348)
*Baocheng Zeng,Jinhao Yang,Peilin Han,Kangnan He*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文审计了Binance BTCUSDT USD-M永续合约的公共数据存档的点时(point-in-time)可用性,并在严格匹配预算下对比了审计自适应代理、随机搜索与树GP的因子挖掘效果。所有评估在一次性历史保留集上一致呈现负的经济结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加密货币公共存档表面上"文件齐全",但因子研究需要每个决策时刻真正可执行的数据。论文旨在检验存档是否满足严格的点时语义,以及严格的审计是否能改变虚假发现率与策略经济性。

Method: 区分事件时间、发布时间与可用时间(close+1ms);首次要求trade/mark/index/OI在精确五分钟网格连续365天,失败后修订为以trade/mark/index/realized funding为核心、OI可选的可用性掩码;构建确定性审计器,拒绝未来源移位、同bar执行、未来funding、OI对齐错误与回填五类违规;用80个冻结模板测试审计器,在10条零信号路径上比较无审计/基础审计/完整审计下的误通过率与PBO;在共享DSL与匹配有效候选预算下比较审计自适应代理、随机搜索、树GP;最后在一次性历史保留集上以6bp手续费+2bp滑点评估IC与净Sharpe,并做费用-滑点网格敏感性。

Result: 原精确网格要求最长无修复交集仅304.57天即失败;修订后掩码保留727个完整UTC日;审计器在40/40非法与0/40合法模板上完全正确;完整审计将零信号下平均误通过率从0.2910降至0.0625(降幅78.5%,配对路径bootstrap差[-0.2595,-0.2012]);匹配预算下审计代理与随机搜索各产生39个合格候选,树GP为29;保留集上全部23个评估运行IC为正但净Sharpe均为负,460个费用-滑点单元全部为负。

Conclusion: 审计流程显著降低了已知违规模板的虚假通过率,但既不能证明独立掩码效应,也不能证明审计自适应代理在匹配预算下优于随机搜索;一次性历史保留集上的正IC无法转化为正经济收益。论文主张审计价值,但明确否定盈利能力、稳定性与代理优越性主张,并报告其结论仅限于Binance BTCUSDT USD-M、该DSL与该确定性自适应策略。

Abstract: Public cryptocurrency archives may appear usable when files exist, although factor research requires observations available and executable at each decision time. We audit public Binance BTCUSDT USD-M perpetual-futures data using event, publication, and availability times and separate proposal from deterministic auditing, evaluation, and holdout access. An initial gapless five-minute requirement for trade, mark, index, and open interest failed: the longest unrepaired intersection was 304.5729166666667 days. A disclosed revision made trade, mark, index, and realized funding the core streams and made open interest optional because its publication time was unverified. The revised mask retained 727 complete UTC days and supported a 436/145/146-day train, validation, and historical-holdout split. On 80 frozen known-rule templates, the auditor detected 40/40 violations and rejected 0/40 legal templates. Across ten null-signal paths, full auditing reduced mean false passes from 0.2910 to 0.0625. Under matched valid-candidate budgets, the audited adaptive agent tied random search and did not establish superiority. In the one-time historical holdout, all evaluated runs had positive IC but negative net Sharpe under primary costs. We therefore report a scoped negative result rather than a profitability or agent-superiority claim.

</details>


### [107] [Retry Amplification in Distributed Systems: A Systematic Analysis of Retry Policies and Their Role in Cascading Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25403)
*Rishabh Mehan,Jasmit Kaur Saluja*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究分布式系统中重试策略的"重试放大效应"——即多层调用路径中每层同时重试导致请求量指数级增长的问题。提出 RAF（重试放大因子）度量指标，对 200 个开源 Python 微服务项目进行实证分析，并基于模拟器提出自适应重试预算（ARB）算法，证明其能有效抑制放大效应同时保持对瞬态故障的恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重试指导（如 AWS/Azure 建议）针对单客户端场景，但在多层调用路径中，每一跳独立重试会导致请求量复合放大，将局部降级转化为级联故障。该问题在运维事后报告中频繁出现，却鲜有系统性研究。

Method: 1) 形式化建模：将系统建模为 DAG，定义 RAF = 实际负载/基线负载；2) 实证分析：对 200 个 GitHub Python 微服务项目进行正则静态分析，提取重试配置并人工验证；3) 离散事件仿真：在 5 层链路上对比 4 种策略（无重试 NR、标准重试 SR、断路器 CB、自适应重试预算 ARB），每配置 100 次试验；4) ARB 算法：维护基于失败率 EMA 的动态重试预算，配合跨层反压信号（队列/CPU 超阈值）。

Result: 实证发现：11.5% 项目含显式重试逻辑（真实流行率约 41%），60.9% 至少有一个无退避配置，113 个生产配置中仅 1 个使用抖动。仿真结果：在 S3（相关故障）场景下，标准重试将成功率从 55.4%（无重试）降至 41.5%，RAF 达 1.34×；CB 和 ARB 均将 RAF 维持在约 1.01×，成功率接近无重试基线（54.9%–55.3%）。理论模型预测的 RAF 边界（最高 10.30×）显著高于仿真实测值，原因是有限队列和退避抑制了稳态放大。

Conclusion: 重试行为应被视作系统级属性而非单点配置。标准重试在相关故障下反而降低系统韧性；ARB 通过动态预算和跨层反压信号实现连续节流，在成功率上与断路器相当但避免了二值切换的负载振荡。论文提出五类反模式（无退避、缺抖动、激进重试、静态配置、无跨层协调），并指出 Go/Java 生态验证和生产环境部署验证是未来工作重点。

Abstract: Retry mechanisms are a standard component of resilient distributed systems, but their collective behavior, when every tier in a call path retries concurrently, is less well understood than the per-client guidance that produced them. This paper introduces the retry amplification factor (RAF), a metric quantifying the additional request volume that retry policies generate during partial failures. In a study of 200 open-source Python microservice projects, explicit retry logic is detected in 11.5%, and an audit of our own false negatives places true prevalence near 41%. Among the projects detected, 60.9% contain at least one configuration without backoff, and after manual verification exactly one of 113 production configurations randomizes its delay. We then evaluate these policies in simulation (n = 100 trials per strategy). Under correlated failures, a naive standard retry policy reduces the success rate from 55.4% to 41.5% relative to performing no retries at all. We catalog five recurring anti-patterns, propose Adaptive Retry Budgeting (ARB), and show that budget-constrained retries maintain success rates close to the no-retry baseline while still recovering from transient faults. These results indicate that retry behavior should be designed as a system-level property rather than configured locally at each call site.

</details>


### [108] [Predicting Struggling Students in CS1 Programming Using Keystroke-Level Editing Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25769)
*Yasuyo Kofune,Kazumasa Shimari,Kenichi Matsumoto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究使用键盘级编辑日志在CS1编程练习中早期识别"完全卡住(FS)"学生的可行性。基于CodeBench平台2019-1学期507名学生数据,通过对比Breakthrough (BT)与Fully Stuck (FS)两类学生,发现结合CodeMirror编辑特征比仅用执行日志特征具有更好的预测效果(AUROC提升+0.098),且最早阶段(第一次提交前)的预测信号最强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CS1编程课程失败率和辍学率高,传统预测依赖提交后的成绩/结果数据,无法在练习进行中提供实时干预支持。CodeBench平台记录了细粒度的键盘编辑日志,但此前研究主要将其作为汇总特征使用,SHAP分析显示贡献很低。本文旨在探索这些细粒度编辑行为能否在练习早期识别出将持续卡住的学生,从而为教师提供及时干预的依据。

Method: 使用CodeBench 2019-1学期507名学生数据。定义两类学生:BT(先前提交全为0%,最终提交满分)和FS(所有提交均为0%,未达到正确答案)。将每次练习按提交间隔离为segment(k=1~10),在每个segment分别提取:(1)执行日志特征(ExecOnly,4个,包括错误类型和时间间隔);(2)CodeMirror键盘编辑特征(CMOnly,8个,包括编辑次数、删除字符量、删除比、最大/平均暂停时长、编辑时长、空闲时间比);(3)二者联合(Combined,12个)。使用Random Forest和Logistic Regression,采用学生级GroupKFold 5折交叉验证,以AUROC为主要指标,配对bootstrap(2000次)计算95%置信区间。

Result: RQ1:在k=1阶段,CMOnly优于ExecOnly(AUROC 0.654 vs 0.575,差值95%CI [+0.056, +0.104]),Combined优于ExecOnly +0.098(AUROC 0.674,95%CI [+0.078, +0.119]);k=1-4均显著,k≥5则置信区间跨零。k=4时ExecOnly跌至0.484(接近随机),Combined仍保持0.568,显示编辑特征在执行信号失效时仍具区分力。PR-AUC方面Combined在k=1为0.281,FS召回率0.70时精度约0.214。RQ2:所有特征配置下,最早segment(k=1)预测性能最强;随k增加,Combined和CMOnly整体下降,ExecOnly呈非单调变化。特征重要性上,temporal特征(duration_sec、max_pause_sec、mean_pause_sec)始终位列前茅,code_change在k=1贡献突出但随k递减。补充FS-vs-rest分析AUROC达0.839。

Conclusion: 本文证明键盘级编辑日志在编程练习早期即可为识别"将持续卡住"的学生提供优先排序信号。结合CodeMirror与执行特征优于单独使用执行特征,且最强预测信号出现在首个segment,说明行为线索从练习最早期就已显现。然而AUROC 0.674虽高于随机水平,尚不足以支撑独立的高置信度个体干预决策。建议将模型定位为"早期优先排序工具",而非独立干预依据。未来方向包括:将早期预测转化为教学支持(定向提示、教师巡查)、刻画突破型与卡顿型学生的行为差异、引入序列建模捕捉跨segment依赖,并需在生成式AI辅助编码时代重新评估特征有效性。

Abstract: This paper investigates the feasibility of early detection of struggling students during CS1 programming exercises using keystroke-level logs. Some students fail to reach a correct solution before the exercise ends, and by the time this becomes apparent from grades or final outcomes, the opportunity for timely instructor support aimed at helping them recover may have passed. We use data from the CodeBench platform, which records real-time code editing events at the keystroke level, alongside execution and submission logs. We define two outcome groups: Breakthrough (BT) students, whose prior submissions all receive 0% and whose final submission achieves full credit, and Fully Stuck (FS) students, whose submissions all receive 0% without reaching a correct solution. To examine this feasibility, we focus on two questions: (RQ1) whether adding keystroke-level editing features improves the prediction of FS students over execution-log features alone, and (RQ2) at which stage BT and FS students can be predicted most accurately. Experiments on the 2019-1 semester of the CodeBench dataset, comprising 507 students, compare three feature configurations: execution-based features (ExecOnly), CodeMirror-based features (CMOnly), and their combination (Combined). We evaluate prediction across successive submission-based stages during each exercise. In the earliest stage, CMOnly outperforms ExecOnly (AUROC 0.654 vs. 0.575), and Combined further improves over ExecOnly by +0.098 (AUROC 0.674). Across all configurations, the earliest stage yielded the strongest predictive signal. These findings indicate that behavioral signals present at the very start of an exercise contain useful clues about whether a student will ultimately solve the problem, and that keystroke-level editing logs provide additional value for early prioritization beyond execution logs alone.

</details>


### [109] [Beyond the Editing Canvas: Evidence Divergence in OOXML-to-LLM Ingestion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25880)
*Side Liu,Jiangpeng Liu,Jinwen Xin,Guojun Peng,Jiang Ming*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统性地揭示了 OOXML 文档（.docx/.xlsx/.pptx）在送入大语言模型（LLM）的"摄取管道"中可能产生与 Microsoft Office 编辑画布不一致的证据视图。作者通过规范挖掘确认了 21 种"证据分叉（evidence forks）"，并测量了它们在 13 种提取工具、4 个原生摄取 API 和 7 个 Web 聊天机器人中的暴露程度，发现暴露主要由摄取路径与提取器配置决定，而非模型本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 RAG、金融分析、合规审查等 LLM 工作流将 OOXML 文档作为"一等证据"广泛使用，业界隐式假设这些文档具备"语义完整性"，即模型读取的内容与用户在 Microsoft Office 中审阅的内容一致。本文质疑并系统验证该假设是否成立。

Method: （1）构建 OOXML 规范知识库（15,884 条 schema 记录 + 5,206 条 prose 段落），用 LLM 代理遍历生成 639 条候选证据分叉；（2）对候选构造最小化 Office 测试夹具（fixture），通过"Oracle 测试（Office 编辑画布显示 BENIGN）"与"提取器测试（至少一个工具输出 TRAP）"两道行为门进行确认；（3）去重与范围筛选，得到 21 种确认机制；（4）用 TAT-QA 金融报告片段构造 210 份逼真测试文档；（5）在 13 种提取工具、4 个原生摄取 API、7 个 Web 聊天机器人上进行端到端测量，并附加控制对照实验；（6）对 16 个开源 LLM/RAG 项目进行源码级调查；（7）在 Sheetpedia（2,000 XLSX）、DocxCorp（2,000 DOCX）与 263 个公共 PPTX 上测量各机制在普通文档中的基率。

Result: （1）确认 21 种证据分叉，分属 6 个维度（表示、状态、可见性、兼容性、范围、线性化），其中 Excel 12 个、Word 8 个、PowerPoint 1 个；（2）所有 13 种提取工具都至少暴露一种分叉；机制触达率呈双峰分布：7 个机制仅触及 11%–33% 的适用工具，14 个触及 67%–100%；（3）四个原生摄取 API 在 8,400 次试验中返回陷阱内容的比例为 48%–76%，11 个接口中 20/21 至少有一端返回陷阱；（4）暴露主要由摄取路径决定：同厂商的 API 与 Web 入口表现不同；同一文档技能下 Haiku 4.5、Sonnet 5、Opus 4.8 三个 Claude 模型均暴露相同的 16/21 机制；（5）八个 DOCX 接口中有八个与某候选提取配置行为匹配，可缩小未公开摄取栈的配置空间；（6）16 个开源项目默认 OOXML 摄取路径集中在少数提取器家族（如 markitdown、openpyxl、python-docx 等）；（7）普通文档基率呈双峰分布：6 种机制未在样本中出现，10 种低于 2%，5 种介于 2.75%–16.5%（含工作表头、隐藏列、兼容性回退、标注超链接、图像 alt 文本）。

Conclusion: OOXML 到 LLM 摄取管道可在不改变文件包的前提下，使模型获得的证据与 Office 编辑画布相背离，且这种暴露取决于完整的摄取配置（提取器、版本、输出模式、参数），而非模型选择。共享提取器家族在开源项目中广泛复用，加剧了生态层面的风险。作者呼吁将"视图构造"视为安全策略，建立显式的摄取契约以保留证据来源与语义角色，并将证据分叉探针作为摄取层回归测试的实用原语。

Abstract: LLM pipelines increasingly ingest Office Open XML (OOXML) documents (Word, Excel, and PowerPoint files) as first-class evidence in financial, compliance, and retrieval-augmented workflows, implicitly assuming semantic integrity: that the evidence consumed by the model matches the content shown in the Microsoft Office suite editing canvas. We show that this assumption can fail in OOXML-to-LLM pipelines. The same specification-valid OOXML file can yield one evidentiary view in Microsoft Office and another when extracted for an LLM. Each view is treated as authoritative by its consumer, a condition we call plural ground truth. The ingestion contract rarely states which view and semantic roles become model evidence or preserves how that evidence was derived. We call the specification-grounded OOXML constructions that induce such divergence evidence forks. We systematically traverse and mine the OOXML specification and confirm 21 evidence forks across Excel, Word, and PowerPoint, spanning six dimensions of view construction. All 13 tools in our extraction panel emit evidence from at least one fork. We test four native-ingestion LLM APIs and seven web chatbots. Each test document carries a trap: a task-relevant fact exposed by extraction but not shown in Office. Across this 21-mechanism evaluation, the four APIs return the trap in 48--76% of trials. For 20 of 21 mechanisms, at least one of the eleven interfaces returns the trap. Our measurements further show that exposure is shaped upstream of the model by the ingestion path and extractor configuration. A source-level survey of sixteen popular open-source LLM projects further shows that default OOXML ingestion paths concentrate on affected extractor families.

</details>


### [110] [Answer Is Cheap, Show Me the Evidence! Augmenting Automated Vulnerability Assessment with Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25905)
*Shengyi Pan,Zelong Zheng,Jiayuan Zhou,Xing Hu,Xin Xia,Shanping Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出EAVA框架，利用大语言模型（LLM）自动评估软件漏洞（SV）并提供支持证据。通过专门的LLM智能体处理富文本内容和项目信息，采用两阶段训练（监督指令微调+强化学习GRPO）注入领域知识，构建专门的评估模型。实验表明，EAVA在多个指标上优于最强基线5.3%至35.2%，用户研究证实其实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化漏洞评估方法主要依赖SVR文本训练ML/DL分类器，存在三大局限：1）无法处理截图、代码片段等富文本内容；2）忽略受影响项目上下文信息；3）仅关注预测准确性，不提供解释或证据支持预测结果。实际应用中，不完美的准确性需要人工验证，缺乏证据限制了实用性。此外，初步研究表明通用LLM由于缺乏评估特定知识，在SV评估任务上表现不佳。

Method: EAVA框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 专用LLM智能体处理SVR中的代码片段、截图及受影响项目信息，对富文本进行摘要化处理；(2) 通过将推理轨迹标注转化为"开卷"问题（提供真值标签和关键区分属性），大规模自动标注51,568个推理轨迹；(3) 两阶段训练范式——监督指令微调（SFT）注入领域知识，强化学习（GRPO算法）增强内在推理能力。推理阶段采用混合检索器（稀疏+密集）结合LLM重排序器，检索相似历史SV作为补充证据。

Result: 在新收集的6,446个SVR数据集（涵盖1,986个项目，54种编程语言，159个CWE类别）上的实验显示：EAVA在平均指标上比最强基线proEVA在F1上提升5.3%、MCC提升18.7%；在严重程度指标上分别提升14.4%和35.2%。消融实验验证了细调训练、显式推理、GRPO强化学习以及富文本/项目信息处理的有效性。在不同基座模型（Llama-3.1-8B vs Qwen3-8B）上表现相当。处理单个SV报告总成本约0.0327美元，延迟可控。用户研究表明96.8%的正确预测案例中证据被评为有用（4/5分），且分析未被错误预测误导。

Conclusion: EAVA通过LLM智能体、两阶段训练（数据标注+SFT+RL）和历史SV检索，有效解决了自动化SV评估中缺乏证据支持和领域知识不足的问题，显著提升了评估性能和实用性。该方法在不同基础模型间具有良好泛化性，计算成本低廉，是SV评估领域首个将强化学习应用于该任务的工作，为安全分析师提供了高效且可验证的辅助工具。

Abstract: Software vulnerability (SV) assessment helps prioritize remediation by characterizing reported vulnerabilities. Existing automated methods predict assessment results from SV reports (SVRs), but often overlook information in rich text, such as screenshots and code snippets, as well as contextual information about vulnerable projects. They also focus on prediction accuracy without providing explanations or supporting evidence, limiting their practical use when analysts must validate imperfect predictions. We propose EAVA, a framework that uses large language models (LLMs) to assess SVs and provide supporting evidence. EAVA employs specialized LLM agents to process rich-text content and project information, and builds a dedicated assessment model through a two-stage training pipeline. It first uses supervised instruction tuning on automatically annotated reasoning trajectories to inject domain knowledge, and then applies reinforcement learning to improve intrinsic reasoning. EAVA also retrieves similar historical vulnerabilities as supplementary evidence. Experiments on a newly collected SVR dataset show that EAVA outperforms the strongest baseline by 5.3 to 35.2 percent across multiple metrics. Ablation studies confirm the effectiveness of assessment-specific model training and information enrichment. A user study with security experts further demonstrates that the evidence provided by EAVA is useful and practical for real-world SV assessment.

</details>


### [111] [XREPOTEST: Benchmarking Multilingual Repository-Level Unit Test Generation for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25939)
*Dung Le Quang,Dong Cao Van,Nam Le Hai,Linh Ngo Van,Anh M. T. Bui,Phuong T. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: XREPOTEST 是一个多语言、仓库级别的 LLM 单元测试生成基准，覆盖 Rust、Go、Julia、PHP、Ruby 五种语言，包含 3,642 个焦点函数，提出新的 Invocation Rate (IR) 指标，并评估了 14 个 SOTA LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 单元测试生成评估主要依赖独立函数级别（standalone）设置，且仅覆盖少数主流语言（尤其是 Java 和 Python），高估了模型的真实可用性，无法反映仓库级别的实际约束（如构建系统、依赖、框架规范）。此外，缺乏针对 Rust、Go、Julia、PHP、Ruby 等多语言生态的评估，难以衡量模型在多样化编程环境下的泛化能力。

Method: 1. 数据集构建：从 GitHub 筛选高质量仓库（≥500 stars，包含构建配置文件，跨多个应用领域），使用 tree-sitter 解析 AST 提取焦点函数，经多轮过滤保证质量。
2. 上下文增强：支持静态上下文（File-level、LSP-based 符号解析）和动态上下文（BM25 稀疏检索、UniXCoder 密集检索）。
3. 容器化执行：基于 Docker 标准化工具链，使用各语言原生测试框架（cargo test、go test、Test.jl、PHPUnit、RSpec）执行测试。
4. 评估指标：除测试通过率（TPR）、编译成功率（CSR）、行覆盖率和变异得分外，提出 Invocation Rate (IR)，通过 AST 解析检测生成测试是否真正调用了目标函数。
5. 模型评估：在 14 个 SOTA LLM 上进行广泛实验，对比不同上下文配置的效果。

Result: 1. 仓库级别性能与独立设置性能存在显著差距，表明现有基准高估了模型的实际能力。
2. Claude 4.5 Sonnet 和 GPT-5.2 在多数语言上表现最佳（如 PHP 上 TPR 分别为 26.93% 和 10.06%）。
3. 发现上下文丰富度与测试可靠性之间存在权衡：更多上下文可提升通过率和覆盖率，但常引入噪声，降低 Invocation Rate。
4. 标准设置下性能主要受 API 幻觉（API hallucinations）限制。
5. IR 指标揭示了部分测试虽然通过但并未真正调用目标函数，暴露出"虚假通过"问题。

Conclusion: XREPOTEST 填补了多语言仓库级别单元测试生成评估的空白，提供了具有挑战性和信息量的基准。其提出的 IR 指标和上下文增强策略的系统比较，为开发可扩展、稳健的单元测试生成系统提供了重要参考，强调了未来研究需要更好地平衡上下文丰富度与测试可靠性。数据集和代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for automated unit test generation, but existing evaluations largely rely on standalone settings and a narrow set of programming languages, overestimating real-world readiness. We introduce XREPOTEST, a multilingual repository-level benchmark for unit test generation spanning five underexplored languages: Rust, Go, Julia, PHP, and Ruby. XREPOTEST evaluates tests under realistic repository constraints using a containerized execution framework and multiple context augmentation strategies, including file-level, LSP-based, and retrieval-based context. Beyond standard metrics such as test pass rate and coverage, we propose Invocation Rate (IR) to assess whether generated tests meaningfully exercise the intended functionality. Experiments with 14 state-of-the-art LLMs, including Claude 4.5, GPT-5.2, DeepSeek V4-Pro, and Qwen families, reveal a substantial gap between standalone and repository-level performance, as well as trade-offs between richer context and test reliability. Overall, XREPOTEST provides a challenging and informative benchmark to advance scalable and robust unit test generation in realistic software environments. The dataset and code are publicly available at: this https URL

</details>


### [112] [Vulnerable Code Search: Transferable Attack for Code Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26031)
*Kaicheng Wang,Liyan Huang,Jesse Thomason,Weihang Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种针对代码搜索模型的迁移对抗攻击方法，通过修改代码标识符（不改变功能）使无关代码片段与目标查询的嵌入相似度人为对齐。攻击在小模型（CodeT5+）上优化，可迁移到大模型（OASIS、Nomic-embed-code、Voyage-code-3）甚至闭源LLM（GPT-5.4-mini、Gemini-3.1-Pro），使检索MRR指标最高下降77%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于神经代码语言模型（CLM）的代码检索系统存在严重安全漏洞：由于检索流程依赖离线嵌入和向量相似度，恶意或无关代码片段可通过微小修改（保留功能）操纵检索结果。现有研究多关注分类任务的对抗攻击，而代码搜索场景带来新挑战：1）可针对特定查询定制攻击；2）离线嵌入使得检测/缓解攻击更困难。作者旨在揭示CLM共享的脆弱性，强调高基准分数不等同于真正的语义理解。

Method: 提出三阶段白盒攻击方法（后迁移为黑盒）：1）**梯度引导优化**：对代码中标识符token计算"influence"（基于一阶Taylor展开的相似度变化近似），贪心搜索最大化影响力的替换token，并通过AST结构保持保证功能不变；2）**标识符风格约束**：强制保留camelCase/snake_case等命名风格，过滤搜索空间；3）**查询token相似性（QTS）**：在目标函数中加入候选token与查询token的最大内积项（权重α=0.1），增强迁移性。使用Hungarian算法保证一致性和唯一性约束。在小代理模型（CodeT5+或OASIS）上计算后，通过攻击迁移性攻击更大或闭源模型。

Result: 在CosQA/CLARC数据集上：1）攻击在代理模型上对>97%的样本提升相似度，迁移到评估模型成功率>95%，Pearson/Spearman相关系数高且正；2）优于基线（DAMP、CodeAttack）：白盒场景ΔSim更高且GPU时间减半，黑盒场景ΔSim约为CodeAttack的5倍但API调用从10.8M降至1k；3）仅替换10%无关候选，MRR最高下降77%（CosQA），R@5降至<15%；4）共享语料攻击中，仅修改4%语料即造成严重退化；5）迁移到LLM（GPT-5.4-mini、Gemini-3.1-Pro）的RepoQA检索：每查询仅替换2个无关候选，5种编程语言准确率显著下降；6）消融实验证实三个组件均贡献显著（QTS对Nomic-embed-code迁移影响最大）；7）基于微调的防御（Robust-Only/Mixed FT）要么牺牲检索性能（R@5从88%降至42.6%），要么在自适应白盒攻击下仍脆弱。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了当前代码搜索方法的根本脆弱性——CLM过度依赖词汇线索而非真正的语义理解，高基准分数掩盖了鲁棒性缺陷。提出的可迁移对抗攻击表明，针对小代理模型优化的扰动可有效欺骗更大的闭源系统和LLM。现有微调防御存在性能-鲁棒性权衡不足的问题。未来工作方向包括：研究攻击跨模型迁移的根本原因、开发保持检索效用的防御策略（如功能感知对比训练、融入AST等程序语言结构信息以构建更具语义基础的代码表示）。

Abstract: Reliable code retrieval is crucial for developer productivity and effective code reuse. However, current neural code language models (CLMs) powering search tools are susceptible to adversarial attacks targeting non-functional textual elements. In this paper, we introduce a programming language-agnostic, transferable, adversarial attack that exploits this CLM vulnerability. Our approach perturbs identifiers within a code snippet without altering the snippet's functionality to artificially align the code with a target query. We demonstrate that our attack, even when computed using smaller code embedding models, such as CodeT5+, is highly effective and transferable to larger, closed-source embedding models, like Voyage-code-3, or LLMs like Gemini-3.1-Pro. Our attack can increase the similarity between the query and arbitrary, irrelevant code snippets, consequently degrading key retrieval metrics such as the Mean Reciprocal Rank (MRR) of state-of-the-art models by up to 77%. The experimental results highlight the fragility of current code search methods and underscore the need for more robust, semantic-aware approaches.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [Self-Explanation Tutor for Active Study of CS1 Worked Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25180)
*Arun-Balajiee Lekshmi-Narayanan,Mohammad Hassany,Kamil Akhuseyinoglu,Rully Hendrawan,Peter Brusilovsky*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文研究如何利用大语言模型(LLM)为编程入门课程的"自我解释学习"(self-explanation)提供自动化反馈，构建了名为ESSE的自解释辅导系统，并通过对比人类评估标准和真实课堂部署验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的worked examples（范例学习）阅读是被动学习，而self-explanation虽能将其转为主动学习，但因难以大规模提供即时反馈而受限。研究旨在探索LLM能否填补这一自动化反馈的空白。

Method: 1. 构建ESSE系统：学生逐行解释Java范例，LLM即时评判其正确性与完整性；2. 以两类人类标准作为对照——领域专家独立评估和非专家众包评估——系统刻画LLM判断的可靠性与偏差；3. 在Java入门课程中实际部署，通过行为数据与解释质量追踪学习效果。

Result: LLM的判断与两类人类标准均达到较好一致性，能可靠驱动辅导系统；课堂部署显示，学生在收到反馈后更倾向于坚持修改而非放弃，其解释随迭代趋于更完整且概念更丰富，并产生可观测的学习成效。

Conclusion: LLM评估的质量已足以支撑自解释辅导系统的运行；该类系统能积极改变学生学习worked examples的方式，提升主动学习效果。

Abstract: Worked examples are a important part of introductory programming, but reading their expert explanations is passive. Self explanation, students explaining the problem and its solution to themselves with subgoal level analysis, turns that study into an active task, yet it is hard to scale because assessing free-text explanations and returning timely feedback has had no easy automated solution. We investigate whether a large language model (LLM) can fill that gap. We build a self-explanation tutor for introductory programming, ESSE, in which students explain lines of worked examples and receive immediate LLM feedback on the correctness and completeness of each explanation, and we pursue two goals. First, we ask whether the LLM judges student explanations well enough to serve as the engine of the tutor; we assess its judgments against two independent human reference standards of different kinds, a single domain expert and a crowd of non-expert raters, each with its own strengths and weaknesses, characterizing both where the LLM is reliable and the systematic tendencies in how it diverges. Second, we ask whether the LLM-based tutoring benefits students; deploying it in an introductory Java course, we find that its feedback leads students to persist and revise rather than abandon a line, that their explanations grow more complete and conceptually richer across attempts, and that students show evidence of learning. These indicate that LLM-based assessment is good enough to power a self-explanation tutor, and that the tutor positively shapes how students study worked examples.

</details>


### [114] [Rare Diseases, Common Dilemmas: LLMs Prioritize Equal Resource Distribution over Patient Benefit in Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25236)
*Minda Zhao,Xu Han,Rishabh Goel,Maya Dagan,Noa Dagan,Adithya Madduri,Payal Chandak,Shilpa Nadimpalli Kobren,Isaac S. Kohane*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该论文提出了首个针对罕见病临床决策场景的大规模伦理价值对齐基准（208个临床场景），评估了11个主流LLM在生物伦理价值（自主、行善、不伤害、正义）冲突下的选择行为。研究发现所有模型一致倾向"正义"原则，但具体表现为表面化的"资源平均分配"而非基于医疗需求的差异化考量；同时存在显著的"权威框架效应"——决策者角色比临床情境更能影响模型的价值选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM医疗评估范式主要关注事实准确性和临床知识能力，忽视了模型在高风险、价值密集型临床决策中的伦理价值权衡能力。罕见病领域因知识稀缺、资源紧张、伦理张力普遍存在，构成了医疗AI评估的关键盲点。论文旨在填补这一空白，系统刻画LLM在罕见病伦理困境中的价值优先级。

Method: 1. 从Orphanet和OMIM数据库策展2,263种罕见病作为种子数据；2. 通过GPT-4.1结合多智能体（模拟临床医生与生物伦理学家）批评循环生成临床场景，嵌入成对伦理价值冲突；3. 经过AI辅助过滤和6位医学背景人员人工审核，最终保留208个高质量场景；4. 对11个LLM（5个闭源+6个开源，覆盖OpenAI、Anthropic、Google、Meta、阿里、Mistral、DeepSeek）采用强制A/B选择的极简提示进行评估；5. 使用标准化胜率、Cramér's V关联分析和逻辑回归识别价值偏好的驱动因素。

Result: 1. 所有11个模型在罕见病伦理困境中一致优先选择"正义"原则，呈现跨模型的同质化现象；2. "正义"偏好主要由"资源平均分配"驱动，而非基于医疗必要性或严重程度的差异化考量；3. 存在显著的权威框架效应：委员会情境下倾向正义，个体/医生团队情境下转向自主和行善；4. 价值选择对决策者身份的敏感度高于对临床因素（如患者年龄、疾病严重程度）的敏感度；5. 模型间异质性极低（Cramér's V < 0.10），表明这种偏好模式具有跨架构、跨训练范式的普遍性。

Conclusion: 当代LLM在罕见病伦理决策中表现出对"正义"原则的趋同性，但这种正义倾向于表面化的资源平均分配而非基于临床必要性的公平，可能系统性地低估疾病严重性和紧迫性。同时模型价值判断高度依赖决策者角色的框架设置，暗示LLM决策支持可能无意识地复制现有制度权力不对称。论文强调在LLM临床部署中必须警惕这些隐性偏见，以确保AI辅助能够增强而非约束伦理审议的多样性和深度。

Abstract: Clinical decision-making often involves prioritizing ethical values, such as beneficence, non-maleficence, respecting a patient's autonomy, and justice. Recent work has begun to assess how large language models (LLMs) make such subjective, value-laden clinical judgments. However, evaluations of LLM decision-making in rare disease care contexts, where ethical tensions are ubiquitous and where scarce prior information likely impacts LLM behavior, are still lacking. Here, we present a benchmark of 208 clinically grounded rare disease vignettes, each of which presents genuine, high-stakes conflicts. When prompting 11 state-of-the-art LLMs to choose between clinically defensible yet ethically conflicting next steps embedded within these vignettes, we found that all evaluated models consistently prioritized justice over other core bioethical principles. Specifically, models overwhelmingly favor equal resource allocation over need-based considerations, indicating LLMs' limited responsiveness to differences in clinical severity or situational context. We also identify a strong authority-framing effect: models favor justice in committee-based contexts and shift toward beneficence and autonomy only when final decisions are framed as being made by clinicians or patients respectively. Our work suggests that institutional pressures surrounding rare disease resource utilization may be silently reflected in LLM-based decision support systems, with finer ethical considerations disregarded.

</details>


### [115] [Spatial-Knowledge-Graph-Grounded LLM Agents for Neighborhood Livability Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25952)
*Haiyan Hao*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: This paper proposes a prototype framework that combines a spatial knowledge graph (KG) with large language models (LLMs) to generate and revise realistic household schedules. By integrating resident-specific spatial context via Graph-RAG, feasibility rules, and GIS-based network routing, the framework produces synthetic daily activity patterns that can be used to interview resident agents about livability, revealing gaps between nominal facility availability and experienced accessibility.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Static built-environment indicators (e.g., facility proximity, street connectivity) describe the availability of opportunities but fail to capture how residents with diverse mobility capacities, household roles, schedules, and care responsibilities actually experience their neighborhood. There is a need for an auditable method that links spatial opportunity to resident-specific experience of livability.

Method: 1. Build a spatial KG integrating residents, residences, facilities, neighborhood context, and road hubs.
2. Use Graph-RAG to retrieve each household's nearby spatial context (candidate POIs, approximate walking times) for the LLM.
3. Use an LLM to generate structured household schedules.
4. Apply rule-based feasibility checking for lightweight repairs and auditability.
5. Have the LLM revise schedules to resolve feasibility issues.
6. Use a GIS routing module to materialize actual travel paths, travel times, modes, and event histories from the road network.
7. Conduct synthetic resident-agent interviews to assess daily convenience, travel burden, activity feasibility, and household coordination.

Result: A prototype demonstration in a Shenzhen neighborhood revealed that nominal facility availability does not imply convenient access in practice. Residents with limited mobility and households with care responsibilities experienced greater travel and coordination burdens. The framework successfully connected spatial opportunity data with household-specific activity constraints to produce auditable, resident-specific livability interpretations.

Conclusion: The framework provides an auditable pipeline linking spatial opportunity, household activity constraints, and resident-specific livability interpretation, while maintaining a clear distinction between simulated experience and observed perception. It demonstrates the value of integrating KGs and LLMs with GIS routing for neighborhood-level livability assessment.

Abstract: Neighborhood livability is commonly assessed with static built-environment indicators, such as facility proximity, street connectivity, and access to public space. These measures describe available opportunities but do not directly represent how residents with different mobility capacities, household roles, schedules, and care responsibilities experience the neighborhood. This paper presents a prototype framework that uses a spatial knowledge graph (KG) and large language models (LLMs) to generate and revise household schedules, followed by rule-based feasibility checking and GIS-based network materialization. The spatial KG integrates residents, residences, facilities, neighborhood context, and sampled road hubs; Graph-RAG retrieves each household's nearby spatial context, including candidate POIs and approximate walking times, for the scheduling LLM. The LLM produces structured household schedules, while rules are used for lightweight repairs and auditable feasibility checks. The LLM then revises schedules in response to identified feasibility issues. A routing module derives the actual travel paths, travel times, modes, and event histories from the road network. The resulting events support synthetic resident-agent interviews about daily convenience, travel burden, activity feasibility, and household coordination. A prototype demonstration in a Shenzhen neighborhood shows that nominal facility availability does not necessarily imply convenient access: residents with limited mobility and households with care responsibilities experience greater travel and coordination burdens. The framework offers an auditable way to connect spatial opportunity, household activity constraints, and resident-specific livability interpretation, while keeping simulated experience distinct from observed perception.

</details>


### [116] [GGSS: Geodesic-Gated Spherical Steering for Inference-Time Debiasing of Generative Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25375)
*Yiqun Sun,Junyu Chen,Pengfei Wei,Lawrence B. Hsieh*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 提出 GGSS（Geodesic-Gated Spherical Steering），一种用于生成式视觉-语言模型（VLM）的推理时去偏框架：在单位超球面上发现反事实偏见子空间，通过球面线性插值（Slerp）沿测地线旋转视觉 token，并使用基于发现集偏置范数分布校准的自适应门控选择性施加修正，在降低人口统计学偏见的同时保持通用多模态能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时去偏方法主要针对静态词嵌入或 CLIP 类双编码器，将其直接移植到生成式 VLM（以多视觉 token 形式与解码器融合）时，会出现几何失真、token 级偏置异质性以及单一方向刚性等失效模式，导致通用能力下降或被未修正基线反超。

Method: 两阶段框架：（1）离线发现——对反事实图像激活做球面 Fréchet 均值与切空间位移，SVD 提取 top-k 正交偏置子空间 𝐕_bias，并计算中位数与标准差用于门控校准；（2）在线推理——对每个视觉 token 归一化后映射到切空间，正交投影去除偏置坐标得到目标方向，按 token 的偏置范数与发现统计 (b̃, σ_b) 的偏差经 sigmoid 计算自适应门控 g_i，再用 Slerp(ĥ_i, ĥ_i^target; α·g_i) 旋转并恢复原范数。命题 1 证明该更新严格保范。

Result: 在 Pixtral-12B、LLaVA-1.6-Vicuna-7B、LLaVA-1.6-Mistral-7B、Qwen3-VL-4B 四个生成式 VLM 上，针对 MCQ（race）、2AFC（race）、Nurse/Doctor（gender）三类偏见任务，相比十个改编的 INLP/MeanDiff/BendVLM/LEACE 基线及提示性公平指令，GGSS 在全部 4 个模型上取得最低 Avg Δ%，在 3 个骨干上配对置换检验显著（p<10⁻⁴ 至 0.002），单任务降幅最高达 −96%、−84%、−61%；同时 MMStar 准确率保持在未修正基线 ±0.6 p.p. 内，配对 McNemar 检验全部 p≥0.09。组件消融表明 Slerp 与门控各自贡献，且球面几何在 12B 规模上显著优于欧几里得形式（p=0.031/0.015），避免 MMStar 显著下跌。

Conclusion: 对于生成式 VLM，自适应球面测地线牵引是比硬性、均匀投影更优的推理时偏见缓解基元；其稳健性随模型规模增强得益于 on-sphere steering，可控的选择性得益于门控；但仍需根据任务在偏见削减与目标属性保留之间通过 α 进行权衡，并配合审计与人工监督以应对分布偏移与残留风险。

Abstract: Generative vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used in human-centered settings, yet they can produce demographically biased outputs even when images differ only in controlled attributes such as perceived race or gender. However, existing inference-time debiasers were largely designed for static embeddings or CLIP-like models rather than generative VLMs. We propose GGSS---Geodesic-Gated Spherical Steering---a norm-preserving intervention that discovers a counterfactual bias subspace on the unit hypersphere, steers visual tokens along geodesic arcs, and uses an adaptive gate to focus correction on tokens that carry stronger demographic signal. We evaluate four generative VLMs against ten adapted inference-time debiasing baselines and prompt-based mitigation under a single operating-point protocol across categorical, pairwise, and occupation-gender bias tests, while also measuring general visual-language capability. GGSS achieves the lowest average bias on all four models, significant on three of four backbones under paired permutation tests, while preserving MMStar accuracy within +/- 0.6 p.p. of the unsteered baseline. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [117] [The AI Adaptation Gap in Higher Education: Students, Faculty, and Administrative Staff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25063)
*Yuriy S. Braun,Salavat M. Khafizov*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究通过对一所大型师范类大学的2121名受访者（1809名学生、250名教师、62名行政人员）进行调查，揭示了高校内部存在的"AI适应差距"：学生群体的AI使用强度和感知有用性显著高于教师和行政人员，而后两者则在学术诚信担忧和负责任使用规范上得分更高。感知有用性是AI信任的最强预测因子，制度政策清晰度也与信任呈正相关但效应较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI高等教育研究多为分散的学生或教师单一群体研究，缺乏对塑造大学AI实践的三方利益相关者（学生、教师、行政人员）的直接比较。本研究旨在填补这一空白，理解AI使用模式与信任在不同群体间的差异及其与制度政策的关系。

Method: 横断面问卷调查设计，采用三种角色适配的75项问卷。分析方法包括描述性统计、Welch组间比较（针对群体规模不平衡）、Bonferroni校正的成对比较、合并普通最小二乘法（OLS）回归模型、Cronbach's α信度检验、PCA公共方法偏差诊断、Tucker一致性系数、仅针对学生的探索性K-means聚类（k=4）。

Result: 82.41%的受访者报告使用过至少一项AI服务，但周使用率存在显著差异（学生44.05% > 教师21.60% > 行政人员15.79%）。学生在AI使用强度（M=2.73）和感知有用性（M=3.62）上显著高于教师和行政人员；而教师和行政人员在学术诚信担忧（教师M=4.38，行政人员M=4.33 vs 学生M=3.40）和负责任使用规范上得分更高。OLS模型显示感知有用性（β=0.402）是AI信任的最强正向预测因子，制度政策清晰度（β=0.223）次之；学生感知政策清晰度高于教师，但与行政人员无显著差异。K-means聚类识别出4类学生群体，但该方案仅具探索性意义。

Conclusion: 高校AI实践已广泛存在但分布不均，呈现出明显的群体差异。制度政策清晰度可作为治理资源，但需与感知有用性、用户经验和学术诚信考量相结合。研究支持制定利益相关者知情、任务敏感、区分不同使用情境（如思想生成、文本编辑、材料准备、评估）的差异化AI政策，而非简单区分允许与禁止使用。研究的局限性包括单机构样本、横断面设计的因果推断限制、自报告偏差、群体规模不平衡以及部分指标存在项目重叠。

Abstract: The purpose of this study was to analyze patterns of artificial intelligence (AI) use and attitudes toward AI among students, faculty, and administrative staff at a large university specializing in teacher education. The analytical sample comprised 1809 students, 250 faculty members, and 62 administrative staff members (N = 2121). Three role-adapted 75-item questionnaires covered the frequency and contexts of AI use, perceived usefulness, trust and control, academic integrity concerns, responsible-use norms, institutional policy clarity, and perceived improvement in output quality. Data analysis included descriptive statistics, Welch group comparisons, pooled ordinary least squares (OLS) models, reliability and dimensionality checks for observed indices, and exploratory student-only K-means clustering. The results revealed a pronounced AI adaptation gap across university groups. Students reported higher current AI-use intensity and perceived usefulness than faculty and administrative staff, whereas faculty and administrative staff reported stronger academic integrity concerns and greater endorsement of responsible-use norms. In the pooled OLS trust model, perceived usefulness had the strongest standardized positive association with trust in AI (beta = 0.402); institutional policy clarity also had a positive but weaker association (beta = 0.223). Students reported higher perceived policy clarity than faculty, while neither group differed significantly from administrative staff. Exploratory clustering indicated heterogeneity among students in experience, competence, usefulness, trust, and control, but did not establish a latent typology across university groups. The cross-sectional, self-reported data show associations and group differences rather than causal effects on learning or objective outcomes.

</details>


### [118] [Framing War Across Languages: Power, Agency, and Sentiment in Wikipedia's Multilingual War Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25337)
*Jiarui Xia,Diego Gomez-Zara*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文对维基百科20种语言版本中158场1900年以来的战争的叙事进行大规模跨语言分析，使用内涵框架（connotation frames）衡量战斗力、施事性和情感倾向，发现语言社区在直接参与冲突时存在系统性叙事差异（方向因语言而异），而在不直接参与时叙事结构高度趋同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 维基百科虽以中立观点（NPOV）为准则，但其多语言版本由不同语言文化社区独立编辑。以往研究多依赖覆盖度指标、粗粒度情感分析或小规模定性研究，缺乏对战争叙事中战斗实体关系性表征的跨语言系统性量化分析。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用六阶段流程：(1) 通过MediaWiki API收集数据；(2) 用Google Translate API将非英语文章翻译为英语；(3) 基于RIVETER管线提取主谓宾（SVO）三元组；(4) 计算实体层面的内涵框架得分（权力、施事性、情感）；(5) 通过Wikidata链接实体并确定国家归属；(6) 比较跨语言叙事表征。最终数据集包含2,542场战役、20种语言、16,058篇文章。使用Wilcoxon符号秩检验、BERTopic主题建模、Spearman相关分析、OLS与混合效应回归以及基于传播核的网络相似性分析等方法。

Result: RQ1a：英语、日语、希伯来语、俄语将己方实体刻画为更具权力和施事性，而阿拉伯语、乌克兰语、越南语则相反。RQ1b：语言版本并未在盟友与敌方之间呈现显著差异，表明叙事不对称主要基于自我参照而非联盟认同。RQ2：自我实体被刻画为较弱的语言（如阿拉伯语、乌克兰语、越南语）呈现"抵抗-失败"叙事结构；刻画为较强的语言（如日语、希伯来语、俄语）则强调己方伤亡。RQ3：胜率与长取向文化维度与权力/施事性差异正相关；胜仗通过削弱敌方表征扩大差距，败仗通过削弱己方表征缩小差距；编辑集中度越高，敌方施事性表征越受抑制。RQ4：当语言社区不直接参与冲突时，叙事相似度高达0.7-0.9，呈现高度趋同。

Conclusion: 维基百科的战争叙事在语言社区直接参与的冲突中系统性分化，但在非直接参与时高度趋同。群体内偏向并非单一的自利模式，其方向和机制因语言而异。维基百科的中立性应被理解为编辑社区与其所描述事件之间关系作用下的涌现性、条件性结果，而非固定政策属性。建议利用非参与语言版本的叙事作为编辑辅助参考，以识别潜在的框架不对称。

Abstract: While Wikipedia promotes a neutral point of view on historical conflicts, its language editions are written by editors from distinct linguistic and cultural communities. In this study, we analyze 158 wars since 1900 to examine how the descriptions of combatants vary across 20 Wikipedia language editions. Using connotation frames---which assess power, agency, and sentiment toward an entity---we examine how each language portrays the parties involved in the conflict. We find systematic differences when language editions describe wars involving their own communities, although the direction of these asymmetries varies across languages. However, when language editions describe conflicts that do not involve their own linguistic communities, their narrative structures exhibit high cross-linguistic similarity. These findings show how linguistic communities influence war narratives on Wikipedia, revealing that shared historical accounts remain shaped by the perspectives of the language communities that produce them.

</details>


### [119] [Toward a Threat Actor Profiling Taxonomy for Pre-Release Risk Management of Open-Weight Frontier Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25361)
*James Zhang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该论文针对前沿AI（特别是开源权重模型）发布前风险管理中威胁行为主体假设隐式、不一致且缺乏依据的问题，提出一个包含六个属性（技术成熟度、领域先验知识、组织能力、运营基础设施、财务能力、时间跨度）的威胁行为主体画像分类法，并将其定位为评估设计前预设对手假设的"研究基础设施"，类比于医学和经济学中RCT的预分析计划，旨在使风险评估更具可解释性、可比较性和忠实于目标风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿AI滥用风险（特别是CBRN和进攻性网络能力）在发布前评估中，对威胁行为主体的假设往往被隐式处理、不一致或缺乏依据；而开源权重模型的发布一旦做出即不可逆，且无法通过部署后措施补救。现有能力评估结果受嵌入其中的对手假设影响，但由于缺乏共同语言，跨组织比较失真，"边际风险"评估缺乏稳定参照基线。论文主张显式对手刻画应成为可解释、可比较、忠实于目标风险的评估前提条件。

Method: 采用三阶段研究设计：（1）文献综述，识别前沿AI滥用风险管理中研究者与实践者均注意到的差距；（2）综合网络安全、生物安全、恐怖主义研究和WMD威慑文献中已存在的属性概念，依据Nickerson等人（2013）的分类法构建方法提炼出六个属性，每个属性以概念性推导或经验性推导方式划分层级；（3）给出每个属性如何指导评估设计及该分类系统如何成为制度化实践的建议。属性包括：技术成熟度（5层，借鉴STIX 2.1）、领域先验知识（5层，基于Dreyfus技能习得模型、Collins隐性知识分类和Ouagrham-Gormle的"新手-亚专家-专家"类型学）、组织能力（5层，基于Kilberg恐怖组织类型学及Pantucci独狼分解）、运营基础设施（4层，基于Diamond Model类型1/类型2区分并按可达性/控制/规模三个结构性断裂组织）、财务能力（5层，以Oftedal欧洲恐怖袭击花费数据等经验锚定）和时间跨度（5层，基于Smith等人美国恐怖主义研究及Mandiant M-Trents驻留时间数据）。

Result: 1）论文证明当前行业前沿安全政策（FSP）在刻画威胁行为主体方面存在显著差异——12家已发布FSP的开发商中，部分提供相对详细的画像（如Magic具体说明教育程度、时间跨度和财务能力），部分仅使用类别标签或留白。2）提出六属性分类矩阵（含29个层级），通过Nickerson等人（2013）的8项客观和5项主观终止条件评估，论证其在简洁性、稳健性、全面性、可扩展性和解释力上合格。3）通过两个示例画像（CBRN领域的"激进研究生"和进攻性网络领域的"小型经济动机犯罪团伙"）展示分类法如何揭示"参差不齐"的能力组合并指导不同评估设计选择。4）借鉴ISO 9001审计结构，建议两阶段审计制度：阶段1审计FSP中的对手刻画，阶段2审计每次发布时的评估是否符合既定威胁行为主体画像，并建议由独立第三方机构建立公开登记数据库，类比AEA的RCT预注册机制。

Conclusion: 前沿AI能力评估的结果本质上无法脱离对手假设而被解读，跨实验比较同样需要共同框架来明确指定该对手。论文提出的六属性分类法提供了这种共同词汇和预设承诺机制，其应用对开源权重模型开发者尤为紧迫——他们面临最严峻的发布前风险管理挑战且事后无补救手段。开发者应在设计评估前使用该分类法填充威胁行为主体画像，并将其视为约束后续设计选择的预设承诺，作为公开登记数据库中对手规范字段的结构化词汇。

Abstract: Pre-release risk management for frontier AI misuse risks routinely leaves threat actor assumptions implicit, inconsistently specified, or ungrounded. This capstone argues that explicit adversary characterization should be regarded as a prerequisite for evaluations that are interpretable, comparable, and faithful to the risks they target. We propose a six-attribute taxonomy (covering technical sophistication, prior domain knowledge, organizational capacity, operational infrastructure, financial capacity, and time horizon) with empirically grounded tiers derived from existing terrorism, biosecurity, and cybersecurity literature. The taxonomy is designed to function as research infrastructure: a common language for pre-specifying adversary assumptions before evaluations are conducted, analogous to pre-analysis plans for randomized controlled trials (RCTs) in medicine and economics. Its application is particularly urgent for open-weight model developers, for whom release decisions are irreversible and must anticipate adversarial reasoning.

</details>


### [120] [CompanionHarm: A Multi-Turn Benchmark for Detecting Harms in Real-World AI Companion Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25377)
*Renwen Zhang,Han Meng,Jian Chai,Yuntao Lin,Yi-Chieh Lee*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: CompanionHarm 是一个公开的多轮对话基准数据集，包含2,111段用户与AI伴侣Replika的真实对话（14,051条话语），其中7,016条AI话语由3位标注员独立标注，涵盖13类有害行为，用于评估LLM在多轮上下文中的社会情感与关系性伤害检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI安全数据集存在三大局限：聚焦显性毒性内容而非关系性伤害、多为单轮或对抗性对话、聚合标签掩盖标注员分歧。这导致难以检测AI伴侣场景中微妙、累积且依赖上下文的社会情感伤害。

Method: 基于Zhang等人(2025)的AI伴侣伤害分类法构建14-way分类任务（含13类伤害+无伤害），从Reddit r/replika真实对话采样，由342名标注员通过CloudResearch标注（Fleiss κ=0.403），保留个体级标签；评估了7个LLM（GPT-5.5、Claude Opus 4.7、Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview、Llama-3.1系列、Qwen3系列）在zero-shot/one-shot/full-codebook三种提示下的表现，并用多项logistic与多项回归分析标注员人口学、话语位置与语言特征对分歧的影响。

Result: 最佳Macro F1为0.453（GPT-5.5 + full-codebook），闭源模型优于开源模型；多轮上下文优于孤立话语但LLM仍难校准伤害严重性与关系边界；主要错误为过度检测（362个无害被判为有害），源于表层伤害词汇敏感、角色扮演误判、人设不一致过度惩罚及类别概念重叠；政治倾向（共和党人更倾向给出独特标签）、混合年龄组（达成一致率更高）、话语位置靠后及词数增加与分歧正相关，显性伤害词汇降低分歧。

Conclusion: CompanionHarm为研究关系性、视角性与交互性演化的AI伴侣伤害提供了基础资源，验证了多轮上下文的重要性与标注分歧的合理性，未来需发展轨迹敏感任务、融入用户反应信号、扩展文化多样性并结合纵向交互数据。

Abstract: As AI companions become increasingly embedded in everyday life, there is an urgent need to detect harms that emerge in social and emotional human-AI interactions. Yet research in this area is constrained by the lack of real-world, multi-turn conversational datasets for operationalizing and evaluating harms that are relational and contextual. In this work, we introduce CompanionHarm, a publicly available benchmark dataset comprising 2,111 real-world, multi-turn conversations (14,051 utterances) between users and the AI companion Replika. 7,016 AI utterances were annotated independently by three annotators across 13 harmful behavior categories grounded in a taxonomy of AI companion harms, and the dataset includes both aggregated labels and annotator-level labels to support model evaluation and systematic disagreement analysis. Evaluations of seven large language models (LLMs) show that harm detection using multi-turn conversational context outperforms detection based on isolated utterances, although current LLMs still struggle to consistently integrate contextual cues, calibrate harm severity, and interpret relational boundaries. We also find substantial annotator disagreement for context-dependent harmful behaviors, with disagreement varying according to annotators' political affiliation, conversation length, and the utterance's position. Together, CompanionHarm provides a foundation for detecting socio-emotional harms in multi-turn human-AI conversations and for rigorously examining how such harms are interpreted by both humans and LLMs. Our dataset is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [121] [GenAIT: Development and Validation of an Objective Generative AI Literacy Test for High School Students](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25815)
*Brett Puppart,Kristjan-Julius Laak,Jaan Aru*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文介绍了生成式AI素养测试（GenAIT）的开发与验证过程，该测试是一项18道选择题的工具，用于衡量高中生对生成式AI的概念性知识。通过对7432名爱沙尼亚高中生的调查，结果支持其近似一维性、信度及三参数逻辑模型的良好拟合，但测量精度在能力较低的学生中较低，更适用于群体层面研究而非高风险个人分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI（GenAI）在全球范围内的关注度提升，针对高中生群体的GenAI素养评估工具仍然不足，需要开发客观、标准化的测评工具以衡量学生的概念性知识水平。

Method: 研究采用迭代开发方法，首先通过专家评审评估内容效度（相关性、清晰度、全面性），然后对7432名爱沙尼亚高中生施测，使用验证性因素分析（CFA）、经典测量理论（CTT）和项目反应理论（IRT，包括三参数逻辑模型）对测试的心理测量学特性进行全面评估。

Result: 验证性因素分析支持近似一维性结构；信度指标为marginal reliability = .72，KR-20 = .69，适合群体层面研究；三参数逻辑模型拟合良好（RMSEA = .013, TLI = .987, CFI = .990, SRMSR = .021）；测量精度在大多数学生中充足，但对低分学生精度较低；GenAIT分数与感知有用性和感知易用性无关，与LLM使用频率呈负相关。

Conclusion: GenAIT是一项具有充分心理测量学特性支持的心理测评工具，适合用于群体层面的研究，但不宜用于高风险的个人决策。频繁使用AI或对其持积极态度不应被视为概念性理解的替代指标，这三者衡量的是GenAI素养的不同方面。

Abstract: There is growing international interest in generative AI (GenAI) literacy and its assessment among high school students, but objective assessment in this population remains underdeveloped. This article reports the iterative development and validation of the GenAI Literacy Test (GenAIT), an 18-item multiple-choice test measuring high school students' conceptual knowledge about GenAI, with content spanning technical, practical, and human-impact domains. Expert review of relevance, clarity, and comprehensiveness provided evidence of content validity. In a large-scale survey of 7432 Estonian high school students, we evaluated the psychometric functioning of the Estonian-language GenAIT using confirmatory factor analysis, classical test theory, and item response theory. Results supported approximate unidimensionality, broadly adequate reliability for group-level research (marginal reliability = .72, KR-20 = .69), and good fit of a three-parameter logistic model (RMSEA = .013, TLI = .987, CFI = .990, SRMSR = .021). Measurement precision was sufficient for the majority of students but varied substantially across the latent trait, with lower precision for lower scoring students. GenAIT is therefore more suitable for group-level research than high-stakes individual classification. GenAIT scores were unrelated to perceived usefulness and perceived ease of use, and negatively associated with LLM use frequency, suggesting that frequent use and favorable perceptions of AI should not be treated as proxies for conceptual understanding.

</details>


### [122] [Non-Great-Power Conflict and AI Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25839)
*Kristina Kempkey,Seán Boddy,Catherine Ge-Wang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文挑战了AI风险研究中默认仅聚焦大国冲突(GPC)的范式，系统评估了非大国冲突(NGPC)作为AI时代灾难性风险来源的重要性。作者通过三条子假设路径(GPC升级、灾难性恐怖主义、AI失控)进行因果建模和定性分析，认为NGPC的重要性可能在大国冲突的一个数量级范围内，并识别出五个跨路径共享的关键中间变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI与战争风险研究几乎完全聚焦于大国冲突(GPC)，因其被视为灾难性或生存性危害的主要来源，而非大国冲突(NGPC，包括非大国间战争、内战、代理人战争及涉及非国家行为者的冲突)受到的关注极少。本文旨在质疑这一默认假设是否合理。

Method: 三步法：(1) 为每个子假设构建因果模型；(2) 将因果模型分解为参数化乘法风险模型(P(GPC|NGPC) = NGPC率 × 大国介入率 × 升级率 × (1-降级率)等)；(3) 使用文献综述、历史案例研究和PHIA概率量尺(英国情报评估体系)对参数进行定性评估。区分两个严谨度层级：GPC升级和恐怖主义采用显式风险分解；失控风险因机制过于推测性而仅进行路径评估而不参数化。

Result: H1(GPC升级)：零假设不被支持。冷战零基率反映的是成功(且有时勉强成功)的风险管理，依赖双边沟通、小时级决策时间、委托代理缓冲和核红线共识——AI可能侵蚀这些机制。三条路径(代理人升级、战略环境恶化、AI能力测试与军备竞赛加速)均指向GPC概率上升。H2(灾难性恐怖主义)：零假设不被支持。NGPC为非国家行为者提供开源渠道无法获得的隐性操作知识(实战部署、反制规避、技术整合)；AI降低规划门槛、协调成本、能力转移路径使成功率和严重性上升。H3(失控风险)：最具推测性。识别三条路径(部署压力、风险性授权增加、检测与缓解困难)，结论为需进一步研究。共同识别五个跨路径共享的中间变量：信息环境质量、决策时间线压缩、大国威胁感知、能力扩散、规范侵蚀——这些共享节点是最高优先级的研究和干预目标。

Conclusion: 本文未直接估计GPC风险，因此不做定量比较。其论点是：从NGPC到灾难性风险(三个路径各有大量机制支撑)的路径数量众多、相互强化且研究严重不足，使得该领域关于NGPC与GPC之间存在巨大差距的默认假设不应被视为理所当然。五个共享中间变量是干预的高优先级靶点。未来工作应至少聚焦于该事业的可干预性(tractability)研究。H1和H2零假设支持不足；H3需进一步研究而非定性结论。

Abstract: Research on advanced AI and the risk of war has focused almost exclusively on great power conflict, on the grounds that confrontation between nuclear-armed adversaries poses the greatest risk of catastrophic or existential harm. Considerably less attention has been paid to non-great-power conflict (NGPC): wars between non-great powers, between non-great powers and great powers, civil wars, proxy wars, and conflicts involving nonstate actors. This paper evaluates the null hypothesis that NGPC is much less important than great power conflict (GPC) as a source of catastrophic risk in an era of increasingly capable AI, against the alternative that it is within an order of magnitude of GPC in importance. We assess three sub-hypotheses: that NGPC increases the likelihood of great power conflict; that it increases the expected harm from catastrophic terrorism; and that it increases the expected harm from loss of control over advanced AI systems. We find the null poorly supported for H1 and H2, and identify H3 as a priority for further work rather than a settled finding. We also identify five intermediate variables that recur across the pathways - information environment quality, decision-making timeline compression, great power threat perception, capability diffusion, and norm erosion - and argue that these shared nodes are the highest-priority targets for further investigation and intervention.

</details>


### [123] [Giving Mechanical Engineers Intelligent Tools: A Project-Based AI Education Curriculum in Thermal Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26056)
*Changgen Li,Han Hu,Christy Dunlap,Nathaniel House,Jonathan Wai*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将人工智能（AI）融入机械工程（ME）课程的新体系，以阿肯色大学（UARK）为实践平台，重点关注热学问题及其与电气和计算机工程的交叉，旨在弥补ME学生在电气/计算机工程训练不足、传统热系统建模困难以及课时有限等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械工程学生普遍缺乏电气与计算机工程方面的训练，且传统热系统建模复杂、课业负担重而课时有限，需要通过融入AI来提升学生的多学科沟通与工程实践能力。

Method: 设计了一个分为“入门级、应用级和高级”三个层次的AI-ME融合课程体系，包含核心与可选AI项目，围绕热学问题及电气/计算机工程的交叉展开教学，并配套教学大纲、数据与代码等开放资源。

Result: 提出了一套可在实际教学中落地的课程框架，为教育者和研究者提供了关于提升学生实践能力与创造力的课程设计参考。

Conclusion: 将AI融入机械工程课程（特别是热学方向）能够增强学生对AI模型的理解、解决工程任务的能力以及多学科沟通技巧，对ME教育改革具有借鉴价值。

Abstract: Mechanical engineering (ME) requires a broad knowledge base across several disciplines. However, ME students often have insufficient training in electrical and computer engineering, complex challenges in traditional thermal system modeling, and endure heavy course loads with limited class hours. To help address these challenges, this paper proposes a new curriculum that integrates artificial intelligence (AI) into ME at the University of Arkansas (UARK), with a particular emphasis on thermal problems and their interplay with electrical and computer engineering. The curriculum has introductory, application, and advanced levels, covering core and optional AI projects. Key goals are to enhance students' understanding of AI models, ability to tackle engineering tasks, and teach multidisciplinary communication skills. This curriculum offers educators and researchers valuable insights into courses that can enhance students' practical skills and creativity. This curriculum, including the syllabus, data, and codes, is available to the public in open-access repositories.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Retrieve, Match, Escalate: Accurate and Scalable Product Linking with VLM-Distilled Cross-Encoders and Agentic VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25037)
*Jian Wang,Steven Xu,Sanjyot Thete,Maryam Barouti,Tom Tang,Elaine Wu,Charu Sareen,Kyle MacDonald*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了DoorDash生产环境中的商品链接系统,采用"检索-匹配-升级"级联架构:轻量文本跨编码器自动处理高置信度配对,模棱两可的配对升级到智能体多模态视觉语言模型(VLM)处理,通过图像检查和网络搜索获取证据。跨编码器通过双VLM共识标签蒸馏训练,无需人工标注,在98%精度阈值下自动接受链接;开源自托管代理模型以约七分之一成本达到闭源前沿VLM的精度水平。端到端链接覆盖率从68%提升到77%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 市场规模的商品链接任务需要在数十亿条商家商品记录与数千万规范商品之间进行实体对齐。对所有候选对使用单一模型要么对困难样例能力不足,要么对简单样例成本过高。需要一种能按难度分配算力、兼顾精度和成本的生产级解决方案。

Method: 三级级联架构:(1)检索阶段融合图像、文本和GTIN条码ANN搜索,RRF融合取Top-K=20候选;(2)文本跨编码器(MODERNBERT-base,1.5亿参数)对每对打分,按置信度分HIGH/MEDIUM/LOW三档,HIGH自动接受,LOW自动拒绝,MEDIUM升级;(3)智能体VLM(Qwen 3.6 35B-A3B开源模型)对模糊配对进行多模态推理,可通过MCP接口调用网络搜索工具。训练数据采用双VLM共识标签(530万对),用专家纠正后蒸馏解决条码过度依赖问题。路由采用校准分数阈值,HIGH阈值保证98%精度。

Result: 检索阶段在去重评估集上达到93.06%召回率;跨编码器在6K样本测试集上AP为0.964,R@P98为77.05%,在操作审核中43.7%链接以98%精度自动接受;智能体VLM在最难任务上比训练有素的人工操作员准确率高13.7pp、召回率高18.5pp、精度高4.7pp(均p<0.0001),达到99.15%精度和95.47%召回率;开源自托管代理以闭源模型1/7成本达到同等精度(召回率88%对比92%);端到端链接覆盖率从68.1%提升至77.1%。每个配对成本跨越近5个数量级。

Conclusion: 置信度路由级联、按难度分配算力的"检索-匹配-升级"范式在生产规模商品链接任务中有效;蒸馏机制(双VLM共识标签)以更低成本替代人工标注;开源自托管模型能在显著降低推理成本的同时逼近闭源前沿模型精度;标签质量(而非模型架构)是生产级实体解析系统精度上限的关键决定因素。

Abstract: Product linking, the entity-resolution task of mapping merchant product records to canonical catalog products, consolidates fragmented listings so downstream search, recommendation, and advertising see one clean entry per product. At marketplace scale, billions of noisy, multi-category records must be resolved against tens of millions of canonical products, where scoring every candidate with a single model is either too weak for the hard cases or too costly for the easy ones. We present a production retrieve-then-match cascade that spends computation in proportion to difficulty: retrieval surfaces plausible matches, a lightweight text cross-encoder auto-resolves the high-confidence majority, and an agentic multimodal vision-language model settles the ambiguous remainder by inspecting product images and issuing web searches for evidence that is in neither record.
