The cross-encoder is distilled from millions of dual-VLM-consensus labels, retiring human annotation from the training set, and is calibrated to auto-accept links at a 98% precision bar validated against a smaller operator-certified audit. The agent is a self-hosted open-weight model that reaches a closed frontier VLM's precision at a four-point recall cost (88% versus 92%) for roughly one-seventh the per-pair cost, with no fine-tuning. Per-pair cost spans nearly five orders of magnitude from the cheap cross-encoder to the frontier VLM, so escalating only the hard tail to the agent raises end-to-end link coverage from the cheap stage's 68% to 77%.

</details>


### [125] [VLM-based automatic multi-granularity graph representation of building layouts for design informatics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24886)
*Song Guo,Zhuoshi Chen,Maosu Li,Weimin Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于视觉-语言模型（VLM）的自动化多粒度图表示方法（Level-of-Graphs, LoGs），用于将公共建筑（以学术图书馆为例）的平面图图像转换为任务自适应的图结构知识表示，无需人工标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑平面图蕴含丰富的功能空间关系知识，对设计检索、知识推理和BIM增强至关重要。然而，公共建筑布局复杂，传统的图表示构建方法依赖大量人工标注或部分自动化的深度学习方法，难以大规模、无标注地从平面图图像中提取任务自适应的图结构。此外，现有方法通常采用单一粒度的图表示，无法满足不同下游任务对空间抽象层次的多样化需求，形成非结构化视觉信息与结构化知识库之间的信息瓶颈。

Method: 1. 定义三层次图表示（LoGs）：LoG 1（粗粒度，节点为房间）、LoG 2（中粒度，节点为功能组件）、LoG 3（细粒度，节点为家具组合）。2. 提出VLM自动构建流水线，包括四个阶段：节点识别（采用Gemini分区四次扫描增强图像）、边推断（以节点为中心的迭代推理，区分硬连接H和软连接S）、文本解析与图构建（生成Basic Graph和Connectivity-aware Graph CaG）、自底向上粗化（按功能类别合并生成LoG 2和LoG 1）。3. 评估指标体系：图保真度度量（节点/边数量、匹配率、语义一致性、拓扑一致性）和计算复杂度度量（GNN消息传递复杂度、特征提取复杂度）。4. 在147个全球学术图书馆平面图上验证，并以布局质量评估（图级回归任务）和功能分区预测（节点级GNN半监督分类任务）作为下游测试。

Result: 1. VLM生成的图与人工标注图在功能组成和全局结构上高度一致：匹配节点比率≥92%，单张平面图生成三层图的平均时间为509.3秒。2. VLM在局部功能识别上表现良好，但在跨区域连通性推理上存在局限。3. 中粒度（LoG 2）图最适合节点级功能分区预测任务（Macro F1=0.647，复杂度仅为细粒度的65%）。4. 粗粒度（LoG 1）图最适合图级布局质量评估任务（Spearman ρ=0.610，复杂度仅为细粒度的16%）。5. 粗化过程起到特征选择机制的作用，丢弃局部冗余细节并集中任务相关的空间信号。6. 揭示了建筑师对图书馆布局质量的评价知识：规模较大、空间延展、高连接性枢纽与阅读区比例高、报告厅比例低的布局质量更高。

Conclusion: 本文提出的LoGs将城市信息学中的多粒度原则扩展到建筑内部布局表示，弥合了视觉平面图与数据驱动设计工作流之间的鸿沟。VLM基于零样本流水线消除了人工标注瓶颈，使公共建筑大规模布局图构建可行。粒度-任务对齐的实证发现表明，粒度应作为显式设计决策而非固定属性。本研究为设计信息学提供了一条可扩展、无标注的路径，将复杂建筑信息形式化为任务自适应的结构化表示，未来可扩展至几何属性估计、三维空间理解、生成式设计任务及其他公共建筑类型，并可与BIM等AEC工作流集成。

Abstract: Architectural floorplan images encode rich relational knowledge among functional spaces, which underpins design retrieval, knowledge-based reasoning, and BIM enrichment through the building lifecycle. However, it remains challenging to automatically construct task-adaptive graph representations for public buildings. To address this gap, we first define a multi-granularity Level-of-Graphs (LoGs) for public building layouts. Methodologically, we present a Vision-Language Model (VLM)-based automatic LoG construction through node identification, edge inference, text parsing, and graph coarsening. VLM-generated representations are systematically evaluated and tested in real-world tasks, using 147 academic library floorplans worldwide as a case study. Experiments showed VLM-generated graphs were broadly consistent with human-labeled graphs (matched node ratio >= 92%; 509.3 s per floor plan for three-LoG graph generation). Meso-grained graphs yield the best node-level zone prediction (Macro F1 = 0.647, at 65% of fine-grained complexity), while coarse-grained graphs are most effective for graph-level layout quality evaluation (Spearman's \r{ho} = 0.610, at 16% of fine-grained complexity). By enabling scalable, annotation-free extraction of structured layout information from floorplan images, this study advances design informatics by converting plan images into knowledge representations, thereby enhancing the utilization of design information across the building life cycle.

</details>


### [126] [SIMGUIDE: Procedurally Grounded Multi-Context Representations for Personalized Agent Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24888)
*Chirag Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Personalized AI agents overwhelmingly treat users as single entities: a flat profile concatenated into a prompt. This fails when the same person holds different priorities across life contexts -- and fails catastrophically when those priorities conflict. The core problem is not that agents lack information about users; it is that the format of user representations determines whether an agent can act on that information at all. We introduce SIMGUIDE, a method that structures user context into typed, domain-specific blocks called Sims and grounds each constraint with procedural examples drawn from past decisions. To evaluate this, we construct SIMBENCH, a diagnostic suite of 47 preference-conditioned planning tasks where the correct plan depends on which user context is active -- a property no existing benchmark tests. Declarative Sim constraints alone do not outperform retrieval-based personalization (RAG). Procedurally grounded Sims outperform RAG on GPT-4o (+7.9 Preference Adherence points, $p = 0.013$), and this advantage replicates on 100 $\tau$-bench tasks across both GPT-4o and Claude Sonnet~4.5 ($p \leq 0.023$). At the parametric level, the same principle holds: training distribution dominates whether parametric adaptation succeeds at all. Task-matched LoRA fine-tuning improves generation quality by 12.8 ROUGE-L points over the unadapted base model, and routing adapters by Sim type rather than user identity adds a further 7.3 points, robust to 28% routing error. Representation format -- not representation content -- is the first-order design variable.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI enhancement unavailable (llm_error). Original arXiv metadata is preserved. Error: AI returned empty structured response

Method: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Result: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (llm_error). Original arXiv metadata is preserved. Error: AI returned empty structured response

Abstract: Personalized AI agents overwhelmingly treat users as single entities: a flat profile concatenated into a prompt. This fails when the same person holds different priorities across life contexts -- and fails catastrophically when those priorities conflict. The core problem is not that agents lack information about users; it is that the format of user representations determines whether an agent can act on that information at all. We introduce SIMGUIDE, a method that structures user context into typed, domain-specific blocks called Sims and grounds each constraint with procedural examples drawn from past decisions. To evaluate this, we construct SIMBENCH, a diagnostic suite of 47 preference-conditioned planning tasks where the correct plan depends on which user context is active -- a property no existing benchmark tests. Declarative Sim constraints alone do not outperform retrieval-based personalization (RAG). Procedurally grounded Sims outperform RAG on GPT-4o (+7.9 Preference Adherence points, $p = 0.013$), and this advantage replicates on 100 $\tau$-bench tasks across both GPT-4o and Claude Sonnet~4.5 ($p \leq 0.023$). At the parametric level, the same principle holds: training distribution dominates whether parametric adaptation succeeds at all. Task-matched LoRA fine-tuning improves generation quality by 12.8 ROUGE-L points over the unadapted base model, and routing adapters by Sim type rather than user identity adds a further 7.3 points, robust to 28% routing error. Representation format -- not representation content -- is the first-order design variable.

</details>


### [127] [Reliable LLM-Powered Decision Engines for Large-Scale Supply Chain Operations: Architecture, Safety, and Performance Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24889)
*Nirmal Kumar Jingar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为LLM-DE（LLM-Powered Decision Engine）的混合架构，将大语言模型（LLM）与数学优化、概率预测和安全约束决策过滤相结合，用于大规模供应链中的智能化、韧性化决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大规模供应链具有高度不确定性、动态性和易中断性，传统的基于规则和纯优化系统难以提供及时且具备韧性的决策；同时，异构数据源（如交易需求信号和非结构化中断报告）的增多为兼具推理、适应与优化能力的智能系统提供了契机。

Method: 提出LLM-DE混合架构，整合以下组件：(1) LLM用于语义推理，理解非结构化中断报告等异构数据；(2) 概率预测模块处理需求信号；(3) 数学优化模块用于库存优化和运输路径规划；(4) 安全约束决策过滤层提供性能与安全保障；实现端到端的决策流程，涵盖需求预测、库存优化、运输路由和中断缓解。

Result: 研究表明，语言推理与优化及形式化约束的融合能够产生更智能、更安全且更具可扩展性的供应链决策；验证了基于LLM的操作决策系统在不确定性下的可靠性。

Conclusion: LLM-DE为下一代智能供应链基础设施提供了理论与实践基础，可在不确定性和大规模复杂性下可靠运行。

Abstract: Current large-scale supply chains are highly uncertain, dynamic, and disruption prone that are challenging to serve up timely and resilient decisions through traditional rule-based and optimization-only systems. The increasing supply of heterogeneous data sources, such as transactional demand signals and unstructured disruption report, presents a chance of intelligent systems, which could reason, adapt and optimize at the same time. A hybrid architecture that combines large language models (LLMs) with mathematical optimization, probabilistic forecasting, and safety-constrained decision filtering is proposed in this paper as a performance of a Decision Engine, which is called LLM-Powered Decision Engine (LLM-DE). In comparison to purely data-driven or heuristic solutions, LLM-DE integrates semantic reasoning with LLM with a set of performance and safety guarantees that allow safe decision-making in large-scale supply chain processes. The suggested framework enables the end-to-end decision making such as demand forecasting, inventory optimization, and transportation routing and disruption mitigation. The findings affirm that language-based reasoning combined with optimization and formal constraints can be used to come up with not only smarter but also safer and more scalable supply chain decisions. This research provides a new architecture, a complete pipeline of algorithm, and a formulation based on mathematical constructs of the operational decision systems incorporating LLM. The proposed model offers a pragmatic and theoretical basis of the next-generation intelligent supply chain infrastructures that can be implemented to work dependably in the face of uncertainty and massive complexity.

</details>


### [128] [AI-Powered Mental Health Chatbots in Africa: A Systematic Review and Culturally Adaptive Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24890)
*Matshepo Lebese,Pitso Tsibolane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了2017-2025年间发表的52项实证研究，评估AI聊天机器人在非洲心理健康领域的应用潜力与文化适配性，并提出"文化适应性数字心理健康（CADMH）"框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲心理健康服务面临基础设施不足、社会污名和专业人员短缺等长期问题。全球范围内AI聊天机器人作为低成本、可及的心理支持工具兴起，但其在非洲情境下的文化、语言和基础设施适配性尚不明确，需进行系统性评估。

Method: 对2017-2025年间发表的52项实证研究进行系统性综述，批判性分析其在文化、语言和基础设施层面与非洲情境的相关性，并基于发现构建整合非洲文化价值观、多语种设计、移动优先优化和伦理保障的CADMH框架。

Result: 研究发现AI聊天机器人具有提升心理服务可及性、缓解焦虑与抑郁症状、扩展心理社会支持的潜力；但大多数系统仍基于西方模式和英语设计，缺乏非洲本土化适配，在本地相关性、包容性和可持续性方面存在显著缺口。

Conclusion: 研究阐明了将AI聊天机器人整合到非洲医疗保健中的机遇与障碍，为未来研究、实践和政策制定提供了指导，强调需加强文化适应性设计、多语种支持和伦理规范以推动可持续的数字心理健康干预。

Abstract: Mental health challenges in Africa remain under-addressed due to inadequate infrastructure, stigma, and a chronic shortage of professionals. Artificial Intelligence (AI)-powered chatbots are emerging globally as low-cost, accessible tools that can offer psychological support. This paper presents a systematic review of 52 empirical studies published between 2017 and 2025, critically analysing their cultural, linguistic, and infrastructural relevance to African contexts. The findings demonstrate the potential of AI chatbots to improve accessibility, reduce symptoms of anxiety and depression, and expand psychosocial support, yet reveal limited African-specific adaptation. Most systems remain rooted in Western models and English-language designs, leaving critical gaps in local relevance, inclusivity, and sustainability. Based on the findings, the authors develop a Culturally Adaptive Digital Mental Health (CADMH) framework that integrates African cultural values, multilingual design, mobile-first optimisation, and ethical safeguards. The study highlights opportunities and barriers for integrating AI chatbots into African healthcare, offering guidance for research, practice, and policy.

</details>


### [129] [Measurement-Budget Allocation in Quantum Learning with Finite-Shot Generalization Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24891)
*Ferhat Ozgur Catak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究近 term 量子硬件上有限测量预算 B=nS 在训练态数 n 和每态 shot 数 S 之间的分配问题。通过 Rademacher 复杂度分析推导出包含经典有限样本项和量子原生有限 shot 项的分布无关泛化界，并给出闭式代理分配规则，在代理最优点达到 B^{-1/4} 的最坏情形收敛率。PennyLane 仿真实验验证了理论界不被违反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近 term 量子设备（NISQ）的电路执行受限于测量预算 B=nS。现有量子泛化界要么假设无穷 shot，要么固定 shot 数，未能在固定预算下联合优化训练态数 n 和每态 shot 数 S。许多 QML 研究默认采用"大 S 小 n"的配置，但这一选择缺乏统计依据，可能导致样本受限。

Method: 将经典 Rademacher 复杂度框架适配到有限 shot 量子测量场景：(1) 利用迹的线性性和矩阵范数不等式，证明完整二元测量类 ℋ_POVM 的无限 shot Rademacher 复杂度上界为 √(d/n)；(2) 用 Hoeffding 集中不等式结合损失的 Lipschitz 性质，将有限 shot 偏差纳入界中，得到定理 3.3 的两间隙结构（样本项 + shot 项 + 置信项）；(3) 在 B=nS 约束下，将原始对数目标函数用 log(2B/δ) 保守替代，推导出闭式代理最优分配 n* 与 S*。

Result: 主要定理 3.3 给出分布无关泛化界：R_ℓ(M) ≤ R̂_{n,S,ℓ}(M) + 2L√(d/n) + L√(log(4n/δ)/(2S)) + √(log(2/δ)/(2n))。在保守代理最优点，n*=2√(2dB/log(2B/δ))，S*=B/n*，最坏情形界以 O(B^{-1/4}) 衰减。PennyLane 实验（2/4 量子比特 VQC，9 个合成基准）在所有测试配置中经验单侧泛化间隙均低于理论界，界-间隙比在 94× 到 463× 之间，反映分布无关最坏情形保证的保守性。

Conclusion: 该工作为 NISQ 量子学习系统提供了一个保守的统计规划规则：样本受限（n≪n*）时应增加训练态、减少 shot；shot 受限（n≫n*）时则反之。代理分配公式应被视为统计规划工具而非硬件调度器，不能涵盖硬件噪声、读出错误等问题。将该界推广至完全自适应的 shot-noisy 训练是公开问题。

Abstract: On near-term quantum hardware, estimating a Born probability requires repeated circuit executions. A quantum learning experiment with a fixed measurement budget $B$ must therefore decide how many distinct training states $n$ to use and how many shots $S$ to allocate to each state. We study this tradeoff for binary quantum classifiers with fixed or independently selected measurement operators $M$, where the ideal score is $\Tr(M\rho)$. We prove a distribution-free generalization bound that separates the finite-sample and finite-shot contributions. The sample term scales as $\sqrt{d/n}$, while the shot term scales as $\sqrt{(\log n)/S}$; under the constraint $B=nS$, these two terms move in opposite directions. Minimising a conservative closed-form surrogate of the bound gives the allocation rule $\nstar = 2\sqrt{2dB/\log(2B/\delta)}$ and $\Sstar = B/\nstar$. This surrogate has the same asymptotic scaling as the exact minimizer and yields a worst-case rate of $B^{-1/4}$. The guarantee is intentionally conservative, since it applies to the full class of binary quantum measurements. We complement the theory with PennyLane simulations using 2-qubit and 4-qubit variational quantum circuits on nine synthetic binary classification benchmarks. In all tested configurations, the one-sided empirical generalization gap remains below the theoretical bound. The result provides a conservative statistical guideline for allocating measurement budgets in finite-shot evaluation and pre-experimental planning for near-term quantum learning systems, complementing hardware-level scheduling and circuit-design considerations. Extending the guarantee to fully adaptive shot-noisy training remains an open problem.

</details>


### [130] [Natural Language Input, Semantic Track Representation, and LLM Inference: Making the Maritime Information Exchange Model Tractable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24892)
*Frederick Roth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的实用架构，使MIEM及Rich Semantic Track模型变得切实可用。该架构通过让操作员以自然语言输入观测，由LLM翻译为类型化的语义断言记录（SARs），并结合知识图谱与第二阶段LLM推理（异常检测与假设排序），消除了对手动形式化本体编码的依赖，使其可立即部署于国防与执法场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义轨道模型在国防和执法领域长期受限于操作员必须学习形式化本体语言并手动编码观测，这成为其大规模应用的关键障碍。本文旨在通过LLM技术消除这一障碍，使语义追踪模型能够被非专业操作员直接使用，并防止该方法被专利化封闭。

Method: 提出基于LLM的两阶段管道架构：(1) 第一阶段LLM将自然语言观测翻译为类型化的语义断言记录（SARs），即用单一紧凑结构捕捉n元关系的命名案例框架（case frames）；(2) SARs被累积至知识图谱中；(3) 第二阶段LLM在图谱上执行推理、异常检测和假设排序。两个完整示例（9/11前兆指标场景与海上货物检查场景）展示了从自然语言到SAR再到推理输出的全流程。理论上将语义轨道表征与神经流形几何（neural manifold geometry）相联系。

Result: 通过两个详细示例（9/11前攻击指标情景和海上货物检查情景）验证了完整流水线的可行性：自然语言输入→SAR表示→推理输出均可端到端运行，证明该架构可基于现有LLM技术立即部署。

Conclusion: 该架构使Track Model和MIEM能够借助当前技术即刻落地部署；通过发表确立了该方法的在先艺术（prior art），防止其被专有封闭；并为该方法提供了连接语义轨道表征与神经流形几何的理论基础。

Abstract: We describe a practical architecture for making the Maritime Information Exchange Model (MIEM) and the broader Rich Semantic Track model tractable using current large language model (LLM) technology. The barrier to adoption of semantic track models in defense and law enforcement has been the requirement that operators learn formal ontology languages and manually encode observations as typed logical assertions. We propose eliminating this barrier entirely: operators contribute observations in natural language; an LLM translates these into typed Semantic Assertion Records (SARs), which are named case frames that capture n-ary relations in a single compact structure; a knowledge graph accumulates the SARs; and a second LLM pass performs inference, anomaly detection, and hypothesis ranking over the graph. We work through two detailed examples (a 9/11-era pre-attack indicator scenario and a maritime cargo inspection scenario) showing the full pipeline from natural language input to SAR representation to inference output. We argue that this architecture makes the Track Model and MIEM immediately deployable with current technology, establishes prior art against proprietary enclosure of the approach, and grounds the method in a theoretical framework connecting semantic track representations to neural manifold geometry.

</details>


### [131] [Same-Player Verification for Account Consistency in Counter-Strike 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24893)
*Xuchen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于 CS2 demo 行为轨迹的"同玩家验证"(same-player verification)框架，用于游戏平台账号历史一致性审查。通过提取 252 个游戏理解特征和 Transformer 序列嵌入构建"demo-player 行为指纹"，训练 LightGBM 配对模型判断两段行为是否来自同一真实玩家。6 折人-不重叠测试集平均 AUC 0.931，95% 精度下召回率 0.722；多 demo 历史聚合后 K=10 时 AUC 提升至 0.986。特征分析表明准星控制、射击节奏、移动-停-射击协调等低层级操作是最强身份信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 竞技 FPS 平台面临账号借用、代练、高水平玩家使用低级别账号等"操作者与账号历史不一致"的公平性问题。现有机制(身份验证、举报、人工审查)难以系统性地将当前比赛行为与多场历史比赛进行自动化对比，亟需一种常规化、可扩展的一致性审查方法。

Method: 1) 定义"demo-player observation"，从 1,330 场 CS2 demo 中提取 13,300 条观察；2) 构建两段式行为指纹：252 维游戏理解特征(8 个子族：aim/mech/combat/move/util/time/econ/ctx) + 基于 Transformer 的 32×16 战斗窗口序列嵌入；3) 配对比较层引入显式对称比较特征(绝对差、相对差、均值、共同零指示、异或零指示)；4) LightGBM 作为配对打分器；5) 多人不重叠(person-disjoint)划分，按 1:9 负采样，共 663,590 对；6) 账号级聚合：当前 demo 与 K 段历史两两打分，用 LLR 或均值聚合为账号级一致性信号。

Result: 主结果(6 折均值)：配对 AUC 0.931，AP 0.894，F1 0.806，95% 精度下召回率 0.722。表征消融 E1→E5 验证了显式比较特征的贡献最大(+0.089 AUC)。特征消融表明移除 aim 子族使 AUC 下降 0.074，为最强身份信号；aim/mech/combat 三族合并贡献了绝大部分判别力(移除后 AUC 下降 0.175)。账号历史聚合：K=10 时 AUC 提升至 0.986，95% 精度召回率 0.988。跨时间(中位 19 天，p90 25 天)AUC 仅下降 0.008；同地图 AUC 0.975，跨地图 AUC 0.947。补充职业 demo 数据未带来稳定提升。

Conclusion: CS2 demo 行为可支持有监督的同玩家验证与账号级一致性建模；稳定的低层级机械习惯(准星控制、射击节奏、移动-停-射击协调)比单场表现指标更具身份鉴别力；多 demo 历史聚合能将账号级 AUC 从 0.931 提升至 0.986。局限在于标签噪声、跨时间/跨地图仍带来小幅下降、职业数据存在分布偏移；未来工作包括扩展数据规模、迁移至 Valorant/PUBG/Apex/R6、探索流式部分比赛验证和人机协同审核流程。

Abstract: In competitive first-person shooter (FPS) games such as Counter-Strike 2 (CS2), account-integrity review often asks whether an account's recent behavior remains consistent with its historical operator. This consistency question arises in cases such as temporary substitution, rank boosting, and high-skill players using lower-ranked accounts, where manual review requires comparing a current match against multiple historical matches. We formulate this review task as same-player verification: we encode the behavioral trajectory of a single player in a match replay (demo) as a demo-player behavioral fingerprint, and train a model to judge whether two behavioral observations come from the same real player. Grounded in CS2 game understanding, the fingerprints cover crosshair control, movement-stop-fire coordination, economy/buy, combat/engagement, and temporal rhythm. From 1,330 CS2 demos we extract 13,300 demo-player observations, and sample 663,590 same/different pairs from an 88.4M candidate-pair space for supervised training and evaluation. The final pairwise model reaches an average ROC AUC of 0.931 and achieves 0.722 different-player recall at 95% precision. Feature analysis shows that the strongest identity signals come from low-level operations, especially crosshair control, firing rhythm, and movement-stop-fire coordination, indicating that stable low-level mechanical habits are more informative for this verification task than single-match performance outcomes. In the account-history aggregation evaluation, increasing history depth raises AUC from the K=1 single-pair baseline of 0.931 to 0.986 at K=10. These results show that CS2 demo behavior can support supervised same-player verification and account-level identity-consistency modeling through multi-demo history aggregation.

</details>


### [132] [Semantic Graph Unification for Industrial Digital Threads: Bridging 11 Heterogeneous Manufacturing Systems Through Ontology-Driven Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24918)
*Grama Chethan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个基于本体驱动的RDF语义图框架，通过五阶段ETL管道统一11个工业数据源（涵盖9个领域），利用97条owl:sameAs链接进行实体解析，并基于287个MCP工具将知识图谱暴露给LLM代理，以解决制造企业异构系统间的数据孤岛问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代制造企业运行ERP、MES、PLM、SCADA等多种异构系统，各系统具有独立的数据模型和API，形成数据孤岛，阻碍整体分析、根因调查和工业4.0可追溯性。点对点集成以O(n²)扩展并积累脆弱依赖关系。

Method: 1) 基于本体驱动的RDF知识图谱统一方法，参考ISA-95、OPC UA、eClass、AAS、RAMI 4.0等标准，构建78个RDFS类、108个对象属性、243个数据属性的本体；2) 五阶段ETL管道进行自动化实体解析；3) 自动发现引擎应用九类策略（跨工位关联、报警覆盖、ECN影响、CUSUM/EWMA漂移检测等）；4) 通过287个MCP工具以SPARQL原生语义层暴露给LLM代理；5) 在航空航天、消费品、医药、医疗器械、涡轮叶片五个行业模板上演示。

Result: 核心实证结果：阻断24个跨系统工具使召回率从1.00降至0.31（F1从1.00降至0.48），表明69%的可发现信号需要跨系统图连接。留一法消融确认九种策略中有六种贡献独特信号。针对65信号清单（16个正例、49个空例）的验证获得F1=1.00（95% Clopper-Pearson CI [0.79, 1.00]），但清单由作者构建，属于验证而非独立验证。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了语义图统一方法在打破制造数据孤岛方面的有效性，证明跨系统集成对发现关键信号至关重要。但验证存在局限性（清单为作者构建），五个行业模板证明本体架构在不同制造垂直领域具有稳定性。

Abstract: Modern manufacturing enterprises operate heterogeneous systems -- ERP, MES, PLM, SCADA, QMS, SCM -- each with its own data model and API. The resulting silos prevent holistic analysis, delay root-cause investigation, and obstruct Industry 4.0 traceability. Point-to-point integration scales as O(n^2) and accumulates brittle dependencies. This paper presents an open framework for semantic graph unification of industrial digital threads. An ontology-driven RDF knowledge graph unifies data from 11 simulated sources across nine domains through a five-stage ETL pipeline with automated entity resolution spanning 97 owl:sameAs identity links. The ontology encompasses 78 RDFS classes, 108 object properties, and 243 data properties, drawing on ISA-95, OPC UA, eClass, the Asset Administration Shell, RAMI 4.0, and additional standards. An automated discovery engine applies nine strategy categories -- cross-station correlation, alarm coverage, ECN impact, CUSUM/EWMA drift detection -- to surface insights spanning system boundaries. The primary empirical result: blocking 24 cross-system tools reduces recall from 1.00 to 0.31 (F1 from 1.00 to 0.48), showing that 69% of discoverable signals require cross-system graph joins. Leave-one-out ablation confirms six of nine strategies contribute unique signals. Verification against a 65-signal manifest (16 positive, 49 null) yields F1 = 1.00 (95% Clopper-Pearson CI [0.79, 1.00]); as the manifest was author-constructed, this constitutes verification not independent validation. The graph is exposed to LLM agents via 287 Model Context Protocol tools as a SPARQL-native semantic layer. Five industry templates (aerospace, CPG, pharma, medical devices, turbine blades) demonstrate schema stability across manufacturing verticals.

</details>


### [133] [post-graph-rag: A PostgreSQL-Native Graph RAG Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24921)
*Chandan Rajah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: post-graph-rag 是一个开源的 Graph RAG 引擎，将文本块嵌入、规范化的实体图、提及边和社区摘要统一存储在单个 PostgreSQL 数据库中（结合 pgvector）。其核心设计包括：抽取阶段的质量门控（拒绝模糊谓词、代词名称、纯量名词等，对谓词进行规范化与受控词表对齐）、基于别名的实体解析（合并同一实体的不同表述）、显式否定标记（保留正向谓词并置 negation flag）、以及基于文档顺序的时态超替换模型（exclusive predicate groups + as-of 查询）。在三个不同语域的语料（Wikipedia、Dumas 小说、波音 10-K 年报）上与 LightRAG 对比，post-graph-rag 在保持相当答案质量的同时，构建出更密集、更可查询的图谱，并支持时态演化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Graph RAG 系统存在三类问题：(1) 基础设施复杂——需要同时维护向量库、图数据库和文档存储并保持一致性；(2) 图质量低劣——基于 LLM 的三元组抽取无拒绝机制，输出大量无意义的边（如 relates_to、代词指代、孤立数字）；(3) 时间建模缺失——纯累加式图谱将过时事实与当前事实等同对待，导致在事实变化的语料上给出错误答案。论文旨在通过单一存储、抽取时验证门控和时态关系模型系统性地解决这三类问题。

Method: 系统架构采用单库 PostgreSQL 部署：使用 pgvector 的 HNSW 索引进行向量检索，使用普通边表（foreign key + JSONB payload）进行图遍历。三类顶点表（documents、entities、communities）与三类边表（relations、doc_mentions、community_members）。多租户通过 realm（schema 级隔离）+ space（应用级子分组）两级分区实现。抽取阶段采用 fail-closed 策略：(a) 文档上下文贯通——将已发现的标准实体名称传递给后续 chunk 以解决代词；(b) gleaning——第二轮提示召回遗漏三元组；(c) 验证门控——拒绝模糊谓词（relates_to 等）、代词/连词名称、纯量值、超长名、自环、低置信度及孤儿端点；(d) 谓词规范化（小写、下划线连接、词形归一）与可选受控词表前缀匹配 snapping；(e) 别名解析——通过最长形式规则与类型非降级规则合并同名实体；(f) 相关性计数——以独立贡献 chunk 数作为边的 weight；(g) 否定显式化——正向谓词配 negated flag。时态模型：(1) 有效性区间仅在文本明确陈述时填充，无区间视为始终有效；(2) 超替换——声明互斥谓词组（exclusive predicate groups），后置文档（按索引顺序）覆盖先前不相容断言；(3) as-of 查询——按指定日期过滤；(4) 幂等重索引——基于 doc_key + content_hash，孤立实体转为 dormant 而非删除。检索采用六种模式（mix/local/global/hybrid/naive/bypass），双层关键词分解（low-level + high-level）用于查询调节，包含双通道关系检索（k 跳遍历 + 关系向量相似度，按配额 q 交错合并），社区报告基于相似度+重要性+簇大小的混合排名。

Result: 在三个语料（Wikipedia 4 篇 127k 字符；Dumas 三部曲 645k 字符；Boeing 五份 10-K 年报 587k 字符）上，post-graph-rag 与 LightRAG 1.5.6 在相同抽取模型（MiniMax-M2.7）和嵌入模型（text-embedding-3-small）下对比。Wikipedia 上：523 vs 450 实体，671 vs 421 关系；边标签/关系比 57% vs 109%（LightRAG 边均标签>1）；在受控词表下达 11%。查询延迟：mix 模式 8.2s vs 10.1s，global 模式 3.1s vs 6.1s。Dumas 上：3,976 vs 1,447 关系，13 项超替换（LightRAG 为 0）。Boeing 上：8 项超替换，222 条边具备 stated validity（LightRAG 不支持）；as-of=2024 才能检出的边 (777X deferred production costs)-[reduced_by]->(777X program)[2020-12-31..open)。跨模型敏感性测试：MiniMax-M2.7 构建最密集图（268 个带别名实体），gemma-4-31B 构建最可查询图（44 个不同谓词、94% 词表覆盖），Llama-3.3-70B 从未使用 negated 字段。gleaning 使关系数提升 40%。词表机制使可查询谓词占比从 12% 提升至 44%。Boeing 语料上实体粒度约束使检索召回率提升 2×–9×。超替换需密集抽取与声明的互斥组配合才能触发，5% 稀疏采样触发 0 次超替换。

Conclusion: Graph RAG 并不需要多套专用存储的联邦——文本块、嵌入、实体图、提及边与社区摘要可共存于单一 PostgreSQL。两个设计承诺解释了主要差异：(1) 将抽取输出视为不可信输入：拒绝占位结构、模糊谓词、代词/连词名称、纯量值、用反向谓词表达否定；每个机制都承担明确功能，gleaning 提升 40% 关系数，词表机制将不可查询的扁平长尾转化为具有可用头部峰值的分布。(2) 时间建模：纯累加式图谱等同于声称所有事实同时成立，对任何事实演化的语料均不成立；通过文档顺序关闭不相容关系，使图谱能回答"轨迹"类问题。时态能力在对比基线中无对应实现。论文亦警告领域：仅替换抽取模型即可使别名发射率变化 5.7×、否定捕获率由 0 提升至 21、词表覆盖率由 33% 提升至 94%；未固定抽取模型的 Graph RAG 对比结果不可复现。限制包括：仅三个语料、英语长文本、单次运行、无参考答案评分、无跨系统索引吞吐量比较、超替换需密集与词表配合、检索配额 q 为参数而非定值（10 题 2 图基础下两个图结论相反，仅按问题形状分组后一致）。

Abstract: Graph-based retrieval-augmented generation connects facts that no single passage states, but current implementations pay for that three times: in infrastructure, requiring a vector store, graph database and document store to be kept consistent; in graph quality, because an extraction pipeline that never refuses output fills the graph with edges that assert nothing; and over time, because a graph that only accumulates treats superseded and current facts alike. post-graph-rag is an open-source engine addressing all three. Text chunks with embeddings, a canonical entity graph and community summaries live in one PostgreSQL database, with pgvector for search and edge tables for traversal. Extraction-time invariants run before anything is written: vague predicates, pronominal names and bare quantities are rejected; predicates are normalised onto an optional vocabulary; entities resolve to one vertex per canonical name via model-supplied aliases; and denied relations keep the positive predicate under a negation flag. A temporal layer lets relations carry a validity period from the prose, lets a later document supersede an earlier incompatible assertion from document order alone, and answers as-of queries. Against LightRAG on three corpora under identical extraction and embedding models, post-graph-rag builds a denser graph everywhere, up to $2.4\times$ the relations per entity, and a more queryable one: distinct edge labels run at 0.46 to 0.58 per relation, 0.11 under a controlled vocabulary, against 0.77 to 1.33. It answers comparably with lower query latency, and supports temporal evolution the baseline lacks: 13 and 8 relationships superseded on a novel sequence and a decade of filings, against zero. These are engineering measurements, not a benchmark result. Code: this https URL , this https URL

</details>


### [134] [FrontierChallenge: Evaluating Scientific Workflow Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24979)
*Liangcai Su,Zhaopeng Feng,Zhuo Chen,Zhen Zhang,Xiang Lin,Ruilin Li,Handuo Zhang,Ning Wang,Kailong Wen,Yueqi Guo,Feng Xing,Yiling Guo,Chenxiong Qian,Simon Shaolei Du,Lidong Bing,Xinyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FrontierChallenge 是一个跨领域的端到端科学工作流基准测试，涵盖 300 个任务（本次发布 97 个）。研究发现前沿模型即使平均得分很高，也难以可靠完成完整的科学任务交付，Pass Rate 最高仅 20.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试多关注最终答案、孤立程序或单一领域，无法衡量智能体在多阶段科学工作流中交付完整、一致产出的能力。作者旨在填补这一空白，评估智能体能否从固定输入到最终交付物完整执行科学工作流。

Method: 构建 300 个端到端科学工作流任务池，本次发布 97 个，涵盖六个领域：量子化学、分子动力学、材料表征、分析化学、生命科学、电化学/环境。使用 12 个前沿模型配合 3 种智能体脚手架（Codex、Claude Code、Frontier Agent）进行评估。主要指标为 Pass Rate（任务完全完成率），辅助指标为 Avg. Score（平均分）。每个任务配有固定输入、输出契约和可执行的 Grader。

Result: 最佳配置（GPT-5.6 Sol + Codex 与 Grok 4.6 + Claude Code）仅完成 20/97 任务，Pass Rate 为 20.6%，尽管最高 Avg. Score 达 87.9。分析化学和电化学/环境领域部分进展与完整交付差距尤为突出：Avg. Score 分别达 87.6 和 94.9，但 Pass Rate 仅 4% 和 0%。75.5% 未通过的 Claude Code 轨迹以"完成"语言结尾。

Conclusion: 高平均分和自信的完成声明都不能可靠表明科学任务已完整交付。完整、契约级别的交付能力仍是一个尚未解决的关键能力，需要显式契约追踪、跨产物验证和基于证据的完成检查才能构建更可靠的科学智能体。

Abstract: Scientific agents increasingly analyze data, execute code, and produce research artifacts, yet most benchmarks emphasize final answers, isolated programs, or a single domain. We introduce FrontierChallenge, a cross-domain benchmark comprising 300 end-to-end scientific workflows. In this paper, we release and evaluate 97 of these tasks, spanning quantum chemistry, molecular dynamics, materials characterization, analytical chemistry, life science, and electrochemistry/environment. Each task provides fixed inputs and specifies a bundle of required scientific deliverables. We evaluate twelve frontier models with three agent scaffolds. Pass Rate measures the fraction of tasks satisfying the full-completion criterion, while Avg. Score captures partial progress. Each of the best-performing configurations completed only 20 of the 97 released tasks, yielding a Pass Rate of 20.6%. Partial progress translated especially poorly into complete delivery in analytical chemistry and electrochemistry/environment: Avg. Scores reached 87.6 and 94.9, but the highest Pass Rates were only 4% and 0%. Among non-passing Claude Code trajectories, 75.5% still ended with language claiming completion. These findings show that neither high partial scores nor confident claims of completion reliably indicate that a scientific task has been fully delivered, highlighting the need to evaluate end-to-end workflow execution and the completeness of scientific deliverables together.

</details>


### [135] [Solving Robust POMDPs with Omega-regular Objectives via Partially Observable Stochastic Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24986)
*Durgam Latha,Dion Reji,S. Akshay,Djordje Zikelic,Shankaranarayanan Krishna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有 ω-正则目标的鲁棒 POMDP（RPOMDP）的求解问题，首次在 (s,a)-矩形、多面体不确定性集的 RPOMDP 与交替控制 POSG（ac-POSG）之间建立了多项式时间双向等价归约，由此推导出 RMDP 和 RPOMDP 在确定性、几乎确定性、极限确定性和定量胜利等不同胜利模式下针对安全性、可达性、Büchi、co-Büchi 及一般 ω-正则目标的完整计算复杂性图景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 POMDP 假设转移概率精确已知，但在实际应用中通常仅知道其属于某个不确定性集合。现有研究多集中于奖励目标，无法处理安全攸关应用中的逻辑正确性属性（如 LTL 规范）。因此，需要在不确定性转移模型下研究 ω-正则目标的求解复杂性。

Method: 方法核心是构造两类多项式时间归约：① RPOMDP→ac-POSG：将每个状态-动作对扩展为 min-玩家状态，其有效动作为不确定性多面体的顶点，min-玩家在多面体内随机化等价于环境选取概率分布；② ac-POSG→RPOMDP：经过 Pre-min 变换（插入虚拟 max-状态消除 min-玩家对观测的中间信息优势）和合并步骤，将 min-玩家的选择折叠为 dummy 状态下的多面体不确定性集。所有归约均保持观察历史与策略的内存类，且构造线性时间内完成。

Result: 建立了 (s,a)-矩形多面体 RPOMDP 与 ac-POSG 在所有四种胜利模式（确定性、几乎确定性、极限确定性、定量）下关于一般 ω-正则目标的双向多项式时间等价。作为推论：RMDP 在定量胜利下达到 NP∩coNP 上界和下界；1s-RPOMDP 在几乎确定/定量胜利下达到 EXPTIME-完全；RPOMDP 在 co-Büchi 几乎确定性及 Reach/Büchi 定量胜利下不可判定；同时也获得了若干可达性/安全性/Büchi/co-Büchi 的 EXPTIME-完全结果。

Conclusion: 本文首次在 ω-正则目标下证明了鲁棒 POMDP 与部分可观察随机博弈的语义等价，填补了从博弈到鲁棒 MDP 反向归约的技术空白。该等价性为 RMDP 和 RPOMDP 在多种 ω-正则目标下的计算复杂性提供了完整图景。未来工作可研究更一般不确定性设置或动作可见策略下的复杂性边界。

Abstract: Robust POMDPs (RPOMDPs) generalize classical POMDPs to the setting where exact transition probabilities are not known -- rather, they are only known to belong to some uncertainty set of values. In this work, we study the problem of solving RPOMDPs with general omega-regular objectives, which subsume a broad class of objectives such as reachability, safety, and linear temporal logic (LTL) objectives. We show that, for (s,a)-rectangular RPOMDPs with polytopic uncertainty sets, the problem of solving RPOMDPs under omega-regular objectives can be reduced to solving partially observable stochastic games (POSGs) under omega-regular objectives. Moreover, we show for the first time that reductions can be constructed in both directions, establishing the semantic equivalence between (s,a)-rectangular RPOMDPs with polytopic uncertainty sets and POSGs. This allows us to derive a range of new computational complexity results, including both upper and lower complexity bounds, on solving RPOMDPs with different omega-regular objectives. As a corollary, we also derive new computational complexity results for RMDPs.

</details>


### [136] [CVE-SAI: Counterfactual Visual Evidence-Guided Selective Attribute Indexing for Risk-Controlled E-commerce Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25023)
*Xiaolong Sun,Qichao Wang,Hangyu Li,Liang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CVE-SAI 提出了一种面向电商搜索的视觉属性自动索引框架,将属性推断与风险受控的索引准入解耦,通过反事实视觉证据(聚焦区域失真 FZD 和证据引导注意力重分配 EGAR)生成候选冻结值,并基于有限样本校准在 5% 不安全准入预算下选择准入策略,在 ABO 数据集上显著提升了属性推断、证据定位、认证准入覆盖率以及检索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态商品属性补全方法仅优化属性答案准确率,缺乏对视觉支持的验证,且将瞬态预测与持久索引准入混为一谈,缺乏对事实错误或视觉无支持值的显式风险控制,导致一次性预测错误会变成持续的检索污染。

Method: 方法包含三大阶段:(1) 产品文本无关的视觉推断——FZD 通过反事实干预构建属性特定的视觉依赖代理,EGAR 利用该代理优化本体约束评分;(2) 冻结候选审计——对候选值依次进行证据必要性、证据保留性、干扰变换稳定性及候选特定目录文本冲突四项审计,目录文本只能提高准入门槛而不能修改候选值;(3) 独立校准的索引准入——在独立族级校准集上以 Bonferroni 校正的 Clopper-Pearson 单侧有限样本界在 5% 不安全准入预算下选择唯一准入策略,仅将准入值写入可逆的自动属性字段。

Result: 在 ABO 衍生的五个视觉属性基准上,CVE-SAI 取得 Macro VAA 72.36%(较 MOON 提升 2.95 个百分点)和 Ans.-F1 84.47%;FZD 的 Patch AUPRC 达 49.16%;在 5% 风险预算下 CWC@5% 达 44.50%(较 MOON 提升 11.31 个百分点),UnsafeWWR 降至 2.36%;受控 Lucene 检索 NDCG@10 达 0.6749,UAIE@10 降至 0.50%,均为自动准入系统中最佳;组件消融验证了各模块的必要性,跨骨干与跨属性分析均显示稳健性。

Conclusion: 通过将属性推断与持久索引准入分离,基于视觉证据支撑的候选审计和独立有限样本风险校准,CVE-SAI 能够在限制持续索引污染的同时提升检索效果;未来工作将扩展至多图像商品、演化本体及跨目录校准,同时保持推断、审计与准入三阶段的解耦结构。

Abstract: Multimodal product models can complete missing e-commerce attributes, yet current methods still optimize attribute-answer accuracy without verifying visual support, conflate transient prediction with persistent index admission, and lack explicit risk control over factually incorrect or visually unsupported values. We address these gaps with Counterfactual Visual Evidence-Guided Selective Attribute Indexing (CVE-SAI), which first infers and freezes an ontology-constrained candidate from the primary image and attribute question without catalog text, and then decides whether that candidate should enter the index. Focus-Zone Distortion (FZD) constructs an attribute-specific visual-dependence proxy through a controlled counterfactual intervention, and Evidence-Guided Attention Redistribution (EGAR) uses the proxy to refine ontology-constrained scoring. The canonical candidate is frozen before evidence necessity, evidence retention, nuisance-transformation stability, and candidate-specific catalog-text conflict audits; catalog text can only tighten admission and cannot revise the candidate. Independent family-level calibration selects one policy with a simultaneous one-sided finite-sample bound under a 5% unsafe-admission budget. Experiments on five visual attributes derived from Amazon Berkeley Objects show that CVE-SAI improves attribute inference and evidence localization, achieves the highest certified admission coverage under the shared risk protocol, and yields the strongest controlled retrieval performance with the lowest unsafe auto-induced exposure among automatic-admission systems. Separating inference from admission therefore enables visually supported attribute completion to improve retrieval while limiting persistent index contamination.

</details>


### [137] [LifePlanner: Evaluating LLM Agents for Geo-spatial Planning with Social Media Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25039)
*Zhen Dong,Yuning Peng,Yutao Shi,Lei Zhong,Yongsen Mao,Yuan Liu,Haiping Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LifePlanner是一个用于评估LLM智能体在地理空间规划任务中表现的基准测试，通过整合地图数据和大规模社交媒体帖子（来自小红书），通过MCP工具集提供访问。它包含四个任务类别（地点感知、邻近发现、路由、行程设计）和三个难度级别（L0单步查询、L1多步检索计算、L2隐式约束下的决策）。实验显示前沿LLM在简单检索上表现良好但在复杂规划上性能急剧下降，L2通过率仅40.2%。失败主要源于证据获取不完整、工具使用不精确以及约束整合能力弱，而非模型规模或推理长度不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地理空间规划基准测试存在两大局限：1）环境层面——多数基准仅使用清洁的地理空间数据库和工具，缺少现实规划中常见的开放性社交信号（如社交媒体帖子）；2）评估协议层面——任务意图狭窄、难度分层薄弱，难以系统地洞察规划任务和复杂度之间的趋势。因此需要一个更真实、更系统化的评估平台来推动LLM智能体在真实城市环境中的规划能力。

Method: LifePlanner包含两个核心组件：（1）环境——覆盖约10平方公里城市空间，包含超过3,600个地点和约200,000条社交媒体笔记及评论，通过MCP服务器提供检索工具和基本分析工具。数据库构建采用四阶段流水线（地理空间骨架构建、社交媒体证据收集、属性蒸馏、访问分离）。评估时智能体只能访问原始社交媒体内容，蒸馏的动态记忆字段不向被测智能体公开。（2）评估协议——667个合成查询涵盖四个任务类别和三个难度级别（L0单步查找、L1多步检索与计算、L2混合推理与约束整合），采用LLM数据引擎生成评估案例并由人工交叉验证。评估指标包括结果指标（属性准确性、地点集IoU、路由偏差、顺序一致性、语义约束满足、通过率）和过程指标（信息基础、工具效率、推理纯度、Token使用）。

Result: 对10个主流LLM（Claude-Sonnet-4.6、Qwen3.6-Plus、GPT-5.4、Gemini-3.1-Pro-Preview、GLM-5.1、Kimi-K2.6、MiniMax-M2.7、Qwen3.5-27B/35B-A3B/9B）的实验显示：（1）任务难度是性能下降的最一致来源，平均通过率从L0的89.4%降至L2的40.2%，Token使用从24.6k增至251.1k；（2）任务类型间性能差异显著，地点感知任务L2可达90%通过率，而行程设计L2仅约25%；（3）模型规模并非决定性因素，Qwen3.5-27B优于GPT-5.4；（4）更多探索是必要但不充分的，Gemini使用最少Token却因过早停止而性能不佳；（5）失败主因是证据获取不完整（IG L2仅64.6%）、工具使用效率低（TE L2仅44.6%）、约束整合能力弱（SC L2仅65.2%），而非推理失真（RP L2仍达86.4%）。

Conclusion: LifePlanner验证了其作为综合平台的价值，揭示了当前LLM智能体在现实城市规划中的关键局限：任务难度暴露出简单检索与复杂约束规划间的巨大差距；失败主要源于不完整的证据获取、弱约束整合，而非模型幻觉；模型规模扩大和延长推理不足以解决问题，未来智能体需要针对有效工具使用、大规模多模态数据库中的定向探索以及基于证据的忠实规划进行更强的训练。

Abstract: Geo-spatial planning, like trip design, is a realistic testbed for LLM agents because it requires grounded tool use, noisy evidence retrieval, and multi-constraint reasoning. Most benchmarks, however, only provide clean geospatial data and tools, missing the open-ended social signals that people use in daily planning. We introduce LifePlanner, a benchmark that enriches map data with large-scale local social media posts and provides access through an MCP toolset. LifePlanner provides an evaluation suite spanning four task categories and three difficulty levels. Experiments show frontier LLMs perform well on simple retrieval but degrade sharply on complex planning, with the Pass Rate dropping to 40.2%. Results show that failures mainly stem from incomplete evidence acquisition from such a large multimodal database, imprecise tool use, and weak constraint integration rather than model size or reasoning length, suggesting that future progress requires effective grounded planning instead of scaling alone.

</details>


### [138] [SimVerity: When Does Simulated Agent Success Survive Physical Deployment?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25067)
*Zhonghao Zhan,Yefan Zhang,Krinos Li,Hamed Haddadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SimVerity 是一个引擎无关的"判决迁移保证层"(verdict-transfer assurance layer)，位于智能体测试栈外部，系统量化模拟器对智能家居代理的"通过"判决在真实部署中是否成立。它在目标部署上重放匹配场景，用独立鉴定的物理见证者（摄像头）交叉验证代理执行，并以冻结的风险档案预测哪些模拟通过会在部署中失败，最终输出"通过/弃权/升级"三态处置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模拟评估将智能体成功视为静态属性，但物理部署是一个展开的过程。完成度、上报状态、可观测效应、稳态后果在同一执行中可能背离。作者用一个跑分案例说明：一个高级模拟器在全部240次灯控试验中判定通过，但摄像头仍捕获到42次亚秒级失败——这些失败对稳态检查完全不可见。"虚假通过"(false clearance)是一种监督失败，而非基准噪声。本文旨在填补判决迁移(verdict transfer)作为保证契约的空白。

Method: 1) 问题形式化：定义 Verdict Fidelity (VF) 与 False Clearance Risk (FCR)，弃权(abstain)为一类结果，缺证不静默变为同意。2) 系统设计：版本化清单固定场景与执行坐标；轨迹适配器做六阶段语义对齐；见证者鉴定后才作证（光照分离度 ≥ max(10, 20×MAD)）；属性监控器按报告完成/报告读后写/可观测效应/稳态后置/效应顺序/扇出完成六类评估。3) 风险档案：每个条件类用 Jeffreys 平滑率估计部署失败风险 r̂_c = (k_c+½)/(n_c+1)，沿冻结的条件阶梯回退。4) 实验：跨三层梯级（模拟器 S → 软件梯 R_soft → 物理梯 R_phys），涉及 SimuHome、Home Assistant、智能灯、门磁、摄像头；冻结6个预测器作为结构性粒度阶梯；两轮预注册队列（自然光照/窗帘受控）；纳入 ReAct 与 planner-executor 现场代理；第二模拟器 S5-HES 追溯回放冻结锚点。

Result: 1) 属性选择性保真度：同一执行中，完成度可全失败而稳态效应从不失败，报告与可观测判决按路径变化。P1校准240次：240完成失败、20上报失败、42可观测失败、0稳态失败。P3保留测试：36次中上报12、可观测7、稳态0失败。2) 失败可预测：第一队列3/3场胜path-only，Brier 0.1113 vs 0.1616；第二队列8/8场胜path-only（sign与Wilcoxon p=0.0039），Brier 0.0878 vs 0.1995；总计11/11保留会话胜出属性盲基线。3) 代理可审计性依赖于"模型-客户端/服务配置"整体：生产型 Hermes 100% 轨迹匹配；自定义 ReAct/规划器跌至52–88%；将 ReAct 切换到注册的前沿配置后恢复到100%。4) 第二模拟器 S5-HES 在所有重叠案例上从不与首模拟器分歧，看似降低风险完全由粗时钟强制弃权造成，仅物理测量能暴露两者共同的盲点。5) 跨站点复制（Site B/C）：二元继电器-灯审计零虚假通过，机制为确认门控上报。

Conclusion: 模拟器"绿灯"是对物理世界的承诺，而非保证。SimVerity 把判决迁移变成一个显式决定——通过、弃权或升级部署前即给出处置。失败模式按属性与读边界选择性出现并可预测；可审计性归属可执行配置而非模型标签；模拟器互查不能替代部署锚点测量。作者主张"发布审计，而非答案"——答案天然是部署局部的。

Abstract: Simulated evaluation is widely used to benchmark AI agents, yet how much evidence a simulated pass provides about physical deployment has not been systematically quantified. We present SimVerity, a verdict-transfer assurance framework: it replays matched scenarios on target smart home deployments and cross-validates agent execution against independently qualified physical witnesses. Our evaluation highlights that deployment success is a real-world process, not a static property in simulation: completion, reported state, observable effect, and settled outcome diverged within the same execution. Although an advanced simulator cleared all 240 light trials, a camera caught 42 sub-second failures invisible to settled-state checks. False clearance was predictable: a risk profile learned from measured trials and locked before evaluation predicted failures on a path it never physically measured, beating a property-blind baseline in all eleven held-out sessions across two cohorts. Agent auditability was also measurable: switching one agent loop's model-client/serving configuration raised its scenario-matching share from 52-88% to 100%. Finally, a second qualified simulator added no independent cross-check: it never disagreed on any overlapping case, and only physical measurement exposed their shared blind spots. SimVerity turns verdict transfer into an explicit decision: clear, abstain, or escalate before deployment.

</details>


### [139] [Auto-Policy, not Auto-Skill: Compiled Agent Skills for the Physical World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25091)
*Zhonghao Zhan,Hamed Haddadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出，当前的 Agent Skills 格式（如 AutoSkills、Hermes Agent 等自演化框架）仅用 Markdown 和脚本来描述"技能如何被调用"，却把"何种调用可被允许执行"这一策略决策留给了模型本身，造成了策略层面的安全缺口。作者将这一缺口命名为"借用权威"（Borrowed Authority）：恶意或被误用的 Skill 可通过附加自然语言形式的权限声明（如"住户在家"）驱动物理动作，而接收方缺乏类型化机制来拒绝此类未经验证的跨代理权限声明。论文提出 Edge Skillguard——一个内嵌于 Skill 工件内部的类型化权威层，覆盖世界状态、租约和传感器证据等谓词。在边缘多代理测试平台（Home Assistant 148 实体 + NATS JetStream + Tailscale mesh）的实证评估中，该守卫以 5 种攻击变体在 60/60 借权请求中全部拒绝放行（良性请求 60/60 全部放行），并在 5× 规模（300+300）和跨主机 Tailscale mesh 上保持正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体正从云端沙箱走向物理执行环境，但 Agent Skills 格式仅承载程序性知识（如何做），而把授权决策（是否被允许做）留给 LLM 自行判断。现有的自演化 Skill 框架（AutoSkills、Hermes Agent）声称提升了效率，却未填补安全缺口。两条已公开的攻击路径——恶意 Skill 污染云软件、LLM 控制的机器人被越狱引发物理伤害——其交集（恶意 Skill 引发现实物理危害）尚未被记录但即将发生。论文将此命名为"借用权威"攻击类，论证亟需在 Skill 工件内部植入可机器检查的类型化授权层。

Method: Edge Skillguard 定义为一个八元组 Π=(Q, q₀, X, E, A, G, δ, H, C)，包括编排状态、世界状态、事件、动作、守卫谓词、转移关系、LLM 空洞和跨代理契约。它以内嵌于 Skill 工件的类型化 JSON Schema 策略 + 纯函数求值器实现，对入站信封与底座状态执行七种类型化谓词算子（eq/neq/in/not_in/includes/fresher_than/eq_field）。底座依赖五项前置条件：JSON Schema 严格枚举、broker 鉴定的 sender_id、JetStream WorkQueue 持久收件箱、审计镜像发件箱、畸形信封边界拒绝。求值器输出两类结果：放行→收件箱发布；拒绝→结构化 policy_block 日志。部署分三层：进程内、边缘 NATS broker、跨主机 Tailscale mesh。

Result: 论文在连接 Home Assistant（148 实体）的实时边缘控制面板上，以 60 个 Borrowed Authority 请求 ×5 种攻击变体（陈旧在场时间戳、缺失在场源、错误受让人租约、过期租约、租约范围错配）+ 60 个良性对照进行评估。四条件对比中：Skill 与 NL M2M 全部被劫持（60/60 错误执行），ESG-LO 仅租约消融拦截 36/60，全策略 ESG 实现 60/60 攻击拦截 + 60/60 良性通过。延迟分层：进程内 1.5–3.2 μs、本地 broker 273 μs、Tailscale mesh 5.7 ms；5× 规模（300+300）p95 399 μs / 7.9 ms，正确性维持。失败原因以谓词级信息暴露（如"presence_observed_at 187 秒前"）。边界情形（受信任主体被攻破、物理证据伪造）被有意允许，需互补防御。

Conclusion: 论文将"物理后果型 Skill 攻陷"确立为代理攻击矩阵中尚未被公开报告但即将出现的第四象限；将"借用权威"形式化为一种编排层而非脚本层的权威混淆攻击。Edge Skillguard 作为一套手写但可机器检查的类型化守卫，验证了在真实边缘环境中以可忽略的延迟代价完全拦截此类攻击且不误伤良性请求。结论主张：高风险 Skill 应把类型化调用策略与程序性知识共同打包，使物理动作依赖机器可检验证据而非对等代理的自然语言主张。论文将其定位为概念性 workshop 论文，明确边界：手写守卫、单边缘测试床、未涵盖被攻破可信主体与伪造物理证据等情形，后续研究方向包括基于轨迹的策略自动合成、具身反馈与安全的策略演进。

Abstract: Self-evolving Skill harnesses (AutoSkills, Hermes Agent) generate more advisory orchestration automatically; their reported gains are efficiency, not safety. This misses the actual gap: a Skill describes how an agent should behave; a Policy decides which behavior is allowed to become an action. Today's format covers the first with markdown and scripts; the second is left to the model. Generating more Skills scales the gap, not the safety, especially when a wrong invocation can unlock a door or move money. Two adjacent attacks are documented: malicious skills compromising cloud software, and jailbroken LLM-controlled robots causing physical harm. Their intersection, malicious agent skills causing physical harm, follows directly but has not been reported. We name this class Borrowed Authority: Skills format gives the receiving agent no typed way to reject an inter-agent permission claim, so a malicious or misused Skill can drive actuation by attaching one. We propose Edge Skillguard, a typed authority layer that lives inside the Skill artifact rather than between tools as workflow engines do, with guards over world state and sensor evidence. On a live edge control-plane testbed, the guards reject 60/60 borrowed-authority requests across five attack variants without blocking benign requests, and the result holds at 5x scale and across hosts over a Tailscale mesh. These results suggest that high-risk Skills should co-package typed invocation policy with procedural knowledge, so that physical actions depend on machine-checkable evidence rather than peer-agent claims.

</details>


### [140] [PhysElite: How Far Are LLMs from Solving Olympiad-Level Physics Problems?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25097)
*Ruoran Xu,Wending Gao,Liyunfeng Chen,Aixin Shi,Haoyu Cheng,Zixiang Fang,Yiqiang Zou,Qiufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PhysElite 是一个大规模双语多模态奥林匹克级物理推理基准，包含 11,586 道竞赛级物理题，每题配有示意图、逐步中英双语解答推导和最终答案。论文对 18 个开源和闭源多模态大语言模型进行评测，发现即使最强模型也仅达到 33.7% 的答案准确率，远低于人类选手 48.5% 的基线，并通过步骤级过程评估诊断了模型的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理评测基准存在两大核心局限：(1) 缺乏高难度数据集，多以 K-12 或入门级题目为主，而 IPhO、APhO 等奥赛级数据集规模小且更新缓慢，易导致模型饱和；(2) 缺乏对视觉形式、知识点和逐步解答过程的全面覆盖，仅基于最终答案的评分无法定位推理链中的具体错误来源，也限制了思维链监督和过程级强化学习的训练信号。

Method: 1. 数据构建：从约 15,000 张日常奥赛训练材料图像出发，经 OCR 识别（300 DPI 扫描 + Tesseract v5.3.0）、人工校对与 LaTeX 化转换、模糊匹配去重（阈值 0.95），最终保留 11,586 道题目。2. 标注体系：采用人机协作的两层难度标注（1-7 级，参考解答长度与 LLM 推理 token 数）与五大学科分类（力学、电磁学、热力学、光学、现代物理），由 MLLM 投票候选 + 专家终审。3. 评估协议：评估 18 个模型（10 闭源 + 8 开源；12 个 System-1 + 6 个 System-2），中英双语分别评测取均值；同时提供示意图 + 过程图 + 文本提示三种增强设置。4. 评分机制：双指标——Answer Score（最终答案严格匹配）与 Process Score（步骤分解为 1.0/0.5/0.0 三档，按模型自身推导路径而非参考解对齐评分），由 GPT-5.2、Claude-Opus-4.6、Gemini-3-Pro 三方裁判取均值，经 200 题人工核验 MAE 为 0.09。5. 错误分析：六类错误分类（物理感知、物理定律、代数符号、不完整推导、几何视觉、数值错误），结合首错位置、软/硬失败比例、学科与难度分层剖析。

Result: 1. 整体表现：Grok-4.2 以 33.7% 答案准确率领跑，Claude-Opus-4.6 28.1%，所有模型均低于 30%，最强开源模型 Qwen3-VL-235B-A22B 仅 20.0%，与人类基线 48.5% 存在显著差距。2. 过程 vs 答案：所有模型的步骤分均高于答案分，弱模型差距可达 3.4 倍，表明模型在推导早期仍具部分正确性但最终失败。3. 推理模式：System-2 思维链带来约 5.6 点增益，但 Claude-Opus-4.6（System-1）超越多个推理模型，说明基础能力不可替代。4. 视觉增强：加入过程图使六个模型的过程分普遍提升（+1.7 至 +5.5 点），效果远超文本提示，凸显图表信息的独特价值。5. 错误诊断：首错深度与基础能力正相关；Claude-Opus-4.6 首错平均在第 2.48 步；约 64% 失败集中于推理前端（物理感知 31% + 物理定律误用 25% + 几何误读 8%）；光学为跨模型通病（首错率最高），热力学最稳定；多数模型呈"U 型"难度曲线，Medium 比 Hard 更难，疑因官方赛题风格的后训练偏置。6. 语言效应：几乎所有模型英语得分高于汉语，Qwen3-VL-235B 因中文预训练占优而为唯一例外。

Conclusion: PhysElite 证明当前前沿 MLLM 距奥林匹克级物理专家水平仍有显著鸿沟；步骤级评估揭示了模型失败集中于问题建模与定律选用而非代数执行端；视觉过程图比文本提示更具引导价值；单一难度标签不足以解释跨基准表现，题目来源分布与风格对齐对结果影响显著。该基准兼具严格评测与过程监督训练资源双重价值，未来工作将致力于消除评分边界模糊性并构建完全私有的数据集以降低数据泄露风险。

Abstract: Understanding how (multimodal) large language models perform on physics problems requires benchmarks that reflect the difficulty and breadth of expert-level physical reasoning. Existing physics benchmarks remain limited in the following two important ways: (1) short of high-difficulty datasets, and (2) lack of comprehensive coverage of visual forms, knowledge points, and step-by-step solution processes. As a result, model performance on current datasets may not be fully representative of their ability to solve complex physics problems. To address these issues, we present PhysElite, a large-scale bilingual multimodal benchmark for Olympiad-level physics reasoning. PhysElite contains 11,586 Olympiad-tier problems. For each problem, we provide corresponding visual diagrams, step-by-step bilingual Chinese-English solution derivations, and the final answer. We benchmark 18 open-source and closed-source MLLMs, and find that even the strongest model reaches only 33.7% answer accuracy. We additionally conduct step-level process evaluation to diagnose where models fail in the reasoning chain. Our datasets are released at this https URL .

</details>


### [141] [Towards Reliable, Generalizable, and Specific In-Context Knowledge Editing via Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25100)
*Xuzhong Wang,Maiqi Jiang,Tejal Nair,Girija Bhusal,Yanfu Zhang,Haipeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MO-IKE，一种基于多目标强化学习的上下文知识编辑方法，通过将演示构建建模为约束马尔可夫决策过程，同时优化可靠性、泛化性和特异性三个相互冲突的目标，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的参数化知识静态且易过时，亟需在不重新训练的情况下更新目标事实。上下文知识编辑（IKE）虽具有免训练和适用于黑盒模型的优点，但其效果依赖于精心构建的提示。现有IKE方法在演示数量与质量之间权衡不足，且先前基于强化学习的方法（如DR-IKE）仅优化单一可靠性目标，牺牲了特异性，导致编辑过度优化问题。此外，现有方法忽视了不同类别演示（COPY、UPDATE、RETAIN）的全局排序与比例平衡。

Method: 1. **问题形式化**：将IKE的提示构建建模为约束马尔可夫决策过程（Constrained MDP），状态为当前构建的提示，动作空间包括从COPY、UPDATE、RETAIN候选池中选择演示或选择STOP停止符。2. **多目标奖励塑形**：以编辑成功率（ES，R）为主要奖励，将泛化性（PC）和特异性（RR）作为成本约束，通过固定乘子的拉格朗日松弛将多目标标量化：r(s,a) = R(s,a) - λ_PC·C_PC(s,a) - λ_RR·C_RR(s,a)。3. **策略优化**：使用基于BERT的检索器，对当前提示进行编码并通过可训练投影矩阵计算与候选的softmax分布；采用GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）算法，采样G个候选轨迹，计算组内归一化优势，通过裁剪替代目标与KL散度惩罚更新参数。

Result: 在Llama-3.2-3B和Mistral-7B-v0.3等五个冻结LLM上，MO-IKE在CounterFact、ZsRE、Wiki-Counterfact和311K的UniEdit基准上均达到最优。具体地：Llama-3.2上编辑成功率从85.0%提升至92.0%，特异性（保留率）相对DR-IKE提升23.0%，总体调和均值从54.9升至73.5；Mistral-v0.3上总体得分从62.0升至77.4。零样本跨模型迁移（Llama-3.2训练→Mistral-7B测试）性能几乎无下降。消融实验验证：移除保留约束导致RR显著下降，扩展动作空间至全部演示类别带来一致增益，奖励权重敏感性测试显示模型对超参数稳健。

Conclusion: MO-IKE通过多目标强化学习框架有效解决了上下文知识编辑中可靠性、泛化性与特异性之间的竞争权衡，是首个在IKE任务中实现三者协同优化的方法。其实验表明：(1) 显式的保留惩罚是防止编辑过度优化、保护邻近事实的必要条件；(2) 全类别动作空间的设计使检索器能够学到全局最优的演示组织方式；(3) 训练的检索器具有跨模型零样本泛化能力。然而存在局限：评估数据集规模有限，固定λ权重的影响未充分探索，RR绝对值仍较低（~60%），且框架在其他上下文学习任务上的迁移性有待验证。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are powerful but limited by static parametric knowledge that becomes outdated once pretraining ends. Knowledge editing addresses this problem by updating model behavior on target facts without full retraining. In particular, in-context knowledge editing has gained attention because it is training-free and readily applicable to black-box LLMs. Recent reinforcement learning (RL)-based approaches improve over fixed retrieval strategies by adapting prompt construction to the quantity-quality trade-off. Despite initial success, they fail to model the prompt as a structured entity under the distinct and often competing objectives of reliability, generality, and specificity. Previous methods largely optimize a single objective and make decisions over only part of the prompt construction process, thereby overlooking both the balance of different objectives and the global organization of demonstrations. We propose Multi-Objective In-context Knowledge Editing (MO-IKE), a multi-objective RL algorithm that formulates prompt construction for in-context knowledge editing as a Constrained Markov Decision Process. MO-IKE trains a dynamic retriever to optimize competing objectives in knowledge editing, enabling more balanced and globally coherent prompt construction. On Llama-3.2, MO-IKE improves edit success (reliability) from 85.0% to 92.0%, paraphrase consistency (generality) from 77% to 79%, while increasing retention rate (specificity) by 23.0% compared to prior RL-based methods.

</details>


### [142] [FuzzingBrain-Bench V1: Evaluating Open-Ended Bug Discovery by LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25158)
*Ze Sheng,Aleksandar Kezic,Zhicheng Chen,Jeff Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FuzzingBrain-Bench V1 是一个用于评估大语言模型在开源软件中发现软件漏洞能力的基准测试。它采用"开放式"评估方式，不再要求模型复现预定义的目标漏洞，而是统计模型通过模糊测试 harness 触发的不同崩溃签名数量。基准包含 77 个挑战，源自 43 个开源项目，覆盖 C/C++ 和 Java/JVM。Claude Opus 4.8 表现最佳，在 60/77 个挑战中触发崩溃，得分 196/579。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 漏洞发现基准（如 CyberGym、SEC-bench）通常要求模型生成能触发特定预定义目标漏洞的 PoC 输入，这种评估方式可能忽略模型发现的其他有效崩溃，无法真实反映模型的漏洞发现能力。此外，现有基准局限于 C/C++ 内存安全漏洞。作者提出需要一种更全面、更开放的评估方法来衡量 LLM 的真实漏洞发现能力。

Method: 1. 挑战构建：从 FuzzingBrain V2、QuartetFuzz、OSS-Fuzz 及项目官方渠道选取已确认漏洞，构建包含漏洞版本源代码、harness 代码、sanitizer 配置和 harness 二进制文件的 Docker 镜像；PoC 在镜像内运行 3 次验证稳定性后保留挑战，移除所有识别信息。

2. 系统架构：Agent 循环运行在主机，通过 MCP stdio 传输与挑战容器通信；提供三个工具——setup（返回任务信息）、exec（无网络的文件系统访问）、run_poc_on_harness（运行候选输入并返回 raw 输出）。

3. 崩溃签名与去重：签名为归一化的故障类型加上最多 3 个相关函数名，用"|"分隔；每次候选输入运行 3 次，3 次均产生相同签名才被计入；挑战内维护签名集合，集合内已有的视为重复。

4. 评分公式：score = Σ min(3, sig_c) × D_c，其中 D_c 为难度系数（1-5），每个挑战最多计入 3 个签名。

5. 难度分级：基于 3 个参考模型的表现分为 5 个 tier——D1（3 个模型均崩溃）、D2（2 个崩溃且至少 1 个发现 ≥3 个签名）、D3（其他）、D4（仅 1 个模型崩溃且 ≤2 个签名）、D5（无模型崩溃）。

6. 实验设置：对话轮次预算 100 轮，单次时长 1800 秒，3 个 Anthropic 模型在全部 77 个挑战上测试。

Result: 1. 总分：Opus 4.8 得 196/579（33.85%），触发 60/77 挑战；Sonnet 4.6 得 156/579（26.94%），触发 50/77；Haiku 4.5 得 58/579（10.02%），触发 35/77。

2. 难度分层表现：低难度层（D1/D2）中 Sonnet 略优于 Opus，因 Opus 倾向于提前终止 episode（median 51 轮 vs. Sonnet/Haiku 的 100 轮）；高难度层（D3/D4）Opus 反超。

3. 取消每挑战 3 个签名上限后，Opus 得分增长 33.16%，Sonnet 30.77%，Haiku 12.07%，模型层级不变。

4. 成本与时长：平均每挑战成本 Opus $2.51、Sonnet $3.29、Haiku $0.56；Opus 在 D5 挑战成本 $4.54/挑战，episode 时长随难度递增（Sonnet 反向递减）；总 agent 循环时间分别为 10.5、22.3、5.5 小时。

5. 未解决挑战：13 个含确认漏洞的挑战未被任一模型触发，表明当前模型在更复杂漏洞上仍有不足。

6. 错误类型分布：45 个挑战为内存安全类（堆/栈溢出、UAF、空指针等 7 种 CWE），32 个为拒绝服务及其他类（含 OOM、未捕获异常、可达断言、未定义行为、内存泄漏等 7 种 CWE）。

Conclusion: FuzzingBrain-Bench 提出了一种突破第三代基准局限的第四代评估范式：不局限于复现预定义漏洞，而是统计模型能通过 harness 触发的不同崩溃签名数，覆盖内存安全、DoS、JVM 异常等多种 CWE 类型。评估显示 Opus 4.8 优于 Sonnet 4.6 和 Haiku 4.5，但所有模型在 13 个挑战上均失败，表明当前 LLM 漏洞发现能力仍有提升空间。未来工作包括：(1) 将语料库扩展至 1000 个挑战；(2) 集成 MemorySanitizer 和 ThreadSanitizer 等更多 sanitizer 拓宽签名范围；(3) 用更广泛的模型面板和约束求解算法重新计算难度系数，以解决当前难度系数由参考面板自身结果导出的循环性问题，并通过与外部排行榜对比验证。

Abstract: Evaluating the ability of large language models (LLMs) to discover software bugs is increasingly important. Existing benchmarks typically evaluate this capability by asking the model to generate a proof-of-concept input that triggers a predefined target vulnerability. However, this setup may overlook valid crashes discovered by the model when they do not match the predefined target. As a result, the evaluation may not reflect the model's real capability. We present FuzzingBrain-Bench, a benchmark for assessing AI models' ability to discover bugs in open-source software. Models are given an open-source project and a sanitizer-instrumented harness in a self-contained Docker image. Their goal is to generate inputs that trigger as many distinct crashes as possible through the harness. A model's performance on each challenge is scored based on the number of distinct crash signatures it produces, capped at a predefined maximum and weighted by a difficulty coefficient. FuzzingBrain-Bench V1 consists of 77 challenges drawn from 43 open-source projects, with 36 C, 32 C++, and 9 Java/JVM challenges. We evaluate Claude Haiku 4.5, Claude Sonnet 4.6, and Claude Opus 4.8 on the full benchmark. Claude Opus 4.8 performs best, triggering crashes in 60 of 77 challenges and achieving a score of 196 out of 579. None of the three models triggers a crash in 13 challenges. The FuzzingBrain-Bench corpus and harnesses are publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [143] [Tunable Tool-Call Rates in LLM Agents via Representation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25198)
*Yuqi Chen,Vincent Siu,Yang Liu,Dawn Song,Chenguang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练、通过残差流中的单一线性方向来控制指令微调LLM智能体工具调用率的推理时干预方法，可将调用率从近0%单调调节至90%以上，同时具备知识选择性和工具泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在工具调用决策上表现不佳：过度调用增加延迟和成本甚至引发不可逆副作用，调用不足则导致模型在需要外部知识时自信地给出错误答案。现有方法如后训练和提示工程成本高且难以在推理时灵活调整，亟需一种轻量级、无需训练的推理时控制机制。

Method: 方法包含三个无需训练的步骤：(1) 前向传播倾向性代理——通过模型工具调用专属起始token的log概率衡量调用倾向；(2) 提取方向——在多工具环境中按倾向性排序，用高/低倾向查询池的残差流差均值（difference-of-means）提取单一线性方向v_ℓ；(3) 推理时干预——在选定解码层ℓ将α·v_ℓ加到残差流上，α为正鼓励调用，α为负抑制调用。还使用了投影钳制（clamping）和方向消融（ablation）来验证该方向承载决策信号。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B上，α从-2到+3可将调用率从近0%单调提升至79%-100%，且调用格式保持良好。在PopQA开放域问答上，配合实时搜索执行，扫描α可绘制成本/准确率Pareto前沿，准确率从0.29提升至0.56（约每题1.1次搜索），结合搜索型提示可达0.58。诱导的调用精准落在低流行度尾部问题上，体现知识选择性。该方向可迁移到6个未见工具（翻译、天气、单位转换、邮件、SQL、股票），抑制效果与各工具自身方向相当甚至更强。同一方案在Qwen3、Qwen3-8B、Qwen3-30B-A3B（MoE）、Gemma-4-E4B（多模态）和gpt-oss-20b等五种密集/MoE/多模态模型上一致有效，基线从欠用到过用范围均被覆盖。消融研究证实该投影承载决策信号而非仅沿其移动。

Conclusion: 工具调用决策由残差流中单一可解释的线性方向编码，可在不训练、不改提示的情况下提供连续推理时控制，具备知识选择性、工具泛化性和调用/选择解耦特性。但该方法不控制工具执行质量，对推理优先模型需绕过推理阶段，且过强正向干预会破坏工具调用格式。

Abstract: Deciding whether to call a tool is a core competence of an LLM agent, and a costly one to get wrong: needless calls add latency, accrue cost, and may trigger irreversible side effects, while missing calls leave the model confidently wrong on questions it could only answer through tool-calls. Models manage this balance poorly, both over-using and under-using tools. Existing methods such as post-training and prompt engineering are expensive and difficult to modify at inference time. We show that whether an instruction-tuned model calls a tool can be controlled by a single linear direction in its residual stream, extracted without any training from the model's own tool-use preference signal and turned into an inference-time intervention with no prompt change. Adding the direction with strength $\alpha$ moves the call rate monotonically from near $0\% $ to over $90\%$ while keeping calls well-formed. The steering works in both directions: dialing it down suppresses calls, and dialing it up induces new calls that land precisely on the questions the model cannot answer from its own knowledge. We also show that the direction generalizes to unseen tools with strength comparable to each tool's own direction and without favoring any specific tool choice. With live tool execution, a single sweep of the steering traces a cost/accuracy Pareto frontier and nearly doubles open-domain QA accuracy ($0.29 \! \rightarrow \! 0.56$); the same recipe transfers across a diverse range of models spanning dense, MoE, and multimodal architectures, without any training. Our code is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [144] [Federation Is Nearly Free, Reasoning Is Not: Tradeoffs for AI Co-Scientists in Protein Characterization Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25215)
*Maia Kapur,Timothy Boe,Abby Jerger,Paul Rigor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了自然语言驱动的自主科研智能体工作流中的灵活性与可重现性、可观测性的权衡。通过对蛋白质功能表征这一可验证任务进行控制性消融实验，发现LLM选择对预测质量起主导作用（Opus约92%-94%，o4-mini约40%-50%），而经典强化学习（PPO）策略以零token成本、最快延迟和完美一致性达到接近前沿的准确率（88%）。联邦拓扑对性能影响可忽略。结论建议：常规可验证任务应采用廉价的确定性策略，灵活的LLM推理则保留用于开放性科学发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言驱动的科研智能体工作流在灵活性与推理能力方面具有优势，但牺牲了确定性、可重现性和可观测性。随着智能体跨越机构边界进行通信，联邦拓扑会影响延迟和成本。论文旨在通过受控消融实验，系统性地量化这些权衡，为科学工作流中智能体的部署提供可操作的指导。

Method: 采用控制性消融实验方法，在生产级科研智能体平台上进行评估。任务设定为可验证的蛋白质功能表征任务：给定蛋白质序列，要求智能体通过路由调用常用工具进行功能表征。比较维度包括：(1) 联邦拓扑；(2) 经典强化学习（PPO）vs LLM驱动的控制架构；(3) 不同语言模型；(4) 提示词专业度。结果按蛋白质新颖性进行分层分析。

Result: 关键结果包括：(1) LLM选择对预测质量的影响远超拓扑或提示策略——Opus准确率约92%-94%，而o4-mini仅约40%-50%；(2) PPO策略以零token成本、最快延迟和完美一致性达到88%的准确率（接近最佳LLM），但无推理轨迹；(3) 专家提示的LLM达到最高准确率，但成本高且一致性较差；(4) 提示词依赖性在任务最难时最大；(5) 联邦拓扑对性能施加的惩罚可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 研究为科学工作流中智能体的部署提供了明确指导：对于常规的、可验证的任务，廉价的确定性策略（如PPO）能以完全可重现性提供接近前沿的准确率；而灵活的LLM推理应保留用于开放性的科学发现任务。这一权衡框架有助于在生产环境中合理配置智能体资源。

Abstract: Natural language driven autonomous co-scientist workflows involve a fundamental trade-off between flexibility and reasoning at the expense of determinism, reproducibility, and observability. Such agents increasingly must communicate across institutional boundaries, where federation topology can shape latency and cost. We systematically evaluated these tradeoffs using a controlled ablation on a production agentic platform for science. We use a verifiable task: given a protein sequence, we ask an agent to confidently characterize its function by routing across common tools. We compare federation topology, classic RL vs LLM-driven harnesses, language model, and prompt expertise. We also stratify results by protein novelty. We find that the choice of LLM dominated prediction quality far more than topology or prompting (Opus ~92%-94% vs o4-mini ~40%-50%). The PPO policy was nearly as accurate as the best LLM (88%) at zero token cost, fastest latency, and perfect consistency, but yields no reasoning trace. Expert prompted LLMs reached the highest accuracy but were high-cost and less consistent; prompt dependence was largest when the task was hardest. Federation imposed a negligible penalty on performance. These results offer actionable guidance for deploying agents for scientific workflows: for routine, verifiable tasks, a cheap deterministic policy delivers near-frontier accuracy with complete reproducibility, while flexible LLM reasoning is best reserved for open-ended discovery.

</details>


### [145] [LLM-Driven, Datasheet-Aware Automated Hardware Compatibility Verification for Early-Stage, Pre-Schematic Embedded System Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25217)
*Haotian Qiao,Robert P. Dick*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于LLM的硬件兼容性自动验证框架DEVICES，用于在详细原理图设计之前的早期嵌入式系统设计阶段，基于硬件数据手册和高层级组件连接描述来识别接口级不兼容问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1）传统手动检查数据手册进行兼容性验证效率低、易出错，且设计缺陷晚期发现成本高昂（行业平均2.9次PCB改板、每次4.4万美元）；2）现有LLM"上传-查询"工作流因长且分散的上下文导致属性遗漏，且临时提示缺乏明确完整的验证标准，导致评估结果不一致；3）一次性LLM处理缺乏工程证据与LLM生成操作之间的可独立验证接口，难以实现可信的设计自动化。

Method: 核心方法包括三个主要阶段：（1）设计图构建：LLM根据自然语言连接描述构建包含电源、数据、环境三类网络节点的设计图，将系统级验证分解为连接感知的子任务；（2）分层属性检索：从硬件知识图谱中按验证标准分层、模型感知地检索所需工程属性，支持检索缓存以复用结果；（3）验证脚本生成：为每个网络节点组装任务级紧凑上下文，LLM生成确定性Python脚本执行兼容性评估（数值计算由脚本而非LLM完成）。关键创新包括：域导向的形式化验证标准（每个标准明确指定验证流程、所需属性和确定性兼容性规则）、属性规范中心的提示策略、以及将LLM定位为"通过可追溯验证阶段转换工程信息"而非直接生成答案。

Result: 在7个嵌入式系统设计、34份数据手册（共1210页）上的评估显示：DEVICES达到97.5%的兼容性验证准确率，相比"上传-查询"基线（无验证标准14.9%、有验证标准62.7%）大幅提升；输入上下文大小减少8.6倍（单查询最大约12.6k tokens，平均约4.7k tokens）；候选属性平均减少70.4%；所有使用的属性均忠实于输入上下文，无外部知识引入。消融实验证明验证标准和任务感知上下文构建均为必要组件。

Conclusion: DEVICES证明了基于LLM的、规范驱动的早期硬件兼容性验证的可行性，验证工作量可随相关组件交互而非总体规格内容量扩展。未来LLM能力的提升将增强各局部推理任务而非取代结构化验证架构；DEVICES是一个可扩展的架构，能够利用未来LLM进展实现更具扩展性和可靠性的硬件兼容性评估。当前局限：仅支持文本规格（不支持图形理解，因此暂不支持开关稳压器等以图表形式给出效率的器件），连接描述仍需少量手动构造。

Abstract: We present an LLM-driven, datasheet-aware framework for early-stage hardware compatibility verification that identifies documentation-level interface incompatibilities based on hardware datasheets and high-level component connectivity descriptions. It does not require, and can therefore be used, before detailed schematic simulation and implementation. We view trustworthy LLM-assisted design automation not as directly generating answers from documents, but as transforming engineering information through traceable verification stages. Given hardware datasheets and high-level component connectivity descriptions, the framework constructs a design graph that captures device connectivity and shared interaction domains, retrieves only the engineering properties required by explicit, domain-oriented verification criteria , and generates deterministic scripts for compatibility evaluation. By decomposing compatibility analysis into modular stages and preserving intermediate results, the framework reduces context overhead, improves transparency and tractability, enables scaling, and avoids reliance on LLMs for numerical computation. Evaluated on seven embedded-system designs comprising 34 datasheets, our framework achieves 97.5% compatibility-verification accuracy and an 8.6 times reduction in input context size compared with ``upload-and-query'' workflows. These results demonstrate the feasibility of LLM-assisted, specification-based hardware compatibility verification at an early design stage, as well as the need for, and substantial benefits of, modular task decomposition, formalized verification criteria, and task-aware compact context construction.

</details>


### [146] [FLARE: Verifying MILP Reformulations with LLM-Based Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25220)
*Henry Robbins,Connor Lawless,Madeleine Udell,Ellen Vitercik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FLARE与FLARE-NL两种方法，利用大语言模型和Lean定理证明器在公式层面（而非实例层面）验证MILP（混合整数线性规划）重构的正确性，并构造了一个包含20个问题、109个公式的FormulationBench基准测试集。FLARE通过形式化验证生成机器可检验证书，在NP-hard子集上达到100%准确率；FLARE-NL作为快速低成本的LLM代理，无证书但精度相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型被广泛用于自动生成和强化MILP公式，迫切需要可靠的方法来验证AI生成的公式是否对所有实例保持原优化问题的语义。现有方法仅在实例层面进行数值验证，无法保证对一般实例的普适性。

Method: 作者提出一种构造性的MILP重构定义（Constructive Reformulation），要求在参数空间、可行域和目标值之间存在显式的正向/反向映射和严格单调的目标映射，该定义可被形式化为Lean定理并由自动定理证明器（ATP）机器检查。FLARE利用基于LLM的智能体（Claude Code等）结合Lean-LSP-MCP进行自动形式化和证明生成；FLARE-NL则仅使用自然语言提示引导前沿推理模型直接判断，不产出形式化证书。

Result: 在FormulationBench的16个NP-hard问题54对公式上，FLARE达到100%准确率并生成机器可检查证书，FLARE-NL同样100%准确但速度快30倍、成本低25倍。两者均显著优于执行验证（83.3%）和EquivaMap（81.5%）。在错误重构类型细分中，FLARE和FLARE-NL在最坏情况下均保持100%准确率，而基线方法为0%。作者还利用该方法发现了EvoCut提出的5个无效切割平面和Ferchtandiker et al.提出的4个无效重构。

Conclusion: FLARE是首个能生成机器可检验重构证书的自动化方法，在公式层面提供形式化保证，弥补了实例级验证的盲区。FLARE-NL作为快速代理适用于不需要严格保证的场景。两者结合可在自动化优化建模中实现可靠的验证流程。

Abstract: Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) is a fundamental tool for combinatorial optimization with extensive real-world applications. A central challenge is designing computationally efficient MILP formulations. Large Language Models (LLMs) offer new opportunities to automate the modeling process, from deriving formulations to strengthening them. Reliable automation requires robust methods for verifying that proposed formulations preserve the underlying optimization problem. However, existing approaches evaluate formulations numerically and fail to reason about general problem instances. We resolve this limitation by introducing a constructive definition of MILP reformulation that can be formalized in Lean and machine-checked. We develop FLARE (Formulation-Level Automated Reformulation Evaluation), a method that uses an LLM-based agent and the Lean proof assistant to verify proposed reformulations against a reference formulation. To evaluate our approach, we introduce FormulationBench, a challenging dataset of 20 problems and 109 formulations. FLARE outperforms existing methods, with 100% accuracy on the NP-hard subset of FormulationBench. Furthermore, FLARE produces a machine-checkable certificate for every reformulation it accepts. For cases where formal guarantees are not necessary, we introduce FLARE-NL, a fast and cheap LLM proxy that matches FLARE's accuracy but produces no certificate. These methods enable reliable verification in automated optimization modeling.

</details>


### [147] [Hierarchical MoE for Multi-Modal ILD Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25261)
*Alec K. Peltekian,Gorkem Durak,Halil Ertugrul Aktas,Carrie Lynn Richardson,Mary Carns,Kathleen Aren,GR Scott Budinger,Anthony J. Esposito,Alexander Misharin,Alok Nidhi Choudhary,Ankit Agrawal,Ulas Bagci*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于间质性肺病(ILD)诊断的分层多模态混合专家(MoE)架构,通过两级门控机制整合预训练的影像专家与结构化电子健康记录(EHR),在患者级别交叉验证中取得了最高的平均AUC(0.8750)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MoE架构假设专家容量同质化且路由与领域无关,这与医学影像(尤其是具有区域分布异质性的ILD)的解剖结构和病理特征不匹配。此外,大多数深度学习方法将全肺作为单一整体处理,稀释了区域信号且缺乏可解释性。同时,先前的多模态方法依赖密集交互或固定融合策略,缺乏跨模态和临床特征组的显式结构化路由。

Method: 方法包含三个核心组件:(1)叶级影像专家:基于放射组学引导的重要性权重,使用5个叶特异性3D SwinUNETR骨干网络提取影像特征,影像专家训练后冻结;(2)分层EHR专家:将EHR变量按临床定义分为特征组(全配置7组69变量,选择性配置3组12变量),通过子门控模块学习组特异性贡献;(3)模态级门控:自适应平衡影像与EHR贡献。训练采用三阶段协议,使用患者级别5折交叉验证。

Result: 选择性分层MoE取得了最高平均AUC 0.8750±0.0443,优于影像单独REN(0.8646±0.0467)和SwinUNETR(0.7685±0.0759,p<0.001)。选择性配置优于全配置(0.8496)。消融研究显示:对EHR-CT时间链接窗口(0-90天)具有鲁棒性;模态权重平衡(影像0.498,EHR 0.502);ECE为0.131±0.038,显示合理校准。影像主导路由与高置信度错误相关。

Conclusion: 该分层多模态MoE框架通过可解释的路由机制扩展了解剖信息引导的REN,选择性配置在临床近端特征组上集中路由容量,减少了过度EHR主导,保留了互补性影像证据并改善了校准。局限包括单中心队列、硬皮病相关ILD的特异性、临床动机分组而非学习型分组、缺乏显式缺失指示符等。未来工作需进行更大规模外部验证和组件级消融研究。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) models combine specialized predictors under learned routing, offering a principled mechanism for leveraging heterogeneity in medical data. We present a hierarchical multimodal MoE for interstitial lung disease (ILD) classification that integrates a frozen, pre-trained imaging expert with structured electronic health records (EHR) via two-stage gating. A modality-level gate assigns patient-specific weights to imaging and EHR predictions, while a sub-gating module decomposes the EHR branch into clinically defined feature groups with learned, group-specific contributions. This design preserves stable imaging representations while enabling input-dependent clinical weighting and explicit EHR specialization. Under strict patient-level cross-validation, the model achieved the highest mean AUC among the evaluated methods (0.8750 +- 0.0443), compared with 0.8646 for imaging-only REN and 0.7685 for SwinUNETR. The framework extends interpretability across anatomical regions, imaging--EHR utilization, and clinically defined EHR feature groups.

</details>


### [148] [PhaseShift: Topology-Aware Data Harmonization and Model Consolidation Across Signalized Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25275)
*Yash Ranjan,Artur Kumik,Rahul Sengupta,Anand Rangarajan,Sanjay Ranka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PhaseShift提出了一种拓扑感知的框架，将异构路口的轨迹数据协调为以行为主体为中心的统一表示，训练一个可复用的主干网络，支持池化部署、零样本迁移到新路口以及低数据量的适配。论文基于佛罗里达两个区域五个信号控制路口的实测数据，证明一个共享模型在10秒预测上全面优于各路口独立训练的局部模型，并通过固定窗口数的混合采样实验证明了跨站点组合的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的交通行为模型通常为每个路口单独训练，形成无法跨站点共享证据的模型组合。这种"一站一模型"的工作流成本高昂，且当新的局部条件出现时缺乏原则性的复用路径。本文旨在解决异构路口数据能否支持一个共享模型而不丢失决定行为的关键局部上下文的问题。

Method: 方法包含三个核心组件：(1) 跨域数据协调器——通过自车相对坐标、轨迹诱导的运动路径、归一化信号状态（红/黄/保护绿/许可绿/未知）、可变数量的交互令牌以及基于DTW聚类的轨迹诱导几何，移除站点特有的约定；(2) 共享主干网络——包含8层空间注意力编码器和8层自回归时序解码器，采用25模态高斯混合模型（GMM）输出，以掩码负对数似然为训练目标；(3) 部署生命周期——支持Pooled（池化）、Zero-Shot（留一交叉口零样本）和Fine-Tuned（1000窗口微调）三种部署模式，并提供残差诊断信号。

Result: 基于平衡的10万训练窗口/站，在五个佛罗里达信号控制路口的实验表明：(1) 10秒预测上，Pooled模型在所有5个站点的minADE和minFDE上均优于独立训练的Local模型，中位数降幅分别为36.8%和22.0%，最佳案例为Site 7（67.1%/60.0%）；(2) 留一交叉口零样本部署在4/5站点的10秒双指标上击败Local训练，包括一个跨区域折叠（Site 22，南佛罗里达）；(3) Site 7固定10万预算的组成扫描表明：每种跨站混合物都优于纯局部训练，加入20%跨站数据使10秒误差降低66.2%/58.4%；(4) 在两个高流量站点（7和22）的长自回归滚动中，Local模型在10秒处落后于校准的IDM基线，而预训练主干方案则未出现此逆转。

Conclusion: PhaseShift展示了真实路口数据可在保留必要的局部物理-控制上下文的同时被协调为一个更强大的共享长视野模型。论文提出了实用的生命周期：巩固已知域、在新域上衡量覆盖度、仅在验证显示有意义残差时进行专门化。然而，证据缺口仍在于归因：需要重复种子、匹配的时序对照、额外目标和表征消融，以确定跨站训练为何有效。此外，评估协议衡量的是重放上下文下的条件生成，而非闭环交通仿真。

Abstract: Learned traffic-behavior models are commonly trained separately for each intersection, creating model portfolios that cannot share evidence across sites. We present PhaseShift, a topology-aware framework that harmonizes heterogeneous roadside trajectories into a shared actor-centric representation and trains one reusable backbone. Ego-relative coordinates, trajectory-induced movement paths, normalized signal context, and variable-cardinality interaction tokens remove site conventions while preserving behaviorally relevant topology. The backbone supports pooled operation, zero-shot at a held-out intersection, and low-data adaptation. We evaluate five intersections in two Florida regions on balanced field data, 100k training windows and equal-sized test sets per site under a replay-conditioned, best-of-sampled-trajectory protocol. At 10s, one pooled model lowers both minADE and minFDE relative to trained local models at all five sites, with median reductions of 36.8% and 22.0%. Leave-one-intersection-out deployment, including one cross-region fold, beats local training on both 10-s metrics at four of five sites, although short-horizon performance is less uniform. Fine-tuning with 1,000 target update windows improves on zero-shot at three sites and is the strongest regime at one. At site 7, every cross-site mixture sharply lowers long-horizon error under a fixed 100k-window budget; test-likelihood gains argue against a best-of-sample dispersion-only explanation. Local models fall behind calibrated IDM at the two highest-flow sites after long autoregressive rollouts; pretrained-backbone regimes do not. Within this five-site evaluation, PhaseShift demonstrates consolidation across heterogeneous physical control settings while identifying sites that still require adaptation. The protocol measures conditional single-vehicle generation under replayed context, not closed-loop traffic simulation.

</details>


### [149] [BixBench3: Benchmarking AI agents on research-study-scale computational biology tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25286)
*Zane Koch,Asmamaw T. Wassie,Javier Valdes-Aleman,Jason Lee,Michaela M. Hinks,Samuel G. Rodriques,Andrew D. White,Jon M. Laurent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BixBench3是一个用于评估AI智能体在计算生物学中完成完整研究规模任务能力的基准测试。它包含20个源自已发表研究的任务，涵盖138个数据制品。13个前沿模型的得分范围从0.00到0.48，性能受数据规模、分析链长度和科学领域影响。最佳模型GPT 5.6 Sol仅能复现约48%的科学制品，且较高性能并不必然意味着更高成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM驱动的智能体在科学研究中的应用日益广泛，现有基准测试主要评估短时问答或单步分析能力，缺乏对智能体能否端到端完成整个计算生物学研究规模的系统性评估。因此需要建立一个能够衡量智能体从原始数据到科学结果全流程分析能力的基准。

Method: 构建包含20个任务的基准测试BixBench3，这些任务源自已发表论文，涵盖17种实验测定类型和9个科学领域。每项任务要求智能体接收研究目标、方法学指导和原始数据，执行一系列分析产生数据制品（如峰值调用矩阵、差异表达表），然后通过编程方式将这些制品与原始已发表研究的对应制品进行比较评分。评分采用F1分数（行列恢复）、CCC（数值一致性）、宏F1（分类标签）和重叠F1（基因组区间）四种指标的乘积，阈值0.80表示保留主要生物学含义。使用Inspect AI框架的ReAct循环运行13个前沿模型，每项任务运行24小时上限。

Result: 13个前沿模型得分从0.00（Gemini 3.1 Flash Lite）到0.48（GPT 5.6 Sol）不等；最佳模型GPT 5.6 Sol仅能复现约48%的请求制品。性能随原始数据量增加而下降（<100 GB任务得分0.36 vs >100 GB任务得分0.10），随分析链深度增加而下降（1-2步0.36 vs 3+步0.24）。平均每任务使用6.8小时、1.02亿token和43美元，最长尝试消耗24小时、10.7亿token和525美元。表现最优的模型反而使用的token更少、成本更低，表明最大性能并不需要最大成本。失败模式分析显示，过早终止和重复重试循环与最低得分最相关。

Conclusion: BixBench3是目前报告的生物学领域中时间跨度最长的基准测试。结果表明，AI智能体正在接近能够完成整个研究规模计算生物学任务的能力，最佳模型能保留约一半制品的原始科学解释。但当前智能体在处理大数据集、长分析链和错误恢复方面仍然存在明显困难。值得注意的是，最大性能并不必然需要最大成本，因为最佳模型使用的token和成本反而更低。BixBench3测试的是执行规定分析的能力，而非决定哪些分析值得开展的能力。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) promises to accelerate biological research by automating computational analyses. Yet the ability of AI agents to carry out computational biology at the scale of complete research studies has not been systematically evaluated. Here we introduce BixBench3, a benchmark that measures the capacity of AI agents to process raw biological data through to scientific results. We designed BixBench3 tasks to mirror the delegation of work from a scientist to an agent: the scientist chooses the research question and high-level methods, then delegates implementation of all analyses to the agent. In each task, an agent receives a research objective, methodological guidance, and raw data derived from a published scientific study, and must execute a sequence of analyses to achieve the research objective. The data artifacts resulting from these analyses - such as peak call matrices or differential expression tables - are programmatically graded against the corresponding artifacts generated and reported in the original study. Across 20 BixBench3 tasks encompassing the generation of 138 unique artifacts, we find that 13 frontier models achieve scores ranging from 0.00 for Gemini 3.1 Flash Lite to 0.48 for GPT 5.6 Sol. Agents perform worse on tasks with larger raw datasets (0.36 on tasks with <100 GB versus 0.10 on tasks with >100 GB) and on analyses requiring more sequential steps (0.36 at 1-2 steps vs 0.24 at 3+). On average, agents use 6.8 hours, 102 million tokens, and $43 to complete each task, with the longest attempts consuming 24 hours, 1.07 billion tokens, and $525. Notably, the highest-scoring agents used fewer tokens and were cheaper than less performant options. These results reveal that LLMs vary substantially in their ability to (1) execute multiple sequential analysis steps coherently, (2) manage large quantities of raw data, and (3) work across scientific domains.

</details>


### [150] [FinRiskAtlas: Decision-Aligned Evaluation of Large Language Models for Financial Risk Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25325)
*Suyang Zhong,Jingzhe Zhu,Qi Xu,Liyao Sun,Yin Wang,Qingqing Sun,Shuai Chen,Tianyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FinRiskAtlas，一个面向金融风险审查场景的中文LLM基准，从"决策对齐"视角评估大模型在专业金融审查工作流中的可靠性。其核心由两部分组成：静态基准（9,742实例，覆盖53个任务家族，包括42个领域知识家族和11个下游审查操作）和FinRisk-Ask（基于680个去标识化专业轨迹的离线回放评估）。实验涵盖33个模型配置，结果表明：不同下游操作产生显著不同的模型排名（平均成对Spearman相关系数仅为0.42），基于知识的筛选在特定操作上可造成高达18.01分的遗憾；且频繁进入Ask分支并不必然带来更好的证据获取效果，揭示出广义金融能力分数无法充分反映模型在专业工作流中的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融LLM基准多以数据集或任务形式为评估单元，而非部署系统所需支持的专业决策。其局限性体现在两方面：(1) 评估单元错位——同一案件记录派生的任务可能对应不同专业操作，不同表面格式的任务可能代表同一操作能力；(2) 忽视证据状态的演进——固定输入假设隐含记录充分，但实际金融风险审查是迭代过程，需要判断何时获取额外证据以及获取何种证据。

Method: 方法论包含两个核心设计：(1) 操作中心评估——通过显式评估契约Γ_f=(c_f, I_f, d_f, Y_f, s_f)定义每个家族，其中c_f为能力维度、I_f为模型可见信息域、d_f为专业决策对象、Y_f为审查员产物模式、s_f为评分协议；能力分类采用三层结构（领域知识→证据基础处理→应用审查）。(2) 证据状态控制——FinRisk-Ask通过离线回放专业审查轨迹，模型仅可见决策点前的信息C_t，后续证据仅用于构建专家验证的证据目标E_t^+，采用ERA（证据请求对齐度）作为端到端指标，并通过AskR和CRA分解分支进入与请求定位能力。

Result: 实验覆盖33个模型配置（含Qwen、DeepSeek、Kimi、GLM、GPT、Gemini、Claude等系列）。主要发现：(1) 11个固定证据操作产生非冗余排名（平均成对Spearman相关系数ρ=0.42，37/55操作对相关性低于0.5，机构匹配与风险分类ρ=-0.03），11个操作的最优模型分散于9个不同配置；(2) 基于领域知识的最优筛选（k=1）在信息提取操作上可造成18.01分遗憾，决策观点生成在k=15时仍有6.70分遗憾；(3) FinRisk-Ask中Ling-2.6-1T以AskR=96.57%、CRA=82.58%获得最高ERA=79.75%；Qwen3.7-Max虽有最高CRA=87.67%，但因AskR仅57.80%导致ERA仅50.68%；32个非评估配置中Ask-Proceed召回差距中位数达46.26分，表明模型系统性地更倾向于复现证据请求而非推进决策。

Conclusion: 研究证明广义金融能力分数无法充分反映模型在专业金融工作流中的可靠性，为专业金融审查场景的LLM评估提供了与部署决策对齐的评估单元范式。FinRiskAtlas贡献体现在三方面：(1) 提出以专业审查操作为核心评估单元的操作中心评估范式；(2) 构建基于轨迹的证据状态控制基准FinRisk-Ask及ERA端到端度量；(3) 在33个配置上验证基准粒度对模型选择的影响。未来基准设计应使评估单元更紧密地对齐部署语言模型所需支持的专业决策。

Abstract: Deploying large language models for professional financial review requires more than measuring general financial competence: models must perform the specific review operation required by a workflow and determine whether available evidence is sufficient for a defensible decision. Existing financial benchmarks cover knowledge, reasoning, compliance, and professional tasks, but their evaluation units are often organized around datasets or task formulations rather than the decisions that deployed systems support. We introduce FinRiskAtlas, a Chinese-language benchmark that evaluates financial LLMs along two complementary dimensions: operation execution under fixed evidence states and evidence-state control under evolving review conditions. The static benchmark contains 9,742 instances across 53 task families, including 42 Domain Knowledge families and eleven downstream review operations defined by explicit evaluation contracts. FinRisk-Ask extends this framework through offline replay of 680 pre-action states from 104 de-identified professional trajectories, withholding future evidence during inference and using it only to construct expert-verified evidence targets. Across 33 model configurations, operation-level evaluation yields non-redundant rankings (mean pairwise Spearman correlation 0.42 across downstream operations), and knowledge-based shortlisting can incur up to 18.01 points of regret on individual operations. FinRisk-Ask further shows that entering the Ask branch more frequently does not necessarily improve request targeting or end-to-end evidence acquisition. These results show that broad financial capability scores do not fully capture where models are reliable in professional workflows, motivating evaluation units aligned with the decisions and evidence states that deployed systems must support.

</details>


### [151] [Learning What to Share and What to Personalize: Hierarchical Strategy Co-Evolution for Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25329)
*Yupeng Han,Shuochen Liu,Kai Zhang,Ze Liu,Zhihong Pan,Xianquan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI
