TL;DR: HiPS是一种用于LLM智能体长对话记忆管理的分层策略协同进化框架，将记忆管理策略解耦为全局共享规则（Universal Strategy）和用户个性化增量规则（Persona Delta），并通过基于在线证据的跨层规则流动机制动态校准两者边界，在多个个性化记忆基准上取得一致性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强智能体的记忆管理策略存在两个局限：1）策略在训练前固定，无法根据交互反馈动态调整；2）采用一刀切的全局共享策略，忽略了不同用户行为的差异性，导致少数派用户的学习信号被多数淹没。需要解决"哪些规则应共享、哪些应个性化"的边界发现问题。

Method: HiPS框架包含三大核心机制：(1) Universal Strategy Distillation (USD)——通过对比高低奖励轨迹，使用结构化diff（validate/hypothesize/revise）提炼跨用户通用规则，并通过预测增益（PG）和证据等级（tentative→supported→established）跟踪过滤噪声；(2) Persona Delta Distillation (PDD)——基于分歧度阈值θ_div门控，仅对行为偏离群体规范的用户生成个性化管理规则；(3) Cross-Level Rule Flow——通过泛化（被多用户验证的个性化规则提升至通用层）和特化（被矛盾的通用规则由个性化规则替代）动态校准边界。策略通过子模选择注入提示，使用GRPO结合任务奖励和合规奖励联合训练，并通过分离R_ans（用于蒸馏）和R_follow（用于策略更新）防止自强化循环。

Result: 在PersonaMem、PrefEval、PersonaBench、PERMA四大基准共12个评测设置上全面超越基线（包括Long Context、RAG、Mem0、A-Mem、LightMem、MemAgent、Mem-α、MemSkill等）。消融实验揭示了显著的域内/域外重要性翻转：USD和PG对域内任务最关键，而Cross-Level Flow和门控机制对域外泛化最关键。PersonaMem 32K/128K分别达到73.49/62.01（MemSkill为64.45/55.37）。策略在不同骨干模型（Qwen2.5-7B、GPT-4o-mini、Gemini 2.5 flash、GPT-5）间具有良好可迁移性，记忆库大小随对话增长趋于收敛（约1900 token @ 128K），处理时间近似线性增长。

Conclusion: HiPS通过分层策略协同进化机制成功解决了记忆管理中共享与个性化的边界发现问题，验证了显式、可解释的策略表示相比隐式参数化策略的优势，为构建真正个性化的长期记忆系统提供了新路径。局限性在于当前评估以英文对话为主，且未处理极长期（年级别）的用户基线漂移问题。

Abstract: Memory-augmented agents maintain compact user profiles throughout extended conversations, enabling personalized and consistent responses without the need to process the entire dialogue history. The quality of these user profiles relies on the underlying memory management strategy: at each step, the agent must determine what to retain, compress, or discard. However, existing methods typically employ a static, one-size-fits-all strategy established before training. In practice, the optimal memory decision is inherently user-specific and dynamically evolves alongside policy optimization. To address this, we propose \textbf{HiPS} (\textbf{Hi}erarchical \textbf{P}ersonalized \textbf{S}trategy), a framework that decouples memory management into a globally shared foundation and a user-specific adaptive tier. Specifically, HiPS employs \textbf{Universal Strategy} to extract shared principles from cross-persona trajectories, alongside \textbf{Persona Delta Distillation} to generate tailored rules for users whose behaviors diverge from general patterns. \textbf{Cross-Level Rule Flow} dynamically calibrates their boundary by promoting broadly validated personal rules and demoting contradicted global ones. The architecture establishes a co-evolution loop where a mechanism guarantees that all strategy refinements are anchored to task outcomes. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent improvements over memory-augmented baselines.

</details>


### [152] [Where vs What: Decomposing Structural and Content Failures in LLM-Generated Structured Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25358)
*Yiwei Zhang,Chengke Wu,Li Wang,Jianqiang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文针对LLM生成结构化输出(JSON和表格)中"位置正确但内容错误"与"内容正确但位置错误"两类失败混淆的问题,提出结构-内容分解(SCD)评估框架,发现"结构先退化"现象,并据此设计SA-RLVR强化学习方法提升结构定位能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化输出评估(如JSONSchemaBench、BFCL、SWE-bench、text-to-SQL)将生成失败作为整体衡量,无法区分"放置错误"(正确值放在错误位置)与"值错误"(错误值放在正确位置)。这两种失败模式对应不同的底层缺陷,需要不同干预。论文假设结构复杂度增加时,"正确值、错误位置"的失败会愈发突出。

Method: 1) SCD三层级评估框架:格式有效性→架构合规率(SCR)→值放置准确率(VPA),配合Value Presence(VP)、Structural Compliance Gap(SCG)、Displacement Rate(DR)诊断指标;2) 在JSON(树形拓扑)和表格(网格拓扑)两种域中实例化,使用算法生成的确定性任务,三个复杂度级别S/M/L;3) 评估6个模型(GPT-4o、DeepSeek-V3、DeepSeek-V4-Flash、Qwen3-8B、Qwen2.5-14B、Qwen2.5-7B);4) 通过控制消融探究失败机制(递归深度、种植值数量、语义线索、路径歧义);5) 提出SA-RLVR,将SCD分解指标转化为GRPO的可验证奖励r(y)=VPA+0.3×SCR,在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct上用LoRA训练。

Result: 1) JSON域L级任务中,即便是DeepSeek-V4-Flash也有35%召回值被错放,Qwen2.5-7B错放率达74%,证实"剪刀模式"(VP保持高位而VPA显著下降);2) 表格域同样出现该模式,强模型DR为17-28%,Qwen2.5-7B达98%;3) 消融表明递归深度(而非种植数量)是主要驱动因素,语义线索弱化和字段名重复均显著提升DR(+7.5pp和+8.2pp);4) SA-RLVR将JSON VPA从0.264提升至0.629,远超SFT基线的0.281,并在OOD-Eco等保留架构上泛化至0.858;5) 表格FmtOK从26.5%提升至85.5%,但跨拓扑VPA迁移有限(~0.06-0.09);6) 复合奖励在VPA、VP、SCR三方面均取得最佳平衡。

Conclusion: 结构保真度随复杂度增长比内容准确率退化更早、更快,该现象在树形与网格两种拓扑、不同模型规模与是否使用推理下均稳定存在,提示LLM依赖语义捷径而非真正的拓扑寻址。SA-RLVR验证了将SCD分解指标作为可验证奖励直接优化结构定位的可行性,实现了跨架构的强泛化(对7B模型而言),但跨拓扑(表格域)迁移仍有限,说明结构寻址是一种应被独立评估和优化的能力。

Abstract: Structured outputs such as JSON and tables are central to modern LLM-based systems, yet generation failures are evaluated monolithically, conflating two distinct error modes: placement errors (correct values at wrong positions) and value errors (wrong values at intended positions). We introduce Structure-Content Decomposition (SCD), a framework that independently measures structural fidelity and content accuracy. Applying SCD to nested JSON and table tasks across six models (7B to frontier), we uncover a consistent phenomenon: structural fidelity degrades earlier and more sharply than content accuracy as complexity increases. At the highest complexity, even DeepSeek-V4-Flash (with reasoning) misplaces 35% of recalled values, while Qwen2.5-7B misplaces 74%. Controlled ablations suggest that this pattern is associated with reliance on semantic shortcuts rather than topological understanding of output structure. Based on these findings, we propose SA-RLVR, converting SCD metrics into verifiable rewards for reinforcement learning via GRPO. SA-RLVR successfully optimizes structural addressing across distinct topologies: it lifts JSON Value Placement Accuracy (VPA) from 26% to 63% while generalizing to held-out schemas; moreover, it consistently drives VPA improvements in the table domain, demonstrating that structure-aware rewards can directly enhance multi-domain structural positioning.

</details>


### [153] [Can your AI agent be cheaper? Investigating the effects of task specifications on token spend in agentic coding tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25399)
*Jakub Smékal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究任务规格（task specification）的变化如何影响智能体编码（agentic coding）工作流中的token消耗。研究基于Kimi K3模型在5个SWE-bench Verified任务、12种规格变体、3个思考等级（low/high/max）、各15次重复，共2,700次运行。主要发现：(1) 任务规格显著影响平均token消耗——将完整规格缩减为极简用户故事使成本上升29.7%、回合数增加16.4%；(2) 任务规格不影响运行间方差，几何标准差恒为×1.34；(3) 提示词敏感性高度依赖具体任务，从13%到115%不等；(4) 输出token占总token仅2.7%却占总花费51.1%，降低回合数比压缩输入更能节省成本；(5) 提出一种低成本预测方法——通过一次0.11美元的探测运行即可将跨规格、跨思考等级的完整成本分布预测到36%误差以内，远优于无探测的161%误差和先前的代理自评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体编码系统的部署日益广泛，长程推理与工具调用使token消耗成为成本与效率的核心考量。已有研究分别考察了"固定提示词换模型"和"固定语义换表层措辞"两种维度的影响，但任务规格的详细程度本身如何影响token消耗、以及这种消耗是否可预测，仍是空白问题。对于实践者而言，回答"通过任务规格和思考等级能在多大程度上控制成本"以及"在未见任务上能在多大程度上预测成本"至关重要。

Method: 1) 任务选择：从SWE-bench Verified中选取5个任务（scikit-learn-14053、astropy-13579、xarray-7393、django-15503、astropy-14365），使用mini-swe-agent脚手架在标准Docker镜像中执行。2) 规格构建：基于GitHub Spec Kit模板构建包含8个章节（header、user story、acceptance scenarios、edge cases、functional requirements、key entities、success criteria、assumptions）的完整规格，并系统性地构造10种变体（完整规格、7种单章节移除、2种大幅缩减），加2个锚点（原始失败测试输出作为下限结构锚点、直接给出解法的oracle作为信息量上限锚点）。3) 实验设计：5任务×12规格×3思考等级×15重复 = 2,700次运行，全部使用Kimi K3（Modal端点，温度1.0）。4) 统计分析：对每个章节移除效果拟合贝叶斯层次模型，估计跨任务的典型效应与任务间异质性，报告后验中位数与90%可信区间，并进行先验敏感性分析。5) 预测方法：留一任务交叉验证，从4个训练任务学习共享的"成本形状"（相对成本结构），对未见任务用k次探测运行（默认完整规格+低思考等级）校准其绝对水平，预测该任务在其他32种配置下的成本。

Result: 1) 提示词移动均值：移除单章节（功能需求、假设、边缘情况、关键实体、成功标准）使成本变化-5.4%至-2.4%，但将规格缩减为极简用户故事使成本上升29.7%、回合数增加16.4%（在全部5个任务上方向一致）。2) 提示词不移动方差：相同规格下的运行间几何标准差为×1.34，所有规格变体均不显著改变此值，绝对方差与平均成本几乎成正比（log-log斜率1.08, r=0.95）。3) 任务间异质性高：同一削减在scikit-learn-14053上仅增13%，但在xarray-7393上增115%；任务间分歧随削减幅度增大而扩大，单章节移除为4%-8%，大幅削减达29%-40%。4) 思考等级与规格细节可替代：从low到max，最贵与最便宜规格的成本比从×2.13降至×1.61，移除acceptance scenarios的回合增幅从20.1%降至2.1%，但绝对美元差距反而扩大。5) 解决率不受规格影响：所有章节移除对解决率的90%可信区间均包含零（±7.5分内），说明成本差异不源于任务难度变化。6) 接受场景（具体Given/When/Then）比成功标准（抽象断言）更重要——前者移除稳定增加回合数，后者无显著效应，具体性而非存在性是关键。7) 原始失败测试输出（无结构）反而最便宜，仅为完整规格的0.73×至1.10×，因其提供了本地化信息。8) 成本结构：输出token占token总数2.7%却占花费51.1%，缓存命中率达96.3%，因此降低回合数（而非压缩输入）才是主要的节省路径。9) 预测：单次0.11美元探测运行将未见任务跨32种配置的预测误差从161%降至36%，相关系数r从0.04-0.12提升至0.67-0.78；10次探测可达25%误差。

Conclusion: 本文证实：(1) 任务规格是控制智能体编码成本的有效杠杆，但杠杆大小高度依赖具体任务，实践者应直接测量而非假设；(2) 运行间方差是模型与服务的固有属性，无法通过提示词工程消除，更可预测的成本只能来自更便宜的成本；(3) 在高缓存命中率的推理栈中，输出token主导花费，节省成本的途径应聚焦于减少回合数而非压缩输入；(4) 单次廉价探测运行足以预测一个未见任务在完整规格×思考等级网格上的成本分布，使系统化的成本评估成为可能。局限在于仅使用单一模型与5个任务，且预测方法尚未建模任务特定的提示词敏感性。

Abstract: Agentic coding workflows are now widely deployed in real-world systems. With long-horizon reasoning and tool use, token usage has become an important consideration for both cost and efficiency. Two engineers using AI will solve the same problem differently. How the specification of a task shapes an agent's token spend, and whether that spend can be predicted in advance, are open questions. Here, we study the effects of different task specifications on agentic token spend with the Kimi K3 model at three thinking efforts. Across $2,700$ runs, we show that reducing a full task specification to a bare user story raises token spend by $29.7\%$, while run-to-run variance remains unaffected by any prompt changes. We show that prompt-sensitivity is task-dependent, running from $13\%$ to $115\%$. We fit a simple predictor that can price a full distribution of task specifications and thinking effort configurations from a single cheap probe on an unseen task within $36\%$, improving over prior work in predicting token spend. Our work provides initial results quantifying the effects of task specification on agentic token spend and introduces a method that can be used to systematically evaluate the cost of AI coding workflows.

</details>


### [154] [Paint What You See: Benchmarking Dexterous Visual Tool Use in Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25417)
*Shudong Liu,Dongyang Chen,Enci Zhang,Jinwei Liang,Zheng Ma,Lewei Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EASEL是一个用于评估多模态智能体"灵巧视觉工具使用"能力的基准测试，以参考图像引导的画布重建为核心代理任务，并辅以语义任务。通过对25个模型的系统评测，揭示了当前多模态智能体在闭环视觉动作中的系统性短板，并验证了轨迹监督数据的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估多聚焦于网页导航、GUI操作和软件工程等容忍度较高的任务，缺乏对"视觉证据与执行精度紧耦合"能力的评测。作者提出"灵巧视觉工具使用"概念——智能体需根据像素级视觉线索直接推理工具参数并通过闭环反馈持续修正，而不能将执行委托给外部分割或生成模型。

Method: 1) 设计EASEL基准：包含110个参考引导重建样本（分为Programmatic、Spatial Geometry、Abstract/Cartoon、Natural Images四类）和5个语义任务（圆轮廓、苹果轮廓、书写"hello"、简单迷宫、困难迷宫），动作空间为13维参数化的二次贝塞尔brush_stroke（含路径、半径/透明度、颜色）；2) 构建EASEL-Data数据集：从11k参考图像经笔触渲染策略生成250步轨迹，采用两阶段课程（C1：早期重建308k样本；C2：全程轻量补全132k样本）共440k样本，并以LoRA微调Qwen3.5-9B得到EASEL-9B；3) 评估指标分结果质量（重建相似度SSIM+RGB L1+Edge IoU的加权和、语义任务Dice/识别准确率/路径进度）与轨迹质量（@50%、AUC、最佳相似度、Final-Best Gap）。

Result: 25个模型评估显示当前多模态智能体存在系统性困难：最佳模型Gemini 3.1 Pro重建相似度仅0.535，远低于数据策略的0.665-0.715；轨迹诊断揭示两种失败模式——早期饱和（前5%步内达到峰值后停滞）与峰值后退化（持续动作但效果下降）；语义任务暴露精确标注与路径规划上的能力边界，困难迷宫所有模型均无法解决；EASEL-9B以相对6.3%提升超过基线，排名第三，且在MMVP、POPE等感知基准上保持甚至小幅提升。

Conclusion: EASEL将灵巧视觉工具使用操作化为精度关键、闭环参数化视觉动作，揭示了多模态智能体在闭环修正方面的根本局限；两阶段课程监督证明轨迹级训练数据可带来可衡量但有限的改善，早期阶段贡献主要增益；该范式可拓展至医学标注、CAD草图、具身操作等需要精细视觉操控的领域，并兼容基于结果优化与强化学习方法。

Abstract: Evaluation is shifting from static QA toward agentic settings where models act through external tools. We identify a critical yet underexplored capability within this space - dexterous visual tool use: fine-grained, closed-loop parameterized visual action in which models infer tool parameters from visual evidence, and those parameters directly govern the final result. Existing benchmarks cover web navigation, GUI operation, and software engineering, but rarely target this coupling between visual evidence and execution precision. We propose EASEL, a benchmark evaluating a controlled instance of dexterous visual tool use that adopts reference-guided visual reconstruction as its primary proxy task: the agent incrementally paints a canvas to match a reference image. EASEL additionally includes semantic tasks spanning region annotation, handwriting, and path planning. We further provide EASEL-Data, a 440k-sample two-stage curriculum dataset for trajectory supervision, and EASEL-9B to investigate its effect on this capability. Evaluation of 25 models reveals that current multimodal agents systematically struggle on EASEL. Reconstruction similarity bottlenecks at low levels (0.40-0.54), while trajectory diagnostics expose severe closed-loop instability - models typically saturate early or degrade post-peak. Semantic tasks reveal sharp capability boundaries in precision annotation and path planning. EASEL-9B, trained on EASEL-Data, surpasses the base model by a relative 6.3%, ranking third among all evaluated models.

</details>


### [155] [Distance Is Not Enough: Forget-Retain Alignment Gap Predicts LLM Relearning Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25429)
*Yi Chen,Hanna Hsieh,Shuhong Liu,Chuanbo Hua,Zihan Ma,Kun Wang,Joo-Young Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的预测指标 FRAG（Forget-Retain Alignment Gap），通过衡量 unlearning 更新与 forget-critical 权重（遗忘关键权重）的对齐程度，并惩罚与 retain-critical 权重（保留关键权重）的对齐，来更可靠地预测大语言模型在重学习攻击下遗忘的鲁棒性。基于同一权重重择性原则，作者还提出 FRP（Forget-Retain Pruning，遗忘-保留剪枝）方法，通过对那些对遗忘至关重要但对保留不关键且权重大小足以产生有意义编辑的权重进行剪枝，使更新集中在这些权重上，从而在保持可用性的同时提高抗重学习能力。实验表明，权重选择性比单纯的权重空间距离更能解释鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器遗忘（machine unlearning）方法在编辑时看似成功遗忘，但经过短暂的微调（重学习攻击）后，被"遗忘"的知识往往能够恢复。先前工作提出以原始模型与遗忘后模型在权重空间中全局 ℓ₂ 距离作为鲁棒性预测指标，但该指标具有误导性：随机或破坏性的大幅度更新也可能产生很大距离却并非真正遗忘。核心问题在于：遗忘鲁棒性应取决于更新作用于哪些权重，而非单纯取决于更新幅度。

Method: 1. 提出 FRAG（Forget-Retain Alignment Gap）：无需训练的标量预测指标。基于少量遗忘与保留校准样本，计算各输入通道在两类数据上的激活范数，构建 forget-critical 重要性张量 ℱ 和 retain-critical 重要性张量 ℛ；将遗忘更新 ΔW 的平方与两类重要性张量分别计算余弦相似度 A_f、A_r，以 FRAG = A_f − γ·A_r（默认 γ=1）衡量更新是否集中于遗忘关键权重且避开保留关键权重。计算仅需校准前向传播与权重比较，无需实际运行重学习攻击。2. 提出 FRP（Forget-Retain Pruning）：基于同一原则的剪枝方法。在每个权重行内，按排名组合 S = rank(ℱ) − β·rank(ℛ) + λ·rank(|W|) 打分，按预设稀疏度 ρ 剪除得分最高的权重，从而使更新天然地集中在 forget-critical 而避开 retain-critical 的权重上。

Result: 1. 在 TOFU（LLaMA-3.2-1B/3B，forget01/05/10）与 WMDP-cyber（Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct、Zephyr-7B-β）以及 MUSE-News（LLaMA-2-7B）上，FRP 在大多数攻击条件下实现最低的 post-attack ES/ΔES（在效用可比的水平上，FRP 的恢复量低 1.9–4.9 倍），勾勒出优于 GA、GradDiff、NPO、RMU、SP 的鲁棒性–效用前沿。2. FRAG 与 ΔES 的 Spearman 相关性在 pooled 健康检查点上达到 −0.78（全局 ℓ₂ 仅为 −0.36）；3B 模型上差距更显著（−0.72 vs −0.17）。即使剔除所有 FRP 检查点，FRAG 仍达 −0.74，ℓ₂ 跌至 −0.10（在 3B 上甚至反号），证明预测能力并非来自自评。3. 噪声扰动控制实验：方向盲的高斯噪声可产生与 FRP 相近的 ℓ₂ 距离且 ΔAcc 更负，但 FRAG 正确将其降至远低于 FRP，证明 retain 惩罚项不可或缺。

Conclusion: 本文从权重选择性视角重新审视重学习鲁棒性，指出"哪些权重移动"比"移动多远"更关键。所提 FRAG 指标无需训练即可可靠预测鲁棒性，并与现有鲁棒性攻击协议呈现出高相关性；所提 FRP 通过把遗忘集中在 forget-critical、避开 retain-critical 的权重来实现更强的抗攻击能力。两者共同表明 forget-retain 对齐为预测与提升 LLM 遗忘鲁棒性提供了更可靠的基础。局限性：实验仅覆盖 TOFU、WMDP-cyber 与 MUSE-News，规模与多语言覆盖有限；FRAG 在密集型 unlearning 方法间分辨率较低；FRP 当前为非结构化剪枝，尚可推广到结构化剪枝、低秩编辑等参数高效干预。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to make a model forget specific data, yet unlearned LLMs often fail to stay unlearned: brief fine-tuning can revive removed knowledge. Existing robustness predictors rely on global weight-space displacement, but distance alone can be misleading when random or destructive updates collapse performance. We argue that relearning robustness depends on update structure: robust unlearning should affect forget-critical weights while sparing retain-critical ones. We introduce the Forget-Retain Alignment Gap (FRAG), a training-free predictor that scores an update's forget-retain alignment without running a relearning attack, and separates selective from dense updates more reliably than global distance. Building on the forget-critical, retain-sparing principle, Forget-Retain Pruning (FRP) improves relearning robustness. Our results suggest that weight selectivity better explains robustness than distance alone. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [156] [Training Alignment Auditors via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25460)
*Paul Rosu,Rowan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出通过强化学习训练LLM审计智能体以提升前沿模型对齐审计能力。核心方法是设计成对比较奖励（pairwise reward），让审计智能体在Petri框架下探测植入隐藏行为的目标模型，同时引入无隐藏行为的校准样本以维持低误报率。最终在Haiku 4.5上训练后，其综合审计能力匹配Opus 4.6，并能在跨脚手架（AuditBench对抗性硬化目标）上泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前前沿模型的预部署评估越来越多依赖自动化LLM审计器，但现有审计系统在连贯调查和审计真实性方面存在不足，且在对抗性硬化目标面前表现不佳。需要通过强化学习提升审计器能力，使其能发现未知类型的隐藏行为。

Method: 1) 评估框架：构建32种植入隐藏行为的系统提示目标（涵盖欺骗、谄媚、安全颠覆、认知操纵四大类），设计四个评估维度（审计质量、误报校准、生产模型问题发现、真实性）。2) 训练方法：在Petri脚手架中用强化学习训练Haiku 4.5审计器，关键设计包括：(a) 用LLM裁判（知晓隐藏行为）进行成对比较奖励而非绝对评分；(b) 引入50%无隐藏行为的校准样本防止误报坍塌；(c) 训练环境逐步迭代：从生产模型训练→二元奖励→成对奖励（组内/参考对比），每步针对前一步失败原因改进。3) 采用对比聚类分析训练后的审计策略。

Result: 最佳配置（参考成对奖励+50%校准）训练三周期后：综合得分48.7匹配Opus 4.6的48.4（未训练Haiku 4.5为44.2），审计质量72.7超越Opus 4.6的68.0，误报率<1%。在AuditBench对抗性硬化目标（KTO微调的Llama 3.3 70B，使用不同脚手架Claude Code）上的迁移评估中，STC检测率从基线11.5%提升至28.1%。策略分析显示最佳配置收敛于"受控系统性实验"的科学审计风格，而非对抗性或操纵性策略。失败的二元奖励模型则退化为对抗性、操纵性审计模式。

Conclusion: 奖励设计是提升审计器能力的关键因素。绝对评分奖励导致奖励黑客行为（模型学会表面对抗而非真实调查），而成对比较奖励配合校准样本训练能引导模型学习通用调查技能。多目标训练在该规模下未显示显著优势。成对比较奖励的成功表明：对于需要泛化到未知行为类型的对齐审计任务，偏好式学习优于基于规则评分的方法。训练出的审计能力可跨目标类型和脚手架迁移，证明学到的是真实调查能力而非训练环境伪影。

Abstract: Alignment auditing of frontier models increasingly relies on LLM auditors to surface undesirable behaviors at scale, but current automated auditors can struggle with coherent investigation and audit realism. In this work, we improve LLM auditors with reinforcement learning. In our best training environment, the policy investigates target models that potentially possess hidden behaviors planted via their system prompt. An LLM judge, which knows whether the target has a hidden behavior, holistically compares the policy's investigation to a reference investigation to determine the reward. With systematic ablations, we find that pairwise rewards yield more robust training compared to pointwise rewards, and that adding targets without planted behaviors helps maintain a low false positive rate. Training improves investigation quality against targets with planted behaviors, the rate of concerning behaviors surfaced in unmodified production models, and audit realism, while false-positive rates stay below 1%. Furthermore, auditing capabilities generalize across scaffolds: performance on AuditBench's adversarially fine-tuned targets substantially improves [Sheshadri et al., 2026].

</details>


### [157] [PonsRAG: A Pons-Inspired RAG Bridging Cognitive Islands for Coordinated Long Narrative Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25486)
*Rongchen Zhao,Yu Chen,Juyuan Wang,Zhouting Mo,Jianxing Yu,Wenqing Chen,Jingping Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 PonsRAG，一个受生物脑桥（pons）启发的新型检索增强生成（RAG）框架，用于解决长叙事推理（LNR）中存在的"认知孤岛"和"跨层证据断裂"两大难题。该框架通过构建包含角色层、情节层和桥接层的三层索引架构，并配合协调推理流水线，在四个长叙事基准测试上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 RAG 方法在长叙事推理任务中面临两大核心挑战：（1）认知孤岛（Cognitive Islanding）——不同知识层中语义相关的证据彼此孤立，无法建立跨层连接；（2）跨层证据断裂（Cross-layer Evidence Disconnection）——各层独立检索导致上下文碎片化、冗余化，难以形成连贯的推理链。现有单层索引（如 HippoRAGv2、RAPTOR）只能提供证据的部分视图，而多层索引方法（如 ComoRAG、HiRAG）虽提供多视角，但缺乏跨层协调机制。

Method: PonsRAG 模仿生物脑桥在神经系统中连接和协调不同脑区的功能，包含两大核心组件：
1. **三层索引构建（离线阶段）**：
   - **角色层（Char Layer）**：基于以角色为中心的模式提取实体及其描述，并构建角色知识图谱。
   - **情节层（Plot Layer）**：基于事件为中心的视角提取叙事事件，采用 Zettelkasten 启发的"记忆卡"结构封装多粒度表示（事件、类型、全局聚类摘要、原始 chunk、时序位置）。
   - **桥接层（Pons Layer）**：作为连接角色节点与情节节点的加权二部图，边的权重综合了语义相似度（sim(d_u, r_v)）和逆频率平衡项（受 IDF 启发），并通过稀疏控制器 τ 过滤弱关联。
2. **协调推理流水线（在线阶段）**，包含四个步骤：
   - **Query Anchor（查询锚定）**：通过 PPR 在角色图谱上选择 top-k₁ 角色节点；通过多粒度评分（MGS，综合事件、摘要、chunk 三个粒度的相似度）选择 top-k₂ 情节节点。
   - **Pons Awaken（跨层唤醒）**：将锚定节点作为种子信号，使用 Co-HITS 算法在 Pons 边上进行双向强化传播，发现潜在的跨层节点。
   - **Pons Match（最优跨层配对）**：将候选子图构建为最大权重二部图匹配问题，使用匈牙利算法求解 1-to-1 最优匹配，过滤多对多噪声。
   - **Flow Filter（流过滤）**：按时间戳重排匹配对，并使用 LLM 过滤模块按查询语义意图进行剪枝，输出最终叙事序列输入生成器。

Result: 在 NarrativeQA、EN.QA、EN.MC（来自 ∞Bench）和 NoCha 四个长叙事基准上（使用 GPT-4o-mini 作为骨干 LLM，上下文长度 6K tokens）的实验结果表明：
- **单步 QA**：PonsRAG 在所有基准上均优于最强基线，MC 任务平均准确率相对提升 11.56%，EN.MC 上准确率达 77.73%（超过次优基线 10%+）。
- **多步 QA**：与 IRCoT 结合后，NoCha 准确率提升从 5.82% 扩大到 13.75%。
- **消融实验**：三层索引缺一不可，移除 Pons 层导致 EN.MC 准确率下降约 20%；Pons Match 是最关键的推理步骤（移除后 EN.MC 准确率下降 10%+）。
- **跨层语义距离分析**：文档越长，跨层语义距离 D_cross 越大（从 0.231 增至 0.377），PonsRAG 相对基线的优势也随之扩大（如 ACC 提升达 15%）。
- **稀疏控制器 τ 分析**：最优区间为 0.50–0.75。
- **匹配约束分析**：1-to-1 严格匹配优于 1-to-N 宽松匹配，后者会引入"迷失在中间"效应。
- **查询类型分析**：PonsRAG 在需要联合推理的 Mix 类型查询上优势最显著（领先 ComoRAG 约 8%）。
- **效率对比**：相比 HiRAG，PonsRAG 以 1/80 的 token 成本和 1/8 的时间成本取得约 1.6 倍的性能。

Conclusion: PonsRAG 通过受生物脑桥启发的三层索引和协调推理机制，有效解决了长叙事推理中的认知孤岛问题，将孤立的多层检索转变为基于桥接约束的协同跨层证据选择。该方法在四个长叙事基准上均达到最优，且在更长文档和更复杂的混合推理查询上优势更为明显，验证了结构化跨层检索在长上下文场景中的有效性。局限性在于目前仅在 LNR 基准上评估，未来可扩展到多跳 QA 和更广泛的长上下文任务。

Abstract: Long Narrative Reasoning is an essential capability for processing and reasoning over complex narratives. While retrieval-augmented generation provides a promising framework, existing methods still face two critical challenges: cognitive islanding and cross-layer evidence disconnection. To address these issues, we propose PonsRAG, a coordinated RAG framework inspired by the biological pons. PonsRAG consists of two key components: Triple-Layer Indexing, which organizes documents into a connected knowledge structure to bridge cognitive islands, and Coordinated Reasoning, which retrieves evidence across distinct layers and integrates cross-layer information into a unified context. We evaluate PonsRAG on four long-context narrative benchmarks, and experimental results show that it outperforms the strongest baseline, achieving a 11.56% relative improvement in average accuracy on multi-choice tasks.

</details>


### [158] [CaSKG: Counterfactual-Causal Skill Graphs for Scalable Agent Skill Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25500)
*Zhiyuan Li,Linyuan Gao,Xuechun Ding,Hongwei Chen,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CaSKG提出了一种离线构建的反事实-因果技能图框架，通过多信号候选图构建、方向条件化的反事实探测（移除/替换/重排序）、Beta平滑聚合及状态门控发布，为LLM智能体提供校准过的紧凑技能检索上下文，在ALFWorld和ScienceWorld上以六个LLM骨干全面超越GoS等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体可复用技能库不断扩大，全库提示成本过高、向量检索忽略技能间流程依赖、现有图检索依赖未经可靠性评估的弱边——亟需解决"边置信度校准"这一核心瓶颈，使检索传播受可信边控制。

Method: 四阶段流程：(1)多信号候选图诱导（语义/词法/输入输出/结构+修复证据+LLM评分器）；(2)从候选集中选择预算前沿F，对每条边施加三个方向条件化反事实探测（rem/sub/ord）；(3)用Beta(1,1)累积器聚合探针证据为可靠性分数c_ij，按对称阈值τ_c分配状态（confirmed/uncertain/rejected/unvalidated）并用衰减系数ρ_unc/ρ_scaf门控发布权重；(4)冻结发布图后，推理时用个性化PageRank从任务种子做查询条件扩散。

Result: 在ALFWorld ID-140和ScienceWorld U211上用MiniMax-M2.7、GLM-5.2、Kimi-K2.6、Qwen3.5-397B-A17B、DeepSeek-V4-Flash、GPT-5.6-Luna六个模型，CaSKG在12个模型-基准组合上均取得最高任务分数；ScienceWorld六模型宏平均分由GoS的72.62升至80.50，ALFWorld成功率从80.01%升至86.79%，且环境交互步数均更少。消融显示多信号候选（提升6.43pp）、反事实门控发布（相对全发布+2.14pp）、可扩展至2000技能库。

Conclusion: 边置信度校准是可扩展智能体记忆的有效设计原则：宽候选+选择性发布可在保留先决条件、状态变更、验证、完成步骤等流程结构的同时，压缩检索上下文并降低交互成本。

Abstract: Reusable skill libraries allow large language model (LLM) agents to reuse procedural knowledge across tasks, but they also turn memory access into a challenging retrieval problem. Full-library prompting preserves coverage at high context cost, vector retrieval returns compact neighborhoods but treats skills as independent text, and graph-based retrieval can recover workflow context only when the edges that carry relevance are reliable. We propose CaSKG, a counterfactual-causal skill graph framework that calibrates procedural relations before retrieval. CaSKG first builds a high-recall directed candidate graph from semantic, lexical, input/output, and structural evidence, with repair evidence and an optional LLM judge further refining candidate scores. It then applies direction-conditioned textual counterfactual probes that remove, substitute, and reorder skill pairs, aggregates the evidence with Bayesian smoothing, and publishes a state-filtered weighted graph for task-conditioned expansion. The graph is constructed offline and used without changing the downstream agent policy or task interface. Across six LLM backbones on ALFWorld ID-140 and ScienceWorld U211, CaSKG achieves the highest task score in all twelve combinations of model and benchmark. Relative to Graph-of-Skills (GoS), it improves the six-model macro-average ScienceWorld score from 72.62 to 80.50 and ALFWorld success from 80.01\% to 86.79\%, while reducing mean environment steps on both benchmarks. Qualitative and ablation analyses further show that calibrated edges help retrieval preserve prerequisites, state-changing actions, verification routines, and final completion steps. These results position edge-confidence calibration as an effective route to compact and executable skill retrieval at scale\footnote{Code is available at: this https URL }.

</details>


### [159] [Agentic Game Development as a Verifiable Trajectory Data Engine for Scaling World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25518)
*Pengfei Zhou,Hexin Wang,Zhengfeiyang Zhang,Yixing Ma,Zhenglin Wan,Kaipeng Zhang,Wangbo Zhao,Yang You*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将游戏开发作为世界模型（world model）可扩展训练的数据引擎。核心思路是：当前空间智能（spatial intelligence）的瓶颈不在数据量或算力，而在于缺乏可验证的奖励信号（verifiable reward）。作者提出RLHEV（人类-引擎联合验证的强化学习）训练范式，结合游戏引擎的自动结构化检查与开发者的人类接受反馈，并构建了AWoMo（Agentic World Model）智能体系统，通过Unified World-Development Protocol (UWDP)记录完整的开发轨迹（意图-编辑-检查-渲染-人工决策-修复）作为训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前空间世界模型（如视频生成、3D生成、物理仿真）主要依赖CLIP分数、FVD、MLLM-as-judge等模糊代理奖励进行后训练，这些信号噪声大、易被博弈，无法支持类似代码智能体那样的强化学习后训练循环。作者认为空间智能正面临"Bitter Lesson"的数据中心版本——单纯堆视频数据和算力无法建立可扩展的反馈通道。

Method: 1. RLHEV（Reinforcement Learning with Human-Engine Verification）：融合引擎密集结构化奖励（如碰撞、物理、导航、可玩性检测）与人类稀疏但权威的接受信号。
2. AWoMo（Agentic World Model）：基于意图-动作-验证-审查四接口的智能体世界模型，循环执行propose-render-verify-repair-review。
3. UWDP（Unified World-Development Protocol）：统一的轨迹记录协议，将设计意图、场景程序、引擎检查、渲染证据、人工决策、修复链接打包为可训练的多模态数据。
4. 模型核心UnifiedGameAssetModel（2.89B参数，基于Cosmos 3用MoT架构），先在AWoMo数据上继续预训练，再用RLHEV后训练。
5. 奖励形式化：以人类效用减去引擎检查的惩罚函数，受引擎硬门控约束。

Result: 1. UnitySceneBench（200样本Unity资产编辑分类）：Full RLHEV在最佳8种子下主分0.681，比最强非全基线高+0.098；理解与生成两端在720训练预算下均最优（生成质量0.8197 vs RLVR的0.7934）。
2. 域内OOD泛化：源预训练+目标适配使Unity分布转移MLLM-judge分从0.25提升至0.75。
3. 跨引擎迁移：Unity→Unreal从0.25升至0.35；Unity→Godot从0.15升至0.35。
4. 体现式诊断：AWoMo增强训练在R2R成功率+0.79%、Gym MuJoCo rollout return +9.96%、D4RL MuJoCo归一化分数+48.43%。
5. 协议轨迹vs.快照：在极小目标标签预算下，使用UWDP trace的Spearman相关系数(0.719)远高于仅用snapshot特征(0.159)。

Conclusion: 游戏开发提供了一种实用的递归数据引擎——引擎作为可执行规范的解析器与自动验证器，开发者提供最终接受判定，二者共同形成可扩展的反馈通道。通过RLHEV与AWoMo，世界模型可以在"提议-渲染-检查-修复-审查"的循环中持续自我改进，将空间理解与生成统一于场景程序表示。该路径已在UnitySceneBench、跨引擎迁移与体现式任务中给出正向但有限的诊断性证据，下一步需扩展至可玩关卡、playtest智能体与人类研究。

Abstract: A common strategy for scaling world models is to train on more crawled video with more compute. We argue that this strategy is inefficient: scaling world models also requires a recursive data engine that offers grounded reward signals. The success of code agents illustrates why this matters. As code is executable, compilers and runtimes can provide high-quality rewards for Reinforcement Learning (RL) post-training of LLMs. By contrast, spatial generation still relies largely on fuzzy proxies such as CLIP scores. These signals are fuzzy and biased, making them hard to support RL post-training. Compared with these, game development provides a missing reward environment for spatial world models. A scene encoded by a game engine is an executable world specification: the engine can efficiently check collision, physics, navigability and bounded playability, while the developer provides the global verification signal by judging whether the scene should be accepted. Game development also provides real-world long-horizon trajectory data for RL post-training. We therefore propose Reinforcement Learning with Human-Engine Verification (RLHEV), a post-training paradigm that combines dense engine signals with implicit human acceptance feedback from the development process.

</details>


### [160] [Using profiles of cognitive capability to assess AI suitability for workplace tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25623)
*Jonathan Prunty,Marko Tešić,Patrick Quinn,José Hernández-Orallo,Lucy Cheke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于认知能力画像的AI工作场景适配评估框架，通过将AI系统的能力画像与工作任务的认知需求画像在共享的认知能力空间中进行映射，来系统化地评估AI在不同领域、组织、角色甚至单个工作任务上的适配性。该框架包括认知能力画像（基于带需求标注的基准测试推断AI的能力水平）和任务需求加权（通过领域专家评估任务对各项能力的相对重要性）两个核心组件，并在合成代理、六款AI系统和六个职业领域410名员工的真实数据上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织在部署AI系统时面临范围界定（scoping）难题：哪些任务适合自动化、哪些应保留给人类、哪些适合人机协作。传统的聚合基准测试分数无法有效预测AI在实际工作场景中的表现，而人类对模型能力的判断又容易过时。同时，AI系统具有"锯齿状"（jagged）能力剖面，即在某些维度上表现卓越而在另一些维度上意外薄弱，这使得部署后的失败模式难以预测。已有研究表明，AI可靠性是企业部署AI的首要障碍。作者旨在将AI部署决策从直觉判断转变为基于认知能力的系统性证据评估。

Method: 该框架由三阶段组成：（1）认知能力画像：基于认知科学文献定义18项核心认知能力（记忆系统、执行控制、客体与空间理解、社会与交流能力），通过LLM评判员按照评分量规对基准测试题目进行认知需求标注（0-5级），最终聚类为8个能力维度（如语义记忆、语言、社会认知等）；利用19,535道题目的标注电池，采用Measurement Layouts贝叶斯项目反应模型推断各AI系统的能力剖面，选用软最小池化（soft-min pooling, τ=1）和共享截距以保证可识别性。（2）任务需求加权：通过问卷调查（410名员工，6个职业领域）和专家访谈，收集领域专家对每个工作任务所涉及的核心认知能力的相对重要性评估（每任务分配100分给最重要的5项能力）。（3）适配映射：采用加权幂平均（p=0，即几何平均）将能力水平与任务需求重要性权重结合，产生每个AI系统对每个任务的适配评分。方法包含合成代理恢复性验证（20个代理，跨τ值的恢复率分析）、跨样本验证（公司员工vs在线样本）和敏感度分析（p和s参数扫描）。

Result: 恢复性分析：在合成数据上，τ=1时所有维度的能力恢复率均在0.76-0.92之间，共享截距使水平恢复率从r=0.12提升到r=0.98，下游适配性恢复率达到ρ=0.91（接近oracle上限）。AI能力剖面：六个AI系统（3款Google、3款OpenAI）的剖面形状高度相似——均在语义记忆、语言和社会认知上最强，在动作规划与模拟、工具性推理和客体永久性上最弱；Gemini 3系列在信息整合与控制、动作规划方面具有显著优势。任务重要性：六个职业领域的工作活动共享一个核心认知基础（计划、语义记忆、工作记忆、语言和程序记忆最为重要），领域差异主要体现在次级能力上（如仓储物流更强调空间推理，制造业更强调计划，酒店销售更强调心智理论与情绪感知）。适配性排序：Gemini 3.1 Pro在几乎所有任务中排名第一，Gemini 3 Flash紧随其后，其余四款系统形成一个较低的重叠组；排序在合理参数范围内高度稳健。整体而言，研究、Admin和数据分析构成最高部署优先级。

Conclusion: 该论文提出了一种可系统性评估AI适配性的方法论框架，其核心贡献在于：（1）将现有AI基准测试按认知能力而非任务领域重新组织，使评估从聚合分数转向能力层面的可解释剖面；（2）将任务需求评估从对AI能力的预测转向对工作本身的认知需求刻画，二者独立更新；（3）框架可灵活应用于不同组织、角色甚至个体职责粒度。主要发现表明，当前AI系统在语言、知识和社交认知方面高度趋同，而在规划和认知控制方面差异最大；不同职业领域的工作活动共享稳定的认知核心。论文也坦率指出了局限：当前测量的是任务重要性而非任务需求（缺乏校准的概率预测），电池主要基于文本基准而对多模态和具身能力覆盖不足，以及未来工作方向——将该认知能力框架扩展到对人类工人的画像，从而实现异构劳动力中的人机任务分配。

Abstract: Organisations deploying AI face a scoping problem: which tasks can be automated, which should remain with humans, and which are best shared between the two. Aggregate benchmark scores provide little insight into where systems will succeed or fail in practice, while human judgements of model capabilities quickly become outdated. We introduce a pipeline that profiles agents and tasks using a shared set of core cognitive capabilities. Cognitive capability profiling infers an agent's capabilities from performance on a benchmark battery annotated for the cognitive demands of each item. Task requirements weighting elicits from domain experts the relative importance of these same capabilities for their work. As both use a common set of cognitive dimensions, they can be updated independently as models and roles change, and combined to estimate AI suitability at the level of a domain, organisation, role, or individual duty. We validate capability recovery on synthetic agents, profile six AI systems, and elicit task requirements from 410 employees across six occupational domains. AI systems differed more across cognitive dimensions than across model families, while workplace activities converged on a shared cognitive core. The resulting scores provide a comparative scoping tool for identifying promising candidates for piloting and areas where current systems are unlikely to be well suited. We discuss extending the framework to profile human workers alongside AI systems, moving from AI suitability towards human-machine task allocation.

</details>


### [161] [Narcissus: Program Synthesis Using Context-Aware LLM Approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25657)
*Tilman Hinnerichs,Sebastijan Dumancic,Neil Yorke-Smith*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Narcissus 提出了一种上下文感知的 LLM 启发式方法，用于枚举式程序合成。它通过保留 LLM 提议作为抽象语法树（AST），并利用前缀对齐、子程序复用和正则化三个信号对语法规则的扩展进行打分。在五个领域和两种搜索后端上的实验表明，Narcissus 在所有预算下均优于静态引导，并且无需在搜索过程中调用 LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当目标任务语言在训练数据中较为罕见时，LLM 生成的程序常常违反语法或无法满足规格说明。静态启发式方法（如 HySynth）将 LLM 提议压缩为全局规则频率，这会丢失规则的上下文信息，并错误地剪枝掉所有未被提议覆盖的规则，恰好在提议错误时限制了搜索空间。Narcissus 旨在解决这一局限性，通过引入上下文感知能力，在提议具有误导性时仍能保持搜索的完整性。

Method: Narcissus 的方法包含五个步骤：(1) 从 LLM 采样提议；(2) 将提议解析为 AST 并修复语法错误；(3) 从 AST 中挖掘重复片段作为宏规则，扩展语法；(4) 基于修复后的树构建上下文感知启发式，结合三个信号——前缀对齐（proposals 在相同上下文下使用相同规则的比例）、子程序复用（proposals 重复的片段）和正则化（基于提议大小的偏好加上保持每个规则可达的下限）；(5) 使用基于成本的波束搜索或遗传搜索运行启发式引导的合成。

Result: 在 SLIA、BV、DeepCoder、ARC 和 ARGA 五个领域以及成本引导波束搜索和遗传搜索两种后端上的评估显示，Narcissus 在所有提议支持水平下均优于静态启发式和重提示基线。它在 SLIA 上的求解速度比静态启发式快约一个数量级，并在 ARC 上解决了 40% 的任务，而原始提议仅解决 13%，且整个搜索过程无需调用 LLM。消融实验表明，三个信号在不同提议质量下均起关键作用。

Conclusion: Narcissus 通过引入上下文感知能力，显著提升了 LLM 引导的程序合成效果。它不仅在高提议支持时表现优异，在弱提议支持时也能优雅退化至无引导枚举，避免了静态启发式的剪枝问题。未来的工作包括根据提议特性自适应调整信号权重、跨任务共享挖掘的片段以构建可增长的可复用库，以及扩展至其他 LLM 难以可靠生成语法正确程序的领域。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) excel at programming, but not when the task fixes the target language: prompted with a grammar rare in their training data, their programs usually break the grammar or fail the given specification. Enumerative synthesizers search the space of syntactically correct programs systematically guided by LLMs; the state of the art guides them by approximating LLM proposals into rule frequencies, which loses where each construct belongs and prunes every rule the proposals miss, exactly when the proposals are wrong. We present Narcissus, a synthesizer that keeps the proposals as syntax trees and scores each expansion of a candidate program in its context: does a proposal with the same surrounding structure continue the same way, and does the expansion rebuild a fragment the proposals repeat? A regularization term keeps every rule reachable, so wrong proposals delay the solution but cannot hide it. Across five domains and two search backends, Narcissus beats static guidance at every budget and consistently outperforms re-prompting the LLM to fix its own proposals; it reaches proposal-like programs an order of magnitude sooner and solves $40\%$ of ARC tasks where the raw proposals solve $13\%$, all without a single LLM call during search.

</details>


### [162] [ToST: A Tree-of-Thought Socratic Teaching Framework for Multi-Path Guidance and Parallel Thinking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25775)
*Feng Ling,Heng Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ToST框架,将思维树(ToT)引入苏格拉底式教学,采用"一题多解"(1PMS)范式,通过并行播种与多路径自适应指导实现非线性教学引导,并构建MPSG-Bench基准(31K对话)进行系统评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM苏格拉底式教学普遍采用"一题一解"(1P1S)的线性推理范式,导致教学灵活性差、错误恢复弱,无法有效培养学生从多角度并行思考的能力(parallel thinking)。同时缺乏针对非线性教学能力的系统性评估基准。

Method: 核心方法包括三部分:(1)并行推理树(PRT)结构:将同一问题的多种解法表示为带属性(复杂度、创新性、深度)的树形结构,支持多路径建模;(2)并行播种(Parallel Sowing):通过启发式提问引导学生从多视角探索问题,初始化学生推理树;(3)多路径自适应指导(MPAG):包含Tree-based Student Cognitive Manager(基于PathMatch/NodeMatch进行进度估计与错误定位)和Automatic Path Analyzer(基于Product-of-Experts公式综合路径效用、对话增益、认知负荷进行路径选择,并通过带惯性阈值的贪心切换规则决定是否切换路径)。教师模型基于Qwen2.5-Math-7B-Instruct用LoRA在MPSG-Bench上微调。

Result: 在GSM8K、MATH-500、AIME24、AIME25四个基准上,ToST指导成功率平均提升11%,TreeAcc-R较SocraticLM在GSM8K上提升达20%,在挑战性AIME25上较最强通用LLM基线(GPT-5)提升约8%。消融实验证明Parallel Sowing和MPAG均不可或缺:移除PS导致探索策略数减少(如MATH-500从2.12降至1.53),移除MPAG则降低解题质量;PS单独使用仅提升原始学生3.71%。人工评估(7名专家+10名本科生)显示ToST在帮助性、清晰度、继续探索意愿等维度获得最高评分,SOLO阶段跃迁分析显示73.3%的S3→S4向上跃迁。

Conclusion: ToST通过显式建模多路径推理树与SOLO理论驱动的评估框架,有效突破了传统1P1S苏格拉底教学的线性局限,显著提升了并行思维指导能力与错误恢复鲁棒性。局限性:实验局限于数学领域;依赖预先构建的高质量PRT;未系统考察AI指导过度依赖的伦理风险。未来工作将扩展至更多领域并增强伦理保障。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) exhibit strong problem-solving abilities, positioning them as promising agents for Socratic teaching to guide students through step-by-step heuristic questioning. However, existing approaches typically adopt a one-problem-one-solution paradigm, restricting the teaching guidance to a single linear reasoning path. This design limits instructional flexibility, weakens error recovery, and restricts students' ability to engage in parallel thinking to explore multiple valid solutions. To overcome these, we propose ToST, a Tree-of-Thought Socratic Teaching framework that explicitly supports multi-path guidance under a one-problem-multiple-solutions paradigm. ToST employs Parallel Sowing, a parallel-thinking-oriented questioning strategy to encourage students to approach problems from diverse perspectives, and a Multi-Path Adaptive Guidance mechanism to provide more robust and non-linear instructions across alternative solution trajectories. Concurrently, to fill the void in systematically evaluating such non-linear instructional capabilities, we advance the task of multi-path Socratic guidance by establishing MPSG-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark that includes a dataset of 31K multi-path teaching dialogues and a five-dimensional evaluation framework grounded in the SOLO (Structure of Observed Learning Outcomes) theory to assess parallel-thinking guidance. Experimental results demonstrate that ToST significantly enhances guidance success rates while empowering students to navigate and explore multiple solution paths more effectively under both automatic and human metrics.

</details>


### [163] [LocalLSTC: A Long Short-Term Control Architecture for Locally Deployed GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25777)
*Weiming Li,Helen Paik,Yulei Sui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 LocalLSTC，一种训练免费的架构，通过按时间范围（长期控制 vs 短期执行）组织控制信息，解决本地部署 GUI Agent 在较弱推理后端下的跨步控制失败问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 SOTA GUI Agent 框架依赖前端 API 模型（如 GPT-5），将活动任务阶段、累积证据和运行时反馈隐式嵌入不断增长的交互轨迹中，每步都需要重新构建这些控制信息。当替换为本地弱推理后端（如 Qwen3.5-9B）时，OSWorld SR-100 平均从 60.9% 骤降至 37.7%，且 91.6% 的失败轨迹至少包含一种控制失败（阶段、评估、循环、恢复、终止错误）。作者将此现象定义为"隐式轨迹级控制负担"，并主张显式组织跨步控制信息是本地部署 GUI Agent 的独立架构维度。

Method: LocalLSTC 将控制按时间范围分离：（1）长期控制状态 C_t = (g_t, E_t, f_t)，跨交互持续保存活动子目标、子目标对齐证据、运行时反馈；（2）短期执行承诺 K_t = (ĝ_t, A_t)，代表当前步骤的有界承诺（有序动作列表）。（3）L2S（Long-to-Short Planning）：基于持久状态和当前观察形成执行承诺，支持单次推理内最多 3 个候选提案的比较。（4）S2L（Short-to-Long Control）：通过 Step Abstraction 将异构执行轨迹（GUI、Bash、软件 API）转化为子目标对齐证据，并依据路由状态（continue/advance/error/stall/finish）更新持久控制。系统采用 JSON 模式输出，包含 thought、subgoal、actions 三字段，支持 pyautogui GUI 操作、Bash/Python 执行、软件 API 调用及 WAIT/TERMINATE/INFEASIBLE 保留动作。执行前进行验证与规范化，运行指纹检查抑制重复动作，context refinement 在子目标推进时压缩已完成证据，Final Verification 对 TERMINATE 请求进行最终验证，失败则返回执行状态。

Result: 主要结果：（1）骨干敏感性：4 个 SOTA 框架（Agent S3、GTA1、CoAct-1、HiSA）将 GPT-5 替换为 Qwen3.5-9B 后，OSWorld SR-100 平均从 60.9% 降至 37.7%，所有失败轨迹中 91.6% 至少含一种控制失败。（2）基准性能：使用 Qwen3.6-27B + GTA1-7B，OSWorld SR-100 达 64.7%（最强本地结果，提升 10.8 点），WindowsAgentArena SR-100 达 65.3%（最强本地结果，SR-50 提升 18.1 点，逼近最强 API 结果 0.1 点）。（3）控制失败减少：OSWorld 上 Any 控制失败发生率从 4 个基线平均 59.4% 降至 43.1%，None 发生率达 42.3%。（4）消融实验（Qwen3.5-9B 骨干）：移除 L2S 使 SR-100 从 49.1% 降至 36.4%（-12.7 点），移除 S2L 降至 41.5%（-7.6 点）；Step Abstraction 贡献最大（-17.5 点），其次为持久子目标（-11.1 点）。（5）运行时事件分析显示，停滞/重复事件后 Full 模型的评估分数为 34.3%，显著优于 w/o L2S（18.8%）和 w/o S2L（11.8%），验证了恢复行为质量提升。（6）GPT-5.6-sol 标注与人类标注者一致性 F1 达 86.1%/85.2%，Cohen's κ 为 0.686/0.647。

Conclusion: LocalLSTC 通过将持续控制状态与步骤级执行分离，按时间范围组织控制信息，有效缓解本地部署 GUI Agent 的隐式轨迹级控制负担。在 Qwen3.6-27B 骨干下达到 OSWorld 与 WindowsAgentArena 两项基准上的最强本地结果，跨 Ubuntu 与 Windows 环境均有效，验证了时间范围控制组织作为独立架构维度的价值。消融实验确认 L2S 与 S2L 两侧机制均有显著贡献，其中 Step Abstraction 与持久子目标贡献最大。该研究确立了"时间组织控制信息"作为本地部署 GUI Agent 的一个独立架构维度。

Abstract: Modern GUI-agent frameworks achieve strong desktop task performance with frontier API models, yet persistent control information often remains implicit in growing interaction trajectories. At each step, the planner reconstructs the active task stage, accumulated evidence, and runtime feedback before deciding the next action. This dependence becomes more pronounced under weaker local reasoning backbones. Across four representative state-of-the-art frameworks, replacing GPT-5 with Qwen3.5-9B reduces average OSWorld SR-100 from 60.9\% to 37.7\%. Trajectory annotation further identifies at least one control failure in 91.6\% of failed trajectories. To address this problem, we introduce LocalLSTC, a training-free architecture that organizes control by temporal scope, maintaining persistent cross-step state to guide short-term execution commitments. Long-Term Control maintains the active subgoal, subgoal-aligned evidence, and runtime feedback across interactions, while Short-Term Execution realizes bounded commitments for the current step. Long-to-Short Planning forms each commitment from persistent state, and Short-to-Long Control integrates execution outcomes back into that state for progress assessment, recovery, and termination. With Qwen3.6-27B, LocalLSTC reaches 64.7\% SR-100 on OSWorld and 65.3\% on WindowsAgentArena, outperforming the strongest prior local results on both benchmarks. Ablations further support contributions from mechanisms on both sides of execution. These findings identify temporal organization of control information as a distinct architectural dimension for locally deployed GUI agents.

</details>


### [164] [Choose Your Game Wisely: Measuring Game-Theoretic Structures in Real-World Vehicle Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25917)
*Yueyuan Li,Rongcheng Nie,Weijie Xi,Mingyang Jiang,Songan Zhang,Hanyang Zhuang,Ming Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于轨迹的交互测量框架，用于从真实车辆轨迹中量化同时性、顺序性和非对称性交互结构，并在六个公开数据集上验证了实际驾驶交互中三类博弈论结构（同时移动、顺序移动、领导者-跟随者）共存的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的博弈论车辆交互模型（Nash同时移动、Stackelberg领导者-跟随者）隐含了不同的时间结构假设，但这些假设在真实驾驶行为中是否成立、能否从轨迹中直接测量，尚未得到系统性验证。同时，仅依靠时空接近性或时间先后无法判定是否存在可测量的行为响应。

Method: 1) 基于地图约束参考轨迹定义速度残差，检测行为变化起始时刻；2) 利用400 ms容差将事件分类为同时(concurrent)、顺序(sequential)或单侧(one-sided)；3) 基于源-目标方向对，检查目标车辆是否在源行为变化后产生可测响应(响应/预主动/无响应)，并计算响应时滞；4) 依据顺序反转次数判断排序的稳定性(stable vs alternating)；5) 使用场景级聚类自助法估计置信区间，并拟合含场景随机截距的GLMM比较交互类型差异。

Result: 在INTERACTION、highD、inD、rounD、Waymo、nuPlan六个数据集上提取共约11,879个交互事件：1) 同时与顺序行为变化在跟随、汇入、冲突三类交互中均占可观比例（并发33.8%–58.7%，顺序33.7%–54.6%）；2) 顺序交互中稳定排序占80.5%–100%，显著多于交替排序，表明持续非对称角色是常见结构；3) 仅约41.4%的可评估定向对产生可测响应，约64%的响应发生在400 ms容差内，说明时间先后未必伴随可测行为依赖。

Conclusion: 真实驾驶交互同时呈现同时、顺序和持续排序的时间结构，三种博弈论时序形式应被视为针对不同交互场景的互补抽象，而非普适模型。同一空间几何在不同数据集中可能展现不同的时序组织，单凭几何关系不足以决定合适的交互结构；时间先后与行为依赖应分开考量。

Abstract: Game-theoretic models provide principled frameworks for modeling vehicle interactions, but their underlying temporal assumptions have not been systematically examined against real-world driving behavior. In particular, it remains unclear how simultaneous, sequential, and asymmetric interaction structures can be measured from vehicle trajectories. This paper develops a trajectory-based interaction measurement framework to identify interaction events and quantify behavioral change onset, temporal organization, post-onset response dynamics, and ordering stability. The framework uses behavioral deviations to verify candidate interactions. We evaluate the framework on six real-world trajectory datasets, including INTERACTION, highD, inD, rounD, Waymo Open Motion, and nuPlan, covering diverse road geometries, traffic environments, and interaction types. The results show that concurrent and sequential behavioral changes both constitute substantial proportions of observed following, merging, and conflicting interactions. Among sequential interactions, stable ordering is more prevalent than alternating ordering, indicating that persistent asymmetric roles are a common interaction structure. Importantly, temporal precedence does not necessarily coincide with a measurable behavioral response, indicating that temporal ordering alone may not be sufficient to characterize behavioral dependence. These findings show that real-world interactions exhibit concurrent, sequential, and persistently ordered temporal structures. Different game-theoretic formulations are therefore better regarded as complementary modeling abstractions for different interaction regimes rather than as a universal structure governing all vehicle interactions.

</details>


### [165] [Repair or Resample? Rethinking Failure Debugging in LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25920)
*Zhongwen Luan,Xiaoyu Zhang,Ming Hu,Yue Yang,Jiongchi Yu,Xiaohong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文针对基于大语言模型（LLM）的多智能体系统（MAS）在长视野复杂任务中的可靠性问题，提出核心质疑：现有MAS调试与修复方法究竟是在因果性地修复失败，还是仅借助LLM采样的随机性进行"随机性恢复"？为此，作者构建了SymTrace（一种选择性重放框架，通过边界日志记录和事件依赖图精确重构干预前的执行前缀）和SymFail（536条人工标注的失败轨迹数据集），在AG2、CrewAI、Magentic-One三大主流MAS框架上进行大规模实证研究。研究发现：无指导重跑方法的失败复现率仅67.97%、修复率仅6.90%；而基于症状驱动的节点干预方法（Suspicious-Node Intervention）单次干预修复率达20.15%，较最强基线提升191.89%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM多智能体系统在长视野复杂任务中的部署日益广泛，其可靠性已成为阻碍实际应用的核心瓶颈。现有调试与修复方法（如rerun、self-reflection、critic-agent）通常依赖完整轨迹的重采样来"修复"失败，但实际上可能只是在借助LLM的随机采样偶然绕过了原失败，而非真正定位并修复了失败机制。此外，缺乏能够可靠复现MAS失败的控制框架，使得"修复成功"难以与"采样成功"区分开来，阻碍了对修复方法有效性的科学评估。

Method: 1. **SymTrace框架**：采用双模工作流。Snapshot模式通过边界日志记录（Boundary Logging）拦截LLM请求-响应对和工具调用-观察对，构建事件依赖图G=(V,E)和观察事件顺序O，并打包为Replay Bundle。Replay模式通过结果注入（Result Injection）和边界匹配（Boundary Matching）严格按记录顺序回放干预点之前的前缀，通过Live Resume在干预点后恢复实时执行。从而精确保持干预前的执行历史固定，使下游变化可归因于干预而非上游重采样。
2. **SymFail数据集**：基于WebArena-Verified Hard和AssistantBench的200个任务，在AG2、CrewAI、Magentic-One三大框架上各执行一次，得到600个初始执行，其中536个评估器确认的失败被保留。由4名具有软件工程背景的标注员进行人工标注，包含C1-C4四类非互斥的可追踪症状类别（任务约束违反、重复或停滞进度、未解决的运行时条件、计划-行动-结果不一致）、失效节点定位、证据和最终审定标注。标注员间一致性Fleiss'κ在主类别上为0.62。
3. **评估方法**：采用rep_k和pass@k指标，对比Unguided Full Rerun、Self-Reflection、Critic-Agent三种任务级基线，以及Last-Node、Random-Node两种节点级对照；提出Suspicious-Node Intervention方法，通过确定性规则与语义判断器联合检测可疑节点（C1-C4症状），在干预点注入条件化修复指令。所有对比使用deepseek-v4-flash模型，温度0.00，95% Wilson置信区间和McNemar+Holm校正进行统计推断。

Result: 1. **RQ1（失败复现）**：SymTrace将单次复现率从67.97%提升至80.78%，三次一致复现率从41.42%提升至52.43%，所有重用前缀节点通过内容哈希验证（100.00%前缀精确性）。
2. **RQ2（任务级修复）**：三种任务级基线在pass@3下修复率均不超过6.90%。Unguided Full Rerun在所有MAS上表现最佳；Self-Reflection和Critic-Agent在相同三次尝试预算下未显示出相对无指导重跑的统计显著优势（在Holm校正后所有R Q2对比均不显著），表明反馈方法没有针对性地解决失败机制。
3. **RQ3（节点级症状驱动干预）**：Suspicious-Node Intervention以一次干预实现20.15%的总体修复率（AG2 16.37%、CrewAI 25.00%、Magentic-One 18.78%），是最强任务级基线（6.90%）的2.92倍，相对提升191.89%。在所有15个配对对比中均显著优于基线（Holm校正后p_H < 0.05），且在API调用效率（每千次调用修复数、每次修复调用数）上也全面领先。
4. **结论**：现有任务级方法本质上依赖随机再生而非针对性修复；成功的重运行并不证明原失败机制被识别并修正。局部化的症状证据能提供比完整轨迹重采样更一致的修复信号。

Conclusion: 本研究通过SymTrace选择性重放框架和SymFail标注数据集，在三大主流MAS框架上首次系统区分了"因果性修复"与"随机性恢复"。核心结论：① 完整轨迹重采样在因果归因上不可靠，无指导重跑的失败复现与修复率均处于低水平（67.97%和6.90%）；② 反馈式方法（Self-Reflection、Critic-Agent）在相同预算下未超越无指导重跑，说明其额外反馈未可靠针对原始失败机制；③ 基于症状的局部节点干预能够以单次干预实现2.92倍的修复率提升（提升191.89%），证明精确执行控制比重复随机再生更有效；④ 可靠的MAS修复评估应保留干预前历史、定位预期变化、并评估是否修复了失败机制而非仅产生新结果。该研究为MAS调试与修复提供了可操作的方法论指导，强调"终端任务成功"不能作为修复有效性的充分证据。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems (MASs) are increasingly applied to long-horizon complex tasks, their reliability has emerged as the core bottleneck hindering their real-world deployment. Existing MAS debugging and repair methods typically rely on rerunning and resampling the entire execution trajectory. However, a fundamental question remains to be answered: do these methods causally repair MAS failures or merely stochastically repair by leveraging the randomness of LLM sampling? To evaluate the effectiveness of MAS repair methods, we introduce SymTrace, a controlled evaluation framework that records the MAS execution trajectory and establishes intervention anchors. During replay, it effectively reconstructs the execution before the anchor using recorded logs and only regenerates the downstream trajectory, thereby enabling the reliable reproduction of MAS failures. We further construct the dataset SymFail, comprising 536 human-annotated failure trajectories with graph-linked locations, categories, and trace evidence. Based on these foundations, we conduct a large-scale empirical study across three mainstream MAS frameworks. Our findings reveal that existing unguided rerun methods are highly unreliable, exhibiting low failure reproduction and repair rates (only 67.97% and 6.90%, respectively). Building upon these findings, we further explore the effectiveness of a symptom-driven intervention method, which successfully repairs 20.15% of the failed cases (a 191.89% improvement to state-of-the-art repair methods). This study aims to provide actionable insights for MAS debugging and repair research, paving the way for the robust deployment of multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [166] [Formal, Executable and Explainable Runtime Monitoring of Spoken Air Traffic Control Operational Procedures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25926)
*Roberto Luvini,Giacomo Longo,Alessandro Armando,Enrico Russo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向空中交通管制（ATC）的运行时验证（RV）框架，用于监控管制员-飞行员之间的口语化操作程序。该框架将无线电通信解析为与飞机实体关联的事件，与监视数据和机载观测融合为带时间戳的有限轨迹，并将源自ICAO的程序义务编码为带显式时间约束的时序逻辑公式（基于LTLf与MTL扩展的五种模式：bounded-response、invariant、precedence、pending-response、bounded-prohibition），通过三维语义（真/假/不确定）在执行轨迹上评估并输出可解释的违规判定（附带违反的义务及支持观测）。在真实交通场景中完整流水线F1达0.85；在1,495个合成场景中验证逻辑全部正确；并成功复现了Überlingen（2002）与Comair 5191（2006）两起历史事故中调查机构所记录的违规序列，且检测时间早于碰撞发生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着全球航班量持续增长、管制员负荷加大以及人员编制受限，ATC安全关键操作中程序偏差的持续监测变得越来越困难。现有安全网（如STCA、A-SMGCS）仅能检测冲突或跑道侵入，而无法将无线电语音内容、飞机状态与ICAO程序义务进行语义层面的联合验证；且程序违规是历史上多起致命事故（如Überlingen、Comair 5191）的关键诱因。已有方法在观测接地（R1）、多源融合（R2）、时序及时效推理（R3）、证据化判定（R4）四个需求上覆盖不全，亟需一种可形式化、可执行、可解释的统一监控框架。

Method: 1) 形式化模型 M = ⟨AP, Π, Φ, ⊨⟩：AP为参数化航空实体（飞机、飞行高度、跑道、Agent等）的多类一阶词汇上的原子命题集合；Π为带秒级时间戳的有限时序轨迹；Φ为基于LTLf并引入MTL度量算子 F≤δ 的时序语言。2) 五种逻辑模式：bounded-response、invariant、precedence（¬α W β）、pending-response、bounded-prohibition，对应readback、state-consistency、resolution-advisory、runway-incursion、advisory-priority、clearance-precedence、repeated-instructions、advisory-direction八类公式族，覆盖ICAO Doc 4444与TCAS规范。3) 满足关系 ⊨ 采用强X、全局G、强Until、弱Until、度量F≤δ 的有限轨迹语义；监控器在开放轨迹上采用三值语义（真/假/不确定），对未到截止时间的待定义义务报告为"尚未违反"。4) 流水线架构：Acquisition（无线电+ADS-B）→ Atom Extraction（faster-whisper转写+LLM结构化解析+Shapely机场建模）→ Trace Construction（呼号解析、按事件时间排序、派生原子插入）→ Verification（对每条公式按结构递归评估，最坏二次复杂度，违规附位置及证据命题）。5) 评估：基于ATCO2与TartanAviation公开语料，使用qwen2.5-7b（在线）、qwen3.8-27b与gemma4-26b-a4b（离线，含thinking/非thinking模式）作为解析器。

Result: 1) 实现正确性：255个触发事件派生1,495个合成场景（664合规、831违规），覆盖50种公式形式、五类公式族，监控器全部返回预期判定。2) 与专家标注一致性（三小时KAGC地面真值12个违规）：qwen3.8-27b非思考模式最佳，P=0.79、R=0.92、F1=0.85；qwen2.5-7b F1=0.62但延迟最低（中位0.3秒/语句）。3) 算力开销：ASR RTFx中位45.1；大LLM解析中位1.58秒/语句；公式评估中位0.119秒/事件；整套流水线可在单台RTX 4090工作站上运行。4) 事故复现：Überlingen——21:35:01检出advisory-direction与resolution-advisory（碰撞前31秒），21:35:03检出advisory-priority（碰撞前29秒），与BFU报告完全一致；Comair 5191——06:05:15检出呼号错误，06:06:00检出跑道侵入，06:06:16检出错误跑道起飞（碰撞前19秒），与NTSB报告一致。5) 相关工作比较：在R1–R4四个监控需求上，首次实现联合完整覆盖。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将管制员-飞行员口语交换与监视/机载数据统一于带显式时间约束的时序逻辑规范下，提供了形式化、可执行且可解释的ATC程序运行时监控框架，填补了现有方法在观测接地、多源融合、时效推理与证据化判定方面的覆盖缺口。其三值语义支持在线开放轨迹的渐进判定，并能提前于碰撞时刻检测程序偏差。未来工作将扩展公式集以覆盖本地程序惯例、扩大交通语料验证、并探索在其他交通领域（海事、铁路等）的应用，同时需通过人机协同评估其在真实ATC工作流中的集成与告警校准。

Abstract: Air traffic control procedures are executed through spoken exchanges between controllers and pilots. These interactions are essential to the safety of air transportation: failures in their execution can create severe operational hazards, as evidenced by past fatal accidents. Assessing whether an instruction has been followed requires relating what was said to the aircraft concerned, its state, and the obligations that pilots must meet. We present a runtime verification framework that monitors such procedures by checking controller-pilot exchanges, surveillance data, and onboard observations. The framework parses radio communications into events linked to the entities they concern and merges them with surveillance and onboard observations into a time-stamped trace. The ICAO-derived obligations as formalized as temporal formulas with explicit time bounds and evaluated over execution traces. Every violation is reported along with the breached obligations and the observations that support the verdict. With real traffic, the complete pipeline reaches an F1 of 0.85 against blind human-annotated violations; in 1,495 synthetic situations derived from two public corpora, the monitor logic returns the expected verdict in every case. In two historical accidents reconstructed from official investigation reports, the monitor identifies the same procedural deviations documented by the investigators.

</details>


### [167] [How Robust Are Automated Fact-Checking Systems? A Cross-Benchmark Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25934)
*Aida Usmanova,Zangir Iklassov,Markus Leippold,Ricardo Usbeck*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对自动事实核查（AFC）系统进行了跨基准的统一评估，测试了9个模型（从随机基线到AVeriTeC 2025冠军）在4个不同领域数据集（AVeriTeC、SciFact、ClimateCheck、ClimateFEVER）上的完整两阶段"检索-验证"流水线表现。研究发现经典基线（如TF-IDF+LogReg）在某些领域能超越SOTA系统，检索质量是主要瓶颈，系统排名高度依赖领域和评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AFC系统评估存在两大缺陷：(1) 缺少简单基线作为下界参考，无法判断报告的提升是真正的语言理解进步还是数据集特定的工程调优；(2) 系统通常针对单一基准开发和测试，无法保证跨领域泛化能力。已有研究要么只评估检索阶段，要么只评估单一任务分类，缺乏对完整两阶段流水线在多样化数据集上的跨基准评估。

Method: 在相同条件下评估4个结构多样化的数据集（AVeriTeC开放网络、SciFact生命科学、ClimateCheck气候社交媒体、ClimateFEVER气候维基百科），测试9个模型涵盖：随机基线、稀疏检索（TF-IDF、BM25）+ LogReg、微调Transformer（DistilRoBERTa、Longformer）、零样本LLM（Llama 3.1-8B/70B），以及AVeriTeC 2025共享任务前两名系统（Sanctuary、AIC CTU）。分别评估检索阶段（Recall@K、F1@K）和验证阶段（准确率、macro-F1），并进行oracle检索实验揭示检索瓶颈，使用bootstrap 95%置信区间进行统计显著性检验。

Result: (1) 在ClimateCheck上，仅使用claim的经典基线（TF-IDF+LogReg）优于零样本LLM和AVeriTeC 2025顶级系统，因为噪声检索会损害验证性能；(2) 系统排名高度依赖领域和指标：在SciFact上最佳模型（macro-F1 0.70）在ClimateCheck上跌至0.31，AVeriTeC冠军和亚军在不同指标/数据集下互换排名；(3) 将检索证据替换为gold标注可使验证准确率提升14-22个百分点，证明检索是主要瓶颈；(4) 顶部系统对ClimateCheck和ClimateFEVER的标签预测几乎随机，而经典基线保持稳定。

Conclusion: 简单的稀疏检索+逻辑回归基线应作为AFC评估的强制下界；单一基准结果不足以证明泛化能力，必须进行跨域评估；检索与验证阶段应解耦评估并报告oracle上限以定位瓶颈；macro-F1和per-label F1应同时报告以揭示多数类偏差；标注质量应被量化——由于ClimateFEVER等数据集的标注噪声（κ=0.334），低于约5个百分点的性能差距可能不可靠。在AFC系统能证明跨域可靠性之前，排行榜排名不应被视为真实场景实用性的证据。

Abstract: Automated fact-checking (AFC) systems retrieve evidence and predict claim veracity, yet evaluations omit simple baselines, systems are developed for a single benchmark and cannot be trusted to generalise across domains. No prior work cross-evaluates the full two-stage retrieve-then-verify pipeline across diverse datasets, complementing retrieval-only studies (Thakur et al., 2021) and single-stage benchmarking studies (Calamai et al., 2025). We benchmark nine models, ranging from random and sparse baselines to fine-tuned transformers, zero-shot LLMs, and the two highest-ranked systems from the AVeriTeC 2025 shared task, across four datasets spanning scientific, open-web, and climate domains. Three findings stand out: (1) on ClimateCheck claim-only and fine-tuned models outperform zero-shot LLM and top-performing AVeriTeC 2025 systems, highlighting that noisy evidence can degrade veracity prediction; (2) system rankings are strongly domain- and metric-dependent: the best model on SciFact (macro-F1 0.70) drops to 0.31 on ClimateCheck, while the AVeriTeC 2025 winner and runner-up swap rankings based on evaluation metrics and datasets; (3) replacing retrieved evidence with gold annotations improves veracity accuracy by 14-22 points across models, confirming retrieval remains primary bottleneck. We release code, pre-processed datasets, and all results to support reproducible AFC research.

</details>


### [168] [Candidate supply and answer selection shape the value of LLM judging in multi-agent systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25937)
*Jia-Hao Ji,Sijie Li,Jiabei Cheng,Zixi She,Jin-Tai Yu,Zhiyuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究多智能体系统（MAS）即使内部已生成正确答案，最终却报告错误答案的现象。通过解耦生成、通信与选择流程，引入LLM评判信号，发现将答案频率与评判分数结合可使准确率从63.82%提升至约71%，主要机制是挽救被多数错误答案淹没的正确候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统中存在"有能力答对却报错"的悖论，传统方法同时改动生成、通信与选择规则，难以诊断失败原因。作者试图将这一复合过程拆解为可独立分析的可控阶段，以解释共识形成中的"模因漂移"现象。

Method: 1. 将多智能体推理建模为"候选生成→同伴通信→终选"的进化式流水线；2. 在MMLU-Pro、GPQA、MedXpertQA、MuSR（15,336题）上分析评判器可靠性，并单独分析Humanity's Last Exam；3. 通过重放81,390个来自16,278题的固定候选池，控制变量以隔离评判信号的效果；4. 改变最终选择规则，将答案频率与LLM评判分数相结合。

Result: 三点发现：（1）正确答案常已存在于候选池中，但系统仍可能收敛于错误答案；（2）评判器可靠性并非模型固有属性，而是随任务、生成器及正确答案的稀有程度而变化；（3）仅修改终选规则（结合频率与评判分数）即可将准确率从63.82%提升至70.82–70.95%，主要通过挽救被多数错误压制的正确候选实现。

Conclusion: 在所研究系统中，增加候选数的价值取决于其能否让正确答案变得"存在、频繁或可识别"。通过解耦生成、识别与选择三个环节，本研究为设计能保护已生成正确答案的多智能体架构提供了诊断基础。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) sometimes already have the potential to answer correctly, but still report a wrong answer. Explaining this outcome is difficult because generation, communication and final answer-selection rules usually change simultaneously. We conceptualize multi-agent reasoning as an evolutionary pipeline of candidate generation, peer communication and terminal selection, wherein consensus without quality control can exhibit patterns of memetic drift. We study two questions: (1) when an LLM judge provides effective selection pressure by supplying a signal of answer correctness for candidates generated in a multi-agent system, and (2) when using that signal improves the reported answer. To map judge reliability, we analysed 15,336 questions from MMLU-Pro, GPQA, MedXpertQA and MuSR, with Humanity's Last Exam analysed separately. To test these rules, we replayed 81,390 fixed candidate pools drawn from 16,278 questions across five benchmarks. We report three findings. (1) A correct answer is often already present among the generated candidates, but the system can still converge on and report a wrong answer. (2) Judge reliability is not a fixed trait of the model, but varies with the task, the generator and how rare the correct answer is. (3) Combining answer frequency with the judge's evaluation changed only the final answer-selection rule and raised accuracy from 63.82% to 70.82-70.95%, primarily by rescuing correct answers that were outnumbered by popular errors. In the systems studied here, the value of generating more candidates depends on whether those extra samples make correct answers present, frequent or recognisable. By isolating generation, recognition and selection, these findings establish a diagnostic basis for designing multi-agent architectures that protect generated correct answers from being lost.

</details>


### [169] [LivingRAG: Augmenting Graph RAG with Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25960)
*Yuzhuo Cui,Zongye Zhang,Qingjie Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LivingRAG 是一个为在线问答流设计的 Graph RAG 框架，通过引入可写可复用的"经验存储"（experience store），使系统能够复用先前已验证的推理经验，提升多跳问答的准确性并降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 和 Graph RAG 系统在推理时通常独立处理每个查询，丢弃了 LLM 响应中有用的推理信号。这导致后续相关的查询需要重新检索证据和从头推理，限制了在线问答流中的复用能力。作者观察到两个查询可能访问图中的邻近区域或遵循相同的推理模式（例如都是比较"谁出生得更早"），即使涉及不同实体。因此需要一个能保留图邻域先验和可复用推理模式的在线 Graph RAG 系统。

Method: LivingRAG 在基于 LinearRAG 的图检索骨架上增加了一个可写经验存储。每个经验包含：(1) 源查询、(2) 稀疏激活图（sparse activation map over graph entities）、(3) 紧凑推理摘要（compact reasoning summary）、(4) 验证元数据。两类复用路径：① 激活图融合（activation-map fusion）——在图检索时将存储经验的激活向量与当前查询的初始激活向量融合，引导检索走向有用证据邻域；② 推理脚手架（reasoning scaffold）——将紧凑推理摘要注入 LLM 提示，作为生成的参考推理模式。写回控制（write-back）：候选经验需通过两项质量门控——基于 NLI 的事实基础性检查（Grounding Score, GS）和新颖性检查（Novelty Score, NS）——才被写入存储，从而防止错误推理被放大复用。

Result: 在 2WikiMultiHopQA、HotpotQA、MuSiQue、MuSiQue-full 和 WixQA 五个基准上，LivingRAG 在所有数据集上均取得最佳 LLM 准确率（如 2Wiki 87.00%、MuSiQue-full 58.42%、WixQA 70.25%），优于 Vanilla RAG、LightRAG、GFM-RAG、HippoRAG2 和 LinearRAG 等强基线。效率方面：完成 token 加权减少 22.7%，估计总成本降低 12.1%（从 $51.05 降至 $44.87），提示 token 仅增加 3.5%。消融实验验证了激活图融合、推理脚手架和质量门控三者的必要性。质量门控仅接受 27.4% 的候选经验（1592/5817），证实了选择性写入的有效性。此外，在 HippoRAG2 风格的不同检索器上也展示了初步的可迁移性。

Conclusion: LivingRAG 表明，经核验的经验复用是一种简单有效的方式来增强 Graph RAG：通过写入和复用已验证的推理经验（通过激活图融合指导检索、通过推理摘要辅助生成），可以在不修改模型参数的情况下，在在线问答流中同时提升准确性和降低推理成本。该方法的核心启示是，Graph RAG 的推理不应仅是"读"操作，而应包含可控制、可验证的"写"操作。

Abstract: Graph-based RAG improves multi-hop question answering by organizing evidence as a knowledge graph. However, most existing RAG systems process each query in isolation and discard useful reasoning from the LLM's response after inference. As a result, later related queries need to retrieve evidence and reason from scratch. We propose LivingRAG, a Graph RAG framework with writable and reusable reasoning experience. LivingRAG adds a writable experience store to a graph-based retrieval backbone, enabling verified experiences to be reused during inference in two ways. Stored graph signals help retrieval find entities and passages that were useful in earlier related queries. Stored summaries provide a reference reasoning pattern for answer generation. We analyze online QA streams and find reusable signals from shared entities, graph neighborhoods, and question templates. Experiments on multi-hop QA benchmarks show that LivingRAG improves accuracy over strong RAG baselines and reduces completion-token use when relevant prior experience is reused.

</details>


### [170] [Quantitative Analysis of $ω$-Regular Robust MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25968)
*Ali Asadi,Krishnendu Chatterjee,Ehsan Kafshdar Goharshady,Mehrdad Karrabi,Alipasha Montaseri,Ali Shafiee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文解决了线性定义不确定性集下(s,a)-矩形鲁棒MDP中ω-正则目标（奇偶目标）的精确定量分析问题，证明了智能体和环境均拥有纯无记忆最优策略，并给出了结合定量单步改进与定性几乎必然改进的策略迭代算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒MDP中已有工作研究了定性分析（几乎必然/正概率问题），但定量分析（计算精确的满足概率值）仍是开放问题。精确的定量值对于在无几乎必然保证时区分策略优劣至关重要，对安全关键系统的逻辑正确性验证具有重要意义。

Method: 方法分为两部分：(1) 对鲁棒马尔可夫链（RMC），将安全目标建模为多项式规模的线性规划（基于流量守恒特征），通过极大终组件分解将奇偶目标归约为安全目标；(2) 对RMDP，提出策略迭代算法，交替使用定量单步改进（基于一步最坏值 ν(s,a) > 当前值）和定性几乎必然改进（利用值类、紧致动作、紧致面构造陷阡值类RMDP并调用几乎必然奇偶过程）。此外，通过将不确定性多面体替换为其顶点的有限博弈来证明位置确定性。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 证明RMDP中智能体和环境均存在纯无记忆最优策略；(2) 对线性定义RMC的奇偶目标给出多项式时间算法；(3) 对RMDP给出多项式空间的精确策略迭代算法（最坏 |A|^|S| 次迭代）；(4) 决策问题属于 NP∩coNP 且至少与回合制随机奇偶博弈一样难；(5) 实验表明在分支因子较大的Garnet和Inventory基准上优于显式归约至随机博弈的方法，在分支因子小的Frozen Lake上则相反。

Conclusion: 该论文完整解决了线性定义(s,a)-矩形RMDP上ω-正则目标的定量分析问题，给出了多项式空间精确算法。未来方向包括：将结果推广到非线性凸不确定性（椭球、散度球等）、放宽(s,a)-矩形性、以及研究不确定性集从样本估计的学习场景。多项式时间精确算法仍是一个公开问题。

Abstract: Robust Markov Decision Processes (RMDPs) generalize classical MDPs by allowing uncertainty in transition probabilities and optimizing against their worst-case realization. We consider $(s,a)$-rectangular RMDPs with \emph{linearly defined} uncertainty sets and study parity objectives, which are a canonical representation of $\omega$-regular objectives. An uncertainty set is linearly defined if it is described by linear inequalities over the transition distribution together with auxiliary variables, which capture the standard $L_1$ and $L_\infty$ balls as well as general polytopic uncertainty sets. The quantitative value is the supremum, over all agent policies, of the satisfaction probability guaranteed against the adversarial environment. Previous work studied the qualitative analysis, namely the almost-sure (resp. positive) problem that asks whether a single agent policy guarantees satisfaction with probability one (resp. positive probability) against every environment policy. In this work, we solve the exact quantitative problem. Our contributions are threefold. First, we show that both the agent and the environment admit pure memoryless optimal policies. Second, we give a polynomial-time algorithm for quantitative parity on linearly defined robust Markov chains and use it as a subroutine in a policy-iteration algorithm for RMDPs. The algorithm combines quantitative one-step improvements with qualitative almost-sure improvements. Finally, we report experiments comparing our approach with the explicit reduction to stochastic games.

</details>


### [171] [SciMIF: Understanding Multimodal Instruction Following in Scientific Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25973)
*Ye Shen,Yuting Zheng,Dun Pei,Zijian Chen,Wenlong Zhang,Qi Jia,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SciMIF 是首个面向科学领域的多模态指令遵循评测基准，覆盖 5 个学科、10 类功能约束组、42 条学科适配约束，共 2,527 个样本，用于系统评估 MLLM 在复杂科学指令下的约束遵循能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令遵循评测基准（IFEval、FollowBench、MM-IFEngine 等）主要关注通用文本/格式约束，缺乏对科学领域特定约束（如化学键数量、地理层级、生物序列长度等）的评估；同时科学正确性与指令遵循性往往被混为一谈，掩盖了模型的真实能力短板。

Method: 1) 由领域专家分析 22 个任务、13 个源数据集，构建包含 10 个功能约束组、42 条学科适配约束的双层分类法；2) 设计数据增强流程（种子准备 → 隐含约束识别 → 科学约束注入 → 通用约束注入（最多 N=3 类，每类最多 k=3 次重试）→ 人工核验）；3) 引入双自动校验（included ∧ unchanged），使用 DeepSeek-Chat 进行校验，确保注入的约束显式存在且原参考答案不变；4) 采用 CSR、ISR、DRFR 三个指标，并结合 Script-Based 与 LLM-as-a-Judge 双协议进行约束验证。

Result: 1) 各学科表现差异显著：化学最具挑战性（GPT-5.2 在化学 ISR 仅 46.33%，物理达 69.56%），化学/地理涉及更多细粒度结构与空间层级约束；2) 闭源模型整体优于开源（GPT-5.2 总体 ISR 65.67% vs Qwen3.5-397B-A17B 57.39%）；3) 存在「扩展悖论」——InternVL3.5 8B (51.37%) 与 38B (51.64%) 几乎持平，Qwen3.5 27B (51.37%) 反而优于 122B (50.41%)，参数规模提升无法线性改善约束遵循；4) 通用约束遵循率显著低于科学约束（如 GPT-5.2: 74.65% vs 88.74%），模型更关注与任务语义耦合的科学约束；5) Letter (51.30%)、Number (68.62%)、Format (72.18%) 等细粒度约束表现最差，反映符号级处理与科学实体识别的双重不足；6) 科学正确性与指令遵循能力弱耦合（CF 率 < 30%，φ 系数 0.067~0.207），Pearson χ² 检验证实二者并非同一能力。

Conclusion: SciMIF 填补了科学多模态指令遵循评测的空白，揭示了当前 MLLM 在科学指令遵循上的三大核心局限：学科间性能不均衡、参数规模无助于约束遵循、科学正确性与指令遵循弱耦合。未来方向包括：面向约束环境的应用导向训练、结合外部符号引擎与计算工具的结构感知方法、以及将科学正确性与指令遵循联合优化。

Abstract: Understanding instruction-following capabilities in scientific domains is essential for effectively leveraging Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to advance the development of scientific fields. In this work, we introduce SciMIF, a novel benchmark designed to evaluate the capability of MLLMs in following complex scientific instructions. Specifically, based on an extensive analysis of 22 distinct tasks across 5 representative scientific disciplines, we propose a comprehensive taxonomy comprising 10 constraint groups that captures both general functional requirements and discipline-specific characteristics. Guided by this taxonomy, we develop a high-fidelity instruction injection pipeline to systematically augment existing scientific datasets. We conduct comprehensive experiments on multiple state-of-the-art closed-source and open-source MLLMs. Our findings reveal significant performance disparities across different scientific disciplines, with chemistry posing greater challenges for current MLLMs. Furthermore, we observe that increasing the model scale does not yield corresponding improvements in constraint adherence, and current models still struggle severely with fine-grained constraints and instructions requiring the deep application of disciplinary knowledge. SciMIF fills the current void in evaluating multimodal instruction adherence within scientific domains, laying a crucial foundation for future enhancements of MLLMs in rigorous scientific applications. Data and code will be released at this https URL .

</details>


### [172] [Multi-Granularity Context-Enhanced RAG over Multimodal Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25986)
*Zongyu Wu,Yilong Wang,Xiaochen Wang,Minhua Lin,Zhichao Xu,Fenglong Ma,Xiang Zhang,Suhang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CEMMKG框架，通过为多模态知识图谱（MMKG）中的视觉元素设计多粒度的局部和全局文本上下文，并在图像到图谱构建及跨模态融合两个阶段中有效注入该上下文，显著提升多模态GraphRAG性能，且可适配不同MMKG-RAG方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多模态知识图谱的RAG方法在处理不同模态时相互独立，视觉信息提取仅依赖有限的邻近文本上下文，导致图像与文本之间存在语义鸿沟，限制了多模态GraphRAG的性能。如何系统性地定义和利用文本上下文来弥合这一鸿沟仍缺乏深入研究。

Method: 方法包含两部分：(1) 多粒度文本上下文构建——为每个视觉元素构建局部上下文（周围文本+基于显式引用的参考句/参考段落/参考段落摘要）和全局上下文（文档摘要或原始摘要）；(2) 多阶段上下文利用——在图像到图谱阶段注入完整上下文（含局部和全局），在跨模态融合阶段仅注入局部上下文，引导实体对齐。

Result: 在从MMLongBench-Doc中筛选的VisionHeavy子集（106题）上，以MMGraphRAG为骨干，采用"参考句+文档级全局上下文"配置取得最佳整体性能：严格准确率34.91%，软准确率36.84%，显著优于基线MMGraphRAG的23.58%/24.17%。在RAG-Anything上也取得一致提升（严格准确率从28.89%提升至35.56%），验证了方法的广泛适用性。同时实验表明，过长或粒度不当的上下文（如完整段落）反而会降低性能。

Conclusion: CEMMKG系统性地研究了文本上下文在MMKG构建中的作用，证明了恰当设计的多粒度上下文能有效提升多模态GraphRAG性能；上下文设计需在信息量与相关性之间取得平衡，过多的上下文会引入噪声；该框架可灵活集成到不同MMKG-RAG方法中，具有良好的通用性。未来可扩展至视频、音频等其他模态。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is widely used to mitigate hallucination issues in large language models (LLMs) and multimodal large language models (MLLMs). In particular, knowledge graph (KG)-based RAG leverages structured knowledge to provide (M)LLMs with high-quality external information. Building on these works, recent studies have explored multimodal knowledge graphs (MMKGs) as knowledge bases for GraphRAG. This enables Graph RAG to integrate knowledge across multiple modalities, thereby further enhancing its performance. However, existing MMKG-based RAG methods generally follow a common pipeline in which different modalities are largely processed independently before being fusion. As a result, textual context is only used to a limited extent during visual information extraction and subsequent multimodal knowledge fusion. This brings a semantic gap between images and text which limits the multimodal GraphRAG performance. To address this issue, we propose a novel framework for constructing a Context-Enhanced MMKG (CEMMKG) to better support multimodal GraphRAG. The proposed CEMMKG enriches each image with complementary textual context at both local and global scopes. Local context goes beyond the surrounding text by incorporating sentences that are semantically related to the image, while global context provides a summary of the entire passage. We further introduce a multi-granularity design for the local context, allowing it to capture semantically relevant information at different levels of detail. Extensive experiments on the selected vision-centric dataset validate that CEMMKG is effective in leveraging contextual information to improve MMKG-based RAG performance. Moreover, its effectiveness across different MMKG-based RAG methods demonstrates its broad applicability.

</details>


### [173] [ProgRouter: Online Progress-Guided Orchestration for Multi-Agent LLM Workflows under Quality-Cost Tradeoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25992)
*Somgyuan Li,Ahmed M. Abdelmoniem,Shiqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProgRouter提出了一种面向多智能体LLM工作流的在线、进度引导的路由框架，通过多视图进度评分器、双路径进度预测器以及自适应元门控机制，在工作流各步动态选择LLM智能体，以在满足时间和成本预算的前提下保持任务完成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的级联式LLM路由方法采用一次性查询级决策，无法适应多步多智能体工作流的动态、状态依赖特性。固定的单模型策略和一次性路由方法要么因能力不足导致重复调用违反能耗预算，要么因始终使用大模型而过耗资源，且难以感知工作流的实时进度与剩余难度。

Method: 1) 多视图任务进度评分器：结合粗粒度工作流结果状态（invalid/recoverable/partial success/complete）与细粒度信号（子任务完成率、进度趋势、工作流状态质量）通过分层聚合产生统一进度得分；2) 双路径任务进度预测器：结构化路径（基于表格特征的树模型）与语义路径（基于轻量句编码器MiniLM的嵌入+树模型）通过自适应元门控机制（XGBoost）融合，预测每个候选LLM的步进进度增益；3) 在线路由决策：受Lyapunov drift-plus-penalty启发，构建虚拟成本队列跟踪长期平均能耗约束违反，结合进度差距感知价值项、预算感知的时间/成本指数惩罚，形成综合路由评分；通过ε-贪心在线探索与更新机制持续学习进度预测器，无需离线训练数据。

Result: 在HumanEval Plus、MBPP、MATH-500和ASQA四个基准上，ProgRouter在满足长期平均能耗预算的前提下，相对MasRouter和CASCADIA等基线在任务通过率/引用精度、能耗和执行时间上均取得最优或近最优的Pareto前沿表现。例如在MBPP上以3376J最低能耗和79.4%最高通过率全面优于基线，在ASQA上达到92.1%最高引用精度。消融实验验证了双路径预测器、多视图评分器和预算感知目标的各自贡献，并表明仅靠进度预测而不联合考虑剩余难度和长期预算会导致严重性能退化（17.7%通过率）。

Conclusion: ProgRouter通过将可观测工作流信号转化为统一的进度表示，结合在线双路径进度预测和成本/时间预算感知路由目标，实现了跨异构智能体工作流的步骤级自适应LLM选择，是首个明确以任务进度驱动进行多智能体LLM路由的框架，为可持续、成本高效的智能体LLM服务提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model (LLM) workflows have emerged as a powerful paradigm for solving complex, open-ended tasks through collaborative reasoning among specialized LLM agents, but they incur substantial operating costs due to repeated LLM invocations and long-horizon context accumulation. Existing cascade routing methods make one-shot, query-level decisions and cannot adapt to the dynamic, state-dependent nature of multi-step workflows, in which the right LLM at each step depends on evolving task progress, remaining task difficulty, and cost-efficiency requirements. We present ProgRouter, an online progress-guided routing framework that adaptively selects LLM agents across workflow steps to preserve task-solving quality while adhering to time and cost budgets. ProgRouter introduces a multi-view task progress scorer that combines coarse workflow outcome regimes with fine-grained signals on subtask completion, progress trends, and workflow state quality. Then, a dual-path task progress predictor and an adaptive meta-gating mechanism estimate the progress gain for each candidate routed LLM. ProgRouter makes online step-wise routing decisions that balance progress gain, task time budgets, and long-term operating cost efficiency. Experiments on HumanEval Plus, MBPP, MATH-500, and ASQA, spanning agentic code generation, mathematical reasoning, and retrieval-augmented long-form question answering, demonstrate that ProgRouter reduces the operating cost relative to key baselines while maintaining strong task-solving performance.

</details>


### [174] [AsymSpec: Context-Asymmetric Speculative Decoding for Agentic LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26004)
*Sheng Liang,Yongyue Zhang,Nathanael Brian,Hang Lv,Hao Wang,Chen Zhang,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AsymSpec是一种上下文非对称的推测解码框架，通过让轻量级草稿器读取完整输入而大型验证器仅基于压缩视图工作，结合对比式δ融合和发散感知接受门，在代理型LLM工作流中以0.2-0.3倍计算成本恢复约90%的全上下文精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理型LLM管道在检索、工具使用和多轮交互中上下文不断累积，导致推理成本激增。部署方通常压缩输入以控制延迟，但会牺牲任务精度。标准推测解码（SD）虽然能无损加速生成，但强制草稿器与验证器共享相同上下文，无法解决"精度-开销"的权衡问题。

Method: 框架包含两个核心机制：(1) 对比式δ融合——同一草稿器分别在完整上下文和压缩上下文上做前向传播，将两者的logit差δ=a-b注入验证器预测中，以隔离由额外上下文引入的信息增益；(2) 上下文发散接受门（CDA）——基于Jensen-Shannon散度动态调整每位置的接受阈值，无需额外超参数。框架天然支持跨模态扩展（视觉-语言草稿器+纯文本验证器）。

Result: 在四个代理能力（长上下文多跳QA、多轮指令遵循、工具使用、多模态推理）和两个端到端基准（GAIA、SimpleQA）上，AsymSpec以0.2-0.3倍计算成本恢复约90%的全上下文精度，吞吐量提升1.3-1.7倍。精度恢复随压缩严重程度单调递增；在近无损任务上增益自然消失，验证了机制的正确性。机制消融表明CDA门和δ融合均为必要组件，相同模型跨上下文的δ构造（a-b）显著优于原始增广logit或SCD风格构造。

Conclusion: AsymSpec打破了标准推测解码的对称上下文约束，通过将上下文恢复任务卸载到轻量级草稿器，在压缩验证器延迟下实现接近全上下文的推理精度。该方法在压缩损失严重的场景下效果尤为显著，为生产环境中的代理型管道提供了可预测的效率-精度权衡机制。但其受限于压缩视图保留的信息量和草稿器的提取能力，且需要访问验证器logit，不适用于仅暴露生成文本的专有API。

Abstract: Agentic LLM pipelines face escalating inference costs as context accumulates across retrieval, tool use, and multi-turn interactions. To control latency, deployments routinely compress inputs, but this degrades task accuracy. Speculative decoding (SD) accelerates generation losslessly, yet it assumes the drafter and verifier share an identical context, preventing SD from resolving the accuracy-overhead trade-off. We propose AsymSpec, an asymmetric speculative decoding framework that breaks this symmetry: a lightweight drafter reads the full input while the large verifier operates on the compressed view. The drafter steers the verifier via a contrastive $\delta$-fusion of logits, modulated by a divergence-aware acceptance gate that preserves verification stability and high draft acceptance rates. Evaluated across four agentic capabilities and two end-to-end agent benchmarks, AsymSpec reaches $\approx 90\%$ of full-context accuracy on average, delivering $1.3$--$1.7\times$ throughput speedups at $0.2$--$0.3\times$ the compute cost on isolated text capabilities. These results show that asymmetric context access yields substantial gains precisely when compression discards critical reasoning signals.

</details>


### [175] [Imitation Learning for Connection-Tableau Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26009)
*Fredrik Rømming,Mantas Bakšys,Martin S. Fixman,Sean B. Holden*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将子句连接表（clausal connection tableaux）的证明构造形式化为形式演算所诱导的转换系统中的策略学习问题，把 leanCoP 风格的搜索以及 plCoP/rlCoP 风格的规划统一为同一接口下的策略；通过在已找到的证明上进行模仿学习（proof cloning + proof aggregation，DAgger 风格），训练一个对符号重命名不变的图神经网络打分器来对证明编辑进行评分，并在 M2k、MPTP2078-bushy 和 TPTP v9.2.1 上，在固定步骤预算下证明所学策略可比 leanCoP 多解最多 46% 的问题，且步骤数少一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动定理证明中的证明对象构造与搜索控制是耦合的；现有学习式连接证明器把学习嵌入各自专属的搜索流程，缺乏统一的抽象。作者希望以"形式演算决定哪些步骤合法、策略决定如何选择"这一更一般的视角，把连接表证明构造转化为可策略学习的转换系统，从而把整个策略学习工具箱直接用于定理证明，并评估不同搜索支架（完整符号回溯 vs 纯网络驱动）对性能的影响。

Method: 1) 把每个问题的子句连接表构造建模为转换系统 𝒫(M)=(S, A, s₀, T, S✓)，状态为带注释的部分连接表，动作为附加/剪枝的合法演算编辑；搜索栈、深度限制、剪枝等控制信息属于策略记忆而非系统状态。2) 把 leanCoP、plCoP/rlCoP 等解释为该系统上的有状态策略，并用三种记忆 U_id（迭代加深）、U_dfs（深度优先栈）、无记忆构造三种策略 π_id、π_dfs、π_markov。3) 用"证明回放"从已找到的闭合表中提取最优轨迹，方法为证明聚合（PA，DAgger 风格）：每轮用当前策略再发现证明，回放并加入训练集，重复 N 轮；训练目标为交叉熵行为克隆。4) 打分器为对符号重命名不变的 R-GCN，节点分语法/出现/目标三類，关系包括成员、原子、首项、参数、连接、实例、父、路径等；矩阵子图只编码一次，目标节点随证明增长重编码；候选编辑由编辑类型嵌入与端点节点嵌入经 MLP 评分。5) 在 M2k、MPTP2078-bushy 和 TPTP v9.2.1 上固定五个 leanCoP 选项配置（default、conj、cut、cut,comp(7)、def,scut）进行评估；超参由 M2k cut,comp(7) 上星搜索确定；预算为 1000 转换步与 120 秒时限。

Result: 在三个数据集与五个选项配置下，学得策略在每一行都优于 leanCoP 基线；其中 π_dfs 与 π_id 各占约一半的最优行。MPTP cut,comp(7) 配置下：早停时 π_markov 步数最少（11.9，约比基线 188.4 少一个数量级），π_dfs 新增 94 个证明、净解决最多（363 个），π_id 仅丢失 1 个基线证明。训练至收敛后丢失大幅减少（π_makrov 由 27 降至 1，π_dfs 由 12 降至 3），π_dfs 达到 396 个问题，比基线 281 多约 41%；与所有基线相比最高多解 46% 问题。聚合迭代分析显示三种策略从第一轮即超基线，随后趋于平稳。

Conclusion: 将连接表构造抽象为转换系统上的策略学习，使得无外部监督器、对符号重命名不变的 GNN 模仿学习即可在固定步骤预算下显著超越 leanCoP 基线；搜索支架越少，搜索越短但保留率越低，打分器充分拟合后保留率损失基本恢复；π_id 几乎不丢失基线证明，π_dfs 新增证明最多并最多可比基线多解 46% 问题，π_markov 以一个数量级更少的步数找到证明；模仿信号仅来自成功证明本身，未涵盖回溯编辑，是其一致性受限的主要原因。

Abstract: An automated theorem prover builds a proof step by step, choosing at each point what to add and what to remove. We cast this construction as a policy acting in a transition system induced by a formal calculus, which fixes which steps are sound: for clausal connection tableaux, leanCoP-style search and plCoP/rlCoP-style planning then become stateful policies over one interface, and policy-learning methods apply directly. We equip such policies with a graph neural network that scores proof edits from structure that transfers across problems, train it by imitation learning from found proofs, and measure how performance holds as we remove search scaffolding, from full symbolic backtracking to a policy the network drives alone. Within a fixed step budget on M2k, MPTP2078-bushy, and TPTP v9.2.1, learned policies solve up to 46% more problems than leanCoP, and reach proofs in an order of magnitude fewer steps.

</details>


### [176] [Trace Integrity for LLM Data Agents: A Vision for Auditable Structured Reasoning in Real-World Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26036)
*Srimonti Dutta,Akshata Kishore Moharir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出"追踪完整性"(Trace Integrity)概念,指出答案正确性不足以作为LLM数据代理的可靠性信号,因为正确答案可能由无效计算过程产生。作者引入执行契约(Execution Contracts)和CAIT Rate指标,在BIRD Mini-Dev上验证了答案准确率、追踪有效性和静默失败风险是相互分离的评估信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM数据代理正成为操作数据的接口,但答案正确性是不足够的可靠性信号。在结构化数据任务中,基准测试正确的答案可能由无效追踪(invalid trace)产生——例如使用错误的聚合、连接、过滤或模式绑定。现有方法如思维链提示生成自然语言推理,但这些推理不能可靠地指定真实系统所需的算子级程序,作者将此部署失败模式称为"结构鸿沟"(Structure Gap)。

Method: 论文提出四个核心组件:(1) Trace Integrity——评估计算追踪是否显式、可执行、模式有效、算子忠实、可重放、答案一致、可审计;(2) Execution Contracts——结构化工件,将用户意图绑定到模式元素、算子计划、假设、可执行查询、验证状态和最终答案;(3) Isolation Principle——默认在值级数据访问前指定预期计算;(4) CAIT Rate——衡量正确答案中由无效追踪支持的比例。在BIRD Mini-Dev 100个样本上,用Claude Haiku 4.5对比Direct SQL、Operation Summary + SQL和Contract-First SQL三种提示条件。

Result: 三种方法的答案准确率分别为20%、22%和24%,追踪完整性通过率为39%、43%和40%,CAIT Rate分别为55%、59.1%和45.8%。Contract-First SQL获得最高答案准确率(24%)和最低CAIT Rate(45.8%),但其追踪完整性通过率(40%)低于Operation Summary + SQL(43%)。结果证明答案准确率、追踪有效性和静默失败风险是相互独立的部署信号,仅依赖答案正确性评估会高估系统可靠性。

Conclusion: LLM数据代理不应仅回答结构化数据问题,还应留下可检查、重放、质疑的计算追踪。生产环境的数据代理应存储执行契约、在执行前验证契约、在答案与追踪不一致时发出警报,并将失败的追踪复用为回归测试。该工作为LLM数据代理提供了实用的评估框架,将追踪视为一等公民对象,而非仅关注最终答案。

Abstract: Answer accuracy is an insufficient reliability signal for LLM data agents. In structured-data tasks, a benchmark-correct answer can be produced by an invalid trace. This paper introduces Trace Integrity, a deployment reliability criterion for evaluating whether the computation recorded behind an answer is explicit, executable, schema-valid, operator-faithful, replayable, answer-consistent, and auditable. We identify the Structure Gap as the deployment failure mode that makes Trace Integrity necessary: natural-language reasoning and free-form rationales do not reliably specify the operator-level programs required by real-world systems. We operationalize Trace Integrity with execution contracts, structured artifacts that bind user intent to schema elements, operator plans, assumptions, executable queries, verification status, and final-answer linkage. We also introduce CAIT (Correct Answer / Invalid Trace) Rate, which measures how often answer-only evaluation counts computationally unsupported outputs as successes. In an empirical demonstration on BIRD Mini-Dev, Direct SQL, Operation Summary + SQL, and Contract-First SQL achieve answer accuracies of 20%, 22%, and 24%, while their Trace Integrity Pass Rates are 39%, 43%, and 40% and their CAIT Rates remain high at 55%, 59.1%, and 45.8%, showing that answer accuracy, trace validity, and silent-failure risk are distinct evaluation signals. Real-world LLM data agents should, therefore, be evaluated not only by whether their outputs match a reference answer, but by whether those outputs are backed by auditable computation.

</details>


### [177] [SwarmWorld: Stigmergic technological evolution in societies of language-model agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26081)
*Subhadeep Pal,Fiona Y. Wang,Markus J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SwarmWorld是一个模拟环境，用于研究去中心化的大语言模型智能体如何通过共享物理世界中的间接协调（stigmergy）而非预设角色或通信，自发演化出功能性技术社会。论文表明，共享物理世界使智能体群体在技术组合的广度和鲁棒性上优于独立搜索，但并不总能产生最强的单一技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体系统通常依赖直接对话、预设角色或集中式工作流，尚未验证去中心化的同质智能体能否仅通过共享环境构建功能性技术并超越独立搜索能力。本研究旨在填补这一空白，验证集体智能是否能从去中心化的物理印记协调中涌现。

Method: SwarmWorld将认知与后果分离：智能体在固定动作和材料架构内提出架构和控制器，而确定性模拟器决定功能。50-200个同质LLM智能体探索空间环境、处理资源、测试材料、构建持久化人造物并编写可执行控制器。设计了四种机制分辨条件（全文化、无通信、无显式文化、独立搜索），并在移除智能体后进行保持性评估。同时通过第二个火山材料世界（AshenRealm）和第三个蛋白质世界（Protein Realms）验证架构的可转移性。

Result: 共享社会在技术组合广度（组合鲁棒性0.2474 vs 0.1794）和验证发明数量（5.75-7.00 vs 2.75）上优于最佳独立搜索包络，同时具有更高的保持性鲁棒性。然而，独立搜索保留了最强的单一最终技术（0.3488 vs 0.2380）。智能体自发分化出探索者、建造者、维护者和协调者等行为表型；显式文化机制增强了协作和跨智能体程序继承，但功能益处取决于结果和时间尺度。物理印记本身足以支持有能力的群体，而交互驱动持久的技术生态学。
