Conclusion: 集体优势是有界的：交互在建立和维护技术生态学时最有价值，而不是寻找单一破纪录的对象。共享物理世界而非沟通本身是集体能力的主要来源。智能体、持久人造物、可执行血统和世界构成一个不断演化的技术社会，类似于技术进化而非传统多智能体协调。该框架为研究语言模型群作为演化社会提供了可复现的实验路径，未来可向更高保真度的模拟器和物理体现扩展。

Abstract: Collective intelligence can emerge when individuals coordinate through a shared environment, allowing local actions to accumulate into durable social organization. Language-model agents offer a new substrate for this process, yet most multi-agent systems rely on direct conversation, predefined roles, or centralized workflows. It remains unclear whether decentralized agents can build functional technologies and outperform independent search. Here, initially homogeneous LLM agents in SwarmWorld self-organize without assigned roles or recipes into evolving technological societies. Agents explore a spatial environment, process resources, test materials, construct persistent artifacts, and write executable controllers evaluated by a deterministic simulator under unseen disturbances after the agents are removed. SwarmWorld splits cognition from consequence: agents propose architectures and controllers within fixed action and material schemas, while the simulated world determines function. Shared societies develop broader, more resilient technological portfolios than a strong best-of-N isolated-search baseline, although isolated search remains competitive for the strongest artifact. Agents differentiate into exploration, construction, maintenance, and coordination behaviors, transitioning as the world matures. Technologies accumulate through collaborative construction, executable inheritance, and persistent agent-artifact networks, with most reuse beginning through physical observation rather than communication. Explicit cultural mechanisms amplify collaboration and organization, but functional benefits depend on outcome and timescale. Physical stigmergy alone supports capable societies, while interaction drives persistent technological ecologies rather than universally superior individual inventions.

</details>


### [178] [Planetary Prediction Engine: Autonomous Geospatial Prediction via Intelligent Data Selection and Foundation Model Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26088)
*Evelyn Ma,Rama Kumar Pasumarthi,Kishwar Shafin,Mandar Sharma,Mimi Sun,Hamed Sadeghi,Dav M. Ebengo,Mbulayi Onesime,Rouslan Solomakhin,John Wamburu,William Ogallo,Aisha Walcott-Bryant,Sanxing Chen,Arbaaz Muslim,Yael Mayer,Ronald Ho,Roy Lee,Ruth Alcantara,Abdoulaye Diack,Monica Bharel,Lambert Rosique,Jeremy Amez-Droz,Christopher Haire,James Manyika,Yossi Matias,Niv Efron,Gautam Prasad,Shravya Shetty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Planetary Prediction Engine (PPE)，一个端到端的自主地理空间预测AI系统。用户通过自然语言查询即可自动完成多模态数据发现、特征融合与模型优化，涵盖空间回归、超分辨率降尺度、空间传播建模及流行病学实时预测四类任务。该系统在US空间回归（CDC健康指标R² 76.8% vs 60.0%；FEMA风险指数64.9% vs 60.0%）、尼日利亚食品安全降尺度（R² 66.1% vs 31.5%）和2026年DRC埃博拉疫情实时预测（Recall@10 83.3% vs ~73%）等任务上均超越专家基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建行星尺度的预测模型面临两大瓶颈：(1) 地理空间数据生态碎片化，需要手动检索、多模态融合与迭代建模，工程量大（一次流行病学预测流程可达700+步骤，耗时数周）；(2) 现有AutoML/AI研究系统缺乏自主发现并融合大规模地理空间数据、对齐PDFM/AlphaEarth等多模态嵌入以及参数化机制流行病学模型的能力。PPE旨在通过自然语言接口降低专业门槛，让非专家也能即时构建专家级预测模型。

Method: 系统采用三阶段模块化架构，由LLM作为各阶段编排器（temperature=0）：(1) **智能数据选择**——六子阶段流水线：地理约束发现→基于文献的信号发现（直接信号+代理信号）→查询Data Commons/Google Earth Engine/Google Maps Platform等权威仓库→开放网络兜底检索→五维度评分排序（来源许可权重5x）→DataFrame组装。(2) **多模态数据集策划**——四准则Feature Gate防泄漏（数学成分/共享调查/因果方向/时间过滤）、分裂隔离插补、协变量与PDFM（330/512维）+ AlphaEarth（64维）嵌入融合。(3) **AutoML与预测**——自动识别任务类型（Nowcasting/Super-Res/STM/SEIR），搜索线性/Boost/XGBoost/MLP四类模型族，含预训练风险评估、过拟合守卫ΔR²与单次自校正循环。模型族搜索支持随机/空间分组/k折验证策略。

Result: 在三类任务、全球南北场景下均超越专家基线：(1) **空间回归（US）**：21项CDC健康指标R² 76.8% vs 60.0%（+16.8pp）；FEMA全国风险指数64.9% vs 60.0%；SVI 66.2% vs 58.6%。(2) **超分辨率降尺度**：尼日利亚食品安全（ADM1→ADM2）66.1% vs 31.5%（翻倍）；US SVI（county→ZCTA）37.6% vs 11.0%（+26.6pp）。(3) **流行病学实时预测**：2026年DRC Bundibugyo埃博拉疫情，Recall@10达83.3%（在5次周滚动预测中正确识别18个新入侵卫生区中的15个），比公开SOTA贝叶斯基线（~73%）提升10.3pp。消融实验验证：多模态融合（PDFM+AEF+协变量）优于任一单模态；智能数据选择与AutoML搜索需协同优化方达峰值性能；存在"噪声-分辨率权衡"——在SVI超分辨率任务中加入高频AlphaEarth卫星特征反而降低性能（R² 40.1% vs 52.0%）。

Conclusion: PPE通过端到端自主工作流（自然语言查询→数据发现→多模态融合→AutoML→预测）降低了行星尺度分析的技术门槛。实验表明，自主多模态数据发现与针对性模型优化相结合，可在公共卫生、食品安全、社会经济脆弱性与流行病学实时预测等多个领域达到或超越专家级表现。其核心优势源于"数据-模型协同优化"——数据策划与模型搜索的乘性交互是性能提升的主要驱动因素。局限性包括：依赖预训练嵌入（未端到端微调）；高频卫星特征在细粒度上引入噪声；因果方向过滤的正式验证仍是开放问题；流行病学评估仅限单一疫情。后续方向：时空长周期预测、实时流数据集成、前沿检测与机制模型结合的集成架构。

Abstract: Addressing critical global challenges, from food security and disaster risk to disease outbreaks and socio-economic vulnerability, demands high-fidelity geospatial modeling. However, building predictive planetary models remains bottlenecked by a fragmented data ecosystem, requiring manual data retrieval, multimodal data curation and fusion along with iterative model selection. We present the Planetary Prediction Engine (PPE), an autonomous AI system that executes this end-to-end workflow directly from natural-language queries. PPE synthesizes multimodal datasets on the fly, retrieving spatiotemporally relevant covariates across open-web and Earth observation platforms (Data Commons, Google Earth Engine) and fusing them with geospatial foundation model embeddings (PDFM, AlphaEarth). Simultaneously, it searches over task-tailored model architecture families with automated overfitting guards. Across diverse tasks, geographies, and scientific domains, PPE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art or manually tuned expert baselines. For US spatial regression, PPE improves mean $R^2$ across 21 CDC health indicators (76.8% vs. 60.0%), FEMA national risk indices (64.9% vs. 60.0%), and the Social Vulnerability Index (66.2% vs. 58.6%). For spatial downscaling in data-scarce settings, PPE integrates localized proxies to double baseline accuracy in Nigerian food security indicators ($R^2$ of 66.1% vs. 31.5%). For epidemiological nowcasting of the 2026 DRC Bundibugyo Ebola outbreak, PPE achieves a Recall@10 of 83.3% (identifying 15 of 18 newly invaded health zones across five weekly forecasts), a +10.3 percentage-point improvement over the public state-of-the-art modeling (~73%). By combining autonomous multimodal planetary data discovery with targeted model optimization, PPE lowers the technical barrier to planetary-scale analytics, enabling rapid, customized, expert-level deployment.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [Data Citation for Large Language Models: A Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25663)
*Gianmaria Silvello*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文系统论述了"为大型语言模型引用数据"这一尚未解决的学术挑战，将其与已有但有限的文档级引用研究区分开来，并提出三大研究方向：训练数据归因、推理时数据引用、知识图谱事实引用，强调需要数据库、信息检索、知识表示与AI社区协同推进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM引用研究几乎完全聚焦于"验证性"功能（输出是否可被来源支撑）且仅作用于文本文档，忽略了学术引用同等重要的"credit（学术贡献归属）"与"provenance（出处追溯）"功能，更未触及数据层面的引用。随着LLM成为主要信息入口且广泛使用RAG、知识图谱等结构化数据源，如何对训练数据集、检索数据子集、知识图谱三元组进行引用以实现可验证性、可追溯性与公平的贡献归属，成为比文档级引用更困难且社会影响更深远的开放问题。

Method: 本文为立场/挑战论文（position/challenge paper），未提出新算法，而是基于既有工作进行综合分析：梳理学术引用的三大功能（验证、credit、provenance）、回顾数据引用基础设施（FORCE11、DataCite、Scholix、RDA、FAIR、CRediT、Make Data Count）与数据库credit distribution理论（Dosso & Silvello, 间接引用、传递性credit），结合LLM引用忠实度实证研究、KG-LLM神经符号集成研究，归纳出三大相互关联的研究方向及五项协同设计要素。

Result: 论文识别并界定了三个相互关联的研究方向：(1) 训练数据归因——影响力函数、Data Shapley等方法计算昂贵且为近似估计，无法构成真正学术意义的"引用"，且对封闭模型不可行；(2) 推理时数据引用——RAG中结构化数据集缺乏完整元数据，子集粒度与查询结果fixity难以固定，训练语料中良好数据引用样例稀缺导致自强化缺陷；(3) 知识图谱事实引用——单三元组无书目元数据，需结合nanopublications/PROV-O等溯源表示，并通过provenance图上的credit propagation网络问题实现多源贡献分配。论文进一步提出五项协同设计要素：统一引用模型、溯源感知架构、大规模异构credit分布机制、数据级评估基准、扩展现有标准的基础设施。

Conclusion: 数据引用是LLM数据引用中应优先解决且最切实可行的子方向，因数据源在推理时可用且可锚定到可检索的版本化制品或生成查询；RAG是天然切入点。若LLM系统持续缺乏规范的数据引用，将使大规模消费结构化数据而无处归的现象被常态化，且缺口将持续恶化。解决该问题需要DB、IR、KR、AI社区联合开发模型、算法、基准与标准，使知识完整性在推理时仍可追溯。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly mediate access to information, and a growing body of work asks whether they cite the sources behind their outputs. That work treats citation as a verification device and applies it to textual documents. Scholarly citation serves two further functions, credit and provenance, and it applies to data as much as to text. This paper argues that data citation for large language models is an open challenge, distinct from document-level citation grounding and harder to solve. We ask how such models should cite data so that outputs stay verifiable, provenance stays traceable, and credit reaches data creators and curators. We set out three research directions. Training data attribution has to turn influence estimates into references for corpora absorbed into model parameters. Data citation at inference time has to identify datasets, subsets, and query results at the right granularity and fixity. Citing knowledge graph facts has to define what a reference to a single triple denotes and how credit propagates along provenance. Progress on all three depends on joint work across the database, information retrieval, knowledge representation, and artificial intelligence communities.

</details>


### [180] [VisDocAgentBench: Benchmarking Agents for Visually Rich Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17889)
*Lexiang Hu,Yanzhao Zhang,Mingxin Li,Dingkun Long,Yikang Li,Fuwei Zhang,Yisen Wang,Zhouchen Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: VisDocAgentBench 是一个针对视觉丰富文档的封闭式语料基准，统一评估静态检索器与智能体式检索。它包含100篇文档的2,375页和120条均衡分布在直接/单桥/双桥证据结构上的查询，通过关系保持的构建流程、全文审查与困难负样本验证确保质量。实验揭示强静态检索器在直接项上接近完美但在双桥项上几乎失败，而智能体能大幅恢复性能，但规划器选择和模态表征至关重要，视觉检索始终优于OCR文本检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉文档检索评估主要依赖一次性查询-页面匹配，而智能体搜索基准多关注下游问答或报告生成，缺少对迭代证据获取中文档排序的直接评测。视觉丰富文档的相关性依赖于语言、布局、结构化视觉元素以及语料上下文，亟需一个同时评估静态与智能体范式的封闭式基准来揭示表征、搜索与证据整合的瓶颈。

Method: 提出 VisDocAgentBench 基准：采用角色专属描述符（查询锚点、语义桥、视觉目标描述符）与有向关系构建三级证据路径（L1直接、L2单桥、L3双桥），经自动筛选、全文双盲审查、约束化查询撰写与困难负样本验证。统一排序输出契约下评估静态检索器（Qwen3-VL-Embedding、BM25、RRF、Nemotron-ColEmbed 晚期交互）与工具调用智能体（多款闭源大模型与 Qwen3.5 开源模型），覆盖视觉与OCR文本两条路径，并通过消融与支持上下文干预进行诊断。

Result: 强晚期交互检索器在直接项上 Recall@1 达97.50%，但在双桥项上仅2.50%；最佳智能体 Claude Opus 5 视觉路径 R@1 达67.50%、OCR路径37.50%，所有规划器在视觉检索上一致优于OCR文本。消融显示迭代搜索与页面检查是关键能力，移除检查导致R@1下降15.84–21.67点；提供完整支持上下文使视觉L3的R@10提升22.50点。剩余失败集中于目标发现、候选检查与证据角色整合，跨文档路径仍是最大挑战。

Conclusion: VisDocAgentBench 证明静态查询-页面匹配无法解决依赖语料上下文的分布式证据，而智能体能显著恢复性能但受规划器选择与表征模态制约。视觉检索在全局发现上优于OCR文本，页面检查与针对性文本访问提供互补的验证作用。研究呼吁联合发展模态保持的表征与证据导向的搜索策略，并构建能整合放大证据与上下文布局的多模态基础模型。

Abstract: Visually rich documents encode relevance through language, layout, structured visual elements, and corpus context, yet retrieval is typically evaluated by one-shot query--page matching. Agentic-search benchmarks usually score downstream question answering or report generation, leaving document ranking under iterative evidence acquisition underexplored. We introduce VisDocAgentBench, a closed-corpus benchmark comparing static and agentic retrieval under a shared ranked-output contract. It contains 2,375 pages from 100 documents and 120 unique-target queries balanced across direct, one-bridge, and two-bridge evidence structures. Relation-preserving construction yields semantic, relational, and visual queries, followed by full-document review and hard-negative validation. A strong late-interaction visual retriever reaches 97.50% Recall@1 on direct items but 2.50% on two-bridge items, exposing the limits of query--target matching when relevance depends on corpus context. Agents recover much of this loss, but planner choice and retrieval representation remain decisive. Every planner performs better with visual retrieval, whose best R@1 reaches 67.50% versus 37.50% for OCR-text. Ablations identify iterative search and page inspection as consequential capabilities, and providing the complete support context improves ranking on both routes. Trace analysis localizes the remaining losses to target discovery, candidate examination, and evidence-role integration. These findings motivate retrieval agents that combine modality-preserving discovery with evidence-directed verification.

</details>


### [181] [CRAMER: Control via Request-Aware Masking for Editing Recommenders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25370)
*Zhiyuan Julian Su,Naihe Feng,Zhen Luther Qin,Ga Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CRAMER 是一种轻量级框架，通过将用户的自然语言请求映射为结构化的行-列门控掩码，对冻结的 Transformer 序列推荐模型进行后验控制，避免了重训练和 LLM 推理开销，在多个数据集上优于现有请求感知基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列推荐模型难以即时响应用户的自然语言请求，而现有的请求适应方法要么需要重训练整个骨干网络，要么依赖大语言模型的推理能力，计算开销大，难以在大规模推荐服务中部署。

Method: 受模型控制理论启发，CRAMER 将用户请求视为控制信号，通过预训练语言模型编码请求，经线性投影生成门控 logits，利用 Gumbel-Top-k 采样得到 k-hot 二值门控向量，再分解为行-列掩码作用于冻结 Transformer 的 FFN 和 W_O 矩阵；训练目标结合预测损失与 KL 稀疏正则项，使用直通估计器实现离散门控的端到端优化。

Result: 在 ReDial、KuaiSAR、Beauty、CDs&Vinyl 四个数据集上，基于 SASRec 和 BERT4Rec 两种骨干，CRAMER 在 HR@10/20、NDCG@10/20、MRR@10/20 六项指标上均优于 Query-SeqRec、BLaIR、LLM-ESR、REARANK 四个基线，且推理仅增加 0.018 秒运行时开销和约 1355 MiB GPU 内存，远低于 REARANK 的 9.256 秒；案例研究还证明掩码能产生与请求语义一致的可解释推荐分布偏移。

Conclusion: CRAMER 建立了请求感知序列推荐的新范式，通过参数掩码实现高效、可控且可逆的后验控制，无需重训练即可即时融合用户即时意图与长期偏好，具有良好的跨数据集、跨骨干、跨领域适应能力。

Abstract: Sequential recommendation models, while powerful, have limited flexibility in responding to immediate user requests, making it difficult to adapt their recommendations to the user's timely interests. Unfortunately, existing user request adaptation methods often incur high computational overhead due to either 1) retraining the entire backbone network or 2) leveraging the inference ability of large language models (a.k.a. prompt engineering), limiting their applicability in large-scale recommendation services. This paper presents Control via Request-Aware Masking for Editing Recommenders (CRAMER), a framework that takes users' natural-language requests to immediately change sequential recommendation models' behavior. Specifically, inspired by the model control theory, CRAMER treats user requests as control signals to modulate frozen backbone parameters through masking, achieving instant adaptation to diverse requests while avoiding costly retraining. Experiments on multiple large-scale benchmark datasets show that CRAMER outperforms four state-of-the-art request-aware baselines across multiple recommendation metrics while achieving minimal overhead. Moreover, the proposed framework exhibits enhanced controllability and cross-domain adaptability, establishing a new paradigm for request-aware sequential recommendation.

</details>


### [182] [When Stale Constraints Go Unchecked: Budgeted Verification Failures in Inherited Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25553)
*Kazuki Nakayashiki*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究持久记忆智能体在验证预算稀缺时，能否通过预算内的验证策略调整避免使用已被新权威记录取代的陈旧约束所导致的决策错误。结果表明，原生验证分配导致约74-77%的决策与陈旧记忆一致，而将一个验证槽位重新分配到关键溯源路径可在预算不变的情况下将当前记录一致决策提升60-74个百分点，识别出陈旧记忆错误的可避免份额接近其结构性上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久记忆智能体在继承整合记忆时，可能继承写入时为真但已被新记录撤销的约束。当验证预算稀缺时，智能体是否会发现这种撤销，若未发现，该错误是否可在不增加预算的情况下避免。此问题源于此前研究中所有约束均为真时的验证分配测量被刻意留作安全动机案例而未经验证。

Method: 采用2×2×3析因设计，包含记忆形式（stated/removed）、世界状态（valid/superseded）和验证策略（native/forced-critical/forced-noncritical）。验证预算固定为两个记录，forced-critical策略将其中一个槽位重新分配到目标记忆的关键溯源路径。使用六个模型（Claude Opus 5/Sonnet 5/Haiku 4.5和GPT-5.6 Sol/Terra/Luna），共执行5,400个确认性episode（主运行1,800+复现1,800+原始域外测试900+修正后域外鲁棒性复现900），所有规范包均预先SHA256哈希并通过OpenTimestamps锚定，修正后域外测试的完整规范在执行前外部存证。

Result: 在stated形式superseded世界中，原生分配下约77.3%、74.7%、74.7%的决策与陈旧记忆一致（主运行、复现、原始域外）。将一个槽位重新分配到关键路径使当前记录一致决策分别提升+74.0、+72.7、+61.3个百分点，在六个模型中方向均为正（六分之六），三个run均为此结果。修正后域外鲁棒性复现给出+73.3个百分点。陈述约束时智能体仅在约20-23%的episode中选择检查其溯源路径，而移除同一约束时该比例升至67-73%。当记录与记忆一致时，强制关键路径策略几乎不改变决策（+0.7至+2.0个百分点）。估计值在除原始域外外的每个run中都接近其结构性上限100×(1-原生目标核验率)。

Conclusion: 本研究识别出陈旧继承记忆错误的绝大部分可归因于验证分配而非推理缺陷。在约束被supersede时，验证预算稀缺导致的错误接近其结构性上限，且通过预算内重新分配一个槽位即可基本消除。生产者记忆系统可能需要与语义相关性分离的新鲜度或supersession信号，因为相关性恰恰是将智能体引向陈旧记忆、远离其来源的机制。干预策略使用实验者对关键路径的知识，并非可部署的调度器；该研究测量了可避免错误份额及其大小，但未识别原生分配的内部机制，也未评估自然整合链中陈旧约束的出现频率。

Abstract: An agent that inherits a consolidated memory may inherit a constraint that was true when written and has since been withdrawn by a newer authoritative record. Under a scarce verification budget, does the agent recover the withdrawal, and if not, is the error avoidable without spending more? We model supersession explicitly -- historical provenance is immutable; what changes is which record is current -- and assign by design the memory's form, the world's state (source current or superseded), and the verification policy at a fixed budget of two records: the agent's own allocation, or the same budget with one slot re-assigned to the critical provenance path or to a random record. With a constraint stated, agents inspected its provenance path in about one episode in five; when that constraint had been superseded, native allocation produced stale-consistent decisions in 77.3%, 74.7% and 74.7% of episodes across a primary run, a fresh-wording replication and a held-out domain. Re-assigning one slot to the critical path raised current-record-consistent decisions by +74.0, +72.7 and +61.3 points, positive in six of six models in each of those runs, and changed nothing when the record agreed with the memory. The held-out scenario was later found to contain a temporal inconsistency; a robustness replication with one sentence corrected, deposited externally before execution, gave +73.3 points and is reported alongside the original. The intervention uses knowledge of the critical path and is not a scheduler; it identifies that the share of stale-memory error attributable to verification allocation is close to its structural ceiling. Memory systems may need freshness or supersession signals separate from relevance.

</details>


### [183] [The "Curse of Knowledge" in LLM Query Simulation: Concept Provenance for Tracing Answer-Side Intrusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25245)
*Chenglong Ma,Xinye Wanyan,Danula Hettiachchi,Ziqi Xu,Jeffrey Chan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM生成的搜索查询中可能"泄露"答案侧文档概念的问题，提出"概念溯源（concept provenance）"框架，将查询中的概念划分为背story支持、人类中心、人类尾部、候选答案侧四个区域，从而将罕见的合理人类用词变异与边界违反的答案侧概念入侵区分开。在UQV100上对77,004条查询（8个LLM、5种提示条件）进行实证，发现候选答案侧概念占非通用概念的7.40%，出现在97/100个主题中，但仅解释不到2%的聚合评估方差；删除该类概念产生中等局部检索效应（d=-0.47）。提示工程无法消除入侵，但生成后基于概念溯源的选择可实现99%的消除率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被广泛用于生成搜索查询以补充IR评估，但其查询可能包含预设答案侧文档知识的概念，违反前搜索用户的信息访问边界。现有验证指标（重叠度、多样性、有效性）无法区分稀有的人类尾部用词变异与候选答案侧概念入侵。缺乏这一区分，合成IR评估就无法诊断系统排序变化是由答案侧入侵还是合理用词变异引起。

Method: 提出"概念溯源"框架，将查询概念按优先级分配到四个溯源区域：Z0（背story支持）、Z1（人类中心，工人使用率≥10%）、Z2（人类尾部，工人使用率>0但<10%）、Z3_auto（候选答案侧：人类未使用+非背story支持+文档显著+非通用）。定义隐藏概念入侵率（HCIR）作为度量。在UQV100上使用8个LLM、5种提示条件（generic/high-knowledge/boundary-constrained/oracle-text/oracle-terms）生成77,004条查询，采用两套概念提取流水线（词汇统计与实体短语）。实验包括：(1)溯源区域分布与稳健性（双流水线、阈值敏感性、人工标注）；(2)评估偏移回归分析；(3)删除/注入诊断探针；(4)提示缓解与生成后溯源约束选择。检索系统包括BM25、BM25+RM3、QL及交叉编码器重排序。

Result: (1)候选答案侧概念占非通用概念的7.40%，出现在97/100主题中；主题解释约67%的概念-HCIR方差，条件和模型各贡献<1%。(2)条件梯度：high-knowledge(5.60%) < boundary(6.28%) < generic(6.84%) < oracle-text(8.02%) < oracle-terms(10.16%)。(3)跨流水线token-HCIR Spearman ρ=1.0。(4)人工验证宽松精度68.2%，揭示双重机制：知识入侵45.5% + 部署入侵45.0%。(5)LLM评估存在知识投射偏差，人机一致性仅κ≈0.36。(6)BM25评估中判断率从人类的0.91降至high-knowledge的0.47，但概念-HCIR仅解释<2%的聚合方差。(7)删除Z3_auto概念产生d=-0.47的局部nDCG下降，超过随机删除(d=-0.34)和IDF匹配替换(d=-0.21)。(8)提示工程无法消除入侵；基于溯源的生成后选择实现99/100主题的零入侵，附加人类中心率质量门槛可恢复大部分效用损失。

Conclusion: 概念溯源是一种边界合规诊断工具而非评估偏移预测器。它能识别哪些查询违反了信息访问边界，但不能预测系统性评估扭曲。LLM生成的初始查询确实存在候选答案侧概念入侵现象（兼具知识入侵与部署入侵双重机制），但其密度并非聚合评估失真的主要驱动因素——词汇分歧与池覆盖失配才是主因。提示工程只能减少而无法消除入侵；过生成加溯源约束选择是更有效的部署路径，配合人类中心率质量门槛可在边界合规与查询效用间取得较优权衡。该框架是对现有多样性/有效性指标的有益补充。未来工作需扩展到具有足够主题级人类变异密度的其他集合以及会话级查询模拟。

Abstract: LLM-generated search queries are widely used to augment IR evaluation, yet they may contain concepts that presuppose answer-side document knowledge, violating the information-access boundary of pre-search users. Existing validation metrics, including overlap, diversity, and effectiveness, cannot distinguish rare human-tail variation from candidate answer-side intrusion. We introduce concept provenance, a framework that assigns query concepts to backstory-supported, human-central, human-tail, and candidate answer-side zones, operationalizing a boundary that retrieval metrics alone cannot detect. Applying concept provenance to 77,004 queries across 100 UQV100 topics, 8 LLMs, and 5 prompt conditions with two extraction pipelines, we obtain a cross-pipeline token-HCIR Spearman rho of 1.0 over five condition means. Candidate answer-side concepts constitute 7.40 percent of non-generic concepts and appear in 97 of 100 topics, with topic explaining approximately 67 percent of variance. Human validation yields 68.2 percent relaxed precision, revealing two mechanisms: knowledge intrusion at 45.5 percent and deployment intrusion at 45.0 percent. Diagnostic probes show disproportionate localized retrieval effects, with deletion effect size d = -0.47 compared with d = -0.34 for random deletion, but these concepts explain less than 2 percent of aggregate evaluation variance. Concept provenance therefore serves as a boundary-compliance diagnostic rather than an evaluation-shift predictor. Under the tested conditions, no prompt condition eliminates intrusion; post-generation concept-provenance selection achieves 99 percent elimination.

</details>


### [184] [PlanSightRAG: A Visual-First Multimodal RAG for Automating Question Answering and Compliance Checking for Civil Standard Plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26091)
*Nabaraj Subedi,Shuvo Dip Datta,Ahmed Abdelaty,Shivanand Venkanna Sheshappanavar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PlanSightRAG是一个面向土木工程标准图纸的视觉优先多模态RAG框架。它绕过了OCR,通过ColNomic-3B多向量检索、代理式Planner-Retriever-Auditor-Synthesizer流程和MaxSim热力图,实现对2D工程图的问答和合规检查。该框架在五州交通部4056对基准上达到91.47% Recall@5,且在合规检查CAD合成图上获得100%裁决准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有土木基础设施合规检查依赖工程师人工审阅2D图纸,效率低且易出错。OCR驱动的自动化方法在文字化过程中会丢失工程图关键的几何、布局和符号信息,导致检索性能急剧下降,亟需保留视觉语义的检索与推理框架。

Method: 提出四阶段视觉优先多模态RAG架构:(1)将PDF以200/400 DPI光栅化为图像,使用1024×1024滑动窗口分块;(2)用ColNomic-3B对每块生成32×32多向量patch嵌入;(3)用ColBERT风格MaxSim晚期交互检索,生成锐化热力图作为证据链;(4)下游VQA用Qwen2.5-VL多模型,合规检查采用代理式Planner-Retriever-Auditor-Synthesizer管道。额外引入BM25混合融合和VLM交叉编码器重排序,并支持自主规则定锚(无需人工注入阈值)。

Result: 在4056对五州DOT基准上ColNomic-3B达到91.47% Recall@5(零样本),较最强文本检索器BGE-M3+OCR(36.79%)提升55.9个百分点。在424对测试分割上达92.69% Recall@5。在未见过的Michigan DOT语料上零样本迁移达91.40%。合规检查CAD生成图上,Qwen2.5-VL-72B在预解析阈值下达100%裁决准确率(500单图+100多图),而非VLM OCR基线仅76.4%。自主规则定锚在1,913候选中R@5达100%,端到端裁决准确率100%。但真实931页WYDOT规范上阈值提取降至33%。LoRA微调对检索无提升,full-LM LoRA反而严重退化。H4高分辨率平铺仅带来+4.53 pp法官准确率提升,未达20 pp阈值。

Conclusion: 视觉优先检索显著优于OCR流水线,多向量patch级嵌入对工程图的细粒度细节必不可少。代理式分解、视觉定锚和预解析规则阈值是合规检查可扩展基础。瓶颈已从检索覆盖转向细粒度视觉阅读、实体-尺寸绑定和规则解析。下一步需解决符号替换稳健性、跨机构适配以及从密集真实规范中自主提取阈值的能力。

Abstract: Civil infrastructure compliance checking has long relied on engineers manually reading legacy 2D plans; however, OCR-based automation strips away the geometry and layout essential for interpreting these plans. We present a Visual-First Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) framework called PlanSightRAG. It indexes and reasons directly over plan imagery, integrates a ColNomic-3B multi-vector retrieval, an agentic Planner-Retriever-Auditor-Synthesizer, and MaxSim heatmaps as an evidence trail. We introduce a 4,056-pair benchmark from five state Departments of Transportation (DOT) standard plans (1,898 pages). PlanSightRAG achieves 91.47% Recall@5 on zero-shot retrieval, while on a held-out Michigan DOT corpus, it achieves 91.40%. On synthetic, parametrically-generated compliance drawings, our Qwen2.5-VL-72B pipeline reaches 100% verdict accuracy only when supplied a pre-resolved rule threshold, a controlled ceiling that a non-VLM OCR baseline already reaches at 76.4%. Finally, we demonstrate autonomous visual rule-grounding by extracting numeric limits directly from a specification corpus without any human-supplied rules.

</details>


### [185] [SWIM: Step-Wise Integrated Measure for Session-supervised List Evaluation in Generative Re-ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25104)
*Yuanhao Pu,Chenghao Zhang,Chao Feng,Xunyong Yang,Xiang Li,Yongqi Liu,Defu Lian,Kaiqiao Zhan,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SWIM is a list-level evaluator for the Generator-Evaluator (G-E) re-ranking framework in industrial recommenders (e.g., short-video apps). It models user browsing as a finite-horizon prefix session-level survival process, factorizing a candidate list's session-level contribution into a recursive survival distribution (probability of reaching each position) and reached-position conditional rewards. Implemented with a causal-masked Transformer for parallel estimation, SWIM addresses the limitation of conventional request-level evaluators that ignore session continuity, and significantly outperforms baselines in offline and online A/B tests on Kuaishou.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Conventional re-ranking evaluators (e.g., CAVE) score candidate lists by aggregating point-wise utilities at the request level, implicitly assuming exposure independence across items and requests. On sequential consumption platforms (e.g., short-video feeds), users browse boundary-less sessions where continuation probability and marginal utility depend on historical context, accumulated engagement, and repetitive content. This mismatch between request-level inference and session-level supervision leads to suboptimal list evaluation, motivating a principled session-aware evaluator.

Method: SWIM formalizes the session-level objective as the expected cumulative value before session termination. For a candidate list at request k, it defines: (1) a boundary transition probability q_{k,0} = P(request k reached | session prefix), and (2) step-wise in-list survival probabilities q_{k,t} = P(continue beyond position t | reached t). The list value is computed as V_k = Σ_t (∏_{i< t} q_{k,i}) · r_{k,t}, where r_{k,t} is the conditional expected reward (e.g., click, like, bucketized play time) at each reached position. Architecture: a causally-masked Transformer with a prefix token at position 0 and item tokens at positions 1..T. The prefix token encodes session history via a feature encoder; item tokens are projected from item embeddings, prefix representation, positional embeddings, and request context. Causal attention ensures each position only sees its prefix. Two output heads: (a) a shared sigmoid head predicting survival probabilities q_{k,0:T}, and (b) task-specific heads for binary engagements (BCE loss) and discretized play time (softmax over B buckets, CE loss). Survival supervision uses a prefix-conditioned BCE loss valid only at reached positions. Training objective: L = L_prob + L_rwd (no listwise regression loss needed). Online inference sets q_{k,0} = 1 (request already triggered) and selects the list maximizing V_k. Theoretical analysis proves: (Theorem 5.1) request-only evaluators have irreducible error proportional to Var(q_0* | c), showing SWIM's session-prefix advantage; (Theorem 5.2) the discrete survival parameterization avoids the structural approximation bias of Weibull-restricted priors (as in CAVE) and can represent any valid finite-horizon survival profile.

Result: Offline experiments on RecFlow and KuaiRand datasets (Table 3): SWIM achieves the best NDCG@6 and AUC across all baselines, including DNN, Seq2Slate, DLCM, SetRank, PRM, SORT-Gen, NAR4Rec, PIER, MultiG, and CAVE. On RecFlow, SWIM reaches NDCG 0.2031 and AUC 0.7185, outperforming CAVE (0.1957 / 0.7083) and MultiG (0.1911 / 0.7024). Ablation study (Table 4): removing prefix features, boundary transition q_{k,0}, or the survival chain each degrades performance, confirming the necessity of all components. Online A/B test on Kuaishou (5% traffic, 7 days, >400M DAU, Table 5): SWIM vs. CAVE achieves +0.351% app stay time (CI [0.27%, 0.42%]) and +0.048% 7-day retention (CI [0.01%, 0.09%]), both statistically significant.

Conclusion: SWIM provides a principled, session-aware evaluator for generative re-ranking that explicitly captures user survival dynamics and conditional rewards. The boundary-augmented survival decomposition offers both a representation advantage over request-only evaluators (Theorem 5.1) and greater expressiveness than Weibull-restricted survival priors (Theorem 5.2). The causally-masked Transformer enables parallel estimation satisfying industrial latency constraints. Both offline benchmarks and large-scale online deployment validate its effectiveness. Future directions include counterfactual/off-policy evaluation in generative list spaces and extending session-prefix modeling to longer, more heterogeneous browsing trajectories.

Abstract: Modern industrial recommender systems have increasingly adopted the Generator-Evaluator (G-E) framework for the re-ranking stage. Within this paradigm, the generator produces candidate item lists from a pool filtered by upstream retrieval and ranking modules, while the evaluator scores these lists and selects the highest-scoring one for final exposure per request. However, on sequential platforms (e.g., short-video apps), users consume items continuously, ignoring artificial list boundaries. Conventional evaluators score lists by aggregating point-wise values, implicitly assuming exposure independence. This fails to capture critical session-level dynamics, such as contextual dependencies, user continuation, and diminishing marginal utility from repetitive content. To bridge this gap, we propose SWIM (Step-Wise Integrated Measure), a list-level evaluator that models user behaviors as a finite-horizon prefix session-level survival process. SWIM estimates the prefix-conditioned contribution of the current list to the session-level objective by factorizing it into a recursive survival distribution and reached-position conditional rewards. Leveraging a causally-masked Transformer, SWIM efficiently estimates continuation probabilities and utilities in parallel, satisfying strict industrial latency constraints. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SWIM significantly outperforms baselines in listwise reranking tasks, yielding substantial improvements in overall recommendation engagement.

</details>


### [186] [Rank-Deviation Quality: A Distance-Aware Metric for Multi-Answer Retrieval and Ranking Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25318)
*Xiaokun Zhou,Alessandro Moschitti,Danielle Class*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的检索/排序评估指标RDQ（Rank-Deviation Quality），用于评估多答案场景下的系统排序质量。RDQ基于有序参考列表（ORL），结合输出位置权重和秩偏差惩罚进行打分，无需绝对相关性等级标注。在5,000查询POI数据集和TREC-DL基准上表现出较高的统计区分能力和排名稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估指标在多答案检索场景下存在不足：二值指标无法区分参考项之间的质量差异；NDCG等分级指标需要绝对相关性等级，跨查询难以校准；Kendall τ等秩相关系数要求两个排序覆盖相同的项目集，无法处理检索错误；CMP等偏好指标虽支持序数参考但基于前缀重叠。核心问题是：当多个项目均可接受时，质量既取决于返回了哪些项目，也取决于它们的相对顺序，而没有明确定义的绝对相关性尺度。

Method: RDQ核心定义：给定有序参考列表（ORL，将参考项映射到1至Tq的连续整数秩，支持并列层级）和截断至k的候选排序，得分公式为各检索到的参考项的输出位置权重μᵢ与秩偏差惩罚ρ的乘积之和，再除以理想排序得分总和。惩罚函数有两种：M₁为非对称比率惩罚ρ=min(1, γ★ᵢ/γ(pᵢ))，仅惩罚将低质量项排在高质量项之上；M₂为平衡距离感知惩罚ρ=exp(-d/√|Rq|·α·(1+λ(γ(pᵢ)-1)))，其中d=|γ★ᵢ-γ(pᵢ)|，α控制全局容差，λ控制容差随参考秩的变化，√|Rq|项使固定位移对短ORL惩罚更重。ORL可通过列表式标注、成对偏好聚合（Bradley-Terry等）或分级相关性转换获得。输出位置权重μᵢ可反映UI布局（如垂直列表或轮播）。

Result: （1）5,000查询POI数据集（12系统，66对）：RDQ_m2-a1@5的中位power@100达0.353，为13种配置中最高，比RBP(0.9)的0.287高23%；中位N₈₀为350，小于RBP(0.9)的450。排名稳定性方面，RDQ_m2-a1@5以200查询达到mean τ≥0.8，优于RBP(0.9)的250查询；AUCτ达0.904，为所有配置最高。在≤3参考项的子集上，RDQ是唯一具有非零中位power的指标族。（2）M₂参数敏感性分析显示，α∈[1,4]、λ=0.2附近效果最佳；25个配置中有16个超过RBP(0.9)。（3）TREC-DL 2019–2022（合并221查询，4系统，6对）：n=25时RDQ_m1@10中位power为0.108，与原生NDCG@10的0.102相近；n=100时NDCG更高（0.437 vs 0.367）。RDQ与基线指标在2021–2022年相关性较强（Pearson r 0.81–1.0），2020年最弱（RDQ_m1与CMP完全反向排序）。

Conclusion: RDQ填补了多答案检索评估的空白，兼具区分能力和排序稳定性。当存在校准的分级判断时，NDCG仍是强选择；当评估应模拟用户浏览行为时，RBP等持久性指标更合适；RDQ面向序数参考的设置。M₂配合α∈[1,4]和λ=0.2适用于几乎唯一秩的参考；当参考含大并列层级时，M₁更优。局限包括：参数需人工设定、跨格式比较需转换选择、TREC-DL系统池小、POI标签为GenAI辅助的银标准、ORL完整性假设、与人类偏好的一致性尚需验证。

Abstract: We introduce Rank-Deviation Quality (RDQ), an evaluation metric for retrieval and ranking systems that adapts to queries with varying numbers of reference items, from a single correct answer to many valid results. RDQ scores a candidate ranking against an ordered reference list (ORL): each retrieved reference item contributes its output-position weight multiplied by a rank-deviation penalty, and items outside the ORL receive zero credit. Application-specific parameters control tolerance to misordering. Larger values emphasize retrieving valid reference items, whereas smaller values place more weight on matching their reference order. The output-position weights can reflect visibility in the application's interface, such as a vertical list or a carousel. Unlike metrics that require absolute relevance grades, RDQ operates on ordinal rankings, which annotators can produce through pairwise or listwise judgments. Unlike rank-correlation measures such as Kendall's tau, RDQ accounts for both which items are returned and how they are ordered. On a 5,000-query point-of-interest (POI) dataset with 12 systems, RDQ has the highest median empirical power@100 among the 13 evaluated metric configurations. It reaches mean tau >= 0.8 agreement with its own full-query ordering at 200 queries; RBP(0.9), the strongest tested non-RDQ configuration, reaches the same threshold at 250. On TREC Deep Learning benchmarks, where NDCG uses native graded labels and RDQ uses ordinal tiers derived from them, RDQ reaches comparable median power at n=25, while NDCG is higher at n=100.

</details>


### [187] [MOTIF: Motivation-guided Topology Inference for Cold-start Multimodal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25381)
*Yurui Shi,Yuchen Miao,Ximing Hu,Zijun Wang,Chang Han*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出MOTIF框架,通过LLM离线推理用户与物品的动机语义,重建物品-物品拓扑结构,并结合加权图对比学习与语义-结构对齐,实现冷启动多模态推荐。在三个基准数据集上较最强基线提升达6.07%,在冷启动场景下提升超过20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷启动多模态推荐面临三大耦合挑战:稀疏交互掩盖用户意图、冷物品拓扑孤立、基于相似度的物品图导致语义漂移。现有方法单独处理语义建模、图重建与表征鲁棒性,无法联合应对上述问题,亟需一种将LLM语义推理转化为图拓扑结构的统一框架。

Method: MOTIF包含四大模块:1) 语义动机推理——离线LLM从多模态内容推断物品功能/场景/受众与用户意图,编码为语义向量;2) 知识增强图重建——融合原始多模态相似度、LLM动机语义与LLM关系先验(互补/替代/共享场景),构建加权物品图,保留Top-K邻居;3) 加权图对比学习——仅对最后一层施加扰动,经SE挤压-激励网络逐维门控重校准;4) 语义-结构对齐——将LLM语义向量与图嵌入映射至共享空间做对比对齐。预测阶段仅使用图嵌入,避免在线LLM调用,联合损失为BPR+GCL+对齐+L2正则。

Result: 在Amazon-Baby、Amazon-Sports、MicroLens-50K三个数据集上,MOTIF在R@20和N@20上全面优于图协同过滤、图对比学习、多模态推荐、冷启动与LLM增强五类基线,总体相对提升4.92%–6.07%。在极冷用户、冷用户、冷物品场景下提升尤为显著,分别达约20%、11%、26%。消融实验验证了LLM推理、图重建、加权对比学习、SE重校准与对齐模块各自贡献,且直接融合LLM语义会损害性能。图质量诊断显示重建图将孤立冷物品比例从0.412降至0.017,平均冷度从0.7升至19.8,在线推理时延与轻量图模型相当。

Conclusion: MOTIF将LLM衍生知识转化为图结构与表征监督信号,优于直接语义融合;预测仅依赖图嵌入避免在线LLM开销,为冷启动多模态推荐提供了语义-拓扑协同重建的有效范式。未来工作将探索自适应图先验与更高效的语义推理。

Abstract: Cold-start multimodal recommendation faces three coupled challenges: (i) sparse interactions obscure user intent, (ii) cold items remain topologically isolated, and (iii) similarity-based item graphs may cause semantic drift. To address these issues, we propose MOTIF, a Motivation-guided Topology Inference framework for cold-start multimodal recommendation. MOTIF integrates Semantic Motivation Reasoning, Knowledge-enhanced Graph Reconstruction, Weighted Graph Contrastive Learning, and Semantic-Structural Alignment. It uses offline LLM reasoning to infer motivation semantics, reconstructs transferable item-item topology, and learns robust graph embeddings without injecting generated text into prediction. Experiments on three multimodal benchmarks show consistent gains over graph-based, multimodal, cold-start, and LLM-enhanced baselines, with up to 6.07% relative improvement over the strongest recent baseline.

</details>


### [188] [DocPC: Document-Level Visual Retrieval via Representative Page Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25434)
*Chengsong You,Junwei Zhou,Nan Du*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出DocPC，一种面向文档级视觉检索的框架，通过选取代表性页面并拼接为网格图像，将每篇文档的索引从O(N)压缩至O(1)，并构建了首个带多正例标注的文档级视觉检索基准DocViRe。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉文档检索以页面为单位，与企业搜索、法律审查等需要检索完整文档的真实场景脱节；朴素"页→文档"聚合存在索引成本线性增长与跨页相关性捕获能力不足的双重缺陷，且文档级检索天然存在多正例监督问题。

Method: 代表性页面组合（Representative Page Composition）：从多页文档中选取K个代表页面（默认First-4），拼接成2×2网格图像，经单一VLM前向传播得到多向量文档表示。训练采用多正例InfoNCE对比损失与基于ApproxNDCG的Top-K列表式损失的稀疏组合（每m=40步施加一次列表式损失）。同时构建DocViRe基准，涵盖7个领域，含LLM生成+三人独立人工审核的多正例标注。

Result: DocPC-ColQwen在DocViRe上取得44.09%的平均NDCG@5，优于最强页面级基线（38.91%），同时将索引图像、向量及存储减少约10.1倍，端到端索引时间减少约7.7倍；在所有7个领域上均优于独立的同页面MaxP聚合（最大提升来自工业+21.08与教育+12.00）。

Conclusion: 文档级网格组合在相同监督下比页面级MaxP聚合更有效，且显著降低索引成本；稀疏调度的列表式损失是结合多正例对比学习与排序优化的有效方式。未来工作包括查询感知的页面选择、混合网格检索+页面重排，以及覆盖更广语言与下游RAG场景。

Abstract: Visual document retrieval has advanced by encoding page screenshots with vision-language models, bypassing OCR pipelines. However, existing methods remain page-centric, misaligned with real-world scenarios requiring complete document retrieval. A naive page-then-document aggregation suffers from linear indexing cost and degraded retrieval when relevance spans multiple pages. We propose DocPC, a document-level visual retrieval framework based on Representative Page Composition: selecting representative pages and composing them into a single grid image for document-level indexing, reducing indexed images, vectors, and storage by 10.1x and end-to-end indexing time by roughly 7.7x. To handle multi-positive supervision prevalent at the document level, we combine multi-positive contrastive learning with sparsely scheduled listwise optimization. We also introduce DocViRe, a benchmark with multi-positive relevance annotations. DocPC-ColQwen achieves NDCG@5 of 44.09 on DocViRe, outperforming the strongest page-level baseline at 38.91 while reducing storage by 10.1x. Code is available at this https URL . Data is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [189] [Query Expansion Is More Than Generation: Improving Dense Retrieval through Better Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25521)
*Siyuan Sun,Mihai Surdeanu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 AnchorQE 及其在线变体 SC-AnchorQE，揭示了 LLM 查询扩展在冻结密集检索器中失效的关键原因不在于生成内容，而在于集成方式。通过对查询和扩展分别编码后做向量插值，并利用无标签流前缀估计插值因子，无需训练即可在多个基准上稳定提升检索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 查询扩展方法常使冻结密集检索器性能下降；以往工作多关注"生成什么"，却忽视"如何集成"这一被低估的变量，使得生成质量与集成方式的影响相互混淆。

Method: 1) 固定生成、固定检索器的对照协议，隔离集成方法这一变量；2) AnchorQE：对原查询 q 与扩展 z 分别用 E_q 编码并归一化后做线性插值 q̂_α = normalize((1−α)q + αz)；3) 证明其与加权 CombSUM 排序等价，但只需一次索引请求；4) SC-AnchorQE：从流前 B=8 个无标签查询计算 r_top1 与 r_sup，取合取乘积 α_stream = r_top1·r_sup 作为冻结的插值因子；5) 给出角度边界定理界定锚定对扩展影响的极限。

Result: 在 TREC-DL、LoTTE、BEIR 上，固定扩展诊断显示 AnchorQE(α=.15) 在 20/20 的 QE 策略-基准对比中超越 published 集成方式，相对提升最高 12.89%；SC-AnchorQE 在 19/20 对比中超越无 QE 的 DR 基线，并相对开发集调优的固定 AnchorQE 最高再提升 3.81%；3 生成器 × 3 检索器 = 9 配置中 44/45 优于 DR 基线、41/45 优于固定 AnchorQE；推理时仅需 1 次索引请求，延迟 .19ms（vs CombSUM 4.33ms）。

Conclusion: 查询扩展的有效性在很大程度上取决于集成方式而非生成内容；通过在向量层面对查询进行显式锚定并以无监督方式估计信任因子，可在不重训、不重建索引的前提下稳定提升零样本 LLM-QE 的检索性能，且具有跨生成器、跨检索器的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate query expansions without task-specific training, yet the same expansions often make a frozen dense retriever worse. We identify an underexplored factor: prior work has often focused on what text is generated, while how generated text is incorporated into dense retrievers has received less systematic attention. By holding generated expansions fixed, we show that performance degradation can often be attributed to the integration method itself. We introduce AnchorQE, a training-free method that separately encodes the original query and its expansion before interpolating them. The interpolation factor is estimated using an unsupervised online strategy that operates over a small part of the unlabeled test stream. Intuitively, our strategy assigns high expansion trust only when expansions are both retrieval-strong and consistent with the original query's retrieved evidence. We show that AnchorQE improves retrieval effectiveness by up to 12.89% when compared to widely-used expansion-only or text-level concatenation baselines across TREC-DL, LoTTE, and BEIR. Further, we show that our online strategy to estimate the interpolation factor outperforms a fixed weight tuned on a development partition by up to 3.81%.

</details>


### [190] [TransRetrieval: Scaling Up Transformer-Based Retrieval for Industrial Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25528)
*Zhifei Zheng,Yunfei Liu,Bin Liu,Qiren Zhu,Hanbing Liu,Ziru Xu,Han Zhu,Jian Xu,Qi Qi,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TransRetrieval 是一个面向工业级推荐检索场景的 Transformer 框架,通过加权平均聚合、目标 token 压缩和位置式域嵌入三大设计,解决了异构特征的 token 范数发散问题,在严苛延迟约束下实现了可预测的对数线性扩展,在阿里展示广告平台带来 2.53% 的收入提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统的扩展法则长期未能像 NLP 那样成立,根本原因是特征异构性导致 token 范数严重失衡;此外检索阶段面临 10^8 量级候选池与严格延迟约束,以及多域训练带来的成本倍增问题,均阻碍了 Transformer 在工业检索中的规模化部署。

Method: 三项核心设计:(1) 加权平均聚合——以 Σv_i 为分母同时抵消特征基数与权重异质性,恢复 Transformer 所需的同质 token 假设;(2) 目标 token 压缩——用 3 层 MLP 将全部目标侧特征压缩为单个 D 维 token,削减 85% 每候选 FLOPs,释放的预算用于加深加宽模型;(3) 位置式域嵌入——以元素加方式注入域信息,零额外 FLOPs 实现跨域参数共享。系统层面通过共享用户侧 KV 缓存、GPU 端图内检索和算子融合将推理延迟降低约 11 倍。

Result: 在 40 亿交互工业数据集与公开 KuaiRand 基准上,计算量从 0.1 扩至 2 MFLOPs/target,Recall@2000 分别提升 +19.3 pt 与 +22.2 pt,呈高拟合度的对数线性关系 (R²=0.82/0.88);在阿里展示广告 5% 流量的 A/B 测试中,平台收入提升 2.53%,RPM 提升 1.28%,P99 延迟与生产基线持平。

Conclusion: 加权平均聚合以零参数一行修改解决了异构 token 范数问题;压缩并非损失而是再分配,把节省的 FLOPs 投入到更宽更深的 backbone 显著优于堆叠更多 token;位置式域嵌入使稀疏域真正从跨域数据共享中受益,多域联合训练成为扩展资产而非成本。三项设计首次系统验证了检索阶段的扩展法则。

Abstract: Applying scaling laws to recommendation retrieval is hindered by feature heterogeneity: naively stacking Transformer layers yields diminishing returns because heterogeneous fields produce severe token-norm divergence. We present TransRetrieval, a Transformer-based retrieval framework that scales with both computational budget and cross-domain data. The key enabler is (1) weighted average aggregation, which restores the homogeneous-token assumption Transformers rely on. Building on this, we introduce (2) target token compression that cuts per-candidate FLOPs by 85% while preserving cross-attention expressiveness, and (3) position-style domain embeddings that unify multiple domains at negligible additional cost, turning cross-domain data into a scaling asset. On a 40-billion-interaction industrial dataset and the public KuaiRand benchmark, scaling compute from 0.1 to 2 MFLOPs per target yields +19.3/+22.2 pt Recall@2000, confirming robust log-linear scaling. In online A/B tests, TransRetrieval lifts platform revenue by 2.53% under the same end-to-end latency constraint as the production baseline.

</details>


### [191] [An Event is Worth One Token: Event Tokenization for Industrial-scale LLM Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25546)
*Fan Xia,Zhaoheng Zheng,Iman Setayesh,Ruogu Lin,Yiqin Pan,Samarth Mittal,Wentao Bao,Vinti Pandey,Sachin Patil,Jianpeng Cheng,Jun Xiao,Zhuang Wang,Xiangjun Fan,Sri Reddy,Minghai Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出AMBER框架，将每个用户交互事件压缩为单个紧凑的"Event Token"，以解决工业级LLM推荐系统中历史序列快照分辨率低导致的性能瓶颈。核心贡献包括：识别出"snapshot resolution"作为新的可扩展维度，通过异步tokenization与缓存机制解耦快照分辨率与在线服务计算开销，并在工业级排序与召回基准上推进了计算-质量Pareto前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推荐系统在序列建模中，每个位置仅编码文本、语义ID或少量类别特征，丢弃了用户、物品、上下文和结果等丰富的时序快照信号。在自回归建模下，这种弱查询表征会导致性能退化的逐位置累积。工业推荐系统受限于服务计算开销，需在序列长度与每事件快照分辨率之间做严格权衡：传统pointwise模型保留当前查询的丰富异构特征但稀疏表示历史，而自回归模型擅长序列建模但被迫将详细历史事件简化为语义ID或手工特征子集。

Method: AMBER由两个核心组件组成：(1) Event Tokenizer g_θ：通过特征编码（稀疏类别/嵌入/密集数值特征的独立映射）、交互与摘要（c个可学习[CLS]令牌经L层双向Transformer编码器处理）、LLM空间投影（MLP映射为d_z维Event Token）三步压缩异构事件特征；采用统一tokenizer配合动态特征掩码支持多角色输入。(2) User LLM f_ψ：消费Event Token进行自回归预测，初始化自1B参数Llama。训练采用三阶段：预对齐（冻结LLM训练Tokenizer）、联合训练（端到端优化）、循环训练（周期性重训）。序列设计：检索任务每事件1个token，排序任务每事件2个token（交错上下文与标签token），分别采用InfoNCE和BCE损失。服务架构采用异步上游计算与缓存机制。针对循环训练表征漂移问题，提出对抗正则化（DANN）+ EMA更新的组合策略，配合分片循环训练。存储压缩采用Matryoshka Dropout和量化感知训练（INT4服务，8×存储缩减）。

Result: 在工业级排序任务上，AMBER相比Incumbent (fair)降低NE 0.40%，相比Semantic IDs+CU embeddings提升1.60%；与Incumbent混合后在10分钟/1分钟特征延迟下分别带来0.10%/0.16%的Ensemble NE提升。检索任务上Soft Recall在所有checkpoint age下保持0.31%-0.51%的提升。特征组消融显示物品特征贡献最大（移除后NE退化6.65%），Semantic IDs单独信息有限（需补充CU embeddings和稀疏特征）。统一tokenizer在四token/二token schema下均带来0.02%正向迁移。架构消融确认四层BiTransformer为最佳性价比选择，与DHEN(×12)相当。规模化分析揭示关键发现：在仅考虑训练FLOPs时User LLM扩展最优，但纳入服务FLOPs后Event Tokenizer和snapshot resolution扩展更优。与16层LLM联合训练的token在下游扩展中表现更强。漂移缓解评估显示DANN在20天checkpoint间隔下对齐效果最优（cos_mean=0.956, knn_acc=0.856），NE相对无正则化改善0.02%。大规模在线评估中，Event Tokens作为现有非LLM排序器的即插即用特征，在Facebook全流量场景下带来统计显著的0.06% NE改善。

Conclusion: AMBER验证了"snapshot resolution"作为工业推荐系统有效可扩展维度的重要性，通过事件级token化将异构特征融入事件序列，通过异步tokenization实现计算高效扩展，并通过对抗领域自适应训练缓解表征漂移。三大主要优势：(1) 与LLM的端到端联合训练支持高效下游扩展；(2) 优化总训练与服务FLOPs而非组件级训练FLOPs对系统级扩展决策至关重要；(3) 领域对抗训练结合EMA更新缓解循环服务中的表征漂移。未来工作方向包括：采用MoE扩展Event Tokenizer容量、维持高分辨率短期序列以按需tokenization未缓存事件、以及将Event Tokens与文本联合建模以构建结合语言理解与推荐信号的个性化LLM。

Abstract: LLM-based recommendation has scaled along model capacity and sequence length, yet each position encodes only text, semantic IDs, or a few categorical features, discarding rich user, item, context, and outcome signals available at each event. Under autoregressive modeling, this yields weak queries at each position and, since each position becomes context for the next, the degradation compounds across the sequence. We propose an event-centric paradigm that represents each interaction by its full temporal snapshot, and identify a new scaling dimension we term snapshot resolution: the amount of information encoded per event. To efficiently scale snapshot resolution, we introduce AMBER (Autoregressive Modeling via Bottlenecked Event Representation), which compresses each temporal snapshot into a compact Event Token, a new LLM input modality. The representation is learned end-to-end, while Event Tokens are pre-computed and cached for serving, decoupling snapshot resolution from real-time serving compute. On industrial-scale ranking and retrieval benchmarks, AMBER advances the compute-quality Pareto frontier relative to alternative recommendation paradigms. At sufficient capacity, a single unified tokenizer even outperforms dedicated per-entity tokenizers, demonstrating positive transfer across structurally different entity types. AMBER's Event Tokens also transfer across model architectures: when integrated into a heavily optimized non-LLM ranker as serving-time historical features, they yield statistically significant improvements. Further scaling Event Tokenizer capacity provides additional improvements.

</details>


### [192] [RetrievalRouter: Joint Modality and Architecture Selection for Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25625)
*Emre Kuru,Mehmet Onur Keskin,Reza Farahbakhsh,Noel Crespi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出RetrievalRouter，一种轻量级、查询感知的路由器，仅根据查询文本联合选择文档检索流水线的模态（文本/多模态）和架构（稀疏/稠密/晚期交互），在11个金融、科学和开放域基准上同时实现比最佳静态基线更高的准确率和更低的延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代文档检索流水线在模态（文本或多模态）和架构（稠密或晚期交互）两个维度上各有取舍：最有效的流水线（多模态晚期交互）太慢且成本太高，无法大规模运行，而最快的流水线在复杂文档上无法检索到证据。实践者不得不在"漏检证据"和"不可用延迟"之间做静态选择，缺乏在查询级别自适应选择的原则性方法。

Method: RetrievalRouter由基于Qwen3-0.6B（带LoRA适配器）的查询编码器和映射到5个路由动作（BM25、Text-Dense、Text-Rerank、Multimodal-Dense、Multimodal-Rerank）的单层线性决策头组成。训练时对每个查询在5个动作上构建融合nDCG@5和归一化延迟的奖励向量，通过温度τ=0.1的softmax转换为软标签，最小化KL散度。超参数λ∈[0,1]控制准确率-延迟权衡，扫描λ即可描绘完整Pareto前沿。路由开销约15ms。

Result: 在11个基准（含REAL-MM-RAG、T2-RAGBench、MMDocRAG）上，RetrievalRouter相对于最佳静态基线ML：nDCG@5提升2.5%（达0.755），延迟降低12.4倍（0.666s vs 8.283s）；相对于工业标准MR：nDCG@5提升3.0%，延迟降低1.7倍。在准确率导向设置下显著优于Arabzadeh等人（2021）的自适应策略选择方法，在延迟导向设置下数值上同时优于其准确率和延迟。路由器会随视觉复杂度提升自动增加多模态流水线分配比例，并在Wiki-SS等文本密集场景中将20%查询路由至BM25，证明BM25是被选为有效选择而非廉价后备。同时发布了涵盖80,000+查询的查询级流水线选择基准数据集。

Conclusion: 文档检索中的准确率-延迟权衡本质上是一个路由问题而非架构问题。RetrievalRouter将每个查询调度到能回答该查询的最廉价流水线，为真正需要昂贵流水线的查询保留资源，使实践者不再面临准确率与部署延迟的二选一困境。该工作同时贡献了跨11个基准的现代流水线系统实证分析、首个面向单语料库的联合模态-架构查询级路由器，以及查询级流水线选择基准。主要局限包括：需维护4个向量索引（约45GB，以多模态晚期索引为主）、约40GB VRAM占用、依赖查询文本可能导致路由语义歧义（如"总结第5页表格"无法判断文档布局）、仅在域内数据划分上验证跨域泛化能力有待进一步研究。

Abstract: Document retrieval increasingly supports high-stakes information access in finance, healthcare, and law. Modern retrieval pipelines vary both in modality (text or multimodal) and in retrieval architecture (dense or late-interaction). These choices impose a hard compromise: the most effective pipelines are too slow and expensive to run at scale, while the fastest fail to retrieve evidence from complex documents. Practitioners must therefore choose between missed evidence and unusable latency, with no principled basis for adapting that choice at the query level. We show that this compromise is unnecessary. Not every query requires the same pipeline. Across benchmarks spanning financial and scientific corpora, no static pipeline dominates. We introduce RetrievalRouter, a lightweight query-aware router that learns, from the query text alone, which retrieval pipeline best fits each query. A single tunable parameter exposes the full accuracy-latency frontier, and for every static baseline, RetrievalRouter offers an operating point that is simultaneously more accurate and faster. Against the best static baseline, RetrievalRouter is 2.5% more accurate and 12.4 times faster. Furthermore, compared with prior adaptive strategy selection methods, RetrievalRouter achieves significantly higher nDCG@5 across accuracy-oriented settings, while matching or numerically outperforming them on both nDCG@5 and latency in latency-oriented settings. Our code and data are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [193] [D3ER: Supporting Multi-Modal Recommendation via Disentangle and Distillation-based Dynamic Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25737)
*Bingnan Wang,Yi Li,Xiongxin Tang,Fanjiang Xu,Jiangmeng Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态推荐（MR）中联合学习模态同质判别信息（HOI）与模态异质判别信息（HEI）会削弱各自有效性的问题，提出了一种基于解耦与蒸馏的动态集成框架 D3ER。该方法首次将梯度提升引入多模态推荐，交替学习 HOI 与 HEI，并结合知识蒸馏与全局修正正则化来降低存储开销与局部最优风险，在三个真实数据集上均取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MR 方法通常联合学习跨模态共享的同质判别信息（HOI）与各模态独有的异质判别信息（HEI），但作者通过实证发现这种朴素集成策略会削弱两类信息各自的判别能力，导致基于样本的预测偏好被稀释。因此，亟需一种有效的集成策略来获取全面面向样本的判别信息。

Method: 提出 D3ER 框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 特征组件解耦（FCD）模块，通过实例级 InfoNCE 损失与基于 Radon 变换的分布级 Wasserstein 距离进行跨模态对齐获得 HOI，并通过分离损失获取 HEI；(2) 知识蒸馏增强的梯度提升（KDBoost）模块，将梯度提升形式化为拟合前一累积模型负梯度的优化目标以交替学习 HOI 和 HEI，并通过蒸馏步骤以空间高效方式更新累积模型，同时引入全局修正正则化缓解贪婪学习的局部最优问题。

Result: 在 Amazon 三个数据集（Baby、Sports、Clothing）上，以 MGCN、DiffMM、PGL 作为嵌入编码器骨干，D3ER 在 Recall@20/50 与 NDCG@20/50 指标上均显著超越 15 个基线方法，最高达 7.58% 的相对提升；消融实验验证了 FCD 模块、KDBoost、实例级/分布级对齐损失以及全局修正正则化的必要性；t-SNE 可视化证实了 HOI 与 HEI 的有效分离；推理效率与骨干模型相当。

Conclusion: D3ER 通过解耦 HOI 与 HEI 并采用交替式梯度提升集成策略，有效克服了朴素集成对各类判别信息的稀释问题，为多模态推荐提供了一种兼具理论保证（更紧的泛化误差界与目标误差界）与实际性能的解决方案。

Abstract: Incorporating items' information shared among multiple modalities into a fused representation, multi-modal recommendation (MR) has demonstrated documented success than canonical unimodal recommendation. Although several attempts have been made to extract the discriminative information unique in each modality, existing methods suffer from a core limitation: the joint learning of modal-homogeneity discriminative information (HOI) and modal-heterogeneity discriminative information (HEI) tends to weaken their individual effectiveness. To remedy this deficiency, we propose a novel method, dubbed Disentangle and Distillation-based Dynamic Ensemble for multi-modal Recommendation (D3ER). We introduce gradient boosting into MR for the first time to formalize the optimization objective for alternately learning HOI and HEI. This design enables models dedicated to each type of information to focus on their proficient samples, thereby promoting specialized optimization. Furthermore, to mitigate the inherent high storage cost and risk of local optima in gradient boosting, we enhance our framework with knowledge distillation and a global correction regularization. Experiments on prevalent real-world datasets confirm the superiority of our proposed method on MR.

</details>


### [194] [Hamiltonian Spectral-Temporal Dissipative Dynamics for Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25755)
*Shuiying Liao,P. Y. Mok*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出Hamiltonian Spectral Recommender (HSR)，将序列推荐中的用户偏好演化建模为二阶阻尼Hamiltonian动力学系统，通过频域闭式解进行高效传播，在三个基准数据集上超越Transformer和SSM基线，同时具有更优的计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列推荐模型普遍将用户偏好演化视为仅依赖当前隐状态的**一阶过程**，无法充分捕捉真实用户行为中的惯性(inertia)、周期性(periodicity)和突变(abrupt shifts)等二阶动力学特征。作者受Hamiltonian力学启发，从物理学的二阶动力学角度重新审视偏好演化问题。

Method: HSR包含五个组件：(1) 嵌入层将离散物品映射为稠密向量；(2) Hamiltonian谱编码器堆叠L个相同块，每块包含三个阶段——投影映射、全局Hamiltonian谱传播（利用RFFT在频域求解二阶阻尼振子方程κ-mω²+icω的可学习传播子Ψ/D）、局部脉冲细化（深度可分离时序卷积）与门控融合；(3) 末相空间恢复（通过RFFT-导数-IRFFT从最终位置轨迹重新推导动量以保持相位空间一致性）；(4) 一步Euler外推q̂=qL+Δt·M⁻¹·pL用于下一步预测；(5) 预测层计算用户表征与物品嵌入的点积得分。频域闭式解将传播复杂度从O(T²)降至O(T log T)。

Result: 在MovieLens-1M、Amazon-Beauty、Amazon-Video-Games三个基准数据集上，HSR在大部分Hit@10/NDCG@10/MRR@10指标上达到SOTA，相对最佳基线提升1.82%-17.42%；消融实验验证了Hamiltonian结构、局部脉冲、谱传播、相空间外推和可学习谱核各自的关键作用；超参数分析表明3-4个谱块和d=256为较优配置；学习到的物理参数（质量m、阻尼c）展现出符合数据集特性的可解释行为模式（稀疏数据集阻尼更高、密集数据集惯性更大）；效率方面参数比Mamba4Rec少21.91%、训练时间减少48.87%、吞吐量提升77.09%，且对噪声注入和冷启动场景具有更强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: HSR成功将二阶Hamiltonian动力学引入序列推荐领域，通过物理驱动的频域传播子、可解释的物理参数（质量/阻尼/刚度）以及基于动量的相空间外推机制，为偏好演化提供了新的建模范式。该方法在准确性、可解释性和计算效率之间取得了良好的平衡，适合实际部署；未来工作可探索非线性势能、自适应耗散以及多模态上下文感知的扩展。

Abstract: Sequential recommendation requires understanding how user preferences evolve over time, yet most existing models treat such evolution as a first order process where the next state depends solely on the current latent representation. Nevertheless, real user behavior often exhibits richer dynamics, including inertia, periodicity, and sudden shifts that cannot be fully captured by these first order assumptions. Motivated by these behavioral characteristics, we reconceptualize sequential recommendation through the lens of second order dynamical systems and introduce the Hamiltonian Spectral Recommender (HSR), which recasts preference evolution as a dissipative Hamiltonian system in a latent phase space of position (stable preference) and momentum (short-term tendency). The linear time-invariant structure of the governing equation admits a closed-form solution in the frequency domain. A learnable dissipation mechanism further captures natural interest decay, while a short local impulse refinement module models abrupt behavioral fluctuations commonly observed in sparse interaction logs. This design jointly accounts for global periodic patterns, inertial evolution, and localized shocks, where three phenomena that are underrepresented in existing sequential models. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that HSR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art Transformer-based and state space model (SSM)-based recommenders.

</details>


### [195] [PUMA: Post-Hoc Sparsification of Universal Multimodal Embeddings for Efficient Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25780)
*Matteo Attimonelli,Alessandro De Bellis,Franco Maria Nardini,Claudio Pomo,Cosimo Rulli,Rossano Venturini,Tommaso Di Noia*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PUMA提出了一种后处理稀疏化方法，通过TopK稀疏自编码器将冻结的通用多模态嵌入转换为紧凑稀疏码，无需重训骨干网络。在Qwen3-VL-Embedding上，多个检索基准上达到与稠密检索相当或更优的性能，同时实现8–16×存储压缩和最高25×加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用多模态嵌入器（支持文本、图像和组合查询的单一骨干）虽然提供了统一的检索接口，但其稠密表示带来高昂的内存和推理成本。现有工作研究了稀疏文本检索、多模态对比预训练中的稀疏化以及用于表示分析的多模态稀疏自编码器，但后处理稀疏化用于检索服务这一方向尚未被充分探索：能否在不重训骨干网络的情况下将冻结的多模态嵌入器转换为稀疏检索器，同时保持跨任务的质量？

Method: PUMA是一个三阶段后处理稀疏化管道：(1) 用冻结嵌入器缓存稠密嵌入；(2) 检索感知的稀疏预训练，结合重构损失、跨模态支持对齐损失（促使跨模态对激活重叠原子）、点积蒸馏损失（保持稠密打分几何）、AuxK特征复活（恢复死掉的字典原子）和渐进式k退火；(3) 对比检索微调，使用InfoNCE损失优化稀疏编码，同时保留重构和蒸馏作为正则项。稀疏自编码器使用D=16d的过完备字典，每个向量恰好有k个非零条目。

Result: 在5个M-BEIR基准上评估，涵盖文本到图像和组合图像检索。在Qwen3-VL-Embedding-2B上，PUMA在CIRR和Fashion200K上显著优于稠密检索，在FashionIQ和MSCOCO上统计上无差异，仅在VisualNews上落后。存储减少8–16×（FP32），在大规模候选池上精确搜索最高加速25×。消融实验证实了各组件的重要性。通过与Raw TopK、匹配内存PCA、训练稠密自编码器和仅编码器稀疏训练的比较，排除了简单降维或域适应的解释。分析识别出两种后处理稀疏化的失败模式：TopK前支持不足和检索不对齐的活跃支持。

Conclusion: PUMA证明了后处理稀疏化是实现高效多模态检索的可行路径，能以极低的存储和搜索成本保留通用嵌入器的泛化能力。未来工作需联合解决字典覆盖充足性和活跃特征的检索对齐这两个瓶颈。

Abstract: Universal multimodal embedders enable retrieval across text, image, and combined queries, but their dense representations incur high memory and inference costs. Post-hoc sparsification could reduce these costs but remains underexplored for multimodal retrieval. We introduce PUMA, a sparse autoencoder recipe that maps universal multimodal embeddings to compact sparse codes without retraining the backbone: a pretraining stage preserves dense dot-product geometry, after which the sparse encoder is fine-tuned for retrieval. We evaluate on five benchmarks covering text-to-image and composed image retrieval. On Qwen3-VL-Embedding-2B, PUMA is statistically indistinguishable from or improves over dense retrieval on four of five datasets. We further identify two failure modes of post-hoc sparsification: insufficient pre-TopK support and retrieval-misaligned active support. PUMA reduces vector storage by 8-16x (FP32) and is up to 25x faster than exact dense scoring on larger candidate pools, enabling efficient multimodal retrieval.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [GreenLeaf Law Embed Tiny: A Compact Embedding Model for Legal Domain Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24936)
*Surya Saka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 GreenLeaf Law Embed Tiny（GreenLeaf-Tiny），一个参数量为 0.6B 的紧凑型法律领域嵌入模型。该模型通过两阶段训练（知识蒸馏 + 领域微调）、高质量人工标注数据以及支持多级量化的推理架构，在 MLEB 和 MTEB(Law) 上取得具有竞争力的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律信息检索对准确性和可解释性要求高，而现有方案要么是缺乏领域专业性的一般嵌入模型，要么是不透明的专有商业系统。法律领域又常涉及机密文档，难以使用外部 API。因此需要一款紧凑、高效、可本地部署的法律专用嵌入模型。

Method: 1) 两阶段训练：先用大型教师模型进行知识蒸馏，再用 340 万对查询-段落数据进行对比学习与硬负样本挖掘进行领域微调。2) 数据集 JudicialMind Legal Corpus（3.25M 自动 + 150K 人工标注），覆盖 35 种语言、40+ 法域。3) 架构：28 层 Transformer（hidden=1024, 16 heads, 2048 上下文）+ 64 维法域嵌入 + 32 维时间嵌入，支持分层编码处理超长文档。4) 推理支持 BF16/INT8/Binary 三级量化。

Result: 在 MLEB 上取得 75.11%，MTEB(Law, v1) 上取得 64.38%，超过同尺寸的 Qwen3 Embedding 0.6B（69.9%）与 BGE-M3（61.5%）。关键消融：蒸馏+领域微调比直接微调提升 +9.24；法律感知硬负样本比随机负样本提升 +3.59；仅占 4.4% 的人工数据贡献 +8.2 分；INT8 量化仅损失 0.31%。但仍落后于 Kanon 2 Embedder（1.8B, 81.9%）与 Dinghy Law 8B（72.58 on MTEB(Law)）。

Conclusion: 域内专门训练（高质量数据 + 硬负样本挖掘 + 架构适配）能够提升专业领域性能，但模型规模对复杂判例检索仍然重要。GreenLeaf-Tiny 在资源受限、隐私敏感的法律部署场景中具有实用价值；未来方向包括稀疏注意力扩展上下文、多模态结构利用、持续学习与联邦学习等。

Abstract: We present GreenLeaf Law Embed Tiny, a 0.6B parameter embedding model for legal domain retrieval. GreenLeaf-Tiny achieves 75.11% on the Massive Legal Embedding Benchmark (MLEB) and 64.38% on MTEB(Law, v1),demonstrating competitive performance among models under 1B parameters. Our approach combines a two-stage training pipeline that first distills knowledge from a larger teacher model into a compact student architecture, then applies domain-specific fine-tuning with hard negative mining; a carefully curated dataset of 3.4 million query-passage pairs, including 150,000 human-curated samples across diverse legal jurisdictions; and an efficient inference architecture supporting multiple quantization levels (BF16, INT8, binary) enabling deployment in resource-constrained environments. We provide detailed analysis of our training methodology, architectural choices, and comprehensive evaluation across legal retrieval tasks. Our results demonstrate that domain-specific training with high-quality data can improve performance for specialized domain applications

</details>


### [197] [Multi-Modal Anomaly Detection: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24937)
*Xudong Mou,Zexin Wu,Chuan Luo,Shiru Chen,Xudong Liu,Chunming Hu,Renyu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这是一篇关于多模态异常检测（MMAD）的综述论文，提出了一种基于"假设驱动"的全新分类视角，将现有方法分为正常性假设（normality-assumption）和异常性假设（anomaly-assumption）两大范式，并系统梳理了基础模型在MMAD中的作用、相关基准数据集及未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态异常检测综述通常按架构或模态组合对方法进行分类，未能揭示异常检测的本质问题。MMAD领域文献分散于不同领域（工业检测、医疗、视频监控等）和模态组合中，缺乏统一的理论视角来理解不同方法如何定义和区分异常。本文旨在填补这一空白，从异常定义的根本假设出发，建立一个更具解释性和统一性的分类框架。

Method: 论文采用"假设驱动"的视角进行综述：将MMAD方法分为两大互补范式——（1）正常性假设方法：通过表征学习、跨模态对齐和知识增强建模正常模式；（2）异常性假设方法：通过粗粒度、结构性和语义性异常注入来锐化决策边界。在此框架下，进一步梳理基础模型（视觉-语言模型、多模态大语言模型等）在MMAD中的作用机制，并整理代表性基准和评估协议。

Result: 论文系统梳理了MMAD领域的五大核心挑战：模态异质性（CH1）、边界模糊性（CH2）、敏感度不平衡（CH3）、表征脆弱性（CH4）和性能衰减（CH5）。在正常性假设范式下，归纳出三类方法：正常特征学习（通用型和边界约束型）、跨模态表征学习（语义同步和局部一致性验证）、领域知识增强（跨模态知识蒸馏和原型记忆机制）。在异常性假设范式下，归纳出三类方法：粗粒度注入（外部暴露和内部扰动）、结构性注入（时序破坏和关系破坏）和语义注入（知识驱动提示和生成式数据补全）。论文还提供了涵盖工业检测、医疗诊断、视频监控等多领域的基准数据集对比，并讨论了零样本评估、模态缺失鲁棒性等新兴评估协议。最后提出以"基础模型驱动的假设融合"为统一范式，以及三个核心开放问题：可靠异常假设生成、跨模态冲突下的语义推理、持续环境适应。

Conclusion: MMAD正从孤立的检测流水线转向融合表征学习、异常模拟、语义推理和部署感知适应的整体感知系统。基础模型正成为关键催化剂，能够降低模态异质性并实现知识丰富的异常定义。本文的假设驱动框架为该领域提供了概念参考和发展路线图，指明了自适应、可解释、可靠的MMAD系统的发展方向。

Abstract: Multi-Modal Anomaly Detection (MMAD) detects rare abnormal events from heterogeneous data sources and is increasingly used in safety- and reliability-critical applications such as industrial inspection and cybersecurity. Yet the literature is fragmented across domains and modality combinations, and existing surveys usually group methods by architecture rather than by how abnormality is defined and separated in multi-modal settings. We survey MMAD from an assumption-driven perspective. We formalize the problem, identify five intrinsic characteristics underlying its core challenges, and organize prior work into two complementary paradigms. The first, normality-assumption methods, models regularity via representation learning, cross-modal alignment, and knowledge enhancement. The second, anomaly-assumption methods, sharpens decision boundaries through coarse-grained, structural, and semantic anomaly injection. We also investigate how foundation models are reshaping MMAD through scalable pretraining, flexible cross-modal transfer, and emerging reasoning capabilities. Finally, we compile representative benchmarks and evaluation protocols across domains and highlight open problems and future directions for robust, adaptive, and interpretable MMAD systems.

</details>


### [198] [ExFold: Unified Expert Folding for Training-Free MoE Prefill-Decode Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24938)
*Juntong Wu,Yifei Liu,Junyi Chen,Siqi Fan,Chaoran Feng,Minghao Li,Liujie Zhang,Weihang Chen,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ExFold是一个统一的免训练MoE推理加速框架，通过"专家折叠"机制将预填充和解码两个阶段统一为一个预算约束下的输出近似问题，使用标量投影器将排除专家的贡献映射到保留专家上，在vLLM中实现最高1.41× TTFT和2.45× TPOT加速同时保持约99%的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型推理面临两个阶段的不同瓶颈：预填充受限于token级专家计算，解码受限于batch级激活专家集的内存流量。现有免训练加速方法仅优化单一资源代理（每个token执行的专家或batch激活的专家），且要么丢弃排除专家的贡献，要么仅隐式近似，导致在预算收紧时近似误差累积放大。

Method: ExFold将预填充和解码统一为预算约束下的输出近似问题：执行阶段特定的受限专家集，同时通过校准的标量投影器将预算排除专家的贡献投影到保留专家上。基于专家输出方向对齐但幅度不同的观察，通过无标注数据上的前向传播校准成对的标量投影器矩阵和投影损失矩阵。预填充加速变为token级Top-K折叠，解码加速变为batch级专家池折叠，两阶段仅在保留专家的选择方式上有所不同，排除贡献的恢复由同一折叠机制处理。实现为vLLM插件，配备轻量级Triton专家折叠CUDA内核。

Result: 在Qwen3-30B-A3B、GLM-4.5-Air、DeepSeek-V2-Lite、DeepSeek-V4-Flash（284B参数）和Qwen3.5-35B-A3B等多个MoE架构上验证。在Qwen3上P4+D32设置下保留约99%的原始平均质量，在DeepSeek-V4-Flash上P3+D64保留97.68%质量。实现最高1.41× TTFT加速（在QPS 8时）和2.45× TPOT加速（在QPS 8时），离线吞吐量提升1.20×。标量投影器仅需约6 MiB存储开销，可融入路由权重，无需在线向量变换。

Conclusion: ExFold建立了输出恢复作为MoE推理加速的实用基础，通过阶段特定的保留集选择器与共享的有向投影器相结合，仅修改路由元数据即可联合加速预填充和解码。因为折叠仅改变路由元数据，它与内核、调度和并行优化互补，可作为MoE推理加速的通用方法。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models scale capacity for strong quality while keeping per-token compute bounded through sparse expert activation. Yet low-latency MoE serving is increasingly challenging, because it spans two inference phases with fundamentally different bottlenecks: prefill is dominated by token-wise expert computation, whereas decode is constrained by memory traffic from the batch-wise activated expert set. However, existing training-free acceleration methods optimize only a single resource proxy, either the experts each token executes or the experts a batch activates, and either discard the excluded experts' contribution or leave it only implicitly approximated. In this paper, we propose ExFold, a unified training-free expert-folding framework for jointly accelerating MoE prefill and decode. ExFold casts both prefill and decode as one budgeted output-approximation problem: execute only a phase-specific constrained expert set while projecting the contribution of budget-excluded experts onto retained experts using calibrated scalar projectors. Motivated by the observation that many expert outputs are directionally aligned but differ in magnitude, ExFold calibrates a pairwise scalar-projector matrix on unlabeled data and uses it at inference time to fold excluded expert contributions into retained experts. Under this view, prefill acceleration becomes token-level Top-K folding, and decode acceleration becomes batch-level expert-pool folding. The two phases differ only in how retained experts are selected, while excluded contributions are recovered by one shared folding mechanism. We implement ExFold as a plug-and-play plugin in vLLM, with a lightweight expert-folding CUDA kernel, delivering up to 1.41x TTFT and 2.45x TPOT speedups while retaining about 99% of the original average quality.

</details>


### [199] [When Does Frequency Decomposition Benefit Physics-Informed Neural Networks? A Preliminary Ablation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24940)
*Shubham Rai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出双分支频谱门控架构DBSG-PINN，通过部分受控的消融实验系统考察频率分解对PINN的增益条件，发现其优势与PDE频谱复杂度正相关，但在单尺度问题上可能失效甚至有害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN存在频谱偏置（spectral bias），现有频率感知方法（Fourier特征、Sinusoidal激活等）多默认其普适有效，却缺乏对"何种频谱条件下真正有益"的系统分析。论文质疑频率分解是否对所有PDE普遍有益，聚焦其与PDE频谱特征之间的依赖关系。

Method: 设计DBSG-PINN架构：低频分支（tanh激活）建模平滑分量，高频分支（sin(ωx)激活）建模振荡分量，sigmoid门控网络输出空间变化的混合权重。构建四个变体进行消融：Full（含门控）、NoGate（固定0.5混合权重）、LowOnly（仅tanh分支）、HighOnly（仅sin分支）。在5个一维PDE基准（Burgers、Allen-Cahn、Reaction-Diffusion、Multimodal Wave、1D Wave）上对比，控制优化器、迭代次数、损失权重一致。评估指标涵盖相对L2误差、L∞误差、PDE残差、谱误差及LF/HF频段恢复率。

Result: 在多模态波方程（k∈{1,3,5}）上Full模型相对L2误差降低达59.2%，频谱恢复最优；Allen-Cahn上Full略优于LowOnly（0.1038 vs 0.1108）；Burgers上门控无显著优势（差距<5%）；1D Wave上Full模型全面劣于NoGate（L2误差约高53%，谱误差约高68%）。反应扩散方程作为反例（单模态解但Full仍领先）。结论支持"频谱越复杂，门控收益越大"的假设。

Conclusion: 频率分解对PINN的增益并非普适，其价值取决于PDE的频谱复杂度：在真正多尺度问题上收益最大，在平滑单尺度问题上收益甚微甚至为负。论文以探索性研究的姿态呈现，明确承认单随机种子、五个一维基准、容量近似匹配（而非严格匹配）三大局限，主张下一步需多种子验证并扩展至高维基准。

Abstract: Partial differential equations (PDEs) often have high-frequency and multi-scale features that neural networks struggle to approximate. Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) build the governing equations directly into training, but suffer from spectral bias: they learn low-frequency components faster than high-frequency ones. Techniques such as Fourier feature embeddings and sinusoidal activations address this, but most studies assume they help across the board without checking which spectral regimes actually benefit. We introduce a dual-branch, spectrally-gated architecture (DBSG-PINN) that splits low- and high-frequency components into separate subnetworks joined by an adaptive gate, and use it to run a partially controlled ablation of frequency decomposition and spectral routing. We test this on five one-dimensional benchmark PDEs, ranging from smooth, single-scale problems to oscillatory, multi-scale ones. Frequency decomposition helps most on the spectrally complex benchmarks, cutting relative $L_2$ error by up to $59.2\%$ on a multimodal wave problem, but gives little benefit on smoother PDEs. On one benchmark (1D Wave), it performs substantially worse than a simpler fixed-combination variant. The gate's benefit scales with how spectrally rich the target solution is: the full model's advantage over the ablations is largest on multi-scale benchmarks and smallest (or negative) on single-scale ones, consistent with the gate exploiting frequency structure rather than acting as noise,though we do not directly visualize or quantify its spatial activations in this study. All results come from a single training seed across five 1D benchmarks, so we present this as an exploratory study meant to raise questions rather than answer them, and outline the additional seeds and benchmarks needed to test whether the pattern holds.

</details>


### [200] [FAMPWQ: Fisher Information-based Adaptive Mixed Precision Weight Quantization for Effective LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24945)
*Gongwei Lee,Ji Liu,Juncheng Jia,Ji Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FAMPWQ，一种基于Fisher信息的自适应混合精度权重量化方法，通过量化扰动的Fisher信息度量层敏感度，并结合PPO强化学习分配各层比特宽度，在7个模型和5个基准上显著优于7种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型(LLM)参数量巨大，部署到资源受限设备面临严峻挑战。现有后训练量化(PTQ)方法采用统一比特宽度或基于简单启发式的敏感度评估（如权重大小、梯度范数），忽略了LLM各层对量化的异质敏感度，导致在低比特（尤其是低于3.5比特）场景下性能急剧下降。现有敏感度指标未能反映量化引入的特定噪声对损失几何的影响。

Method: 方法包含两阶段：(1) 量化扰动Fisher敏感度度量：对每层权重注入b比特量化的舍入扰动δθ=Qb(θ)-θ，计算扰动前后经验Fisher信息矩阵(FIM)对角向量的相对变化作为层敏感度sl；(2) 基于PPO强化学习的自适应比特宽度分配：将组合优化问题转化为带存储约束惩罚的损失函数最小化问题，使用轻量级残差网络构建Actor-Critic架构，Actor逐层生成比特宽度分配，Critic引导策略优化，通过TD advantage和clip surrogate objective更新。问题被形式化为在满足平均压缩率约束下，最小化总精度退化的组合优化问题。

Result: 在7个模型(LLaMA-7B/13B, LLaMA2-7B-chat/13B-chat, Qwen2.5-7B/14B, Mistral-7B-v0.1)和5个基准上的实验表明，FAMPWQ显著优于7种基线(RTN, GPTQ, GPTQv2, OmniQuant, AMQ, OWQ, AWQ)：PPL降低最多达3.39，零样本推理准确率提升最多达6.87%，LLM-as-a-judge评测中胜率最高达76%(对比RTN)。FIM敏感度与Oracle敏感度的Pearson相关系数达r=0.91(p<10^-14)。预处理总时间在14B模型上低于70分钟，可与GPTQ/AWQ后端兼容。

Conclusion: FAMPWQ通过量化感知的Fisher信息度量和PPO驱动的比特分配，有效解决了LLM各层量化敏感度异质性问题，在3比特压缩前沿显著优于现有PTQ方法，实现了内存受限设备上LLM的高效部署。Fisher敏感度与实际性能退化高度相关，证明了该度量作为量化特定层敏感度代理的有效性。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed remarkable achievements of Large Language Models (LLMs) in multiple domains, while the excessive resource requirements of LLMs hinder the deployment on resource-constrained devices. Although model quantization stands out as an effective approach, conventional quantization approaches typically incur severe performance degradation due to uniform bit-width or simple heuristic sensitivity evaluation. In this paper, we propose a novel Fisher information-based Adaptive Mixed Precision Weight Quantization approach, i.e., FAMPWQ, which performs layer-adaptive weight quantization for effective LLM inference on commodity GPUs. First, we propose a system model with a novel Fisher information metric to measure the layer-wise sensitivity to quantization. Second, we propose a reinforcement learning-based bit-width allocator in FAMPWQ, which generates an adaptive bit-width allocation strategy based on the Fisher information sensitivity metric. Extensive experiments on 7 models and 5 benchmarks demonstrate that FAMPWQ significantly outperforms 7 baseline approaches in terms of PPL (up to 3.39 smaller), accuracy (up to 6.87% higher), and LLM-as-a-judge comparison (up to 76% win rate).

</details>


### [201] [CAT-GS: Balanced Multimodal Learning via Calibrated Gating and Fusion Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24947)
*Mahir Shahriar Tamim,Sharjil Khan,Md. Samiul Alim,Tanvir Ahmed Khan,Shafin Rahman,Nabeel Mohammed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAT-GS（Calibrated, Adaptive, Thresholded Gating with Fusion Surgery）是一种面向多模态学习的反向传播阶段优化控制器。它不改模型架构或任务损失，而是通过四个组件联合处理模态不平衡问题：(1) 基于教师置信度的温度缩放与EMA平滑校准，(2) 阈值化margin驱动的门控策略（warm-up dropout/弱势模态优先/弱偏混合），(3) 梯度预算再分配以防止硬门控下的梯度饥饿，(4) 融合层PCGrad以消除跨模态冲突。在CREMA-D、AV-MNIST、VGGSound、UR-FUNNY（三模态）、CG-MNIST（合成）以及AVE、CMU-MOSI上的实验表明，CAT-GS在融合精度上优于或匹配OGM-GE、G²D、UMT等强基线，且门控更平滑、融合梯度冲突更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端多模态训练存在三种耦合的失败模式：(1) 模态不平衡——强势分支主导梯度优化；(2) 门控不稳定——噪声置信度导致模态选择抖动（gate thrashing）；(3) 融合干扰——各模态梯度在共享融合层方向冲突。现有方法（梯度调制如OGM-GE、置信度门控、梯度投影如PCGrad）通常只孤立解决其中一个问题。

Method: CAT-GS在loss.backward()与optimizer.step()之间介入，包含四个组件：①校准可靠性估计（温度缩放+EMA平滑教师logits得到批量级可靠性p̂）；②margin-阈值化自适应门控（依据|p̂ᵃ−p̂ᵛ|与τ_low/τ_high切换warm-up dropout/硬门控/弱偏混合三种机制）；③梯度预算再分配（用EMA参考梯度范数ĝ^m对门控后梯度做有上限的重缩放以稳定更新幅度）；④融合层PCGrad（仅在融合输出层fc_out上做单边投影解决负余弦相似度的梯度冲突）。文中还给出了局部一步单调下降理论保证及到M>2模态的扩展。

Result: 在CREMA-D上融合精度达86.29%±0.15（vs Joint-Train 67.47%、G²D 85.89%）；AV-MNIST 73.21%±0.08；VGGSound 53.24%±0.24（弱于G²D/UMT约0.5–0.6pp）；UR-FUNNY三模态A-V-TXT 67.55%±0.84（最优）；CG-MNIST 97.42%±0.23；AVE 74.2%±0.2；CMU-MOSI三模态78.3%±1.0。门控总变化（TV）显著降低，融合梯度负余弦比例显著下降，累积梯度预算比从3.2×降至1.7×。消融显示梯度预算再分配贡献最大（去掉后降至83.20%），EMA次之，PCGrad第三。训练开销仅增加约2–5%。

Conclusion: CAT-GS通过在优化阶段统一整合可靠性校准、机制门控、预算稳定和融合手术，提供了一种稳定且平衡的多模态训练方案。其优势在模态可靠性随训练变化且融合头是显著共享瓶颈时最为明显；在VGGSound等大规模数据集上由于教师可靠性信号弱、大标签空间使其优势减弱。依赖预训练单模态教师提供可靠性信号是其主要局限，未来工作方向包括无教师可靠性估计、token级门控及大规模预训练场景下的扩展。

Abstract: End-to-end training of multimodal neural networks often exhibits unstable neural dynamics characterized by three coupled failure modes that degrade learning: (i) modality imbalance, where one branch dominates gradient-based optimization; (ii) unstable gating, where noisy confidence cues induce erratic modality selection; and (iii) fusion interference, where modality-specific gradients conflict at the shared fusion layer. We propose CAT-GS (Calibrated, Adaptive, Thresholded Gating with Fusion Surgery), a neural dynamics-based optimization controller for intelligent computing applications. CAT-GS operates during backpropagation without modifying model architectures, fusion modules, or task losses. Through calibration of teacher-derived reliability via temperature scaling and EMA smoothing, CAT-GS stabilizes neural dynamics using a margin-thresholded policy to switch between warm-up dropout, weak-modality prioritization, and weak-biased blending, stabilizes gradient magnitudes under aggressive gating via capped gradient-budget renormalization, and applies fusion-only PCGrad to reduce destructive cross-modal interference at the primary shared bottleneck. We evaluate CAT-GS on audio--visual multimodal pattern recognition benchmarks (CREMA-D, AV-MNIST, and VGGSound), a tri-modal setting (UR-FUNNY), controlled synthetic data (CG-MNIST), and additional cross-domain benchmarks (AVE and CMU-MOSI). CAT-GS improves or matches fused multimodal accuracy against strong imbalance-aware baselines (including OGM-GE, G$^2$D, and UMT) across settings, and yields smoother gating behavior with fewer conflicting fusion gradients.

</details>


### [202] [Demystifying Reinforcement Learning Post-Training of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24949)
*Donovan Clay,Saket Gollapudi,Sankar Harilal,Min Jang,Jacob Morrison,Sewoong Oh,Natasha Jaques*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过控制实验，系统拆解了RL后训练（特别是RLVR）的三个关键组件——基础模型分布、奖励信号粒度、提示词分布——如何影响LLM后训练的成功率，揭示了"覆盖率原则"对稀疏奖励的根本限制、密集奖励可突破该限制的能力，以及所谓"虚假奖励"效应取决于提示词分布的发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL后训练已成为增强LLM推理能力的主流方法，但许多研究者将其视为"黑箱"。文献中存在相互矛盾的发现（如虚假奖励提升性能、RL是否能学到新行为），本文旨在通过受控实验澄清这些争议，并理解各组件如何影响后训练结果。

Method: 在受控沙盒环境中进行两组实验：1）目标字符串生成任务（电影台词）和数学推理任务（AIME问题），通过SFT+和SFT-人为操纵基础模型的初始概率分布；2）使用多种奖励函数（稀疏二值、基于Levenshtein距离的密集奖励、基于PRM的指数密集奖励）；3）使用Qwen和OLMo系列模型在窄（数学）和宽（如WildChat、Tulu-3）提示词分布上施加随机奖励。通过跟踪策略熵、概率质量、基准性能指标来分析动态。

Result: 1）覆盖率原则验证：稀疏奖励下，基础模型必须对目标行为赋予足够概率，否则RL无法探索到正信号；2）密集奖励突破限制：Levenshtein距离和PRM密集奖励可使模型学习基础模型中近零概率的新行为（Qwen3-1.7B的Base模型从0.5%提升至48.8%，AIME问题从10%提升至92%）；3）虚假奖励效应：窄提示词分布下训练虚假奖励会强化已有的正确先验（如Qwen数学），宽分布则导致全局熵增加和能力崩溃（如OLMo GSM8K从86%降至32%）；4）SFT→DPO逐阶段检查点对随机奖励的熵增长具有递进抗性。

Conclusion: RL后训练的成功不取决于算法选择，而取决于基础模型的先验分布、奖励信号的粒度和提示词分布的交互。稀疏RL严格受限于基础模型的初始化，但密集奖励可以克服这一限制。"虚假奖励"现象并非RL学习新行为，而是窄提示词分布下对先验的强化。这一框架统一了文献中相互矛盾的发现，为NLP研究者提供了实用指南。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training has emerged as a powerful framework for enhancing the capabilities of large language models (LLMs), enabling impressive reasoning, math, and coding capabilities. Yet for many researchers and practitioners, the principles behind classical RL remain a "black box". In this work, we deconstruct the RL post-training algorithm, investigating each step to clarify what is actually happening beneath the surface. By isolating the mechanics of RL with Verifiable Rewards in a controlled and simplified environment, we examine how RL outcomes are shaped by the base model's prior distribution, the granularity of the reward signal, the diversity of the prompt distribution, and model scale. We use the entropy of the policy's output distribution as a lens to compare the distributions learned through pretraining, SFT, and RL post-training, revealing how each stage shapes model certainty. Our investigation sheds light on how these choices interact to affect post-training success. For example, we show that the effect of so-called 'spurious rewards' depends on the prompt distribution used for post-training. We also provide insight into why the success of RL post-training depends on whether the base model already places sufficient probability mass on the desired behavior, linking it to the classical concept of exploration in RL. Ultimately, we provide this primer as a resource to those in the NLP community wishing to incorporate RL as a tool in their toolbox.

</details>


### [203] [Resource-Efficient Pruning for Transformer via Low-Rank Importance Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24973)
*Peng Liu,Huibing Zeng,Yiqun Zhang,Yang Yi,Jigang Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REP-LIE：一种基于LoRA低秩梯度估计的Transformer结构化剪枝框架，通过低秩重要性估计与轻量微调实现资源高效压缩，在BERT-base、LLaMA-7B和Mistral-7B上均取得竞争性性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有剪枝方法依赖全梯度计算且需要预微调，带来巨大资源开销，难以在资源受限环境下部署大模型。LoRA虽能减少微调成本，但仍依赖全梯度进行重要性估计，开销显著。

Method: REP-LIE框架包含三大核心：(1)利用LoRA低秩矩阵A和B的梯度推导出基于一阶Taylor展开的重要性估计公式，避免计算预训练权重梯度；(2)引入稳定性评分（基于多次迭代排名方差）缓解重要性估计的随机性；(3)设计轻量微调策略，仅更新低秩矩阵恢复性能，并采用动态稀疏度调度的渐进式剪枝算法结合FLOPs约束优化。

Result: 在BERT-base的GLUE任务上（50%稀疏度），REP-LIE在所有任务上均优于CoFi、PGB、EBERT等方法，内存占用仅476MB（比Post-FT降低69.4%）。在LLaMA-7B和Mistral-7B上（20%-50%稀疏度），PPL和零样本推理均显著优于LLM-Pruner、Compresso、SlimGPT，且剪枝时间最短、内存最低（50%稀疏度下仅5.4GB，16分钟完成）。在88%高稀疏度下BERT-base平均性能仍保持78.64%。低秩梯度在r=64时与全秩梯度的Spearman相关性超过0.75。

Conclusion: REP-LIE将重要性估计与性能恢复统一在同一低秩参数空间，将传统"先剪枝后微调"范式转变为"剪枝与微调同步进行"，为资源受限环境下的大模型部署提供了一种可扩展的压缩方案。未来可探索在已压缩模型（如蒸馏或量化模型）上的应用以及剪枝与量化的联合优化。

Abstract: With the rapid development of large-scale pre-trained language models based on Transformer architectures, their high computational and memory costs have become a major obstacle to deployment, especially in resource-constrained environments. Traditional pruning methods typically depend on full gradient-based importance estimation, and they necessitate prior finetuning of the model to achieve satisfactory performance. This process often results in intolerable resource consumption. This paper proposes REP-LIE, a new approach to enable resource-efficient pruning during the process of finetuning. REP-LIE leverages the gradients of LoRA low-rank matrices to estimate the importance of weights without requiring full gradient computation. To address the inherent randomness in importance estimation, a stability score is introduced, serving as the basis for iterative pruning of unimportant model parameters. The pruned model is further finetuned through lightweight updates, eliminating the need for full-parameter optimization in the process of finetuning. Extensive experiments on both medium-scale encoder models and large-scale generative models (LLaMA-7B and Mistral-7B) demonstrate that REP-LIE still achieves competitive performance compared to existing approaches.

</details>


### [204] [Clearing the Underbrush: AI-Enhanced RF Interference Suppression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24974)
*Rahul Jain,Pierre Trepagnier,Rick Gentile,Joey Botero,Alexia Schulz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在已有的基于自回归Transformer的RF干扰抑制方法基础上，新增有限标量量化(FSQ)分词器层，提出RF Transformer + Tokenizer架构，并在QPSK语音信号(感兴趣信号)与DVB-T2数字电视信号(OFDM干扰)的实验场景下，验证了该模型在干扰抑制质量上的优势，同时通过混合精度(AMP)与Torch-TensorRT等推理优化技术在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin边缘平台上实现了低延迟的实时通信应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 战术边缘场景下的软件定义无线电面临严格的体积、重量与功耗(SWaP)约束，且传统干扰抑制方法(匹配滤波、LMMSE、 SIC等)对结构化干扰效果欠佳或计算量过大。深度学习能够同时学习感兴趣信号(SOI)与干扰信号的特征，无需了解干扰信号的设计细节或信道传播条件，从而实现更优的干扰抑制效果。然而现有AI方法的延迟与吞吐量难以满足战术边缘实时通信的需求，需要在保持干扰抑制性能的前提下提升推理效率。

Method: 1. RF FSQ分词器：通过MLP、下采样、上采样与Transformer块的组合，将连续RF数据映射为有限离散值的紧凑表示，并以MSE重建损失训练。2. RF Transformer + Tokenizer架构：在原有RF Transformer基础上，在输出端接入FSQ分词器以恢复连续信号，采用自回归生成机制、旋转位置编码、实部虚部堆叠及随机时移/相位旋转增强。3. 训练流程：对QPSK语音SOI和DVB-T2干扰进行预处理(带通滤波、采样率对齐、幅度控制、 SINR混合)，先训练分词器，再训练Transformer预测分词化表示。4. 推理优化：使用PyTorch AMP(FP16/BF16)与Torch-TensorRT进行核融合加速编译。实验在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin与H100 NVL上进行，对比RF WaveNet、RF Transformer Decoder与RF Transformer + Tokenizer的延迟与吞吐量。

Result: 1. 音频质量指标(PESQ、SDR、LSD、Mel-CD)及可懂度指标(STOI、eSTOI)显示，AI模型全面优于传统方法，RF Transformer + Tokenizer在SINR低至-6 dB时仍保持良好干扰抑制效果，显著优于其他模型。2. 延迟方面：Jetson上B=1时RF WaveNet与RF Transformer Decoder延迟均小于1秒，RF Transformer + Tokenizer约5秒。3. 优化后效果：Torch-TensorRT FP16使RF Transformer Decoder推理时间提升1.2-5.7倍，在B=16时整体延迟92 ms，输出吞吐约1.2 MHz，超过705.6 kHz输入采样率，满足实时通信需求。4. RF Transformer + Tokenizer因参数规模较大(约2.81亿)且包含自回归循环，吞吐较差且Torch-TensorRT编译失败，适合对延迟容忍度更高的数据传输场景。
