Conclusion: 本文成功将AI增强的干扰抑制技术向实用化推进，验证了在数字调制SOI与数字调制干扰场景下，FSQ分词器的引入能够提升RF Transformer的干扰抑制性能。通过AMP与Torch-TensorRT等推理优化技术，在战术边缘GPU平台上实现了低延迟、高吞吐的实时抗干扰通信能力。该方法无需干扰信号与信道的先验知识，可广泛应用于战术通信、频谱共享(如5G与军用雷达S波段共存)及其他商业场景。

Abstract: AI-based structured interference rejection has grown more popular because deep learning approaches can outperform traditional methods by jointly considering the signal of interest (SOI) and the signal mixture (SOI plus interference). This work builds on a previous AI-enabled approach utilizing autoregressive transformer-based models by adding a Finite Scalar Quantization (FSQ) tokenizer layer which aims to improve the interference rejection performance while keeping overall latency to a minimum. Additionally, we experiment with other inference optimization techniques with the goal of speeding up inference without much accuracy loss. We explore this space with an experiment where the SOI is a digitally modulated radio frequency (RF) signal and the structured interference is a digital television signal, an extremely common type of Orthogonal Frequency-Division Multiplexing (OFDM) transmission. Our results achieve low latency and increased interference rejection over traditional techniques and prior work with other AI-enabled methods. We demonstrate the benefits of the AI-enabled approaches via audio metrics such as Perceptual Evaluation of Speech Quality (PESQ). Additionally, we explore a variety of applications and detail how our interference rejection algorithm may be used in operationally-relevant scenarios.

</details>


### [205] [D$^3$-MOPD: Adaptive Dynamic Domain ScheDuling for Efficient Multi-Teacher Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24987)
*Zechen Sun,Zhiwei Zhang,Fei Zhao,Juntao Li,Mu Chuan,Huayu Deng,Guojian Zhan,Wenliang Chen,Yao Hu,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 D³-MOPD，一种面向多教师在线策略蒸馏（MOPD）的零开销动态域调度器。它复用训练过程中已产生的逐域 reverse-KL 信号，异步调整域间采样比例，使计算资源从已收敛域流向仍在学习的域。在 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 学生模型蒸馏自四个领域教师（数学、代码、指令跟随、工具使用）的实验中，D³-MOPD 关闭了 97% 的学生-教师性能差距（vanilla MOPD 为 63%），以约 3× 更少的 rollout 步骤达到相同峰值性能，并在七个基准测试中的三个上超越专家教师。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vanilla MOPD 在训练前固定各域的数据混合比例，但学生对不同领域的收敛速度差异极大（部分域早期即趋于平稳，部分域则在全部训练预算内持续提升）。固定混合导致对快收敛域的计算浪费，并使慢收敛域训练不足。这一"静态混合 vs. 异步收敛"的不匹配是系统性效率瓶颈。

Method: 方法核心包括三个组件：（1）分层数据源（stratified data source）：根据目标混合比例严格分配每批次样本，并加入批次级抖动（jitter）保证 batch-to-batch 变化；（2）进程外监听器（off-process watcher）：异步读取训练日志中已记录的逐域 reverse-KL 值，不修改核心训练循环；（3）复合信号调度规则：将"剩余 KL 差距"（归一化的初始 KL 比例）与"下降速度"（最近 R 个非重叠窗口的 KL 相对下降均值，clip 到非负）相乘得到 s_k = KL̃_k · v_k；再经 max-归一化后通过带温度 T 和逐域下限 ε 的 softmax 映射为合法混合比例 p_k。训练循环完全不变，仅替换数据加载器并启动监听进程。

Result: 在 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 学生、四个领域教师（Math/Code/IF/Tool-use）的设置下：D³-MOPD 关闭 97% 的学生-教师归一化差距（vanilla 为 63%）；峰值平均准确率 62.34（vanilla 为 61.36），仅需 95 步即达峰（vanilla 需 143 步），仅需 47 步即超过 vanilla 的最优平均分 61.4，实现约 3× 加速；在 HMMT(N)、IFEval、OJBench C++ 三项基准上超越专家教师（normalized score > 1.0）。在 Qwen3.5-4B 学生上的迁移实验同样验证了有效性（归一化得分 1.01 vs. 0.86）。消融实验表明：复合信号中 velocity 与 gap 两部分均不可缺（R=3 的速度平滑优于 R=1，批次抖动 η=0.30 优于 η=0），吞吐量几乎无差异（520 vs. 531 tokens/GPU/s）。分析显示 D³-MOPD 自然形成隐式课程——对每个域在其可提升窗口集中分配计算资源。

Conclusion: 本文识别并解决了 MOPD 中静态域混合与异步域收敛之间的系统性不匹配。D³-MOPD 通过零开销调度器将训练循环已计算的 reverse-KL 信号转化为动态数据混合，无需修改核心训练循环，可与任意损失函数和教师改进正交兼容。理论分析（指数衰减模型下的命题 1）证明了复合信号近似于归一化 KL 的边际下降量，从而作为贪心最优分配准则。实验与消融均证实该方法在峰值性能、收敛速度和模型规模可迁移性上的一致优势。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) distills several domain-expert teachers into a single student by minimizing per-domain reverse-KL divergence on the student's own rollouts. Existing approaches typically fix the per-domain data mixture before training, overlooking the fact that different domains converge at substantially different rates: some plateau early while others continue to improve throughout the training budget. A fixed mixture therefore wastes compute on fast-converging domains and undertrains slower-converging ones. To address this, we propose D$^3$-MOPD (Dynamic Domain ScheDuling for MOPD), a zero-overhead scheduler that repurposes the per-domain reverse-KL signal already produced during training to adapt the domain mixture online. Running asynchronously outside the training process, an off-process watcher periodically tracks each domain's KL trajectory, estimates remaining headroom and current improvement rate, and accordingly adjusts the domain sampling ratios without altering the core training loop. Our D$^3$-MOPD scales naturally to arbitrary numbers of domains, and the expected benefit grows as more domains introduce more diverse convergence patterns for the scheduler to exploit. On a Qwen3.6-35B-A3B student distilled from four domain-expert teachers, D$^3$-MOPD closes 97% of the average student-to-teacher performance gap, compared with 63% for vanilla MOPD, reaches the same peak performance with an approximately 3$\times$ reduction in rollout steps, and surpasses the specialist teachers on three of seven benchmarks.

</details>


### [206] [DeMMO: Longitudinal and Cross-Disease Modelling of Digital Mobility Outcomes via Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25073)
*Menghui Zhou,Zhipeng Yuan,Vitaveska Lanfranchi,Po Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeMMO是一个可解释的多任务学习框架，用于纵向建模数字移动结局（DMO）与多种临床结局在跨疾病（PD、MS、PFF）中的关系。其核心创新是从纵向系数映射中自动学习带符号的跨疾病、跨结局关系图，在不共享受试者的前提下实现选择性信息共享。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DMO研究多为单疾病单次就诊的横断面分析，且现有时序多任务学习框架虽能建模单疾病进展，却无法联合建模跨疾病的多预测结局，尤其在疾病队列受试者互不重叠时存在根本局限。Mobilise-D大规模纵向数据集的发布为解决此问题提供了契机。

Method: DeMMO将每个疾病-结局目标表示为纵向DMO系数矩阵，结合融合Lasso时序平滑、稀疏组Lasso进行稳定与访视特异性特征选择。核心技术贡献是从纵向系数映射直接学习对称零对角的关系矩阵A，实现跨疾病、跨结局的选择性知识迁移。优化采用交替优化策略：固定A时用加速近端梯度更新系数矩阵，固定系数矩阵时将关系学习转化为6维自由参数（4个目标的6条边）的闭式二次问题求解。

Result: 在Mobilise-D数据集（24个DMO、5次访视、4个临床结局：PD H&Y、PD MDS-UPDRS III、MS EDSS、PFF SPPB损伤）上，DeMMO整体nMSE=0.722±0.032、wR=0.515±0.030，显著优于9个强基线（Ridge、Lasso、FRoTS、MAGPP、MLP-MSE/L1、Rank-N-Contrast、RankSim、ACCon），其中整体nMSE和wR相对最强基线有统计学显著提升（p=0.0015, p=0.0148）。20个结局-访视任务中DeMMO在18个排名第一或第二、12个唯一最佳。稳定性选择识别出可靠的纵向DMO模式：MS EDSS主要依赖cadence P90和长时段步行速度P90，PFF选取更广泛的步速/节律/步行量混合，PD H&Y持续选择10-30秒步行段的平均速度。关系图显示PD两结局间正相关最强（0.49），其次为MS-PFF（0.47）。两个非凸变体（var1/var2）性能均劣于凸版本。

Conclusion: DeMMO提供了一个可解释的纵向DMO建模框架，能够在非配对疾病队列间选择性共享信息。DMO相关性在访视间相对稳定但在结局间差异显著，提示应采用结局特异性的DMO组合而非通用面板。所识别的DMO模式是临床验证的候选指标，未来工作需在独立队列、其他疾病和更长随访中评估其泛化性。

Abstract: Digital mobility outcomes (DMOs) derived from wearable sensors characterise mobility in daily life and offer a promising means of monitoring disease progression. Yet most DMO studies examine one disease at one visit; they do not model how multivariate DMO relationships with multiple clinical outcomes evolve jointly across diseases. Technically, existing temporal multi-task frameworks can model progression within an individual disease, but they do not jointly model multiple prediction outcomes across diseases, particularly when disease cohorts do not share participants. To address these gaps, we propose DeMMO, an interpretable framework for longitudinal, multi-disease, and multi-outcome learning. DeMMO represents each disease-outcome objective by a longitudinal DMO coefficient matrix and combines temporal regularisation with stable and visit-specific feature selection. Its central technical contribution is an automatic cross-disease and cross-outcome relation-learning mechanism that learns signed relations directly from these longitudinal mappings, enabling selective information sharing without paired participants. We evaluate DeMMO on the recently released, large-scale, multicentre Mobilise-D dataset, which provides a new opportunity to study 24 harmonised real-world DMOs over five visits across multiple mobility-limiting conditions. Against nine strong linear, longitudinal, and deep-regression baselines, DeMMO achieves the best overall and outcome-specific prediction performance, with significant improvements over the strongest baselines. Stability selection further identifies reliable longitudinal DMO patterns for subsequent clinical validation and disease monitoring. The implementation code and experimental results are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [207] [NVExplain: Explaining Time Series Forecasting with Latent Trajectory Analysis and Structure-Preserving Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25080)
*Muyan Anna Li,Manikandan Ravikiran,Aditi Gautam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Time series forecasting models are widely used in high-stakes settings, yet their predictions remain difficult to interpret because existing post-hoc methods often ignore temporal dependence and fail to provide horizon-specific explanations. We propose a model-agnostic explainability framework that explains forecasting predictions by attributing each forecast horizon to temporally relevant historical lags. The framework models forecasting as a latent trajectory and introduces semantic flow to quantify how information evolves across time in the model's internal representations. By aggregating semantic flow, it constructs a lag-horizon attribution matrix that captures horizon-resolved temporal influence. To improve explainability, we further generate structure-preserving perturbations and fit sparse local surrogate models, producing human-readable and temporally coherent explanations. We evaluate the method using faithfulness and stability diagnostics across multiple benchmark datasets. Results show that the semantic-flow variant achieves competitive or superior faithfulness compared to standard post-hoc baselines, while being substantially more computationally efficient. Stability analysis further demonstrates that the explanations are robust and identifies regimes where interpretation should be applied with caution.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI enhancement unavailable (llm_error). Original arXiv metadata is preserved. Error: AI returned empty structured response

Method: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Result: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (llm_error). Original arXiv metadata is preserved. Error: AI returned empty structured response

Abstract: Time series forecasting models are widely used in high-stakes settings, yet their predictions remain difficult to interpret because existing post-hoc methods often ignore temporal dependence and fail to provide horizon-specific explanations. We propose a model-agnostic explainability framework that explains forecasting predictions by attributing each forecast horizon to temporally relevant historical lags. The framework models forecasting as a latent trajectory and introduces semantic flow to quantify how information evolves across time in the model's internal representations. By aggregating semantic flow, it constructs a lag-horizon attribution matrix that captures horizon-resolved temporal influence. To improve explainability, we further generate structure-preserving perturbations and fit sparse local surrogate models, producing human-readable and temporally coherent explanations. We evaluate the method using faithfulness and stability diagnostics across multiple benchmark datasets. Results show that the semantic-flow variant achieves competitive or superior faithfulness compared to standard post-hoc baselines, while being substantially more computationally efficient. Stability analysis further demonstrates that the explanations are robust and identifies regimes where interpretation should be applied with caution.

</details>


### [208] [The Von-Neumann State-Space Transformer for neural decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25088)
*Morteza Sarafyazd*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VN-SST（冯·诺依曼状态空间Transformer），通过将Transformer的固定FFN替换为低秩指令库（共享基础算子+K个低秩指令），并由携带的SSM低维状态解码的每token代码合成实际权重矩阵。在三个运动皮层神经解码基准和两个文本基准上，VN-SST在数据/参数稀缺场景下显著优于现代Transformer，且能将32指令库压缩至每个token仅约2.5-3.2比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1. 皮层计算呈显著低维性：少量潜变量驱动高维神经元响应；2. 神经解码需数据/参数高效模型；3. 标准Transformer的FFN对所有token应用相同算子，缺乏token特异性；4. 现有soft-MoE仅混合输出而非混合算子，不符合冯·诺依曼"存储程序"思想。

Method: VN-SST架构三大核心：(1) 三路并行记忆通路——局部注意力（因果窗口）、选择性SSM（携带慢潜状态）、delta-rule快权重关联记忆；(2) 低秩指令库替代固定SwiGLU：W(t)=W₀+Σcₜ,ₖUₖVₖᵀ，代码cₜ从低维SSM读出yt^ssm经MLP+tanh生成；(3) 跨段携带状态(s,M)，截断BPTT训练。三角色映射：SSM/快权重=记忆，低维状态读出=指令指针，MLP=控制器，programmable SwiGLU=执行单元。

Result: 1. 参数缩放（~25%数据）：MC_RTT R²=0.351 vs 0.207；MC_Maze 0.716 vs 0.655；Area2 0.696 vs 0.632。2. 数据缩放（~150K模型，4档预算）：VN-SST在所有基准所有预算下均不低于Transformer，数据越稀缺优势越大。3. 上下文缩放（L=16→128）：MC_RTT上Transformer从0.61降至0.57，VN-SST从0.60升至0.68。4. 控制比特诊断：K=32时仅用2.55-3.20 bits（3.4-6.5有效算子），R²在K上几乎平坦。5. 语言任务：tiny-Shakespeare PPL 49.4 vs 60.6，WikiText-2 45.1 vs 52.1，幂律斜率更陡。

Conclusion: 当数据/模型稀缺时，有效的归纳偏置并非单纯更多参数，而是由低维动态引导的可编程算子——正如皮层运行于少量精选指令。可复用贡献：(1) 参数高效方式使FFN计算可按token编程；(2) 可量化训练模型实际使用了多少程序。程序容量是控制通道而非精度杠杆。

Abstract: Cortical computation is strikingly low-dimensional: a handful of latent variables, carried in a neural population's activity, steer the higher-dimensional responses of individual neurons. Our aim is sample efficiency-models that decode well from limited data and at small parameter budgets. In a standard Transformer layer, the feed-forward block applies the same operator to every token. We suggest a von-Neumann inspired hypothesis of efficient computation as an alternative for neural decoding: a controller decodes an instruction and then executes a token-specific operator; the usual realization-a soft mixture of experts-only blends their outputs, not operators. We introduce a von-Neumann State-Space Transformer (VN-SST), a memory-augmented Transformer whose feed-forward block is a low-rank instruction bank: a shared base operator plus a small set of learned low-rank instructions, from which a per-token code synthesizes the weight matrix actually used at that token. The code is read from a low- dimensional projection of a carried state-space memory, so a slow latent trajectory acts as an instruction pointer-mirroring how low-dimensional dynamics may route cortical computation. On three motor-cortex neural-decoding benchmarks, VN-SST is far more data-efficient than a modern Transformer, each jointly predicting spikes and decoding behavior. This model wins by a wide margin on the scarcest benchmark, leads on the other two, and turns longer context into rising rather than falling accuracy. We evaluated that the network compresses a large instruction bank to a few bits per token, so program capacity acts as a control channel, not an accuracy lever. The same model is also more parameter-efficient on two small text benchmarks used for language modeling (LLMs), suggesting a generic mechanism.

</details>


### [209] [Understanding the Energy Scaling of Large Language Model Inference Across Context Lengths and Attention Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25096)
*Molka Chkir,Syed Muhammad Danish,Jos Höll,Arghavan Asad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对四种采用不同注意力机制的开源大语言模型（OPT-1.3B、Phi-3 Mini、Gemma-2-2B、Mistral-7B）进行了系统性的解码阶段能耗实证研究，揭示了注意力架构是决定解码能耗随上下文长度扩展的主导因素，而非模型规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）推理阶段的能耗和环境影响日益受到关注，据估计推理可能占模型全生命周期能耗的90%。现有研究多聚焦训练阶段或聚合能耗测量，缺乏对不同注意力机制（MHA、GQA、GQA+SWA）在解码阶段能耗扩展特性的系统表征，难以指导能效导向的架构与部署选择。

Method: 在Narval集群的NVIDIA A100-SXM4-40GB GPU上，采用FP16精度和贪心解码，使用NVML硬件计数器测量GPU能耗。评估四个模型在128、512、1024、1800 tokens上下文长度，1、2、4、8批量大小下，分别测量：每token能耗、每token延迟、每请求延迟。区分解码与预填阶段，每组配置重复10次，测量前后各设20ms稳定期，并以2次预热推理稳定GPU状态。

Result: 主要发现包括：(1) 注意力机制是解码能耗随上下文扩展的主导因素——MHA模型能耗增长17-18%，而GQA模型仅增长3-4%，GQA+SWA几乎恒定；(2) 模型规模决定绝对能耗基线，较小MHA模型可能比较大GQA模型每token能耗更低；(3) MHA模型在生成过程中存在2-10%的序列内能耗漂移（KV缓存增长所致），GQA漂移极小（≤2%），GQA+SWA漂移几乎为零；(4) 批处理（batch size 1→8）一致地将每token能耗和每请求延迟降低高达87%，是评估中最有效的部署级优化；(5) 长上下文下MHA模型的批处理收益衰减（128→1800 tokens时从84%降至67%），而GQA模型保持82%以上。

Conclusion: 注意力机制设计与模型规模在推理能效中承担互补角色：前者决定上下文扩展成本，后者决定绝对能耗基线。GQA及GQA+SWA在长上下文场景中显著优于MHA；批处理是跨模型跨上下文均有效的最强优化手段，可同步降低能耗与延迟。研究为面向能效的LLM架构选择与部署配置提供了量化依据。

Abstract: The growing adoption of large language models (LLMs) has raised increasing concerns about the energy consumption and environmental impact of inference. This paper presents a systematic empirical study of decode-phase energy consumption across representative open-source LLMs employing Multi-Head Attention (MHA), Grouped Query Attention (GQA), and Grouped Query Attention with Sliding Window Attention (SWA) to characterize how attention architecture influences decode-phase energy consumption under varying inference workloads. We evaluate four models across different context lengths, batch sizes, and generation workloads while measuring GPU energy using NVIDIA hardware counters. We examine the effects of context length, attention mechanism, Key-Value (KV) cache growth, and batching on decode-phase energy consumption. Results show that attention mechanism is the primary factor governing how decode energy scales with context length. MHA models exhibit substantially steeper energy growth than GQA models, whereas GQA with SWA maintains nearly constant energy consumption. We further show that model size primarily determines absolute energy consumption, while batching reduces both energy per generated token and request latency by up to 87%. These findings provide practical guidance for selecting energy-efficient LLM architectures and inference configurations.

</details>


### [210] [Flower Hub: A Reproducible Benchmarking Platform for Federated Learning in Simulation and Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25114)
*Yan Gao,Mohammad Naseri,Javier Fernandez-Marques,Dimitris Stripelis,Lorenzo Sani,Davide Eynard,Fan Zhang,Hong Jia,Ting Dang,D. B. Emerson,Fatemeh Tavakoli,Ole Werger,Lars Wulfert,Petros Demetrakopoulos,Sofia Tsekeridou,InSeo Song,KangYoon Lee,Honghao Li,Lingjuan Lyu,John P Dickerson,Daniel Janes Beutel,Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Flower Hub，一个面向联邦学习的开源可复现基准测试平台，通过将基准应用封装为可执行、版本化的标准化包，实现模拟与部署环境间的无缝切换，并配套发布了一个涵盖医疗、金融、法律、安全和音频五大领域的联邦学习基准套件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前联邦学习（FL）基准测试面临三大核心挑战：(1) 评估基础设施与算法代码紧耦合，难以复现和扩展；(2) 评估主要依赖模拟，与真实部署环境差距大；(3) 缺乏统一的基准打包格式，各基准沦为孤立项目。此外，许多FL论文仍在CIFAR-10、MNIST等中心化数据集上评估，无法反映真实FL部署中的统计异构性、数据分散性和系统限制。

Method: Flower Hub构建在Flower框架之上，通过解耦应用逻辑与基础设施来实现其核心设计：(1) 开发者只需编写ServerApp（服务器端聚合策略）和ClientApp（客户端训练与数据流水线），无需关心底层分布式通信；(2) 提供统一的`flwr run`命令，使同一基准应用无需修改代码即可在模拟运行时（Simulation Runtime）和部署运行时（Deployment Runtime）间切换执行；(3) 采用标准化打包结构，包含固定依赖版本、显式配置（任务相关和系统相关）和机器可读元数据，并支持版本控制与自动兼容性识别；(4) 内置系统监控框架，实时采集通信开销、内存占用、训练延迟等系统级指标。论文同时构建了五个现实场景的基准任务（跨机构+跨设备），评估了六种主流联邦优化算法（FedAvg、FedProx、FedAvgM、FedAdam、FedAdagrad、FedYogi），并通过跨区域部署实验（服务器在加拿大，客户端分布在美国、波兰、西班牙、日本）验证了平台的可部署性。

Result: 五个基准任务分别为：医疗图像分割（BraTS-GLI，5客户端跨机构，Dice评分78.57%）、金融欺诈检测（PaySim，5客户端跨机构，PR-AUC 56.40%）、法律指令微调（5个法律NLP源，5客户端跨机构，F1 72.97%）、钓鱼URL检测（合并两数据集，100客户端跨设备，ROC-AUC 98.48%）和音频标注（UrbanSound8K，50客户端跨设备，准确率52.56%）。在所有任务中，FedProx表现最佳或接近最佳，FedAvg和FedAvgM表现相似且收敛行为相当；FedOpt系列（FedAdam、FedAdagrad、FedYogi）表现普遍欠佳或不稳定，原因是其对超参数敏感，尤其在极端类别不平衡（如金融欺诈）或强非IID条件下。系统性能方面，3D U-Net（医疗）通信开销最高（3.85 GB），MLP（金融）通信最低（0.01 GB）；数据预处理和通信开销在端到端延迟中占比显著。部署实验表明，所有基准应用均可在跨区域真实环境中无需修改代码地成功执行，通信成本随轮次近似线性增长。

Conclusion: Flower Hub将FL基准测试从一次性、孤立的研究代码提升为可移植、可执行、可复用的基准应用，通过解耦应用与基础设施、标准化打包格式和无缝的模拟-部署切换，填补了FL基准生态中系统交换层的空白。其五大跨领域基准为社区提供了基础参考。局限在于目前侧重基线评估而非性能极限，尚未涵盖个性化、隐私、鲁棒性等FL设置，部署实验规模有限。未来工作将扩展基准套件、纳入更广泛评估，并支持大规模长期部署。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) has emerged as a key approach for training models across decentralized data, yet benchmarking in FL remains difficult to reproduce, compare, and extend. Existing evaluations are often tied to custom infrastructure, released as incomplete research code, and conducted primarily in simulation, which limits portability and practical relevance. We present Flower Hub, a platform for publishing, discovering, and executing decentralized and federated applications. We show how it enables reproducible benchmarking by packaging benchmarks as executable, versioned applications with standardized metadata, pinned dependencies, and explicit evaluation workflows. We instantiate this approach with a multi-domain benchmark suite spanning cross-silo and cross-device settings, and including tasks in medical imaging, financial tabular learning, legal instruction tuning, phishing URL detection, and audio tagging. We further demonstrate that the same benchmarking application can run across both simulation and deployment runtimes without changing the application code, enabling unified evaluation across varying learning environments. Beyond model quality, our benchmark design supports system-aware reporting, including runtime and communication metrics. This work advances benchmarking in FL settings from ad hoc code artifacts towards portable, executable, and reusable benchmark applications.

</details>


### [211] [GRAPE: Gradient Refinement and Progress-Aware Exploitation for Query-Efficient High-Dimensional Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25116)
*Richard Cornelius Suwandi,Feng Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRAPE是一种用于高维贝叶斯优化的两阶段框架，通过梯度精炼和进度感知利用来提高查询效率。它解决了现有方法过度关注下降概率而忽视下降幅度的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高维贝叶斯优化方法通常优先考虑下降概率而非下降幅度，导致过于保守的步骤仅产生微小改进，浪费了查询资源。在昂贵的黑盒函数优化场景中（如对抗攻击和LLM提示优化），这种效率损失尤为突出。

Method: 提出两阶段局部BO框架：（1）梯度精炼阶段使用闭式采集函数最大化梯度方差的单步缩减来锐化局部梯度后验；（2）进度感知利用阶段通过最大化下降条件下的期望减少量来选择更新方向。使用Matérn-5/2核高斯过程进行建模，结合投影梯度上升求解方向优化。

Result: 在黑盒对抗攻击任务上（MNIST d=784, CIFAR-10 d=1024），相比基线方法平均加速5.4倍；在LLM提示优化任务上，最终平均遗憾比次优方法降低3.8个对数单位。消融实验表明进度感知利用是更关键的设计选择。

Conclusion: GRAPE通过耦合梯度精炼和进度感知利用，为高维黑盒优化提供了查询高效的解决方案。理论证明保证了梯度精炼的单调不确定度缩减和进度感知方向向真实最速下降方向的收敛性。然而仍存在初始化敏感性、GP推理的立方复杂度等局限性。

Abstract: Optimizing expensive, high-dimensional black-box functions remains a central challenge in modern machine learning and scientific discovery. While local Bayesian optimization mitigates the curse of dimensionality, existing techniques often prioritize the probability of descent over the magnitude of progress. This leads to overly conservative steps that yield negligible improvement, wasting queries on directions that are nearly certain to descend but offer little decrease. We introduce Gradient Refinement and Progress-Aware Exploitation (GRAPE), a two-stage framework that first sharpens the local gradient posterior via a closed-form acquisition function, then selects update directions by maximizing the expected decrease conditional on descent. Theoretical analysis proves that this gradient refinement stage monotonically minimizes local uncertainty and that the progress-aware direction converges to true steepest descent as the posterior sharpens. Empirically, GRAPE demonstrates superior query efficiency across high-dimensional tasks: in black-box adversarial attacks, it achieves an average 5.4$\times$ speedup over baselines, and on large language model prompt optimization tasks, it outperforms the second best method by a reduction of 3.8 log-units in the final average regret.

</details>


### [212] [Toward Machine Learning with the Unit as a Primitive: Learning from Unit-Linked Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25118)
*Heyang Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将"单位"（persistent individual）作为监督学习的显式语义原语，主张学习任务应首先声明持久参照体及其"同一性"标准，再考虑响应律族与共享结构；形式化了一个由分词器与共享响应律形式组成的可学习对 $(T_\phi, R_\theta)$，并分离了预言机预测值、证据可达性、学习器近似和单行不可区分性四个层面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准监督学习以样本 $(x_i, y_i)$ 为基本对象，持久个体（患者、用户、设备等）及其同一性关系通常隐含在元数据或建模惯例中。这种隐式处理在涉及同一体的多次事件、固定个体下的多查询、或事件到个体归因不确定时会造成概念模糊，特别是无法区分"世界是均匀的"与"观测到的行级律仅仅是异质族的边际"。

Method: （1）将单位 $u$ 提升为任务语义层原语：任务先声明持久参照体的总体 $\mathcal{U}$ 及同一性准则，再选择学习对象；（2）监督学习语义对象为单位条件响应律族 $\{P_u^\star(dy|x)\}$，均匀性为特例；（3）假设1（共享形式）：所有单位通过上下文单位 token $Z_u^c = T_\phi(\mathcal{O}_u, c)$ 经由同一共享响应律形式 $R_\theta$ 进行预测；（4）结构化类要求 $R_\theta$ 对 token 的依赖是简单关系，线性预测器 $\eta = \alpha_\theta(x,c) + \langle\psi_\theta(x,c), z\rangle$ 为运行实例（定义2）；（5）学习对象为 $(T_\phi, R_\theta)$ 对；（6）区分直接单位访问（受信解析器查表）与单位溯因（从事实证据形成同类 token）；（7）提出四个层面：预言机价值 ≠ 预测可达性 ≠ 学习对实现 ≠ 组件可识别性；（8）证明：价值-可达性分解（命题1）、端到端 KL/TV 误差界（命题2、引理3）、单行不可区分性与链接配对分离（命题4）、行/单位加权差异与按记录/按单位划分（推论5、6）。

Result: （1）证明在响应充分性条件下预言机价值分解为可达价值与残留价值（命题1），且可达项受单位-证据互信息上界限制；（2）证明端到端预测 KL 散度上界为单位信念误差与固定单位响应律误差之和（命题2，公式38），并给出 TV 距离的查询局部误差传播界（引理3）；（3）证明单行未链接观测无法区分受限异质世界与均匀混合世界（每样本 Bernoulli(1/2)），但受信同单位配对通过协方差 1/16 vs 0 提供一致性检验（命题4）；（4）证明行加权与单位加权风险仅在记录重数相等时一致（推论5），按记录划分以正概率混合已知单位新事件与未见单位（推论6）；（5）将上述框架与 DiscoSCM 因果特例进行对象级对应，明确因果估值需要附加跨世界耦合假设；（6）以推荐系统为实例展示：用户 ID 提供查表 token $z_\theta(k)$，物品映射与用户 token 的内积构成线性运行实例。

Conclusion: 单位原语把"哪个持久个体"与"该个体此刻发生什么"分离为两个建模问题，使监督学习语义对象自然成为单位条件响应律族；通过共享形式假设与 token 化结构可将该族压缩为可学习对 $(T_\phi, R_\theta)$。均匀性、混合性、记录重复与单位分解是独立概念层：均匀性是世界侧响应族性质，单位不敏感是学习侧规格性质，行加权与单位加权对应不同估计量，未链接单行观测原则上不能区分受限异质与均匀混合，而受信同单位配对提供受限正边界。实际评测应同时报告单位信念质量、固定单位响应质量与部署边际预测质量，并在单位不相交划分下检验新单位泛化。

Abstract: Machine learning is usually formalized through samples, while the persistent individual to which multiple observed or possible events refer often remains implicit. We propose the \emph{unit} as an explicit primitive at the level of task semantics. A learning task first declares a population of persistent referents and a sameness criterion; the realized value $u$ denotes the selected referent. Supervised learning is the main formal specialization. Its semantic object is a family of unit-conditioned response laws. Homogeneity is the special case in which those laws coincide; a sample-only conditional is silent as to whether the world is homogeneous or the observed law is only the marginal of a heterogeneous family. What is learned from data is a pair $(T_\phi,R_\theta)$: a tokenizer that produces a contextual unit token and one shared response-law form that reads it. The structured class takes that form to be a simple relation in the token; a linear predictor is the running instance. The token is the learner-side representation through which the task-side unit affects prediction, while a learner specification that omits unit information is unit-insensitive; homogeneity remains a property of the world-side response family. When identity is unresolved, the world-side law mixes unit-conditioned targets, while the learner composes its shared form with a token. A trusted resolver may fix the unit and supply a lookup token; otherwise \emph{unit abduction} forms a token of the same type from factual evidence. Unlinked single-row observations can fail to distinguish a heterogeneous unit world from a homogeneous pooled world; trusted same-unit pairs separate a restricted witness. The formal results concern this supervised specialization.

</details>


### [213] [When Does Context Routing Help? A Systematic Study of Multi-Modal Fusion in Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25128)
*Ruizhe Zhou,Gaoyuan Du,Xiaoyang Liu,Haoqi Yao,Deepayan Chakrabarti,Jiating Lin,Yixuan Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文系统性地研究多模态时间序列预测中辅助上下文何时能真正提升预测性能。作者提出两个必须同时满足的数据集级条件：(1)目标序列不被"最后值捷径"主导（低自相关），(2)上下文相对于历史对目标具有非零的条件互信息（δ>0）。通过在MoME（14.3B参数MoE模型）和4种融合机制的受控实验，论文证明当两条件满足时文本条件专家调制带来显著MSE降低（+29%至+51%），否则贡献坍塌至调制通路的容量下限。通过添加捷径（抑制77–93%）和渐进式破坏上下文质量（从+44%降至负值）两项因果干预验证结论。在27个Monash数据集上验证了自相关诊断（Spearman r=0.888），并提供TRY/SKIP/INCONCLUSIVE三态预训练诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态时间序列预测方法通过日益复杂的融合机制集成辅助上下文，许多工作报告了大幅提升，但不清楚这些提升是来自上下文信息的真实利用，还是架构的附带效应（额外参数、正则化、残差连接）。论文聚焦一个更窄、可检验的问题：辅助上下文在何种条件下能帮助预测器？

Method: 1) 信息论理论基础：定义h步自相关ρ_h和条件互信息δ=I(C;X_{t+h}|X_t)；Fact 1（分布无关零条件：δ=0时任何预测器都无法受益）和Fact 2（捷径上限：MSE_shortcut=σ²(1-ρ_h²)）；高斯假设下推导RBU上界公式。2) MI估计：Kraskov k-NN估计器(k=3) + 200次置换检验 + PCA降维。3) 实验：MoME（Qwen1.5-MoE-A2.7B，14.3B参数，EiLM调制可切换）+ PatchTransformer测试床（4种融合机制，捷径可控）。4) 三种因果干预：切换调制/添加捷径（3数据集×2×2设计）/渐进破坏上下文。5) 大规模验证：27个Monash数据集。

Result: 主要发现：(1) MoME在6个数据集上的路由贡献范围从容量下限到+51%(HealthUS)，4个受益大的数据集共享ρ<0.8+信息性文本特征；高自相关数据集(ρ≥0.99)贡献处于容量下限。(2) 添加捷径使MoME路由贡献在3个数据集上被抑制77-93%。(3) 渐进破坏上下文质量使上下文特定收益从+44%(干净)降至负值(50-75%掩码)。(4) 4种融合机制中，MI非显著数据集均无正向收益；MI显著数据集部分机制显示小幅正贡献(+2-5%)。(5) 线性回归探针在所有数据集上对上下文增益为零或负，证明上下文-目标关系本质非线性。(6) Monash数据集自相关与捷径主导性强相关(Spearman r=0.888, p<0.0001)。(8 数据集诊断验证零误判，INCONCLUSIVE处理低功效场景。

Conclusion: 两个数据集级条件同时成立时辅助上下文才真正帮助多模态时间序列预测：(1)目标不被最后值捷径主导，(2)上下文具统计信息量。论文提出可校准的预训练诊断(TRY_FUSION/SKIP_FUSION/INCONCLUSIVE)，在测试数据集上功效充足设置中无假阳性。实践启示：低质量上下文比无上下文更差(部分破坏使模型被误导)；高自相关数据(ρ>0.95)应使用简单基线而非复杂融合；融合架构应去除或控制残差捷径以准确评估上下文价值。论文明确承认证据不对称性：负向(SKIP)判断广泛可靠，大幅正值仅来自单一模型族(MoME)，测试床仅在方向上佐证。

Abstract: Multi-modal time series forecasting methods integrate auxiliary context into temporal predictions through increasingly sophisticated fusion mechanisms. A growing body of work reports substantial gains, yet it is often unclear whether they reflect genuine use of the context or incidental architectural effects. We ask a narrower, checkable question: when can auxiliary context help a forecaster at all? We identify two dataset-level conditions that must both hold: (1) the target is not dominated by a last-value shortcut (low autocorrelation rho_h), and (2) the context carries information about the target beyond history (non-zero conditional mutual information delta; when delta=0 no predictor can benefit---a distribution-free result). Through controlled experiments on MoME (a 14.3B-parameter mixture-of-experts model, 6 datasets, 10 seeds) and four additional fusion mechanisms implemented within a single-backbone testbed (5 datasets), we find that when both conditions hold, text-conditioned expert modulation contributes a sizeable MSE reduction; when either fails, the contribution collapses to the capacity floor of the modulation pathway and carries no context-attributable signal. We establish causality through two interventions: adding a shortcut to MoME suppresses routing contribution by 77-93% across 3 datasets; progressively corrupting context quality drives the context-specific benefit from +44% to negative. We validate the autocorrelation component of our diagnostic on 27 Monash Archive datasets. We provide a calibrated pre-training diagnostic that, on the datasets we test, yields no false positives in well-powered settings. We are explicit about the asymmetry of our evidence: the negative arm is broadly reliable, while the large positive magnitudes come from a single model family (MoME) and are corroborated only in direction by the testbed.

</details>


### [214] [Drift Variation Autoencoder: Unifying Generation and Representation Learning through Conditional Posterior Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25138)
*Jiarui Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Drift Variation autoencoder (DVA) 提出一个统一的框架,将条件生成和表征学习视为同一统计目标——给定随机观测 C 的后验 P(X|C)——的两种不同表现。通过带掩码的编码器和条件 Flow Matching 干净预测损失同时训练,并证明了在仿射高斯路径下,clean-prediction 表征间隙 Δ_rep^x(E)=0 与后验充分性 P(X|Z)=P(X|C) 等价。该框架自然扩展到多模态场景,要求完整模态元组始终作为 Flow 目标。在 CrossGeom-4 基准上通过受控实验证实了联合目标注意力能显著降低共享未观测因子上的不一致性(90.1%-92.8%)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机掩码、裁剪或模态移除使确定性重建成为一个不完整的目标:同一观测可能对应多种合理补全。传统流水线将生成和表征学习作为独立目标分别优化,缺乏对表示正确性作为生成器所建模的不确定性自然结果的保证。本文旨在回答:一个普通生成目标能否同时刻画并训练它所使用的表征?即 P(X|Z)=P(X|C) 这一后验充分性条件能否作为单一训练目标的副产品出现。

Method: 方法核心是 Drift Variation autoencoder 框架:1) 将后验 P(X|C) 定义为共同目标;2) 条件 KL 分解为 I(X;C|Z) 表征缺陷和生成器近似;3) 通过仿射高斯路径 X_t=α_t X+σ_t X_0 和干净预测损失 R_w(E,D)=E[w(t)‖D(X_t,t,Z)-X‖²] 同时训练编码器 E_θ 和条件 Flow 解码器 D_φ;4) 推导风险正交分解为路径方差、表示缺陷和模型近似;5) 利用高斯去噪器恒等式和特征函数论证证明 Δ_rep^x(E)=0 当且仅当 P(X|Z)=P(X|C);6) 零噪声端点处的精确条件场生成 P(X|Z),从而在联合最优下生成 P(X|C);7) 将结果扩展到连续多模态乘积空间,要求完整模态元组始终为 Flow 目标。

Result: 在 CrossGeom-4(18 次受控运行,三种几何层级×两种解码器×三种种子)上:1) 可观测因子的线性探针 R² 达 0.9990-0.9992;2) 相同初始 ODE 噪声下,打乱联合模型的有效编码器条件使条件误差增加 13.5×-15.7×;3) 联合目标注意力相对独立目标解码器将两个输出共享的未观测因子不一致性降低 90.1%-92.8%;4) 可见模态被生成和重建,直接验证完整元组目标;5) 条件因子的 W1 误差降低 61.2%-76.9%;6) 联合解码器将无条件跨模态因子不一致性从 1.598-1.667 降至 0.177-0.213(降低 86.6%-89.4%)。但无条件模式均衡仍不完美(TV 距离 0.086-0.108,高于有限样本参考约 0.034),限制了实证主张的范围。

Conclusion: 本文证明了一个统计原理与采样目标的精确零集等价:条件 Flow Matching 的干净预测风险在编码器依赖项上与剖面化条件 KL 具有相同的后验充分零集。生成和表征学习因此在同一总体风险的不同项上同步,但 Flow Matching 与 KL 在数值上并不相等。多模态扩展要求完整模态元组始终作为 Flow 目标,使得跨输出残余依赖而非仅逐模态保真度成为显式的实证要求。CrossGeom-4 支持这一有限模型机制,但无条件模式不平衡和合成规模使主张范围较窄。下一个实证步骤是在自然数据上评估相同的后验充分瓶颈是否带来语义和密集迁移的提升。

Abstract: Stochastic masking, cropping, or modality removal makes deterministic reconstruction an incomplete target: one observation can admit many clean completions. This work takes the corresponding posterior $P(X\mid C)$ as the common statistical object for conditional generation and generatively sufficient representation learning. Drift Variation autoencoder trains a masked encoder $Z=E(C)$ and a conditional flow decoder with one clean-prediction Flow Matching loss. The analysis first decomposes the ideal conditional KL into generator approximation and the representation deficiency $I(X;C\mid Z)$. It then derives orthogonal risk decompositions for conditional Flow Matching. For an affine Gaussian path, the clean-prediction representation gap is zero if and only if $P(X\mid Z)=P(X\mid C)$. Thus the encoder-dependent excess clean-prediction risk induced by Flow Matching and the profiled ideal conditional KL have the same posterior-sufficient zero set, without being numerically equal objectives. An exact conditional field with a zero-noise endpoint then generates $P(X\mid Z)$ and hence $P(X\mid C)$ at a joint ideal optimum. The result extends to continuous multimodal product spaces when the complete modality tuple remains the Flow target for every observation mask. On CrossGeom-4, an 18-run controlled benchmark, observable factors have linear-probe $R^2$ of $0.9990$-$0.9992$, shuffling the joint model's encoder condition increases conditional error by $13.5\times$-$15.7\times$, and joint target attention reduces disagreement on an unobserved factor shared by two outputs by $90.1$-$92.8\%$ relative to independent target decoders. Visible modalities are also generated and reconstructed, directly validating the full-tuple objective. Unconditional mode balance remains imperfect, delimiting the empirical claim to a controlled multimodal proof of concept.

</details>


### [215] [Bayesian Flow Networks for Offline Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25163)
*Ludvig Killingberg,Helge Langseth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BFN-RL，基于贝叶斯流网络(BFN)的离线强化学习统一生成建模框架，可在单一概率框架下原生处理离散与连续轨迹规划。通过分类规划器生成未来状态序列,并结合学习的逆动力学模型将其转换为动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的序列建模离线RL方法在连续控制中表现出色,但难以原生处理离散规划任务(需要离散/分类分布而非标准高斯构造),因此需要一个能统一处理离散与连续空间的生成框架。

Method: 核心方法采用BFN,通过迭代演化分布参数而非对加噪数据进行去噪。利用输入分布、输出分布、发送分布和接收分布的概念,在训练时最小化KL散度,在生成时执行贝叶斯更新。使用无分类器引导方式以return为条件,并使用inpainting技术固定当前状态。分类任务使用分类BFN,连续任务使用连续BFN(采用一阶求解器)。动作通过学习的逆动力学模型从生成的状态序列中获得。

Result: 在5个离散任务(MiniGrid三任务、FrozenLake-8x8、Sokoban-7x7)中,BFN-RL与匹配的分类扩散基线总体竞争力相当,在Sokoban上一致优于基线(成功率提升9.7个百分点)。在D4RL连续控制任务中,BFN-RL平均得分77.0,与Decision Diffuser(81.8)及其他SOTA方法竞争力相当。验证了BFN作为分类与连续域统一规划框架的有效性。

Conclusion: BFN-RL确立了贝叶斯流网络作为离散与连续轨迹规划的通用生成基础。共享框架自然扩展到联合连续-分类环境,其中每个轨迹变量可在单一模型内分配对应分布。当前状态通过inpainting固定,return条件避免额外分类器的需要。结果支持BFN作为跨数据模态离线轨迹规划的通用框架。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) leverages static datasets to learn decision policies without real-time environment interaction. While recent sequence-modeling approaches rely on continuous diffusion models for trajectory synthesis, applying these methods to discrete planning tasks requires a categorical formulation rather than the standard Gaussian construction. We present BFN-RL, a unified generative modeling framework for offline RL based on Bayesian Flow Networks (BFNs). By iteratively evolving distribution parameters rather than noisy data instances, BFN-RL natively models both discrete and continuous trajectory spaces within a single probabilistic formulation. The categorical planner generates future state sequences, and a learned inverse-dynamics model converts consecutive generated states into actions. Evaluations in discrete planning and continuous control show that BFN-RL can generate effective trajectories across both categorical and continuous state spaces. Our results establish BFNs as a versatile generative foundation for offline trajectory planning across data modalities.

</details>


### [216] [Hyperbolic Latent Geometry for Tree-Structured Prototype Networks: A Local-vs-Global Trade-off](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25199)
*Peter Flo,Luca Grossmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在层次分类问题中，原型网络的类别原型应放置在欧氏空间还是 Poincaré 双曲球上，以最优地满足树结构正则化而不损害分类似然。基于 WikiArt 数据集（27 种艺术风格、81,446 幅画作）和冻结的 CLIP ViT-B/16 特征，进行了 150 次种子复现的正则化最大似然拟合，扫描嵌入维度、曲率和正则化强度。研究发现一个稳健的局部效应：双曲原型在最近邻图中显著更好地保留树的拓扑结构（兄弟召回率@5 提升 +8.7 pp，堂兄弟召回率提升 +15.2 pp），该优势在三种参考树定义（手工谱系、CLIP 派生、DINOv2 派生）下均成立。然而，在全局树保真度上，结论对参考树和指标选择不稳定，作者不宣称优胜者。欧氏原型与原始 CLIP 特征上的逻辑回归在分类上持平，表明其对潜在几何没有可检测的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多实际分类问题具有外部指定的类标签层次结构（如艺术风格演化、生物分类）。将层次信息融入概率分类模型的自然方式是向类别原型的布局添加树结构正则化。本文探讨的核心问题是：对于带有此类正则化的原型分类器，原型的潜在流形选择（欧氏空间 vs. 双曲 Poincaré 球）是否影响正则化的满足程度与数据似然之间的权衡。这一问题具有理论基础——双曲空间的指数体积增长与树的指数增长相匹配，理论上应能以更低失真嵌入树结构。

Method: 1. **数据与特征**：使用 WikiArt-Refined 数据集（27 种艺术风格、81,446 幅画作），采用冻结的 CLIP ViT-B/16 提取特征，缓存为 float16 张量。
2. **模型架构**：两层 MLP（带 GELU 和 dropout）将 CLIP 特征映射到潜在流形（欧氏 R^d 或曲率为 c 的 Poincaré 球 B^d_c），输出层对原型距离做 softmax 得到类别条件似然。欧氏与双曲分支仅在最终变换和度量上不同，其余骨干网络、MLP 宽度、dropout、批大小、优化器和调度均共享。
3. **树结构正则化**：对原型两两距离矩阵 D 与参考树距离矩阵 T 的"形状"做均值归一化后的平方误差惩罚，强度由 λ 控制。λ=0 退化为交叉熵基线。
4. **参考树**：三种手工参考树（默认谱系、按年代分组、扁平零假设），加两种从 CLIP/DINOv2 类均值特征凝聚聚类得到的数据驱动经验树（仅用于评估）。
5. **优化**：Adam 优化 MLP 头部，Riemannian Adam（geoopt）优化双曲原型。正则化器在每步对 351 个非对角类对计算一次。
6. **扫描配置**：150 次运行（3 种子 × 多配置），扫描嵌入维度 d∈{2,4,8,16,32,64}、曲率 c∈{0.1,0.3,1,3}、正则化强度 λ∈{0,0.1,0.3,1,3}。
7. **评估指标**：分类（top-1、top-5、平衡准确率）、全局树保真度（Spearman 相关、平均/最坏乘性失真）、局部树保留（兄弟/堂兄弟召回率@k，k∈{5,10}），并与原始 CLIP 特征上的逻辑回归和 k-NN-5 基线进行校准比较。
8. **统计检验**：在维度扫描的 18 个（维度, 种子）对上进行配对 t 检验。

Result: 1. **局部树结构**：双曲原型显著更好地恢复局部树结构。在默认树上，d=8 时兄弟召回率@5 双曲为 0.195，欧氏为 0.149；在 18 个配对配置上均值差距为兄弟召回 +8.7 pp、堂兄弟召回 +15.2 pp，符号一致性 0.94，配对 t 检验 p < 10^-4。该优势在用 CLIP 或 DINOv2 经验树重新定义兄弟/堂兄弟集时仍然成立。双曲原型在 Poincaré 球的近边界处稳定下来（平均半径 0.561，边界 0.577），处于指数体积区域；欧氏原型则分散分布（平均半径 2.96）。
2. **校准**：逻辑回归在原始 CLIP 特征上达到 64.1% top-1，与欧氏拟合的 64.3% 统计持平；k-NN-5 在原始 CLIP 特征上兄弟召回率@5 为 0.160，与欧氏拟合的 0.149 持平但低于双曲拟合的 0.195。最佳欧氏配置（65.9% top-1）在兄弟召回率上低于 k-NN 基线；最佳双曲配置（60.6% top-1）在兄弟召回率上高于 k-NN 基线 2.8 pp。只有双曲拟合在局部检索上超越编码器基线。
3. **正则化强度扫描**：λ=0.1 时正则化在两种几何中均 Pareto 改善（欧氏 d=8 top-1 提升 0.7 pp 至 65.0%，树 Spearman 从 0.292 升至 0.345）。λ≥1 时分类崩溃（欧氏 46.9%，双曲 37.4%），但树 Spearman 升至约 0.7。双曲的兄弟召回率在所有 λ 下稳定，欧氏仅在最大 λ 下上升（此时分类已崩溃）。
4. **训练树与不平衡鲁棒性**：用年代树和平坦树训练，欧氏仍在所有树上赢 top-1，双曲仍赢兄弟召回率。逆频率加权训练保留几何差距。经验树构建中替换 DINOv2 编码器反转了全局 Spearman 的小差距方向，但保留双曲的局部召回领先。
5. **全局树保真度不稳定**：在完整维度扫描上，类中心/默认树 Spearman 的配对 t 检验 p=0.68，符号一致性恰好 0.50。平均树失真显著偏向欧氏（+0.040，p<10^-4），两种全局指标互相矛盾。替换为 CLIP 经验树反转 Spearman 方向；替换为 DINOv2 经验树再次反转。手工树与编码器派生树仅弱相关（ρ≈0.08, 0.12）。
6. **混淆结构**：两种模型的错误均集中在树相邻风格的对角块附近；双曲模型的块结构在对角线附近更柔和，更多混淆散布到直接兄弟风格。

Conclusion: 在树正则化原型模型中，潜在流形的选择对树保真度产生局部效应，即使全局效应不稳定。双曲原型几何是唯一在局部层次保留上有意义超越冻结编码器的训练模型（兄弟召回率@5 在 d=8 时：双曲 0.195 vs. 欧氏 0.149 vs. k-NN-5 0.160）。分类差距虽真实但不提供几何洞见——线性头部在相同特征上可达 64.1% top-1，表明欧氏原型距离不会扭曲分类，而双曲原型距离会。全局树保真度比较在参考树和指标间不稳定，作者明确不宣称胜者。该研究在真实层次分类问题上对两种自然潜在几何给出了经验性区分：双曲几何提供局部结构价值，欧氏几何不提供且在分类上与线性头部持平。局限性在于仅在 WikiArt（西方画作为主）和冻结 CLIP-ViT-B/16 编码器上验证，27 叶分类的层次深度有限；不能推广到微调编码器或更深层次。未来方向包括使用 Möbius 层实现真正双曲头部、在双曲空间中构建经验参考树、在更深层次（如 iNaturalist）上复现。

Abstract: We study a tree-structured regularizer over class-prototype layouts in a hierarchical-classification model and ask whether the choice of latent manifold for the prototypes (Euclidean R^d vs. the Poincare ball B^d_c) affects how well that regularizer can be satisfied without distorting the data likelihood. The two manifolds differ only in their volume growth: hyperbolic space grows exponentially with radius and embeds trees with provably lower distortion than R^d of matched dimension, so the structured regularizer should be cheaper to satisfy on B^d_c. Across 150 seed-replicated regularized maximum-likelihood fits spanning embedding dimension, curvature, and regularizer strength on WikiArt (27 styles, 81,446 paintings, frozen CLIP ViT-B/16 features), we find a single robust effect: Poincare prototypes preserve the topology of the nearest-neighbor graph in latent space substantially better than matched Euclidean prototypes (sibling recall@5 +8.7 pp, cousin recall +15.2 pp; paired-t p < 10^-4, sign agreement 0.94), and the gap holds across three reference-tree definitions (hand-built lineage, CLIP-derived, and DINOv2-derived). On classification, Euclidean prototypes are tied with logistic regression on raw encoder features, indicating no detectable contribution from the latent geometry; only the hyperbolic fit improves on a k-NN encoder baseline for local retrieval. Global tree-fidelity comparisons are unstable across reference trees and we do not claim a winner. The results give an empirical separation, on a real hierarchical-classification problem, between two natural latent geometries for a class-structured regularizer.

</details>


### [217] [Learning Mixtures of Plackett-Luce Models for Multi-Objective Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25200)
*Dongyue Li,Ziniu Zhang,Lu Wang,Hongyang R. Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MoPLEx算法，通过排序增强和梯度估计解决混合Plackett-Luce模型在多目标对齐中的不可识别性问题，并在效率和性能上显著优于基于Bradley-Terry的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AI对齐和偏好优化中，标注者往往来自异构子群体或采用多维评价标准，导致同一组候选响应产生不同的排序。单一排序模型或现有的Bradley-Terry(BT)混合模型无法准确捕捉这种异质性偏好，且当排序长度m≤2k−1时混合模型存在不可识别性(m为排序长度，k为簇数)，限制了其识别真实簇结构的能力。

Method: 本文提出MoPLEx算法，包含两个核心组件：(1) 排序增强——利用基础语言模型生成额外候选响应并将其追加为部分排序底部的未排序项，以增加排序长度m'至满足m'−m≥k的可识别性条件；(2) 基于梯度的估计——在输入嵌入空间对模型输出做一阶泰勒近似，仅对a个锚点响应执行完整前向传播，其余m−a个响应的得分通过梯度近似估计，从而将计算复杂度从O(km)降至O(ka)。基于这两个步骤，设计了融合EM算法来拟合混合Plackett-Luce模型，通过交替进行E步(计算后验聚类概率)和M步(最大化加权对数似然)进行优化。模型以k组LoRA适配器形式在基础语言模型上实现。

Result: 在UltraFeedback和Persona数据集上，MoPLEx的聚类准确率平均提升43.7%，排序准确率平均提升15.2%，显著优于MiCRo、MaxMin-RLHF、EM-DPO等基于BT混合模型的基线。梯度近似在最高340亿参数的CodeLlama-34B模型上误差仍低于5%。计算效率方面，运行时和GPU内存开销分别降低最多3倍和3倍；当a=6时可将排序长度扩展至m=32，相比完整PL训练在m=20时再获4.6%性能提升。消融实验验证了PL模型、生成响应、混合结构和梯度估计各组件的必要性，且高采样温度(约2.0)有利于生成多样化响应。

Conclusion: 本文提出的MoPLEx算法成功解决了混合排序模型在排序长度不足时的不可识别性理论限制，并通过梯度估计大幅提升了计算效率。实验证明该方法在多目标对齐场景下显著优于现有方法，为处理异构偏好的多路排序建模提供了一种可扩展且理论可靠的框架。未来工作方向包括：(1)考虑生成响应可能排名更高的更精确概率估计；(2)动态调整簇数以适应持续学习场景；(3)将该方法扩展到多轮对话中用户偏好动态演化的场景。

Abstract: We consider the problem of learning a mixture of $k$ Plackett-Luce models given multi-way ranking responses from annotators that may represent heterogeneous underlying preferences. This problem has many applications in AI alignment and preference optimization. Prior work has studied mixtures of Bradley-Terry models from pairwise comparisons. However, uncovering mixture models is theoretically unidentifiable when $k$ exceeds $m/2$, where $m$ is the length of a ranking. We propose an efficient implementation to address this limitation, which involves first augmenting the rankings to a larger size by generating new responses from a base language model, followed by a gradient-based estimation to reduce inference cost in the input embedding space. Based on this procedure, we then design an expectation-maximization algorithm with these two steps to fit a mixture of Plackett-Luce models, called MoPLEx. Extensive experiments are conducted to verify this approach. First, we show that the gradient-based approximation estimates true probabilities with less than 5% error on models with up to 34 billion parameters. Second, we show that MoPLEx improves clustering and ranking accuracy by an average of 43.7% and 15.2% over baselines using single ranking and mixtures of Bradley-Terry models, on preference optimization datasets. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of MoPLEx for tackling multi-way rankings from heterogeneous preferences through measuring alignment between gradients.

</details>


### [218] [Output Dilution: Redundant but Fragile Representations in MoE Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25231)
*Orion Reblitz-Richardson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了MoE架构中存在一种"输出稀释"现象：尽管MoE模型在道德内容编码上与密集模型探测准确率相当（均达99%），但其编码脆弱性高4.2倍，根源在于MoE块对残差流的贡献规模比密集MLP小约74倍，导致信号极易被噪声淹没，且训练过程中专家从未出现道德特化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究MoE架构是否通过将表征空间划分为离散专家模块而实现道德特征的专家级特化，从而为对齐干预提供天然切入点。若道德特征均匀分布于所有专家，则MoE与密集架构在对齐研究中无实质区别；若集中于特定专家，则可利用专家剪枝、专家微调等干预手段。

Method: 以OLMoE-1B-7B（64专家、top-8路由、6.9B总参数）为研究对象，密集OLMo-2 1B作为架构对照。采用240对道德基础理论探针数据集，通过：(1)逐专家线性探针分析（1024个专家-层组合）；(2)高斯噪声扰动下的脆弱性测试（σ∈{0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0,10.0}）；(3)三组分扰动实验（路由器/专家输出/聚合输出）；(4)前馈输出规模测量；(5)11个训练检查点（5K至1.2M步）的轨迹分析。全部实验在MacBook Pro M4 Pro（24GB）上运行。

Result: 四大核心发现：(1) 64个专家在16层中均匀编码道德内容（基尼系数<0.03，1020/1024专家探针>75%准确率），无特化现象；(2) OLMoE的道德编码脆弱性比OLMo-2高4.2倍（平均临界噪声σ*=0.92 vs 3.81），尽管峰值探测准确率均为99%；(3) MoE块前馈输出规模平均比密集MLP小74倍（最低5×、最高167×），原因是top-8聚合稀释了每个专家的贡献——这直接解释了脆弱性差距；(4) 训练轨迹显示道德编码从最早检查点（step 5K）即存在（92.1%准确率），基尼系数始终<0.03，top-5专家排名Jaccard相似度≈0.09（近随机），特化从未出现。路由器对道德内容无偏好（最大1.8%），且自身最稳健（σ*=9.56）。

Conclusion: MoE架构不创造道德特征的专家特化，反而因稀疏聚合机制产生结构性脆弱性：信息内容完整保留但信号规模被稀释约两个数量级，使探测准确率与编码安全性出现巨大背离。这一发现表明：(a) 探测准确率作为独立对齐指标不足，必须配合脆弱性测试；(b) 特征冗余是训练动力学而非架构同质性的产物，负载均衡损失可能加剧均匀化倾向；(c) MoE模型的微调可能因相对扰动更大而产生更大脆弱性偏移；(d) 在稀疏架构中，冗余编码不等于鲁棒编码。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models appear to encode moral content as robustly as dense models, yet prove far more fragile in their encoding. In OLMoE-1B-7B, linear probes recover moral valence from nearly every expert-layer combination, with mean peak-layer accuracy above 90%. But these representations collapse under levels of activation noise that a dense model of matched size easily tolerates, with a 4.2-fold difference in robustness. We trace this to output dilution. Because the MoE block averages across active experts before contributing to the residual stream, the feedforward signal reaching downstream layers is nearly two orders of magnitude smaller than in a dense MLP. Moral information, our interest, survives aggregation intact but at a scale trivially overwhelmed by perturbation. Routing itself remains stable under noise while the vulnerability originates entirely in the diluted aggregate. Checkpoint trajectories confirm this is architectural, not learned. Experts never specialize and accuracy saturates within the first few thousand steps. In sparse architectures, redundant encoding does not imply robust encoding.

</details>


### [219] [Neural-Bayesian Structure Learning for Discrete Choice Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25258)
*Hyunsoo Yun,Eun Hak Lee,Jiaru Zhang,Ziran Wang,Eui-Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Neural-BSL 将可微分结构学习与基于随机效用的离散选择模型联合训练，将选择结果保留在图外以避免互斥性扭曲属性结构，并支持通过拓扑顺序进行干预传播，预测交通方式分担及下游旅客/出行属性变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离散选择与机器学习模型仅基于观测数据，将协变量视为平行输入，缺乏内部机制确定属性间的因果依赖结构，无法准确预测干预情形下属性间的连锁变化。

Method: 提出 Neural-Bayesian Structure Learning (Neural-BSL) 框架：(1) 在单一可微分过程中耦合可微分结构学习与随机效用离散选择估计；(2) 将观察到的选择结果置于图外，作为替代方案特定的效用比较，以避免互斥选择结果扭曲属性结构；(3) 属性结构与随机效用参数联合学习；(4) 学习到的结构通过结构加权的属性交互进入选择模型；(5) 干预评估按拓扑顺序更新被干预属性及其下游属性，重计算效用与选择概率。

Result: 使用首尔陈述偏好数据与伦敦显示偏好数据进行评估：(1) 预测性能与常规基准模型相当；(2) 可恢复行为学上一致的依赖结构；(3) 在政策场景中，通过学习到的结构传播干预改变了模式分担的预测再分布，并揭示了这些响应背后的下游旅客与出行调整。

Conclusion: Neural-BSL 成功地将可微分因果结构学习融入离散选择建模，能够在保持竞争力的预测性能同时，提供行为学可解释的属性依赖结构与干预传播机制，为交通等领域的政策评估提供结构化因果推断工具。

Abstract: Conventional discrete choice and machine learning models are estimated primarily from observational data and typically treat explanatory covariates as parallel inputs, providing no internal mechanism for determining how related attributes should adjust when one is deliberately changed. This paper proposes Neural-Bayesian Structure Learning (Neural-BSL), a framework coupling differentiable structure learning with random-utility-based discrete choice estimation in a single differentiable procedure. To prevent mutually exclusive choice outcome from distorting the recovered attribute structure, the observed choice is maintained outside the graph as an alternative-specific utility comparison, while the attribute structure and random-utility parameters are learned jointly. The learned structure enters the choice model through structure-weighted attribute interactions and provides the structural basis for propagating interventions through downstream attributes. An intervention is evaluated by updating the intervened attribute, propagating its model-implied downstream changes in topological order, and then recomputing utilities and choice probabilities. This yields both predicted mode-share responses and the associated changes in downstream traveler or trip attributes. We evaluate Neural-BSL using stated-preference data from Seoul and the revealed-preference data from London. Neural-BSL achieves predictive performance comparable to conventional benchmarks while recovering behaviorally coherent dependency structures. Across policy scenarios, propagating interventions through the learned structure changes the predicted redistribution across modes while exposing the downstream traveler and trip adjustments underlying those responses.

</details>


### [220] [SHSP: Structure-Aware Hierarchical Solution Prediction for Mixed-Integer Linear Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25282)
*Zherong Zhang,Guanlin Li,Chengrui Gao,Haopu Shang,Ke Xue,Jixiang Lu,Weiyong Yang,Chao Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SHSP（Structure-Aware Hierarchical Solution Prediction）是一种针对混合整数线性规划（MILP）的学习型求解加速框架，核心创新在于将传统的"一次性并行边际解码"范式替换为基于变量耦合图的分层条件解码机制，按耦合强度从弱到强顺序解码变量，并引入置信度感知的mask-and-repair机制缓解错误累积，同时设计结构感知的变量固定策略。在四个标准MILP基准上与三种下游学习引导搜索方法集成，平均解间隙减少54%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MILP学习型解预测方法普遍采用一次性（one-shot）并行解码所有变量的边际概率，变量间的条件依赖只能通过GNN的消息传递隐式捕获，建模组合结构的负担完全落在GNN的表征能力上，导致预测出相互冲突的赋值，反而误导下游求解器。论文旨在通过显式建模变量间的结构依赖关系来克服这一局限。

Method: 1) 构建变量耦合图（Variable Coupling Graph）：从约束结构出发，通过"期望违约"和"系数重要性"两个互补启发式因子计算变量对的耦合强度；2) 结构感知分层预测：基于变量耦合评分（VCS）将变量按弱到强分为K个层次，逐层解码，每层条件化于前面层次的预测；3) 置信度感知的mask-and-repair机制：以与置信度成反比的概率随机mask低置信度预测并收集到修复集，最终在完整上下文下重新预测修复；4) 结构感知的变量固定策略：优先固定高耦合评分且高置信度的变量，通过共享约束收紧其他变量的可行域。训练时采用teacher forcing策略从1.0线性衰减到0。

Result: 在Combinatorial Auctions (CA)、Workload Appointment (WA)、Item Placement (IP)、Set Covering (SC)四个标准基准上，与Neural Diving (ND)、Predict-and-Search (PaS)、Apollo-MILP三种下游框架以及Gurobi、SCIP两个求解器集成，SHSP在CA上几乎完全消除原始间隙（PaS下减少99.7%，Apollo下100%），IP上减少94.3%，SC上减少42.1%，WA上减少38.1%；Apollo+SHSP在CA上甚至超过了Gurobi 3600秒预算的解质量。消融实验验证了mask-and-repair机制、分层预测器和结构感知固定策略三个组件各自的贡献。在MIPLIB的IIS子集上也验证了真实场景下的适用性。SHSP额外推理开销小于0.2秒，图构建时间0.26-15.23秒，均远小于求解时间。

Conclusion: SHSP通过将一次性并行解码替换为基于变量耦合图的分层条件解码，显式建模了MILP变量间的结构依赖关系，突破了现有GNN隐式捕获依赖的局限。其置信度mask-and-repair机制有效缓解了多步推理中的错误累积，结构感知固定策略进一步利用耦合结构收紧可行域。该框架可作为即插即用的替代模块与多种学习引导搜索方法集成，在四个标准基准和两个求解器上一致性显著优于一次性预测基线，平均解间隙减少54%，且额外计算开销可忽略。未来的工作方向包括以端到端方式学习耦合评分以及扩展到更广泛的问题类别。

Abstract: Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) is a fundamental optimization paradigm in combinatorial optimization and has been widely applied across real-world domains. Due to its NP-hard nature, obtaining optimal solutions for large-scale or highly constrained MILP instances remains computationally prohibitive. Learning-based solution prediction has therefore emerged as a promising approach to provide high-quality variable assignment for solver acceleration. However, existing methods typically adopt a one-shot prediction paradigm that predicts the marginal probabilities of all variables simultaneously. As a result, the conditional dependencies among variables are only implicitly captured through message passing, with the burden of modeling the combinatorial structure falling entirely on the representational capacity of graph neural networks. To address this limitation, we propose the Structure-Aware Hierarchical Solution Prediction (SHSP) framework that replaces the parallel marginal decoding of one-shot methods with a novel hierarchical conditional decoding mechanism. Specifically, SHSP constructs a variable coupling graph from the constraint structure, decodes variables sequentially along a hierarchy of increasing coupling strength, and conditions each hierarchy on previously predicted assignments. To mitigate error accumulation during the decoding process, SHSP further incorporates a confidence-aware mask-and-repair mechanism to identify and correct unreliable intermediate predictions. We integrate SHSP with multiple learning-guided search methods, and evaluate it on four standard MILP benchmarks. Experimental results demonstrate that SHSP significantly outperforms existing one-shot prediction baselines, achieving a 54% average reduction in solution gap.

</details>


### [221] [InsightSR: Refining Symbolic Regression Search Spaces via Parallel Semantic and Structural LLM Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25291)
*Yating Ling,Wenjing Cun,Zhitang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: InsightSR提出了一种将大语言模型(LLM)作为引导层嵌入PySR遗传编程引擎的符号回归框架，通过语义种子路径和结构特征路径双通道协同作用，主动重构搜索空间而非直接生成表达式，并结合闭环反馈机制实现迭代式自我修正的符号发现。在Feynman、LLM-SRBench和真实世界数据集三个基准上显著超越现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统符号回归方法在巨大的组合搜索空间中效率低下，且缺乏物理约束机制，导致生成的表达式虽数值准确但常违反物理规律。现有LLM增强方法存在两个局限：推理开销大，且仅沿单一维度（结构假设生成或输入空间变换）利用LLM引导，未能发掘两者协同潜力。

Method: 四阶段迭代闭环：(1)上下文初始化与单位合成——提取变量物理量纲，LLM推断模糊维度；(2)并行引导策略——语义种子路径生成维度一致的函数骨架（含算子复杂度偏置），结构特征路径推荐非线性特征变换并跨代累积；(3)PySR引导搜索——采用复杂度偏置机制和精英保留热启动；(4)多维评估与战略反馈——LLM评估候选表达式并分类特征效用，结果存入动态知识库用于下一轮迭代引导。核心是将搜索从原始变量上的深层树构建转化为富语义特征集上的浅层组合。

Result: Feynman基准达到95%精确恢复率（R²=0.9999），噪声鲁棒性优异；LLM-SRBench的LSR-Transform任务达到80.18%准确率（先前最优50.45%），各学科领域NMSE降低数个数量级；真实世界数据集上分布内/分布外测试均显著优于基线，Oscillator 1/2的ID NMSE达9.55e-11和2.45e-9。定性案例显示恢复表达式与真值高度匹配。

Conclusion: 通过将LLM用作搜索空间的主动重构器而非表达式生成器，结合语义与结构双路径协同及闭环反馈，InsightSR将符号发现从开放式生成转变为迭代式自我修正的精化过程，有效解决了物理一致性与搜索效率问题，为自动化科学发现提供了新范式。

Abstract: Symbolic regression (SR) seeks to discover parsimonious mathematical laws from observational data, yet conventional approaches often struggle with the vast combinatorial search space of physically meaningful expressions. We present InsightSR, a framework that embeds Large Language Models (LLMs) as a guiding layer around the PySR genetic programming engine. Rather than relying on LLMs to generate expressions directly, InsightSR uses LLMs to progressively transform the search space itself through two complementary pathways: a Semantic Seed Pathway that proposes dimensionally consistent functional skeletons, and a Structural Feature Pathway that recommends nonlinear feature transformations. These transformations accumulate over iterations, broadening the input space and shifting the symbolic search from constructing deep expression trees over raw variables to assembling shallow trees over a rich, semantically informed feature set. A post-generation feedback loop evaluates candidates, categorizes features by their empirical utility, and refines the guidance for the next iteration, transforming the discovery process from open-ended generation into iterative, self-correcting refinement. Across three benchmarks, InsightSR achieves a 95% exact recovery rate on the Feynman benchmark and 80.18% accuracy on the LLM-SRBench LSR-Transform task, substantially outperforming state-of-the-art genetic programming and neural-symbolic methods while maintaining strong out-of-distribution generalization on real-world datasets.

</details>


### [222] [Neither Precision Nor Architecture Alone: Controlled Tests of Failure Remedies for Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25327)
*Jinyuan Zhang,Peng He,He Hu,Yin Yuan,ShengShuo Jiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过受控对照实验，系统比较了两种针对PINN在刚性问题/对流主导问题上失败的修复方案——FP64精度提升与SSM+子序列对齐架构——发现二者作用于不同维度（精度作用于优化器停止测试，对齐作用于假设类），各自覆盖不重叠的PDE-种子切片，互不可替代，且响应具有强烈的种子特异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN在刚性问题和对流主导PDE上频繁失败。2025年两篇论文提出竞争性修复方案：Xu et al.(2025a)用SSM骨干+子序列对比对齐替代MLP，Xu et al.(2025b)用FP64替代FP32以修复L-BFGS停止伪影。但两种方案均未经同一对照框架联合验证，二者是否可互换、在何种条件下有效尚不清楚。

Method: 预注册对照实验：144次本地受控运行+85次独立验证运行，覆盖对流(β=50)、反应(ρ=5)、波动三类1D PDE。在匹配的PDE-种子单元内单因素变化精度(FP32/FP64)、内层L-BFGS tolerance_change、骨干(MLP/无对齐SSM/对齐SSM/PINNsFormer)、对齐权重α∈{0,100,300,1000,3000}。成功标准为未取整rL2<0.05。通过Proposition 1（精度约束停止下限）和Proposition 2（对齐作为超定约束）形式化两种修复机制的作用维度。所有结果按种子报告。

Result: 硬对流(β=50)上：无对齐SSM在两种精度下0/5成功，对齐SSM在FP32恢复2/5、FP64恢复3/5种子；FP64仅将MLP从0/5提升至1/5。反应(ρ=5)上：SSM骨干单独即达3/5-4/5，对齐达5/5。精度切换对个体种子产生相反方向影响；波动问题上FP64降低中位误差但成功率无统计显著增益(McNemar p=0.727)。收紧tolerance_change可降低中位误差但成功率不变，且运行时间增加数倍至数十倍。两种修复作用于不相交的regime-and-seed切片，互不可替代。

Conclusion: 精度与架构/对齐并非可互换的修复方案：精度作用于优化器停止测试的roundoff下限，对齐作用于假设类并通过超定约束消除bump型伪解。实践上应联合评估精度、停止、骨干、对齐，并按种子报告结果。FP64+适度tolerance是降低误差的高效点；收紧至10⁻⁹以下边际收益为负。无单一通用建议，per-seed reporting至关重要。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) frequently fail on stiff or advection-dominated PDEs, and two recent accounts offer competing remedies: switching from FP32 to FP64 to repair an L-BFGS stopping artifact, or replacing the MLP with a state-space-model (SSM) backbone plus sub-sequence alignment to counter architectural simplicity bias. We test both under matched, seed-paired controls in a pre-registered 144-run study spanning convection, reaction, and wave, plus an independent 85-run convection/wave study; success is relative $\ell_2$ error below $0.05$. The two remedies act on disjoint regime-and-seed slices: neither substitutes for the other. On hard convection ($\beta{=}50$), alignment recovers 2/5 seeds in FP32 and 3/5 in FP64, where the unaligned SSM succeeds on 0/5 seeds at either precision and the vanilla MLP moves only from 0/5 to 1/5 across the precision switch---the recoveries trace to the alignment objective, not the backbone. On reaction the backbone alone already succeeds on 3/5--4/5 seeds, so each remedy covers a regime the other does not. Responses are also seed-specific: the same precision switch flips individual seeds in opposite directions and, on wave, lowers median error with no statistically significant success gain. Tightening the inner L-BFGS tolerance in an independent repeated-step runner likewise lowers median error at a large runtime cost, with success counts unchanged. Precision, stopping, backbone, and alignment must therefore be evaluated jointly and reported per seed.

</details>


### [223] [Escaping Low-Dimensional Overlap: Multi-Task Model Merging via High-Dimensional Sparse Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25354)
*Yihang Zhang,Shengke Sun,Junjie Wen,Feng Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于稀疏自编码器（SAE）的高维稀疏解缠模型融合框架，通过将任务向量投影到高维稀疏特征空间进行特征级融合，并结合零阶优化的关键层选择策略，在多任务模型合并中有效缓解任务干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并方法（参数算术法如TIES-Merge、DARE，参数分解法如TSV、Twin-Merging）均在原始参数空间内操作，当任务特定特征高度纠缠时，无法有效分离有用任务方向与干扰成分，限制了高冲突场景下的合并性能。论文从机制可解释性中的"叠加"现象出发，将任务干扰归因于参数空间中特征的叠加纠缠。

Method: 提出"高维稀疏解缠融合"框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 改进的稀疏自编码器（SAE）：采用Top-K硬稀疏激活、残差拟合、字典归一化和正交正则化，将层任务向量投影到高维稀疏潜空间进行特征级融合；(2) 分组排序零阶优化器（GR-ZOO）：基于零阶梯度估计对层进行重要性排序，仅在关键任务层应用SAE解缠以降低计算开销；(3) 区分化参数融合策略：在稀疏潜空间中按余弦相似度区分共享特征与特有特征，分别采用均值融合与加法融合。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-1.5B和Qwen2.5-7B上的实验表明，该方法在数学推理、代码生成、指令遵循和通用知识任务上均优于Task Arithmetic、TIES-Merge、DARE、Fisher-Merge等基线。在Qwen2.5-7B三任务设置下平均得分达68.49；在Qwen2.5-1.5B高冲突四任务设置下平均得分36.48，相较最强基线DELLA提升2.78%。消融实验验证了SAE各组件、投影空间选择（优于PCA和随机投影）以及GR-ZOO层选择（接近全梯度上界）的有效性。

Conclusion: 论文从叠加现象角度揭示了模型合并中任务干扰的本质，提出的高维稀疏解缠框架通过将任务向量投影到高维稀疏特征空间进行融合，有效减少了低维参数空间中的特征重叠干扰。GR-ZOO进一步降低了计算成本，使方法具备实际可行性。实验证明该方法在多任务合并中达到最优性能，并在高冲突场景下表现出强鲁棒性。

Abstract: Model merging provides an efficient way to construct multi-task generalist models without additional training, but its performance often degrades under severe task interference. Task interference in model merging primarily stems from \textit{superposition}, where task-specific features become entangled within the parameter space. This entanglement renders conventional decomposition methods insufficient for effectively isolating useful task directions from interfering components. In this paper, we propose a sparse-representation-based merging framework that uses Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) to project task vectors into a high-dimensional sparse feature space, enabling feature-level disentanglement before fusion. To reduce computational overhead, we further introduce a lightweight Group-Ranked Zeroth-Order Optimizer (GR-ZOO) to identify task-critical layers for selective merging. Experiments on both Qwen2.5-1.5B and Qwen2.5-7B demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms representative baselines, including Task Arithmetic, TIES-Merge, DARE, Fisher-Merge,and several recent training-free merging methods, across mathematical reasoning, code generation, instruction following, and general knowledge tasks. In a highly conflicting four-task setting on Qwen2.5-1.5B, our method further achieves a 2.78\% improvement over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [224] [Refusal geometry reflects refusal training: diverse refusal prefixes can raise stable rank and weaken refusal vector ablation attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25390)
*Andrey Labunets*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以 OLMo-2-0425-1B-Instruct 为案例研究，揭示拒绝行为在激活空间中呈现低维几何结构源自安全训练过程：拒绝补全首 token 的重复会导致梯度和激活更新集中在低维子空间，从而降低残差的稳定秩，使单向量拒绝消融攻击更有效；而增加拒绝开头的多样性可提高残差稳定秩，削弱该攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究观察到对齐语言模型的拒绝行为可由单一方向或低维子空间介导，并可被拒绝消融攻击绕过。然而，为何安全关键特征会集中在如此低维的结构中尚不清楚。理解这一几何性质的训练起源，有助于设计对消融攻击更具鲁棒性的安全对齐方法。

Method: 方法结合理论分析与实证验证：(1) 推导首 token 交叉熵损失对最后一层激活的梯度，证明稳定秩由首 token 频率直接控制（引理 3.1：sr(E_r)=1/max f_k）；(2) 利用条件数给出跨层稳定秩传播的界（定理 3.2），并定义稳定秩传递因子 τ 与 top-k 源子空间共享映射及相对拟合误差 ϵ；(3) 在 OLMo-2-0425-1B-Instruct 上进行冻结模型子集分析和受控微调实验，操纵拒绝首 token 的多样性 m∈{1,...,16}，测量良中心残差 ΔH 的稳定秩及消融前后拒绝率/有害率变化。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 在 SFT 阶段即出现功能性拒绝方向，后训练阶段保持几何一致性但消融效果更强；(2) 梯度诱导激活更新 𝒢 与良中心残差 ΔH 在均值方向余弦、中心化右奇异子空间重叠和稳定秩三方面近似匹配（支持仿射子空间关系）；(3) 首 token 多样性增加时，E_r、P-E_r 和各层 G^(l) 的稳定秩均上升，τ^{15→7}≈2.16–2.27 表明存在跨层稳定秩放大；(4) 固定模型和化学领域微调实验中，sr(ΔH) 越高的子集/检查点，差分均值单向量消融的拒绝/有害率下降幅度越小；(5) 数据集首 token 多样性显著高于冻结模型实际生成的多样性。

Conclusion: 拒绝行为对消融攻击的脆弱性部分源于安全训练数据的首 token 分布：重复的拒绝开头（如"I'm"）使目标矩阵低秩，诱导低稳定秩的激活变化，形成近似一维的拒绝机制，易被单向量消融。增加拒绝补全首 token 的多样性是一种简单的"加固杠杆"，可提高激活残差的稳定秩，从而减弱差分均值消融攻击；但仅靠多样性并非完整防御。该工作建立了训练数据 token 级特性、梯度谱特性与激活几何之间以及攻击鲁棒性之间的联系。

Abstract: Refusal training protects AI models from jailbreaks by training models to decline unsafe queries, reducing the risk of misuse. Recent work finds that refusal behavior in aligned language models can be mediated by a single activation direction or a low-dimensional refusal subspace shared across harmful prompts: ablating those directions suppresses refusals while largely preserves other model capabilities. Yet it remains unclear why safety-critical features in a wide range of models emerge and concentrated, low-dimensional structure. In a case study of OLMo-2-0425-1B-Instruct we find that the refusal geometry reflects refusal training: activation updates resulting from refusal-completion first-token losses explain the resulting refusal direction and refusal subspace. We study refusal directions through the training dynamics across refusal datasets and reveal that their brittleness is associated with repetitive refusal starts, which in turn is linked to concentration of gradients and refusal features in a low-dimensional subspace. Across frozen-model analyses and controlled synthetic fine-tuning, we find evidence of a hardening lever: diverse refusal starts can raise stable ranks of gradients and activation changes, making refusals harder to remove with a vector ablation attack.

</details>


### [225] [Physics-Informed Foresight Pruning for Sparse PINN Solvers of Nonlinear PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25564)
*Ahmad Ishaque Karimi,Uvini Balasuriya Mudiyanselage,Kookjin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对物理信息神经网络(PINN)过参数化问题，提出了一种基于PDE残差敏感性的初始化前剪枝方法PI-SAP，并在Gray-Scott等非线性PDE上验证了其相较于传统NTK-SAP在残差保真度方面的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN依赖过参数化模型来优化耦合的解与微分残差目标，但其所需容量与剪枝准则尚不明确。现有NTK-SAP仅保留输出端训练动力学，可能忽略对PDE残差（涉及导数项）影响显著的参数。

Method: 提出PI-SAP（物理信息谱感知剪枝），通过PDE残差对掩码扰动的敏感性来计算参数显著性；并引入两个诊断扩展：条件感知NTK-SAP与PINN块感知SAP。架构基于PirateNet。

Result: 在Gray-Scott方程所有剪枝率下PI-SAP均改善平均PDE残差，在70%/90%高稀疏度下显著降低解误差与高频误差；Ginzburg-Landau、Burgers、Convection方程上无统一最优准则。诊断显示解精度、残差保真、核条件化是相互独立的目标。

Conclusion: PI-SAP在高稀疏度下对导数敏感型结构具优势，但稀疏PINN应视为多目标设计问题，需显式平衡解端与残差端训练动力学，未来工作应联合调节两者并引入交叉核项。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) often rely on over-parameterized models to optimize coupled solution and differential-residual objectives, leaving unclear how much capacity is necessary and what pruning should preserve. We study foresight pruning at initialization for sparse PirateNet PDE solvers. Standard neural tangent kernel spectrum-aware pruning (NTK-SAP) aims to preserve output-side training dynamics but may overlook parameters whose main influence arises through derivatives in the governing equations. We introduce physics-informed spectrum-aware pruning (PI-SAP), which assigns saliency using sensitivity of the PDE residual. Experiments on the Gray-Scott equations, complex Ginzburg-Landau equation, Burgers' equation, and linear convection equation show that PI-SAP more consistently preserves Gray-Scott residual fidelity and is competitive under aggressive sparsity. However, no criterion is uniformly optimal across equations or sparsity levels. Small-batch PINN-NTK diagnostics further show that residual fidelity, solution accuracy, and kernel conditioning are distinct objectives, motivating pruning methods that explicitly balance solution-side and residual-side training dynamics during optimization.

</details>


### [226] [LDAC-Net: A Learnable Multi-Lag Differencing Attention-Convolution Network for Drift-Robust Recognition with Low-Cost MOX Gas Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25646)
*Xin Zhang,Liangxiu Han,Yue Shi,Tam Sobeih*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 LDAC-Net，一种端到端可学习的多滞后差分注意力-卷积网络，直接处理低成本金属氧化物（MOX）气体传感器的多通道原始信号，无需外部手工差分预处理。其核心 LDFE 前端通过窗口条件统计仿射归一化（WSAN）补偿每窗口的基线漂移和尺度变化，并通过可学习多滞后差分（LMLD）跨多个滞后加权组合时间差分来暴露响应动态。紧凑的注意力-卷积主干随后建模局部瞬态和长程时间依赖。在 50 类 SmellNet-Base 任务上达到 68.2% Top-1 准确率，显著优于现有 FOTD 预处理基线，并在 SmellNet-Mixtures 和 62 通道 eNose-Drift 基准上验证了跨任务与跨传感器阵列的强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本 MOX 气体传感器信号受慢速化学瞬态、会话相关基线漂移、尺度变化和跨通道相关性干扰，现有管道依赖固定一阶时间差分（FOTD）预处理，需手工选择滞后且可能丢弃有用的绝对响应信息；漂移补偿与动态提取被解耦于识别模型之外。作者主张：传感器感知预处理应被学习并联合优化，而非使用手工差分。

Method: LDAC-Net 包含三大模块：（1）LDFE 前端——WSAN 通过零初始化 MLP 从每窗口的均值与标准差预测每通道仿射残差（"泄漏式"实例归一化），保留绝对响应水平中的类别信息；LMLD 在固定滞后集合 P={5,10,25,50} 上计算时间差分，通过可学习每通道权重、1×1 卷积跨通道混合和 SE 通道门控自适应加权组合。（2）注意力-卷积（AC）混合块——采用 Conformer 风格结构，结合 macaron FFN、多头自注意力和 ConvModule，使用随机深度正则化。（3）单查询注意力池化头。模型宽度 d=128，约 71.7 万参数。训练采用 AdamW、cosine 调度、mixup、TimeCutout、Channel Dropout 及 EMA，使用会话级 K 折交叉验证。

Result: 在 SmellNet-Base（50 类）任务上，LDAC-Net 取得 68.2% Top-1 / 89.4% Top-5 / 65.2 macro-F1，比最强 FOTD 基线高约 14.4 点，比原始 Transformer 高 30 点以上。消融研究证实 WSAN（-14.0 点）、LMLD（-5.8 点）、SE 门控（-6.2 点）、通道混合（-4.9 点）、单滞后（-4.6 点）均有显著贡献（p<0.05）。窗口长度 T=300 为最佳，从 T=100 的 57.8% 提升至 68.2%。在 SmellNet-Mixtures 上将 Top-1@0.1 从 45.4% 提升至 50.5%。在 62 通道 eNose-Drift 长期漂移基准上达到 70.6% Acc@1 和 69.6% macro-F1，比数据集调优 FOTD 基线分别高 8.0 与 3.0 点。在与非平稳 Transformer、Autoformer、TCN、Neural-ODE 的对比中也均为最优。

Conclusion: 实验证明：传感器漂移补偿与动态提取应通过可学习前端在网络内部完成，而非依赖外部固定 FOTD；可学习的多滞后差分在跨任务（成分比例预测）和跨传感器阵列（含强长期漂移）场景下均能良好迁移，展示了相对于手工差分的优势。当前主要限制在于训练数据规模较小（SmellNet-Base 约 1650 个训练窗口）以及分析窗口长度仍为全局超参数。未来工作将引入更大、更多样化的真实世界多会话数据集，并以可学习的输入自适应窗口替代固定 T。

Abstract: Portable electronic-nose systems based on low-cost metal-oxide (MOX) gas sensors offer a practical solution for gas and odour recognition, but their signals are affected by slow chemical transients, drifting sensor offsets, scale variation, and cross-channel correlations. Existing pipelines commonly use fixed first-order temporal differencing (FOTD), which requires a manually selected lag and may discard useful response information. We propose LDAC-Net, an end-to-end learnable multi-lag differencing attention-convolution network that operates directly on multi-channel MOX signals. Its learnable differential feature enhancement front-end combines window-conditioned statistical affine normalisation, which compensates for window-specific offset and scale variation, with learnable multi-lag differencing, which weights and combines temporal differences across multiple lags. A compact attention-convolution backbone subsequently models local transients and longer-range temporal dependencies. On the 50-class SmellNet-Base task, LDAC-Net achieves 68.2% top-1 accuracy, exceeding the best FOTD-preprocessed comparison model by approximately 14 percentage points and the raw-input Transformer by more than 30 points. Ablation studies confirm the contributions of both proposed components. The representation also transfers to SmellNet-Mixtures, improving accuracy from 45.4% to 50.5%, and generalises to the 62-channel eNose-Drift benchmark under strong long-term drift, achieving 70.6% top-1 accuracy and 69.6% macro-F1. These results outperform the best comparison model with dataset-retuned FOTD preprocessing by 8.0 and 3.0 points, respectively, demonstrating that learnable, sensor-aware preprocessing is more effective than fixed handcrafted differencing for low-cost MOX gas-sensor recognition.

</details>


### [227] [It's a matter of timescale: non-linear utility in successor features and multi-objective planning and learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25723)
*Liam P.H. Mertens,Lucas N. Alegre,Florent Delgrange,Diederik M. Roijers,Ann Nowé,Peter Vamplew*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出多目标强化学习与后继特征方法中现有优化准则（SER、ESR、RSR）各自仅在单一时间尺度上处理非线性效用，而真实问题常需同时在多个时间尺度上评估效用，揭示了该领域的一个显著空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多目标规划与强化学习中，SER（跨多次轨迹的长期平均效用）、ESR（单次轨迹的期望效用）、RSR/后继特征（单步即时效用）三种准则各自处理不同时间尺度的非线性效用，但现实决策问题（如毒性暴露）中的非线性效用效应可能在多个时间尺度上同时发生，现有方法均无法涵盖这一需求。

Method: 以毒性暴露为动机示例，定义急性（每日剂量）、亚急性/慢性（累积剂量）两种sigmoid型效用函数，构建3名员工×3任务的MOMDP调度问题，分别使用EUPG（ESR）、NLPPO（SER）、SFDQN（RSR）以及结合三者效用的MONES进化策略方法进行实验对比。

Result: 实验表明不同时间尺度准则导致截然不同的最优策略：SER和组合方法产生"牺牲"低技能员工的策略以最大化期望效用，而ESR和RSR则产生更均衡的轮换策略；当同时考虑多个时间尺度时，组合方法揭示出仅看单一尺度时无法发现的安全隐患，表明需要专门的多时间尺度联合优化方法。

Conclusion: 本文论证了多目标RL文献中存在一个重要空白——缺乏同时优化多个时间尺度上非线性效用的方法论与算法；提出未来方向应基于分布式返回值与奖励函数，并结合策略梯度、进化策略等技术解决这一复杂效用景观问题。

Abstract: Time is of the essence when dealing with multiple reward signals and non-linear utility. In this paper we argue that the current main approaches in multi-objectiveRL (SER and ESR), and successor features, are insufficient. While each approach deals with non-linear effects on user utility on different timescales, none of them take into account that different effects happening on different timescales can happen within the same decision problem. We motivate that this can indeed be the case by an example, both intuitively and numerically, leading to a new perspective, and a significant and non-trivial gap in the literature.

</details>


### [228] [TailSFT: Filtered Fine-Tuning Improves Post-Training Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25756)
*Sadhika Malladi,Samy Jelassi,Dylan Foster,Jordan T. Ash,Akshay Krishnamurthy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TailSFT 是一种简单改进的监督微调（SFT）方法，通过过滤已拟合样本（相对于初始策略的损失下降最大的样本），将训练重点放在数据分布的尾部（欠建模区域）。在 OLMo-3 7B 上的实验表明，该方法能显著提升 pass@16（覆盖度），并在后续 GRPO 训练中带来 pass@1 的持续提升，证明了其为 RL 后训练提供更好初始化的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 SFT 的交叉熵目标与下游强化学习所需的覆盖度（coverage）并不对齐——模型可能通过拟合已掌握的响应而降低对潜在有用响应的概率。已有研究指出，覆盖度和 pass@K（特别是大 K 值）是 RL 后训练性能的重要预测因子。本文质疑现有 SFT 流水线是否真正产生了最适合 RL 的模型，呼吁采用阶段感知（stage-aware）的模型开发方法。

Method: TailSFT 采用序列级过滤策略：记录每个样本在初始策略 π₀ 下的损失 ℓ⁰，在训练过程中计算当前损失 ℓᵗ 与 ℓ⁰ 的差值，将差值最小（即已拟合最充分）的 γᵢ 比例样本过滤掉，只在剩余样本上计算交叉熵损失。这种"偏移过滤"（offset filtering）相对初始策略进行，而非使用绝对阈值。理论上可证明，相比标准 ERM 和绝对过滤，偏移过滤在覆盖度上不会更差，且在特定条件下可严格优于两者。此外，作者提出基于基础模型和标准 SFT 运行的轻量级覆盖度比率诊断（ρ₁₆），用于预测 TailSFT 最有帮助的场景。

Result: 1. 图导航实验：所有过滤变体（绝对、量化、偏移）均显著提升 pass@8 覆盖度，代价是交叉熵和 pass@1 略有下降。2. 理论分析：在专家条件设定下，偏移过滤的覆盖度不低于最优绝对阈值或 ERM，且可严格优于两者。3. OLMo-3 7B 大规模实验：18 个数据集-基准对中，TailSFT 在 15 个上提升 pass@16（最高达 +16.79% 在 CruxEval-O），pass@1 变化不一。4. GRPO 验证：从 TailSFT 检查点初始化的 RL 训练在所有匹配比较中均获得 pass@1 提升（+1.21% 至 +3.93%），且训练初期奖励上升更快（最高 2.5×）。5. 覆盖度比率诊断：在 ρ₁₆>1 的 11 个设置中，10 个获得正覆盖度增益，覆盖度损失被有效预测。

Conclusion: 本文证明了 SFT 阶段的目标函数选择对后续 RL 训练具有重要影响。TailSFT 通过牺牲局部 SFT 指标（交叉熵、pass@1）换取更优的覆盖度，从而为 RL 提供更好的初始化。其结果支持一种更广泛的"阶段感知"模型开发理念——中间检查点应以对后续训练的支持效果而非独立性能为评判标准。覆盖度比率诊断为实践者提供了识别 TailSFT 适用场景的实用工具。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning post-training drives reasoning and agentic capabilities in modern AI systems, yet a growing body of work shows that it is most effective when used to fine-tune an already capable base model. We question whether existing pipelines yield models that are most suitable for reinforcement learning. Building on prior work highlighting the role of coverage and pass@K as predictors of post-RL performance, we design a simple modification to supervised fine-tuning, TailSFT, which filters out already fit sequences during training, thereby focusing learning on under-modeled regions, or the tail, of the data distribution. We justify and validate the design choices in TailSFT, particularly the specific filtering criteria, through a combination of controlled experiments and theoretical analysis. On OLMo-3 7B, TailSFT often improves pass@16 performance on math and coding evaluations, with gains up to 17% absolute, while incurring minimal computational overhead. These higher-coverage checkpoints consistently translate to up to 4% absolute pass@1 gains in subsequent GRPO runs, demonstrating that TailSFT checkpoints serve as better initializations for RL. We further introduce a lightweight diagnostic for identifying settings where TailSFT is most likely to help. More broadly, our results motivate a principled, stage-aware approach to model development, in which intermediate checkpoints are judged by how effectively they support subsequent training.

</details>


### [229] [VINCENT: Validated Interaction Network for Cross-drug Explanation of Therapeutics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25841)
*Fan-Sheng Chuang,Xuchen Li,Yujing Bian,Kaixiong Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VINCENT提出了一种后训练框架，通过交叉药物证据提取、扰动验证和反馈迭代，从固定药物协同预测器中识别化学一致的motif对，解释预测协同性的分子区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释协同模型将解释机制嵌入预测器架构，缺乏对跨药物区域分数的扰动验证及反馈优化，无法同时满足化学一致性、扰动稳定性和与预测器行为对齐三个要求。

Method: 四阶段闭环框架：(1)交叉药物信号提取(注意力矩阵与Integrated Gradients正交集)；(2)构建三种分子内亲和度(结构、交互模式、反馈)；(3)约束motif分配(谱聚类+环完整性)；(4)重复局部扰动验证(16次试验的二阶有限差分)并通过EMA反馈更新亲和度，3次外层迭代优化。

Result: 在25对文献注释子集上达到0.826 motif召回率(基线0.49-0.66)，Jaccard 0.689，hit rate 76.6%；全部71对测试集上TP/TN分离度3.36，Pearson相关系数0.423，显著优于无反馈变体和受控基线。

Conclusion: 闭环扰动验证能更准确地恢复文献支持的分子区域，同时产生更好反映预测器决策行为的跨药物交互分数，验证了将验证证据反馈回区域构建的有效性。

Abstract: Drug synergy prediction estimates whether two drugs produce a stronger joint effect than expected from their individual activities. For drug combination discovery, a single synergy score is often not enough: researchers also need to know which molecular regions jointly drive the prediction. We study motif-pair synergy explanation, which identifies pairs of chemically coherent regions, one from each drug, that jointly contribute to predicted synergy. Existing interpretable synergy models expose atom- or substructure-level signals, but their explanations are built into the predictor architecture, and none validates cross-drug region scores under repeated perturbations or feeds that evidence back to refine the explanation. A reliable motif-pair explanation should instead be chemically coherent, perturbation-stable, and aligned with predictor behavior. We introduce VINCENT (Validated Interaction Network for Cross-drug Explanation of Therapeutics), a post-training framework for a fixed interaction-aware synergy predictor. VINCENT extracts atom-pair evidence from attention and gradient signals, groups atoms into chemically coherent motifs, and validates candidate motif pairs through repeated local perturbations. The validated evidence is fed back to refine motif assignments, yielding explanations that satisfy these three criteria. On a 25-pair literature-annotated subset, VINCENT achieves a mean motif recall of 0.826 (95% CI: 0.78-0.87), compared with 0.49-0.66 for baselines. Across all 71 test pairs, its validated interaction scores yield a TP/TN separation of 3.36. These results show that closed-loop perturbation validation recovers literature-supported molecular regions more accurately than existing alternatives while producing cross-drug interaction scores that better reflect predictor behavior.

</details>


### [230] [Towards A Unified Information Bottleneck Framework for Time Series Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25897)
*Xu Zheng,Zichuan Liu,Zhuomin Chen,Mayur Akewar,Janki Bhimani,Jason Liu,Mo Sha,Jingchao Ni,Wei Cheng,Dongsheng Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TimeX++框架，基于信息瓶颈(IB)原理统一了时间序列的归因解释和反事实解释，解决了现有方法中归因的分布外(OOD)问题和反事实的对抗性不稳定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列可解释性研究将归因解释和反事实解释视为两个独立任务：归因方法缺乏因果验证，反事实方法容易产生对抗性噪声而非有意义的解释。两者之间的割裂导致了解释质量受限，需要一个统一的理论框架来弥合这一差距。

Method: 基于信息瓶颈(IB)原理构建统一目标函数 min I(X;X') - α·I(X';Y)，通过三个关键设计实现：(1) 用KL散度替代互信息作为紧凑性约束；(2) 通过条件生成器将解释实例投影回原始数据分布，解决OOD问题；(3) 通过结构锚定损失将反事实扰动严格限制在归因瓶颈定义的语义区域内，防止对抗性扰动。框架包含共享的归因瓶颈提取器(基于Transformer的编码器-解码器)和两个条件生成器(TimeX_a++和TimeX_cf++)。

Result: 在10个合成和真实世界数据集上的实验表明，TimeX++在12个评估案例中有9个优于现有最佳基线，AUPRC平均提升11.01%，AUP提升10.87%。在分布一致性方面(KDE、KL散度、MMD指标)显著优于传统掩码策略，反事实解释在50%噪声注入下仍保持80%以上的有效性。推理速度比最快的反事实基线(AB-CF)快100倍以上。

Conclusion: TimeX++通过信息瓶颈原理成功统一了归因和反事实解释，提供了因果验证的归因和语义合理的反事实，在性能和效率上均达到领先水平。主要局限是超参数对不同数据集的敏感性。

Abstract: Explaining deep learning models operating on time series data is crucial in various applications that require transparent and interpretable insights into model behavior. {Existing explanation methods generally fall into two categories: attribution-based explanations, which identify the temporal regions most responsible for a prediction, and counterfactual explanations, which reveal how an input should be modified to alter the model's decision.} {Despite valuable insights, these two fields are largely studied independently. This disconnect leaves attribution methods lacking causal validation, while counterfactual methods suffer from severe instability, producing adversarial-like noise instead of meaningful explanations.} In this work, we revisit time-series explainability from an information-theoretic perspective and show that existing explainers are vulnerable to trivial solutions and distributional shifts. To address these limitations, we propose a unified objective function for explainable time series learning that bridges attribution and counterfactual reasoning within a single framework. Building upon the Information Bottleneck principle, our formulation explicitly prevents trivial explanations and out-of-distribution counterfactuals. {Based on this objective function, we introduce {\modelname}, a novel explanation framework that learns a parametric transformation network to construct explanation-embedded instances, where preserved information yields attribution explanations and controlled information removal produces stable counterfactual explanations.} We evaluate {\modelname} on synthetic and real-world benchmarks against state-of-the-art baselines. Extensive quantitative and qualitative results show that {\modelname} consistently outperforms competing methods, yielding faithful attributions and stable counterfactual explanations.

</details>


### [231] [One Symptom, Three Levers: A Critical Review of On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25936)
*Justin Robert,Raheel Qader*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于"在线自蒸馏"（On-Policy Self-Distillation, OPSD）的批判性综述，聚焦其在数学推理任务中的"坍缩"（collapse）失败模式。作者将坍缩视为一种症状，并归纳出控制它的三个杠杆：（A）信号施加位置（token 权重与散度选择）、（B）特权信息的性质、（C）信号变化时机（教师动态与引导衰减）。本文不报告新实验，主要贡献是为该领域提供统一术语，区分已确立结论与开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线自蒸馏（OPSD）通过让模型以自身作为教师、借助特权信息（如参考解答、计划、环境反馈）生成稠密 token 级监督信号，免去了对更大教师模型的依赖。早期结果显示其以远少于 RL 的生成 token 数即可达到可比准确率。但该方法存在严重的"坍缩"问题——推理路径多样性持续收窄。作者认为需要从结构上厘清这一失败模式的成因，并建立统一的分析框架，以澄清该领域不同论文中同名异述或异名同述的现象。

Method: 本文为综述性论文，未提出新算法或新实验。其方法论特点包括：（1）将 OPSD 置于 SFT → RL → 在线蒸馏 → 自蒸馏的谱系中分析；（2）以"症状-杠杆"结构组织文献：先诊断坍缩的两类成因（RL 梯度侵蚀熵 + 特权信息引发的点互信息 PMI 机制），再围绕三个杠杆（信号几何 / 特权信息 / 循环稳定性）逐一梳理已确立结论与争议；（3）引入机器人学与自监督视觉学习中的"特权信息可重建性"准则作为评估标准；（4）提出 pass@k 与 G-Pass@k 等多样性的可测度量，并讨论基准脆弱性（方差、污染、Qwen 家族特异性）。

Result: 本文报告三项已确立结论：（1）特权信息的安全性取决于学生是否能在测试时自主重建——若必须预设或记忆，则有害；最终答案最不可迁移；（2）坍缩在均值分数或熵上不可见，仅 pass@k 能揭示——自蒸馏模型可显示更高熵却产生更少推理路径；（3）密度本身并非关键变量，决定性的是信号施加位置、所编码内容与变化时机的控制。开放问题包括：加权准则的统一、token 与问题粒度的最优性、oracle/CoT/计划/评分量表在同一协议下的对比、教师更新规则的系统比较、密度-遗忘关系的精确定位。

Conclusion: OPSD 应被视为一种研究阶段的后 SFT 微调方法，而非开箱即用的训练方案。它在"以较少生成 token、无需外部教师"方面具有吸引力，但失败模式已被充分记录：对推理模型而言，特权信息选择不当会降低性能；在持续学习中稠密自蒸馏比标准 RL 遗忘更严重甚至坍缩。在生产中使用须配合本文 §1.4 列出的评估防护措施。该方法本质是"用一个信息不对称代替能力不对称"，关键在于如何控制这一不对称。局限性在于：仅覆盖数学推理，文献多为半年内预印本，实验集中在单一模型家族且参数不超过数十亿。
