Abstract: On-policy distillation trains a language model on its own generations while a teacher scores them token by token. It combines the dense supervision of imitation learning with the on-policy sampling of reinforcement learning. But it requires a second, larger model to act as teacher. On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) removes that cost. The teacher is the model itself, conditioned on privileged information the student will not have at test time, such as a reference solution, a plan, or environment feedback. The teacher is no stronger than the student, only better informed. Early results were promising, with accuracy comparable to reinforcement learning at a fraction of the generated tokens. But the same asymmetry that produces the signal also biases it. One failure mode now dominates the field: collapse, the progressive narrowing of the set of reasoning paths the model can produce. Collapse is not specific to OPSD, though privileged information aggravates it. This review treats collapse as a symptom governed by three levers: (i) where the signal is applied, that is, how tokens are weighted; (ii) what the teacher is shown, that is, the nature of the privileged information; and (iii) when the signal changes, that is, the teacher's dynamics and the decay of guidance. We restrict our scope to mathematical reasoning, where the method originated and where its failure modes are best documented. We report no new experiments. The contribution is structural: a shared vocabulary for phenomena named differently across papers, and a clear line between what is settled and what is still disputed.

</details>


### [232] [DualOPSD: Adaptive Privileged Teachers for On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26019)
*Yutong Chen,Guangfu Guo,Zhichao Xu,Kunpeng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) uses a privileged copy of the student model to provide dense supervision without an external teacher. OPSD keeps this privileged teacher fixed, even though the student distribution and output style change during training. We propose DualOPSD, an asymmetric alternating framework that adapts both policies. The student first learns from the privileged teacher. The teacher then moves toward the updated student distribution on the same student trajectory. This update makes later supervision responsive to the learner and does not require another rollout. On Qwen3-8B in non-thinking mode, DualOPSD improves avg@12 over OPSD by 23.61, 13.89, and 10.00 points on AIME 2024, AIME 2025, and HMMT 2025. Results at 1.7B and 4B show that the accuracy gain depends on model scale. Across all three scales, DualOPSD reduces truncation. The 4B diagnostic also shows lower KL in both directions between the teacher and student.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI enhancement unavailable (llm_error). Original arXiv metadata is preserved. Error: AI returned empty structured response

Method: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Result: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (llm_error). Original arXiv metadata is preserved. Error: AI returned empty structured response

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) uses a privileged copy of the student model to provide dense supervision without an external teacher. OPSD keeps this privileged teacher fixed, even though the student distribution and output style change during training. We propose DualOPSD, an asymmetric alternating framework that adapts both policies. The student first learns from the privileged teacher. The teacher then moves toward the updated student distribution on the same student trajectory. This update makes later supervision responsive to the learner and does not require another rollout. On Qwen3-8B in non-thinking mode, DualOPSD improves avg@12 over OPSD by 23.61, 13.89, and 10.00 points on AIME 2024, AIME 2025, and HMMT 2025. Results at 1.7B and 4B show that the accuracy gain depends on model scale. Across all three scales, DualOPSD reduces truncation. The 4B diagnostic also shows lower KL in both directions between the teacher and student.

</details>


### [233] [How Much Rank Does LoRA Need? Rank-Error Bounds for Transformer Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26052)
*Gerard Conangla Planes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为Transformer注意力层的LoRA秩选择提供了任务依赖性的理论分析。通过证明注意力KL散度的上下界，给出了给定秩预算r下可达误差的精确刻画，并揭示了softmax饱和会显著降低匹配注意力分布所需的秩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践中LoRA秩的选择通常依赖经验或网格搜索，但这些方法无法区分"容量不足"与"优化困难"。本文旨在回答一个表征性问题：给定目标注意力函数，下游任务在每个秩预算下能达到多好的近似？

Method: 1) 全局softmax界：将中心化分数误差通过ψ尺度函数转化为KL散度上下界（定理3.1）；2) 任务加权谱界：定义D*=G^{1/2}Δ*Σ^{1/2}，其尾部能量T_r给出秩-r误差的谱刻画（定理4.1）；3) 替代路线：当概率下界太弱时，提供目标Fisher界（定理5.1）和高质量界（定理5.2）；4) 饱和分离：构造Walsh族证明匹配有限logits需秩k，但softmax饱和后仅需k-⌊k/3⌋秩（定理6.1）；5) 扩展到融合多头LoRA（定理7.1）和联合Q/K LoRA（定理8.1，含因子化间隙ρ）。

Result: 1) 主定理（Thm 4.1）：在实现性、几何和矩条件下，ℰ_r被ψ(√T_r)的常数倍夹住；2) 定理6.1精确构造了k个上下文、<4k²+8个token的Walsh族，其softmax闭界秩恰为k-⌊k/3⌋；3) 线性token族达到7/4的秩压缩比；4) 联合Q/K扩展揭示了因子化间隙ρ_{rQ,rK}刻画了有效秩-s更新能否被分离秩预算的Q/K因子实现；5) 提供了基于校准数据的实际估计流程。

Conclusion: LoRA秩需求是任务依赖的，不由ΔW的原始奇异值决定，而由任务查询和键实际激活的部分决定。该理论将秩启发式和事后扫描替换为注意力层表征误差的秩索引双边bracket。softmax饱和可显著降低所需秩；目标Fisher和高质量界提供了主下界失效时的替代方案；融合多头和联合Q/K分析揭示了秩共享和因子化约束的影响。

Abstract: Choosing the rank of a low-rank adaptation (LoRA) update is usually an empirical task. In this paper, we provide a task-dependent theory of the approximation error achievable at each LoRA rank for Transformer attention. We fix a pretrained attention head, a target attention function, and a distribution over inputs from the downstream task, and bound the smallest expected Kullback--Leibler (KL) error achievable by a rank-$r$ query LoRA update. When target attention probabilities are bounded away from zero, we prove a lower bound of the error proportional to $\psi(\|d\|_2)$, where $d$ is the difference between candidate and target attention scores and $\psi(t)=\min\{t^2,t\}$. We also prove an unconditional upper bound $\min\{\|d\|_2^2/4,\sqrt2\|d\|_2\}$. Under explicit realizability, geometry, and moment conditions, we then bound the best rank-$r$ error between an explicit multiple of $\psi(\sqrt{T_r})$ and $\min\{T_r/4,\sqrt{2T_r}\}$, where $T_r$ is the downstream-weighted tail energy of the target update. We also provide target-Fisher bounds when candidate scores remain within a fixed range of the target scores, and an unrestricted lower bound when a subset of tokens carries most of the probability mass. These spectral bounds describe finite-score approximation. We then construct explicit families in which softmax saturation makes the rank required to match the attention function strictly smaller than the rank required to match the finite logits. Finally, we extend the analysis to fused multi-head LoRA and joint query/key updates, exposing the effects of rank sharing and query/key factorization constraints.

</details>


### [234] [ICON Decomposition: Multivariate Concept-Level Explanations of Deep Representations for Model Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26083)
*Roshan Prakash Rane,Marco Simnacher,Manuel Pfeuffer,Marc-Andre Schulz,Nys Tjade Siegel,Maximilian Dreyer,Frederik Pahde,Wojciech Samek,Sonja Greven,Kerstin Ritter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ICON Decomposition，一种多元方差分解式的概念级可解释性方法，用于深度神经网络模型审计。通过将 C-XAI 重新定义为方差分解问题（结合偏最小二乘回归与 Type I 序贯平方和分配），克服了现有单变量解码方法（线性探针、CAV）因概念共线性产生大量假阳性的问题。在合成数据、皮肤癌分类和神经影像分析三个实验中，ICON 在恢复真实概念重要性、检测捷径学习依赖以及产出可操作的稀疏解释方面均优于七种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在生物医学应用中常利用训练数据中的虚假关联（捷径学习），这对临床决策和研究结论的可靠性构成威胁。现有的概念级可解释性方法（线性探针、CAV）采用单变量解码框架，独立评估每个概念，导致：(i) 因概念共线性产生大量假阳性（将相关性误判为使用）；(ii) 不同层、不同概念类型的分数不可比；(iii) 无法量化未被概念集解释的表征方差。这些局限性使得模型审计难以产出可操作的稀疏假设。

Method: ICON Decomposition 将 C-XAI 重新定义为多元方差分解问题。核心步骤：(1) 使用偏最小二乘（PLS）在 DNN 层表征 H_l 与概念-结果矩阵 C'=[y, c_1,...,c_q] 之间迭代提取正交潜在概念对（ICON）及其激活向量；(2) 通过 PLS 的内关系与载荷计算每个 ICON 所解释的方差比例，形成方差分配，使各 ICON 重要性 + 残差方差 σ²_l = 1；(3) 对每个 ICON 内部的多重共线原始变量，使用基于权重平方排序的 Type I（序贯）平方和分配，将共享方差归给贡献最大的主导变量，从而抑制弱相关变量的假阳性。最终生成有界、跨层可比的稀疏重要性分数，并显式报告未被解释的份额 σ²_l。

Result: (1) 合成数据（ToyBrains）：90 个模型上，ICON 在 H_{L-2} 和 H_{L-1} 上的 TVD 误差最低（0.33 vs 最佳基线 0.52；0.23 vs 0.38），且随概念数增多或样本量减少优势扩大；对零重要性概念的误判率仅为 0.06（最佳基线为 0.16）。(2) 皮肤癌（ISIC 2019）：在概念-结果共线性和概念-概念共线性变化下，ICON 对未训练的概念重要性始终 <0.01，而基线产生 0.05–0.12 的假阳性；ICON 的 microscope 重要性随捷径学习强度从 ≈0 增至 0.44，基线仅变化 0.02–0.15。(3) 神经影像：ICON 识别出 binge-drinking 模型以性别为捷径（通过性别平衡重训练验证：准确率从 60% 降至 53%）；脑龄模型中 ICON 仅归因于年龄本身（≈63%），而探针产生多个年龄相关假阳性概念（性别、家庭收入、就业状态等），这些概念在 OOD 测试（|ΔMAE|≤0.069 年）和条件偏 R² 检验中均未被验证。

Conclusion: ICON Decomposition 通过将概念级可解释性从单变量解码范式转变为多元方差分解范式，显著减少了概念共线性引起的假阳性，提供了跨层、跨概念类型可比的可解释输出，并量化了未被解释的方差份额。其产出是稀疏的少量可检验假设，而非大量"可解码但未使用"的概念列表，支持后续通过重训练或分布外测试进行因果验证。该方法已在生物医学模型审计（皮肤癌、神经影像）中验证其实用价值，未来可扩展至非线性编码（如核 PLS）和因果可控的解释，并有望改进基于 CAV 的模型引导操作。

Abstract: Deep neural networks often exploit spurious associations in their training data, a failure known as shortcut learning. Concept-based explainability methods screen for shortcuts by testing whether concepts such as a patient's sex or scanner settings can be decoded from a network layer. Because each concept is evaluated in isolation, these methods can mistake correlations between concepts as evidence that the model uses them. We introduce ICON decomposition, which instead quantifies how much of a layer's variance each concept explains after accounting for all other concepts and the outcome. On synthetic data with known ground truth, ICON recovers concept importance more accurately than seven alternative baseline methods. On skin-lesion and brain-imaging models, it isolates the concepts on which a model genuinely relies, quantifies the representation unexplained by any of the supplied concepts, and yields sparse explanations that we validate by retraining and out-of-distribution testing.

</details>


### [235] [TraceML: An Empirical Analysis of Human-Agent Planning in Machine Learning Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26086)
*Jiarui Yan,Weiwei Sun,Sijie Li,Wenhan Li,Yiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TraceML 是一个版本级的人类与智能体 ML 开发轨迹数据集，在 134 场 Kaggle 竞赛（4,465 条人类轨迹）以及其中 7 场竞赛上与 Codex、MLEvolve 两种智能体脚手架（207 条轨迹）配对。研究发现：人类在数据、验证、模型、集成之间交替迭代并会"重拾"已放弃方案；而 Codex 陷入围绕集成权重与提交调优的单一循环，MLEvolve 则在原地对模型做小幅突变，两者均缺乏方向转变与回溯能力。基于人类实践提炼的简短规划提示能部分弥合差距，但无法改变智能体的整体"努力形态"，提示指令只能触及可被显式命名的行为，无法触及更深层的搜索节奏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的结果导向型基准（如 MLE-bench、MLAgentBench）只对最终提交打分，无法解释 LLM 智能体在 Kaggle 等长周期 ML 开发任务上为何落后于人类专家。要弥合这一差距，必须把"开发过程"——而不只是"最终成绩"——放到同一可比框架下分析。

Method: 1) 数据构建：从 Meta Kaggle 重建 4,465 条人类轨迹（保留 DAG 血缘、过滤赛后修改与刷分重提），在 7 场配对竞赛上用 Codex CLI 与 MLEvolve 采集 207 条智能体轨迹，统一为"代码版本"序列。2) 标注：定义两轴 schema（8 阶段版本状态 + 136 细标签；动作/意图/编辑幅度/分数效应四类转移标签），用 gpt-5.4-mini 教师标注后蒸馏为两个 Qwen3-1.7B 学生标注器（state 与 action）。3) 分析：在 12 小时统一预算下，用 PCA、JSD、聚类自助法比较人类与智能体的动作分布、回溯率、转折率；构建"反循环 + 人类先验 + 周期自检 + 任务先验"的规划提示（≈1k token），通过 5 臂对照（baseline / harness / Abl-A / Abl-B）评估其行为与得分影响。

Result: 人类与智能体的差距是"过程差距"而非简单的分数差距：(1) 粗粒度动作分布上，MLEvolve 与人类明显分离（0.09–0.12 bits），Codex 与顶级人类接近；细粒度上两者各自坍缩成单一循环（Codex 重加权集成 78%、MLEvolve 种子/层变异）。(2) 人类 25% 转移为"转折"，Codex 仅 9%，MLEvolve 虽 58% 但常无效（+0.089 vs −0.008）。(3) 人类 9.1% 回溯旧方案且 78.5% 回溯后提升；Codex 在 658 个合格版本中仅回溯 1 次，MLEvolve 为 0；但二者从分数挫折中恢复的能力与人类相当甚至更优。(4) 规划提示使 Codex 在 5/7 场竞赛得分提升（gquest 0.371→0.429、hms KL 1.050→0.718、ranzcr AUC 0.545→0.877、amex 0.023→0.781），2 场在噪声内，无回退；Abl-B（仅节奏无内容）全部 ≤ 基线，说明增益来自提示内容而非重复注入。但提示无法触及"工作模式切换率"（harness 后仅 2.2%，人类 13.2–14.1%），即"努力形态"不变。

Conclusion: TraceML 将人类与智能体的 ML 工程置于同一版本级 schema 下，证明差距源自两类机制层面的缺失：① 缺乏可检索的自身历史（不重拾旧方案），② 缺乏根据运行状态动态切换策略的控制器（Codex 不转，MLEvolve 乱转）。短规划提示只能闭合"可被指令化的部分"；剩余部分属于智能体设计本身。论文公开语料、schema、标注器权重与提取流水线，使新脚手架可与人类实践做"过程级"对照评测。

Abstract: Large language models write correct code for isolated problems but remain far weaker at autonomous machine-learning development, where an agent must revise data pipelines, models, and validation over hours of feedback, and on most competitions still finishes below strong human competitors. Outcome-based benchmarks record this gap but not its cause, because they grade the final submission and discard the development process behind it. We introduce TraceML, which pairs human and agent work on the same competitions under one version-level schema: 4,465 human Kaggle trajectories across 134 competitions, seven of which are also worked by two agent scaffolds, giving 430 paired human and 207 agent trajectories. Every code version carries its score, its timestamp, and labels for the action taken, its intent, the edit size, and the score effect. Read this way, the gap becomes concrete. Experts alternate data work, validation, model changes, and ensembling, and return to approaches they had set aside. Each agent scaffold instead collapses into a narrow loop: Codex spends its steps re-weighting ensembles and tuning submissions, MLEvolve mutates its model in place, and neither pivots at the human rate nor reopens abandoned work. A short planning prompt distilled from human practice moves the behaviors it names toward the human profile and lifts scores, but the effort profile stays agent-shaped: instruction closes only the part of the gap that reduces to instructions. We release the corpus, the schema, the labelers, and the extraction pipeline at this https URL .

</details>


### [236] [Resilient Decentralized Wireless Federated Learning via Gradient Tracking with AdamW](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25535)
*Nguyen Van Thieu,Ti Ti Nguyen,Ons Aouedi,Vu Nguyen Ha,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QEF-GT-AdamW算法，将梯度跟踪、AdamW自适应优化与双流有偏量化+误差反馈结合，并通过本地回退策略应对无线丢包，专为非IID数据下资源受限的无线物联网去中心化学习设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去中心化无线联邦学习方法在异构数据（non-IID）和受限无线链路（带宽、功率、空中时间预算、衰落、丢包）下面临通信开销大、鲁棒性差的问题，亟需一种联合考虑自适应优化、压缩与不可靠通信的可部署框架。

Method: 1) 在每个时隙中，建模计算依赖的空中时间预算与基于Rician衰落的Marcum Q函数中断概率；2) 采用去中心化梯度跟踪缓解数据异构；3) 引入AdamW自适应更新（解耦权重衰减）提升稳定性；4) 对模型流与跟踪流分别进行Top-K有偏量化+误差反馈（EF）以降低通信量；5) 丢包时采用本地回退策略（缺失邻居权重重分配至自环），保持行随机混合矩阵；6) 在Perron加权目标下建立收敛性证明，并在PL条件下给出线性收敛速率。

Result: 在15节点无线网络上对MNIST（非IID标签偏斜）和CIFAR-10（Dirichlet α=0.5）的Logistic回归实验中，QEF-GT-AdamW在10% Top-K稀疏率下：1) 仅需约30%（MNIST）/35%（CIFAR-10）稀疏率即可逼近全精度GT-AdamW精度，而QGT-AdamW需约90%；2) 丢包率在0.2 MHz及以上带宽、或0.02 W及以上功率下近乎为零；3) 相比CHOCO-SGD（无客户端漂移缓解）和无量化GT-AdamW（丢包率在低带宽下达100%），在精度-通信权衡和鲁棒性上均更优。

Conclusion: QEF-GT-AdamW在收敛速度与无线资源受限下的鲁棒性方面优于现有代表性基线，实现了精度、通信效率与抗无线损伤三者间更优的折中，为去中心化无线联邦学习在IoT边缘的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Wireless Internet-of-Things (IoT) edge networks require decentralized learning (DecL) methods that can operate reliably under both heterogeneous local data and communication-constrained wireless links. However, existing decentralized optimization schemes often incur substantial communication overhead and degraded performance when transmissions are constrained by strict airtime budgets, fading channels, and packet losses. This paper proposes QEF-GT-AdamW, a communication-efficient and outage-resilient algorithm for DecL over wireless communication (WCom) networks. The proposed method combines gradient tracking to mitigate the effect of non-IID data, AdamW-based adaptive optimization to improve training stability, and dual-stream biased quantization with error feedback to reduce communication payloads for both model and tracking exchanges. To address unreliable broadcast communication, the proposed framework further employs a local fallback strategy when scheduled packets are not successfully received. We explicitly model the effect of bandwidth, transmit power, airtime constraints, and fading channels on DecL performance, and establish convergence guarantees for the proposed algorithm under compressed and unreliable wireless communication. Experimental results on heterogeneous MNIST and CIFAR-10 settings show that QEF-GT-AdamW consistently improves robustness and convergence performance over representative DecL baselines while achieving favorable accuracy-communication trade-offs under limited wireless resources.

</details>


### [237] [Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Aggregation in Semi-Supervised Federated Learning with non-IID Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25794)
*Rene Glitza,Luca Becker,Rainer Martin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 pFedMARL，一种基于多智能体强化学习（MARL）与 TD3 算法的联邦学习框架，通过服务端智能体动态调整客户端聚合权重、客户端智能体平衡全局与本地模型贡献，在非IID 数据分布和对抗客户端场景下联合优化全局模型鲁棒性与个性化客户端模型，并在音频频谱Transformer 半监督训练任务上取得优于 FedAvg、Ditto 和本地训练的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在非IID 数据分布下易产生偏差且性能下降，同时面临对抗客户端发送恶意更新的风险；现有方法如 FedAvg 个体一致性差、Ditto 需额外本地训练、服务器端 DRL 方法缺乏个性化，因此亟需一种能同时兼顾全局鲁棒性、对抗鲁棒性与客户端个性化的自适应聚合方法。

Method: 将 FL 聚合建模为 MARL 问题，采用 Twin Delayed DDPG（TD3）算法：服务端智能体基于客户端验证损失、余弦相似度、L2 距离、批次数量等观察输出聚合权重（Softmax 归一化），客户端智能体输出标量因子动态平衡全局正则化与本地训练损失（公式3）；奖励定义为验证损失的负对数；服务端与各客户端智能体共享经验回放并独立在线更新，无需预训练。

Result: 在 DCASE Task 2 数据集（15 客户端，3 种非IID 划分：QS、LS、CS，含 2 个对抗客户端）训练 450k 参数的音频频谱 Transformer（同时进行掩码重构与分类）；100 轮通信后，pFedMARL 在三种场景下 MSE 与 F1 均匹配或优于 FedAvg、Ditto 与本地训练，对抗客户端的聚合权重被自动抑制至约 0.1，良性客户端在约 50 轮后逐步降低全局正则化以增强个性化，并在准确率、鲁棒性与公平性间取得平衡。

Conclusion: pFedMARL 通过协作式 MARL 有效应对非IID 与对抗威胁，无需预训练即可同时生成鲁棒全局模型与个性化客户端模型，在对抗场景下表现稳定且免去 Ditto 所需的二次本地训练；未来工作将拓展至其他数据模态并验证大规模客户端下的可扩展性。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables distributed training of machine learning models while preserving data privacy. However, FL struggles with heterogeneous, non-IID client data distributions, resulting in sub-optimal and biased global models. In this paper, we propose pFedMARL, a novel approach leveraging Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) with Twin Delayed Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (TD3) to dynamically adapt aggregation strategies in FL settings. Our method employs a server-side agent adjusting client contributions to optimize global model robustness and client-side agents balancing global and local updates to personalize models effectively without pre-training. We demonstrate superior performance of pFedMARL for training a semi-supervised audio spectrogram transformer, matching or outperforming FedAvg, Ditto, and local training approaches across multiple non-IID scenarios and in the presence of adversarial clients. Our results indicate that pFedMARL actively improves accuracy, robustness, and fairness, making it suitable for real-world deployments.

</details>


### [238] [Dynamic Influence-Weighted Distillation for Single-IMU Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24904)
*Bingxuan Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对单IMU人体活动识别场景，提出动态影响加权（DIW）知识蒸馏方法，利用训练阶段四个IMU的特权信息提升推理阶段仅使用右臂IMU的学生模型。在WEAR数据集上，DIW将池化OOF macro-F1从监督学习的56.18%提升至63.85%，且不改变部署时的传感器配置和学生模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多位置IMU可改善活动识别，但部署时所有传感器都需佩戴带来负担。固定权重知识蒸馏对每个样本同等对待logit和feature目标，而学生可能并非对所有样本均等受益，因此需要一种样本级、自适应的蒸馏加权机制，将训练阶段多位置信息有效迁移到单传感器部署模型。

Method: 提出动态影响加权（DIW）：（1）冻结的四IMU教师提供logit和feature目标；（2）将每个外层折的训练参与者分为拟合集D_fit和折内元集D_meta；（3）对当前学生做一次分离的look-ahead候选AdamW更新，在D_meta上评估并取归一化梯度方向；（4）通过有限差分探针测量每个拟合样本的logit和feature损失沿该元梯度方向的导数I_{i,k}；（5）保留超过数值分辨率阈值的正影响，在批量内做最大值归一化得到[0,1]范围的样本级门控w_{i,k}，分别用于logit和feature蒸馏损失。学生为含80,915参数的紧凑时序卷积网络（2个残差1D卷积块），教师为共享编码器+融合投影，191,507参数。评估采用22名受试者的subject-disjoint五折交叉验证，68,298个完整窗口，19个标签。

Result: 在WEAR数据集上：监督学习OOF macro-F1为56.18%，固定权重KD为57.16%，DIW达63.85%，分别提升7.66和6.68个百分点。DIW在19个标签中的18个和22名受试者中的21名上超过监督学习。四IMU教师参考为70.48%。消融实验：均值匹配KD为59.93%，静态门控图为62.52%，无look-ahead为61.60%，批量共享门控为60.62%，均低于Full DIW的63.85%。门控诊断显示：logit门正率从79.4%降至69.5%而中位数从0.242升至0.374；feature门正率从61.1%升至71.3%；"两者皆正"状态始终占主导（49.8–54.4%）。

Conclusion: 更强的多位置教师并不自动产生更强的单位置学生，固定权重蒸馏仅恢复少量性能。DIW通过对每个样本独立门控logit和feature目标，显著提升单IMU部署模型的性能，且不改变部署输入或模型前向图。消融表明增益不仅来自降低平均KD强度，还来自在线look-ahead评估和样本级门控分配。但研究存在局限：仅使用一个数据集、一个保留传感器位置、一个随机种子，且DIW独有地使用了元集标签，需进一步控制实验分离门控机制与额外标签反馈的效应。

Abstract: Inertial sensors at multiple body locations can improve activity recognition, but requiring every sensor at inference increases the deployment burden. We study whether four synchronized IMUs available during training can improve a student that uses only the right-arm IMU during fitting and inference. A frozen four-IMU teacher provides logit and feature targets. Fixed-weight knowledge distillation applies each target with the same strength to every fitting sample, although the student may not benefit equally from them. We introduce dynamic influence weighting (DIW), which tests a one-step candidate update on separate fold-internal training participants. DIW then assigns separate sample-wise gates to the logit and feature losses. On WEAR, we evaluate 19 labels and 68,298 complete windows from 22 participants using subject-disjoint five-fold cross-validation. Pooled out-of-fold macro-F1 is 0.561820 for Supervised and 0.571623 for Fixed-weight KD. DIW reaches 0.638451, gains of 7.66 and 6.68 percentage points, respectively. It exceeds Supervised for 18 of 19 labels and 21 of 22 held-out participants. All three routes retain the same 80,915-parameter right-arm student at inference. Under this protocol, DIW converts training-only multi-position information into a stronger single-IMU model without changing deployed sensing or the student forward graph.

</details>


### [239] [MacroAgent: Regularity-Aware Macro Legalization with LLM-Agent-Designed Contour Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24946)
*Jiaxi Jiang,Xufeng Yao,Yuxuan Zhao,Yuntao Lu,Peiyu Liao,Zuodong Zhang,Yibo Lin,Bei Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MacroAgent是一个四阶段的VLSI宏单元合法化（macro legalization）框架，通过LLM智能体自动设计多种规则性感知（regularity-aware）的轮廓（contour）生成启发式算法，在解决宏单元重叠的同时最大化布局规则性，在学术和工业流程中均显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代VLSI设计中宏单元（macro）占据核心区域大面积，其位置对最终QoR（布线长度、拥塞、时序等）有重大影响。现有的宏单元合法化方法存在三大缺陷：（1）鲁棒性差（仅依赖单一或两种启发式）；（2）计算成本高（如模拟退火）；（3）忽略宏单元间的规则性（regularity），导致不规则的沟道（channels）增加死区（deadspace）和布线长度。研究者探索能否用LLM替代人类专家设计启发式算法以解决此类EDA领域的NP-hard问题。

Method: MacroAgent框架包含四个顺序阶段：(1) 基于尺寸感知与距离的聚类（KD-tree + α=10, β=1.5）；(2) 规则性感知轮廓生成（核心创新——利用LLM智能体进化设计启发式轮廓算法）；(3) 基于模板的宏匹配（轮廓内生成均匀网格，通过匈牙利算法最小化位移）；(4) 簇间细化（约束图LP，保持簇内相对位置）。LLM智能体流程：初始化N=10个启发式→5种提示策略（E1多样性探索/E2综合创新/M1结构性修改/M2参数调整/M3简化）→评估→迭代G=20代，共生成110个候选，最终保留8个构成Pareto前沿的算法。关键创新是将EDA问题抽象为领域无关的几何问题（alpha shape、MST、KDE、Voronoi等），使通用LLM（Claude 4.0 Sonnet）无需领域微调即可生成有效启发式。规则性指标R=C_min/C_actual，基于Hanan网格的沟道计数方法。

Result: 在TILOS基准上：相比DREAMPlace宏合法化，规则性提升2-8倍，布线长度降低5%，拥塞相当；相比Sequence Pair方法，布线长度降低4%。在更困难的Chipyard基准上：DREAMPlace在5个测试用例中3个失败，Sequence Pair在1个失败，MacroAgent全部成功，布线长度改善3%-5%。8×8 IoU矩阵表明8种LLM发现的轮廓算法几何多样性显著（平均off-diagonal IoU=0.71）。通过Cadence Innovus端到端PPA评估（工业流程）：相比DREAMPlace基线，布线长度降低2.9%，WNS改善42.6%，TNS改善68.3%（Ariane136和NVDLA的TNS改善超90%），功耗相当；集成到Innovus宏布局流程中时布线长度额外降低1.8%。运行时间为秒级，远低于基于模拟退火的Sequence Pair方法。

Conclusion: MacroAgent成功证明了LLM智能体可在无需领域微调的情况下，通过将EDA问题抽象为领域无关的几何子问题，自动设计有效的启发式算法用于宏单元合法化。该框架在鲁棒性、解质量（规则性、布线长度、时序）和计算效率方面均显著优于现有学术和工业基线方法。该范式可推广至其他NP-hard的EDA算法设计问题，为EDA社区注入新活力。未来工作应聚焦于扩展LLM智能体设计范围至聚类和簇间优化阶段，并进一步提升规则性感知能力。

Abstract: Macros constitute a large part of the core area in modern very large-scale integration (VLSI) designs. Moreover, macro positions have a significant impact on the final quality of result (QoR), and macro legalization is typically the final step in determining the macro positions. However, existing approaches related to macro legalization either lack robustness or incur substantial computational costs or neglect the regularity between macros. To address these limitations, we introduce MacroAgent. The novel framework is a four-stage approach: clustering, contour generation, template matching, and inter-cluster refinement. We propose leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) to discover multiple, effective heuristic regularity-aware contour algorithms. This framework successfully generates robust and effective algorithmic solutions for macro legalization. Compared with state-of-the-art macro legalization works, experimental results on TILOS and Chipyard benchmarks demonstrate a 2 to 8 fold improvement in layout regularity, a 3% to 5% reduction in routed wirelength with comparable congestion after global routing, and significantly better robustness with an acceptable runtime. Furthermore, end-to-end evaluation through Cadence Innovus place-and-route confirms that the regularity improvements translate into tangible PPA gains, including 2.9% lower routed wirelength and 68.3% TNS improvement over the DREAMPlace macro legalization baseline; it also achieves 1.8% lower routed wirelength when integrated into the Innovus macro placement flow.

</details>


### [240] [AFDBench: A Reasoning-First AI Scientist for NationalWeather Service Forecast Discussions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24954)
*Manmeet Singh,Somnath Luitel,Prabhjot Singh,Manraaj Banga,Naveen Sudharsan,Josh Durkee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AFDBench是首个面向气象推理的基准与AI气象系统，利用Google WeatherNext 2的结构化预报数据生成专业级NWS区域预报讨论(AFD)。通过GRPO强化学习，7B模型在两个未见NWS办公室上将Style-Align从0.318近乎翻倍至0.619，Input-Grounding从0.881提升至0.940。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在生成高风险气象文本时存在数值幻觉问题（如温度错误），而NWS的区域预报讨论(AFD)要求数值精确性与专业气象学语域并存，是AI数值天气预报（如GraphCast、WeatherNext）"最后一公里"文本生成的关键缺口。现有开放源模型无法以NWS专业行文写作，也未能忠实利用输入数据。

Method: 1) 构建AFDBench基准：7,732篇来自13个NWS办公室的专家AFD，配套WeatherNext 2真实JSON预报输入，覆盖7个美国气候区；2) 提出三项评估指标——Met-Align(数值准确率)、Style-Align(NWS专业词汇重叠)、Input-Grounding(对输入温度/风向/气压的忠实度)；3) 采用"推理优先"四字段格式（Instruction/Input/Thinking/Output）；4) 两阶段训练：先对Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct进行LoRA+4-bit量化SFT初始化，再用GRPO结合三项可验证领域奖励（温度精度R_temp、综合场准确率R_syn、格式合规R_fmt）进行强化学习。

Result: 在1,033个来自BOX(波士顿)和MRX(莫里斯敦)两个完全未见办公室的样本上：Style-Align从0.318提升至0.619(近翻倍)，Input-Grounding从0.881提升至0.940；Met-Align保持在约14%，主要受限于单时间步输入；SFT单独使用在4-bit量化下未产生可测量的改善；GRPO训练3,349步约17小时，R_fmt从0.12升至0.30，生成熵稳定于0.85未出现模式坍缩。

Conclusion: AFDBench证明领域特定的GRPO奖励可有效教会7B模型以NWS专业语域写作并忠实解读AI气象数据，地理泛化能力良好；但单时间步输入是Met-Align上限的主要瓶颈，未来需引入多时间步预报、人工专家评估、完整精度SFT及更大模型架构验证。AI气象系统的演进路径仍为"工具→合著者"阶段，全自主气象预报的治理与安全问题尚远。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) hallucinate numerical values when generating high-stakes meteorological text, posing risks for weather communication. We present AFDBench, an AI meteorologist that generates professional Area Forecast Discussions (AFDs) by reasoning through structured AI weather forecast data from Google's WeatherNext 2. We introduce AFDBench, the first benchmark for evaluating generative meteorological reasoning, comprising 7,732 expert written discussions from 13 National Weather Service (NWS) offices paired with real AI weather forecast inputs, and three complementary metrics: Met-Align (numerical accuracy), Style-Align (professional dialect adherence), and Input-Grounding (fidelity to source weather data). Zero-shot evaluations reveal that open-source LLMs achieve low Style-Align (~0.33) and moderate Input-Grounding (~0.88), failing to write in the professional NWS register or faithfully use their input data. We apply Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with domain-specific rewards targeting temperature accuracy, synoptic correctness, and format compliance. On 1,033 held-out samples from two unseen NWS offices, GRPO nearly doubles Style-Align from 0.318 to 0.619 and improves Input-Grounding from 0.881 to 0.940, demonstrating that reinforcement learning teaches a 7B-parameter model to write like a professional meteorologist and faithfully interpret AI weather data.

</details>


### [241] [Why and When Neural Networks Improve Local Approximation in Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24963)
*Chengkuo Bian,Pengcheng Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对神经网络代理模型在无导数优化(DFO)中效果矛盾的争议，提出并系统验证了决定学习式局部模型能否带来收益的三个关键因素：算法角色(Role)、有效半径(Radius)和基方法余量(Room)。核心发现是：代理模型仅在"安全辅助"而非"替换核心量"的嵌入方式下、在其可靠泛化半径内、且基方法仍有改进空间时，才能提升优化效率；在117个基准测试中将高精度求解实例从67提升到84，但替换角色则降至65。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献对神经网络代理在DFO中的作用存在矛盾结论：同一族模型在某些求解器上能减少评估次数，在另一些上却毫无改善甚至恶化。论文旨在通过分离并量化真正决定代理价值的机制性因素来化解这一矛盾，明确训练曲线报告的拟合精度并非优化收益的可靠预测指标。

Method: 1) 区分两种嵌入角色——"替换"(replacement，代理梯度替代有限差分梯度)与"安全辅助"(assistance，候选点经真实目标函数验证)。2) 定义"半径感知局部泛化"(radius-aware local generalization)：通过定义4/5给出均匀误差剖面 $e_f(x,\Delta), e_g(x,\Delta)$ 与有效泛化半径 $r_{\text{gen}}(x)$，并引入非零误差下界 $\epsilon_{g,0}$，证明在完全线性标准下任何拟合网络的半径均为零。3) 给出两个分析命题：命题1基于Lipschitz连续性与填充距离得到均匀梯度误差界 $e_g(x,\Delta)\le\epsilon_g+(L_f+L_m)\varepsilon$；命题2给出近似梯度下降方向的充分条件 $\langle\nabla f,-g_m\rangle\le -\|\nabla f\|(\|\nabla f\|-\epsilon_g)$。4) 提出ARAS自适应半径感知代理框架：以 $\kappa_k=\|\nabla m_k\|/\|g_k^{FD}\|$ 与 $\varrho_k$（训练残差）作为闸门统计量，在3202次外迭代上验证其预测能力，并给出收敛定理保证基方法的 $\mathcal{O}(n\epsilon^{-2})$ 复杂度。5) 实验固定网络类(Sobolev训练的单隐层Softplus网络，宽 $6n$，900次Adam迭代)、训练流程与基方法，仅变动嵌入机制；在117个Moré-Garbow-Hillstrom问题上对照FD基方法、NN辅助(安全)、NN辅助(仅值)、NN替换、自研DFO-TR、DFO-TR+NN辅助及Py-BOBYQA共六种求解器；附加273个区域的半径扫描、200个噪声级别配对比较、90次Monte Carlo库存模型实验和3202次迭代的诊断验证。

Result: 在117个基准实例上，安全辅助将高精度($\tau=10^{-5}$)求解数从基方法的67提升至84，梯度替换则降至65；移除训练损失中的梯度项使代理接受率从0.703降至0.148。半径扫描表明均匀梯度误差随半径单调上升，从 $\Delta=10^{-2}$ 时的相对误差0.055升至 $\Delta=3.2$ 时的1.57，且在最小半径处存在非零误差下界，导致69%的问题-维度组合的拟合指数低于完全线性要求的 $\beta=1$。噪声扫描(1000次配对比较)显示无统一噪声阈值，基方法运行长度从62.3%预算降至21.7%才是主因。在Monte Carlo库存模型上，确定性接口下所有方法停滞于初始设计；修复采样接口(复制+独立SE)使成本降低10.40单位(95% CI [-12.74,-8.84])，但在此之上添加安全辅助的中位数差异为0.00。诊断研究证实 $\kappa_k$ 与 $\varrho_k$ 与接受率相关(Spearman $\rho=-0.492$ 与 $-0.394$)，但校准显示在确定性基准上闸门并不产生收益。整体上Py-BOBYQA在每一维度均领先($\tau=10^{-5}$ 时103 vs 84)，论文亦指出扩展Wood函数上安全辅助是唯一突破4/9实例限制的方法。

Conclusion: 神经网络代理提升无导数优化的效果由三个经验因素界定，而非训练损失所报告的点拟合精度。安全辅助优于替换角色；代理仅在其有限泛化半径内可靠，缩小半径并不消除误差下界；在基方法尚能推进且其验收机制仍能区分噪声与真实下降时，代理方可加速收敛。在噪声环境下没有共同的噪声阈值，问题可被帮助的最大噪声水平跨越四个数量级；真正缩短的是基方法的运行而非代理成功率。在实际仿真模型中必须先修复接受测试所依赖的采样与方差控制，代理仅在此之后才能被有意义地讨论。三项后续工作：探索代理架构参数计数不随 $n$ 增长的方案、寻找诊断以识别代理优于成熟插值的特定目标类、及在已发布自适应采样求解器上验证努力优先级序。

Abstract: Published experience with neural surrogates in derivative-free optimisation is contradictory: the same family of models that cuts the evaluation count of one solver leaves another unchanged, or makes it worse. We show that the contradiction dissolves once three factors are stated, and that these, rather than the fit accuracy a training curve reports, are what delimit when a learned local model pays. Role: a surrogate that proposes candidates the true objective must still approve helps, while one that replaces a gradient the solver depends on hurts. Radius: a model fitted to an optimisation path is reliable only inside a bounded neighbourhood, and its error neither vanishes as that neighbourhood shrinks nor survives its growth. Room: a surrogate can only accelerate progress the base method is still able to make. We formalise radius-aware local generalisation, relate it to the classical fully linear condition, and test each factor with the surrogate class, training pipeline and base method held fixed. Over 117 benchmark instances safeguarded assistance raises the instances solved to high accuracy from 67 to 84 while gradient replacement lowers them to 65; removing the gradient term from the training loss cuts surrogate acceptance from 0.703 to 0.148; and 1000 paired comparisons over ten noise levels show no noise threshold, only a base method that stops early. The same factors bound the gain: a model-based trust-region solver, which leaves little room, drops from 88 to 86 when the identical surrogate is attached, and released interpolation software stays ahead at 103, and on a Monte-Carlo inventory model repairing the acceptance interface is worth 10.40 cost units against 0.00 for the surrogate.

</details>


### [242] [Physics-Informed Error Field Learning: A Post-Training Optimization Framework for Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24970)
*Jiuyun Sun,Yong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PIEFL框架，在PINN主网络收敛后引入辅助误差网络学习解的残差误差场，通过物理约束的误差控制方程实现后训练优化，无需修改主网络架构即可在相同计算预算下显著提升求解精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN训练后期存在明显的边际收益递减效应：继续优化主网络参数带来的精度提升有限，但计算成本持续增加。需要一种更高效的后期优化策略，将学习目标从完整解场转移到残差误差场上，以更少的计算开销实现精度提升。

Method: 采用级联两阶段架构：(1) 训练主网络PINN获得初始近似解u_p；(2) 冻结主网络参数后，训练辅助误差网络学习误差场u_e = u - u_p。推导满足物理约束的误差控制方程∂u_e/∂t + N_e[u_e] + f_p = 0，其中非线性误差算子N_e[u_e] = N[u_p+u_e] - N[u_p]。引入输出缩放系数α以放大误差网络的输出，降低小幅值误差场的学习难度。最终解为u_final = u_p + α·û_e。误差网络的初始/边界条件由原IC/BC减去主网络预测得到。

Result: 在KdV方程（单孤子解）上，相对L2误差从PINN的9.48e-4降至1.47e-4（ERR约84.83%）；在非线性Schrödinger方程上，单孤子和双孤子解的ERR分别为95.22%和91.49%；在(2+1)维KP方程上，单孤子和双孤子解的ERR分别为80.89%和38.53%。缩放系数α的消融实验表明，α=0.001时效果最优，过大（α=1或0.1）会导致误差网络训练失败。复杂解结构下误差场的精确重建更困难，但PIEFL仍能提供有效修正。

Conclusion: PIEFL作为一种通用后训练优化策略，无需修改现有PINN架构即可与各种增强技术（架构改进、自适应采样、损失平衡等）兼容。数值实验验证了其在典型PDE问题上的有效性，相同计算预算下精度显著优于持续训练策略。未来工作将聚焦于更高效的误差场学习策略及其在高维PDE和复杂物理系统中的扩展应用。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) have emerged as an important class of numerical methods for solving partial differential equations (PDEs). However, during the late-stage optimization process, further parameter updates often yield diminishing accuracy improvements while increasing computational costs. To address this issue, this paper proposes a Physics-Informed Error Field Learning (PIEFL) framework for PINNs. Unlike conventional approaches that continuously approximate the solution field using a single network, PIEFL introduces an auxiliary error network after the primary network achieves satisfactory accuracy and shifts the learning objective from the solution field to the error field. By deriving error control equations under physical constraints, the error network learns the discrepancy between the current approximation and the exact solution, and the learned error correction is combined with the primary prediction to improve solution accuracy. The proposed framework avoids continuous optimization of the entire solution space and focuses computational resources on correcting existing prediction errors. Moreover, PIEFL requires no modification to the primary network architecture, making it compatible with existing PINN models and applicable as a general post-training optimization strategy. Numerical experiments on representative PDEs demonstrate that PIEFL achieves higher solution accuracy under the same computational budget, validating its effectiveness in improving the performance of PINNs.

</details>


### [243] [MSR-IVA: Masked Structural Residual Independent Vector Analysis for State-Aware Fusion of Structural MRI and Dynamic Functional Network Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24978)
*Victor Solomon,Zening Fu,Rafal Angryk,Vince D. Calhoun,Jingyu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MSR-IVA框架，用于将结构MRI与多个部分观测的动态功能网络连接(dFNC)状态进行状态感知融合。该方法通过共享结构分量、状态特异性残差和掩码机制，在保持跨状态结构对应关系的同时，允许状态特异性适应并有效利用所有可用被试数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态融合方法在处理同一结构表征与多个动态功能状态耦合时面临两个核心挑战：(1) 对每个状态独立应用IVA会产生不相关的结构分解，破坏跨状态对应关系；(2) 强制相同分解又会抑制状态特异性关系。此外，并非每个被试都表达所有动态状态，完整案例分析会丢弃大量有效数据。现有方法缺乏对这些问题的统一解决方案。

Method: MSR-IVA采用"共享+残差"参数化策略：W^{g_r} = W^{g_0} + ΔW^{g_r}，其中W^{g_0}为跨状态共享的结构解混矩阵，ΔW^{g_r}为状态特异性残差调整。状态掩码m^{(r)}确保每个sMRI-dFNC融合问题仅使用有效观测被试Ψ_r。目标函数包含Kotz SCV依赖项（建模匹配源依赖）、对数体积正则化（防止秩坍缩）和Frobenius范数残差惩罚（正则化状态特异性适应强度α）。该设计实现了从完全独立到完全共享结构分解之间的软共享机制。实验使用ADNI队列573名被试（296 CN, 160 MCI, 117痴呆），每个状态取20个SCV，α=0.1，Adam优化500轮，5个随机种子。

Result: 在ADNI队列上，MSR-IVA相比IP-IVA基线：匹配源耦合提升6.5%（0.8957→0.9540），非匹配源依赖降低15.7%（0.0598→0.0504），耦合对比度从0.8360提升至0.9035。在124名同时表达两个状态的被试中，跨状态结构源相似度为0.9177（无共享：0.2978，硬共享：1.0000），验证了软共享机制。所有20个SCV在最优匹配下保持跨状态索引对应，SCV置换检验全局p≈1.0×10⁻⁴。

Conclusion: MSR-IVA成功实现了独立结构分解与硬共享分解之间的可控中间状态。通过共享结构分量、状态特异性残差和掩码机制的结合，方法在保持跨状态源对应关系的同时允许状态特异性适应，并能利用每个状态的所有可用观测。状态特异性残差正则化强度α提供了灵活的共享程度控制。代码已开源（github.com/dasjar/MSRIVA）。

Abstract: Multimodal fusion of structural MRI (sMRI) and dynamic functional network connectivity (dFNC) can reveal how brain structure relates to changing functional states. When the same structural latent representation is coupled with multiple states, applying independent vector analysis (IVA) separately to each state can produce unrelated structural decompositions, while forcing identical decompositions may suppress state-specific relationships. In addition, not every subject expresses every dynamic state. We propose masked structural residual IVA (MSR-IVA), a state-aware framework that combines a shared structural representation with state-specific residual adaptations and masks for incomplete state expression. On an Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative cohort, MSR-IVA improved matched source coupling by 6.5% and reduced unmatched dependence by 15.7% relative to the independent pairwise IVA baseline. Among subjects expressing both states, mean absolute cross-state structural source correlation was 0.9177 for MSR-IVA versus 0.2978 for no sharing, demonstrating controlled structural sharing that preserves source correspondence while allowing state-specific adaptation.

</details>


### [244] [Rollout-Decoded Reconstruction for Long-Horizon Prediction in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25017)
*Rishi Shah,Rishav Shrestha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RDR（Rollout-Decoded Reconstruction），一种无新增参数的训练损失项，在自由运行rollout的每一个latent上解码并对真实观测施加重建损失，使解码器在训练时即接触到部署时实际遇到的漂移latent。在Kuramoto-Sivashinsky混沌PDE上将有效预测时间（VPT@0.5）从3.87±0.23提升至6.97±0.42时间单位（1.80×），在10个预注册配置中以1.71–2.50×的优势全部胜出，且额外参数被控制变量排除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在世界模型的解码器仅在后验latent（由观测锚定）上训练，而推理时它必须解码自由运行rollout中已严重偏离训练分布的latent，这一train-deployment不匹配导致长horizon预测崩溃。PlaNet曾提出"解码rollout"方案但因图像域代价过高被搁置，此后无人将其作为研究对象。

Method: 在标准encoder–transition–decoder三元组（MLP编码器+S5预测器+MLP解码器+EMA目标编码器）基础上，增加一项λ·L_RDR损失：对自由运行rollout产生的每个latent z^_{t+k}应用解码器，并与真实场u_{t+k}计算MSE。该项不新增参数，不改变架构；λ=0时退化为标准目标；当λ=0.3为主用权重，并对λ∈{0.1,0.3,0.6,1.0}不敏感。rollout分支在2个warmup epoch后线性打开。

Result: 在KS方程（L=22, 64网格点, ETDRK4积分）的60+60+64划分训练上，头条配置（潜在维度32，解码器宽512，K=128，320 epoch，3种子）下参数完全匹配（193,568）时，VPT从3.87±0.23提升至6.97±0.42 tu（1.80×），并在未参与选择的新种子10–12上确认，10/10预注册配置以1.71–2.50×获胜；分头解码器变体（+25.7%参数）在7/10配置上更差，排除容量解释。潜在宽度扫描（16→48）显示RDR优势随宽度增大（1.41→2.50×），而后验单臂反而下降；在完全可观测系统上，观测空间pushforward在匹配预算下达到7.00±0.26，与RDR持平，限定了潜在瓶颈的收益。控制实验显示RDR对规划器-训练rollout不匹配更鲁棒，但减少数据阶梯下的样本效率优势在步数匹配控制后基本消失。

Conclusion: RDR以单一损失项、无新增参数、且λ=0时退化为标准目标的方式，直接闭合潜在世界模型解码器的训练-部署分布差距，在混沌PDE上实现1.80×的有效预测时间提升并通过预注册协议验证；其可叠加于任何保留解码器的潜在世界模型上。主要局限在于：单一动力系统、三种子的预测结论；控制实验为初步且与数据量与优化步数存在混淆；机制层面（解码器鲁棒性、潜动力学习、隐式正则化三条路径）尚未分解隔离；未在部分可观测、像素观测、规划必须紧凑状态等强制需要latent的设置下验证泛化性。

Abstract: A latent world model trains its decoder on latents anchored to observations, then deploys it on the model's own free-running rollout, hundreds of steps past the last observation. Rollout-Decoded Reconstruction (RDR) closes this gap with a single loss term that free-runs the model during training exactly as evaluation will, decodes every rollout latent, and penalizes reconstruction error against ground truth. The term adds no parameters, costs training-time compute only, and reduces to the standard objective at weight zero, so every comparison in this paper is a one-flag A/B. On the chaotic Kuramoto-Sivashinsky equation, RDR raises valid prediction time (the time to first crossing of normalized error 0.5) from $3.87 \pm 0.23$ to $6.97 \pm 0.42$ time units at an identical 193,568 parameters, a $1.80\times$ improvement confirmed on seeds never used in selection and holding in 10 of 10 preregistered configurations at ratios of 1.71-2.50$\times$. The results come from a single system; a sweep in which the advantage grows with latent width is descriptive, and control experiments on two classic tasks are preliminary.

</details>


### [245] [On the Representational Geometry of Dynamic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25034)
*Richard F. M. Lim,Ruriko Yoshida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从几何角度研究动态规划（DP）的表示结构，证明有穷极小加法DP在图（two-terminal DAG）、代数（tropical多项式）、几何（extended Newton polyhedron）三种语言间构成三层同构的幂等半环，并通过这两个层次刻画了图等价性的完整不变量运算。核心结论是否定性的：关于长度泛化的两个关键机制均失败——半环的两种恒等替换（P_i和Q_i）既不单射也不在DP内封闭；串并联复合不足以生成所有DAG拓扑（反例为Wheatstone图），且仅使用终端运算也不能捕获所有DP复合（反例为clamped min-subarray）。这些负结果从代数几何角度为等变架构设计提供了边界，揭示了仅靠(min,+)结构无法实现长度泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准神经网络架构在动态规划（DP）任务上往往无法泛化到比训练更长的输入，即使DP的递推本身与输入长度无关。这一现象在循环网络和注意力模型中普遍存在，限制了对组合优化问题的可扩展学习。已有的"Tropical Attention"等架构虽显示了一定的长度泛化能力，但其成功背后的代数几何原因尚不清楚。理解哪些结构使DP可在任意长度上工作，以及能否从小实例推断大实例，是设计真正具备长度泛化能力的神经架构的理论基础。

Method: 方法围绕"三重性"（triality）展开：(1) 证明有穷极小加法DP等价于two-terminal DAG上单对最短路径，等价于tropical多项式，其决策边界由extended Newton polyhedron编码；(2) 证明这三者在形式多项式和计算函数两个层级上构成交换幂等半环的同构（Theorem 1），垂直商映射连接两层；(3) 在图语言中给出deshared范式D_f和函数范式D_v，并通过四类局部运算（series-run reweighting、parallel-bundle bookkeeping、tropical-zero adjunction、vertex splitting/merging）证明正规化与完备性（Theorem 2）；(4) 研究两种半环恒等替换P_i（x_i→+∞）和Q_i（x_i→0）在三种语言中的几何含义，证明它们均不单射且偶尔不封闭（Proposition 3）；(5) 通过Wheatstone图证明串并联无法生成所有terminal-only操作，通过clamped min-subarray证明terminal-only操作无法捕获所有DP复合（Proposition 4）。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 三重性定理（Theorem 1）：图/代数/几何在形式和函数两层均构成同构的交换幂等半环；(2) 不变量运算完备性（Theorem 2）：四个局部运算足以将任意DAG化为两种范式，且D_f/G等价、D_v/同函数等价均是可判定的；(3) 替换失败（Proposition 3 + Corollary 21）：P_i和Q_i在三种语言中各有明确几何对应（坐标面/坐标阴影、recession方向/slice），但都不单射，且有时不在DP内封闭（图1的min-subset-sum例子中，P_3和Q_3无法区分全子集与真子集问题）；(4) 复合不足（Proposition 4）：Wheatstone边替换不可由串并联生成；clamped min-subarray合并涉及内部顶点识别，超出terminal-only操作范畴。证明在附录A–G中给出。

Conclusion: 三重性表明任何一种语言（图、代数、几何）中发现的复合结构都可精确传递到另外两种；不变量演算刻画了DAG编码中全部冗余，区分了表示人为造成的差异与计算函数的内在性质。负结果对等变架构设计给出边界：半环原生替换无法将长度T的多面体几何提升到T+1（Section 4），Bellman式推理可用的复合对称性双重不足（Section 5）。这些负结果共同界定了仅靠(min,+)结构在架构中能实现的长度泛化上界，指向实现真正长度泛化所需的更丰富的复合运算。

Abstract: Standard neural architectures often fail to generalize to longer inputs for dynamic programming (DP) targets. We investigate what makes this hard geometrically. Every finite min-plus DP is a shortest path on a DAG, which is equivalently a tropical polynomial whose extended Newton polyhedron encodes the decision boundary of which path wins. We prove these three descriptions (graph, polynomial, polyhedron) form isomorphic semirings at two levels --- formal polynomials and their computed functions --- connected by operations that characterize all structural redundancies. We then address the length-generalization question geometrically: does the decision boundary at length $T$ decide the boundary at $T+1$? We present two structural negatives. The semiring's two native ways to reduce dimension (setting a variable to each identity) are neither injective nor always closed within the DP. Series and parallel composition fail to construct all DAG topologies from smaller sub-DAGs, and even all terminal-only operations do not capture all DP compositions.

</details>


### [246] [The Frame Kernel Method for Multiscale Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25084)
*Branden Frieden,Ryan Whitehead,M. Keith Ballard,Robert M. Kirby,Varun Shankar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"框架核方法"(Frame Kernel Method, FKM)的原生多尺度算子学习方法。该方法通过构建一种新颖的多尺度核框架函数近似技术，将算子学习问题转化为输入与输出函数框架系数之间的映射问题。该方法同时适用于张量积网格和点云，并在多个标准与多尺度偏微分方程基准测试中，相较于流行的神经算子方法显著提升了预测精度，同时支持预测结果的多尺度后验分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多尺度偏微分方程（如异质介质、湍流、多孔流、复合材料等）涉及跨多个数量级空间尺度的相互作用，现有算子学习方法（如FNO、DeepONet等）难以有效编码此类多尺度结构。传统核算子学习方法虽具竞争力，但其表示空间的选取对学习映射的难度有决定性影响——如何在不依赖频率分离、网格连通性或层次细化结构的前提下，直接在多种空间长度尺度上构造多尺度表示空间，是关键难题。

Method: 方法包括三部分：(1) 多尺度核框架近似——利用紧支撑径向基函数（如Wendland族）在嵌套中心集上构建冗余有限框架，每层使用不同的中心密度与支撑半径 $\rho_j = \eta 2^j s_0$，通过QR分解求解最小范数系数实现精确插值；(2) 收敛性分析——证明框架性质（定理1）、给出基于散乱零点不等式的Sobolev误差估计（定理2），并提出加倍收敛速率猜想（猜想3.1，定理3）；(3) 框架核方法(FKM)用于算子学习——在输入端与输出端分别建立多尺度框架表示，将学习任务转化为归一化输入系数到按层次组织的输出系数之间的映射，采用层次级多输出核岭回归实现预测。

Result: 在四个标准PDE基准（腔体流、Darcy流、反应扩散等）和四个多尺度基准（圆柱绕流、3D可压缩NS、组分输运等）的算子学习测试中，FKM在大多数基准上取得了最低的相对ℓ₂预测误差，相比Geo-FNO、Transolver以及单尺度的核方法(VKM)，在腔体流、层流圆柱绕流等问题上实现了超过两个数量级的精度提升。此外，FKM天然提供预测结果的后验多尺度分解，可清晰展现不同物理尺度上的流场结构。

Conclusion: FKM是一种有效且通用的多尺度算子学习方法，通过显式的多尺度框架表示显著提升了多尺度PDE代理建模的精度与可解释性。未来工作包括证明加倍收敛猜想、发展可扩展到百万空间点的稀疏线性代数技术（如sketching、预条件迭代最小二乘、块QR分解），以及将FKM集成到多尺度多重网格求解器中以处理内聚区域模型和非线性弹性问题。

Abstract: We present a natively multiscale operator learning method for the surrogate modeling of (numerical solvers for) multiscale partial differential equations (PDEs). The primary novelty of our method lies in a novel multiscale kernel frame function approximation technique. Leveraging this new kernel frame technique, we cast the operator learning problem as one of learning frame coefficients of output functions as a function of frame coefficients of input functions. The generalization step then automatically allows for a multiscale decomposition of the output functions. Our method is applicable to both tensor-product grids and point clouds. We present interpolation proofs, error estimates, and numerical convergence rates for our frame approximation. We the demonstrate the applicability of our method for the surrogate modeling of inherently multiscale PDEs. The new multiscale frame kernel method is significantly more accurate than popular neural operators on challenging problems from the literature, while simultaneously admitting an a posteriori multiscale decomposition upon generalization.

</details>


### [247] [Multimodal Injury Risk Prediction in Tennis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25126)
*Francisco Erramuspe Alvarez,Shobharani Polasa,Weihao Qu,Jay Wang,Ling Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于网球运动员的多模态预测框架 PART,通过整合可穿戴设备数据、自报问卷、跳跃测试和比赛视频等多源数据,利用机器学习和深度学习预测运动员的综合健康状况、身体能力、损伤风险及比赛风格,并生成运动员准备度评分(ARS)及特定身体部位的损伤预警。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的网球运动表现与损伤预测研究主要依赖主观观察和专家评估,难以捕捉多因素的复杂交互作用;同时,可穿戴设备、视频分析等多源客观数据在网球领域尚未得到充分利用,尤其是对业余球员因错误技术导致的损伤(如网球肘、网球膝)缺乏有效预测工具。

Method: 1) 从 9 名大学生网球运动员收集四类数据:WHOOP 可穿戴设备的生理与睡眠数据、每日自报问卷、Sparta 跳跃测试数据、比赛视频(共 85 个特征,82804 条记录);2) 数据预处理包括缺失值填补、对数变换、交互项、多项式特征、独热编码;3) 构建四个模型:XGBoost 回归器预测整体健康(Recovery Score)、XGBoost 分类器预测损伤风险及身体部位、MLP 与 LSTM 基于 7 天时序数据预测物理能力(PCS)、视频分析基于每分平均击球数(ASP)进行风格分类;4) 通过监督学习(多元线性回归)确定权重,将三个分数融合为 ARS,并基于风格权重进行调节。

Result: 整体健康预测:XGBoost 回归器 MAE=3.82,R²=0.838;损伤风险分类:XGBoost 分类器 AUC-ROC=0.695,准确率=0.852,优于逻辑回归等基线;上肢/下肢损伤预测 AUC-ROC 分别达 0.712 和 0.703;物理能力预测:LSTM 验证损失=0.6059,优于 MLP;所提 PART 框架在 9 名大学生运动员数据上表现良好,并展现出对业余球员的应用潜力。

Conclusion: PART 是首个针对网球的多模态损伤风险预测框架,成功整合多种数据源实现对运动员状态的整体评估及身体部位损伤预警;未来将拓展更精细的损伤部位预测、改进视频数据融合方式,并开发为面向不同水平球员的智能网球教练软件。

Abstract: Machine learning has had a significant positive impact on the prediction of athlete performance and injury risk. Most works in this field rely on subjective observations and expert assessments, which restrict their effectiveness. In sports like soccer, basketball, and wrestling, some studies attempt to address this challenge by integrating data from alternative sources, such as readings from wearable devices, alongside traditional subjective observations and expert assessments to enhance accuracy. However, similar research in tennis remains largely unexplored. In this paper, we propose a multimodal Predictive Athlete Readiness framework for Tennis (PART) to assess both performance and injury risk in tennis players. By leveraging machine learning and deep learning techniques, PART processes multiple sources of data collected from nine collegiate tennis players, including physiological metrics, training and match data, sleep data from wearable devices, self-reported information via daily questionnaires, jump assessments, and motion analysis from match play videos. PART captures four characteristics of tennis players: overall wellness, injury risk, physical capability, and playing style. By integrating these four characteristics by supervised learning, it is capable of providing a holistic assessment of the tennis athlete's condition, along with advanced forecasts of specific body areas at risk such as the upper body (e.g., elbows) or lower body (e.g., knees). Our evaluation, conducted with data from nine collegiate tennis players, shows that PART achieves strong performance in predicting both overall wellness and injury risk. Additionally, our framework also shows promise for recreational tennis players, who often suffer from injuries due to incorrect playing techniques.

</details>


### [248] [Rethinking the Transferable Adversarial Attacks and Robust Defense in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25133)
*Zuobin Xiong,Deval Mukherjee,Homook Cho,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视联邦学习中的可迁移对抗攻击，针对现有投毒攻击和拜占庭攻击依赖不现实假设（如攻击者比例、客户端选择率等）的问题，转而研究更具可行性的逃逸攻击（对抗样本）。作者在FedProx框架下首次从理论上分析了对抗样本在不同客户端模型间的可迁移性，证明其上界由攻击者与受害者之间的数据分布差异及模型参数距离共同决定。基于此理论洞察，提出了基于SVD的特征演化模块（SDFEM）与对抗训练相结合的高效鲁棒防御机制。实验在CIFAR-10和SVHN数据集上验证了理论分析的正确性，并证明所提防御方法在IID和Non-IID设置下均优于现有最先进方法FAT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对联邦学习的投毒攻击和拜占庭攻击依赖诸多不切实际的假设（如攻击者需要控制10%-25%的客户端、客户端总数已知等），在实际大规模FL场景中难以实现。近期研究（如Shejwalkar等）表明即使无防御机制下，这些攻击在大规模FL中也难以达到声称的攻击效果。这促使作者探索一种更可操作的攻击——仅需单个恶意客户端即可发起的逃逸攻击（对抗样本攻击），并研究其理论性能和相应的鲁棒防御。

Method: 1) 在FedProx框架下定义对抗样本可迁移性为攻击者与受害者损失函数值之差的范数；2) 通过Lipschitz连续性和Lipschitz连续梯度假设，推导出模型参数距离的上界，证明可迁移性上界与攻击者和受害者的本地数据分布差异p_V(y_i=c)-p_A(y_i=c)以及梯度最大值G_max相关；3) 提出SVD驱动的特征演化模块(SDFEM)，通过可学习的掩码M对奇异值进行重加权，抑制对对抗扰动敏感的特征通道；4) 在联邦聚合中引入对抗训练，修改本地目标函数以同时优化干净数据和对抗数据上的损失，最小化联合目标函数（包含SDFEM、交叉熵损失和近端正则项）。

Result: 实验在CIFAR-10和SVHN数据集、VGG11和WideResNet-28-10模型上进行，使用VMIFGSM、BIM、VNIFGSM、DIFGSM四种迁移攻击验证：1) 理论验证：模型参数距离与攻击成功率(ASR)呈负相关，分布距离与攻击可迁移性差距呈正相关（Spearman系数r>0.4，p≈0）；2) 防御性能：在IID设置下，标准FedProx面对迁移攻击鲁棒精度近乎0%(<1%)，所提方法相对FAT基线有显著提升（如CIFAR-10 VGG11上VMIFGSM/BIM/VNIFGSM/DIFGSM分别提升+1.69/+1.17/+1.79/+1.86点）；在Non-IID更具异构设置下，WRN28相对FAT提升更大（如CIFAR-10上VMIFGSM提升+9.26点），且干净精度基本保持。

Conclusion: 本文证明联邦学习中对抗样本的可迁移性本质上取决于客户端间的数据分布差异，揭示了异构数据下联邦系统的脆弱性。基于此理论洞察，所提出的SDFEM结合对抗训练的防御机制能有效提升鲁棒精度同时保持竞争力干净的精度，优于FAT等强基线，且在数据异构性更明显的Non-IID场景下优势更加突出。研究表明可迁移性是联邦学习中关键的安全维度，鲁棒感知防御可显著增强实际环境中的FL系统安全性。

Abstract: The development of federated learning (FL) techniques has helped improve the privacy preservation of users' data and extended the applications of machine learning models. However, the involvement of a large number of users in FL also creates open opportunities for different adversaries, such as poisoning attacks, Byzantine attacks, and adversarial example attacks. Yet, recent research has disclosed that existing poisoning attacks and Byzantine attacks can not achieve satisfactory penetration in realistic FL scenarios caused by strong assumptions, \textit{e.g.,} client selection rate, and the ratio of malicious attackers. In this paper, the transferability of adversarial examples among different client models is analyzed to understand the relation between adversarial examples and clients' data distribution. Moreover, to mitigate the attacks of transferable adversarial examples, we design a defense mechanism stemming from the transferability of model robustness by adversarial training. As a result, through theoretical analysis of transferability, we gain insights into adversarial examples and the vulnerability of federated learning systems. Our proposed adversarial attack and defense methods are evaluated via real-life datasets in various settings to show their performance over the existing state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [249] [SNAP-KG: Streaming Node Assignment via Projection for Knowledge Graph Entity Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25149)
*Jui-Chien Lin,Mohammad Mohammadi Amiri,Oshani Seneviratne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SNAP-KG 是首个面向流式知识图谱实体集成的图结构多视图归纳式聚类框架，通过 GNN-to-MLP 蒸馏将新到达实体直接映射到学习到的嵌入空间，无需图访问或重训练即可完成聚类分配，并将下游实体解析和链接预测的候选搜索空间大幅压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在流式知识图谱构建中，新到达实体仅有原始特征向量而无图连接，必须先被分配到语义社区，下游 ER 和 LP 才能在可处理的候选集上运行。现有方法存在根本缺陷：多视图图聚类方法虽能利用多种关系类型，但都是直推式（transductive）的，无法处理未见实体；归纳式 GNN 仅适用于单视图；神经阻塞方法则丢弃关系结构。本文旨在同时满足三个条件：(i) 冷启动无邻域、(ii) 流式低延迟、(iii) 多视图关系结构。

Method: SNAP-KG 框架分离线训练与在线推理两阶段。离线阶段：(1) 对每个关系类型对应的邻接矩阵做自适应邻居过滤（基于余弦相似度分布的标准差决定固定 k 或比例 ρ 截断），过滤仅用于训练，不修改原图；(2) 每个视图用独立 GNN 编码得到视图特定嵌入；(3) 用带可学习位置编码与聚合 token 的 Transformer 编码器融合多视图嵌入；(4) 基于跨视图正邻域的对比学习损失 ℒ_cont 训练聚类可分性；(5) MLP 投影器 f_φ 通过 ℒ_proj 重建融合嵌入，实现 GNN-to-MLP 蒸馏；(6) 联合优化 ℒ_total = ℒ_cont + λ·ℒ_proj。在线推理：x_new 经 f_φ 一次前向传播映射到嵌入空间，按最近聚类中心分配，无需图访问或重训练。聚类可选 K-means，并支持与 IVF-ANN 索引组合用于生产级检索。

Result: 在 ACM、DBLP、IMDB、YELP、MAG 五个基准数据集及 240 万节点的 OGB-WikiKG2 生产级 KG 上验证：(1) 流式推理效率——SNAP-KG-Projector 推理时间比 BMGC-Retrain、DEMM-Retrain 低多个数量级（MAG 上毫秒级 vs. >65 秒/批），ACC/F1 与重训练上界相当；(2) 聚类质量——MAG 上取得最佳 ACC 68.20%、F1 68.35%，其余数据集在最强直推基线 2% 以内；(3) 消融——多视图融合显著优于任一单视图（如 DBLP 上 NMI 从单视图 5.02% 提升到 74.03%）；(4) 下游任务——基准数据集 ER/LP 候选搜索缩减 62-75%，OGB-WikiKG2 缩减 97%，DBLP-ACM 跨数据集归纳迁移 F1 保持 92.64% vs 全扫描 93.45%。

Conclusion: SNAP-KG 是首个图结构多视图归纳式框架，同时支持关系聚类与流式实体的归纳推理，将每实体推理从完整重训练压缩为单次前向传播，多个数量级加速；聚类分配质量与重训练上界持平，同时将 ER/LP 候选搜索缩减 62-97%。框架对聚类算法选择不可知，且因重聚类成本远低于重训练，聚类结构可在不修改模型权重的情况下随图分布演化。进一步可与 IVF-ANN 索引组合实现次线性生产级检索，组合管线的实证评估留待未来工作。

Abstract: Knowledge graph (KG) construction pipelines must continuously integrate newly arriving entities into a growing graph. Unlike inserting triples between existing nodes, a newly arriving entity has no graph connectivity: it emerges from the acquisition phase as a raw feature vector and must be assigned to a semantic community before entity resolution and link prediction can operate over a tractable candidate set. Existing multi-view graph clustering methods exploit multiple relation types as structural views, but are transductive: they assume a fixed graph and cannot assign unseen entities without retraining. We propose SNAP-KG (Streaming Node Assignment via Projection for Knowledge Graph Entity Integration), a framework supporting graph-structural multi-view relational clustering and inductive inference for streaming entities. SNAP-KG trains a projector to map a new entity directly to the learned embedding space using only raw features, enabling immediate cluster assignment without graph access or model retraining. Experiments on five benchmark multi-view graph datasets and a production-scale KG of 2.4 million nodes demonstrate multiple orders-of-magnitude inference speedups over retraining-based approaches and competitive clustering quality. As a candidate scoping mechanism for downstream tasks, SNAP-KG achieves 62-75% candidate search reduction on the five benchmark datasets and 97% on OGB-WikiKG2 for entity resolution and link prediction.

</details>


### [250] [Simultaneous inference of environmental and interaction forces in collective dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25181)
*Nipuni de Silva,Ming Zhong,James M. Greene*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的变分学习框架，用于从轨迹数据中同时推断集体动力学系统中智能体间的交互核（非参数）和环境力（半参数或全非参数），并据此构建了一个数据驱动的模型选择程序来识别主动机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集体动力学（细胞迁移、群体机器人、动物群行为等）中的局部交互机制难以直接获取，而现有方程学习方法常忽略集体动力学的物理结构，且难以同时处理交互力与环境力。

Method: 基于一阶和二阶集体动力学 ODE 框架，将学习问题转化为有限维假设空间下的加权最小二乘问题，通过分段 B 样条基函数非参数地推断交互核 ϕ，同时以半参数或全非参数方式学习环境力 f；并设计了结合兼容性阈值与奥卡姆剃刀（复杂度优先）的模型选择算法。

Result: 在 Kuramoto 同步、SPP 自推进粒子、phototaxis、Cucker-Smale 群体行为、SPPCS 及意见动力学等基准模型上，该方法能准确恢复交互核与环境力（相对误差达 10⁻²–10⁻⁶ 量级），在含噪数据（N/S=10%）和少样本（M=1）条件下仍具有鲁棒性，并能可靠地识别正确的动力学结构。

Conclusion: 通过利用集体动力学的结构对称性将高维问题降至低维，该框架提供了兼具可解释性与预测能力的机械模型估计，并可推广至具有对称性或其他结构化特性的更广泛动力系统；未来工作将聚焦异质智能体、随机动力学、基函数自适应选择及收敛性理论分析。

Abstract: Collective dynamics arise in a wide range of physical, biological, and engineering applications. Examples include cell migration, swarm robotics, social dynamics, and animal behavior. A defining characteristic of these systems is the emergence of large-scale coordination from local interactions among agents; a fundamental question is thus to understand the local interactions that give rise to the observed emergent dynamics. We are interested in methods for learning interactions generally, which can describe a wide class of physical systems exhibiting collective dynamics defined by an interaction kernel, without a priori assumptions on the analytical form of this kernel (i.e. it is nonparametric). The advantage of this kernel-based approach is that it incorporates the underlying physics of the model (i.e. collective dynamics), which more general equation-learning approaches may ignore, potentially limiting their effectiveness for model accuracy and predictions. In this work, we extend existing variational learning approaches to collective systems with both interaction kernels and environmental/intra-agent forces. The proposed framework simultaneously infers the interaction kernel non-parametrically while learning the environmental force using either semi-parametric or fully nonparametric representations. The methodology is validated on several benchmark models exhibiting synchronization, alignment, attraction-repulsion, and external environmental forces. We also introduce a model-selection procedure based on our nonparametric learning framework to identify models that optimally explain a given set of trajectory observations. By exploiting the feature-identification capability of the learned models, the proposed procedure can distinguish among different collective dynamics frameworks and recover mechanistic interaction mechanisms directly from trajectory data.

</details>


### [251] [Transforms for LLM Quantization: The Great Inversion and Format Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25188)
*Ehsan Jokar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本综述提出"大反转"（Great Inversion）原理，系统论述了LLM 4位量化中变换阶段（transform stage）的理论根基：分配灵活编码奖励能量集中，而部署的分组共享尺度量化奖励组内平坦化。二者通过组内优超化理论形式化为对立目标，并讨论了不同数值格式下变换选择的翻转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管变换-然后-舍入已成为4位LLM量化主流流水线的关键步骤，但该领域缺乏专门综述，且其文献正在重新推导更早的理论。论文填补这一空白，将分散的最优性结果统一在一个原则下，并追溯到1963年变换编码的经典谱系。

Method: 论文形式化"大反转"原理：分配灵活的编码代理（D_vr ∝ 几何均值，Schur-凹）与共享尺度AbsMax代理（D_fr = Σ_g max_{k∈g} v_k，组内Schur-凸）在组内优超化序上对立。通过次高斯旋转（如Haar或随机Hadamard）实现无需校准的平坦化（命题2.15），并在WUSH（Chen et al., 2026a）中达到闭式数据感知块变换对FP-AbsMax模型的精确最优和INT-AbsMax的d^{o(1)}近最优。综述涵盖200篇文献至2026年6月截止，分类43种变换方法，并讨论与GPTQ舍入、INT与FP4格式（包括MXFP4的E8M0二次幂尺度与NVFP4的E4M3带尾数尺度）的组合。

Result: 理论证明两代理目标在组内优超化方向上对立（定理4.3），且对一般谱不存在最优性传递（推论4.10）。关键经验发现：MXFP4上全局Hadamard旋转有害（Llama-3.1-8B平均准确率62.9% vs 无变换RTN 69.3%），而块内Hadamard仍有效；NVFP4上旋转大致中性。随机旋转可无数据地将向量峰值因子从√d降至O(√log d)。

Conclusion: 该综述建立了变换设计的基本轴：分配与尺度约束决定哪一极（集中或平坦）被优化，数值格式作为第二轴。揭示了四个开放问题：变换与舍入的联合最优性、部署目标的真正保证、分配灵活编码能否重返推理路径、以及变换-格式协同设计。WUSH是FP-AbsMax模型的最优闭合形式解，而KLT是分配灵活编码下的最优解，二者代表了大反转两极的对立选择。

Abstract: Most competitive 4-bit LLM research pipelines now open the same way: apply a linear, function-preserving transform (rotation, scaling, permutation, non-orthogonal affine) so the outlier mass sits more favorably against the group scales, and only then round. Yet we are aware of no survey dedicated to this transform stage, and its literature is quietly re-deriving an older theory. We identify and formalize the principle that organizes it, the Great Inversion: allocation-flexible coding rewards energy concentration, whereas the grouped shared-scale quantization a deployed matrix instruction performs rewards within-group flattening. Classical transform coding (1963: decorrelate, allocate bits, quantize) spends different bits per coordinate at a fixed total rate; for a Gaussian source at high rate the Karhunen-Loeve transform's concentration minimizes distortion. A deployed operand tile instead carries one absolute-maximum scale per group and equal bits everywhere, with no allocation; on a uniform grid that objective rewards flattening, approached by Hadamard incoherence. We prove that opposition under within-group majorization: the prescriptions point in opposite directions, each backed by a proof against its own objective, and for a generic spectrum no optimality guarantee transfers. A second axis is the number format: the non-uniform FP4 grid makes flattening buy less, MXFP4's power-of-two block scale still rewards a rotation confined to that block, and NVFP4's mantissa-carrying scale largely removes that pull, so the target pole depends jointly on allocation regime and format. We survey 200 works to a June 2026 cutoff; classify 43 transform methods by structure, data-awareness, searched-versus-constructed, and runtime cost; record, where reported, how they compose with GPTQ rounding; distill a first-choice guide by deployment regime; and close with the open problems it exposes.

</details>


### [252] [What Should a Large Language Model See? Physical Invariants as a Data Representation for PDE Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25189)
*Fan Yang,Matt Thomson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"数据解读"(data interpretation)阶段，将时空场数据转化为理论物理学家会关注的物理量（如谱分析、衰减率、线性/非线性判据、平流检测），作为直接输入提供给大语言模型（LLM），用于偏微分方程(PDE)的自动发现。在44个仿真场样本的基准测试中，该方法使方程恢复的F1准确率从原始数据的0.225提升至0.720，几乎提升三倍，且无需训练，计算开销可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的符号回归方法（如FunSearch、LLM-SR等）在PDE发现中，LLM仅通过评估器返回的标量分数了解数据，无法直接"看到"场数据。受人类理论家通过识别场结构特征（如激波→对流、图灵斑→扩散-反应系统）来构建理论的启发，作者提出应赋予LLM类似的数据解读能力。

Method: 在LLM提出方程之前加入"数据解读"阶段，基于傅里叶谱分析回答四个核心问题：(Q1) 时间导数阶数（一阶→振幅单调变化，二阶→振幅振荡）；(Q2) 线性系数（通过衰减率r(k)=−dln|û_k|/dt对波数k的回归得到扩散系数ν和增长系数σ，振荡模式额外估计频率ω(k)得到波速c）；(Q3) 线性vs非线性（测量高频谱能量迁移与回归R²）；(Q4) 是否存在平流（通过空间平移相关性增益g和最优位移s*检测）。这些诊断信息被渲染为约310个token的文本，与导数符号列表一同输入冻结的LLM（QwQ-32B），LLM在文本中推理并以规范格式输出PDE候选结构，最后由最小二乘+稀疏惩罚进行系数拟合与评分。

Result: 在44个仿真样本（含6个分层：线性一阶、反应项、自平流可观测/抑制、波方程/弱可辨识波）的基准测试中，数据解读使F1=0.720、精确恢复14/44样本；原始场切片（10条1D剖面，约1230 token）仅F1=0.225、精确恢复2/43；置换解读（控制实验）降至F1=0.224、精确恢复4/44，证明LLM确实在"阅读"解读结果；最佳固定项集基线为F1=0.391。统计显著性：解读vs基线 p=2.6×10⁻⁵（配对Wilcoxon）；解读vs置换 p<3×10⁻⁷。线性分层达到F1=1.000，误差集中在非线性分层。

Conclusion: 数据解读以极低成本（无训练、单次调用耗时极小）和极少token显著提升LLM驱动的PDE发现准确率，验证了"让LLM像理论家一样阅读数据"这一思路的有效性。该流水线将传统理论构建中最耗时的两个步骤——机制识别与候选验证——分别由数据解读和评估器自动完成，使新场可在数分钟内完成分析。当前局限：样本量小（44个）、数据集仅8项库、无噪声、解读工具仅覆盖有限PDE结构空间。未来工作包括将解读扩展至残差场以迭代修正、对噪声鲁棒性的验证、嵌入进化搜索框架、以及扩充更丰富的特征化工具。

Abstract: Understanding how molecular interactions govern macroscopic behaviour is a central challenge in molecular sciences. However, conventional theory building cannot keep pace with the vast datasets modern experimentation routinely produces. Large language models offer a promising route to automating theory construction, but a spatiotemporal field cannot be directly placed in a prompt. Existing models generally learn about the data only through a score measuring how well each proposal fits it. Here we introduce data interpretation, a stage that measures the field into the quantities a theorist would consult and supplies them to the model as a direct input. On a benchmark of simulated fields, interpretation nearly triples the accuracy of recovered equations relative to showing the raw data, at negligible computational cost and without any training. By allowing a language model to read field data as a theorist does, data interpretation offers a practical route to automated field theory construction that can coevolve with experimentation.

</details>


### [253] [LibriBrain100: One Hundred Hours of Broad and Deep MEG Data for Neural Speech Decoding at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25204)
*Francesco Mantegna,Dulhan Jayalath,Gereon Elvers,Tasha Kim,Benjamin Ballyk,Alex Fung,SungJun Cho,Teyun Kwon,Luisa Kurth,Miran Özdogan,Gilad Landau,Pratik Somaiya,Natalie Voets,Mark Woolrich,Oiwi Parker Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LibriBrain100 是一个超大规模、非侵入性脑磁图（MEG）数据集，包含超过 100 小时的神经语音数据，旨在通过“深度优先”（单受试者 80 小时）和“广度延伸”（32 名受试者各 40 分钟）的双重设计，标准化并加速非侵入式脑机接口（BCI）的语音解码研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前非侵入式脑机接口（尤其是语音解码）领域缺乏类似 ImageNet 那样的标准化大规模数据集和评测基准，导致难以准确衡量真实进展。现有 MEG 语音数据集存在规模小（多受试者但单受试者数据少）或单一受试者缺乏跨被试泛化能力的问题。论文旨在填补这一空白，推动非侵入式脑-文本解码技术的实用化，最终帮助严重瘫痪患者恢复交流能力。

Method: 1. 数据集构建：在 306 通道 MEG 设备上采集数据。深度部分（Subject 0）包含约 80 小时数据，听觉材料涵盖完整 Sherlock Holmes 有声读物、TIMIT/MOCHA-TIMIT（音素控制）以及 The Moth 播客（语义控制）。广度部分（Subject 1-32）包含 32 名受试者各约 40 分钟数据。2. 数据处理：采用标准化预处理（头动校正、Maxwell 滤波、0.1-125Hz 带通滤波、降采样至 250Hz）；使用 Kaldi-based Gentle 工具进行强制对齐，生成词级和音素级标注。3. 评测任务：词分类（50 词词汇表），评估指标为 top-10 balanced accuracy。4. 模型基线：采用 MEG-XL（自监督预训练 + 微调）和 d'Ascoli 等人的监督学习模型，在被试内（Many-to-One）和跨被试（One-to-Many）两种设定下进行基准测试。

Result: 1. Subject 0 上：MEG-XL 微调达到 SOTA 性能；联合 Subject 1-32 训练数据可进一步提升性能（除 Sherlock 子集外）。2. 跨被试泛化：利用 Subject 0 的 80 小时数据预训练后微调 32 名新受试者，性能提升约 15 个百分点。3. 数据效率：即使仅使用约 10 分钟（25%）的微调数据，跨被试性能仍保持稳定，表明预训练策略可大幅降低对新用户的数据需求。4. 模型对比：在深度数据上监督模型更优，在广度数据上自监督预训练模型泛化能力更强。5. 提供了标准数据划分、开源 Python 库（pnpl）、公共排行榜及配套 ML 竞赛。

Conclusion: LibriBrain100 通过兼具大规模深度（单被试 80 小时）和广度（多被试）的设计，显著推进了 MEG 语音解码研究。其标准化基准、开源工具和配套竞赛降低了机器学习研究者的入门门槛。实验证明了预训练+微调策略在数据高效跨被试泛化上的有效性。尽管数据集仅为被动听音范式（未涉及内部/想象语音）且未包含脑-文本基线，但它为未来非侵入式 BCI 的实用化奠定了重要的基础设施。

Abstract: We introduce LibriBrain100, a large-scale MEG dataset for speech decoding designed from the ground up for reproducible, standardised evaluation. LibriBrain100 more than doubles the size of the original LibriBrain release, resulting in over 100 hours of high-quality MEG acquired while subjects listened to naturalistic continuous speech. With $\sim$80 hours from a single subject, LibriBrain100 sets a new record for deep, within-subject neural data (8$\times$ more than the next comparable dataset and roughly 80$\times$ more than other datasets). To demonstrate the payoff of this depth-first design, we evaluate on a word-classification benchmark---an increasingly well-established stepping stone towards the open challenge of noninvasive brain-to-text decoding. Using an existing decoding model, we achieve state-of-the-art performance---validating both the quality of the recordings and the value of within-subject data at scale. Because collecting 80 hours of data per user is impractical for real-world applications, we also collected $\sim$40 minutes of additional data from each of 32 subjects. Using the same word-classification benchmark, we demonstrate the value of broad multi-subject data: supervised finetuning of a pre-trained model can substantially compensate for limited per-subject data. We provide standard train, validation, and test splits, all reproducible through an open-sourced Python library that supports easy downloading, optional preprocessing, and data loading for common deep learning frameworks. In addition, the dataset and evaluation infrastructure are being released alongside an open machine-learning competition with a public leaderboard for standardised benchmarking. Ultimately, our hope is that LibriBrain100 will accelerate progress towards practical non-invasive brain-computer interfaces, capable of restoring communication to people living with severe paralysis.

</details>


### [254] [Representing MAX functions using two-hidden-layer ReLU networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25221)
*Zhimao Wang,Amitabh Basu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用两层隐藏层ReLU网络精确表示MAX函数的问题。作者通过计算机辅助搜索，独立地获得了MAX_5至MAX_8的两层隐藏层ReLU表示，其方法与Ruess等人近期的工作在高层思路上相似，但在处理非线项时利用了更多功能对称性以减少线性系统规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究连续分段线性函数用ReLU网络表示所需的最少隐藏层数是一个长期未解决的问题。已知上界为⌈log₂(n+1)⌉，最佳一般下界仅为2。MAX_N函数是核心问题——解决MAX_N的表示问题即可回答一般情况。已有突破（Bakaev et al., 2026）给出了MAX_5的两层表示，但对N≥6的情况仍未解决。

Method: 作者采用了原子（atoms）方法：将MAX_N表示为形如max{Σmax(x_{a_i},x_{b_i}), Σmax(x_{c_i},x_{d_i})}的项的线性组合。通过：(1)利用对称群Σ_N作用在原子模式上对轨道求和以保证置换不变性；(2)利用额外功能对称性（配对交换、两侧内部置换、左右交换）来缩减系统；(3)区分"明确"与"模糊"原子项，建立显式线性系统求解。与Ruess等人不同，本文不将max项拆分为线性项加ReLU项，而是直接处理max项。

Result: 成功获得了：
- MAX_5：两层k=2表示（线性系统：20约束、131变量），给出两组显式恒等式（7项和5项）
- MAX_6：两层k=2表示（41约束、144变量），给出两组恒等式（7项和9项）
- MAX_7：两层k=3表示（1057约束、4469变量），给出109项恒等式
- MAX_8：两层k=4表示（21953约束、193623变量），给出1290项恒等式
计算资源不足以处理MAX_9和MAX_10（其线性系统规模过大）。MAX_8的表示比Ruess等人（使用k=3）的表示更不紧凑。

Conclusion: 本文证明了使用两层隐藏层ReLU网络精确表示MAX_N对N=5,6,7,8是可能的，且这些表示可通过有限精确线性代数进行验证。两种方法（本文与Ruess等人）虽高层思路相似，但对非线性项的处理方式不同，导致产生的线性系统不同。关键观察是：通过排序腔的对称性约减加上额外的功能对称性，可以将全局分段线性恒等式的验证转化为有限线性系统的求解。找到一个解是充分条件（不是必要条件），因此解的不存在并不排除两层表示的存在性。

Abstract: We study exact representations of $\mathrm{MAX}_N(x)=\max{x_1,\ldots,x_N}$ using two-hidden-layer ReLU neural networks. This problem has been studied in recent years in an attempt to characterize the exact number of hidden layers required to represent continuous piecewise linear functions. The best lower bound is 2, while the current upper bound is logarithmic in $N$. It remains completely open if the right answer is a constant number of hidden layers (possibly even 2!) or not. In fact, a recent breakthrough was the representation of $\mathrm{MAX}_5$ as a two-hidden-layer ReLU function obtained in [Bakaev et al., 2026], and the case of $\mathrm{MAX}_N$ was stated as open for $N\geq 6$ in that paper. Using a careful computer assisted search, we obtain two-hidden-layer ReLU representations of $\mathrm{MAX}_5, \mathrm{MAX}_6, \mathrm{MAX}_7$, and $\mathrm{MAX}_8$. We obtain these by considering rational linear combinations of terms of the form $\max\{\sum_{r=1}^{s}\max(x_{a_r},x_{b_r}),\sum_{r=1}^{s}\max(x_{c_r},x_{d_r})\}$, where $a_r,b_r,c_r,d_r\in\{1,\ldots,N\}$. Each inner maximum of two coordinates can be computed in a first hidden layer, and the outer maximum of the two side-sums can be computed in a second hidden layer. Consequently, every finite linear combination of these terms has a two-hidden-layer ReLU realization. An identity for $\mathrm{MAX}_N$ in this form therefore gives an exact two-hidden-layer ReLU representation of $\mathrm{MAX}_N$. Very recently, two-hidden-layer representations of $\mathrm{MAX}_N$ of the above form were obtained for all $N\leq 10$ in [Ruess et al., 2026]. Our representations are different and were developed independently. While our techniques share most of the high-level ideas presented in [Ruess et al., 2026], there are also some minor differences which may be of interest for future research on this problem.

</details>


### [255] [Trust the Mass: Forced Weights in KV-Cache Eviction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25230)
*Jack Shi,Jerry Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过精确枚举分析，证明在KV缓存淘汰中"保留最大权重"的简单策略已接近最优，最优子集仅能再缩小2-5%的差距。研究揭示当前评估管道的内存计量存在系统性偏差：最强查询无关方法因以掩码存储选择而实际保留完整缓存。论文提出训练免费的ContourKV分配器，基于"丢弃质量"统计量动态分配预算，在160对对比中以93胜22负击败KVzip。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前KV缓存淘汰领域缺乏对"选择能带来多大提升"的理论上限测量，且不同方法间的性能差距来源（内存、查询信息泄露还是计算）未被分解。论文旨在量化强制权重下最优子集的收益上限，并揭示当前基准评估中内存计量与预算执行的方法学问题。

Method: 方法分为三部分：(1) 理论分析——证明在强制权重下选择最优子集是NP完全问题，并推导出基于"丢弃质量"的上界；(2) 精确枚举——在168,192个注意力行（5个模型）上穷举最优子集，量化选择能关闭的差距比例；(3) 部署ContourKV——基于丢弃质量统计的跨头预算分配器，使用KVzip或SnapKV的重要性分数，训练-free，在NVIDIA kvpress管道上与14种基线对比RULER和LongBench。

Result: 关键结果包括：(1) 最佳子集仅比top-mass（保留最大权重）多关闭2-5%的差距（中位数），平衡选择器可恢复全部可关闭差距；(2) 共享评估管道中KVzip等per-head方法因掩码存储实际保留完整缓存，是SnapKV的28-1078倍字节数；(3) 在固定选择上强制预算导致14.1-62.2基准分下降；(4) KVzip的87.6分检索优势归因于问题可见时的排序；(5) ContourKV在160对对比中以93胜22负击败KVzip（预算强制下），与Compactor持平。

Conclusion: 论文得出三个核心结论：(1) 在强制权重下，选择带来的收益上限极低（2-5%），使得方法间的发表差距必然来源于内存或查询泄露；(2) 公平比较需强制预算并测量实际字节数，当前管道中per-head方法因掩码存储而隐藏真实成本；(3) 基于丢弃质量统计的ContourKV分配器在匹配内存下能匹配或超越最强基线，为KV缓存淘汰提供了更公平且高效的评估框架和实用方案。

Abstract: Every deployed sparse-attention or KV-cache-eviction rule keeps a subset of the keys, discards the rest, and renormalizes the attention weights over the kept set. Enumerating the exact best subset under that constraint on $168{,}192$ attention rows from five models shows that keeping the largest weights is already near-optimal, since the best subset closes only a median $2$ to $5\%$ of the remaining gap to full attention. If selection closes this little, published margins between eviction methods must come from elsewhere, so we measure the bytes each method holds. In the shared evaluation pipeline, the strongest query-agnostic methods hold the full cache because their per-head selections are stored as masks, and only ragged per-head storage frees that memory. Enforcing a nominal budget on one fixed selection costs $14$ to $62$ benchmark points. We trace an $87.6$-point retrieval margin to rankings computed while the question is visible. ContourKV, a training-free allocator built from the dropped-mass statistic, wins $93$ of $160$ paired comparisons against that state of the art and loses $22$ at the byte count of the budget-enforcing baselines, and it ties the strongest of them.

</details>


### [256] [Long-Term Behavioral Evaluation for Trusted Collaborator Selection via Bidirectional Mamba](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25232)
*Botao Zhu,Xianbin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于双向Mamba（BM）的长期行为评估模型，用于在协作系统中选择可信协作者，通过结合GNN短期特征融合与双向Mamba长期时序建模来评估设备可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式协作系统（如车联网、工业IoT等）中，准确评估设备长期行为以选择可信协作者至关重要。现有方法存在两个挑战：(1) 仅基于有限历史协作的评估只能捕捉瞬时过去行为，无法反映真实行为；(2) 单向评估仅依赖早期协作，丢失了从后续协作中学习的机会。此外，LSTM的顺序计算限制了其长序列效率，Transformer的二次复杂度也使其难以实用化。

Method: 将连续时间轴划分为多个等长短时隙，在每个时隙内基于历史协作构建设备协作图，并通过GNN聚合邻居消息融合短期行为特征；随后利用双向Mamba（前向与后向扫描）对所有时隙的短期表征进行长期时序依赖建模，最后通过MLP输出长期历史可靠性评估，并以交叉熵损失进行训练。

Result: 基于NS-3仿真平台（500台设备、10000个任务）的实验表明，BM模型在RMSE和MAE指标上均优于LSTM、GNN和QS-Trust等基线方法；在500个时隙的长期评估中，BM表现出最小的波动和最稳定的性能；在不同信任阈值下，BM持续实现最高的任务完成价值（VoC）。

Conclusion: 所提出的BM模型通过结合GNN短期协作关系融合与双向Mamba长期时序依赖捕获，实现了更准确、更稳定的设备长期行为评估，从而支持在动态复杂环境中选择最大化任务完成价值的可信协作者，为构建更鲁棒、智能和可信的协作系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: Effective selection of trustworthy collaborators is crucial to ensuring the successful completion of collaborative tasks, which requires accurate assessments of both long-term device behavior and short-term collaborative dynamics. Consistent device behavior patterns, which are learned from historical collaborations, can be used to predict their reliability in future collaborations. However, accurately assessing device behavior based on historical collaborations remains challenging. First, behavior assessment from limited historical collaborations captures only instantaneous past behavior, failing to represent the devices' true behavior. Second, due to the temporal dependencies of device behavior, a unidirectional evaluation that relies only on earlier collaborations loses the opportunity to learn from subsequent collaborations. Addressing these challenges requires evaluating device behavior based on long-term collaborations while considering both forward and backward temporal dependencies. To this end, this work proposes a bidirectional Mamba-enabled model (BM) for long-term behavioral evaluation. For each short time slot, a graph is constructed among devices based on historical collaborations, and device behavioral features within the slot are then aggregated accordingly. Subsequently, a bidirectional Mamba model integrates these short-term representations across all time intervals, producing a stable and reliable long-term behavior evaluation for each device. Experimental results demonstrate that BM achieves higher evaluation accuracy than baseline methods, thereby enabling the selection of collaborators that maximize the value of task completion.

</details>


### [257] [ShuttleArena: Interpretable Self-Play in Physics-Based Badminton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25246)
*Peize Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ShuttleArena，一个基于物理的单打羽毛球自博弈环境，通过结构化策略（角色条件化输出：接球方使用20路掩码拦截头，击球方使用因子化的方位角、仰角、速度和恢复目标）耦合连续飞行的羽毛球、拦截、结构化击球生成和击球后恢复。训练采用PPO自博弈、稀疏的回合胜负奖励以及因子特定的恢复更新（含CRA），并提出了一套结合竞争性评估、控制探针、人数据合理性检查和消融实验的综合评估套件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 羽毛球AI的核心挑战在于击球选择与击球后恢复不可分割：最佳恢复取决于击球诱导的对手反应，而击球价值取决于击球者能否覆盖对手回球。然而，现有体育类游戏AI研究缺乏一个紧凑、可解释且物理可信的测试平台来同时研究这三个耦合维度。

Method: 1. 环境设计：基于阻力平方动力学的3D羽毛球飞行模型，2D选手运动模型，含拦截可行性和快速反应失误机制。2. 结构化策略：接球方采用20路掩码拦截，击球方将11,000路联合动作分解为方位角(11)、仰角(8)、速度(5)、恢复目标(25)四个因子，共49个组件logits。3. 训练方法：PPO自博弈，分阶段使用纯最近期采样和线性最近期采样的检查点对手池；恢复头采用CRA（反事实恢复优势）进行因子特定的信用分配。4. 评估套件：冻结检查点的轮转胜率矩阵、Bradley-Terry/Elo评级、固定战术状态的受控探针、恢复演化探针、人数据合理性检查以及恢复消融。

Result: 1. 竞争性提升但非单调：后期检查点通常优于早期，但存在非传递性匹配依赖结构。线性最近期训练延长了自博弈饱和点（约3.0M到5-6M步）。2. 战术可解释性：受控探针显示击球几何和恢复选择随对手位置/速度条件化变化。3. 恢复重要性：恢复消融显示学习恢复行为带来约250 Elo的差距（1683 vs 1429）。4. CRA有效性：消融实验显示CRA分支在3.2M步时比无CRA版本高约154 Elo。5. 人数据对比：学习策略产生类似羽毛球的落点结构，但回合较短、恢复偏前场、边线集中度更高。6. 环境稳健性：在阻力±20%、速度±15%、反应时间+50ms等扰动下主要定性结论保持成立。

Conclusion: ShuttleArena建立了一个紧凑且可解释的羽毛球自博弈测试平台，其中击球执行、恢复选择和对手相对战术价值可以同时被学习、检查和评估。该研究证明基于物理的球拍类运动是游戏AI的有用测试平台，需要代理同时协调执行、定位和对手相对战术价值。击球-恢复-对手响应的耦合可以学习且可解释，结构化策略和因子特定信用分配（CRA）有助于恢复策略的学习。

Abstract: Badminton is a compact but challenging domain for game AI: a player must choose a physically feasible shuttle trajectory, anticipate the opponent's interception, and recover to a court position whose value depends on the opponent's next response. The central challenge is that shot selection and recovery are not separable: the best recovery depends on the shot-induced opponent response, while the value of the shot depends on whether the hitter can cover the reply. This paper presents ShuttleArena, a physics-based singles badminton self-play environment that couples continuous shuttle flight, player interception, structured shot generation, and post-shot recovery. The policy uses role-conditioned outputs: a masked interception choice on receiver turns and a factorized hitter action over shot azimuth, shot elevation, shot speed, and recovery target, enabling interpretable tactical probes. Episodes are single rallies rather than full scored games, and training uses Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) self-play against a staged checkpoint opponent pool with sparse terminal rally-outcome rewards and a factor-specific recovery update. Evaluation with frozen checkpoint play, controlled tactical probes, recovery ablations, qualitative rollouts, and a human-data sanity check shows competitive improvement together with interpretable opponent-conditioned changes in shot geometry and recovery behavior. The learned policies produce recognizable badminton-like structure while also reflecting the abstractions of the simulator, and the recovery intervention shows that learned recovery behavior is competitively important. These results suggest that physics-based racket sports are a useful testbed for interactive digital entertainment AI because they require agents to coordinate execution, positioning, and opponent-relative tactical value.

</details>


### [258] [Mitigating LLM sycophancy with RL-based fine-tuning: Bayesian Truth Serum approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25267)
*Serhii Mytsyk,Yiming Zhang,Vikram Krishnamurthy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出将贝叶斯真理血清（BTS）作为GRPO强化学习中的奖励信号，以无标签方式缓解大语言模型的谄媚行为；将模型对同一问题的多次采样视为机制中的"受访者"，奖励那些"出人意料地常见"的答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在RLHF训练中常表现出谄媚性——迎合用户观点而非给出真实答案，降低事实准确性并放大错误信息。现有缓解方法多依赖标注数据或偏好标注，且RLHF中人类偏好本身就倾向于"令人愉悦"的回答而非正确答案。该研究旨在探索一种无需标签、无需偏好标注的机制来缓解谄媚。

Method: 1) 将模型对同一提示的G=64次采样视为BTS机制中的"受访者群体"；2) 每个响应需同时给出答案和对群体答案分布的预测；3) BTS奖励=信息分（log(实际频率/预测频率)）+ 预测分（-KL散度），权重α=1时为零和博弈；4) 用GRPO以该奖励微调模型，组内G个采样正好对应一次优化步；5) 引入角色与目标上下文使群体共享同一先验。

Result: 理论证明：(1) α=1时预测报告的最优解恰为后验预测，诚实回答无法通过扭曲预测获益；(2) 大群体极限下谄媚回答的期望奖励严格低于诚实回答；(3) 群体协调任何对称回答规则的信息分不高于诚实报告。实验上：TF数据集上谄媚翻转率从23%降至4%，有压力时准确率从80%升至93%；5个基座模型均改善；与合成数据微调、精确微调效果相当，优于SMART；Peer Truth Serum（无需预测报告）重现该效应，而Robust BTS无效，论文据此推测"对稀有答案的溢价"是驱动力。计算成本约为有监督基线的80倍。

Conclusion: BTS-GRPO能在不使用任何标签的情况下显著缓解LLM谄媚行为，效果与最优有监督方法相当。机制分析表明关键在于奖励对"意外常见的答案"的溢价，而非真相作为均衡点的理论性质。该方法适用于标注数据稀缺但算力充足的场景。未来工作包括更大模型、自然数据集、开放式生成、预测校准的直接度量，以及无需标签即可检测退化的选择规则。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) frequently exhibit \emph{sycophancy}: they adapt their answers to a user's stated beliefs or preferences instead of reporting what they hold to be true, which lowers factual accuracy and can amplify misinformation. This paper proposes a methodology for mitigating sycophancy that employs the Bayesian Truth Serum (BTS), a peer-prediction mechanism, as the reward in Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to fine-tune an LLM. BTS pays an answer for being \emph{surprisingly common}, that is, more frequent among respondents than those respondents themselves predicted. We treat a group of responses from a model for one question as those respondents, so the reward is a function of the model's own outputs and fine-tuning needs neither labels nor preference annotations. We prove that in the large-group limit a sycophantic response earns strictly lower expected reward than an honest one. We also prove that if the entire group agrees in advance on a symmetric answering rule, it cannot earn a higher information score than under truthful reporting. On our true/false benchmark the reference model's answer-flip rate under user pressure decreases from 23% to 4%, and its accuracy under that pressure increases from 80% to 93%. Our reward outperforms SMART and is comparable to synthetic-data fine-tuning and to pinpoint tuning, all three of which train on labels. It spends considerably more compute in exchange, which makes it suitable when labeled data is scarce. Peer Truth Serum, which also pays a premium for a rare answer but elicits no prediction report, reproduces the effect. A peer-prediction reward computed inside a single GRPO group therefore reduces sycophancy without labels, and comparing mechanisms suggests that the premium paid for a rarer answer drives the effect.

</details>


### [259] [Prefix-Denoising Consistency: Test-Time Verification for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25311)
*Yuki Ichihara,Naoto Iwase,Mohammad Atif Quamar,Junpei Komiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种针对扩散语言模型（DLM）的测试时自验证方法PDC，通过前缀条件再生成并多数投票提升推理准确性。
