<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DLM在低温度解码时多次采样缺乏多样性，导致直接迁移自一致性方法效果有限；现有TiF方法无法生成新的推理轨迹。需要一种利用DLM特性进行测试时验证的方法。

Method: PDC（Prefix-Denoising Consistency）：保留初始生成输出的前缀（保留率ρ∈{0.1, 0.5, 0.9}），重新掩码并生成后缀，获取候选答案后通过多数投票确定最终答案。核心观察：初始正确的答案在再生成中更可能被保留（R⁺），而初始错误的答案更倾向于被修正（R⁺ ≥ R⁻）。

Result: 在数学推理（GSM8K、MATH-500、SVAMP）和常识推理（CSQA、SQA）基准上，PDC在15个模型-数据集设置中的14个优于初始采样，最高提升+5.68百分点；在计算预算受限对比中，13/15优于自一致性；在不同解掩码策略、半AR设置和温度设置下均保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: 前缀条件再生是DLM特有的有效测试时验证原语，PDC通过利用正确轨迹比错误轨迹更稳定的信号，以黑盒方式显著提升扩散语言模型的推理可靠性，无需修改模型或训练。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) have recently become increasingly competitive with autoregressive (AR) models, and even outperform them on certain tasks. Unlike AR models, DLMs produce output through iterative denoising without a left-to-right order. To further improve the performance of DLMs, we introduce PDC (\emph{Prefix-Denoising Consistency}), a test-time self-verification method for DLMs. PDC exploits a distinctive test-time signal in DLMs under prefix conditioned regeneration, correct trajectories are more stable and reproducible than incorrect ones. Concretely, given an initially generated sample, PDC splits the sentence at an intermediate position and regenerates the remaining tokens conditioned on the fixed prefix. Across mathematical reasoning and commonsense reasoning benchmarks, PDC consistently improves upon the initial sample, outperforms independent generations under a computational constrained comparison, and is robust to different unmasking strategies and parameter settings. These results highlight prefix-conditioned regeneration as an effective DLM-specific primitive for test-time verification.

</details>


### [260] [Activation-Space Order-Swap Geometry: A Site-Asymmetry Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25315)
*Anqi Peter Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需拟合的"站点不对称性审计"方法，用于检验Transformer激活空间中顺序交换统计量（order-swap bracket）是否真正反映非线性交互，而非由不同注入位置的Jacobian差异所产生的一阶伪迹。该方法通过四次单点干预的前向传播构建零参数预测，在六个开源LLM家族上验证了一阶项占bracket范数的84.3–97.7%，并表明传统上将bracket读作"非阿贝尔几何证据"的解释存在系统性混淆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Transformer的残差流中，向不同层注入不同方向的steering向量时，先后顺序不同会产生不同的输出结果，即"order-swap bracket"。这一顺序依赖性在表面上具有反对称性、消失于重合向量等代数性质，因而常被解读为存在非阿贝尔结构或方向间交互的证据。然而，这种解读可能被混淆因素所污染：当两个干预发生在不同网络深度（即"站点"）时，即使两个方向之间不存在真正的耦合，仅由站点间Jacobian差异也可产生一个与bracket代数特征完全一致的一阶项。需要一个零参数的判据来区分这种由站点结构本身强制产生的"强制效应"与模型真正学到的"交互效应"。

Method: 核心思想是将Taylor展开施加于order-swap bracket：Br_ij = L（线性一阶项，站点Jacobian差异导致，无交互）+ S（二阶自曲率项，平方在每个方向上，无交互）+ M（混合二阶导数项，真正的交互）+ O(α³)。由此定义零参数基线 L̂_ij = [s₁(v_i)+s₂(v_j)] − [s₁(v_j)+s₂(v_i)]，仅需四次单点注入的前向传播，无需拟合，且未见过bracket本身。Br − L̂在二阶上恰好抵消L和S，剩下反对称化的混合二阶导数，即"二阶差分" D_ij − D_ji。验证手段包括：(1) 对称组合控制（应无法预测反对称对象）；(2) 错位配对控制（用不同对的响应去预测应失效）；(3) ±v注入分裂，分离出一阶项L与自曲率项S；(4) 随机初始化网络重现一阶主导（架构基线）；(5) 已知Hessian代理上的已知正控制；(6) 注入尺度扫描，验证bracket范数按α¹而非α²缩放；(7) 提示词半切分，移除共享提示混杂；(8) 三种零假设（线性松弛、特征变Jacobian、广义反对称交互）以校准阈值。

Result: 在六个开源LLM家族（DeepSeek-7B、Gemma-2-9B、Llama-3.1-8B、Mistral-7B-v0.3、OLMo-7B、Qwen2.5-7B）上，零参数预测 L̂ 与bracket的余弦相似度在所有十八个族×配置单元上不低于0.9869，主配置下范围为0.9955–0.9986，对应bracket范数的5.2–7.6%未被解释（即线性一阶项占84.3–97.7%，均值93.7%）。注入尺度扫描得拟合指数 γ∈[0.886,1.043]，排除纯双线性项的γ=2缩放。在±v分裂设计中，零交互二阶自曲率项S在主配置下是修正残差的1.8–5.2倍。在无混杂提示词切分下，修正残差在6族中的3族（Llama 1.599、OLMo 2.059、Qwen 1.642）超过广义交互零线1.325，其余3族（DeepSeek 1.010、Gemma 1.017、Mistral 1.016）落入线性松弛水平。随机初始化网络重现一阶主导（cos∈[0.9956,0.9988]）。在ViT-B/16和ResNet-50等非语言参考模型上，训练后的残差比例在11/12对比中低于固定高斯方向零线，但这是可移植性检查而非汇总证据。

Conclusion: order-swap bracket中绝大部分（84.3–97.7%）是由站点Jacobian差异强制产生的一阶伪迹，不携带方向间的真正耦合；将其读作"非阿贝尔几何"或"交互"的证据存在系统性混淆。该审计提出一个可复用的测量准则：在将bracket做几何解读之前先运行单点干预基线，若基线已解释bracket，则应改用二阶差分 D 作为交互度量。所有结论限定于不同站点的激活空间干预，不主张表示几何全局非阿贝尔。审计识别出Llama、OLMo和Qwen中通过所有控制的候选交互效应，但未在其他三个家族中发现稳定证据。

Abstract: Order-dependent activation statistics are often interpreted as evidence of interaction, but that interpretation can be confounded by where interventions enter the network. We introduce a no-fit site-asymmetry audit. For a twice-differentiable readout, the open-path order-swap decomposes into a canonical additive response measured by single interventions and an antisymmetrized second difference free of first-order and pure self-curvature terms to second order. Across six open-weight language-model families, the single-intervention baseline explains 84.3-97.7 percent of the bracket norm (mean 93.7 percent), while the no-interaction self-curvature term is 1.8-5.2 times larger than the corrected residual in the two families with the plus/minus injection split. The corrected residual clears a generic-interaction null in three of six families under a confound-free prompt split and two of six after configuration robustness. A known-positive surrogate recovers planted mixed interaction, while a matched site-separation test changes the baseline share and a random architecture reproduces the first-order regime. The same estimator transfers to released non-language references: trained residual fractions fall below a fixed Gaussian-direction null in 11/12 contrasts (5/6 ViT-B/16, 6/6 ResNet-50), a portability check rather than pooled evidence. The contribution is a reusable measurement criterion: run the single-intervention baseline before reading an order-swap vector as interaction or geometric structure; if it explains the vector, form the second difference instead. All claims are scoped to activation-space interventions at distinct sites; we do not claim that representation geometry is globally Abelian.

</details>


### [261] [Two Dimensions Govern Agnostic Multiclass Transductive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25326)
*Pahan Dewasurendra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了多类转导学习（multiclass transductive learning）在不可知（agnostic）设定下的最小最大速率问题，证明其与多类PAC学习拥有相同的两维统计律：由DS维控制的近似项和由Natarajan维控制的估计项共同决定，对数因子内匹配。核心技巧是一种"随机预留"原则，将可见标签分为三块后忽略其余，使隐藏测试点均匀落入未使用的大块，从而将结构化压缩方法转化为针对固定有限总体的转导保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在二元分类情形下，转导学习和PAC学习的不可知最小最大速率一致；然而对任意（甚至无界）标签空间的多类分类是否同样成立一直悬而未决。统一收敛论证可能失效，使得现有PAC界无法直接迁移到转导设置。作者意在闭合这一关于多类转导与PAC速率关系的公开问题。

Method: 方法分下界和上界两部分。下界：构造Natarajan立方（每个碎片坐标独立投掷公平标签）给出 √(d_N/n) 障碍，构造DS伪立方并分析其方向纤维的定向给出 d_DS/n 障碍。上界：提出"随机预留"原则，将n个样本的隐藏索引延迟决定，等价于先从全体中选三块A、B、C（各占n/4），再令隐藏索引在余集V上均匀。学习者仅使用A、B、C三块：①在A上用可实现压缩方案构造有限假说族F_A；②在B上运行无放回抽样的多权重算法生成规模为q的菜单μ；③在C上运行菜单内不可知压缩方案得到最终预测ĥ。三条核心引理——有限总体压缩覆盖、无放回MW菜单引理、随机分裂不可知压缩——共同给出上界。

Result: 主要定理：对任意类ℋ⊆Y^X和n≥2，有c·min{1, d_DS/n + √(d_N/n)} ≤ ε^tr_H(n) ≤ C·min{1, d_DS·log²(en)/n + √(d_N·log³(en)/n)}，对数因子内紧。多类不可知转导样本复杂度m^tr_H(ε) = Θ̃(d_DS/ε + d_N/ε²)，与PAC学习完全相同。DS伪立方的纤维计数给出d_DS/n不可消除，Natarajan立方给出√(d_N/n)不可消除。

Conclusion: 多类不可知转导学习与PAC学习遵循相同的两维统计律：DS维控制d/n近似项，Natarajan维控制√(d/n)估计项。随机预留技巧通过将隐藏点定位到大量未使用样本中，绕过了直接留一法迁移的不稳定性。无放回MW引理是保留快速d_DS/n项的关键新技术。本结果在任意标签空间（包括无界/不可数）下对数因子内闭合了转导vs.PAC的开放问题。局限：对数差距源于弱学习到压缩的提升、压缩族枚举和菜单内压缩；证明为信息论上界，不保证计算高效；不提供对任意有界损失的黑盒约简。

Abstract: In transductive classification, an adversary fixes a labeled population, one label is hidden uniformly, and the learner sees all remaining labels. For binary classes, agnostic transductive and PAC learning have the same minimax rate. Whether this extends to multiclass learning was open, especially for unbounded label spaces where uniform convergence can fail. We resolve the question up to logarithmic factors. For every multiclass class $\mathcal H$ with DS dimension $d_{DS}$ and Natarajan dimension $d_{\mathrm N}$, the optimal agnostic transductive excess error satisfies $\widetilde\Theta\left(\frac{d_{DS}}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{d_{\mathrm N}}{n}}\right).$ The result holds for arbitrary label spaces. The two terms are both necessary. A DS pseudo-cube gives the realizable $d_{DS}/n$ obstruction, while a Natarajan cube with repeated points and fair labels gives the agnostic $\sqrt{d_{\mathrm N}/n}$ obstruction. The upper bound uses a random-reservation principle. The learner deliberately ignores a constant fraction of the visible labels, which makes the true test point uniform in a large unseen block. We combine realizable compression, a label-space reduction, and inside-menu agnostic compression across this finite-population split. A new without-replacement multiplicative-weights lemma preserves the fast $d_{DS}/n$ term. Consequently, agnostic multiclass PAC and transductive learning obey the same two-dimension law up to logarithmic factors.

</details>


### [262] [Beyond Pairwise Feedback: Listwise Vision-Language Supervision for Preference-Based Reward Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25350)
*Srivalli Katkuri,Maxwell Kawada,Juan Wachs*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个将视觉语言模型(VLM)生成的列表式排名与Plackett-Luce(PL)模型相结合的偏好强化学习框架,在Meta-World机器人操作任务中验证了PL奖励模型能够有效替代传统的Bradley-Terry(BT)配对偏好方法,并具有更大的灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统偏好强化学习(PbRL)依赖Bradley-Terry模型进行配对比较(K=2),但VLM具备同时对多个候选进行排序的能力,且查询成本不受人类疲劳限制。配对方式浪费了VLM的列表式排序潜力,理论上Plackett-Luce模型能保留列表中的联合结构信息,具有更高的统计效率。

Method: 采用SAC算法作为策略学习方法,通过VLM(GPT-5.6 Luna)对从回放缓冲区均匀采样的K个观测进行完整排名(K∈{3,4,5}),使用Plackett-Luce似然函数训练奖励模型。对照方法包括BT-Kwise(将列表排名分解为配对训练)、BT-Pairwise和RL-VLM-F,均使用均匀采样,反馈组数M=4。还在Drawer Open任务上做了M=1的消融实验。

Result: 在三个Meta-World任务(Drawer Open、Door Close、Button Press)上,每个环境中至少有一种PL配置(K∈{3,4,5})在平均成功率上达到或超过基线方法。最佳PL配置在Drawer Open上达到86%成功率,与Oracle基线持平;在Door Close上PL(K=5)以54%优于RL-VLM-F和BT-Pairwise的39%。Button Press上PL(K=5)以26%显著优于BT-Kwise(K=5)的7%(p=0.045)。消融实验显示M=1时PL和BT-Kwise性能下降但仍有竞争力。

Conclusion: 列表式VLM偏好监督是奖励学习的一种有竞争力且灵活的方法。PL框架允许通过调整排名大小K适应不同环境和反馈格式需求,保留完整列表似然在某些情况下优于排名分解。存在将其与RL-VLM-F的多阶段提示策略结合的潜在研究方向,同时需要拓展到真实机器人系统进行验证。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have emerged as a powerful source of supervision for reinforcement learning, enabling agents to leverage rich semantic knowledge during training. Inspired by the success of preference-based reward learning (PbRL) in reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF), vision-language model generated image-based preferences provide an effective source for learning reward functions. This can be done by visually comparing two outcomes through the Bradley-Terry (BT) model. However, this pairwise formulation utilizes only two observations at a time, despite VLMs being capable of ranking multiple candidates. The Plackett-Luce (PL) formulation can shape a reward model with listwise rankings as opposed to pairwise preferences, allowing for a more suited use of a VLM based ranking. In this work, to our knowledge, we introduce the first framework that combines VLM-generated preferences with the Plackett-Luce model for reward learning. We evaluate our approach on Meta-World manipulation tasks and show that Plackett-Luce (PL) reward models can train robotic policies from VLM-generated rankings as effectively as pairwise Bradley-Terry, $K$-wise Bradley-Terry, and RL-VLM-F baselines. Across all environments, at least one PL ranking size ($K \in \{3,4,5\}$) consistently performs with or outperforms other methods in mean success rate. Unlike pairwise methods, which are restricted to $K=2$, PL supports different ranking sizes and can therefore be adapted to the environment and desired feedback format. Our best PL configuration achieves an 86% mean final success rate and matches the Oracle baseline on Drawer Open. Overall, these results demonstrate that listwise VLM preference supervision is a competitive and flexible approach to reward learning for reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [263] [PaSta: Noisy Node Classification with Partial Label Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25365)
*Yujing Liu,Yixin Liu,Yu Zheng,Yue Tan,Alan Wee-Chung Liew,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PaSta框架，首次将部分标签学习（Partial Label Learning, PLL）范式引入噪声节点分类（NNC）问题，通过自监督集成标注生成一致性部分标签，并设计双空间损失函数进行分类器训练，采用迭代自训练策略相互增强标注质量与分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实图数据中的节点标签常因弱监督或自动标注而含噪声（错误标签）。现有方法依赖one-hot标签训练分类器，存在两大固有局限：(1) 对标签噪声敏感——交叉熵损失迫使模型对单一类别高置信预测，易拟合错误标签；(2) 难以纠正标签错误——伪标签或修正标签在one-hot约束下可能强化错误监督并累积误差。PLL范式通过允许候选标签集合建模不确定性，但如何为图数据构建可靠的部分标签并有效利用，是两个关键挑战。

Method: PaSta框架包含两大模块：(1) 自监督集成标注模块——采用多种自监督图学习方法（如DGI、GCA、SUGRL）预训练多个标注器的特征提取器，使其不受噪声标签影响且具有表征多样性；随后训练轻量预测器，将各标注器的预测通过投票聚合为非二值化的"一致性部分标签"，保留丰富的类分布信息。(2) 双空间部分标签学习模块——使用两层GCN作为分类器，设计两个互补损失：VaCE损失（标签空间）利用投票计数的加权交叉熵进行细粒度监督；PaSim损失（表示空间）通过一致性部分标签相似度矩阵与嵌入相似度矩阵的L1一致性约束，利用候选标签间的语义关系。(3) 迭代自训练策略：采用小损失策略过滤噪声标签，对高置信无标签节点进行伪标签扩展，循环优化标注模块与分类模块。

Result: 在Cora、Citeseer、DBLP、Computers和Photo五个真实数据集上，PaSta在uniform noise和pair noise两种噪声设置、不同噪声比例（20%/40%/60%）下均取得最优性能，平均超越最强基线BO-NNC 1.1%，超越最弱基线GCN 12.7%。消融实验验证了PaSim损失、VaCE损失、自训练策略及多标注器集成四个组件的有效性。参数敏感性分析表明，τ=3左右性能趋于稳定，且对λ不敏感。

Conclusion: PaSta首次将部分标签学习范式成功应用于噪声节点分类问题，开辟了显式建模标签不确定性的新范式。通过自监督集成标注与双空间部分标签学习的协同优化，有效克服了one-hot标签的两个固有局限，为噪声图数据的鲁棒学习提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Noisy node classification problem is a fundamental yet challenging task for real-world graph-related web services, where node labels are often corrupted or unreliable due to weak supervision or automatic annotation. However, existing methods typically train models based on one-hot labels, which not only makes models susceptible to overfitting on noisy labels, but also leads to error accumulation after pseudo-label-guided enhancement. In this paper, we propose a novel Partial label-based Self-training framework (PaSta for short) that leverages partial label learning technique to overcome the limitations of existing methods. Specifically, PaSta first trains multiple annotators to comprehensively capture the class distribution of nodes and aggregates their predictions to construct high-quality partial labels. Subsequently, we design a partial label-based classification model with two well-crafted loss functions to guide the model learning at both label and representation spaces. To further enhance the robustness against noisy labels, we introduce a self-training strategy where the labels refined by partial label learning are then used to further optimize the annotators in a closed-loop iterative manner. Extensive experiments on five datasets demonstrate that, compared with existing state-of-the-art methods, PaSta achieves an average improvement of 1.1% in classification performance under various noise settings.

</details>


### [264] [Joint Initialization of Flux Networks and Effective Multiplication Factor for Physics-Informed Neural Networks Solving Neutron Diffusion Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25443)
*Qin Hang,Yangdi Yi,Jiayi Li,Xu Wang,Heng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于求解中子扩散K-本征值问题的物理信息神经网络联合初始化方法（JI-PINN），通过低分辨率离散近似解构建通量网络参数与有效增殖系数（keff）的联合初始状态，从而显著降低计算时间并提升训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN在求解中子扩散K-本征值问题时训练速度较慢，主要原因在于通量网络参数的随机初始化和keff的经验设定。初始状态与目标解的较大偏差会增加训练计算成本，导致收敛困难或keff误差较大。因此，需要一种更高效、更鲁棒的初始化策略来改善PINN求解中子扩散K-本征值问题的性能。

Method: JI-PINN方法的核心思想是：1）在低分辨率空间网格上构建离散K-本征值问题并求解，得到通量和keff的近似解；2）通过对近似通量进行空间插值获得连续表示，并通过预训练拟合来初始化通量网络参数，同时将近似keff设为keff的初始值；3）以（θ⁽⁰⁾, keff⁽⁰⁾）为联合初始起点，在物理约束（控制方程残差、边界条件、通量缩放约束）下联合优化通量网络参数θ和keff，最终获得中子通量分布和keff。该方法通过单因子分析研究了空间分辨率Nh和预训练步数Mpre的影响，并在多个算例上进行了随机种子实验、消融实验和性能对比验证。

Result: JI-PINN在四个测试算例（二维两群两材料、IAEA二维基准、二维两群四材料、三维单群立方体）上均显著降低计算时间，分别减少25.4%、38.2%、49.4%和28.9%，同时保持相当的求解精度。此外，keff异常结果（明显偏离参考值）的发生率降低，初始化消融实验证实了通量近似预训练和keff初始化的协同贡献。在多材料区域和三维问题中，该方法仍保持效率优势，但当材料界面复杂、通量局部变化剧烈时，低分辨率近似解的局限性可能导致部分局部偏差在后续训练中无法完全修正，影响最终精度。

Conclusion: JI-PINN为稳态中子扩散K-本征值问题中通量和keff的联合求解提供了一种基于联合初始化的新方法，相比随机初始化PINN，总计算时间减少25.4%–49.4%，同时改善了训练稳定性和结果的可靠性。该方法的性能仍受低分辨率近似解质量的制约，在复杂材料界面和三维多群反应堆堆芯模型上的应用有待进一步研究。未来工作将探索该方法在参数化问题重复计算中的应用，以及与其他高效物理约束求解策略的结合。

Abstract: Efficient determination of the effective multiplication factor (keff) is an important computational task in reactor core neutronics analysis. Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) incorporate neutron diffusion equations and boundary conditions into network training to efficiently determine the neutron flux distribution and keff. To further improve the efficiency of keff calculations using PINNs, a Joint Initialization Physics-Informed Neural Network (JI-PINN) is proposed in this work. In this method, a low-resolution approximate solution to the K-eigenvalue problem is used to construct a joint initial state for the flux network parameters and keff, and both are then jointly optimized under physical constraints. The proposed method was validated on a two-dimensional two-group two-material case, the IAEA 2D benchmark, a two-dimensional two-group four-material case, and a three-dimensional single-group case. For these test cases, the total computational time was reduced by 25.4%, 38.2%, 49.4%, and 28.9%, respectively, while comparable solution accuracy was maintained. The occurrence of anomalous results associated with marked deviations of keff from the reference value was also reduced. The proposed method provides a more efficient and robust initialization strategy for solving neutron diffusion K-eigenvalue problem with PINNs.

</details>


### [265] [Resolving Multi-Modal Regression by Difference-Quotient-Based Clustering:Fast Coarse Conditional-Label Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25467)
*Huang Weiquan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出差商聚类（DQC）方法，通过对样本对的"矛盾率"（输出距离/输入距离）进行聚类，将多模态回归数据划分为低内部矛盾的簇，作为下游条件网络的粗粒度标签前处理步骤，以缓解均值回归病态问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态回归中，平方损失训练的无约束回归器收敛于条件均值，而当K>1时该均值远离所有模态，导致预测值从不出现。这种"均值坍缩"病态的根源在于数据中的成对矛盾（输入相近但输出迥异的样本对），作者试图直接从几何角度消除矛盾而非建模概率分布。

Method: 1) 定义成对矛盾率 r=‖Δy‖/‖Δx‖；2) 差商聚类(DQC)：随机选Kc个种子，每个样本贪心地分配到使其最大内部差商最小的簇；3) 训练logits生成器 g(x) 预测簇标签；4) 训练条件网络 f(x, onehot(c)) 预测输出；5) 推理时用minMSE协议评估（对K个真实输出取最小平方误差）。聚类复杂度为O(n²/2)，可并行化。

Result: 在K=5的合成基准上，DQC在nx=500时测试minMSE达0.19（d6w128），相比随机标签的1.08和均值坍缩的1.33有显著改善，约为oracle(0.09)的2倍。K=10时DQC达0.24，对比oracle 0.16。报告了两条经验规律：(i) 簇内矛盾越大需要越深网络去消解；(ii) oracle标签比等价聚类标签用更少样本即可泛化。簇纯度仅0.36（K=10, nx=500），表明聚类仅是粗粒度对齐。

Conclusion: DQC作为一种无梯度、确定性的O(n²/2)前置聚类方法，能有效缓解多模态回归中的均值坍缩，达到oracle 1.4-3.2倍以内的minMSE。其核心定位是为下游生成式精炼模型（流匹配、扩散模型）提供快速粗粒度条件标签，降低其分支发现负担。局限在于聚类贪婪性、g(x)推理瓶颈、仅在合成数据验证，以及聚类纯度有限。未来工作包括加入低维结构项、迭代残差重聚类、真实数据集验证等。

Abstract: Multimodal regression suffers from the mean-collapse pathology: under squared loss, an unconstrained regressor converges to the conditional mean, which for K > 1 lies away from all modes. We attribute this failure to pairwise contradictions--samples with nearly identical inputs but distant outputs--and propose Difference-Quotient Clustering (DQC), which partitions data to minimize intra-cluster output-vs-input discrepancy. Each sample is assigned to the cluster that minimizes its maximum contradiction ratio; a logits generator and a conditional network are then trained on the resulting labels. Since the generating modality is unknown at test time, we evaluate via minimum squared error (minMSE) against all K true outputs. On synthetic benchmarks (K=5, 10), DQC achieves test minMSE 0.19 (K=5, nx=500), versus 0.09 for an oracle, 1.08 for random labels, and 1.33 for mean collapse. We observe two empirical regularities: larger intra-cluster contradictions require deeper networks, and oracle labels generalize from fewer samples than cluster-derived equivalents. The clustering is a hard, parallelizable O(n^2/2) front-end for coarse conditional assignment, reducing the burden of downstream generative refinement. A second-stage re-clustering on residual errors is outlined as future work.

</details>


### [266] [A Storage-Retrieval Gap in Parametric Knowledge Graph Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25489)
*Martino M. L. Pulici,Cuong Xuan Chu,Evgeny Kharlamov,Volker Tresp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究将知识图谱离线编译为LoRA适配器库（每实体一个），作为参数化知识图谱记忆，查询时通过权重注入而非文本检索，实现零上下文token成本。在MetaQA数据集上发现：子图适配器确实存储了可泛化的封闭式事实知识（单值关系精确匹配提升+0.243），但基于相似性的检索（无论句嵌入还是权重空间几何）均无法从查询中找到正确适配器，揭示了"存储-检索鸿沟"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图检索增强生成（Graph RAG）将子图序列化放入模型上下文窗口，每次查询都产生重复的token成本并暴露源数据。本文探索一种替代方案：将知识图谱离线编译为参数化记忆，以权重形式存储知识，查询时零上下文成本，潜在地解决工业知识图谱中的隐私和效率问题。核心问题在于：适配器微调后是将事实存储在权重中，还是仅学习利用上下文文本？这对参数化记忆的可行性具有决定性意义。

Method: 方法框架包括：(1)离线阶段：对每个锚实体提取k-hop子图，用固定模板将其语言化为文本，然后用QLoRA（4-bit量化，r=8, α=16）在冻结的Qwen3.5-2B基础上微调一个LoRA适配器，构建适配器库B；(2)在线阶段：根据查询选择适配器，注入到基础模型θ = θ₀ + ΣλᵢΔθᵢ，解码答案时上下文中无子图文本。实验使用MetaQA电影知识图谱，过滤后保留150个干净单一电影实体。评估指标包括精确匹配（EM）、任意匹配（AM）和集合召回率，使用贪婪解码和50000次重采样的bootstrap置信区间。检索实验对比三种方法：句嵌入距离、ΔW Frobenius几何距离和随机选择。

Result: 三个主要发现：(1)权重级存储有效：子图适配器在单值关系上获得+0.243的精确匹配增益（基线模型仅0.007），且仅正确的适配器能恢复知识（oracle gap +0.283），错误适配器增量为0.000，随机权重适配器甚至降至基线以下（-0.017）；(2)存储-检索分离：问题嵌入和ΔW Frobenius几何检索均与随机基线无差异（增量0.000），权重几何与子图语义相关性ρ=+0.329，但该几何信号无法预测答案蕴含，功能性检索失败；(3)组合实验无法隔离组合效应，因为MetaQA双跳问题的答案既不在两个子图中也不在合并上下文中。所有适配器大小为10.4 MiB，相比188 B的序列化子图，比例约58000:1，但查询时上下文token为0 vs Graph RAG的60 token中位数。

Conclusion: 参数化知识图谱记忆在存储方面可行：子图训练的适配器确实编码了可在封闭书本设置下恢复且可泛化的事实知识，且知识是实体特异性的。然而，知识以局部方式存储，不会迁移到语义相邻的适配器，因此基于相似性的检索机制（嵌入或权重几何）均无法找到正确适配器。核心开放问题不是存储而是组合：需要一种学习的、查询条件化的机制来选择和组合正确的局部适配器以回答多实体问题，而非依赖语义相似性。此外，部署时需权衡一次性离线编码成本与重复查询节省的上下文成本，参数化记忆适合稳定、频繁查询且上下文预算受限的场景。

Abstract: Graph retrieval-augmented generation places retrieved subgraphs into the model's context window at query time, paying a recurring token cost and exposing source data on every call. We study an alternative: compiling a knowledge graph offline into a bank of LoRA adapters, one per entity, that serve as a parametric knowledge layer queried by injecting weights rather than text, at zero query-time context cost. On the MetaQA dataset, we find that subgraph-trained adapters encode context-free factual knowledge that generalizes to unseen questions: on single-valued relations the adapter gains $+0.243$ exact-match score over a base model that is nearly blind closed-book ($0.007$), and only the correct adapter recovers this knowledge (an oracle gap of $+0.283$ over the base model). However, the stored knowledge is not recoverable by similarity: given a query with no subgraph, embedding-based and weight-space geometry retrieval both perform at chance, because a semantically neighbouring entity's adapter does not contain the answer - knowledge is stored locally and does not transfer. Weight geometry correlates with subgraph semantics ($\rho = +0.329$) but not with functional retrievability. We quantify the byte and context-token costs against graph retrieval-augmented generation and discuss deployment implications. Our results establish that parametric knowledge graph memory is feasible for storing knowledge, and identify selecting and composing the right adapters by a mechanism other than semantic similarity as the central open problem - motivating a learned, query-conditioned composition mechanism.

</details>


### [267] [FedQoS: Federated QoS-Risk Learning for Heterogeneous Indoor-Outdoor Access Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25496)
*Nguyen Van Thieu,Ti Ti Nguyen,Ons Aouedi,Zerihun Huruy,Vu Nguyen Ha,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedQoS是一个联邦学习框架，用于异构室内外环境中预测候选接入链路的QoS失效风险，以支持可靠接入节点选择，避免用户级数据集中化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统接入选择依赖瞬时信号强度（RSSI/RSRP/SINR），但无法捕获由移动性、阻塞、负载和资源竞争导致的未来QoS降级；现有学习方法多采用RL策略，难以解释且需集中数据。本文将接入选择重构为分布式的监督式QoS失效风险预测问题。

Method: 1) 将接入节点建模为联邦客户端，在本地观测数据上训练QoS失效概率预测模型（MLP，BCE损失）；2) 本地训练采用FedProx近端正则化（μ=0.01）稳定非IID异构数据；3) 提出QoS感知的聚合权重 ω_i = (N_i)^q · (1 + λρ_i)，同时考虑数据量和本地QoS失效暴露率；4) 预测后通过 J_{u,a} = p_{u,a} + η_h·1_{a≠a_{old}} 选择接入节点，并加入迟滞余量 ξ_h 和驻留时间 T_dwell 防止乒乓切换；5) 使用Sionna RT/SYS构建基于射线追踪的200m×200m校园场景数据集（含29 AP、1 BS、2 UAV），设置mild和severe两种非IID条件。

Result: 在mild non-IID下，FedQoS的QoS失效率（0.0031）较FedAvg降低约13.9%，较FedProx降低6.1%，较local-only降低43.6%，较历史QoS启发式降低96.6%，且吞吐量仅下降不到0.5%。在severe non-IID下仍具有竞争力（失效率0.1356，与FedAvg/FedProx持平）。预测指标BAcc、Recall、F1均达到或接近集中式学习水平。对q和λ参数的敏感性分析显示存在宽广的性能平台，无需精细调参。

Conclusion: FedQoS证明了联邦QoS风险学习能够在不集中用户级网络数据的前提下，为动态异构无线环境中的接入选择提供显著的可靠性增益，且对非IID条件和聚合参数均具有鲁棒性，具备实用部署潜力。

Abstract: Reliable access selection in dynamic and heterogeneous indoor-outdoor environments is challenging because instantaneous radio measurements alone cannot capture future QoS degradation caused by mobility, blockage, traffic load, and resource competition. This paper proposes FedQoS, a federated QoS-risk learning framework for predicting the future reliability of candidate access links and supporting access-node selection without centralizing user-level network data. In FedQoS, each access node locally learns from its observed network logs, including radio, traffic, load, and service-context features, while a global QoS-risk predictor is trained through federated aggregation. The learned model estimates the probability of QoS failure for each candidate link, and the controller uses these risk scores to select reliable access nodes under dynamic network conditions. To evaluate the framework, we construct physics-based synthetic indoor-outdoor wireless datasets using the Sionna framework, covering normal traffic, mobility, event-driven congestion, and non-IID client observations. Simulation results show that learning-based access selection substantially reduces the QoS-failure rate compared with signal-based and historical-QoS heuristic methods. FedQoS achieves near-centralized predictive performance and provides clear reliability gains under mild non-IID data while remaining competitive under the more challenging severe non-IID condition. These results demonstrate the potential of federated QoS-risk learning for reliable, data-local access selection in dynamic wireless environments.

</details>


### [268] [Reflection Steering: Disentangling Reflection from Reasoning in Activation Space for Token-Efficient Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25542)
*Jiarui Hu,Zhiyuan Wen,Xiaoyun Liu,Jiaxing Shen,Yu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Reflection Steering 是一种无需训练、基于激活空间干预的框架,旨在通过将"反思(reflection)"相关激活与"通用推理(general reasoning)"解耦,减少大推理模型中冗余的验证和反思所消耗的思考 token,同时尽量保持任务准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型(如 Qwen3 系列)在生成推理链时经常出现过度反思(over-reflection),即对已确立的结论反复验证、修正和回溯,造成 token 浪费、延迟增加和"过度思考"问题。现有激活引导方法(如 ReflCtrl)通常用正负样本的均值差构造方向,但该方向与通用推理、长度等信号纠缠,导致准确率-效率权衡不稳定。该论文的核心动机是:反思相关信号与共享的推理结构在激活空间中纠缠,直接抑制混合方向会削弱有效推理,因此需要在干预前将两者分离。

Method: 四阶段流程: (1) **方向估计**:在各层收集反思/非反思位置的隐藏状态,计算均值差得到原始反思方向 d^raw。 (2) **方向净化**:先用 PCA 对 d^raw 进行低秩去噪(投影到主成分子空间);再用池化均值 μ_L 作为"通用推理方向"的 rank-one 代理,通过对 μ_L 零空间投影去除 d^raw 中与共享推理方向对齐的部分,得到单位化的净化方向 d_L。 (3) **层级校准**:在小子集上以多个干预强度 α∈{0.7, 0.3, 0.0} 测试各候选层,要求单层响应近似单调、最小有效减量、无生成崩溃(无限循环),仅保留稳定层。 (4) **有界投影移除**:在选定层采用 h' = h − (1−α)d d^T h 的有界投影移除,而非固定加性位移;α∈[0,1] 控制反思干预强度,α=1 为原始模型,α 越小干预越强。模型权重、解码设置不变。

Result: 在 Qwen3-30B-A3B、Qwen3-8B、QwQ-32B 三个模型和 MATH-500、记录无关 MATH-350、GPQA-Diamond 两个基准上,六个匹配设置下平均减少 16.9% 思考 token。主要结果:Qwen3-30B-A3B / MATH-500 上减少 21.8% token,准确率仅降 0.1pp(95.1%→94.9%),TOST 检验通过 ±1pp 等价性;GPQA-D 上减少 21.0% token,准确率降 1.5pp,未通过等价性检验。消融实验验证:无正交化则压缩升至 43.4% 但准确率降至 67.2%;无 PCA 则压缩降至 15.8%;全层干预准确率降 4.0pp;加性干预使 token 增加 27.8%;前瞻性预注册 500 道 MATH-train 题上 token 下降 21.0%,准确率降 0.6pp(未达 1pp 标准)。

Conclusion: Reflection Steering 通过净化-校准-有界移除三步设计,实现了冻结模型权重下的可控激活干预,显著降低思考 token 同时在数学推理上保持准确率等价。反思相关表征在跨任务、跨模型间具有一定迁移性,但选定层数和最佳 α 仍依赖模型,GPQA-Diamond 未达严格等价标准。未来工作将拓展至更多模型家族,并探索跨层/跨解码步的自适应干预。

Abstract: Large reasoning models often produce reasoning traces with verification, revision, and backtracking. When reflection merely re-checks established results, it wastes reasoning tokens and increases latency. Most existing reflection steering methods add a label-derived mean-difference direction across preset layers, but its entanglement with reasoning and length signals destabilizes the accuracy-efficiency trade-off. In this paper, we propose Reflection Steering, a training-free framework for controlling reflection-associated computation within LLMs by disentangling reflection-related activations from general reasoning. Specifically, we contrast reflective and non-reflective hidden states at each LLM layer, denoise the resulting reflection directions with PCA, and orthogonalize them against general-reasoning directions. To limit downstream amplification from early-layer interventions, we calibrate each layer across multiple intervention strengths on a small set, retain only stable layers, and apply bounded projection removal to their residual-stream activations. We conduct extensive experiments across two public benchmarks and three open-weight LLMs against state-of-the-art activation-steering baselines. Results show that Reflection Steering reduces reasoning tokens by 16.9% on average across six matched settings. Besides, our method further introduces a bounded reflection intervention-strength parameter $\alpha$, enabling deployment-time adjustment to balance token savings, accuracy, and generation stability.

</details>


### [269] [Interpreting Protein Language Model Embeddings via Orthogonal Projection for Protein Fitness Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25548)
*Paulo Yanez Sarmiento,Pia Francesca Rissom,Manuel Pfeuffer,Marco Simnacher,Jordan F. Safer,Sumaiya Iqbal,Henrike O. Heyne,Nadja Klein,Bernhard Y. Renard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于正交投影（PHO）的计算高效方法，用于解释蛋白质语言模型（PLM）嵌入所编码的生化学特征，并量化其对蛋白质适应性预测的贡献。该方法将PHO扩展到高阶和交互效应，并通过消融实验和评估模型验证了PLM嵌入确实编码了与已知生化学性质相关的模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质语言模型（PLM）嵌入在蛋白质适应性预测等下游任务中表现出色，但其高维嵌入缺乏可解释性，无法明确哪些生化学特征驱动了预测结果。这限制了从中获得生物学洞见，也阻碍了在医疗等需要可解释性的领域的应用。

Method: 1. **特征选择**：使用L1正则化逻辑回归从结构性和替换性表格特征中选择与蛋白质适应性相关的子集；2. **特征表示**：训练MLP获取能捕捉高阶和交互效应的隐藏层表示；3. **正交投影**：将PHO（事后正交化）技术扩展到高阶效应，通过将PLM嵌入投影到选定表格特征（或其MLP表示）的正交补空间来消除其线性/非线性效应，公式为 $\tilde{u} = u - x^\top D$，其中 $D = X^+U$；4. **消融研究**：对比投影前后下游分类器性能的下降；5. **对照实验**：使用随机化特征进行投影以排除随机信息移除的影响；6. **评估模型**：将基于嵌入的预测logits对表格特征进行回归，以调整后R²量化生化学特征解释的方差比例。

Result: 1. **性能比较**：PLM嵌入（ESM-1v 650M和ESM-2 650M）性能显著优于表格特征，ESM-1v 650M在Activity和Expression上MCC分别达0.47和0.51；拼接表格特征仅带来微小提升，说明嵌入已包含大部分相关信息；2. **条件独立性检验**：几乎所有情况下嵌入包含超出表格特征的信息，而表格特征超出嵌入的信息较少；3. **消融研究**：正交投影后性能显著下降（如ESM-1v 650M在Expression上MCC从0.51降至0.37），基于MLP表示的投影效果更强（降至0.31），而随机投影几乎无影响；4. **评估模型**：生化学特征可解释嵌入预测logits方差的15%-29%，正交投影后R²接近零，验证了方法的有效性；5. **生物学一致性**：RSA与预测正相关、plddt负相关、脯氨酸替换降低预测等模式均与已知生化学知识一致。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于正交投影的计算高效方法成功验证了PLM嵌入确实编码了与生化学特征相关的模式，并量化了这些特征对蛋白质适应性预测的贡献。该方法不限于特定特征或嵌入，可广泛应用于其他生物学特征和其他PLM，有助于推动疾病机制理解和药物设计中的可解释AI发展。未来工作将扩展到ESM系列以外的PLM及针对特定蛋白质功能微调的PLM。

Abstract: Recently, there has been a growing adoption of protein language models (PLMs) in biomedical science. Their embeddings provide a rich numerical representation of protein sequences which achieve state-of-the-art performance on several downstream tasks including protein fitness prediction. However, PLM embeddings are not directly interpretable and, thereby, it remains unclear what features they encode. To gain insight into which biochemical properties of the protein are driving the prediction, we leverage an orthogonal projection technique that removes linear effects of known tabular features from embeddings and extend it to high-order and interaction effects. In this way, we remove the effects of interpretable biochemical features from PLM embeddings. In an ablation study, we show that this leads to a decrease in performance for a downstream classifier trained only on the embeddings to predict protein fitness. In an additional evaluation, we find that these biochemical features explain a substantial part of the variance in the predictions of this classifier. Hence, we can show that PLM embeddings encode patterns correlated with biochemical properties and quantify their contribution to predicting protein fitness. This computationally efficient approach is not limited to the features or embeddings considered here and is readily transferable to problem settings beyond protein fitness prediction.

</details>


### [270] [Beyond Optimal Rates in Stochastic Optimization: Trajectory-Adaptive Stopping Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25551)
*Liviu Aolaritei,Lucas Lévy,Francis Bach,Michael I. Jordan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对强凸随机优化，构建了完全可观测、轨迹自适应的置信序列（confidence sequences），用于在任意数据依赖时刻以高概率认证SGD的停止条件。所提方法将SGD轨迹视为序贯实验，开发了递归置信序列技术以及一种适用于变化条件均值和无界可预测范围的时序一致的经验Bernstein不等式，并扩展至小批量SGD。实验表明，相比最坏情况确定性停止规则，所提方法可提前若干数量级停止迭代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SGD收敛性分析均基于预先设定的固定时间界T，所得到的保证在数据依赖的停止时刻处不再有效；而基于最坏情形固定时间保证倒推出的确定性停止界过于保守。理论保证与实际自适应停止决策之间存在根本的认证鸿沟。

Method: 针对强凸光滑随机优化，将SGD轨迹视为序贯实验，基于次高斯条件和经验Bernstein构造两类完全可观测的轨迹自适应上置信序列：分别控制末迭代平方距离‖x_t - x*‖²和加权平均迭代的次优性 f(x̄_t) - f(x*)。核心新工具包括：(1) 递归置信序列构造（自定位机制）以处理依赖于未知距离的可预测范围；(2) 适用于时变条件均值和无界可预测增长范围的时序一致经验Bernstein不等式，通过对偶分尺度同时对二次过程和可预测范围进行stitching。进一步将结果扩展到独立同分布条件下的minibatch SGD，利用每批内最大特征值λ_max(Σ̂_t)实现轨迹自适应。

Result: 理论上，所构造的置信序列在任意时刻同时以至少1-α的概率有效，最坏情况下以1/t速率（最多带loglog t因子）衰减，匹配强凸随机优化最优率；可观测化递归构造保留了近1/t衰减率，避免了直接用集直径替代所致的t^{-1/2}退化。经验Bernstein界中由可预测范围增大引入的额外线性项在可观测构造中表现为低阶(L_t/t)^{3/2}。数值实验在SVM对偶问题上：固定置信度99%、目标精度ε=10⁻³下，Hoeffding置信序列（H）比最坏情况界早6.1倍认证停止，经验Bernstein置信序列（EB）早312倍；折算为梯度查询量，EB约5.1×10⁶次查询即可认证，而最坏情况界需约1.6×10⁹次。EB对小批量大小b适应良好：b=1024时EB可恢复最坏情况界与真实值之间53.8%的对数差距（Rakhlin等人RSS12界又比最坏情况界高出约3个量级）。对G和R_0的保守过估对EB的影响是短暂可忽略的。

Conclusion: 本文在强凸随机优化设定下系统建立了轨迹自适应、时序一致、且完全可观测的置信序列框架，将SGD的运行轨迹作为序贯推断对象，解决了固定时间保证在数据依赖停止时刻失效的认证难题。方法在理论上达到最优最坏情形速率，数值上比现有最坏情形和固定时间界提前若干数量级停止。开放问题：经验观察中置信序列与真实值之间的剩余差距是否可通过更精细的SGD动力学分析进一步缩小。

Abstract: Stochastic gradient descent (SGD) is typically analyzed at a deterministic horizon chosen before the algorithm is run, even though practical stopping decisions are made adaptively by inspecting the evolving trajectory. This mismatch creates a fundamental certification problem: fixed-time guarantees do not generally remain valid at data-dependent stopping times, while deterministic horizons derived from worst-case bounds can be highly conservative. We address this problem for strongly convex stochastic optimization by constructing fully observable, trajectory-adaptive upper confidence sequences for the squared distance of the last iterate to the optimizer and the suboptimality of a weighted average. These bounds hold simultaneously over time, attain the optimal $1/t$ decay rate up to iterated-logarithmic factors in the worst case, and adapt to the realized stochastic gradients, allowing SGD to stop as soon as a prescribed accuracy is certified without sacrificing statistical validity. Our approach treats the evolving SGD trajectory as a sequential experiment whose observations provide evidence about the unknown optimization error. To formalize this perspective, we develop new recursive confidence-sequence techniques and a general time-uniform empirical Bernstein inequality for adapted processes with time-varying conditional means and predictable ranges that may grow without bound. We further extend these confidence-sequence constructions to minibatch SGD, with the empirical Bernstein bounds exploiting the realized second-moment structure within each minibatch. Numerical experiments show that the resulting stopping rules can require several orders of magnitude fewer iterations than natural deterministic horizons.

</details>


### [271] [Beyond Scaling: Self-Evolving LLM Agents for Hardware Kernel Optimization via an Experience-Driven Workflow and Experience Graph Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25570)
*Siyuan Chen,Runlin Hou,Shenxiu Wu,Yansong Sun,Junming Cao,Yiyu Zhang,Shudi Shao,Junhao Qiu,Zhichao Lu,Qingfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KOPE提出了一种基于经验驱动的LLM智能体框架，通过Experience Graph Memory和Active Context Management机制，在固定基础模型的前提下，将硬件内核优化过程中的执行反馈转化为可重用的外部知识，在Ascend NPU和RISC-V平台上均显著提升了优化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM内核优化智能体主要依赖模型内置的硬件知识，对于公开训练数据稀缺的新型硬件（如华为Ascend NPU）适应性差；且现有系统很少将一次优化运行的执行反馈组织为后续任务可重用的经验证据，简单保留全部历史轨迹又会与当前任务争夺上下文空间。

Method: 提出KOPE框架，包含两个核心机制：(1) Experience Graph Memory (KOPE-Mem)——以有向无环森林结构记录决策-结果的执行谱系（Journal+Case双表示），通过折扣下游结果评分进行图感知排序；(2) Active Context Management and Injection——采用Hot/Warm/Cold三级动态上下文分层，在固定token预算下按相关性、图谱结果评分和置信度筛选并注入任务相关经验；闭环工作流确保先正确性后性能，仅当正确且性能提升时替换当前最优解。

Result: 在CANN Bench全套53个算子1060个用例上（Ascend 910C）：使用GLM-5.2时，KOPE通过率达84.6%（CANNBot为57.8%，CUDA-Agent为14.7%），得分2004.49；算子加速几何均值是CANNBot的1.54倍。Active Context消融将通过率从60.0%提升至84.6%，token消耗从15.9B降至1.113B（-93.0%）。Experience Graph Memory消融在412个配对时序用例上带来1.434×几何均值加速。RISC-V（Triton/FlagGems）跨硬件验证显示，Ascend源端经验提升通过覆盖率23个百分点，K3目标端经验将固定交集几何均值从0.388×提升至0.514×，两者结合达到0.786×。

Conclusion: 在基础模型固定的条件下，通过结构化记录并选择性重注入目标硬件的执行经验，可显著提升内核优化的功能覆盖率和性能；经验驱动的工作流可跨硬件栈移植，但可行性知识与性能知识的可迁移性存在差异，目标侧反馈是性能提升的主要来源。

Abstract: Hardware kernel optimization requires repeated compilation, correctness testing, profiling, and revision. LLM agents can automate parts of this process, and stronger foundation models, longer context windows, and longer execution horizons have improved optimization within individual tasks. These advances alone do not enable an agent to learn from completed optimization runs. Existing kernel-optimization agents seldom preserve a decision, its observed execution feedback, and the later decisions that use that evidence. Retaining every prior trajectory is also impractical because an expanding history competes with the current task for context. We present KOPE, an experience-driven framework for hardware kernel optimization. KOPE records optimization trajectories with correctness and performance feedback in Experience Graph Memory, then uses Active Context Management and Injection to retrieve relevant experience under a fixed token budget. The graph retains decision order, observed outcomes, and alternative branches, allowing evidence collected on the target hardware to inform later optimization steps and tasks. Under the same GLM-5.2 setting, the geometric mean of KOPE's per-operator speedups is $1.54\times$ that of CANNBot, the strongest competing baseline. In a complete 53-operator ablation, Active Context Management and Injection raises pass rate from 60.0\% to 84.6\%, increases the evaluator-reported positive-field geometric mean from 0.0382 to 0.0661, and reduces optimization token consumption from 15.9B to 1.113B tokens relative to passive agent-led context construction. Enabling Experience Graph Memory raises full-suite pass rate from 55.2\% to 84.6\% and yields a $1.43\times$ geometric-mean speedup on valid timing comparisons. These results support continual optimization through external experience while the foundation model remains fixed.

</details>


### [272] [Individual Fairness in Hierarchical Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25586)
*Binita Maity,Shrutimoy Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究层次聚类中的个体公平性问题，将局部k近邻内的Lipschitz公平性约束形式化为关于被支配超度量（dominated ultrametrics）的可行性问题，刻画了所需的最小乘性松弛量，证明了局部可行性阈值、扰动稳定性、关于k的单调性，并揭示了局部可行性与全局可实现性之间存在Θ(log n)的本质分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统层次聚类方法仅优化全局目标或使用贪心链接规则，不提供对个别数据点的公平性保证。由于层次结构向上传播，早期的合并决策可能在局部近邻之间产生不成比例的扭曲，影响特定个体。该研究旨在刻画调和局部Lipschitz型约束与全局超度量结构所需的最小松弛量。

Method: 将个体公平层次聚类形式化为被支配超度量上的可行性问题：寻找一个超度量u，使得u支配给定度量d（不收缩距离），且在每个点的k近邻内满足乘法公平约束u(i,j)≤α·d(i,j)+β。定义了局部互惠异质性比α_k^mut(d)作为局部可行性阈值；提出了FCAC（Fairness Constrained Agglomerative Clustering）算法，通过将链接顺序与合并高度分配分离、在合并时强制满足支配性和公平性约束来构造可行超度量；利用常数度扩张图构造证明局部-全局间隙的下界，并通过经典树嵌入（FRT）得到O(log n)的充分性上界。

Result: 主要理论结果包括：（1）定理1证明局部互惠异质性比α_k^mut(d)是任何乘性松弛的必要下界；（2）定理2证明在ε稳定邻域假设下，α_k^mut关于ℓ∞扰动是Lipschitz稳定的；（3）定理3通过经典树嵌入证明O(log n)松弛总是充分的；（4）命题1利用常数度扩张图构造一族度量，证明α_k^mut=1但α*=Θ(log n)，揭示局部-全局存在本质分离；（5）定理4证明α*(d,k)关于k单调非递减。实验在合成高斯混合数据和随机3-正则图以及Adult、German Credit、Iris等真实数据集上验证了可行性阈值的存在性、邻域大小饱和性以及数据集相关的失真行为。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了层次聚类中个体公平性的几何可行性理论框架，证明了局部公平性条件与全局超度量可实现性之间存在固有的Θ(log n)间隙，表明仅检查局部近邻不足以保证全局公平性，且公平性的最小成本本质上由度量到超度量的嵌入失真所量化。提出了几何感知算法FCAC在实际数据上表现优于经典FRT嵌入。未来方向包括刻画可行性问题的计算复杂性、为结构化度量类收紧界、设计可扩展近似算法以及将该几何框架推广到其他多尺度表示与公平性概念。

Abstract: Hierarchical clustering produces ultrametric representations that impose strong global geometric constraints and may distort local similarities in ways that disproportionately affect individual data points. We study hierarchical clustering under an individual fairness requirement that bounds relative distortion within local $k$-nearest neighborhoods. We formulate this requirement as a feasibility problem over dominated ultrametrics and characterize the minimal multiplicative slack required for feasibility. We identify a sharp local threshold, prove stability under bounded perturbations, establish monotonicity in $k$, and show an intrinsic $\Theta(\log n)$ separation between local and global realizability. Experiments on synthetic and real world datasets support our theoretical results.

</details>


### [273] [M-Fibration Theory with Applications to Neural Network Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25598)
*Paolo Boldi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文由 Paolo Boldi 提出 M-纤维化（M-Fibration）理论，将经典图纤维化理论从无权图推广到以交换幺半群 M 标记的图，并引入 ε-近似纤维化概念。主要贡献包括：(1) 定义 M-图和 M-纤维化，证明任意 M-图存在唯一的最小基；(2) 提出 ε-近似 M-纤维化和近似公平划分及其度量理论；(3) 将该框架应用于神经网络压缩，包括 MLP 和 CNN，并展示了在 MNIST 和 CIFAR-10 数据集上的压缩效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典图纤维化理论（源自 Grothendieck 和 Boldi-Vigna 的工作）只能处理"静态"标签，无法适应加权图或带代数结构的标签，且对噪声极度敏感——一条多余的弧或标签的微小偏差都会破坏整个理论。而在实际应用中（如神经网络压缩、动力系统分析、生物学网络），常常需要处理加权或噪声数据，并希望找到近似对称结构，因此亟需一个统一的理论框架将这些特例纳入，并解决噪声容忍性问题。

Method: 方法包含五个核心模块：(1) M-图定义：在图上引入标签函数 λ: A_G → M，M 为交换幺半群；(2) M-纤维化定义：图同态 φ: G→H 满足对 H 的每条弧 ā 和每个 x∈φ⁻¹(t(ā))，有 λ(ā) = ⊕_{a∈φ⁻¹(ā)∩G(-,x)} λ(a)，即像弧的标签等于原弧标签在 M 上的求和；(3) 最小基定理：通过迭代细化构造最粗 M-公平划分，得到唯一的最小基（始终为简单图），算法复杂度可达 O(|A_G|·log|N_G|)；(4) ε-近似理论：在 M 上引入度量 d 满足平移不变性，定义局部和全局纤维化误差，引入纤维化距离 fd 和压缩-精度权衡函数 β_G(ε)；(5) 近似划分：定义 ε-公平划分及其算法（贪心种子聚类，1+ε 保证的 Chebyshev 中心）。应用层将神经网络（MLP/CNN）转化为 M-图，神经元作为节点，突触权重作为弧标签，通过逐层归一化度量实现跨层比较。

Result: 主要理论成果：(1) 证明了 M-纤维化的复合封闭性和双射纤维化即为同构；(2) 证明了每个 M-图存在唯一的最小基（定理 4.1），强化了经典结果；(3) 证明了任意全射 M-纤维化 G→H 存在 H→Ĝ 的分解（定理 5.1）；(4) 通过反例说明 M-图范畴中拉回与万有全图性质不普遍成立。ε-近似纤维化误差对复合具有次可加性。实验结果：在 LeNet-300-100 MLP（MNIST）上 ε=0.4 时参数压缩至 79%，精度仅下降 0.23%（97.76%→97.53%）；CNN 上 ε=0.4 时压缩至 93%，精度几乎不变；VGG16-BN（CIFAR-10）ε=0.35 时参数压缩至 44%，精度仍有 90.18%。所有网络在 ε∈[0.35, 0.5] 区间出现精度崩溃的相变。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一套完整且通用的加权图纤维化理论框架——M-纤维化理论，将经典图纤维化扩展至交换幺半群标记的图，并引入 ε-近似纤维化以处理噪声和误差场景。该框架不仅在范畴论层面具备良好的唯一性、最小基存在性等结构性结果，还在算法层面提供了高效的最小基计算方法和带保证的近似压缩算法。在应用层面，该理论为神经网络（包括 CNN）的无损与有损压缩提供了严格的数学基础，实验验证了"先平台后崩溃"的相变现象。开源 Python 库（支持 ONNX 格式）保证了可复现性。该工作既统一了公平划分、加权双模拟、马尔可夫链聚合、消息传递 GNN 等已有概念，又为几何深度学习中的对称性研究提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: The purpose of this paper is to provide a general, comprehensive, theoretical framework that allows one to deal with fibrations on graphs labelled on a commutative monoid. This is a genuine extension of the theory of graph fibrations (as introduced in "Fibrations of Graphs" [Discrete Math., vol. 243, pp. 21-66, 2002]), that makes it possible to deal with weighted graphs, and also graphs labelled with other algebraic structures. The derived theory also lends itself naturally to consider approximate fibrations. As an example, we show how this framework can be applied to the compression of arbitrary neural networks (including CNNs), providing a strong theoretical underpinning to the recent results in "The role of fibration symmetries in geometric deep learning" [Proc. Natl. Acad. Sci. USA, vol. 123, no. 4, p. e2416552123, 2026]

</details>


### [274] [Frequency-aware forecasting for short-term typhoon gust prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25604)
*Xuefei Wang,Tingyi Liu,Heng Zhang,Shengjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出WDANet——一种基于平稳小波分解(SWT)、FiLM跨频调制和双分支编码器-解码器的频率感知深度学习框架,用于台风条件下短期阵风预测。在中国西太平洋海域的细网格阵风预报中,WDANet在24小时预报时效内短时(尤其是前6小时)优于多种SOTA深度学习模型和ECMWF-HRES数值预报,在极端台风事件下能更准确地捕捉阵风峰值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 台风条件下,海上风电机组承受极端阵风冲击,而现有深度学习模型难以同时捕捉大气长期演变趋势与阵风短时高频突变,导致对极值阵风的预报能力不足,无法满足海上风电安全运行和灾害预警的需求。

Method: 1) 可学习平稳小波变换(SWT):采用sym4初始化并允许训练中优化,将多变量气象序列分解为低频近似和高频细节分量;2) 双分支编码器-解码器:两支独立BiLSTM+Attention分别建模趋势和波动,低频支采用全局注意力,高频支采用带高斯时间先验的局部注意力;3) FiLM跨频调制:由低频分支动态生成γ、β参数对高频特征进行仿射变换,实现物理一致的跨尺度信息融合;4) 加性融合得到最终预测。

Result: 在西太平洋400个台风事件(1960–2025)的ERA5数据上,WDANet在24h预报全程取得最低RMSE与MAE(相对iTransformer误差降低约5.6%–6.1%),RAcc略低于CNN-LSTM但绝对误差最优;前6h RMSE较ECMWF-HRES降低56.5%;在极端台风(阵风28.52–46.85 m/s)和台风摩羯(Yagi)案例中,峰值误差(PE)与峰值时间误差(PTE)表现最佳,验证了频率感知机制对极值阵风预报的有效性。

Conclusion: WDANet通过频域解耦与跨频调制有效缓解了现有分解方法的时间错位与尺度独立性假设问题,在短时台风阵风预报上显著优于主流深度学习模型及ECMWF-HRES,展现出在海上风电运行、灾害预警和风险防控方面的应用潜力;但仍受限于ERA5数据近实时性差、缺乏空间信息和长时效预报能力,未来可融合NWP输出与迁移学习进一步提升长时预报与极值重建能力。

Abstract: Accurate gust forecasting under typhoon conditions remains challenging due to the highly non-stationary and multi-scale characteristics of extreme wind fluctuations. Existing deep learning models often struggle to simultaneously capture long-term trends and rapid local variations, resulting in degraded performance during extreme events. We propose WDANet, a frequency-aware forecasting framework that integrates stationary wavelet decomposition, a Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM) strategy, and a dual-branch encoder-decoder architecture, enabling separate modeling of trend and fluctuation components. Taking the offshore regions of the Western Pacific in China as an example, we conduct fine-grid wind gust prediction research. The results demonstrate that WDANet shows advantages for short lead times under the experimental setting across a 24-h forecasting horizon and achieves higher prediction accuracy than ECMWF-HRES within the first 6 h. During extreme wind events, WDANet more accurately captures gust peaks and attains the best RMSE and MAE performance. These results highlight its potential for offshore wind power operation, disaster warning, and risk mitigation.

</details>


### [275] [DCEO: Direct Causal Effect Optimization for Long-Term User Value Modeling in E-commerce Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25635)
*Junzhao Zhang,Tao Zhang,Liren Yu,Feiyi Dong,Zhixuan Zhang,Dan Ou,Haihong Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对电商搜索中商品级排序与用户级长期目标（如GMV）之间的粒度不匹配问题，提出了DCEO（Direct Causal Effect Optimization）框架。该框架通过Actor-Critic架构直接从用户级长期目标学习商品级代理分数：Actor生成上下文相关的融合权重，Critic估计相对因果效应（RCE）作为训练信号，从而学习与最终目标更对齐的个性化代理分数。在淘宝/天猫大规模电商搜索系统的41天在线A/B测试中，DCEO相比传统GMV代理实现+0.36%的GMV提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业电商搜索系统的最终目标是优化用户级长期价值（如n天累计GMV），但排序是在商品级进行的。现有方法通过人工设计的多目标融合（如点击、加购、购买、交易额的加权和）桥接这一粒度差距，但全局共享的手工调参权重缺乏细粒度个性化，且与最终目标的对齐欠佳。预测性关联强的代理分数并不一定能通过排序提升来真正改善最终目标，因此需要优化因果效应而非简单关联。

Method: 1. **问题形式化**：将学习商品级代理分数定义为跨粒度优化问题，用相对因果效应（RCE）衡量代理度量与最终目标Y_u的对齐程度：RCE = E[Y_u((1+δ)P_u) - Y_u(P_u)] / E[Y_u(P_u)]。
2. **Actor**：基于用户特征z_u和请求特征z_ur，通过MLP+softmax生成M个非负权重w_ur（和为1），与17个上游预测分数（impr2click、click2pay、cart2gmv等）做凸组合得到商品级代理分数p_uri。
3. **校准的用户级聚合**：P_u为所有曝光上代理分数的平均值，再用校准模型g_ψ将观测曝光量N_u映射到参考曝光量C=100，消除排序变化对曝光量的影响。
4. **Critic**：h_φ从z_u和P_u预测最终目标Y_u，用MSE损失训练。
5. **Actor损失**：因果效应损失L_CE（用冻结的Critic估计P_u增加5%时Y_u的变化，负向作为损失）+ 条件归一化排序损失L_CNR（用e_ζ模型做高斯归一化后用Bradley-Terr pairwise loss对齐排序），最终L_actor = L_CE + 0.3·L_CNR。
6. **在线服务**：仅部署Actor，将p_uri以λ·log(p_uri)形式加入现有融合公式，其余上游分数和原始融合权重保持不变。

Result: 离线实验：最终模型RCE=0.053。消融实验中，预测关联优化仅得0.022，纯L_CE得0.031，纯L_CNR得0.048，组合达0.053（2.41×提升）。上游分数集合从impr2gmv only(0.027)扩展到17个分数的全集(0.053)逐步提升。不同最终目标（点击/购买/GMV）下Actor学到的权重分布明显不同，验证了目标驱动的自适应特性。1天到4天GMV窗口下impr2click权重从0.331升至0.404，说明长期目标促使更多探索。在线A/B测试（41天）：相比传统pCTR×pCVR×E[price]的GMV代理，GMV+0.36%，点击量+0.36%，购买量+0.12%。

Conclusion: DCEO是首个直接从用户级最终目标通过优化相对因果效应来学习商品级代理分数的框架。其核心贡献在于：(1) 跨粒度优化问题形式化，以RCE表征对齐；(2) Actor-Critic端到端框架，校准聚合连接商品级输出与用户级监督；(3) 在淘宝大规模工业系统中验证有效性和可解释性，41天在线测试GMV提升0.36%。论文也指出了若干局限：RCE是基于观察日志的局部模型估计、可能受未观测混杂影响；固定曝光量校准排除了排序对用户活跃度的影响；Actor受限于上游分数的凸组合，表达能力受限。

Abstract: Industrial e-commerce search systems ultimately aim to optimize the user-level long-term objective, such as n-day cumulative purchases or gross merchandise value (GMV) per user. However, such objectives are defined at the user level, whereas search ranking is based on item-level scores within each request. Existing methods typically bridge this granularity gap through manually designed multi-objective fusion, where predictions of multiple item-level objectives, such as clicks, carts, purchases, and transaction value, are combined into a ranking score that serves as a proxy for the ultimate objective. Such hand-crafted fusion schemes rely on a small set of manually tuned weights, limiting fine-grained personalization and leading to suboptimal alignment with the ultimate objective. In this paper, we propose DCEO (Direct Causal Effect Optimization), a data-driven framework for learning item-level proxy scores that are better aligned with the ultimate objective. We first aggregate the item-level proxy scores into a user-level proxy metric and quantify its alignment with the ultimate objective using a relative causal effect. We then develop an actor-critic framework, where the critic estimates the ultimate objective for a given user-level proxy metric, and the actor dynamically generates context-dependent fusion weights over multiple objectives to construct the item-level proxy scores and is trained to directly optimize the relative causal effect. Extensive offline experiments and analyses demonstrate the effectiveness and interpretability of DCEO. In addition, DCEO has been deployed in a large-scale industrial e-commerce search system, outperforming the conventional GMV proxy by 0.36% in GMV in a 41-day online A/B test.

</details>


### [276] [A Token-Level Analysis of Sampled-Token Reverse-KL On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25643)
*Bing Shao,Jiazheng Zhang,Long Ma,Yujiong Shen,Senjie Jin,Xin Guo,Yuming Yang,Mingxu Chai,Zhiheng Xi,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对反向 KL 散度在策略蒸馏（OPD）中 K2 估计量的逐 token 梯度进行了精确分解，推导出其 ℓ₁ 范数可分解为"教师-学生 log-prob 绝对差 × 学生端 softmax 因子 (1−π_S(y_t|c_t))"的乘积形式。实验发现低学生概率 token 占据了不成比例的梯度范数份额，且同时富集了较大的教师-学生差距。基于此分析，作者提出了一种轻量级的 SuRe（Surprise-aware Reweighting）权重规则作为干预手段，在 Qwen3 数学蒸馏实验中提升多个数学指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 OPD 研究主要关注 rollout 来源或散度选择，但缺乏对采样 token 损失在 token 层面如何分配梯度更新的深入理解。本文旨在回答"哪些 token 获得了大梯度，以及为什么"这一基础问题。

Method: 1. 推导 K2 估计量关于学生 logits 的梯度闭合形式，给出 ℓ₁ 范数分解：‖∇_z L_t^RKL‖₁ = 2·|Δlog p_t|·(1−π_S(y_t|c_t))；2. 在 Qwen3 数学蒸馏设置中进行经验性 token 层面分析，证实梯度范数高度集中于低学生概率 token；3. 提出 SuRe（Surprise-aware Reweighting）：一种 detached、有界、单调的逐 token 权重规则 w_t = 1 + α(1−sg(π_S))，在 α=0 时退化为标准 OPD。

Result: 在 Qwen3-1.7B-Base 和 Qwen3-4B-Base 学生模型上蒸馏 Qwen3-8B 教师，训练于 DeepMath-hard 57K 困难子集：- SuRe (α=1.0) 在 1.7B 学生上 AIME24 pass@8 提升 +6.7pp，AMC23 pass@8 提升 +7.5pp；- 4B 学生上多个指标亦有改善；- OOD 基准（CRUX、IFEval、MMLU-Pro）无明显退化；- 消融实验：方向性很重要（High-reweight 反向权重表现较差），α 扫描显示 α=1.0 在 k∈{1,2,4} 时最优，更大 α=2.0 增益减弱；- 均值归一化 SuRe 进一步提升 MATH-500 至 69.20%/82.20%。

Conclusion: 本文主要贡献是 K2 估计量在反向 KL OPD 中的梯度层面特征化，明确指出梯度范数在学生侧"surprise"token 上的集中现象是一种联合经验模式，而非仅由学生侧因子单独决定。SuRe 作为一种受分析启发的轻量级干预，在评估设置中改善了多个数学指标，但控制实验未能完全将精确 surprise 分配的好处与一般非均匀加权的好处区分开来，表明该分析提供了有用的视角但机制层面仍有开放问题。局限性：仅研究 sampled-token 反向 KL OPD；实验局限于数学推理；未在大规模模型上验证。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) supervises a student on its own trajectories with token-level signals from a frozen teacher, yet how a sampled loss allocates updates across tokens remains poorly understood. We analyze the gradient of the per-token K2 estimator of reverse KL with respect to the student logits. The $\ell_1$ norm of this gradient factorizes into the absolute teacher--student log-probability gap and a student-side softmax factor that grows as the sampled token becomes less likely under the student. In our math-distillation runs, these per-token norms are highly non-uniform: low-student-probability tokens account for a disproportionate share of their sum and are also enriched in large teacher--student gaps. As a lightweight intervention suggested by this analysis, we study Surprise-aware Reweighting (SuRe), a detached, bounded weighting rule that further amplifies this existing allocation. Across two Qwen3 student scales, SuRe improves several math metrics over vanilla OPD and shows no clear degradation on the selected out-of-domain benchmarks. Our primary contribution is therefore a gradient-level characterization of reverse-KL OPD trained with the K2 estimator, with SuRe as one empirical instantiation.

</details>


### [277] [Adversarial Training of Linear Models under Stealthy Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25681)
*Lovisa Eriksson,Dave Zachariah,André M. H. Teixeira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对线性模型提出了一种基于检测器的切换模型，通过"安全恢复模型"的设计将对抗攻击约束为隐蔽性攻击，并推导出凸形式的对抗风险公式，实现清洁数据与被攻击数据间的显式权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗训练方法依赖静态扰动样本生成，对自适应隐蔽攻击缺乏保证；传统检测方案对精心设计的隐蔽攻击无效。作者旨在填补这些空白：使最优攻击策略天然具有隐蔽性，并提供可显式权衡清洁与受攻击数据性能的对抗训练框架。

Method: 1) 特征分为保护特征 x_p 和非保护特征 x_u；2) 设计基于能量检测的异常检测器 δ̃（基于线性回归插值 x̂_u）；3) 切换模型：δ=0 使用标称模型 f_{θ₀}，δ=1 使用恢复模型 f_{θ₁}；4) 定义"安全"恢复模型：f_{θ₁} 对被攻击输入使用插值 x̂ 而非受污染的 x̃_u，从而保证检测到的攻击不会增加损失（命题1）；5) 通过约束 θ₁⊤ = θ₀⊤[I 0; V[x_u,x_p]V[x_p]⁻¹ 0] 构造安全模型（引理1）；6) 推导线性回归（命题2）和逻辑回归（命题3）的凸对抗风险公式，包含攻击概率 γ 和虚假报警率 ᾱ 超参数；7) 使用带动量的随机梯度下降最小化经验风险。

Result: 在合成数据（d=4 的高斯分布，n=2000）和真实数据（信用卡欺诈检测数据集，n=569k）上的实验表明：(a) 提出的切换模型在 γ_test=0 时与标准模型性能一致，在 γ_test 增大时收敛至完全安全模型，中间区域性能更优；(b) 即使攻击概率 γ_train 被误设（如 γ_train=0.05<实际值），模型风险仍低于完全安全模型，并持续低于标准切换模型；(c) 在逻辑回归及其他高斯参数下行为一致。ᾱ=0.01 时实际虚假报警率为 1.3%。

Conclusion: 结合攻击检测器并使恢复模型与标称模型对齐，可强制对抗攻击具有隐蔽性；为线性和逻辑回归推导的凸对抗风险公式具有理论保证，数值实验支持所得模型的最优性。未来工作包括扩展至更复杂模型与检测器、神经网络应用，以及深入研究该方法与对抗训练、正则化之间的联系。

Abstract: Predictive models are widely used in many fields, but are vulnerable to false data injection attacks. To address this, detection schemes and adversarial training have been proposed, but such approaches lack guarantees against stealthy attacks. We therefore propose a detector-based switched model, in which optimal attack strategies are stealthy. For linear prediction models, we derive a convex formulation of the resulting adversarial risk. The model incorporates protected features and introduces a hyperparameter modelling attack probability, enabling an explicit performance trade-off between clean and attacked data regimes. Numerical simulations on real and synthetic data show improved performance on partially attacked data, even for misspecified attack probabilities.

</details>


### [278] [Modeling spatio-temporal locality in multi-step forecasting of geo-referenced time series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25698)
*Annunziata D'Aversa,Gianvito Pio,Michelangelo Ceci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPALT是一种基于线性模型树的新型时空预测方法，通过在树构建过程中局部分组具有相似趋势的时间序列，并在剪枝阶段考虑时空局部性，实现了多传感器多步预测，在可再生能源生产预测任务上优于现有树模型和时空神经网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理分布式传感器的预测面临空间自相关带来的相互依赖问题。现有方法通常在全局层面建模空间维度，难以捕捉局部时空模式，且缺乏对相似趋势序列的局部关联建模能力。

Method: SPALT基于线性模型树（Model Trees），通过树构建启发式将趋势相似的时间序列（即使时间不同）分组到同一节点，并在节点上选择性注入空间特征；同时提出基于Reduced Error Pruning的新剪枝策略以保持时空局部性；支持多步预测并同时输出多个传感器的未来值。

Result: 在3个真实可再生能源生产数据集上的实验表明，SPALT在不同时间跨度的预测中均有效，且性能优于基于树的模型以及融合时空信息的先进神经网络。

Conclusion: 局部时空建模优于全局空间建模，SPALT通过线性模型树的局部分组与剪枝策略，能有效捕捉分布式传感器的时空局部性，为多传感器多步预测提供了一种高效且性能优越的解决方案。

Abstract: Forecasting future measurements from geographically distributed sensors is essential across many domains. However, the spatial distribution of these sensors raises multiple challenges, primarily due to spatial autocorrelation phenomena, that introduce inter-dependencies among nearby locations, that cannot therefore be treated independently. While some existing approaches can capture such phenomena, they generally model the spatial dimension globally across all locations. On the other hand, the method we propose in this paper, called SPALT, focuses on capturing spatial relationships among time series with similar trends, even if they occur at different times, thus modeling the spatio-temporal locality. SPALT leverages linear model trees, which allow us to consider the spatial autocorrelation locally: during the tree-building process, the adopted heuristics group time series exhibiting similar trends into the same node, on which additional features considering the spatial dimension are selectively injected. Additionally, we propose a new pruning strategy, based on Reduced Error Pruning, that also considers the spatio-temporal locality during the tree simplification. Designed for a multi-step setting, SPALT provides forecasts for multiple future time steps across multiple sensors simultaneously. The characteristics exhibited by SPALT can provide significant benefits in different domains, where measurements come from distributed sensors. In this paper, we focus on data produced by sensors located in multiple renewable power plants measuring their energy production at regular, short intervals. Experiments on 3 real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of SPALT in forecasting the production of energy at different time horizons, and its superior performance in comparison with tree-based models and state-of-the-art neural networks that incorporate both temporal and spatial dimensions.

</details>


### [279] [Tropospheric temperature and humidity profile retrieval from Meteosat Flexible Combined Imager based on deep learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25700)
*Alejandro Salgueiro,Johannes Rausch,Julie Thérèse Villinger,Angela Meyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Residual U-Net的深度学习框架，从Meteosat第三代Flexible Combined Imager (MTG-FCI)的全部16个光谱通道中反演全天空对流层温度和湿度廓线，无需数值天气预报(NWP)背景场支持。模型利用128×128像素空间上下文补丁，在欧洲地区14个月的FCI观测与CERRA再分析数据上进行训练，并使用独立的探空气球数据进行验证。温度偏差低于0.4 K，标准差为1.5–1.9 K；相对湿度标准差为12–20%，接近CERRA再分析本身的精度。消融实验表明空间上下文贡献显著，尤其在云下区域效果最明显。特征重要性分析显示模型学习到的通道敏感性与辐射传输理论基本一致，且可见光和近红外通道（含0.9/0.8 µm水汽比值对、3.8 µm短波红外通道）也为反演提供了有价值的信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统从宽带成像仪（如MTG-FCI）反演对流层温度和湿度廓线的方法面临严峻的欠定和病态挑战——成像仪仅有限数量的宽波段红外通道，权重函数厚重、垂直分辨能力弱。运行级反演算法通常依赖NWP背景场来稳定最优估计解，这削弱了反演产品的独立性。现有运行FCI廓线产品依赖NWP背景，仅用于衍生产品，未能充分利用FCI全部16个通道的频谱信息和空间信息。论文试图验证：完全数据驱动、空间感知的深度学习方法能否独立于NWP预报，从FCI观测中提取统计可靠的全天空对流层廓线。

Method: 1) 数据：MTG-FCI全部16个通道（3可见、5近红外、8红外）的辐射/反射率/亮温观测，加上10个辅助变量（地表高程、地表气压、太阳高度角、时角、日角、纬度、经度、卫星天顶角等），目标为CERRA再分析的15层气压（1000–300 hPa）温度和比湿廓线。2) 模型：Residual U-Net + Squeeze-and-Excitation通道注意力 + DeepLab风格多尺度膨胀卷积瓶颈（27.73 M参数），输入128×128像素补丁（26通道），输出128×128×15的温度和比湿廓线张量；MSE损失+AdamW优化器+余弦退火学习率。3) 基线对比：1×1-Net（无空间上下文，像素级MLP）、ancillary-only模型（仅辅助变量，无卫星观测）、ERA5气候态。4) 评估：用IGRA独立探空气球验证偏差和标准差；分云上/云下、昼夜、季节、第二独立测试期评估；用功率谱分析空间分辨率；用通道替换（cross-sample substitution）进行特征重要性分析。

Result: 1) 全天空条件下温度偏差-0.4至0.1 K，标准差1.5–1.9 K（vs CERRA 1.0–1.4 K），优于ERA5气候态（STD 4.5–4.7 K）和1×1-Net（STD 2.2–3.2 K）。相对湿度标准差12–20%（vs CERRA 9–19%）。2) 云下温度STD仅增加<0.4 K，相对湿度STD增加<3%，U-Net比1×1-Net在云下有更大增益——证明空间上下文补偿了云层遮挡的辐射信息缺失。3) 特征重要性分析显示12.3和13.3 µm通道主导（云顶锚定作用）；6.3/7.3 µm水汽通道在湿度反演中贡献符合其权重函数；0.9/0.8 µm比对成功用于近地表水汽反演；3.8 µm短波红外对低层温度湿度贡献显著——这些均符合辐射传输理论，但传统反演很少使用。4) 功率谱显示在17–20 km尺度出现交叉，检索结果在小于CERRA有效分辨率（~33–55 km）的尺度上保留了更精细的可变性。5) 空间RMSE在高卫星天顶角和复杂地形（阿尔卑斯山等）增加。6) 第二独立测试期（DJF 2025/26）显示中低对流层泛化良好，仅近地面略有退化。

Conclusion: 本文证明了空间感知深度学习模型（Residual U-Net结合SE注意力和多尺度瓶颈）能够从地球静止轨道成像仪MTG-FCI的16个宽带通道中提取统计可靠的全天空对流层温度和湿度廓线，且完全不依赖NWP预报背景。模型精度接近运行级再分析产品CERRA，并在精细尺度上恢复出再分析因数值耗散而损失的变率。特征重要性分析表明模型自发学习到的通道敏感性在很大程度上与辐射传输物理一致，并成功利用了传统物理反演中较少使用的可见光/近红外通道（特别是0.9/0.8 µm水汽比值和3.8 µm通道）。该工作为更快速、自主、独立于预报的大气温湿度监测开辟了路径，可与未来MTG-IRS高光谱探测仪反演形成互补。

Abstract: The Meteosat Third Generation (MTG) Flexible Combined Imager (FCI) offers new opportunities for tropospheric temperature and humidity profiling, at higher spatio-temporal resolutions and expanded spectral coverage relative to its predecessor. Vertically resolved retrievals from broadband imagers are inherently challenging, and operational retrieval algorithms typically rely on numerical weather prediction (NWP) background fields to compensate for limited infrared spectral resolution, reducing the retrievals' independence. We develop a spatially aware deep learning framework to retrieve all-sky tropospheric temperature and humidity profiles from FCI, without forecast profiles as input. A Residual U-Net that exploits spatial context across all 16 FCI channels was trained on 14 months of collocated FCI observations and CERRA reanalysis targets over Europe. Validated against independent radiosondes, retrieved temperatures show biases below 0.4 K and standard deviations of 1.5-1.9 K. Retrieved relative humidity standard deviations range from 12-20 %, compared to 9-19 % for CERRA. Performance degrades modestly under clouds, with standard deviation increases below 0.4 K and 3 % RH beneath cloud tops despite limited direct radiative information. Ablation experiments show that spatial context improves retrievals, with the largest gains below cloud tops. Feature sensitivity analysis indicates broad consistency with FCI bands' established radiative transfer characteristics. Visible and near-infrared channels contribute despite not being commonly used in physics-based profile inversions. These results demonstrate that spatially aware deep learning models can extract statistically reliable tropospheric profiles from geostationary imager observations, independent of NWP forecast fields, enabling more rapid autonomous monitoring of the atmosphere.

</details>


### [280] [Fairness-Aware Test-Time Prompt Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25707)
*Yoann Launay,Parameswaran Kamalaruban,Tom Kempton,Stuart Burrell,David Sutton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FairTPT，一种面向视觉-语言模型（VLM）的公平性感知的episodic test-time prompt tuning方法，通过联合最小化目标属性的边际熵和最大化敏感属性的边际熵来缓解子群体性能差异，同时引入ELRA（熵学习率适配器）防止模型崩溃。该方法在CelebA、FairFace、WaterBirds、UTKFace等标准公平性基准上，在保留整体准确率的同时改善了最差群体准确率（WGA）、偏差（Bias）和均等赔率差（EOD），并对超参数变化表现出更强的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的vision-language models（如CLIP）在零样本分类中常继承并放大预训练数据中的系统性偏见，导致不同人口群体间的性能差异。现有去偏方法通常需要重新训练或访问敏感属性标签，且episodic test-time adaptation (TTA) 方法（如TPT、Zero）虽能提升平均准确率，但其对子群体鲁棒性和公平性的影响尚未被系统研究，甚至可能加剧群体间差距。因此亟需一种无需敏感属性标签、无需重训练即可在测试时提升模型公平性的方法。

Method: 方法核心包括三部分：(1) 双重熵目标——通过soft prompt tuning，最小化目标属性预测的归一化边际熵（鼓励准确预测），同时最大化敏感属性预测的归一化边际熵（降低对虚假相关性的依赖）；(2) 提出两种损失形式：S-loss（仅基于敏感属性的边缘熵）和TS-loss（条件化于目标标签的敏感属性边缘熵），以及多目标优化变体FairTPT (MO) 使用UPGrad聚合器通过Jacobian下降求解；(3) 熵学习率适配器（ELRA）——在"线性域"内设定初始学习率（η = 0.01·||t_ctx||/||∇ℓ||），并在每步优化后根据目标熵变化量|Δℓ_ent|动态调整学习率以防止模型崩溃。所有方法在episodic TTA设置下运行，无需敏感属性标签，使用CLIP ViT-L/14作为基础模型。

Result: 在6个数据集-属性配置上（FairFace Gender×Race、CelebA Hair×Gender、CelebA Smiling×Gender、WaterBirds、UTKFace Age×Race、UTKFace Gender×Race），FairTPT和FairTPT (MO) 在聚合结果上同时保持或提升了整体准确率（Acc）、最差群体准确率（WGA），并降低了偏差（Bias）和均等赔率差（EOD），均超过+2.0pp的改善阈值。相比之下，TPT和Zero在子群体指标上聚合后退化超过2.0pp；OrthCali虽然能保持准确率但子群体指标改善不一致且对λ_orth敏感。FairTPT在λ_fair从100到∞的广泛范围内保持稳定（hyperparameter robustness），而移除ELRA后性能显著下降（出现catastrophic forgetting，WGA可跌至25%左右），验证了ELRA的必要性。ASI指标从36.6提升至52.5-56.5（FairTPT/MO），ATC仅轻微下降（58.1→56.4/56.3），验证了公平性与准确性的解耦效果。

Conclusion: FairTPT是首个针对VLM的公平性感知的episodic test-time prompt tuning方法，在无需敏感属性标签、无需重训练的条件下，有效缓解子群体性能差异并保持整体准确率，同时对超参数选择具有显著的鲁棒性。该工作确立了robust TTA的基础，为实际部署中的公平性提供了可行路径。未来方向包括：扩展至online TTA设置、改进子群体级别的校准、以及开发理论指导的熵基去偏策略以避免过度去偏。局限性包括：需要审计者手动指定敏感属性（对错误指定无效）、无法处理未建模的代理变量、以及额外的推理时计算开销（FairTPT约1.03s/图，FairTPT (MO)约1.71s/图）。

Abstract: Vision-language models have displayed remarkable capabilities in multi-modal understanding and are increasingly used in critical applications where economic and practical deployment constraints prohibit re-training or fine-tuning. However, these models can also exhibit systematic biases that disproportionately affect protected demographic groups and existing approaches to addressing these biases require extensive model retraining and access to demographic attributes. There is a clear need to develop test-time adaptation (TTA) approaches that improve the fairness characteristics of pretrained models under distributional shift. In this paper, we evaluate how episodic TTA affects fairness in CLIP classification under subpopulation shifts and develop FairTPT, a novel fairness-aware episodic TTA method that jointly minimizes target marginal entropy while maximizing spurious marginal entropy through soft-prompt tuning. We find that standard episodic TTA generally exacerbates disparities between majority and minority groups, that blinding a model to spurious attributes without degrading target performance is inherently challenging, and that excessive blinding can lead to catastrophic forgetting. This model collapse can be prevented by monitoring test-time changes in target loss within the linear regime, while still achieving fairness improvements on reactive data and preserving overall performance. FairTPT outperforms all state-of-the-art episodic test-time debiasing methods and establishes a foundation for robust TTA, which is essential for achieving fairness in practice.

</details>


### [281] [Are LLM-Enhanced GNNs Privacy-Safe?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25727)
*Longzhu He,Zelang Wen,Chaozhuo Li,Sen Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Large language models (LLMs) have recently advanced graph neural networks (GNNs) by enriching node representations with semantic information, giving rise to LLM-enhanced GNNs that achieve substantial performance gains. However, their vulnerability to privacy attacks, in which adversaries infer sensitive information from model outputs, remains largely underexplored. To bridge this gap, we present a systematic evaluation of privacy risks in LLM-enhanced GNNs through a unified framework consisting of five stages: (1) dataset preparation, (2) victim model training, (3) privacy attack, (4) risk assessment, and (5) defense analysis. Specifically, we conduct experiments on six real-world text-attributed graph datasets covering diverse domains. We consider six representative privacy attack methods targeting three fundamental threats, namely link, label, and membership inference, and construct 42 victim model configurations by combining multiple LLM-based feature enhancers with representative GNN backbones. Extensive experiments show that, despite their utility improvements, LLM-enhanced GNNs consistently exhibit increased vulnerability to privacy attacks compared to shallow text representation baselines. Further analysis reveals that semantic enrichment amplifies link-, label-, and membership-related signals in the embedding space, making them more exploitable by inference attacks. Finally, we evaluate differential privacy as a defense strategy and show that, while it can partially mitigate privacy risks, it introduces significant utility degradation, highlighting a fundamental privacy-utility trade-off in LLM-enhanced graph learning. Overall, this work provides a comprehensive understanding of privacy risks in LLM-enhanced GNNs and offers practical insights for developing more secure and trustworthy graph learning systems.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
