Result: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (llm_error). Original arXiv metadata is preserved. Error: AI returned empty structured response

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently advanced graph neural networks (GNNs) by enriching node representations with semantic information, giving rise to LLM-enhanced GNNs that achieve substantial performance gains. However, their vulnerability to privacy attacks, in which adversaries infer sensitive information from model outputs, remains largely underexplored. To bridge this gap, we present a systematic evaluation of privacy risks in LLM-enhanced GNNs through a unified framework consisting of five stages: (1) dataset preparation, (2) victim model training, (3) privacy attack, (4) risk assessment, and (5) defense analysis. Specifically, we conduct experiments on six real-world text-attributed graph datasets covering diverse domains. We consider six representative privacy attack methods targeting three fundamental threats, namely link, label, and membership inference, and construct 42 victim model configurations by combining multiple LLM-based feature enhancers with representative GNN backbones. Extensive experiments show that, despite their utility improvements, LLM-enhanced GNNs consistently exhibit increased vulnerability to privacy attacks compared to shallow text representation baselines. Further analysis reveals that semantic enrichment amplifies link-, label-, and membership-related signals in the embedding space, making them more exploitable by inference attacks. Finally, we evaluate differential privacy as a defense strategy and show that, while it can partially mitigate privacy risks, it introduces significant utility degradation, highlighting a fundamental privacy-utility trade-off in LLM-enhanced graph learning. Overall, this work provides a comprehensive understanding of privacy risks in LLM-enhanced GNNs and offers practical insights for developing more secure and trustworthy graph learning systems.

</details>


### [282] [Why Does Graph Learning Fail to Fully Benefit from a Text Teacher?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25741)
*Fumiaki Kimino,Ryoma Sato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了将文本教师（如MPNet语言模型）通过GLEM框架引入基于FUG的图学习时，为何无法显著提升跨域节点分类性能。作者设计FUG+GLEM-ITT模型并通过四个递进实验，识别出六个限制性因素：外部锚点的强度-安全性权衡、教师知识未直接注入GCN嵌入、表示空间目标不一致、GCN传播稀释节点文本信息、余弦对齐不保证分类有效轴、以及FUG自监督与教师对齐的力冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态图学习方法（如GLEM）将语言模型和GNN通过变分EM框架交替训练以结合文本和图结构信息，但作者在将其与支持跨域特征迁移的FUG模型结合时，发现并未带来预期的性能提升。这促使作者系统地分析文本教师信息未能有效融入图表示学习的根本原因，为后续设计提供指导。

Method: 作者提出FUG+GLEM-ITT模型，将FUG编码器作为GLEM的M-step模块，TextHead作为独立于GCN输出的外部锚点（避免模仿当前GCN表示）。M步优化FUG损失与四个余弦锚点对齐损失（TextHead、MLP-only、原始文本哈希、外部/随机文本锚点）的加权和。通过Exp2（自拷贝TextHead模仿GCN）、Exp3（原始文本哈希锚点）、Exp4（冻结MPNet教师锚点）三个递进实验，逐步增强外部锚点的"外部性"和"语义强度"，并通过消融分析各因素影响。源域为Amazon Digital Music，目标域为OpenAlex论文网络。

Result: FUG+GLEM-ITT的Full GCN Z在OpenAlex上仅取得0.7480的测试准确率，相比FUG基线的0.7459仅提升约0.21个百分点，平衡准确率甚至略有下降（0.6001 vs 0.6016）。四个递进实验（Exp1-4）的Full GCN Z性能均未显著超越基线。尽管MPNet本身在Raw MPNet列达到0.7888的高准确率（表明教师本身包含目标相关语义信息），但通过余弦对齐传递到GCN Z后性能反而下降。GCN+MPNet集成（0.7845）优于Full GCN Z，表明瓶颈在于从教师到GCN Z的传递路径而非教师质量。对齐指标（如mpnet_cos从0.0095升至0.4341）显著改善，但目标分类性能并未相应提升。

Conclusion: 本文结论是：仅引入强文本教师并通过余弦对齐将图编码器输出与教师表示对齐，并不保证图表示学习性能的提升。改进基于文本的图学习需要精心设计教师知识向图编码器的传递路径、保护GCN传播后节点特定文本信息的机制、以及使学习到的表示与目标任务决策边界对齐的目标。知识转移的成功应通过判断判别性信息是否真正到达GCN表示来衡量，而非仅依赖余弦对齐指标。这些发现为寻求利用文本改进图学习的方法提供了超越FUG+GLEM-ITT特定实现的设计指导。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) are widely used to represent complex interactions and relationships among entities. We investigate a multimodal model that combines two complementary ideas: a self-supervised method that enables a GNN encoder pretrained on one dataset to operate directly on another dataset with a different node-feature dimensionality, without rebuilding the model or realigning the data; and an alternating optimization method that updates a language-model module in an E-step and a GNN module in an M-step, rather than jointly training a large language model and a GNN end to end on a large graph. Despite expectations, the combined model did not sufficiently improve predictive performance. We identify six factors: (1) an external anchor in the E-step has a strength-safety trade-off: a weak anchor has little effect, whereas an overly strong anchor can damage the graph representation; (2) the knowledge of the E-step teacher is not injected directly into the GCN embedding Z; (3) the representation space constructed in the M-step is not optimized for the same objective as the E-step teacher space, resulting in a compromise representation for target classification; (4) GCN propagation averages a node's own textual information with information from its neighbors; (5) cosine alignment does not guarantee axes that are discriminative for classification, so stronger geometric alignment with the E-step text anchor need not sufficiently improve the target decision boundary or classification performance; and (6) the force that preserves the source-side self-supervised geometry in the M-step conflicts with the force that moves the representation toward the E-step teacher. We support these observations through a staged set of experiments that varies the influence of the E-step.

</details>


### [283] [A Constitutive Markov Physics-Informed Neural Operator (MPNO) for Autoregressive Stability in Transient Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25744)
*Wenpu Du,Peng Zhou,Yunlong Xia,Sinuo Xin,Congcong Zhang,Boyang Zhang,Yi Zhang,Wenzheng Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MPNO架构，将自回归稳定性从损失优化目标转为架构属性，通过图拉普拉斯传播算子P=I-αL̃的光谱半径约束ρ(P)≤1保证稳定性，在混凝土穿透等强不连续PDE上实现约20K参数下有界误差的长程自回归 rollout。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子(FNO/WNO/MeshGraphNets)在强不连续瞬态动力学PDE的自回归 rollout 中存在不稳定问题：WNO发散至无穷，MeshGraphNets塌缩至零预测，FNO虽稳定但无结构性保证。稳定性若仅作为损失目标进行优化，无法提供可靠的结构性保证。

Method: 1) 网格-图拓扑映射（六面邻接、节点状态为Cauchy应力+坐标+速度）；2) 物理耦合边权w_ij = T_ij·A_contact·|t_i|·exp(MLP)，其中T_ij为声阻抗调和均值（零可学习参数的物理骨架）；3) 图拉普拉斯L=D-W，归一化L̃=L/λ_max，λ_max由Rayleigh商幂迭代在线估计（O(E)代价）；4) Markov传播算子P=I-αL̃，通过非负对称W保证ρ(P)≤1（α∈[0,1]）；5) R=8轮图传播+APPNP残差连接；6) 硬掩码MSE损失（仅在活跃节点τ=0.01上计算）；7) 边权公式跨场景可迁移（Z→K→均匀权重）。

Result: 在混凝土穿透（100/135/165 m/s，400样本）、Burgers、Darcy三个PDE上验证：MPNO约20K参数下单步Rel-L2=0.7304±0.0008（优于WNO 0.7422，约为FNO 79K参数的1/4）；29步自回归 rollout 在所有测试种子上有界稳定（误差0.85→0.98）；谱外推应力测试（6×/8×/10×λ_max膨胀）下MPNO输出不变（~10⁻⁶偏差），而no_spec的发散种子单调增加（0/6→2/6→3/6→4/6）；Burgers上30步无崩溃；PDEBench 2D欧拉激波管OOD测试中91步全程稳定（FNO第57步发散，WNO第4步发散）；CPU推理~2.4 ms/步，29步约0.1 s，相比LS-DYNA约10⁵倍加速。

Conclusion: 自回归稳定性不能可靠地作为损失函数的优化目标，必须明确构造为架构的光谱属性。MPNO通过将物理先验嵌入边权构造并将谱半径约束ρ(P)≤1写入传播算子架构，实现了与训练轨迹无关的构造性稳定性保证。该方法适用于具有自然图拓扑、受局部守恒律支配且可近似为Markov过程的系统。

Abstract: Neural operators applied to transient-dynamics PDEs with strong discontinuities exhibit autoregressive instability: in concrete-penetration stress-field prediction, the wavelet neural operator (WNO) diverges in autoregressive rollout, while MeshGraphNets collapse to zero predictions. WNO's instability stems from the lack of a structural constraint on the spectral radius of its propagation operator; the Fourier neural operator (FNO) is stable in these measurements but only emergently, not by construction. We propose a constitutive Markov physics-informed neural operator (MPNO) modeling one-step evolution as a Markov (row-stochastic) propagation operator. Physics-coupled edge weights (acoustic-impedance harmonic mean, contact area, and traction amplitude) encode material-interface constitutive information into a nonnegative symmetric adjacency matrix W; after normalizing the graph Laplacian L = D - W by lambda_max, the propagator P = I - alpha*L~ is constructively constrained to spectral radius rho(P) <= 1, suppressing exponential amplification of autoregressive errors. Stability is thus a designable architectural property, not an optimized loss objective. On three PDEs (Burgers and two-dimensional transverse-section concrete penetration), MPNO rolls out stably with bounded error on all test seeds at 100/135/165 m/s; the single-step relative L2 error is 0.7304 +/- 0.0008, better than WNO and comparable to FNO at about one quarter of FNO's parameters. The edge-weight formula transfers across scenarios by replacing material-property variables. With about 20K parameters, MPNO delivers roughly 10^5x inference speedup over LS-DYNA.

</details>


### [284] [Comparing Corrupted Constrained Learning Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25745)
*Laura Iacovissi,Rabanus Derr,Robert C. Williamson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了经典数据处理不等式（DPI）在约束学习场景下的失效现象，并提出了基于"超预测集"的广义DPI，揭示了损失函数与模型类对不等式成立的共同约束条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典数据处理不等式（DPI）表明统计实验的随机化只会增加Bayes风险。然而，机器学习中的学习问题本质上是受约束的——模型类仅包含受限函数族，这导致经典DPI假设不再成立。因此需要研究：约束学习场景下随机化后的实验是否可能具有更低的约束Bayes风险？

Method: 1. 构造一个简单的反例，证明在受限假设类下随机化实验的约束Bayes风险可以低于原始实验，违反经典DPI；2. 提出广义DPI：要求联合分布上的约束Bayes风险（关于特定损失和模型类）下界随机化后分布的约束Bayes风险；3. 引入"超预测集"（superprediction set）概念，证明广义DPI等价于该函数集的集合包含条件；4. 推导该集合包含的充分条件。

Result: 1. 通过反例证明经典DPI在约束学习下失效；2. 证明广义DPI与超预测集的集合包含条件等价；3. 给出该集合包含成立的充分条件；4. 揭示了信息瓶颈等应用需要重新审视其理论假设。

Conclusion: 约束学习场景下需要使用广义数据处理不等式代替经典DPI，超预测集的集合包含条件为判断特定损失-模型类组合下不等式是否成立提供了判据，这对信息瓶颈原理及特征学习方法的理论基础具有重要意义。

Abstract: A key result in statistics is the data processing inequality, originally proved by Blackwell (1951) and later refined by DeGroot (1962) in terms of statistical uncertainty. It states that the Bayes risk of a statistical experiment obtained by stochastically modifying another experiment cannot be lower than the Bayes risk of the original experiment, regardless of the loss function or prior chosen. In machine learning, this result underlies applications such as the information bottleneck principle and some feature learning techniques. However, machine learning problems are constrained learning problems: the model class used does not include all measurable functions. We present a simple counterexample showing that the classical data processing inequality fails to hold in such a setting. Hence, we formulate a generalized data processing inequality, requiring the constrained Bayes risk of a joint distribution (with respect to a loss function and a constrained hypothesis class) to lower bound the constrained Bayes risk on the stochastically modified distribution, regardless of the choice of distribution. We show this inequality to be equivalent to a set containment condition on a specific function set induced by the loss and model class, called the superprediction set. Finally, we derive sufficient conditions for this containment.

</details>


### [285] [Learning from waste: Machine Learning for health risk prediction and computer vision-based sorting in Ghana](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25759)
*Hilda Adwubi Osei,Catherine Tenewaa Osei,Desdemona Yaa Asobayire*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以加纳阿通苏社区2022年实地调查为基础，构建两个数据驱动模型：随机森林分类器用于根据处置方式预测疾病类别，MobileNetV2图像分类器用于自动化垃圾分类。前者证实处置方式对疾病类型具有真实但有限的预测信号，后者证明视觉分类可替代原方案中的多传感器机械分拣系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2022年阿通苏的实地调查仅通过描述性分析报告了居民对废物处置方式与疾病模式之间关系的感知，缺少定量验证；同时原研究提出的多传感器机械分拣方案在资源受限社区中部署成本过高。本文旨在通过机器学习方法对这两方面进行量化扩展。

Method: （1）基于KoboToolbox在阿通苏八个分区收集的470份问卷数据，经清洗后采用80/20分层划分，使用逻辑回归、决策树、随机森林三种候选分类器，通过5折分层交叉验证与网格搜索调参，最终选取随机森林（N_estimators=100，max_depth=5，min_samples_leaf=10）；（2）采用TrashNet数据集（约2500张图像、6类）进行MobileNetV2迁移学习与微调，按70/15/15划分训练/验证/测试集，并通过特征重要性分析与混淆矩阵进行解释与误差分析。

Result: （1）随机森林模型在完整测试集（N=94）上整体准确率0.72、宏F1=0.70；在报告患病的子集（N=69，四类疾病）上准确率0.62、宏F1=0.63；特征重要性显示处置方式指标变量是疾病类型的主要实质预测因子，重要性超过所有人口学特征；（2）MobileNetV2微调后在测试集（N=415）上准确率88.2%、宏F1=0.87，其中trash类表现最弱（F1=0.77），glass-metal、paper-cardboard之间存在对称性混淆。

Conclusion: 研究为阿通苏社区报告的处置—疾病关系提供了部分但真实的量化支持，并证明视觉分类是原多传感器方案的可行且更低成本的替代方案；但两者均存在局限：调查数据为横截面设计且疾病类别为自报，图像模型存在TrashNet受控条件与真实场景之间的领域差距；最终强调仅靠模型性能无法自动转化为公共卫生改善，仍需机构支持、资金与执行配套。

Abstract: The inappropriate disposal of solid waste remains a significant public health and environmental concern worldwide, including in Ghana. Poor sanitation and improper waste management practices contribute to substantial economic costs and avoidable deaths annually. In 2022, a field study in Atonsu, Kumasi, Ghana, reported a community-perceived relationship between household waste disposal and illness patterns, but only through descriptive analysis without quantitative validation. This study extends that investigation using two data-driven approaches. First, a Random Forest classifier was developed to predict illness categories using waste disposal practices and demographic survey data. On a held-out group of respondents who reported illness (N=69), the model obtained a macro F1 score of 0.63, with disposal method emerging as the most important substantive predictor of illness type. Second, a MobileNetV2 image classification model enabled automated waste sorting via visual recognition, achieving 88.2% accuracy and a macro F1 score of 0.87 on the test set (N=415). The vision-based approach offers an affordable, camera-driven alternative to complex multi-sensor systems, making it highly suitable for resource-constrained settings. Taken together, the findings provide quantitative evidence for a community health relationship previously documented only qualitatively. They demonstrate the potential for automated waste-sorting in low-resource environments. Importantly, the results illustrate that technological performance alone does not guarantee public health improvements; effective institutional support and implementation are equally necessary.

</details>


### [286] [Drift-Aware Multimodal User Representation Learning via Multi-Scale Temporal Modeling and Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25773)
*Ziqing Qian,Haohang Chen,Shengqi Dang,Yuhan Xiong,Canyu Shen,Jiaying Lei,Nan Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DUMoE框架，通过多尺度时间建模（静态/短期/长期）与稀疏MoE兴趣适配器，联合建模多模态信号、兴趣漂移与多兴趣结构，并贡献大规模时序多模态数据集，在兴趣分类与交互预测任务上全面优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户偏好具有兴趣漂移特性，存在多尺度时间模式与共存的多重兴趣，但现有方法存在三大局限：多模态方法忽略时间动态、序列模型未充分利用多模态信号、兴趣解耦方法未联合考虑时间演化与异构内容。亟需一个统一框架同时建模多模态、多尺度时间动态与多兴趣结构。

Method: 1) 时序动态感知骨干网络：使用预训练UNITE多模态编码器编码文本/图像，MLP处理静态profile，LSTM建模短期行为，Transformer建模长期依赖，三者融合为统一表示。2) 稀疏MoE兴趣适配器：K个残差MLP专家各自建模特定兴趣子空间，门控网络通过top-K'稀疏路由动态聚合相关专家。3) 三阶段训练策略：骨干预训练→专家特化（冻结骨干，按兴趣标签训练专家）→门控优化（冻结骨干与专家，仅训练门控）。损失函数为兴趣分类focal loss与交互预测BCE loss的加权和。

Result: 在自建的X大规模多模态数据集（14K+用户、7.7M推文、2.9M图像、15个兴趣领域）上：兴趣分类任务Hit@1达0.872（NDCG@3=0.940，KL=0.323），较最强基线MIND提升约6.2%；交互预测任务Recall达0.926、F1=0.888，优于所有基线。消融实验验证了多尺度骨干、MoE适配器、稀疏门控与三阶段训练的必要性。

Conclusion: DUMoE通过统一框架有效解决了用户兴趣漂移问题，联合建模多模态信息、多尺度时间动态与多兴趣结构，在兴趣分类与交互预测任务上均显著优于现有SOTA方法。未来工作方向包括：减少对GPT-5.1伪标签的依赖、自适应时间窗口选择、动态专家数量分配以及在线持续学习机制。

Abstract: Understanding user preferences from noisy and temporally evolving social media behaviors is fundamentally challenging due to interest drift, where user preferences shift across time and exhibit both multi-scale temporal patterns and diverse co-existing interests. To address this, we propose DUMoE, a unified framework for drift-aware multimodal user representation learning. Our model consists of (i) a temporal dynamics-aware backbone that captures and integrates static profiles, short-term behavioral signals, and long-term dependencies into a coherent representation, and (ii) a sparse mixture-of-experts (MoE) interest adapter that disentangles multiple latent interests via expert specialization and adaptive routing. Each expert models a distinct interest subspace, while a gating network dynamically selects and aggregates a sparse subset of relevant experts for each user. To enable stable and effective optimization, we further introduce a three-stage training strategy that decouples backbone learning, expert specialization, and gating optimization. Extensive experiments on real-world social media datasets show that DUMoE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods on both user interest prediction and interaction prediction tasks.

</details>


### [287] [EXAONE Tabular 1.0 : Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25774)
*Moonjung Eo,Min-Kook Suh,Hye-Seung Cho,Jiwon Kim,Seoyoon Kim,Sangjun Nam,Soonyoung Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EXAONE Tabular 是 LG AI Research 发布的紧凑型表格基础模型家族，通过上下文学习实现分类与回归任务，无需针对特定数据集进行梯度更新。其核心贡献是提出 Cross-Axis Summary Transformer (CAST) 架构，在每一层 Transformer 中交替进行特征轴注意力与支持集条件化的样本轴注意力，并通过 item-summary 与 feature-summary token 进行信息交互。模型仅基于合成结构因果模型（SCM）先验进行预训练，分类模型约 20.81M 参数，回归模型约 21.11M 参数。在 TabArena、BCCO、TALENT、ScoringBench 四个公开基准上均取得领先或接近领先的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统表格预测方法（如 XGBoost、LightGBM、CatBoost）虽长期占主导地位，但需要针对每个数据集单独训练，难以快速适应大量新任务。表格基础模型通过上下文学习范式实现"一次预训练、跨任务复用"，消除了逐数据集训练的成本。本文旨在构建一个兼具高性能与高效率的紧凑型表格基础模型，以架构创新为核心重新设计表格上下文学习。

Method: 1. **CAST 架构**：在每个 Transformer 层内，先在每个样本内进行两次特征轴自注意力，再在每个特征上进行支持集条件化的样本轴注意力；通过 item-summary token（沿特征轴拼接）和 feature-summary token（沿样本轴拼接）作为跨轴信息桥梁，全层保留 cell-level 表示。2. **合成 SCM 先验**：五阶段生成流程——采样任务超参数→构建 DAG 与节点噪声→拓扑序计算 SCM→选择观测特征与目标→后处理与过滤，涵盖因果、分类、非线性、噪声与缺失值注入。3. **预测头**：分类采用两层 MLP（最多 10 类），超 10 类通过 ECOC 分解扩展；回归直接预测 999 个分位数，点预测取中心 99.8% 分位数函数的梯形均值。4. **训练**：分类与回归分别训练，使用 Muon 优化矩阵参数、AdamW 优化其余参数，WSD 学习率调度，EMA 权重 (γ=0.999)。5. **推理**：支持测试时集成（默认 8 个估计器）、查询分块、缓存/非缓存执行、FlashAttention/Memory-Efficient Attention 自适应调度。

Result: **TabArena**：分类排名第一（Elo 最高），以单次默认配置超越调优集成与 4 小时 AutoML 管线；回归达到 1.64B 参数 TabFM 的性能水平，推理成本仅为其约 1/11。**BCCO**（106 分类 / 50 回归数据集）：分类以 0.792 均值准确率排名第二（TabFM 0.799），回归以 R²=0.799 排名第一。**TALENT**（188 分类 / 100 回归数据集）：分类以 0.857 排名第二，回归以 R²=0.736 排名第一。**ScoringBench**（102 回归数据集）：R²、RMSE、CRPS 三项指标均获最佳平均排名，在点估计与预测分布质量上同时领先。配对胜率分析显示 EXAONE Tabular 对除 TabFM 外所有基线均赢得多数评估划分（对其他表格基础模型 65–92%，对调优集成 GBDT/神经网络 ≥89%）。

Conclusion: EXAONE Tabular 确立了紧凑型表格基础模型家族的最先进水平，在分类、点回归与概率回归任务上均展现出强大且一致的预测能力，同时具备高效的模型设计。其 CAST 架构通过全层保留 cell-level 表示并交替进行跨轴注意力，有效统一了特征处理与上下文学习阶段，避免了传统多阶段架构中过早压缩为行级表示的瓶颈。模型仅约 20–21M 参数即能在多个独立基准上逼近或超越参数量大数十倍的模型，展示了架构创新相对于参数规模的显著优势。

Abstract: EXAONE Tabular is a compact tabular foundation model family for classification and regression via in-context learning, producing predictions without dataset-specific gradient updates. Pretrained exclusively on a synthetic structural-causal-model (SCM) prior, its central contribution is an architecture-centered redesign of tabular in-context learning. Rather than compressing features into a fixed row embedding before a separate row-level learner, EXAONE Tabular interleaves feature-axis attention within each item with support-conditioned item-axis attention within each feature at every Transformer layer, mediated by item-summary and feature-summary tokens. Across four public benchmarks, EXAONE Tabular combines strong predictive performance with high efficiency. On TabArena, its 20.81M-parameter classification model ranks first overall, surpassing tuned ensembles and 4-hour AutoML pipelines, while regression reaches the performance regime of the 1.64B-parameter TabFM at roughly 1/11 the inference cost. On BCCO and TALENT, EXAONE Tabular ranks second in classification and first in regression. On ScoringBench, it achieves the best mean rank for both point-estimation and predictive-distribution quality, leading the $R^2$, RMSE, and CRPS evaluations. Together, these results establish EXAONE Tabular as a state-of-the-art compact tabular foundation model family, combining strong predictive performance across classification, point regression, and probabilistic regression with an efficient model design.

</details>


### [288] [Geometry-Constrained Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks: Learning Edge Geometry via Banach Duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25807)
*K S Sesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"几何约束Kolmogorov-Arnold网络"(Geometry-Constrained KANs)，将边激活函数重新诠释为凸势的梯度（即Banach对偶映射），通过学习每条边的标量指数p>1来自适应函数空间几何，而非依赖固定的基函数（如样条、切比雪夫多项式等）。在50个符号回归目标上，几何约束KAN在干净数据、小样本和带噪声条件下均匹配或超越固定基线，学得的指数可提供关于目标结构的可解释信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KAN变体在训练前就通过固定基函数（样条、多项式、傅里叶特征等）隐式施加了函数空间几何，这种几何先验与数据失配时会导致模型表现不佳（如图1展示的不连续目标拟合失败）。本文认为关键建模选择不是基函数本身，而是其诱导的几何；应当让几何本身从数据中学习。

Method: 方法核心是将边激活函数视为凸势Ψ_p(z)=|z|^p/p的梯度J_p(z)=sign(z)(|z|+ε)^(p-1)，其中指数p>1为可学习参数（通过p=1+exp(θ)无约束优化）。提出三个架构：(1) Tanh-KAN（Orlicz对偶，4参数/边）；(2) ℓ^p-KAN（幂律对偶，5参数/边）；(3) Banach-KAN（两者组合，8参数/边）。实现中采用处处C^1的正则化对偶映射z(z²+ε²)^((p-2)/2)以保证数值稳定性。理论分析导出命题1：在前馈网络中χ₁有限当且仅当p>3/2（可训练深度条件）。

Result: 在50个符号回归目标（40个AI Feynman + 10个合成压力测试）上：干净数据中位NRMSE方面Banach-KAN达0.030，与切比雪夫并列并优于样条；在Core 18子集上Banach平均秩2.00最佳，全集上2.32与最强样条统计持平。噪声鲁棒性最强：σ从0到1时，ℓ^p-KAN仅退化3.7×（低于交叉验证样条的≈11×），未正则化样条退化21.6×；Banach-KAN退化8.8×。小样本场景（n=50）Banach-KAN获得最多胜场（8/18）。在12个PMLB表格数据集上几何约束族赢得10/12。学得的p值在不同方程族和输入维度间呈现一致的、目标依赖的几何排序（如对数、双曲、三角类方程推动p<2），但信号为初始化锚定的相对信号而非绝对阈值。

Conclusion: 本文将KAN边激活函数统一为凸势梯度的几何构造，将现有激活视为特例，并将指数p提升为可学习参数，使几何本身成为自适应自由度。结论的核心设计原则是：在可比每边参数预算、测量噪声和小样本条件下，诱导的几何（而非原始表达能力）决定了学习效果，学得几何优于通用基函数和通用参数化激活。主要限制是每边形式存在容量上限，固定基KAN随n增大逐渐追上；未来工作包括组合几何约束边与小可学习基成分、扩展至高维模态，以及通过变化范数空间表征KAN与MLP的函数类差异。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) replace fixed activations in deep architectures with learnable univariate edge functions, making the choice of edge parametrisation central. Existing variants rely on fixed bases such as splines, polynomials, or Fourier features, which impose a function-space geometry before data are observed. We introduce geometry-constrained KANs, a family of edge activations derived from Banach duality maps in which the geometry itself is learned through a scalar exponent $p > 1$ per edge. This exponent controls the qualitative response: sub-Euclidean values produce sharp, threshold-like behaviour reminiscent of the $\ell_1$ (LASSO) geometry, $p = 2$ recovers the linear regime, and larger values produce flatter responses near the origin. Across 50 symbolic-regression targets ($40$ from the AI Feynman benchmark plus $10$ synthetic stress tests), geometry-constrained KANs match or beat every fixed-basis baseline on median NRMSE (Banach-KAN $0.030$, tying Chebyshev and improving on splines); on average rank Banach-KAN is best on the $18$-equation core ($2.00$) and statistically tied with the strongest spline on the full benchmark ($2.32$ vs. $2.34$). The clearest gains appear under measurement noise: as $\sigma$ grows from $0$ to $1$, $\ell^p$-KAN degrades only $3.7\times$ -- below even a cross-validated spline ($\approx 11\times$) -- while an unregularised spline degrades $21.6\times$; Banach-KAN degrades $8.8\times$, comparable to a tuned spline but far more stable than the unregularised one. Banach-KAN also takes the most per-equation wins in the small-sample regime, with fixed-basis models catching up only as the training set grows. Learned exponents provide an interpretable, relative signal: at a fixed initialisation they reveal a consistent, target-dependent geometric ordering across equation families and input dimensions.

</details>


### [289] [Canalization Before Generalization: Grokking as a Dynamical Probe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25813)
*Yiming Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于权重衰减脉冲（WD pulse）的干预框架，利用Grokking现象中"训练拟合"与"可见泛化"之间的时间分离，将预泛化平台期作为观察函数选择过程的动力学探针。在三个算法Grokking任务中，发现WD脉冲对泛化时间的响应在平台期早期是无序的，随后在可见泛化之前自发形成稳定的剂量依赖结构（WD增强→提前泛化，WD减弱→延迟泛化），同时扰动分支与基线之间的测试损失壁垒趋于零。作者将此现象称为"函数选择的自化（canalization of function selection）"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过参数化神经网络中，训练数据可被多种不同的解拟合，优化的隐式偏置决定了最终选择哪个解。Grokking现象将训练拟合与可见泛化在时间上分离，为独立研究函数选择过程提供了独特窗口。理解这一隐式偏置何时以及如何发展，对理解深度学习泛化机制具有核心意义。

Method: 1. 任务设置：在三个算法Grokking任务（同余匹配、稀疏奇偶性、模块化加法）上训练神经网络。2. 干预协议：在预泛化平台期沿训练时间轴滑动固定时长（τ=500 epoch）的权重衰减脉冲窗口，改变λ₀为λ₀+Δλ，之后恢复基线值继续训练。扫描脉冲起始时间t₀与扰动幅度Δλ（±0.05至±0.25，共10个剂量），形成WD响应图。3. 测量指标：泛化时间偏移ΔTα、剂量响应线性度R²_corr、测试损失壁垒（线性模式连通性分析）、PCA投影中的更新方向轨迹。4. 基线筛选：仅保留具有清晰长平台期和锐利泛化转变的run。

Result: 1. WD响应图显示预泛化平台期存在动力学重组：早期响应无序，中期（约t₀≈5000-5500）自发形成稳定的剂量依赖结构。2. 该结构在基线测试准确率仍处于随机水平时即已出现，远早于可见泛化（约提前4000 epoch）。3. 剂量响应线性度R²_corr随训练相位单调上升并趋近1，30个独立初始化下均复现。4. 基线权重范数在平台期几乎不变，与R²_corr变化无同步性（Spearman ρ_Δ=-0.03），排除权重范数解释。5. 测试损失壁垒随脉冲施加时间推后而趋于零，表明扰动越来越难以将模型推向与基线线性分离的泛化解。6. 泛化时间的有序响应持续存在，即使壁垒已接近零。7. PCA更新方向投影显示，排序出现后扰动分支与基线沿共享轨迹运行，仅时间上存在偏移。8. 现有进度度量（受限损失、参数移动、特征放大）在Task 1平台期几乎不提供泛化前的先导信号。

Conclusion: 作者将此现象命名为"函数选择的自化（canalization of function selection）"：随着训练进行，局部扰动越来越难以在解空间中改变最终泛化函数的选择方向（侧向自由度收窄），但对沿该方向到达泛化的时间仍可系统性地加速或延迟（时间维度保持可调）。这借用Waddington发育生物学中的"渠化"概念，类比表观遗传景观中山谷变深变窄时扰动难以改变发育方向但仍可改变发育速度的现象。该框架揭示了Grokking中一种先前未被刻画的预泛化动力学重组，并隐含与有限步优化作为热力学不可逆过程（偏好低涨落、低耗散轨迹）之间的概念关联。当前实验限于三个小规模算法任务，推广至复杂任务需要更一般的泛化转变定义方法。

Abstract: For overparameterized neural networks, many solutions can fit the training data equally well while behaving very differently on unseen samples. Grokking separates training fit from visible generalization, providing a window for studying how this selection develops during training. We sweep short, fixed-duration weight-decay (WD) pulses across this plateau and measure how they shift later generalization time. Across three grokking tasks, these shifts are unordered early in the plateau but later form a stable dose ordering, with stronger WD increases leading to earlier generalization and stronger WD decreases leading to later generalization. This ordering emerges before visible generalization in all three tasks. Meanwhile, test-loss barriers between perturbed and baseline generalization checkpoints collapse toward zero while the ordered timing effects persist. We call this combination of increasingly constrained solution selection and persistent dose-ordered timing sensitivity the canalization of function selection.

</details>


### [290] [Learning Continuous Regional Temperature Fields with Lead-Time and Resolution Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25823)
*Chunlei Shi,Jiong Wang,Yi-Lin Wei,Junming Hou,Jinjin Liu,Yecheng Zhang,Dan Niu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CSTF（连续时空温度预报器），将区域2米气温（T2M）预报重新构建为查询条件下的连续时空场评估问题，使得预报时效和输出网格分辨率成为模型的显式查询变量，而非固定输出维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习区域天气预报器通常只能在预设网格上输出固定数量的未来帧，无法满足用户对任意预报时效（如非整点）或不同显示分辨率的查询需求；自回归滚动和事后重采样均无法保证不同分辨率产品描述同一温度状态的一致性。

Method: 1）多尺度气象编码器将9通道ERA5历史场编码为共享潜气象状态Z；2）坐标解码器以预报时效τ（正弦编码+MLP）、空间坐标p和采样密度r=(1/Ho,1/Wo)为查询条件，直接解码任意网格任意时效的T2M场；3）训练目标联合监督损失、空间梯度损失（保持局地热力对比）、时序差分损失（保持短时演变平滑）和尺度一致性损失（不同分辨率查询对同一气象状态保持一致）；4）在东南亚中国区域（128×128，0-6h ERA5-Land）基准上训练与评估，并附加全球尺度诊断可视化。

Result: 在东南亚中国基准上，CSTF在t+3h至t+6h取得最佳分时技巧；聚合指标RMSE=1.123℃、MAE=0.811℃、Bias=0.273（较次优基线ARROW偏差降低17.0%）、空间相关系数0.993；连续时效查询（0.5/2/4.5/6h）和分辨率可控查询（96²/128²/160²/192²）均能保持结构一致性；消融实验验证了空间梯度、时序差分和尺度一致性损失各自对技巧和跨分辨率一致性的贡献。

Conclusion: CSTF通过将预报时效和输出分辨率提升为查询变量而非架构约束，使单一训练模型同时支持标准小时预报、任意中间时效诊断和多分辨率输出一致性推理，在区域T2M预报中兼顾确定性技巧与查询灵活性，为下一代可交互、按需的气象预报产品提供了可行框架。

Abstract: Accurate regional near-surface temperature forecasting is fundamental to short-range weather services and downstream risk assessment. Existing deep learning-based regional forecasters commonly produce a fixed set of future frames on a prescribed grid, limiting their use when forecast products must be evaluated at query-dependent lead times or display resolutions. To overcome these fixed-output constraints, we formulate regional T2M forecasting as query-conditioned continuous spatiotemporal temperature field evaluation and propose the Continuous Spatiotemporal Temperature Forecaster (CSTF), a neural field that turns forecast lead time and output resolution into explicit queries when evaluating 2-m temperature (T2M). Specifically, CSTF first encodes multivariable ERA5 histories into latent meteorological states and then decodes T2M as a coordinate-based field. Accordingly, spatial location, forecast lead time, and output resolution are introduced as queries, enabling standard hourly forecasts, intermediate lead-time diagnostics, and resolution-controllable outputs within a unified field-evaluation framework. Furthermore, to maintain coherence across flexible field queries, we design spatial-gradient, temporal-difference, and scale-consistency objectives that regularize regional thermal structures, lead-wise evolution, and cross-resolution agreement. Experiments on the Southeast China 0-6 h ERA5-Land benchmark demonstrate that CSTF achieves the best aggregate deterministic skill, including a 17.0 percent reduction in Bias, with global-scope diagnostics further illustrating flexible lead-time and resolution-controllable inference.

</details>


### [291] [CEDAR: Controlled and Event-Driven Demand Forecasting via Residual Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25871)
*Junjie Meng,Ranxu Zhang,Zi-an Zhang,Shujun Liu,Xiaoning Qi,Xiaozhou Xu,Yanyong Zhang,Hui Xiong,Chao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CEDAR 框架，将电商销量预测从被动外推重构为决策条件下的仿真问题，通过 Action-Interleaved Transformer 与基于 LLM 事件信号的残差修正模块，在阿里巴巴 1688 大规模真实数据上显著优于主流 TSF 基线，并经线上 A/B 测试验证带来 LTV 和 ROI 的实际提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 TSF 方法本质是被动预测，将商家动作作为协变量融合，仅拟合历史策略下的相关性，导致自回归惯性、内外生动力学混淆、对策略不敏感，难以支撑 what-if 预算规划与反事实分析。

Method: 两阶段框架 CEDAR：Stage I 的 Action-Interleaved Transformer 将状态与动作作为同等级 token，按 s_{t-1}→a_t→s_t 的因果顺序交错输入 Transformer，显式学习动作条件状态转移算子；Stage II 的残差修正模块利用 Qwen-Plus 提取并合成热点标签与节假日事件生成语义文本，再由 BGE-zh-v1.5 编码为 hotspot embedding，与商品状态嵌入通过 cross-attention 预测残差修正项。采用解耦的两阶段训练以隔离可控动力学与非平稳外生冲击。

Result: 在阿里 1688 E-Comm 15-Week 数据集（~3200 万商品轨迹）上，next-5 设置下 MSE=0.182、NMSE=0.083，相对 PatchTST/PETFormer 提升超 56%；next-10 下 MSE=0.414；长程 next-25 下 MSE=1.612，明显优于 PatchTST(2.847) 与 PETFormer(2.561)；消融实验证实 AIT 预测、商品元数据、近期状态、节假日/新闻关键词及两阶段训练均关键；Kaggle Store Sales 公开数据集上同样取得 MSE 从 0.6321 降至 0.5819；线上 A/B 测试（2026 年 1 月，239 商家）显示商家 LTV 提升 13%、店铺 ROI 提升 15%。

Conclusion: CEDAR 通过将动作作为一等模态并解耦外生冲击，实现了在强干预环境下稳定的多步反事实 rollout；大规模离线与在线实验共同验证其在仿真精度与实际预算规划中的有效性与可部署性，作者团队计划发布部分匿名化的工业数据集以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Forecasting in large-scale e-commerce marketplaces is increasingly required to support planning: merchants need to evaluate sales outcomes under future action sequences such as budget schedules, rather than passively predicting what happens next. However, most existing time series forecasting (TSF) approaches remain inherently passive. Even when incorporating operational decisions as auxiliary covariates, they typically optimize for correlation-based extrapolation under historical policies. This design suffers from autoregressive inertia and conflates endogenous market evolution with decision-induced transitions, leading to policy-insensitive rollouts and unreliable counterfactual analysis. To bridge this gap, we propose CEDAR (Controlled and Event-Driven Demand forecasting via Action-aware Residual decomposition), a two-stage framework for robust decision-conditioned simulation. In Stage I, an Action-Interleaved Transformer learns controllable action-conditioned state transitions for rollout under planned interventions. In Stage II, a Residual Correction Module leverages external event signals and LLM-assisted text representations to align noisy event descriptions with product context and correct event-driven deviations. Our study is enabled by a large-scale real-world dataset from Alibaba 1688, comprising approximately 32 million product trajectories with paired state-action sequences and aligned event signals. Extensive offline experiments and online controlled experiments in production demonstrate that CEDAR consistently improves simulation accuracy over strong TSF baselines and delivers practical gains for real-world budget planning.

</details>


### [292] [How Edge of Stability Hinders SCAFFOLD in Federated Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25873)
*Anant Khandelwal,Michael Crawshaw,Mingrui Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过大量实证研究揭示了在联邦学习深度网络中SCAFFOLD算法表现不如FedAvg的机制：边缘稳定性（EoS）和渐进锐化现象导致SCAFFOLD无法准确估计全局梯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理论上SCAFFOLD通过控制变量消除数据异质性的影响，具有独立于异质性的收敛保证，但在深度学习实践中却常常不如简单的FedAvg。已有研究（如Yu et al. 2022）将此归因于深度网络的非凸性，但缺乏具体机制解释。本文旨在探究这一理论-实践差距的根本原因。

Method: 在CIFAR-10、MNIST、FashionMNIST上进行图像分类实验，使用3-7层CNN和MLP，采用全批量梯度（遵循EoS研究惯例）。定义全局锐度和局部锐度（局部Hessian最大特征值的均值）来衡量优化轨迹上的锐度变化；引入新指标"更新偏差"（update misalignment）衡量算法更新方向与真实全局梯度之间的误差。通过去趋势相关分析（detrended correlation）研究更新偏差与锐度的关联。实验在8块NVIDIA A100 GPU上进行。

Result: 1. FedAvg和SCAFFOLD在异质性数据下均表现出渐进锐化和EoS现象，在多种架构和学习率下均成立。2. 平衡锐度与学习率η成反比（与GD相同），受数据异质性程度影响较小，且几乎不受本地步数K影响——这意味着SCAFFOLD理论要求的锐度上界O(1/(ηK))随K增大而趋于零，但实际锐度并不减小，理论条件无法满足。3. 在EoS下，SCAFFOLD的更新偏差与锐度高度相关（去趋势相关系数≥0.54），而FedAvg的更新偏差与锐度几乎无关。4. 在MNIST等不发生EoS的简单任务上，SCAFFOLD显著优于FedAvg；在CIFAR-10等发生EoS的任务上，FedAvg略优于SCAFFOLD。这说明失败的原因不是非凸性，而是高锐度导致的全局梯度估计失准。

Conclusion: 本文提出SCAFFOLD在深度学习中表现不佳的机制：渐进锐化和EoS导致高锐度环境，SCAFFOLD依赖历史梯度构造控制变量的前提（基于L-光滑性）不再成立，无法可靠估计全局梯度。相比之下，FedAvg的更新不受锐度影响，因此在EoS下反而更有效。这一发现解释了SCAFFOLD的理论-实践差距，并将原因从"深度网络的非凸性"重新定位为"深度学习优化特有的高锐度动态"。研究局限在于仅使用全批量梯度和小型CNN架构，未能给出平衡锐度的封闭形式表达式。

Abstract: In federated learning, it is well known that heterogeneous data can (in theory) slow down optimization, and much effort has been directed at designing optimization algorithms that are unaffected by data heterogeneity, such as the SCAFFOLD algorithm. Yet, despite strong theoretical guarantees, SCAFFOLD does not usually outperform the much simpler FedAvg in practice. In this work, we propose that this gap is due to the presence of Edge of Stability (EoS) and progressive sharpening in federated optimization, supported by extensive empirical probing. First, we find that EoS-like dynamics occur with both FedAvg and SCAFFOLD under a variety of architectures and hyperparameters. We observe that the equilibrium value of the sharpness is inversely proportional to the learning rate (as in GD), and interestingly, the degree of data heterogeneity (but not the number of local steps) also affects the equilibrium value. Most importantly, we observe that SCAFFOLD's ability to estimate the gradient of the global objective is severely degraded at the EoS, as measured by the correlation between sharpness and SCAFFOLD's error in estimating the global gradient along the optimization trajectory. This suggests a mechanism for SCAFFOLD's lackluster performance in deep learning: with high sharpness at the EoS, SCAFFOLD cannot reliably estimate the global gradient.

</details>


### [293] [A General-Purpose Molecular Foundation Model Transfers Across Diverse Olfactory Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25893)
*Yikun Han,Yi Wang,Neil Mankodi,Stephen Yang,Ambuj Tewari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究将通用分子基础模型Uni-Mol2在单一嗅觉任务（GS-LF多标签气味描述符预测）上微调后，其学到的分子表示能否迁移到多种下游嗅觉任务。结果表明Uni-Mol2在主要基准及四个下游任务（跨数据集描述符预测、有/无气味二分类、对映体评估、气味混合物可区分性）上均匹配或超越SOTA基线，验证了"一次训练、多任务迁移"范式在机器嗅觉中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器嗅觉受限于标注数据稀缺，进展远落后于视觉、语言等领域。传统QSOR模型多从零训练于小规模气味数据集，难以泛化。分子基础模型在其他领域已展现强大迁移能力，但其在嗅觉领域——尤其是在单一任务微调后能否跨任务迁移——尚不清楚。

Method: 选用3D分子基础模型Uni-Mol2（能捕捉分子空间几何与立体化学信息），在GS-LF基准上以Focal Loss微调以缓解标签不平衡，并通过50模型集成（10组超参×5折）获得最终预测。在四个下游任务中，前三个任务不进行额外的深度学习训练直接评估；混合物任务则使用Uni-Mol2的分子嵌入配合轻量级传统机器学习（ElasticNet、随机森林、梯度提升）进行度量学习。

Result: 1) GS-LF主任务：Uni-Mol2在Macro AUROC（0.899）、AUPRC（0.408）、F1（0.399）等所有指标上均超过POM/OpenPOM基线；2) 跨数据集（Zhang等）：在完整集与非重叠子集上一致优于OpenPOM（AUROC 0.929 vs 0.923；AUPRC 0.385 vs 0.344）；3) 有/无气味分类：AUROC和F1显著提升，但精度略降；4) 对映体分析：Uni-Mol2能为所有11对对映体产生不同预测（L₁距离均值0.36，p<0.001），而2D模型OpenPOM对所有对映体预测完全一致（L₁=0），但在34个差异标签中仅55.9%正确（接近随机水平）；5) 混合物可区分性：在四个数据集中的三个（Snitz 1/2、Ravia）以及综合指标上均最佳，仅Bushdid数据集上略逊于OpenPOM。此外，混淆标签对分析与BERT语义相似度分析表明气味描述符存在显著共现结构，暗示未来应联合学习分子与描述符的表示空间。

Conclusion: 经过化学预训练的3D分子基础模型Uni-Mol2在单一嗅觉任务上微调后，可获得跨多种机器嗅觉任务的强迁移表示，验证了"一次训练、跨任务迁移"范式。3D表示对立体化学信息敏感（2D模型根本无法区分对映体），但准确预测立体化学的感知后果仍是开放挑战。未来方向包括：探索分子基础模型中驱动嗅觉迁移的关键性质（如3D预训练、规模、预训练目标），以及将气味描述符作为可学习表示进行联合嵌入，以实现兼容度预测和零样本泛化到未见过的气味描述符。

Abstract: Foundation models have transformed molecular property prediction, yet it remains unclear whether a molecular foundation model, fine-tuned on a single canonical olfactory prediction task, can learn representations that transfer across diverse machine olfaction problems. We investigate this question by fine-tuning Uni-Mol2 on the GS-LF benchmark for multi-label odor descriptor prediction and evaluating the resulting model, without additional deep-learning training, on four complementary downstream settings: cross-dataset odor descriptor prediction, odorous-versus-odorless classification, enantiomer evaluation, and odor mixture discriminability. The fine-tuned model matches or exceeds the performance of the state-of-the-art olfaction-specific baseline on the primary GS-LF benchmark and consistently transfers across these downstream evaluations. The enantiomer analysis further shows that three-dimensional molecular representations distinguish mirror-image molecules in a way that two-dimensional graph models fundamentally cannot, although accurately predicting the perceptual consequences of stereochemistry remains an open challenge. Together, these results support a train-once, transfer-across-tasks paradigm for machine olfaction and suggest that chemically pretrained molecular representations provide a strong foundation for transferable olfactory prediction.

</details>


### [294] [Forecasting Multiple Observables with SCROLL: Score-Trained Uncertainty for Stochastic Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25898)
*Pavel Prochazka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种用于随机动力系统多任务预测的"自由路由末层信念"(free-routed last-layer beliefs)方法，通过在共享骨干网络上组合各观测量的似然概率，将任务特定的损失缩放吸收为同一梯度过程中的似然参数学习，避免了传统多任务学习中的损失平衡调参问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机动力系统的预测通常涉及多个观测量（未来状态、阈值事件、状态标签等），各有不同的似然结构。传统多任务方法需要手动或学习地平衡各任务损失，但损失缩放与具体任务单位相关，难以通用化。论文旨在解决多任务预测中损失平衡的困难，同时利用随机动力学提供可计算的预测方差真值这一独特优势。

Method: 在共享骨干网络基础上，为每个任务设置独立的末层"自由路由"信念层，将各观测量的似然概率进行组合。具体做法是将单位相关的损失缩放吸收进似然参数中，这些参数在同一次梯度反向传播中与网络权重联合学习，无需额外的损失权重调优或网格搜索。

Result: 1) 在良好设定、同方差的Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程中，学习到的预测分布恢复了分析核，正确设定的基线方法表现相当；2) 在异方差系统中（随机Lorenz-63和真实空气质量数据），所提方法的输入依赖方差有效区分了不同噪声水平：在状态和状态标签任务上取得最佳单次运行的负对数似然（NLL），校准性能仅被NLL更差的基线追平，且计算成本远低于调参网格搜索；3) 在真实序列上，状态预测优势在5个滚动起点中均保持一致。

Conclusion: 该方法在多任务随机动力系统预测中表现优异，验证了"自由路由末层信念"框架的有效性。它避免了传统多任务损失平衡的困难，同时通过共享骨干网与似然参数联合学习的方式，在同方差和异方差场景下均能获得良好的预测精度与校准性能，且计算效率显著优于网格搜索调参。

Abstract: Forecasting a stochastic dynamical system rarely means a single number: one wants several observables---future state, threshold event, regime label---each with its own likelihood. Standard multi-task recipes balance per-task losses, tuned or learned. We instead compose the observables' likelihoods in per-task free-routed last-layer beliefs on a shared backbone; this absorbs unit-dependent loss scaling into likelihood parameters learned in the same gradient pass. Stochastic dynamics supply what static benchmarks cannot: computable ground truth for the predictive variance. Results land where theory puts them: on the well-specified, homoscedastic Ornstein--Uhlenbeck process the learned predictive law recovers the analytic kernel and correctly specified baselines tie. On heteroscedastic systems (stochastic Lorenz-63, real air-quality data) the belief's input-dependent variance separates: best single-run NLL on the state and regime tasks, calibration matched only by arms whose NLL it beats, at a fraction of the tuned grids' cost. On the real series the state margin holds across five rolling origins.

</details>


### [295] [Quantum-Inspired Modeling of Driving Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25907)
*Mohammad Elayan,Omid Armantalab,Wissam Kontar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种受量子力学启发的驾驶员行为建模框架，将每位驾驶员表示为非线性特征空间中演化的密度矩阵，无需监督地恢复出自由流、过渡、拥堵三种可解释驾驶行为剖面，并复现宏观交通现象及支持自动驾驶应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶员行为异质性模型在建模前需预设变量间的交互形式，灵活性不足则牺牲可解释性，灵活性过高则丧失透明性。需要一种同时具备连续性、概率性、上下文依赖性、历史依赖性且交互形式可从数据中学习的统一表征。

Method: 将驾驶员行为变量通过随机傅里叶特征(RFF)映射到高维非线性特征空间，以外积形成观测的秩一矩阵；引入K个密度矩阵作为行为剖面，由低秩因子参数化；通过softmax根据上下文向量计算剖面混合权重；以凸组合方式融合前一状态与上下文加权剖面得到预测状态；用Born规则计算观测似然；引入观测驱动的状态更新与熵正则化，通过最小化负对数似然进行无监督训练。

Result: 在I-24 MOTION数据集上无监督恢复出三个对应自由流、过渡跟驰、拥堵走走停停的可解释剖面，其中拥堵剖面进一步分解为稳态跟驰、加速事件、制动事件三种内部模态；剖面在基本图上按密度-速度分区排列并复现滞后回线；将训练态接入IDM实现上下文自适应目标参数；3秒速度预测在平静/繁忙/波间条件下MAE分别为3.77/4.51/4.98 mph。

Conclusion: 密度矩阵表征在单一框架内统一了连续性、概率性、上下文与历史依赖性以及可学习的变量交互，克服了固定类型与预设函数形式的局限；剖面与基本图和滞后现象的对齐提供了内建的验证准则，为可解释且可信赖的驾驶员行为建模提供了新路径。

Abstract: Driver behavior is heterogeneous, context-dependent, and changes over time, and these properties shape the traffic phenomena we observe. Most models, however, fix in advance which behavioral variables interact and how. Behavior outside that form is absorbed as noise, while models flexible enough to capture it tend to lose interpretability. We introduce a quantum-inspired representation of driver behavior that combines properties usually treated separately or in part: it is continuous, probabilistic, context-dependent, history-dependent, and represents interactions among behavioral variables as learned from data. Each driver is encoded as an evolving density matrix, providing a unified representation of behavioral uncertainty, temporal evolution, and context-dependent behavioral variation. Trained without supervision on the I-24 MOTION dataset, the framework recovers three interpretable driving profiles representing three regimes: free flow, transition, and congestion. The profiles capture the behavioral range of the data and the smooth transitions drivers make between regimes as conditions change. The same representation also reproduces known macroscopic phenomena, aligning with the fundamental diagram and reproducing hysteresis loops. We also show how the representation supports practical use: it supplies context-dependent parameters to classical car-following models, and gives an autonomous vehicle a live behavioral read of the surrounding drivers with a short-horizon forecast of their motion. The framework points toward models of traffic that are interpretable and trustworthy by construction. We release an open-source toolkit on GitHub ( this https URL ) spanning data processing, training, inference, and analysis.

</details>


### [296] [When Pruning Meets Interpretability: Preserving Sparse Autoencoder Robustness in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25941)
*Suchit Gupte,Xueru Zhang,Mohammad Mahdi Khalili*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了后置剪枝对大语言模型稀疏自编码器（SAE）可解释性的影响，提出以"扰动能量"（协方差加权范数）作为统一理论框架，解释了不同剪枝方法对SAE保真度的差异，并发现中间层具有结构性脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE广泛用于解释LLM内部表征，而权重剪枝是部署LLM的标准压缩手段，但两者结合时SAE在剪枝后模型上的可靠性尚不清楚，且缺乏理论框架解释不同剪枝方法为何导致不同程度的SAE退化。组织在密集模型上训练SAE后，需要知道既有分析能否迁移到压缩后的模型，而非重新训练。

Method: 提出扰动理论框架：假设SAE具有Lipschitz连续性，将剪枝视为对激活的扰加，通过三角不等式与协方差分解推导出SAE重建误差上界，核心量化为扰动能量 ε²=tr(ΔWΣΔWᵀ)。证明三种剪枝方法对应于ε²的不同近似层次（恒等矩阵≪对角≪完整协方差）。在四个模型架构（pythia-70m、gemma-2-2b/9b、mistral-7b）上，使用SAELens预训练SAE固定权重，在25%/40%/50%稀疏度下评估SAEBench四类指标（Core、Feature Absorption、SCR、TPP）。基于中间层脆弱性发现提出分层稀疏度分配策略。

Result: 方法排序稳定且与理论一致：SparseGPT ≻ Wanda ≻ Magnitude。Magnitude剪枝使SCR退化高达60%、TPP退化高达80%，而Wanda和SparseGPT保持在基线15–25%以内。Core聚合指标严重低估损害：KL分数看似良好（≥0.96）时SCR/TPP已崩溃。中间层（9–17）跨所有方法和稀疏度均表现出显著更高的敏感性。层敏感性源于上游扰动通过残差连接累积传播，而非局部ε²较大。基于此的分层稀疏度分配（早期层20%→中期60%）在相同平均稀疏度下实现更低困惑度（Wanda: 212→148；SparseGPT: 141→98）。跨架构实验验证方法排序与层敏感性模式一致。

Conclusion: 对于固定SAE，剪枝引起的SAE退化由扰动能量ε²（协方差加权范数）控制，与SAE的Lipschitz常数共同调制。Magnitude剪枝因忽略激活几何而对ε²近似最差；激活感知方法（Wanda、SparseGPT）通过隐式控制ε²更好地保留可解释性。中间层存在由扰动累积传播驱动的结构性脆弱性，分层稀疏度分配是实用缓解手段。局限：未涵盖结构化剪枝、量化、MLP/注意力头SAE；中间层敏感性的理论解释仍为开放问题。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are widely used to interpret the internal representations of large language models (LLMs), yet their reliability under post-hoc model compression remains poorly understood. We present a systematic study of how pruning affects SAE behavior and theoretically show that, for a fixed SAE, its impact is governed by perturbation energy, a covariance-weighted norm. This perspective exposes a key limitation of magnitude pruning: by ignoring activation geometry, it distorts the learned representation space and degrades SAE functionality. Activation-aware methods such as Wanda and SparseGPT, in contrast, implicitly control perturbation energy and are therefore substantially more robust at preserving SAE behavior. We further reveal a consistent structural vulnerability across all pruning methods: middle layers are significantly more sensitive to pruning than early or late layers. Guided by this insight, we propose a layer-wise sparsity allocation strategy, achieving lower perplexity under the same average pruning sparsity. Experiments across four model architectures validate our theoretical findings. Code is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [297] [Spectral Allocation: Why Muon Outperforms Adam, and How to Improve Muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25990)
*Xiaodong Wu,Wenyi Yu,Chao Zhang,Philip Woodland*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过"出样本光谱探测"分析Transformer损失景观沿动量缓冲谱方向的各向异性，揭示了一个由"易变头部"和"宽容主体"构成的稳定光谱配置；基于此提出光谱感知Muon（SAMuon）及其轻量版SAMuon-lite，通过锚定头部、放大主体的静态先验分配，在124M–1B模型上以更少token超越Muon基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon等正交优化器在大语言模型预训练中显著优于Adam，但其加速机制尚不明确——既有理论多基于各向同性曲率假设或仅做样本内聚合分析，无法解释实际Transformer训练中的曲率结构与改进空间。本文旨在通过直接在保留批次上探测动量缓冲奇异方向的真实曲率行为，回答"为什么Muon有效"以及"能否改进Muon"这一开放问题。

Method: 提出"出样本光谱探测"框架：沿真实训练轨迹的检查点，对每个2D权重矩阵的动量缓冲做SVD分解，按谱秩索引d聚合第d个奇异分量构成探针，在独立的保留批次上用局部二次近似（LQA）估计沿每个探针方向的损失最优步长η*_d，由此刻画损失景观的最优光谱配置。基于该配置，提出光谱感知Muon（SAMuon）：保持头部（rank 1）于Muon尺度，将主体按测量到的对数秩线性轮廓放大至γ倍，训练从均匀白化逐步热身至目标轮廓。完整SAMuon用低秩随机SVD实现，简化版SAMuon-lite（k=1）用幂迭代实现，两种变体均不增加持久优化器状态。

Result: 在"modded-nanogpt"的124M/300M/1B模型、1024–4096批大小配置上，SAMuon在所有设置中均优于调优后的AdamW和Muon（Scion实现），以13.3%–24.0%更少的训练token达到相同验证损失；SAMuon-lite以13.3%–22.1%的提升和接近Muon的运行时成本实现大部分增益。改进随批大小增长而扩大。此外，给出了理想化精确白化版本的收敛性证明（随机设置O(T^{-1/4})，确定性设置O(T^{-1/2})）。

Conclusion: Transformer预训练损失景观在动量缓冲的谱方向上具有一个稳定、各向异性的最优步长轮廓：少数易变头部（位于Edge-of-Stability）只能承受很小步长，而大部分宽容主体可承受数倍于此的步长。将Muon的均匀谱分配重塑为锚定头部、放大主体的静态先验（SAMuon/SAMuon-lite），能在不增加显著计算或内存开销的前提下恢复这一被Muon欠利用的主体空间，实现一致且显著的token效率提升。该分析也为Muon优于Adam（优于SGD）提供了首个统一的光谱分配解释。

Abstract: Orthogonal optimisers such as Muon can substantially accelerate large language model pretraining relative to Adam, yet the mechanism remains incompletely understood. We investigate this through an out-of-sample spectral probing analysis of Transformer loss landscapes. At checkpoints along real training trajectories, we decompose each momentum buffer into its singular directions and estimate the loss-optimal step size along each direction on held-out data. The resulting spectral profile is anisotropic yet stable across batches and training stages, and consistent across the optimisers and model scales: a volatile head operating at the Edge-of-Stability supports a much smaller step size than the tolerant bulk, which permits substantially larger steps. This profile provides a unified spectral allocation account of why Muon outperforms Adam, which outperforms SGD. It also exposes a limitation of Muon's uniform scaling: it still underutilises the bulk. Guided by this finding, we introduce Spectral-Aware Muon (SAMuon), which holds the head at the Muon scale and amplifies the bulk using a static spectral prior. We provide two variants: the complete SAMuon follows the measured profile using a low-rank randomised SVD and the simplified SAMuon-lite uses a two-level approximation via rank-one power iteration. Neither method adds persistent optimiser state or notable extra FLOPs beyond Muon at scale, and the idealised exact-whitening versions of both retain Muon's asymptotic convergence rate under standard assumptions. Across "modded-nanogpt" models from 124M to 1B parameters, both variants outperform tuned AdamW and Muon (Scion implementation) baselines in all evaluated model-scale and batch-size configurations. SAMuon requires 13.3% to 24.0% fewer training tokens to reach the same validation loss as Muon, while SAMuon-lite retains most of this gain with near-zero wall-clock overhead.

</details>


### [298] [Robust CurveMoE: Multi-Norm Adversarial Defense for Mixture-of-Experts Models via Mode Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26043)
*Xu Zhang,Ren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Robust CurveMoE，一种面向多范数对抗防御的专家混合（MoE）框架。通过鲁棒模式连通性曲线连接不同范数专化模型，从中采样互补专家并通过稀疏路由实现多范数鲁棒性，同时引入贡献引导的部分曲线更新和鲁棒性约束的专家选择策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将多范数鲁棒性目标在单一参数配置下联合优化，导致训练成本高且范数间鲁棒性存在不利的权衡。需要一种能保留各范数专化能力并自适应利用的框架。

Method: 1) 构建ℓ₁和ℓ∞鲁棒端点间的二次贝塞尔鲁棒连通性曲线；2) 基于梯度敏感性仅对影响力高的层进行专家化，其余层共享；3) 贡献引导的部分曲线更新（基于初始化梯度分数选择参数子集），并理论分析部分与全量更新的目标差距；4) 鲁棒性约束的专家选择（基于最差范数准确率阈值τ_cand过滤专家池），使用Top-1路由微调。

Result: 在CIFAR-100和ImageNet-100上使用WideResNet-28-10和ViT-Tiny，相较MSD和ERMC基线，在干净准确率、各范数准确率和Union准确率上均取得最优。Union准确率分别提升2.37和2.13个百分点。30%部分更新比全量更新减少36%训练时间且性能接近。K=3为效率与鲁棒性的最佳折中。

Conclusion: 鲁棒模式连通性曲线可作为多范数专家的结构化来源；通过稀疏MoE路由选择性地集成这些专家可有效实现多范数对抗鲁棒性，同时降低训练和部署成本。贡献引导的部分更新和鲁棒性约束的专家池选择是提升效率和抵抗路由攻击的关键。

Abstract: Multi-norm adversarial defense aims to protect neural networks against perturbations defined by different norm constraints, but existing methods typically optimize competing robustness objectives within a single parameter configuration, leading to substantial training cost and unfavorable robustness trade-offs. We propose Robust CurveMoE, an efficient mixture-of-experts framework that connects models specialized for different perturbation norms through a low-loss path and exploits the complementary robustness profiles of models along this path. Robust CurveMoE derives clean and norm-specialized experts from robustness-constrained curve locations and selectively expertizes only influential layers, while sharing the remaining parameters across routing paths. To further reduce curve-construction cost, we introduce contribution-guided partial updating, which selects influential curve parameters using initialization-based gradient scores. We also theoretically bound the objective gap between partial and full curve optimization. Experiments on CIFAR-100 and ImageNet-100 with WideResNet and Vision Transformer architectures show that Robust CurveMoE consistently improves clean, norm-specific, and Union accuracy over MSD and ERMC. In particular, it improves Union accuracy by 2.37 and 2.13 percentage points over the strongest baseline on CIFAR-100 and ImageNet-100, respectively. Extensive ablations further validate the effectiveness of partial updating, selective expertization, and robustness-constrained expert selection.

</details>


### [299] [Group-Shared Low-Rank Approximation for Mobile-Efficient Pointwise Convolutions in Large-Kernel CNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26069)
*Hao Luo,Yiting Yang,Wenyi Zhao,Man Jiang,Zhijun Lin,Ghulam Mohiuddin,Ting Jiang,Kunming Luo,Zihao Zhang,Qingsen Yan,Guoqing Wang,Wei Dong,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于SVD启发的通道分组共享（CGS）低秩近似策略，专门用于压缩大核CNN中占主导参数量（>87%）的点卷积（1×1卷积），以实现移动端高效部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大核CNN（如RepLKNet、ConvNeXt、SLaK）虽性能优异，但其二次增长的参数量严重阻碍了边缘设备部署。现有压缩方法聚焦于深度卷积（depthwise convolution），但忽视了点卷积才是参数主导部分（>87%），导致存储成本过高、模型加载时内存带宽受限。

Method: 基于SVD分解的同构思想，将点卷积权重矩阵分割为多个通道组，每组由两部分构成：(1) 跨组共享的高参数量下/上投影矩阵（对应SVD中的左右正交矩阵）；(2) 通道组特定的低参数量可缩放对角矩阵（对应SVD中的奇异值矩阵）。提出CGS-S（Small）、CGS-B（Base）、CGS-L（Large）三种变体，分别对应不同的合并策略。仅对参数密集的后期阶段（Stage 3-4）应用压缩。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K分类任务中，CGS-B相比RepLKNet-31B减少81%参数（79M→15M），精度仅下降4.2%（83.5%→80.0%）。在COCO检测和ADE20K分割任务上也表现优异。在六款主流手机（骁龙835至骁龙8 Gen3等）上的实测显示：RAM占用从~370MB降至~100-125MB，加载时间从~1500-3700ms降至~600-1500ms；结合INT8量化后，延迟降低25%、能耗降低43%。在5G/Wi-Fi 6无线卸载场景下，端到端延迟降低25-27%。

Conclusion: CGS低秩近似策略有效解决了大核CNN在资源受限边缘设备上的部署瓶颈，通过SVD同构的共享投影+组特定对角缩放设计，显著降低存储需求与内存加载压力，同时保持竞争性精度。该方法与INT8量化协同良好，并在多种移动平台（Android手机、IoT终端）上验证了实用可行性。

Abstract: Large-kernel Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) deliver remarkable performance in vision tasks by significantly expanding receptive fields, yet their quadratic parameter growth critically impedes storage-efficient edge deployment. While existing efficient architectures adopt parameter-efficient depthwise separable convolution backbones that leverage techniques like low-rank approximation and weight sharing to compress depthwise convolutions, we identify a critical oversight: pointwise convolutions dominate parameter volume (>87% in models like RepLKNet-31B) and constitute the primary deployment bottleneck on resource-constrained edge devices. This results in prohibitive storage costs and severe memory-loading constraints on resource-limited devices (e.g., smartphones with 4-12 GB Random Access Memory (RAM)). To overcome this, we propose Channel Group-Shared (CGS) low-rank approximation, a novel Singular Value Decomposition (SVD)-based parameter-sharing strategy. CGS constructs a structured low-rank paradigm isomorphic to SVD decomposition, comprising shared (high-parameter-cost) down/up-projection matrices across channel groups within a layer and channel-group-specific (low-parameter-cost) scalable diagonal matrices. This group-sharing design achieves significant parameter reduction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that large-kernel CNNs (RepLKNet, ConvNeXt, SLaK) enhanced with CGS strike an empirically favorable balance between competitive performance and substantially reduced storage costs. Crucially, by alleviating storage constraints, reducing memory bandwidth pressure during loading, and minimizing model loading latency, CGS enables the feasible deployment of pre-trained large-kernel CNN models on edge devices, thereby bridging the gap between high-performance vision models and practical edge deployment.

</details>


### [300] [Agentic Autoresearch for Cell-Edge Power Control: Radically Redefining the Researcher's Role](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26093)
*Ahmad Khan,Akram Bin Sediq,Sara Azadegi Naeini,Raviraj S. Adve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"代理式自动研究"(agentic autoresearch)协议，将无线资源管理中机器学习算法的设计层（架构、损失函数、训练策略等）完全交给自主AI编码代理，仅保留不可变评估器与研究章程作为人类干预点。代理在26小时、81次无人值守实验中，针对多小区网络中NP-hard的最小百分位速率和(SLqP)功率控制难题，自主跨越六大架构族进行探索，最终以单次前向推理达到收敛参考解99.5%的性能，推理成本降低约600倍，并发现具有可证明结构特性的输出参数化——在最小百分位处精确再现经典最大-最小最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统学习优化(learning-to-optimize)方法的架构、损失函数与训练流程均由人工设计，这一设计层成为新问题快速落地与可靠性的双重瓶颈。3GPP等工业标准明确瞄准的小区边缘(cell-edge)吞吐量对应百分位速率优化(SLqP)，远离最大-最小顶点时问题非凸、非光滑且强NP-hard，亟需验证代理式自动设计能否独立应对如此困难的目标。

Method: 采用三文件严谨分离的autoresearch协议：(1) prepare.py——SHA-256哈希锁定的不可变评估器（7小区环绕模型，COST231路径损耗+Rayleigh衰落，17对(K, q)固定留出集，强制10秒推理合约）；(2) train.py——唯一可编辑文件，包含模型、损失、训练循环；(3) program.md——研究章程，规定目标、指标、参考基线与探索协议。内外双循环：内层为2000步Adam（约1-2分钟CPU），外层为代理读取章程与日志，提出单一假设并预注册证伪器，按HeldoutScore（归一化SLqP均值）±0.0005噪声门限决定commit或revert。代理获得对架构族、输入表示、输出参数化、损失函数与任务采样律的完整权威，宽度-深度协议限制最多6个架构族并保护新族在固定窗口内不被撤回。代理由Claude Sonnet 5驱动外循环，Opus 5构建初始评估器与章程。

Result: 81次实验在26小时内完成，冠军方案得分1.4775 vs QFT参考解1.4850，达到最强已知基准99.5%；相对首个可行架构(1.3687)闭合94%差距；单组参数覆盖全部网络规模K与带内百分位目标；Apple M2 Pro上全网格推理2.52s对比参考求解器1583s，约600倍加速；K_q=1列精确再现p*(H)（相对误差3.2×10⁻⁷），由Proposition 2严格保证；最大增益来自诊断性发现（如exp.2的归一化损失、exp.49的cut clamp）而非超参扫描；负结果（容量扩展失败4次、目标软化3次、蒸馏6次）同样信息丰富——实验65蒸馏QFT参考解本身反低于自训练学生，揭示"跨多实现训练胜过模仿逐实例优化器"。

Conclusion: 代理式搜索在存在闭式最优的顶点处收敛到经典解（如本问题K_q=1的p*），在没有经典解的位置产生新结构——这一模式本身是问题诊断而非方法局限。SLqP问题跨越六大架构族、达最强已知基准的无人值守设计，结合可证明的精确性保证与审计日志，证明人类贡献可压缩为陈述问题与构建诚实判官；只要固定模拟器可作公正判官且标量指标可捕获工程目标，学习型无线算法的设计即变为可审计的无人值守过程。charter使用权威无歧义语言、限制架构族数量与禁止编辑评估器被证实为关键设计要素；单次随机搜索不应被视为全局最优设计。

Abstract: Designing machine learning algorithms for wireless resource management is labour-intensive: the architecture, the loss function and the training recipe are all specified by hand. We demonstrate that this design layer can be surrendered to an autonomous agent in its entirety. We adopt the autoresearch protocol, in which an AI coding agent edits a training script, runs a fixed-budget experiment, and retains or discards the change according to a single immutable metric. We grant the agent authority over the architecture family, the input representation, the output parameterization, the loss function and the task-sampling law, and set it a target chosen for its difficulty: sum-least-percentile-rate power control across a multicell network. The formulation targets cell-edge throughput and is non-convex, non-smooth and strongly NP-hard away from its max-min vertex. Safeguards render the results trustworthy: a hash-pinned evaluator, an enforced inference contract and a pre-registered falsifier per experiment. In eighty-one unattended experiments over twenty-six hours, the agent reached $99.5\%$ of a converged minorization-maximization reference in one fixed-cost inference pass, at roughly $600\times$ lower inference cost, closing $94\%$ of the gap from its first working architecture, with one parameter set serving every network size and percentile target. It recovered provable structure rather than tuned constants: the output parameterization it discovered reproduces the exact max-min-optimal allocation at the minimum percentile, for every value of the trained weights.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [301] [Analyzing and Reducing Search Quality Differences in Vector Similarity Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25185)
*Sara Mahdizadeh Shahri,Martin Prammer,Jignesh M. Patel,Akshitha Sriraman*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文指出向量相似性搜索中仅优化平均召回率会掩盖不同查询间显著的召回率差异，提出轻量级运行时系统RCheck，通过按聚类组动态调整搜索开销来缩小召回率差异并提高吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代向量数据库通过近似最近邻搜索（ANNS）以牺牲部分搜索质量为代价提升性能，实践中通常以平均召回率为目标调优参数，但作者发现即使平均召回率达到目标，不同查询之间的召回率仍存在巨大差异。低召回率查询损害用户体验和收入，高召回率查询则浪费计算资源，亟需一种方法检测并缩减这种差异。

Method: 1) 离线阶段使用k-means聚类将嵌入空间划分为k个逻辑组，组定义不修改底层索引结构；2) 运行时通过最近质心查找将每个查询分配到对应组；3) 每个监控周期内对每组采样少量查询并行执行精确搜索以估计组级召回率，并收集吞吐量与p99延迟；4) 维护每组的经验性召回表和性能表，使用线性回归估计召回率/性能随搜索开销变化的斜率；5) 按召回率偏差大小排序各组，对低召回组增加搜索开销（如HNSW的ef_search或IVFFlat的nprobes），对高召回组降低开销，受算子定义的吞吐量和延迟预算约束；6) 通过PostgreSQL SET LOCAL命令将每组搜索开销注入查询路径，无需修改pgvector。

Result: 在pgvector上使用GloVe-100、FairFace、CIFAR-100、DBPedia四个数据集和HNSW/IVFFlat两种索引评估，相同吞吐量下RCheck将平均召回率提升11%–93%，使达标查询比例增加8%–47%；相同目标召回率下吞吐量提升7%–41%，并显著降低p99尾延迟。在GloVe-100上RCheck将吞吐量提升40.91%并缩小了与理想逐查询自适应之间45%的差距。监控间隔15秒、k=50组在GloVe-100上效果最优。系统对嵌入模型和构建参数（M、ef_construction、nlist）的变化具有鲁棒性，运行时开销可忽略。

Conclusion: 优化平均召回率会掩盖ANNS中查询间显著的召回率差异，这种差异在不同索引类型、配置和嵌入模型下持续存在。RCheck作为运行时轻量级系统，通过按组细粒度动态调整搜索开销重新分配计算资源，能够在保持高吞吐量的同时缩小召回率差异，使召回率-吞吐量权衡更接近理想上限，且无需修改索引结构，便于生产部署。

Abstract: Modern database services scalably search over large data collections via Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search, which improves search performance at the cost of search quality, measured by recall. In practice, a database operator seeks to achieve a target mean recall while maximizing throughput across search queries. We show that optimizing for mean recall masks significant differences in recall across queries even when target recall is met. As a result, numerous queries face (1) below-target recall, hurting user experience and revenue and (2) above-target recall, wasting computation to deliver unnecessarily high search quality. Thus, it is critical to detect and reduce recall differences across queries. We design RCheck, a light-weight run-time system that identifies low-recall queries and reduces recall differences while achieving high throughput. RCheck's key design principle is to dynamically, efficiently adapt search effort by increasing effort for queries below target recall and decreasing effort for those above it. RCheck tunes available search effort parameters, making it readily deployable. We evaluate RCheck using the widely-used production-style pgvector database. At the same throughput, RCheck improves mean recall by 11-93% and enables 8-47% more queries to meet target recall compared to the state-of-the-art globally-tuned configuration.

</details>


### [302] [Bolt-on, Verifiable Provenance for LLM-Powered Data Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25210)
*Yiming Lin,Sepanta Zeighami,Aditya G. Parameswaran*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了BLIP框架，用于为LLM驱动的数据处理任务推断可验证的最小来源（verifiable minimal provenance），即输入文本中能复现完整文本答案的最小子序列。该框架采用两阶段方法（剪枝-精炼），保证返回最小来源，并在七个数据集上以低成本实现100%准确率，比最佳基线准确率提升超过30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）作为黑盒处理数据时，生成的答案缺乏可追溯性和可验证性，用户难以判断答案是否可信。现有方法（如嵌入相似度检索或直接让LLM识别来源）无法保证所识别的文本片段能真正复现答案，准确率低，给人工审核带来困难，尤其在法律、医学、金融等高风险领域这一问题尤为突出。

Method: 1. **概念定义**：提出"可验证来源"（verifiable provenance），即输入文本的子序列，能使用同一LLM复现与完整文本相同（或语义等价）的答案；进一步定义"最小性"（minimality），即移除其中任一句子后答案不再等价。2. **任务分类**：定义强单调性（strongly monotonic）和弱单调性（weakly monotonic）任务，并证明弱单调性任务中最小来源也是严格最小来源。3. **两阶段框架（BLIP）**：(a) **剪枝阶段**：将文本划分为等大小块，利用嵌入相似度或LLM评分对块排序，采用自底向上或自顶向下策略快速定位小规模来源；提出自适应策略，根据交叉点切换两种策略。(b) **精炼阶段**：采用Sequential-Greedy（逐一删除句子）或Exponential-Greedy（指数级批量删除）策略保证最小性，并利用KV缓存优化成本。4. **Top-k扩展**：基于最大堆的树搜索方法发现多个不同最小来源。5. **理论分析**：对各策略的正确性和成本进行理论证明，并通过LLM-as-a-judge评估语义等价性。

Result: 在七个数据集（Qasper、NL_DEV、HotpotQA、CUAD、PubMedQA、Movie、Restaurant）上的实验表明：(1) BLIP保证100%的可验证准确率，比最佳基线（LLM生成来源）准确率提升超过30%；(2) 两阶段策略将来源大小压缩至原始文本的3.3%-7.9%，成本仅为原始查询的约1.1-1.3倍；(3) 在TableQA任务上，BLIP的来源恢复率（R=1）和精确恢复率（eR>96%）均显著优于RAG和LLM基线；(4) 用户研究（8名参与者，4个领域）显示BLIP来源有用性评分4.75/5，比RAG（3.1）高，信息量恰当的比例82%，比RAG高61%。

Conclusion: BLIP首次为LLM驱动的数据处理任务引入了可验证来源的概念，并提供了一个即插即用、与具体LLM无关的框架，能以低成本保证返回最小可验证来源。其两阶段策略结合自适应方法和Top-k扩展，在理论和实验上均验证了有效性和高效性，为提升LLM应用的可信度和可解释性提供了实用解决方案。未来工作包括结合文本到SQL查询以及在数据科学工作流中扩展该方法。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are powerful tools for processing data. However, LLMs are also complex black-boxes, returning answers to queries on data, without any indication for where the answer came from or whether it is trustworthy. We introduce the notion of provenance for data processing with LLMs. While existing heuristics (such as embedding similarity or directly asking an LLM) could provide some hints for where the answer was derived, they provide no guarantees that the answer can be derived using the identified provenance, and indeed, are often incorrect. Instead, we propose the notion of verifiable provenance wherein we identify a subset of the input text that reproduces the same (or equivalent) answer as that on the complete text, and introduce the notion of minimality, where the verifiable provenance is as small as possible. To identify such a provenance, a naive solution would require checking all possible subsets of the source data with the LLM, which is prohibitively expensive. We present BLIP, a bolt-on framework for efficiently inferring a small-sized verifiable provenance for any LLM-powered data processing task, with any LLM. As part of BLIP, we introduce eight strategies, each guaranteed to find a minimal verifiable provenance, as well as an adaptive strategy that combines their strengths to reduce cost further. We further extend BLIP to produce multiple minimal verifiable provenances. Experiments on seven datasets show that the provenance generated by BLIP is always guaranteed to reproduce the answer - achieving over 30% higher accuracy than the best-performing baseline with a comparable provenance size. Moreover, BLIP incurs a low cost, comparable to the original query on the original data.

</details>


### [303] [DBcover: A White-box SQL Test Generation Framework for Coverage Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25573)
*Yankai Rong,Shuang Liu,Jinhao Dong,Qiang Yin,Wei Lu,Jianhua Wang,Xiaoyong Du*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: DBcover是一个LLM驱动的白盒SQL测试用例生成框架，通过两阶段上下文采集（全局SQL到路径映射+局部源代码上下文）和统一的图谱结构，引导轻量级LLM高效生成针对未覆盖代码区域的SQL测试用例，在PostgreSQL、MySQL及商业闭源数据库KingbaseES上取得了显著的代码覆盖率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RDBMS测试面临代码库庞大、逻辑复杂的挑战，传统模糊测试难以捕捉SQL输入与执行路径的对应关系，而符号执行存在路径爆炸和可扩展性问题；现有LLM测试生成方法依赖大参数量模型，且缺乏对SQL与内部代码路径映射的显式建模，难以精准触发深层、有状态依赖的代码区域。

Method: 两阶段方法：1）通过轻量级动态分析执行回归测试用例，提取SQL到路径对应关系和函数调用图作为全局上下文，连同目标函数的源代码及覆盖率信息作为局部上下文，统一构建为包含SQL查询、执行路径和函数三类节点的知识图谱；2）种子选择阶段基于调用图最短路径距离选择最接近未覆盖目标的种子测试用例，测试生成阶段采用五步细粒度推理pipeline（全局上下文分析→局部上下文检索→触发条件推导→覆盖改进策略→测试用例合成）引导LLM生成新SQL测试用例。

Result: 在PostgreSQL、MySQL上分别达到80.1%和82.3%的行覆盖率，较种子基线分别提升11.5%和11.1%，显著优于SQUIRREL（仅提升0.4%和0.2%）和ShQveL（覆盖率降至25%–31%）；消融实验中去除全局上下文的变体DBcover_nse在PostgreSQL/MySQL上覆盖率下降4.4%/5.1%，验证了两层上下文策略的必要性；在商业闭源数据库KingbaseES上将数据类型处理模块覆盖率从50.0%提升至80.0%，元数据管理模块从68.0%提升至81.4%。

Conclusion: DBcover通过将结构化程序分析与LLM的代码理解能力相结合，能在轻量级（32B）模型约束下有效生成针对复杂有状态代码区域的SQL测试用例，其两阶段上下文采集与知识图谱组织策略对闭源工业数据库同样适用，证明了该框架在覆盖导向的RDBMS测试中具备实用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Relational Database Management Systems (RDBMSs) are the backbone of modern data-intensive applications, making reliability and robustness critical. However, achieving high coverage in RDBMS testing remains challenging because of large codebases and complex execution logic. Traditional fuzzing relies on random SQL generation and cannot capture the correspondence between SQL inputs and internal execution paths, while symbolic execution suffers from prohibitive cost and scalability limitations. We propose DBcover, an LLM-driven white-box SQL test generation framework based on contextual reasoning. DBcover uses lightweight dynamic analysis to extract SQL-to-path correspondence and call graphs as global context, and collects source-level information around target functions as local context. These contexts are organized in a unified knowledge graph for efficient retrieval and reuse. DBcover then performs two-phase test generation: it first selects a semantically relevant seed whose execution path is close to the uncovered target, and then guides the LLM with global and local context to generate SQL test cases that trigger previously uncovered code regions. Experiments show that DBcover achieves 80.1% and 82.3% coverage on PostgreSQL and MySQL, and is also effective on the enterprise RDBMS KingbaseES, demonstrating its practical applicability to closed-source systems.

</details>


### [304] [PolyMemDB: A Polyglot Database System for AI Memory Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25577)
*Yu Wang,Jiaheng Lu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: PolyMemDB 是一个面向个人智能体长期记忆管理的多模态（polyglot）数据库系统，通过集成图数据库、向量数据库、概率数据库和时空数据库来统一管理异构记忆数据，并结合时间衰减与半环聚合的概率推理引擎，实现细粒度的数据溯源与长程事实冲突消解，从而缓解大语言模型的幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆系统存在两大核心局限：①依赖单一存储范式，难以高效管理多维异构数据（图、向量、概率、时空等）；②缺乏细粒度的数据溯源机制，无法处理长期交互中积累的时序事实冲突，导致 LLM 幻觉加剧。

Method: 系统包含三大核心组件：1) 多模态摄入与索引层：利用 LLM/VLM 完成实体对齐、指代消解、事实归一化和置信度估计；2) 多语言存储层（Polyglot Storage）：将归一化事实按维度分别路由到 Neo4j（关系图）、ProvSQL（概率数据库）、MobilityDB（时空数据库）、ChromaDB（向量库）以及对象存储；3) 概率推理与级联问答：自顶向下三级联邦检索（图 QA → 稠密检索 → 多模态检索）。关键创新在于将每条三元组边维护为历史观测序列 F=(f1,…,fT)，并基于半环框架扩展时间衰减（权重 w_i = c_i · λ^{T-i}），通过容斥原理计算正/负累积联合置信度 P⁺、P⁻，进而聚合为四维认知状态（动作、无动作、冲突、忽略），最终合成净证据可靠度 R∈[-1,1]，为下游 LLM 提供可解释的量化依据。

Result: 系统通过三个演示场景验证：①基于 LongMemEval 的 48 轮长会话问答（229 实体、221 关系），实现了带证据链和正向驱动概率的可视化推理；②基于扩展案例的"毕业旅行"行程总结，支持动态时间窗口与地理多边形联合过滤的时空记忆演化；③以 10 个月内 Alice 是否喜欢跑步为例（R≈0.22，S_conflict=52.3%），展示了概率推理在事实冲突下的细粒度决策能力，避免二值化幻觉。

Conclusion: PolyMemDB 通过多语言存储架构打破单一存储范式瓶颈，并通过融合时间衰减与半环概率推理的数据溯源机制有效解决长期事实冲突；该工作为构建可解释、可追溯、缓解幻觉的智能体记忆系统提供了新范式，并以 demonstration 形式面向会议展示。

Abstract: With the widespread adoption of personal intelligent agents, users generate massive, heterogeneous data during long-term interactions. Leveraging this data as long-term memory helps reduce token overhead and deliver personalized experiences. However, existing memory systems face two primary limitations: they rely on single-storage paradigms that fragment multi-dimensional data, and they lack fine-grained data provenance to resolve long-term factual conflicts, thereby worsening LLM hallucinations. In this demonstration, we introduce PolyMemDB, a novel system tailored for managing agent memory. PolyMemDB has a polyglot storage architecture designed to track and manage various memory types, including graph, vector, probability and spatial-temporal data. To ensure factual consistency and reduce hallucinations, it features a probabilistic inference engine that integrates temporal decay with semiring aggregation, resolving long-term factual conflicts, providing detailed data provenance, and enabling users to trace reasoning chains transparently.

</details>


### [305] [MetaSieve: Faster Relational Deep Learning through SQL-Based Metapath Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25903)
*Fahim Shahriar Khan,Ashraf Aboulnaga*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: MetaSieve 是一种面向关系型深度学习（RDL）的元路径选择层，通过 SQL 连接与聚合查询计算统计量，并基于互信息、成本与覆盖率构建评分函数，在训练前剪枝不具信息量的元路径，从而在保持甚至提升准确率的前提下，将 GNN 训练时间最多减少一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RDL 将多表关系数据库建模为异构图进行 GNN 训练，子图采样成本随外键链路扩展而急剧膨胀；不同元路径对任务的贡献差异很大，盲目随机采样会引入大量与标签无关的高扇出路径，既拖慢训练又可能损害精度，因此需要一种任务相关、轻量、与 GNN 架构无关的元路径选择方法。

Method: （1）基于 schema 枚举跳数 ≤ H 的所有候选元路径；（2）在 DuckDB 中以 frontier 表逐跳增量物化可达行，通过 SQL 聚合得到每个种子节点的 log-count 与 log-rate 统计，并按多批种子并行计算覆盖率；（3）以最近邻 MI 估计（KSG/Ross）得到归一化互信息（NMI），除以采样成本得到批量得分，再用 Student-t 单边 LCB 在 hop 内取百分位，结合 GMM 二分类切分“保留/剪枝”；（4）为兼容 PyG 等不追踪前缀的采样器，采用 prefix-agnostic 启发式将规则转化为采样配置。

Result: 在 RelBench 的 5 个数据库、节点分类与回归任务上，对比随机采样与 MPS-GNN，MetaSieve 在 HeteroGraphSAGE/HGT/RelGT 三种骨干下均显著降低每 epoch 训练时间（最高加速约 10×，如 beer-churn 上 RelGT），且通常提升准确率；预处理时间仅占 RelGT 随机采样 epoch 时间的极小分数，而 MPS-GNN 预处理可达数十个 epoch；消融显示 NMI+log-rate+coverage 全公式以及 prefix-agnostic 规则优于随机剪枝与多数/一致启发式；并通过调节 worker 数、batch 数和 DuckDB 内存上限实现对内存压力的可控调节。

Conclusion: 利用 DBMS 的 join 与聚合能力进行元路径统计与筛选，可在不依赖 GNN 参数的前提下同时显著加速 RDL 训练并改善模型表现，特别能与强骨干 RelGT 互补；该工作还展示了 SQL 工作负载在大规模关系数据上通过 frontier 物化、批并行与磁盘溢出来保持可扩展性，为未来面向 schema 演化与在线更新的元路径选择研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Relational Deep Learning (RDL) is an effective approach to machine learning over multi-table relational databases. In RDL, a database is modeled as a graph in which each row is a node and each foreign-key relation is an edge, and a graph neural network (GNN) is trained on this graph. Training a GNN requires sampling a subgraph around every seed node in the training set, and the cost of training is largely determined by the size of these subgraphs. This paper aims to reduce subgraph size by leveraging the join and aggregation capabilities of relational database systems. We observe that sampled subgraphs are obtained by following metapaths composed of foreign-key links, and that many of these metapaths can be pruned without loss of accuracy. We present MetaSieve, a metapath selection layer that determines which metapaths to retain and which to prune. For each candidate metapath extension, MetaSieve computes statistics via SQL join and aggregation queries and evaluates the extension based on a novel scoring function that prefers lightweight but informative candidates. Metapaths whose scores fall below a threshold are deemed uninformative and pruned. Metapath selection in MetaSieve is lightweight since it relies only on database statistics and task labels, and it is independent of GNN parameters, so it integrates with diverse GNN architectures for classification and regression. Our evaluation on the RelBench benchmark with multiple GNN backbones shows that MetaSieve consistently reduces per-epoch training time by large margins while maintaining and often improving accuracy.

</details>
